[OCH_SRC_GD] All original GD guards, including e>=1 and the exact residual genericity condition
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/HeightAndVanishing/Counts.lean#L9-L22
structure GD (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (K : Type) [Field K] [NumberField K] [Algebra K Ω]
    (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (e : ℕ) {O : Type} [CommRing O] {N : ℕ} [NeZero N] (ψ : DirichletCharacter O N) : Prop where
  l_odd : l ≠ 2
  e_pos : 1 ≤ e
  N_even : Even N
  ord : ¬ l ∣ orderOf ψ
  ine : comp l ψ ^ 2 = 1
  par : PsiEven e ψ
  quad : Module.finrank ℚ K = 2
  imag : IsTotallyComplex K
  l_cop : ¬ l ∣ (discr K).natAbs * classNumber K
  cop : (discr K).natAbs.Coprime N
  eta : ¬ (cycMod ℚ Ω l 1).ker ⊓ (Chr.dirG ℚ Ω (chiP l ψ)).ker ≤ galOf ℚ K Ω
  gen : ¬ l - 1 ∣ 2 * e


[OCH_SRC_COHINP] The unchanged five-field CohInp foundation
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/UnitaryThreeGroups/KatoUse.lean#L19-L24
structure CohInp : Prop where
  loc : Loc.LocalPublishedTheorems
  pt : GlobalTheory.Published.PoitouTateNormalised
  gf : GlobalFiniteness
  ge : GlobalEulerChar
  lemK : ∃ CK : ℝ, LemmaK CK


[OCH_SRC_INPZ] The exact InpZ record already supplied by KIZ2
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/ModularityArgument/KatoQuadraticCharacters/KatoTwoOfUseN.lean#L55-L61

  lemM' : LemmaM'
  lemP : LemmaP
  rigII : RigII
  adK : Af.AdVanishK

[OCH_SRC_ONECHAR] Complete original OneChar contract on every coefficient datum and fixed complex bridge
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/UnitaryThreeGroups/KatoUse.lean#L54-L64
def OneChar : Prop :=
  ∀ (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (K : Type) [Field K] [NumberField K] [IsCMField K] [Algebra K Ω]
    [IsAlgClosure K Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (c : Coef l) (N : ℕ) [NeZero N] (e : ℕ) (ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O N),
    GD Ω K l e ψ → ∀ (ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ) (σ : G K Ω) (t : Qbar l),
    t = algebraMap c.O (PadicAlgCl l) ((Chr.Psi l e (Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ) (galEmb ℚ K Ω σ) : c.O) + 1 +
      (((Chr.Psi l e (Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ) (galEmb ℚ K Ω σ))⁻¹ : c.Oˣ) : c.O)) →
    (∀ ρ : G K Ω →* GL (Fin 3) (Qbar l), Af.Adm3 ι e N ρ ∨ Af.Adm2 ι e (N * (discr K).natAbs) ρ →
      (ρ σ : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) (Qbar l)).trace ≠ t) →
    Vlen Ω c e ψ ≠ ⊤ ∧ ∃ X : Finset (G K Ω →* GL (Fin 3) (Qbar l)),
      (∀ ρ ∈ X, Af.Adm3 ι e N ρ ∨ Af.Adm2 ι e (N * (discr K).natAbs) ρ) ∧ (∀ ρ ∈ X, ∀ ρ' ∈ X, Af.RepIso ρ ρ' → ρ = ρ') ∧
      Vn Ω c e ψ ≤ 2 * ∑ ρ ∈ X, vl l ((ρ σ : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) (Qbar l)).trace - t)


[OCH_SRC_AUT_UNRAM] FinA, AutB, LemW and their one-joint-marked-element unramified-coefficient producer
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/AutomorphicCases/AutomorphyUnramifiedCoefficients.lean#L12-L27
def FinA (g₁ : G ℚ Ω) : Prop :=
  ∀ (U : D.ULoc) (S : D.Ring U g₁), Module.Finite c.O (weightZeroSubalgebra c.O S.r.hom)

def AutB [IsCMField K] (ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ) (g₁ : G ℚ Ω) : Prop :=
  ∀ (U : D.ULoc) (S : D.Ring U g₁) (ξ : entAlg c.O S.r.hom →ₐ[c.O] PadicAlgCl l)
    (r : G ℚ Ω →* GG 3 (PadicAlgCl l)) (hr : HasMult D.η r), IsPush ξ S.r.hom r → TypeI D.η r → D.IsAutI ι r hr

def LemW (ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ) (g₁ : G ℚ Ω) : Prop :=
  ∀ (U : D.ULoc) (S : D.Ring U g₁) (ξ : entAlg c.O S.r.hom →ₐ[c.O] PadicAlgCl l)
    (r : G ℚ Ω →* GG 3 (PadicAlgCl l)), IsPush ξ S.r.hom r → TypeII D.η r → (∀ g ∈ D.η.ker, (r g).mat 1 1 = 1) →
    ∀ W : G ℚ Ω →* GL (Fin 2) (PadicAlgCl l), D.IsExt r W → D.IsModular ι W

def AutomorphyUnramifiedCoefficients : Prop :=
  ∀ (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (K : Type) [Field K] [NumberField K] [IsCMField K] [Algebra K Ω]
    (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (c : Coef l) (N : ℕ) [NeZero N] (e : ℕ) (ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O N) (D : Dat Ω K c e ψ),
    eO l c.O = 1 → ∀ ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ, ∃ g₁ : G ℚ Ω, D.Marked g₁ ∧ FinA D g₁ ∧ AutB D ι g₁ ∧ LemW D ι g₁


[OCH_SRC_ONECHAR_TRANSFER] Injective coefficient transport, unramified realization, actual point push and base trace
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/ModularityArgument/KatoQuadraticCharacters/OneChar.lean#L23-L100
private def chHom : DirichletCharacter O N →* DirichletCharacter O' N where
  toFun χ := χ.ringHomComp f
  map_one' := MulChar.ringHomComp_one f
  map_mul' a b := MulChar.ringHomComp_mul a b f

private theorem chHom_inj (hf : Function.Injective f) : Function.Injective (chHom (N := N) f) :=
  MulChar.injective_ringHomComp hf

private theorem chHom_comp (r : ℕ) (χ : DirichletCharacter O N) : chHom f (comp r χ) = comp r (chHom f χ) := by
  refine MulChar.ext fun a => ?_
  show f (comp r χ a) = _
  simp only [HV.comp, MulChar.ofUnitHom_coe, MonoidHom.comp_apply, MulChar.coe_toUnitHom]
  rfl

private theorem chHom_chiP (hf : Function.Injective f) (l : ℕ) (χ : DirichletCharacter O N) :
    chHom f (chiP l χ) = chiP l (chHom f χ) := by
  have h : chHom f (th0 l χ) = th0 l (chHom f χ) := by
    simp only [th0, lPrime, lPart, map_mul, map_inv, map_pow, orderOf_injective _ (chHom_inj f hf)]
  simp only [chiP, map_mul, map_inv, chHom_comp, h]

private theorem gd_chHom (hf : Function.Injective f) [NeZero N] {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω]
    {K : Type} [Field K] [NumberField K] [Algebra K Ω] {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {e : ℕ} (χ : DirichletCharacter O N) :
    GD Ω K l e (chHom f χ) ↔ GD Ω K l e χ := by
  have h1 : orderOf (chHom f χ) = orderOf χ := orderOf_injective _ (chHom_inj f hf) _
  have h2 : comp l (chHom f χ) ^ 2 = 1 ↔ comp l χ ^ 2 = 1 := by
    rw [← chHom_comp, ← map_pow, map_eq_one_iff _ (chHom_inj f hf)]
  have h3 : PsiEven e (chHom f χ) ↔ PsiEven e χ := by
    show f (χ (-1)) = _ ↔ χ (-1) = _
    rw [← hf.eq_iff (a := χ (-1)), map_pow, map_neg f, map_one f]
  have h4 : (Chr.dirG ℚ Ω (chiP l (chHom f χ))).ker = (Chr.dirG ℚ Ω (chiP l χ)).ker := by
    rw [← chHom_chiP f hf]
    ext g
    simp only [MonoidHom.mem_ker, Chr.dirG, MonoidHom.comp_apply, Units.ext_iff, MulChar.coe_toUnitHom, Units.val_one]
    exact map_eq_one_iff f hf
  exact ⟨fun h => ⟨h.l_odd, h.e_pos, h.N_even, h1 ▸ h.ord, h2.1 h.ine, h3.1 h.par, h.quad, h.imag, h.l_cop, h.cop,
      h4 ▸ h.eta, h.gen⟩,
    fun h => ⟨h.l_odd, h.e_pos, h.N_even, h1 ▸ h.ord, h2.2 h.ine, h3.2 h.par, h.quad, h.imag, h.l_cop, h.cop,
      h4 ▸ h.eta, h.gen⟩⟩

end Transfer

private theorem exists_unram {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] (c : Coef l) {N : ℕ} [NeZero N] (ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O N)
    (h : ¬ l ∣ orderOf ψ) : ∃ (c' : Coef l) (ψ' : DirichletCharacter c'.O N), eO l c'.O = 1 ∧
      ∀ a : ZMod N, ((ψ a : c.O) : PadicAlgCl l) = ((ψ' a : c'.O) : PadicAlgCl l) := by
  haveI : NeZero (orderOf ψ) := ⟨fun h0 => h (h0 ▸ dvd_zero l)⟩
  obtain ⟨c', he, hr⟩ := (coefSup l).2.1 (orderOf ψ) h
  obtain ⟨ζ, hζ⟩ := HasEnoughRootsOfUnity.exists_primitiveRoot c'.O (orderOf ψ)
  have hζ' : IsPrimitiveRoot (ζ : PadicAlgCl l) (orderOf ψ) :=
    hζ.map_of_injective (f := c'.O.val) Subtype.val_injective
  have hmem : ∀ a : ZMod N, ((ψ a : c.O) : PadicAlgCl l) ∈ c'.O := by
    intro a
    by_cases ha : IsUnit a
    · obtain ⟨u, rfl⟩ := ha
      have h1 : ψ u ^ orderOf ψ = 1 := by
        rw [← MulChar.pow_apply_coe, pow_orderOf_eq_one, MulChar.one_apply_coe]
      have h2 : ((ψ u : c.O) : PadicAlgCl l) ^ orderOf ψ = 1 := by
        rw [← SubmonoidClass.coe_pow, h1]; rfl
      obtain ⟨i, -, hi⟩ := hζ'.eq_pow_of_pow_eq_one h2
      rw [← hi]
      exact pow_mem ζ.2 i
    · rw [ψ.map_nonunit ha]
      exact zero_mem _
  refine ⟨c', ⟨⟨⟨fun a => ⟨_, hmem a⟩, Subtype.ext ?_⟩, fun a b => Subtype.ext ?_⟩, fun a ha => Subtype.ext ?_⟩, he,
    fun _ => rfl⟩
  · show ((ψ 1 : c.O) : PadicAlgCl l) = 1
    rw [map_one]; rfl
  · show ((ψ (a * b) : c.O) : PadicAlgCl l) = _
    rw [map_mul]; rfl
  · show ((ψ a : c.O) : PadicAlgCl l) = 0
    rw [ψ.map_nonunit ha]; rfl

private theorem isPush_push {O Γ R κ : Type} [CommRing O] [Group Γ] [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] [CommRing κ] [Algebra O κ]
    (f : R →ₐ[O] κ) (r : Γ →* GG 3 R) : IsPush (f.comp (entAlg O r).val) r (push f r) :=
  fun g => ⟨rfl, fun a b => ⟨Algebra.subset_adjoin ⟨g, a, b, rfl⟩, rfl⟩⟩

private theorem trace_reducible_three_dim {Γ R : Type} [Group Γ] [CommRing R] (η : Γ →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : Γ →* Rˣ) (g : Γ) :
    (r0 η Ψ g).mat.trace = (Ψ g : R) + 1 + (((Ψ g)⁻¹ : Rˣ) : R) := by
  simp [GG.mat, tor_val, Matrix.trace_diagonal, Fin.sum_univ_three, wt1]


[OCH_SRC_ONECHAR_UNRAM] The original same-object unramified-coefficient OneChar consumer
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/ModularityArgument/KatoQuadraticCharacters/OneChar.lean#L102-L156
private theorem oneChar_unram (hI : UpperTheory.V.Z.Kato.InpZ) (hA : Aut.AutomorphyUnramifiedCoefficients)
    (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (K : Type) [Field K] [NumberField K] [IsCMField K] [Algebra K Ω]
    [IsAlgClosure K Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (c : Coef l) (N : ℕ) [NeZero N] (e : ℕ) (ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O N)
    (he : eO l c.O = 1) (hG : GD Ω K l e ψ) (ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ) (σ : G K Ω) (t : Qbar l)
    (ht : t = algebraMap c.O (PadicAlgCl l) ((Chr.Psi l e (Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ) (galEmb ℚ K Ω σ) : c.O) + 1 +
      (((Chr.Psi l e (Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ) (galEmb ℚ K Ω σ))⁻¹ : c.Oˣ) : c.O)))
    (hne : ∀ ρ : G K Ω →* GL (Fin 3) (Qbar l), Af.Adm3 ι e N ρ ∨ Af.Adm2 ι e (N * (discr K).natAbs) ρ →
      (ρ σ : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) (Qbar l)).trace ≠ t) :
    Vlen Ω c e ψ ≠ ⊤ ∧ ∃ X : Finset (G K Ω →* GL (Fin 3) (Qbar l)),
      (∀ ρ ∈ X, Af.Adm3 ι e N ρ ∨ Af.Adm2 ι e (N * (discr K).natAbs) ρ) ∧ (∀ ρ ∈ X, ∀ ρ' ∈ X, Af.RepIso ρ ρ' → ρ = ρ') ∧
      Vn Ω c e ψ ≤ 2 * ∑ ρ ∈ X, vl l ((ρ σ : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) (Qbar l)).trace - t) := by
  classical
  obtain ⟨D⟩ := exists_dat hG
  obtain ⟨g₁, hM, hFin, hB, hW⟩ := hA Ω K l c N e ψ D he ι
  obtain ⟨U⟩ := exists_uLoc hI.toCohInp D
  obtain ⟨S⟩ := exists_ring hI.toCohInp D U g₁ hM
  obtain ⟨M⟩ := exists_model hI.toCohInp hI.lemP D hM S
  have hmult : ∀ f : S.R →ₐ[c.O] PadicAlgCl l, HasMult D.η (push f S.r.hom) := fun f g => S.r.hasMult g
  let ρ : (S.R →ₐ[c.O] PadicAlgCl l) → (G K Ω →* GL (Fin 3) (Qbar l)) := fun f => D.rhoK (push f S.r.hom) (hmult f)
  have hσ : D.η (galEmb ℚ K Ω σ) = 1 := by
    rw [← MonoidHom.mem_ker, D.η_ker]
    exact galEmb_mem ℚ K Ω σ
  have htr : ∀ f, trDiff D.η D.Ψ S.r.hom (galEmb ℚ K Ω σ) f =
      (ρ f σ : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) (Qbar l)).trace - t := by
    intro f
    rw [trDiff, trace_reducible_three_dim, ht]
    congr 1
    exact AddMonoidHom.map_trace (f : S.R →+* PadicAlgCl l) _
  have hadm : ∀ f, TwoPt D.η S.r.hom f → Af.Adm3 ι e N (ρ f) ∨ Af.Adm2 ι e (N * (discr K).natAbs) (ρ f) := by
    intro f hf
    have hp := isPt_push D hM S f hf.1
    rcases (twoPt_shape S hG.l_odd hM f hf).1 with h | h
    · exact Or.inl (admissible_rank_three_type_one hI.toCohInp D hM hp ι h (hB U S _ _ _ (isPush_push f _) h))
    · exact Or.inr (admissible_of_typeII hI.toCohInp D hM hp ι h
        (hW U S _ _ (isPush_push f _) h (typeII_facts hI.toCohInp D hM hp h).1))
  obtain ⟨hlen, T, hT, hTo, hle⟩ := model_upper S M hG.l_odd hM hI.lemM (hFin U S)
    (UpperTheory.V.Z.Kato.ptHyp_of hI D hM S ι hB hW M.x₀ M.base) (galEmb ℚ K Ω σ) hσ
    fun f hf => by rw [htr]; exact sub_ne_zero.2 (hne _ (hadm f hf))
  have hV := vlen_le hI.toCohInp hI.lemM' D hM S M
  have hinj : ∀ f ∈ T, ∀ f' ∈ T, Af.RepIso (ρ f) (ρ f') → f = f' := by
    rintro f hf f' hf' ⟨g, hg⟩
    obtain ⟨s, hs⟩ := (twoPt_shape S hG.l_odd hM f (hT.1 f hf)).2 f' (hT.1 f' hf').1 ⟨g, fun x hx => by
      rw [D.η_ker, ← range_galEmb] at hx
      obtain ⟨y, rfl⟩ := hx
      exact (congrArg Units.val (eq_mul_inv_iff_mul_eq.1 (hg y))).symm⟩
    exact hTo f hf f' hf' s hs
  refine ⟨ne_top_of_le_ne_top hlen hV, T.image ρ, fun x hx => ?_, fun x hx x' hx' h => ?_, ?_⟩
  · obtain ⟨f, hf, rfl⟩ := Finset.mem_image.1 hx
    exact hadm f (hT.1 f hf)
  · obtain ⟨f, hf, rfl⟩ := Finset.mem_image.1 hx
    obtain ⟨f', hf', rfl⟩ := Finset.mem_image.1 hx'
    rw [hinj f hf f' hf' h]
  · rw [Finset.sum_image fun f hf f' hf' h => hinj f hf f' hf' ⟨1, fun s => by rw [h, one_mul, inv_one, mul_one]⟩]
    simp only [← htr]
    exact (Chr.nrm_mono l hV hlen).trans hle


[OCH_SRC_ONECHAR_ORIGINAL] The exact full oneChar implication and return to the original coefficients
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/ModularityArgument/KatoQuadraticCharacters/OneChar.lean#L158-L178
theorem UpperTheory.V.Z.Kato.oneChar :
    UpperTheory.V.Z.Kato.InpZ → Aut.AutomorphyUnramifiedCoefficients → OneChar := by
  intro hI hA Ω _ _ _ K _ _ _ _ _ l _ c N _ e ψ hG ι σ t ht hne
  obtain ⟨c', ψ', he, hψ⟩ := exists_unram c ψ hG.ord
  have h1 : chHom c.O.val.toRingHom ψ = chHom c'.O.val.toRingHom ψ' := MulChar.ext fun a => hψ a
  have hG' : GD Ω K l e ψ' :=
    (gd_chHom c'.O.val.toRingHom Subtype.val_injective ψ').1
      (h1 ▸ (gd_chHom c.O.val.toRingHom Subtype.val_injective ψ).2 hG)
  obtain ⟨h2, h3⟩ := coefIndep Ω l c c' N e ψ ψ' hψ
  have h4 : ∀ g, Units.map (algebraMap c.O (PadicAlgCl l)).toMonoidHom (Chr.Psi l e (Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ) g) =
      Units.map (algebraMap c'.O (PadicAlgCl l)).toMonoidHom (Chr.Psi l e (Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ') g) := by
    intro g
    simp only [Chr.Psi, MonoidHom.mul_apply, MonoidHom.zpow_apply, map_mul, map_zpow]
    congr 1
    exact Units.ext (hψ _)
  have h5 := congrArg Units.val (h4 (galEmb ℚ K Ω σ))
  have h6 := congrArg (fun u : (PadicAlgCl l)ˣ => (u⁻¹).val) (h4 (galEmb ℚ K Ω σ))
  rw [Ne, h2, h3]
  refine oneChar_unram hI hA Ω K l c' N e ψ' he hG' ι σ t ?_ hne
  rw [ht, map_add, map_add, map_add, map_add, map_one, map_one]
  exact congrArg₂ (· + ·) (congrArg (· + 1) h5) h6


[OCH_SRC_LOCAL_DATA] Dat3, HL, actual crystalline/ordinary deformation predicates and chosen inertia/Frobenius
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/PadicHodgeTheory/IsInertiaInfiniteCyclotomic.lean#L11-L85
structure Dat3 (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (O : Type) [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] [Algebra ℤ_[l] O] where
  η : GQ l →* ℤˣ
  Ψ : GQ l →* Oˣ
  τ : GQ l →* Oˣ
  e : ℕ
  Kv : FinExt l
  odd : l ≠ 2
  e_pos : 1 ≤ e
  η_ker : ∀ g, η g = 1 ↔ g ∈ GK Kv
  η_unr : ∀ g ∈ Iq l, η g = 1
  τ_open : IsOpen (τ.ker : Set (GQ l))
  Ψ_cont : AdicCont (maximalIdeal O) fun g => (Ψ g : O)
  Ψ_I : ∀ g ∈ Iq l, Ψ g = cycA l l O g ^ e * τ g

def hodge0 (e : ℕ) (i : ℤ) : ℕ := if i = -(e : ℤ) ∨ i = 0 ∨ i = e then 1 else 0

namespace Dat3

variable {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] [Algebra ℤ_[l] O] (D : Dat3 l O)

def HL : Subgroup (GQ l) := D.η.ker ⊓ D.τ.ker

def rz (A : Type) [CommRing A] [Algebra O A] : GQ l →* GG 3 A := (GG.map (algebraMap O A)).comp (r0 D.η D.Ψ)

section Types

variable {B : Type} [CommRing B] [Algebra O B] [Algebra ℚ_[l] B]

def IsType (r : GQ l →* GG 3 B) : Prop :=
  ∃ ρ : ↥(GK D.Kv) →* GL (Fin 3) B, (∀ g, ρ g = (r g).left) ∧
    PotCrysType D.Kv (colRep ρ) 3 (hodge0 D.e)
      fun γ => algebraMap O B ((D.τ γ : O) + 1 + (((D.τ γ)⁻¹ : Oˣ) : O))

def IsLCr (r : GQ l →* GG 3 B) : Prop :=
  ∃ ρ : ↥D.HL →* GL (Fin 3) B, (∀ g, ρ g = (r g).left) ∧ IsCr D.HL.subtype (colRep ρ)

variable {E : Type} [CommRing E] [Algebra O E] (π : B →+* E)

structure InDcrAt (r₁ : GQ l →* GG 3 E) (r : GQ l →* GG 3 B) : Prop where
  mult : HasMult D.η r
  red : (GG.map π).comp r = r₁
  cont : ∀ a b, QCont l fun g => (r g).mat a b
  cr : D.IsLCr r

abbrev InDcr (r : GQ l →* GG 3 B) : Prop := D.InDcrAt π (D.rz E) r

structure InDtri (r : GQ l →* GG 3 B) : Prop extends D.InDcrAt π (D.rz E) r where
  tri : ∀ g a b, wt a b < 0 → (r g).mat a b = 0

def InDord (r : GQ l →* GG 3 B) : Prop :=
  ∃ (u : GQ l →* GG 3 B) (n : GL (Fin 3) B), D.InDtri π u ∧ CongOne (RingHom.ker π) n ∧
    (∀ a b, 0 ≤ wt a b → ((n : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) B) - 1) a b = 0) ∧ r = conjBy n u

end Types

abbrev Univ : Type 1 := U3.Univ (GCond.noCondition O D.η D.Ψ)

section Ord

open Polynomial

def IsInertiaInfiniteCyclotomic (s : GQ l) : Prop := s ∈ D.HL ∧ s ∈ Iq l ∧ ¬ IsOfFinOrder (cyc l l s)

def IsFr (σ : GQ l) : Prop :=
  (∃ h : σ ∈ D.HL, ((Iq l).comap D.HL.subtype ⊔ Subgroup.zpowers (⟨σ, h⟩ : ↥D.HL)).topologicalClosure = ⊤) ∧
    ∃ (σ₀ : GQ l) (m : ℕ), IsFrob σ₀ ∧ 1 ≤ m ∧ (σ₀ ^ m)⁻¹ * σ ∈ Iq l

def s0 : GQ l := Classical.epsilon (IsInertiaInfiniteCyclotomic D)

def fr : GQ l := Classical.epsilon (IsFr D)

def cc (a : Fin 3) : O :=
  (UpperTheory.V.UnitaryThreeInertia.psi D.Ψ a (s0 D) - UpperTheory.V.UnitaryThreeInertia.psi D.Ψ (a + 1) (s0 D)) *
    (UpperTheory.V.UnitaryThreeInertia.psi D.Ψ a (s0 D) - UpperTheory.V.UnitaryThreeInertia.psi D.Ψ (a + 2) (s0 D))



[OCH_SRC_LOCAL_IDEALS] The literal cc, tr, dd, del, ordinary ideal, saturation, It and all point predicates
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/PadicHodgeTheory/IsInertiaInfiniteCyclotomic.lean#L87-L183

variable {C : Type} [CommRing C] [Algebra O C] (r : GQ l →* GG 3 C)

def sh (a : Fin 3) (s : GQ l) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) C :=
  (r s).mat - Matrix.scalar _ (algebraMap O C (UpperTheory.V.UnitaryThreeInertia.psi D.Ψ a s))

def ordIdeal : Ideal C :=
  Ideal.span ({x | ∃ s ∈ D.HL ⊓ Iq l, ∃ i : ℕ, x = ((r s).mat.charpoly -
      ∏ a, (X - Polynomial.C (algebraMap O C (UpperTheory.V.UnitaryThreeInertia.psi D.Ψ a s)))).coeff i} ∪
    {x | ∃ s : Fin 3 → GQ l, (∀ a, s a ∈ D.HL ⊓ Iq l) ∧ ∃ i j,
      x = (sh D r 0 (s 0) * sh D r 1 (s 1) * sh D r 2 (s 2)) i j})

def tr (a : Fin 3) : C := (sh D r (a + 1) (s0 D) * sh D r (a + 2) (s0 D) * (r (fr D)).mat).trace

def dd (a : Fin 3) : C :=
  algebraMap O C (cc D (a + 1)) * tr D r a - algebraMap O C ((cycA l l O (fr D) : Oˣ) * cc D a) * tr D r (a + 1)

def del : C := (l : C) * dd D r 0 * dd D r 1

end Eq

end Ord

variable (U : D.Univ)

def IY : Ideal U.R where
  carrier := {a | ∃ n : ℕ, del D U.r.hom ^ n * a ∈ ordIdeal D U.r.hom}
  zero_mem' := ⟨0, by simp⟩
  add_mem' := by
    rintro a b ⟨m, hm⟩ ⟨n, hn⟩
    refine ⟨m + n, ?_⟩
    have e : del D U.r.hom ^ (m + n) * (a + b) = del D U.r.hom ^ n * (del D U.r.hom ^ m * a) +
        del D U.r.hom ^ m * (del D U.r.hom ^ n * b) := by ring
    rw [e]
    exact Ideal.add_mem _ (Ideal.mul_mem_left _ _ hm) (Ideal.mul_mem_left _ _ hn)
  smul_mem' := by
    rintro c a ⟨n, hn⟩
    refine ⟨n, ?_⟩
    rw [smul_eq_mul, mul_left_comm]
    exact Ideal.mul_mem_left _ _ hn

def Icr : Ideal U.R := (IY D U).radical

def primesAt0 : Set (Ideal U.R) :=
  {P | P ∈ (D.Icr U).minimalPrimes ∧ ∀ x : U.R →ₐ[O] O, push x U.r.hom = r0 D.η D.Ψ → P ≤ RingHom.ker x}

def It : Ideal U.R := sInf (D.primesAt0 U)

abbrev Rt : Type := U.R ⧸ D.It U

def On {C : Type} [CommRing C] [Algebra O C] (r : GQ l →* GG 3 C) : Prop :=
  ∃ f : U.R →ₐ[O] C, push f U.r.hom = r ∧ D.It U ≤ RingHom.ker f

def PtsCr (J : Ideal U.R) : Prop :=
  ∀ (B : Type) [CommRing B] [Algebra ℚ_[l] B] [Module.Finite ℚ_[l] B] [Algebra O B] [Algebra ℤ_[l] B]
    [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] O B] [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] B] (f : U.R →ₐ[O] B),
    J ≤ RingHom.ker f → D.IsLCr (push f U.r.hom)

def CrPts (J : Ideal U.R) : Prop :=
  ∀ (E : Type) [Field E] [Algebra ℚ_[l] E] [Module.Finite ℚ_[l] E] [Algebra O E] [Algebra ℤ_[l] E]
    [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] O E] [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] E]
    (B : Type) [CommRing B] [Algebra ℚ_[l] B] [Module.Finite ℚ_[l] B] [Algebra O B] [Algebra ℤ_[l] B]
    [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] O B] [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] B] [Algebra E B] [IsScalarTower O E B]
    [IsScalarTower ℚ_[l] E B] (π : B →ₐ[E] E), (∀ a, π a = 0 → IsNilpotent a) → ∀ f : U.R →ₐ[O] B,
    D.InDcr (π : B →+* E) (push f U.r.hom) → J ≤ RingHom.ker f

end Dat3

structure IsCompl {O R E σ : Type} [CommRing O] [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] [Field E] [Algebra O E]
    (I : Ideal R) (x : R →ₐ[O] O) (φ : R →ₐ[O] MvPowerSeries σ E) : Prop where
  ker : RingHom.ker φ = I
  const : ∀ a, MvPowerSeries.constantCoeff (φ a) = algebraMap O E (x a)
  dense : ∀ (N : ℕ) (f : MvPowerSeries σ E), ∃ (a : R) (c : O), c ≠ 0 ∧
    c • f - φ a ∈ maximalIdeal (MvPowerSeries σ E) ^ N
  inj : ∀ (N : ℕ) (a : R), φ a ∈ maximalIdeal (MvPowerSeries σ E) ^ N →
    ∃ c : O, c ≠ 0 ∧ c • a ∈ RingHom.ker x ^ N ⊔ I

def DualDef {G E : Type} [Group G] [CommRing E] (r : G →* GG 3 E) (γ : G → Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) E)
    (r' : G →* GG 3 (DualNumber E)) : Prop :=
  ∀ g, (r' g).right = (r g).right ∧ ∀ a b, (r' g).mat a b = ⟨(r g).mat a b, (γ g * (r g).mat) a b⟩

example (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (hl : l ≠ 2) (hc : AdicCont (maximalIdeal ℤ_[l]) fun g => (cycA l l ℤ_[l] g : ℤ_[l])) :
    Dat3 l ℤ_[l] where
  η := 1
  Ψ := cycA l l ℤ_[l]
  τ := 1
  e := 1
  Kv := botExt l
  odd := hl
  e_pos := le_rfl
  η_ker g := by simp [GK, botExt]
  η_unr _ _ := rfl
  τ_open := by simp
  Ψ_cont := hc
  Ψ_I g _ := by simp

end UpperTheory.V.Loc


[OCH_SRC_POSITIVE_PERIODS] Positive-character Frobenius no-fixed-vector and exact finite-cocycle construction
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/PadicHodgeTheory/PosExact.lean#L227-L683
theorem surj_of_inj (K : FinExt l) (ρ : Representation E ↥(GK K) V)
    [Module.Finite E ↥(Dcris ((PP l).PD K) ρ)] (h : NoFix (GK K).subtype ρ) :
    ∀ y ∈ Dcris ((PP l).PD K) ρ, ∃ z ∈ Dcris ((PP l).PD K) ρ, z - φVB ((PP l).PD K) (LL := E) z = y := by
  have hc : ∀ g (y : V ⊗[ℚ_[l]] Bcr l), actVB ((PP l).PD K) ρ g (φVB ((PP l).PD K) (LL := E) y) =
      φVB ((PP l).PD K) (LL := E) (actVB ((PP l).PD K) ρ g y) := by
    intro g y
    induction y using TensorProduct.induction_on with
    | zero => simp
    | add x x' hx hx' => simp only [map_add, hx, hx']
    | tmul v b => simp [actVB, φVB, ((PP l).PD K).φ_comm]
  have hm : ∀ y ∈ Dcris ((PP l).PD K) ρ,
      (LinearMap.id - φVB ((PP l).PD K) (LL := E) (V := V) : _ →ₗ[E] _) y ∈ Dcris ((PP l).PD K) ρ := by
    intro y hy
    refine Submodule.sub_mem _ hy ?_
    rw [mem_Dcris] at hy ⊢
    intro g
    rw [hc, hy]
  have hi : Function.Injective (LinearMap.restrict _ hm) := by
    rw [← LinearMap.ker_eq_bot, eq_bot_iff]
    rintro ⟨y, hy⟩ h0
    have h1 : y - φVB ((PP l).PD K) (LL := E) y = 0 := congrArg Subtype.val h0
    refine Subtype.ext (h y (fun g => ?_) ?_)
    · rw [actC_eq]; exact (mem_Dcris _ _ _).1 hy g
    · rw [phC_eq K]; exact (sub_eq_zero.1 h1).symm
  intro y hy
  obtain ⟨z, hz⟩ := LinearMap.injective_iff_surjective.1 hi ⟨y, hy⟩
  exact ⟨z, z.2, congrArg Subtype.val hz⟩

end Tied

section Char

variable {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {E : Type} [Field E] [Algebra ℚ_[l] E] [FiniteDimensional ℚ_[l] E]
  [Algebra ℤ_[l] E] [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] E] [TopologicalSpace E] [IsModuleTopology ℚ_[l] E]
  [IsTopologicalRing E]

theorem noFix_char_field (K : FinExt l) (κ : ↥(GK K) →* Eˣ) (hκ : Continuous fun g => (κ g : E)) (w : ℤ)
    (hw : 1 ≤ w) (h : ∀ g : ↥(GK K), (g : GQ l) ∈ Iq l → κ g = cycA l l E (g : GQ l) ^ w) :
    NoFix (GK K).subtype (chRep κ) := by
  obtain ⟨T⟩ := tiedP l
  have hκ' : Continuous fun g => ((κ⁻¹ g : Eˣ) : E) := by
    have : (fun g => ((κ⁻¹ g : Eˣ) : E)) = fun g => (κ g⁻¹ : E) := by
      funext g; simp
    rw [this]
    exact hκ.comp continuous_inv
  have hca : ReciprocityTheory.Gal.ContAction (chRep κ⁻¹) := by
    have : (fun p : ↥(GK K) × E => chRep κ⁻¹ p.1 p.2) = fun p => ((κ⁻¹ p.1 : Eˣ) : E) * p.2 := by
      funext p; simp [chRep]
    unfold ReciprocityTheory.Gal.ContAction
    rw [this]
    exact (hκ'.comp continuous_fst).mul continuous_snd
  have hinv : ∀ g : ↥(GK K), (g : GQ l) ∈ Iq l → κ⁻¹ g = cycA l l E (g : GQ l) ^ (-w) := by
    intro g hg
    rw [MonoidHom.inv_apply, h g hg, zpow_neg]
  have hcr : IsCrys ((PP l).PD K) (chRep κ⁻¹) := by
    refine (isCrField K _).1.1 ((isCrIff K _).1.1 (crUnr _ (isOpenEmbedding_GK K) _ hca (-w) fun g hg x => ?_))
    rw [Subgroup.subtype_apply, ← hinv g hg]
    simp [chRep]
  have hfil := ((charFil _ (isOpenEmbedding_GK K) κ⁻¹ hκ' (-w)
    ⟨⊤, isOpen_univ, fun g _ hg => hinv g hg⟩).2 1).1 (by omega)
  rw [FilD, Ddr_eq, FilT_eq K, inf_eq_left] at hfil
  haveI : IsDomain (PP l).R.B := GlobalTheory.RegularRing.isDomain_of_tied T K
  haveI : Module.Finite E ↥(Dcris ((PP l).PD K) (chRep κ⁻¹)) := by
    haveI := ((PP l).PD K).finK₀
    apply Module.finite_of_finrank_pos
    rw [hcr]
    exact Nat.mul_pos finrank_pos finrank_pos
  have hno : NoFix (GK K).subtype (chRep κ⁻¹) := by
    intro y hy hφ
    have h1 := hfil (iota_mem K _ ((mem_Dcris _ _ y).2 fun g => by rw [← actC_eq]; exact hy g))
    rw [phC_eq K] at hφ
    obtain ⟨v, rfl⟩ := (GlobalTheory.T.tensor_fundamental_exact_sequence_crystalline l _ _ T K E E).2 y hφ
      (GlobalTheory.DRFil.FilV_antitone ((PP l).PD K) (show (0 : ℤ) ≤ 1 by omega) h1)
    by_contra hne
    have hv : v ≠ 0 := by rintro rfl; exact hne (zero_tmul _ _)
    obtain ⟨μ, hμ⟩ := Module.Projective.exists_dual_eq_one ℚ_[l] hv
    have h2 := ctr_mem_Fil K 1 μ h1
    simp only [ιV, AlgebraTensorModule.map_tmul, ctr_tmul, LinearMap.id_apply, hμ, one_smul] at h2
    exact GlobalTheory.DRFil.one_notMem_fil_one ((PP l).PD K) (by simpa using h2)
  intro x hx hφ
  refine char_generic ((PP l).PD K) κ ?_ (surj_of_inj K _ hno) x
    ((mem_Dcris _ _ x).2 fun g => by rw [← actC_eq]; exact hx g) (by rw [← phC_eq K]; exact hφ)
  exact GlobalTheory.Per.TiedSpan.exists_sum_of_isCrys ((PP l).PD K) (GlobalTheory.TateDualCrys.fracInv_of_tied T K)
    (GlobalTheory.TateDualCrys.lineUnit_of_tied T K) _ hcr 1

theorem noFix_char {G : Type} [Group G] [TopologicalSpace G] [IsTopologicalGroup G] (φ : G →* GQ l)
    (hφ : IsOpenEmbedding φ) (κ : G →* Eˣ) (hκ : Continuous fun g => (κ g : E)) (w : ℤ)
    (hw : 1 ≤ w) (h : HasWeight φ κ w) : NoFix φ (chRep κ) := by
  obtain ⟨U, hU, h⟩ := h
  have hφ' : IsOpenEmbedding (φ.comp U.subtype) := hφ.comp hU.isOpenEmbedding_subtypeVal
  obtain ⟨K, e, -, he, hcomp, -⟩ := openField _ hφ'
  have hval : ∀ g : ↥(GK K), φ (e.symm g : U) = (g : GQ l) := fun g => by
    have := DFunLike.congr_fun hcomp (e.symm g)
    simpa using this.symm
  have := noFix_char_field K (κ.comp (U.subtype.comp e.symm.toMonoidHom))
    (hκ.comp (continuous_subtype_val.comp he)) w hw (fun g hg => by
      have := h (e.symm g : U) (e.symm g).2 (by rw [hval]; exact hg)
      rw [hval] at this
      exact this)
  intro x hx hx'
  refine this x (fun g => ?_) hx'
  have h1 := hx (e.symm g : U)
  unfold actC at h1 ⊢
  rw [hval] at h1
  exact h1

end Char

section Filt

theorem filtBy_of_surjective {G A V V' : Type} [Group G] [CommRing A] [AddCommGroup V] [Module A V]
    [AddCommGroup V'] [Module A V'] {ρ : Representation A G V} {ρ' : Representation A G V'} {S : Set (G →* Aˣ)}
    (f : V →ₗ[A] V') (hf : ∀ g x, f (ρ g x) = ρ' g (f x)) (hs : Function.Surjective f) (h : FiltBy ρ S) :
    FiltBy ρ' S := by
  obtain ⟨n, F, h0, hn, hF⟩ := h
  refine ⟨n, fun i => (F i).map f, by simp [h0], by simp [hn, LinearMap.range_eq_top.2 hs], fun i hi => ?_⟩
  obtain ⟨h1, κ, hκ, x, hx, hg⟩ := hF i hi
  refine ⟨Submodule.map_mono h1, κ, hκ, f x, ?_, fun g => ⟨_, hg g, by simp [hf]⟩⟩
  simp [hx, Submodule.map_sup, Submodule.map_span]

variable {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {E V : Type} [Field E] [Algebra ℚ_[l] E] [AddCommGroup V] [Module E V]
  [Module ℚ_[l] V] [IsScalarTower ℚ_[l] E V] {G : Type} [Group G]

theorem noFix_of_filt (φ : G →* GQ l) (ρ : Representation E G V) (S : Set (G →* Eˣ)) (h : FiltBy ρ S)
    (hS : ∀ κ ∈ S, (∃ (μ : V →ₗ[E] E) (v : V), ∀ g, μ (ρ g v) = κ g) → NoFix φ (chRep κ)) : NoFix φ ρ := by
  obtain ⟨n, F, h0, hn, hF⟩ := h
  have hst : ∀ i ≤ n, ∀ g, ∀ v ∈ F i, ρ g v ∈ F i := by
    intro i
    induction i with
    | zero => intro _ g v hv; rw [h0] at hv ⊢; rw [(Submodule.mem_bot E).1 hv, map_zero]; exact zero_mem _
    | succ i ih =>
      intro hi g v hv
      obtain ⟨h1, κ, -, x, hx, hg⟩ := hF i hi
      rw [hx] at hv
      obtain ⟨u, hu, _, hw, rfl⟩ := Submodule.mem_sup.1 hv
      obtain ⟨a, rfl⟩ := Submodule.mem_span_singleton.1 hw
      have hx' : x ∈ F (i + 1) := by rw [hx]; exact Submodule.mem_sup_right (Submodule.mem_span_singleton_self x)
      rw [map_add, map_smul]
      refine add_mem (h1 (ih (by omega) g u hu)) (Submodule.smul_mem _ _ ?_)
      have := add_mem (h1 (hg g)) (Submodule.smul_mem _ ((κ g : Eˣ) : E) hx')
      rwa [sub_add_cancel] at this
  let R : ℕ → Submodule E (V ⊗[ℚ_[l]] Bcr l) := fun i =>
    Submodule.span E {z | ∃ v ∈ F i, ∃ b, z = v ⊗ₜ[ℚ_[l]] b}
  have key : ∀ i ≤ n, ∀ x ∈ R i, (∀ g, actC φ ρ g x = x) → phC E V x = x → x = 0 := by
    intro i
    induction i with
    | zero =>
      intro _ x hx _ _
      have : R 0 ≤ ⊥ := Submodule.span_le.2 (by
        rintro _ ⟨v, hv, b, rfl⟩
        rw [h0] at hv
        rw [(Submodule.mem_bot E).1 hv, zero_tmul]
        exact zero_mem _)
      exact this hx
    | succ i ih =>
      intro hi z hz hinv hφ
      obtain ⟨h1, κ, hκ, x, hx, hg⟩ := hF i hi
      refine ih (by omega) z ?_ hinv hφ
      by_cases hxi : x ∈ F i
      · have : F (i + 1) = F i := by
          rw [hx, sup_eq_left, Submodule.span_singleton_le_iff_mem]; exact hxi
        simp only [R, this] at hz
        exact hz
      obtain ⟨μ, hμx, hμ⟩ := Submodule.exists_dual_map_eq_bot_of_notMem hxi inferInstance
      obtain ⟨μ, hμx, hμ⟩ : ∃ μ : V →ₗ[E] E, μ x = 1 ∧ ∀ u ∈ F i, μ u = 0 :=
        ⟨(μ x)⁻¹ • μ, by simp [hμx], fun u hu => by
          have : μ u = 0 := by
            have : μ u ∈ (F i).map μ := ⟨u, hu, rfl⟩
            rwa [hμ] at this
          simp [this]⟩
      have hdec : ∀ v ∈ F (i + 1), v - μ v • x ∈ F i := by
        intro v hv
        rw [hx] at hv
        obtain ⟨u, hu, _, hw, rfl⟩ := Submodule.mem_sup.1 hv
        obtain ⟨a, rfl⟩ := Submodule.mem_span_singleton.1 hw
        simpa [hμ u hu, hμx] using hu
      have hκx : ∀ g, μ (ρ g x) = κ g := fun g => by
        have := hμ _ (hg g)
        rwa [map_sub, map_smul, hμx, smul_eq_mul, mul_one, sub_eq_zero] at this
      have heq : ∀ g, ∀ v ∈ F (i + 1), μ (ρ g v) = κ g * μ v := fun g v hv => by
        have := hμ _ (hst i (by omega) g _ (hdec v hv))
        rw [map_sub, map_sub, map_smul, map_smul, hκx, sub_eq_zero] at this
        rw [this, smul_eq_mul, mul_comm]
      let M : V ⊗[ℚ_[l]] Bcr l →ₗ[E] E ⊗[ℚ_[l]] Bcr l := AlgebraTensorModule.map μ LinearMap.id
      let N : E ⊗[ℚ_[l]] Bcr l →ₗ[E] V ⊗[ℚ_[l]] Bcr l :=
        AlgebraTensorModule.map (LinearMap.toSpanSingleton E V x) LinearMap.id
      have hM : ∀ g, ∀ y ∈ R (i + 1), actC φ (chRep κ) g (M y) = M (actC φ ρ g y) := by
        intro g y hy
        refine LinearMap.eqOn_span' (f := actC φ (chRep κ) g ∘ₗ M) (g := M ∘ₗ actC φ ρ g) ?_ hy
        rintro _ ⟨v, hv, b, rfl⟩
        simp [M, actC, chRep, heq g v hv]
      have hMφ : ∀ y, phC E E (M y) = M (phC E V y) := by
        intro y
        induction y using TensorProduct.induction_on with
        | zero => simp
        | add x x' hx hx' => simp only [map_add, hx, hx']
        | tmul v b => simp [phC, M]
      have hM0 : M z = 0 := hS κ hκ ⟨μ, x, hκx⟩ _ (fun g => by rw [hM g z hz, hinv]) (by rw [hMφ, hφ])
      have hle : R (i + 1) ≤ (R i).comap (LinearMap.id - N ∘ₗ M) := Submodule.span_le.2 (by
        rintro _ ⟨v, hv, b, rfl⟩
        refine Submodule.subset_span ⟨_, hdec v hv, b, ?_⟩
        simp [M, N, sub_tmul])
      have := hle hz
      simpa [hM0] using this
  intro x
  refine key n le_rfl x ?_
  induction x using TensorProduct.induction_on with
  | zero => exact zero_mem _
  | add x x' hx hx' => exact add_mem hx hx'
  | tmul v b => exact Submodule.subset_span ⟨v, by simp [hn], b, rfl⟩

end Filt

theorem noFix_quot {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {G : Type} [Group G] [TopologicalSpace G] [IsTopologicalGroup G]
    (φ : G →* GQ l) (hφ : IsOpenEmbedding φ) {E : Type} [Field E] [Algebra ℚ_[l] E] [FiniteDimensional ℚ_[l] E]
    [Algebra ℤ_[l] E] [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] E] {V : Type} [AddCommGroup V] [Module E V] [Module ℚ_[l] V]
    [IsScalarTower ℚ_[l] E V] [TopologicalSpace V] [IsModuleTopology ℚ_[l] V] [IsTopologicalAddGroup V]
    [ContinuousConstSMul E V] {V' : Type} [AddCommGroup V'] [Module E V'] [Module ℚ_[l] V']
    [IsScalarTower ℚ_[l] E V'] (ρ : Representation E G V) (ρ' : Representation E G V')
    (hc : ReciprocityTheory.Gal.ContAction ρ) (h : FiltBy ρ {κ | ∃ w : ℤ, 1 ≤ w ∧ HasWeight φ κ w}) (f : V →ₗ[E] V')
    (hf : ∀ g x, f (ρ g x) = ρ' g (f x)) (hs : Function.Surjective f) : NoFix φ ρ' := by
  letI : TopologicalSpace E := moduleTopology ℚ_[l] E
  haveI : IsModuleTopology ℚ_[l] E := ⟨rfl⟩
  haveI : IsTopologicalRing E := IsModuleTopology.isTopologicalRing ℚ_[l] E
  haveI : ContinuousSMul ℚ_[l] E := IsModuleTopology.toContinuousSMul ℚ_[l] E
  refine noFix_of_filt φ ρ' _ (filtBy_of_surjective f hf hs h) ?_
  rintro κ ⟨w, hw, hκ⟩ ⟨μ, v, hv⟩
  obtain ⟨v, rfl⟩ := hs v
  refine noFix_char φ hφ κ ?_ w hw hκ
  have : (fun g => (κ g : E)) = fun g => ((μ ∘ₗ f).restrictScalars ℚ_[l]) (ρ g v) := by
    funext g; simp [hf, hv]
  rw [this]
  exact (IsModuleTopology.continuous_of_linearMap _).comp (hc.comp (continuous_id.prodMk continuous_const))

open GlobalTheory.BKRightExact in

theorem lift_dR {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {P : LocPrime l} {AI : LocAinf l P} (T : LocTied l P AI)
    (K : FinExt l) (LL : Type) [Field LL] [Algebra ℚ_[l] LL] [FiniteDimensional ℚ_[l] LL]
    {V : Type} [AddCommGroup V] [Module LL V] [Module ℚ_[l] V] [IsScalarTower ℚ_[l] LL V]
    [FiniteDimensional LL V] (ρ : Representation LL ↥(GK K) V)
    {V' : Type} [AddCommGroup V'] [Module LL V'] [Module ℚ_[l] V'] [IsScalarTower ℚ_[l] LL V']
    [FiniteDimensional LL V'] (ρ' : Representation LL ↥(GK K) V') (f : V →ₗ[LL] V')
    (hf : ∀ g x, f (ρ g x) = ρ' g (f x)) (hs : Function.Surjective f) (h : IsDR (P.PD K) ρ) :
    IsDR (P.PD K) ρ' ∧ ∀ y ∈ DdR (P.PD K) ρ', ∃ x ∈ DdR (P.PD K) ρ,
        AlgebraTensorModule.map f (LinearMap.id : P.R.BdR →ₗ[ℚ_[l]] P.R.BdR) x = y := by
  let L : FinExt l := ⟨⊥, inferInstance⟩
  letI : Algebra ↥L.1 LL :=
    ((algebraMap ℚ_[l] LL).comp (IntermediateField.botEquiv ℚ_[l] (Om l)).toRingEquiv.toRingHom).toAlgebra
  haveI : IsScalarTower ℚ_[l] ↥L.1 LL := IsScalarTower.of_algebraMap_eq fun x =>
    (congrArg (algebraMap ℚ_[l] LL) ((IntermediateField.botEquiv ℚ_[l] (Om l)).commutes x)).symm
  haveI : FiniteDimensional ↥L.1 LL := Module.Finite.of_restrictScalars_finite ℚ_[l] _ _
  letI : Module ↥L.1 V := Module.compHom V (algebraMap ↥L.1 LL)
  letI : Module ↥L.1 V' := Module.compHom V' (algebraMap ↥L.1 LL)
  haveI : IsScalarTower ↥L.1 LL V := IsScalarTower.of_algebraMap_smul fun _ _ => rfl
  haveI : IsScalarTower ↥L.1 LL V' := IsScalarTower.of_algebraMap_smul fun _ _ => rfl
  haveI : IsScalarTower ℚ_[l] ↥L.1 V := IsScalarTower.to₁₂₄ ℚ_[l] ↥L.1 LL V
  haveI : IsScalarTower ℚ_[l] ↥L.1 V' := IsScalarTower.to₁₂₄ ℚ_[l] ↥L.1 LL V'
  haveI : FiniteDimensional ↥L.1 V := Module.Finite.trans LL V
  haveI : FiniteDimensional ↥L.1 V' := Module.Finite.trans LL V'
  have hd : IsDR (P.PD K) (res ↥L.1 ρ) := by
    unfold IsDR at h ⊢
    rw [DdR_res]
    change finrank ↥L.1 ↥(DdR (P.PD K) ρ) = _
    rw [← Module.finrank_mul_finrank ↥L.1 LL ↥(DdR (P.PD K) ρ), ← Module.finrank_mul_finrank ↥L.1 LL V, h,
      mul_left_comm]
  obtain ⟨h2, h3⟩ := (GlobalTheory.DRSubQuot.deRham_sub_quot l P AI T K L V (res ↥L.1 ρ) V' (res ↥L.1 ρ')
    (f.restrictScalars ↥L.1) hf).2 hs hd
  rw [DdR_res, DdR_res] at h3
  refine ⟨isDR_of_res (P.PD K) ↥L.1 ρ' h2, fun y hy => ?_⟩
  obtain ⟨a, ha⟩ := GlobalTheory.DRFil.exists_mem_FilV (P.PD K) (LL := ↥L.1) y
  obtain ⟨x, hx, hxy⟩ := h3 a y ⟨hy, ha⟩
  exact ⟨x, hx.1, hxy⟩

section Core

variable {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] (K : FinExt l)
  {V : Type} [AddCommGroup V] [Module ℚ_[l] V] [FiniteDimensional ℚ_[l] V] [TopologicalSpace V]
  [IsModuleTopology ℚ_[l] V] [IsTopologicalAddGroup V] [ContinuousConstSMul ℚ_[l] V]
  {V' : Type} [AddCommGroup V'] [Module ℚ_[l] V'] [FiniteDimensional ℚ_[l] V'] [TopologicalSpace V']
  [IsModuleTopology ℚ_[l] V'] [IsTopologicalAddGroup V'] [ContinuousConstSMul ℚ_[l] V']
  (ρ : Representation ℚ_[l] ↥(GK K) V) (ρ' : Representation ℚ_[l] ↥(GK K) V')

theorem exists_fixed (hc : ∀ x, Continuous fun g => ρ g x) (hn : NoFix (GK K).subtype ρ) (c : ↥(GK K) → V)
    (x : V ⊗[ℚ_[l]] Bcr l) (hx : ∀ g, c g ⊗ₜ[ℚ_[l]] (1 : Bcr l) = actVB ((PP l).PD K) ρ g x - x) :
    ∃ x : V ⊗[ℚ_[l]] Bcr l, φVB ((PP l).PD K) (LL := ℚ_[l]) x = x ∧
      ∀ g, c g ⊗ₜ[ℚ_[l]] (1 : Bcr l) = actVB ((PP l).PD K) ρ g x - x := by
  obtain ⟨T⟩ := tiedP l
  letI : Module ℤ_[l] V := Module.compHom V (algebraMap ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l])
  haveI : IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] V := IsScalarTower.of_algebraMap_smul fun _ _ => rfl
  haveI : Module.Finite ℚ_[l] ↥(Dcris ((PP l).PD K) ρ) :=
    (GlobalTheory.Per.FontaineTied.fontCore_all_of_tied l _ _ T K ℚ_[l] ℤ_[l] GlobalTheory.Published.BlochKato.goodO_padicInt
      { V := V, ρ := ρ, cont := hc }).1.1
  have hcm : ∀ g (y : V ⊗[ℚ_[l]] Bcr l), actVB ((PP l).PD K) ρ g (φVB ((PP l).PD K) (LL := ℚ_[l]) y) =
      φVB ((PP l).PD K) (LL := ℚ_[l]) (actVB ((PP l).PD K) ρ g y) := by
    intro g y
    induction y using TensorProduct.induction_on with
    | zero => simp
    | add x x' hx hx' => simp only [map_add, hx, hx']
    | tmul v b => simp [actVB, φVB, ((PP l).PD K).φ_comm]
  have hd : x - φVB ((PP l).PD K) (LL := ℚ_[l]) x ∈ Dcris ((PP l).PD K) ρ := by
    rw [mem_Dcris]
    intro g
    have h1 := congrArg (φVB ((PP l).PD K) (LL := ℚ_[l])) (hx g)
    have h2 : φVB ((PP l).PD K) (LL := ℚ_[l]) (c g ⊗ₜ[ℚ_[l]] (1 : Bcr l)) = c g ⊗ₜ[ℚ_[l]] (1 : Bcr l) := by
      simp [φVB]
    rw [h2, hx g, map_sub, ← hcm] at h1
    rw [map_sub, sub_eq_sub_iff_sub_eq_sub, h1]
  obtain ⟨z, hz, hzx⟩ := surj_of_inj K ρ hn _ hd
  refine ⟨x - z, ?_, fun g => ?_⟩
  · rw [map_sub, sub_eq_sub_iff_sub_eq_sub, ← neg_inj, neg_sub, neg_sub, ← hzx]
  · rw [map_sub, (mem_Dcris _ _ _).1 hz g, hx g]
    abel

open GlobalTheory.BKRightExact in
theorem core_surj (hc : ∀ x, Continuous fun g => ρ g x) (hc' : ∀ x, Continuous fun g => ρ' g x)
    (hdr : IsDR ((PP l).PD K) ρ) (hn : NoFix (GK K).subtype ρ') (f : V →ₗ[ℚ_[l]] V')
    (hf : ∀ g x, f (ρ g x) = ρ' g (f x)) (hs : Function.Surjective f) (c' : ↥(GK K) → V')
    (x' : V' ⊗[ℚ_[l]] Bcr l) (hx' : ∀ g, c' g ⊗ₜ[ℚ_[l]] (1 : Bcr l) = actVB ((PP l).PD K) ρ' g x' - x') :
    ∃ c ∈ Formalism.WeaklyAdmissibleFiltrations.finiteCocycles ((PP l).PD K) ρ, ∃ v, ∀ g, f (c g) - c' g = ρ' g v - v := by
  obtain ⟨T⟩ := tiedP l
  obtain ⟨x', hφ, hx'⟩ := exists_fixed K ρ' hc' hn c' x' hx'
  letI : Module ℤ_[l] V := Module.compHom V (algebraMap ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l])
  haveI : IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] V := IsScalarTower.of_algebraMap_smul fun _ _ => rfl
  letI : Module ℤ_[l] V' := Module.compHom V' (algebraMap ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l])
  haveI : IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] V' := IsScalarTower.of_algebraMap_smul fun _ _ => rfl
  obtain ⟨hdr', hl2⟩ := lift_dR T K ℚ_[l] ρ ρ' f hf hs hdr
  obtain ⟨-, d₂', hd2, hfil⟩ := GlobalTheory.T.crystalline_coboundary_lift l _ _ T K ℚ_[l] ℤ_[l] GlobalTheory.Published.BlochKato.goodO_padicInt
    { V := V', ρ := ρ', cont := hc' } hdr' c' x' hx'
  obtain ⟨d₂, hd₂, e₂⟩ := hl2 _ hd2
  obtain ⟨x, y, c, hy, hx1, hx2, hc, hcx⟩ := GlobalTheory.T.exists_blochKato_cocycle_of_crys_deRham_pair l _ _ T K ℚ_[l] ℤ_[l]
    GlobalTheory.Published.BlochKato.goodO_padicInt { V := V, ρ := ρ, cont := hc } 0 (zero_mem _) d₂ hd₂
  dsimp only at *
  refine ⟨c, hc, ?_⟩
  rw [← hx2, map_sub, map_ιV] at e₂
  obtain ⟨v, hv⟩ := (GlobalTheory.T.tensor_fundamental_exact_sequence_crystalline l _ _ T K ℚ_[l] V').2
    (AlgebraTensorModule.map f (LinearMap.id : Bcr l →ₗ[ℚ_[l]] Bcr l) x - x')
    (by rw [map_sub, hφ, ← map_φVB, ← sub_eq_zero.1 hx1])
    (by
      have := Submodule.sub_mem _ (map_mem_FilV ((PP l).PD K) f hy) hfil
      rw [← e₂] at this
      rw [map_sub]
      convert this using 1
      abel)
  refine ⟨v, fun g => tmul_one_injective ((PP l).PD K) ?_⟩
  have h1 := congrArg (AlgebraTensorModule.map f (LinearMap.id : Bcr l →ₗ[ℚ_[l]] Bcr l)) (hcx g)
  rw [map_sub, map_actVB ((PP l).PD K) f hf, AlgebraTensorModule.map_tmul, LinearMap.id_apply] at h1
  have h2 : actVB ((PP l).PD K) ρ' g (v ⊗ₜ[ℚ_[l]] (1 : Bcr l)) = ρ' g v ⊗ₜ[ℚ_[l]] (1 : Bcr l) := by
    simp [actVB]
  rw [sub_tmul, sub_tmul, h1, hx', ← h2, ← hv, map_sub]
  abel

omit [FiniteDimensional ℚ_[l] V] [TopologicalSpace V]
  [IsModuleTopology ℚ_[l] V] [IsTopologicalAddGroup V] [ContinuousConstSMul ℚ_[l] V] in
open GlobalTheory.BKRightExact in
theorem core_inj {W : Type} [AddCommGroup W] [Module ℚ_[l] W] (σ : Representation ℚ_[l] ↥(GK K) W)
    (hc' : ∀ x, Continuous fun g => ρ' g x) (hn : NoFix (GK K).subtype ρ') (hW : NoFix (GK K).subtype σ)
    (f : V →ₗ[ℚ_[l]] V') (hf : ∀ g x, f (ρ g x) = ρ' g (f x)) (hi : Function.Injective f)
    (p : V' →ₗ[ℚ_[l]] W) (hp : ∀ g x, p (ρ' g x) = σ g (p x)) (hs : Function.Surjective p)
    (hex : Function.Exact f p) (c : ↥(GK K) → V) (x' : V' ⊗[ℚ_[l]] Bcr l)
    (hx' : ∀ g, f (c g) ⊗ₜ[ℚ_[l]] (1 : Bcr l) = actVB ((PP l).PD K) ρ' g x' - x') :
    ∃ x : V ⊗[ℚ_[l]] Bcr l, ∀ g, c g ⊗ₜ[ℚ_[l]] (1 : Bcr l) = actVB ((PP l).PD K) ρ g x - x := by
  obtain ⟨x', hφ, hx'⟩ := exists_fixed K ρ' hc' hn _ x' hx'
  have h0 : AlgebraTensorModule.map p (LinearMap.id : Bcr l →ₗ[ℚ_[l]] Bcr l) x' = 0 := by
    refine hW _ (fun g => ?_) ?_
    · rw [actC_eq, ← map_actVB ((PP l).PD K) p hp, ← sub_eq_zero, ← map_sub, ← hx',
        AlgebraTensorModule.map_tmul, hex.apply_apply_eq_zero, zero_tmul]
    · rw [phC_eq K, ← map_φVB, hφ]
  obtain ⟨x, hx⟩ := ((rTensor_exact (Bcr l) hex hs) x').1 h0
  replace hx : AlgebraTensorModule.map f (LinearMap.id : Bcr l →ₗ[ℚ_[l]] Bcr l) x = x' := hx
  have hinj : Function.Injective (AlgebraTensorModule.map f (LinearMap.id : Bcr l →ₗ[ℚ_[l]] Bcr l)) :=
    Module.Flat.rTensor_preserves_injective_linearMap (M := Bcr l) f hi
  refine ⟨x, fun g => hinj ?_⟩
  rw [map_sub, map_actVB ((PP l).PD K) f hf, hx, ← hx', AlgebraTensorModule.map_tmul, LinearMap.id_apply]

end Core

section Transport

variable {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {G : Type} [Group G] {K : FinExt l} (e : G ≃* ↥(GK K))
  {E V : Type} [Field E] [Algebra ℚ_[l] E] [AddCommGroup V] [Module E V] [Module ℚ_[l] V]
  [IsScalarTower ℚ_[l] E V] (ρ : Representation E G V)

def tr : Representation ℚ_[l] ↥(GK K) V := resCoef ℚ_[l] (ρ.comp e.symm.toMonoidHom : Representation E _ V)

theorem tr_apply (g : ↥(GK K)) (v : V) : tr e ρ g v = ρ (e.symm g) v := rfl

theorem actC_tr (g : G) (x : V ⊗[ℚ_[l]] Bcr l) :
    actC ((GK K).subtype.comp e.toMonoidHom) ρ g x = actVB ((PP l).PD K) (tr e ρ) (e g) x := by
  rw [← actC_eq]
  induction x using TensorProduct.induction_on with
  | zero => simp
  | add x x' hx hx' => simp only [map_add, hx, hx']
  | tmul v b => simp [actC, tr_apply]

theorem noFix_tr (h : NoFix ((GK K).subtype.comp e.toMonoidHom) ρ) : NoFix (GK K).subtype (tr e ρ) :=
  fun x hx hφ => h x (fun g => by rw [actC_tr, ← actC_eq]; exact hx _) hφ

theorem isDR_tr (h : IsDr ((GK K).subtype.comp e.toMonoidHom) ρ) : IsDR ((PP l).PD K) (tr e ρ) := by
  refine (isCrIff K (ρ.comp e.symm.toMonoidHom : Representation E _ V)).2.1 ?_
  have h1 : ((GK K).subtype.comp e.toMonoidHom).range = (GK K).subtype.range := by
    rw [MonoidHom.range_comp, MonoidHom.range_eq_top.2 e.surjective, ← MonoidHom.range_eq_map]
  have h2 : Ddr ((GK K).subtype.comp e.toMonoidHom) (resCoef ℚ_[l] ρ) =
      Ddr (GK K).subtype (resCoef ℚ_[l] (ρ.comp e.symm.toMonoidHom : Representation E _ V)) := by
    unfold Ddr
    refine e.surjective.iInf_congr e fun g => ?_
    simp [actD, resCoef]
  unfold IsDr at h ⊢
  rw [← h1, ← h2, h]

end Transport

end UpperTheory.V.Loc.PosPhi

open UpperTheory.V.Loc.PosPhi in
theorem UpperTheory.V.Loc.posExact :
    LocalPublishedTheorems → ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {G : Type} [Group G] [TopologicalSpace G] [IsTopologicalGroup G] (φ : G →* GQ l) (hφ : IsOpenEmbedding φ) {E : Type} [Field E] [Algebra ℚ_[l] E] [FiniteDimensional ℚ_[l] E] [Algebra ℤ_[l] E] [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] E] {V : Type} [AddCommGroup V] [Module E V] [Module ℚ_[l] V] [IsScalarTower ℚ_[l] E V] [FiniteDimensional ℚ_[l] V] [TopologicalSpace V] [IsModuleTopology ℚ_[l] V] [IsTopologicalAddGroup V] [ContinuousConstSMul E V] {V' : Type} [AddCommGroup V'] [Module E V'] [Module ℚ_[l] V'] [IsScalarTower ℚ_[l] E V'] [FiniteDimensional ℚ_[l] V'] [TopologicalSpace V'] [IsModuleTopology ℚ_[l] V'] [IsTopologicalAddGroup V'] [ContinuousConstSMul E V'] (ρ : Representation E G V) (ρ' : Representation E G V'), ContAction ρ → ContAction ρ' → Positive φ ρ → ∀ f : V →ₗ[E] V', (∀ g x, f (ρ g x) = ρ' g (f x)) → (Function.Surjective f → ∀ c' ∈ UpperTheory.V.Loc.finiteCocycles φ ρ', ∃ c ∈ UpperTheory.V.Loc.finiteCocycles φ ρ, (fun g => f (c g)) = c') ∧ (Function.Injective f → Positive φ ρ' → ∀ c ∈ Z1 ρ, (fun g => f (c g)) ∈ UpperTheory.V.Loc.finiteCocycles φ ρ' → c ∈ UpperTheory.V.Loc.finiteCocycles φ ρ) := by
  intro _ l _ G _ _ _ φ hφ E _ _ _ _ _ V _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ V' _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ ρ ρ' hc hc' hpos f hf
  obtain ⟨K, e, he, he', rfl, -⟩ := openField φ hφ
  haveI : ContinuousSMul ℚ_[l] V := IsModuleTopology.toContinuousSMul ℚ_[l] V
  haveI : ContinuousSMul ℚ_[l] V' := IsModuleTopology.toContinuousSMul ℚ_[l] V'
  have hk : ∀ x, Continuous fun g => tr e ρ g x := fun x => hc.comp (he'.prodMk continuous_const)
  have hk' : ∀ x, Continuous fun g => tr e ρ' g x := fun x => hc'.comp (he'.prodMk continuous_const)
  constructor
  · rintro hs c' ⟨hc'1, x', hx'⟩
    obtain ⟨c, ⟨hc1, x, hx⟩, v, hv⟩ := core_surj K (tr e ρ) (tr e ρ') hk hk' (isDR_tr e ρ hpos.1)
      (noFix_tr e ρ' (noFix_quot _ hφ ρ ρ' hc hpos.2 f hf hs)) (f.restrictScalars ℚ_[l]) (fun g x => hf _ x) hs
      (fun g => c' (e.symm g)) x' (fun g => by rw [hx', actC_tr, e.apply_symm_apply])
    obtain ⟨v, rfl⟩ := hs v
    have h1 : (fun g => c (e g)) ∈ UpperTheory.V.Loc.finiteCocycles ((GK K).subtype.comp e.toMonoidHom) ρ := by
      refine ⟨⟨hc1.1.comp he, fun g h => ?_⟩, x, fun g => ?_⟩
      · simp only [map_mul]
        rw [hc1.2, tr_apply, e.symm_apply_apply]
      · rw [actC_tr]; exact hx _
    have h2 := (z1fMap ((GK K).subtype.comp e.toMonoidHom) ρ ρ').1 hc (⟨v, rfl⟩ : d0 ρ v ∈ ReciprocityTheory.Gal.B1 ρ)
    refine ⟨_, Submodule.sub_mem _ h1 h2, funext fun g => ?_⟩
    have := hv (e g)
    rw [tr_apply, e.symm_apply_apply, LinearMap.restrictScalars_apply] at this
    rw [Pi.sub_apply, amitsur_d0_apply, map_sub, map_sub, hf, ← this, sub_sub_cancel]
  · rintro hi hpos' c hc1 ⟨-, x', hx'⟩
    have hst : ∀ g, LinearMap.range f ≤ (LinearMap.range f).comap (ρ' g) := by
      rintro g _ ⟨v, rfl⟩
      exact ⟨ρ g v, hf g v⟩
    let σ := ρ'.quotient (LinearMap.range f) hst
    have hq : ∀ g x, (LinearMap.range f).mkQ (ρ' g x) = σ g ((LinearMap.range f).mkQ x) := fun _ _ => rfl
    have hqs : Function.Surjective (LinearMap.range f).mkQ := Submodule.mkQ_surjective _
    have hex : Function.Exact f (LinearMap.range f).mkQ := LinearMap.exact_map_mkQ_range f
    obtain ⟨x, hx⟩ := core_inj K (tr e ρ) (tr e ρ') (tr e σ) hk'
      (noFix_tr e ρ' (noFix_quot _ hφ ρ' ρ' hc' hpos'.2 LinearMap.id (fun _ _ => rfl) Function.surjective_id))
      (noFix_tr e σ (noFix_quot _ hφ ρ' σ hc' hpos'.2 (LinearMap.range f).mkQ hq hqs))
      (f.restrictScalars ℚ_[l]) (fun g x => hf _ x) hi ((LinearMap.range f).mkQ.restrictScalars ℚ_[l])
      (fun g x => hq _ x) hqs hex
      (fun g => c (e.symm g)) x' (fun g => by
        have := hx' (e.symm g)
        rw [actC_tr, e.apply_symm_apply] at this
        exact this)
    refine ⟨hc1, x, fun g => ?_⟩
    rw [actC_tr, ← hx, e.symm_apply_apply]

open Formalism.BasicNotions Formalism.BasicNotions.Loc ReciprocityTheory.Gal Module Topology UpperTheory.V.Loc UpperTheory.V.U3

private theorem multiplicity_one_positive {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {E : Type} [Field E] [Algebra ℚ_[l] E] [FiniteDimensional ℚ_[l] E]
    [Algebra ℤ_[l] E] [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] E] {η : GQ l →* ℤˣ} {Ψ : GQ l →* Eˣ} {H : Subgroup (GQ l)} {e : ℕ}
    (hD : TriDat η Ψ H e) {B : Type} [CommRing B] [Algebra ℚ_[l] B] [Module.Finite ℚ_[l] B] [TopologicalSpace B]
    [IsModuleTopology ℚ_[l] B] [IsTopologicalRing B] [Algebra E B] [IsScalarTower ℚ_[l] E B] (π : B →ₐ[E] E)
    (hπ : ∀ a, π a = 0 → IsNilpotent a) (T : Tor η B) (hT : T.Good π Ψ) :
    Positive (MonoidHom.id (GQ l)) (resCoef E T.M1) := by
  haveI : ContinuousConstSMul E B := contConstSMul_alg E
  haveI : Module.Finite E B := Module.Finite.of_restrictScalars_finite ℚ_[l] E B
  have hc : ContAction (resCoef E T.M1) := T.contAction_torus_module hD.ηlc hT.cont hT.cont_inv
  have w0 : wt1 (0 : Fin 3) = 1 := by decide
  have w1 : wt1 (1 : Fin 3) = 0 := by decide
  have w2 : wt1 (2 : Fin 3) = -1 := by decide
  have hid : IsOpenEmbedding (MonoidHom.id (GQ l)) := IsOpenEmbedding.id
  refine ⟨?_, ?_⟩
  · have hφ : IsOpenEmbedding ((MonoidHom.id (GQ l)).comp H.subtype) := hD.isOpen.isOpenEmbedding_subtypeVal
    have hcH : ContAction ((resCoef E T.M1).comp H.subtype : Representation E H (B × B)) :=
      Continuous.comp (g := fun p : GQ l × B × B => resCoef E T.M1 p.1 p.2)
        (f := fun p : H × B × B => ((p.1 : GQ l), p.2)) hc (by fun_prop)
    rw [((isCrys_isDR_restrict_openSubgroup _ hid _ hc).2 H hD.isOpen).2]
    refine (isCrys_isDR_restrict_openSubgroup _ hφ _ hcH).1 (crUnr _ hφ _ hcH e fun g hg x => ?_)
    have hg' : (g : GQ l) ∈ Iq l := hg
    have hu : ∀ i, T.t g i = Units.map (algebraMap E B : E →* B) (Ψ g ^ wt1 i) := fun i =>
      Units.ext (hT.ine g hg' i)
    change T.M1 g x = ((cycA l l E g ^ (e : ℤ) : Eˣ) : E) • x
    rw [zpow_natCast, ← hD.ΨH g g.2 hg', Tor.adjointSuperdiagRep_apply, if_pos (hD.ηH g g.2)]
    simp only [hu, w0, w1, w2, ← map_div]
    ext <;> simp [Algebra.smul_def]
  · let ε : GQ l →* Eˣ := (Units.map (Int.castRingHom E : ℤ →* E)).comp η
    have h2 : (2 : B) * algebraMap ℚ_[l] B (1 / 2) = 1 := by
      rw [← map_ofNat (algebraMap ℚ_[l] B) 2, ← map_mul]
      norm_num
    refine UpperTheory.filtBy_restrictScalars_of_residual_characters π hπ T.M1 _ ![((1 : B), (1 : B)), (1, -1)] ![ε * Ψ, Ψ] ?_ ?_ ?_
    · rw [eq_top_iff]
      rintro ⟨a, b⟩ -
      have : (a, b) = (algebraMap ℚ_[l] B (1 / 2) * (a + b)) • ((1 : B), (1 : B)) +
          (algebraMap ℚ_[l] B (1 / 2) * (a - b)) • ((1 : B), (-1 : B)) := by
        ext
        · simp only [Prod.fst_add, Prod.smul_fst, smul_eq_mul]
          linear_combination (-a) * h2
        · simp only [Prod.snd_add, Prod.smul_snd, smul_eq_mul]
          linear_combination (-b) * h2
      rw [this]
      exact add_mem (Submodule.smul_mem _ _ (Submodule.subset_span ⟨0, rfl⟩))
        (Submodule.smul_mem _ _ (Submodule.subset_span ⟨1, rfl⟩))
    · have he : (1 : ℤ) ≤ e := by exact_mod_cast hD.e_pos
      intro j
      refine ⟨e, he, H, hD.isOpen, fun g hg hI => ?_⟩
      have h1 := hD.ΨH g hg hI
      fin_cases j
      · simp [ε, hD.ηH g hg, h1]
      · simp [h1]
    · have key : ∀ v : B × B, π v.1 = 0 → π v.2 = 0 →
          v ∈ (RingHom.ker (π : B →+* E)) • (⊤ : Submodule B (B × B)) := by
        rintro ⟨a, b⟩ ha hb
        have : (a, b) = a • ((1 : B), (0 : B)) + b • ((0 : B), (1 : B)) := by simp
        rw [this]
        exact add_mem (Submodule.smul_mem_smul ha trivial) (Submodule.smul_mem_smul hb trivial)
      intro g j
      have hu : ∀ i, Units.map (π : B →* E) (T.t g i) = Ψ g ^ wt1 i := fun i => Units.ext (hT.red g i)
      have ha : π ((T.t g 0 / T.t g 1 : Bˣ) : B) = Ψ g := by
        have : Units.map (π : B →* E) (T.t g 0 / T.t g 1) = Ψ g := by simp [map_div, hu, w0, w1]
        exact congrArg Units.val this
      have hb : π ((T.t g 1 / T.t g 2 : Bˣ) : B) = Ψ g := by
        have : Units.map (π : B →* E) (T.t g 1 / T.t g 2) = Ψ g := by simp [map_div, hu, w1, w2]
        exact congrArg Units.val this
      have hn : ((-1 : ℤˣ) = 1) = False := by decide
      rw [Tor.adjointSuperdiagRep_apply]
      generalize ((T.t g 0 / T.t g 1 : Bˣ) : B) = a at ha ⊢
      generalize ((T.t g 1 / T.t g 2 : Bˣ) : B) = b at hb ⊢
      apply key <;> rcases Int.units_eq_one_or (η g) with hg | hg <;> fin_cases j <;>
        simp [ε, hg, hn, ha, hb, Algebra.smul_def]

theorem UpperTheory.torsionDatum_finiteCocycles_baseChange :
    LocalPublishedTheorems → ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {E : Type} [Field E] [Algebra ℚ_[l] E] [FiniteDimensional ℚ_[l] E] [Algebra ℤ_[l] E] [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] E] {η : GQ l →* ℤˣ} {Ψ : GQ l →* Eˣ} {H : Subgroup (GQ l)} {e : ℕ} (hD : TriDat η Ψ H e) {B : Type} [CommRing B] [Algebra ℚ_[l] B] [Module.Finite ℚ_[l] B] [TopologicalSpace B] [IsModuleTopology ℚ_[l] B] [IsTopologicalRing B] [Algebra ℤ_[l] B] [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] B] [Algebra E B] [IsScalarTower ℚ_[l] E B] (π : B →ₐ[E] E) (hπ : ∀ a, π a = 0 → IsNilpotent a) {B' : Type} [CommRing B'] [Algebra ℚ_[l] B'] [Module.Finite ℚ_[l] B'] [TopologicalSpace B'] [IsModuleTopology ℚ_[l] B'] [IsTopologicalRing B'] [Algebra ℤ_[l] B'] [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] B'] [Algebra E B'] [IsScalarTower ℚ_[l] E B'] (π' : B' →ₐ[E] E) (hπ' : ∀ a, π' a = 0 → IsNilpotent a) (p : B →ₐ[E] B'), Function.Surjective p → ∀ (T : Tor η B) (T' : Tor η B'), T.Good π Ψ → T'.Good π' Ψ → (∀ g i, p (T.t g i : B) = (T'.t g i : B')) → ∀ β' ∈ UpperTheory.V.Loc.finiteCocycles (MonoidHom.id (GQ l)) T'.M1, ∃ β ∈ UpperTheory.V.Loc.finiteCocycles (MonoidHom.id (GQ l)) T.M1, ∀ g, p (β g).1 = (β' g).1 ∧ p (β g).2 = (β' g).2 := by
  intro hP l _ E _ _ _ _ _ η Ψ H e hD B _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ π hπ B' _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ π' _ p hp T T' hT hT' ht β' hβ'
  haveI : ContinuousConstSMul E B := contConstSMul_alg E
  haveI : ContinuousConstSMul E B' := contConstSMul_alg E
  have hc : ContAction (resCoef E T.M1) := T.contAction_torus_module hD.ηlc hT.cont hT.cont_inv
  have hc' : ContAction (resCoef E T'.M1) := T'.contAction_torus_module hD.ηlc hT'.cont hT'.cont_inv
  have hu : ∀ g i j, p ((T.t g i / T.t g j : Bˣ) : B) = ((T'.t g i / T'.t g j : B'ˣ) : B') := fun g i j => by
    have h : ∀ i, Units.map (p : B →* B') (T.t g i) = T'.t g i := fun i => Units.ext (ht g i)
    rw [← h, ← h, ← map_div]
    rfl
  obtain ⟨β, hβ, hpβ⟩ := (posExact hP (MonoidHom.id (GQ l)) IsOpenEmbedding.id (resCoef E T.M1) (resCoef E T'.M1)
    hc hc' (multiplicity_one_positive hD π hπ T hT) (p.toLinearMap.prodMap p.toLinearMap) (fun g x => by
      change Prod.map p p (T.M1 g x) = T'.M1 g (Prod.map p p x)
      simp only [Tor.adjointSuperdiagRep_apply]
      split_ifs <;> simp only [Prod.map, map_mul, map_neg, hu])).1 (hp.prodMap hp) β'
    ((z1fMap (MonoidHom.id (GQ l)) T'.M1 (resCoef E T'.M1)).2 LinearMap.id (fun _ _ => rfl) β' hβ')
  refine ⟨β, (z1fMap (MonoidHom.id (GQ l)) (resCoef E T.M1) T.M1).2 LinearMap.id (fun _ _ => rfl) β hβ, fun g => ?_⟩
  rw [← hpβ]
  exact ⟨rfl, rfl⟩


[OCH_SRC_POSITIVE_COMPARE] Exact finite-cocycle comparison on filtered positive modules
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/PadicHodgeTheory/TorsionDatumFiniteCocyclesCompare.lean#L1-L63
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.PadicHodgeTheory.TriDefs
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.PadicHodgeTheory.PosExact
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.PadicHodgeTheory.Z1fMap
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.PadicHodgeTheory.IsCrysOfInertia
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.PadicHodgeTheory.AdmRes
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.PadicHodgeTheory.FiltByRestrictScalarsOfResidualCharacters
open Formalism.BasicNotions Formalism.BasicNotions.Loc ReciprocityTheory.Gal Module Topology UpperTheory.V.Loc UpperTheory.V.U3

theorem UpperTheory.torsionDatum_finiteCocycles_compare :
    LocalPublishedTheorems → ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {E : Type} [Field E] [Algebra ℚ_[l] E] [FiniteDimensional ℚ_[l] E] [Algebra ℤ_[l] E] [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] E] {η : GQ l →* ℤˣ} {Ψ : GQ l →* Eˣ} {H : Subgroup (GQ l)} {e : ℕ} (hD : TriDat η Ψ H e) {B : Type} [CommRing B] [Algebra ℚ_[l] B] [Module.Finite ℚ_[l] B] [TopologicalSpace B] [IsModuleTopology ℚ_[l] B] [IsTopologicalRing B] [Algebra ℤ_[l] B] [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] B] [Algebra E B] [IsScalarTower ℚ_[l] E B] (π : B →ₐ[E] E) (hπ : ∀ a, π a = 0 → IsNilpotent a) {B' : Type} [CommRing B'] [Algebra ℚ_[l] B'] [Module.Finite ℚ_[l] B'] [TopologicalSpace B'] [IsModuleTopology ℚ_[l] B'] [IsTopologicalRing B'] [Algebra ℤ_[l] B'] [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] B'] [Algebra E B'] [IsScalarTower ℚ_[l] E B'] (π' : B' →ₐ[E] E) (hπ' : ∀ a, π' a = 0 → IsNilpotent a) (p : B →ₐ[E] B'), Function.Surjective p → ∀ (T : Tor η B) (T' : Tor η B'), T.Good π Ψ → T'.Good π' Ψ → (∀ g i, p (T.t g i : B) = (T'.t g i : B')) → ∀ δ' ∈ UpperTheory.V.Loc.finiteCocycles (MonoidHom.id (GQ l)) (chRep T'.χ2), ∃ δ ∈ UpperTheory.V.Loc.finiteCocycles (MonoidHom.id (GQ l)) (chRep T.χ2), ∀ g, p (δ g) = δ' g := by
  intro hP l _ E _ _ _ _ _ η Ψ H e hD B _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ π hπ B' _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ π' hπ' p hp T T' hT hT'
    hTT δ' hδ'
  haveI : ContinuousConstSMul E B := contConstSMul_alg E
  haveI : ContinuousConstSMul E B' := contConstSMul_alg E
  haveI : Module.Finite E B := Module.Finite.of_restrictScalars_finite ℚ_[l] E B
  have hc : ContAction (chRep T.χ2) := contAction_chRep (T.continuous_χ2 hD.ηlc hT.cont hT.cont_inv)
  have hc' : ContAction (chRep T'.χ2) := contAction_chRep (T'.continuous_χ2 hD.ηlc hT'.cont hT'.cont_inv)
  have hcE : ContAction (resCoef E (chRep T.χ2)) := hc
  have hcE' : ContAction (resCoef E (chRep T'.χ2)) := hc'
  have hid : IsOpenEmbedding (MonoidHom.id (GQ l)) := IsOpenEmbedding.id
  have hI : ∀ g ∈ H, g ∈ Iq l →
      T.χ2 g = Units.map (algebraMap E B : E →* B) (cycA l l E g ^ ((2 * e : ℕ) : ℤ)) := by
    intro g hg hgI
    have h := (hT.chars hD).ine ⟨g, hg⟩ hgI
    simp only [Tor.ch_apply, Subgroup.coe_subtype] at h
    change Units.map _ (η g) * (T.t g 0 / T.t g 2) = _
    rw [hD.ηH g hg, map_one, one_mul, h 0, h 2, map_zpow, algebraMap_cycA, div_eq_mul_inv, ← zpow_sub]
    congr 1
    simp [wt1]
    ring
  have hpos : Positive (MonoidHom.id (GQ l)) (resCoef E (chRep T.χ2)) := by
    refine ⟨?_, ?_⟩
    · refine (((isCrys_isDR_restrict_openSubgroup _ hid _ hcE).2 H hD.isOpen).2).2 ?_
      have hH : IsOpenEmbedding ((MonoidHom.id (GQ l)).comp H.subtype) := hD.isOpen.isOpenEmbedding_subtypeVal
      have hcH : ContAction ((resCoef E (chRep T.χ2)).comp H.subtype : Representation E H B) :=
        (((T.continuous_χ2 hD.ηlc hT.cont hT.cont_inv).comp continuous_subtype_val).comp continuous_fst).mul
          continuous_snd
      refine (isCrys_isDR_restrict_openSubgroup _ hH _ hcH).1 (crUnr _ hH _ hcH ((2 * e : ℕ) : ℤ) fun g hg x => ?_)
      change (T.χ2 g : B) * x = _
      rw [hI g g.2 hg, Algebra.smul_def]
      rfl
    · refine UpperTheory.filtBy_restrictScalars_of_residual_characters π hπ _ _ (fun _ : Unit => (1 : B))
        (fun _ => (Units.map (π : B →* E)).comp T.χ2) ?_ (fun _ => ?_) (fun g _ => ?_)
      · simp [Ideal.span_singleton_one]
      · refine ⟨((2 * e : ℕ) : ℤ), by have := hD.e_pos; omega, H, hD.isOpen, fun g hg hgI => ?_⟩
        apply Units.ext
        change π (T.χ2 g : B) = _
        rw [hI g hg hgI]
        exact π.commutes _
      · rw [Ideal.smul_eq_mul, Ideal.mul_top, RingHom.mem_ker]
        change π ((T.χ2 g : B) * 1 - π (T.χ2 g : B) • (1 : B)) = 0
        simp
  have hχ : ∀ g, p (T.χ2 g : B) = (T'.χ2 g : B') := by
    intro g
    have h : ∀ i, Units.map (p : B →* B') (T.t g i) = T'.t g i := fun i => Units.ext (hTT g i)
    rw [Tor.χ2_apply, Tor.χ2_apply, map_mul, map_intCast, ← h 0, ← h 2, ← map_div]
    rfl
  obtain ⟨δ, hδ, hδp⟩ := (posExact hP _ hid _ _ hcE hcE' hpos p.toLinearMap (fun g x => by
      change p ((T.χ2 g : B) * x) = (T'.χ2 g : B') * p x
      rw [map_mul, hχ])).1 hp δ'
    ((z1fMap (MonoidHom.id (GQ l)) (chRep T'.χ2) (resCoef E (chRep T'.χ2))).2 LinearMap.id (fun _ _ => rfl) δ' hδ')
  exact ⟨δ, (z1fMap (MonoidHom.id (GQ l)) (resCoef E (chRep T.χ2)) (chRep T.χ2)).2 LinearMap.id (fun _ _ => rfl) δ hδ,
    fun g => congrFun hδp g⟩


[OCH_SRC_ANNIHILATOR] Actual finite-cocycle plus cyclotomic-annihilator decomposition
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/ModularityArgument/CrystallineOrdinaryIdeals/CocycleCrysAddAnn.lean#L1-L84
import Mathlib
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.PadicHodgeTheory.TriDefs
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.ModularityArgument.CrystallineOrdinaryIdeals.FinrankDualInvariantsScalarRepEqFinrankKerSmulStatement
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.ModularityArgument.CrystallineOrdinaryIdeals.SpanCharacterSubCyclotomicEqOfPositiveWeightStatement
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.ModularityArgument.CrystallineOrdinaryIdeals.PositiveScalarRepOfCharacterPositiveWeightStatement
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.ModularityArgument.CrystallineOrdinaryIdeals.CocycleMemCrystallineSupSubrepOfPositiveStatement
open Formalism.BasicNotions Formalism.BasicNotions.Loc ReciprocityTheory.Gal Module UpperTheory.V.Loc UpperTheory.V.U3 UpperTheory.V.Alg IsLocalRing Polynomial Topology TensorProduct

noncomputable section
namespace GlobalTheory.CrysAnn

variable {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime]

theorem actC_resCoef {G E B : Type} [Group G] [Field E] [Algebra ℚ_[l] E] [CommRing B] [Algebra ℚ_[l] B]
    [Algebra E B] [IsScalarTower ℚ_[l] E B] (φ : G →* GQ l) (ρ : Representation B G B) (g : G)
    (y : B ⊗[ℚ_[l]] Bcr l) : actC φ (resCoef E ρ) g y = actC φ ρ g y := by
  induction y using TensorProduct.induction_on with
  | zero => simp
  | tmul a b => simp [actC, resCoef]
  | add x y hx hy => rw [map_add, map_add, hx, hy]

def smulRep {G E B : Type} [Group G] [Field E] [CommRing B] [Algebra E B] (N : Type) [AddCommGroup N]
    [Module B N] [Module E N] [IsScalarTower E B N] (χ : G →* Bˣ) : Representation E G N where
  toFun g := (LinearMap.lsmul B N (χ g : B)).restrictScalars E
  map_one' := by ext; simp
  map_mul' g h := by
    ext x
    simp [mul_smul]
    exact smul_comm _ _ _

end GlobalTheory.CrysAnn

open GlobalTheory.CrysAnn in

theorem GlobalTheory.CrysAnn.cocycle_crys_add_ann : LocalPublishedTheorems → ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {G : Type} [Group G] [TopologicalSpace G] [IsTopologicalGroup G] (φ : G →* GQ l) (hφ : IsOpenEmbedding φ) {E : Type} [Field E] [Algebra ℚ_[l] E] [FiniteDimensional ℚ_[l] E] [Algebra ℤ_[l] E] [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] E] {B : Type} [CommRing B] [Algebra ℚ_[l] B] [Module.Finite ℚ_[l] B] [TopologicalSpace B] [IsModuleTopology ℚ_[l] B] [IsTopologicalRing B] [Algebra ℤ_[l] B] [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] B] [Algebra E B] [IsScalarTower ℚ_[l] E B] (π : B →ₐ[E] E) (hπ : ∀ a, π a = 0 → IsNilpotent a) (χ : G →* Bˣ), Continuous (fun g => (χ g : B)) → ∀ w : ℤ, 1 ≤ w → (∀ g, φ g ∈ Iq l → χ g = cycA l l B (φ g) ^ w) → ∀ σ : G, ((Iq l).comap φ ⊔ Subgroup.zpowers σ).topologicalClosure = ⊤ → ∀ c ∈ Z1 (chRep χ), ∃ c' : G → B, (∀ g, ((χ σ : B) - (cycA l l B (φ σ) : B)) * c' g = 0) ∧ c - c' ∈ finiteCocycles φ (chRep χ) ∧ (2 ≤ w → c' = 0) := by
  intro hP l _ G _ _ _ φ hφ E _ _ _ _ _ B _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ π hπ χ hχc w hw hχI σ hσ c hc
  haveI : ContinuousConstSMul E B := contConstSMul_alg E
  haveI : Module.Finite E B := Module.Finite.of_restrictScalars_finite ℚ_[l] E B
  set δ : B := if w = 1 then (χ σ : B) - (cycA l l B (φ σ) : B) else 1 with hδ
  have hspan : Ideal.span (Set.range fun g => (χ g : B) - algebraMap E B (((cycA l l E).comp φ g : Eˣ) : E)) =
      Ideal.span {δ} := by
    have h := GlobalTheory.T.span_character_sub_cyclotomic_eq_of_positive_weight φ hφ π hπ χ hχc w hw hχI σ hσ
    have e : ∀ g, algebraMap E B (((cycA l l E).comp φ g : Eˣ) : E) = (cycA l l B (φ g) : B) := fun g =>
      congrArg Units.val (algebraMap_cycA (E := E) (B := B) (φ g))
    simp only [e, h, hδ]
    split_ifs <;> simp
  let I : Submodule B B := LinearMap.ker (LinearMap.lsmul B B δ)
  letI : TopologicalSpace I := moduleTopology ℚ_[l] I
  haveI : IsModuleTopology ℚ_[l] I := ⟨rfl⟩
  haveI : IsTopologicalAddGroup I := IsModuleTopology.topologicalAddGroup ℚ_[l] I
  haveI : ContinuousConstSMul E I := ⟨fun a => IsModuleTopology.continuous_of_linearMap
    ((LinearMap.lsmul E I a).restrictScalars ℚ_[l])⟩
  haveI : FiniteDimensional ℚ_[l] I :=
    Module.Finite.of_injective (I.subtype.restrictScalars ℚ_[l]) Subtype.val_injective
  let ρV : Representation E G B := resCoef E (chRep χ)
  let ρW : Representation E G I := smulRep I χ
  have hV := GlobalTheory.T.positive_scalar_rep_of_character_positive_weight φ hφ π hπ χ hχc w hw hχI ρV (fun g x => rfl)
  have hW := GlobalTheory.T.positive_scalar_rep_of_character_positive_weight φ hφ π hπ χ hχc w hw hχI ρW (fun g x => rfl)
  have dV := GlobalTheory.T.finrank_dualInvariants_scalarRep_eq_finrank_ker_smul χ ((cycA l l E).comp φ) ρV (fun g x => rfl) δ hspan
  have dW := GlobalTheory.T.finrank_dualInvariants_scalarRep_eq_finrank_ker_smul χ ((cycA l l E).comp φ) ρW (fun g x => rfl) δ hspan
  have kW : LinearMap.ker ((LinearMap.lsmul B I δ).restrictScalars E) = ⊤ := by
    ext x
    simp only [LinearMap.mem_ker, Submodule.mem_top, iff_true]
    exact Subtype.ext x.2
  have hdim : finrank E ↥(H0dual ((cycA l l E).comp φ) ρW) = finrank E ↥(H0dual ((cycA l l E).comp φ) ρV) := by
    rw [dV, dW, kW, finrank_top]
    exact (LinearEquiv.refl E I).finrank_eq
  let i : I →ₗ[E] B := I.subtype.restrictScalars E
  obtain ⟨cW, -, hf⟩ := GlobalTheory.T.cocycle_mem_crystalline_sup_subrep_of_positive hP φ hφ ρW ρV hW.1 hV.1 hW.2 hV.2 i Subtype.val_injective
    (fun g x => rfl) hdim c hc
  have hz : w ≠ 1 → ∀ g, (cW g : B) = 0 := by
    intro h1 g
    have := (cW g).2
    simp only [I, LinearMap.mem_ker, LinearMap.lsmul_apply, hδ, if_neg h1, one_smul] at this
    exact this
  refine ⟨fun g => (cW g : B), fun g => ?_, ?_, fun h2 => funext fun g => hz (by omega) g⟩
  · by_cases h1 : w = 1
    · have := (cW g).2
      simp only [I, LinearMap.mem_ker, LinearMap.lsmul_apply, hδ, if_pos h1, smul_eq_mul] at this
      exact this
    · show _ * (cW g : B) = 0
      rw [hz h1 g, mul_zero]
  · obtain ⟨⟨hc1, hc2⟩, x, hx⟩ := hf
    exact ⟨⟨hc1, hc2⟩, x, fun g => (hx g).trans (by rw [actC_resCoef])⟩


[OCH_SRC_DIMENSION] The exact positive Z1/Zf1 dimension difference
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/ModularityArgument/CrystallineOrdinaryIdeals/FinrankCocyclesEqCrysAddDualInvOfPositive.lean#L1-L59
import Mathlib
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.PadicHodgeTheory.TriDefs
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.PadicHodgeTheory.TateRat
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.PadicHodgeTheory.PosBasic
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.PadicHodgeTheory.Z1fMap
open Formalism.BasicNotions Formalism.BasicNotions.Loc ReciprocityTheory.Gal Module UpperTheory.V.Loc UpperTheory.V.U3 UpperTheory.V.Alg IsLocalRing Polynomial Topology

namespace GlobalTheory.CocycleDim

theorem sandwich {E M : Type} [Field E] [AddCommGroup M] [Module E M] (B S Z : Submodule E M)
    (hBS : B ≤ S) (hSZ : S ≤ Z) [Module.Finite E B]
    (hQ : Module.Finite E ↥((S.comap Z.subtype).map (B.comap Z.subtype).mkQ)) :
    Module.Finite E S ∧
      finrank E S = finrank E ↥((S.comap Z.subtype).map (B.comap Z.subtype).mkQ) + finrank E B := by
  let f : S →ₗ[E] (Z ⧸ B.comap Z.subtype) := (B.comap Z.subtype).mkQ ∘ₗ Submodule.inclusion hSZ
  have hr : LinearMap.range f = (S.comap Z.subtype).map (B.comap Z.subtype).mkQ := by
    rw [LinearMap.range_comp, Submodule.range_inclusion]
  have hk : LinearMap.ker f = B.comap S.subtype := by
    ext x
    simp [f]
  have eB := Submodule.comapSubtypeEquivOfLe hBS
  have hkf : Module.Finite E ↥(LinearMap.ker f) := by
    rw [hk]; exact Module.Finite.equiv eB.symm
  have hrf : Module.Finite E ↥(LinearMap.range f) := by rw [hr]; exact hQ
  have hS : Module.Finite E S := by
    rw [Module.finite_def]
    refine Submodule.fg_of_fg_map_of_fg_inf_ker f ?_ ?_
    · rw [Submodule.map_top]; exact Module.Finite.iff_fg.1 hrf
    · rw [top_inf_eq]; exact Module.Finite.iff_fg.1 hkf
  refine ⟨hS, ?_⟩
  rw [← LinearMap.finrank_range_add_finrank_ker f, hr, hk, eB.finrank_eq]

theorem top_image {E M : Type} [Field E] [AddCommGroup M] [Module E M] (B Z : Submodule E M) :
    (Z.comap Z.subtype).map (B.comap Z.subtype).mkQ = ⊤ := by
  rw [Submodule.comap_subtype_self, Submodule.map_top, Submodule.range_mkQ]

end GlobalTheory.CocycleDim

open GlobalTheory.CocycleDim in
theorem GlobalTheory.cocycleDim_positive : LocalPublishedTheorems → ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {G : Type} [Group G] [TopologicalSpace G] [IsTopologicalGroup G] (φ : G →* GQ l) (hφ : IsOpenEmbedding φ) {E : Type} [Field E] [Algebra ℚ_[l] E] [FiniteDimensional ℚ_[l] E] [Algebra ℤ_[l] E] [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] E] {V : Type} [AddCommGroup V] [Module E V] [Module ℚ_[l] V] [IsScalarTower ℚ_[l] E V] [FiniteDimensional ℚ_[l] V] [TopologicalSpace V] [IsModuleTopology ℚ_[l] V] [IsTopologicalAddGroup V] [ContinuousConstSMul E V] (ρ : Representation E G V), ContAction ρ → Positive φ ρ → Module.Finite E ↥(Z1 ρ) ∧ finrank E ↥(Z1 ρ) = finrank E ↥(finiteCocycles φ ρ) + finrank E ↥(H0dual ((cycA l l E).comp φ) ρ) := by
  intro hP l _ G _ _ _ φ hφ E _ _ _ _ _ V _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ ρ hρ hpos
  haveI : FiniteDimensional E V := Module.Finite.of_restrictScalars_finite ℚ_[l] E V
  obtain ⟨hH0, -, hfinf, hdimf⟩ := posBasic hP φ hφ ρ hρ hpos
  obtain ⟨hf1, -, e1, e2⟩ := tateRat hP (v := l) (l := l) φ hφ ρ hρ
  rw [if_pos rfl, hH0, finrank_bot, e2] at e1
  have hBf : Module.Finite E ↥(B1 ρ) := by
    unfold B1; infer_instance
  have hBS := (z1fMap (l := l) φ ρ ρ).1 hρ
  have hSZ : finiteCocycles φ ρ ≤ Z1 ρ := fun c h => h.1
  obtain ⟨-, hSd⟩ := sandwich _ _ _ hBS hSZ hfinf
  replace hSd : finrank E ↥(finiteCocycles φ ρ) = finrank E ↥(finiteCohomology φ ρ) + finrank E ↥(B1 ρ) := hSd
  have hQ : Module.Finite E ↥(((Z1 ρ).comap (Z1 ρ).subtype).map ((B1 ρ).comap (Z1 ρ).subtype).mkQ) := by
    rw [top_image]; exact Module.Finite.equiv (LinearEquiv.ofTop ⊤ rfl).symm
  obtain ⟨hZ, hZd⟩ := sandwich _ _ _ (coboundaries_le_cocycles ρ hρ) le_rfl hQ
  rw [top_image, finrank_top] at hZd
  refine ⟨hZ, ?_⟩
  change finrank E ↥(Z1 ρ) = finrank E (H1 ρ) + finrank E ↥(B1 ρ) at hZd
  rw [hZd, hSd, e1, hdimf]
  ring


[OCH_SRC_ANNIHILATOR_COMPARE] Surjectivity of the annihilator subrepresentation in the nonfinite cocycle quotient
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/ModularityArgument/CrystallineOrdinaryIdeals/CocycleMemCrystallineSupSubrepOfPositive.lean#L1-L41

theorem GlobalTheory.cocycle_eq_crys_add_sub : LocalPublishedTheorems → ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {G : Type} [Group G] [TopologicalSpace G] [IsTopologicalGroup G] (φ : G →* GQ l) (hφ : IsOpenEmbedding φ) {E : Type} [Field E] [Algebra ℚ_[l] E] [FiniteDimensional ℚ_[l] E] [Algebra ℤ_[l] E] [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] E] {W : Type} [AddCommGroup W] [Module E W] [Module ℚ_[l] W] [IsScalarTower ℚ_[l] E W] [FiniteDimensional ℚ_[l] W] [TopologicalSpace W] [IsModuleTopology ℚ_[l] W] [IsTopologicalAddGroup W] [ContinuousConstSMul E W] {V : Type} [AddCommGroup V] [Module E V] [Module ℚ_[l] V] [IsScalarTower ℚ_[l] E V] [FiniteDimensional ℚ_[l] V] [TopologicalSpace V] [IsModuleTopology ℚ_[l] V] [IsTopologicalAddGroup V] [ContinuousConstSMul E V] (ρW : Representation E G W) (ρ : Representation E G V), ContAction ρW → ContAction ρ → Positive φ ρW → Positive φ ρ → ∀ i : W →ₗ[E] V, Function.Injective i → (∀ g x, i (ρW g x) = ρ g (i x)) → finrank E ↥(H0dual ((cycA l l E).comp φ) ρW) = finrank E ↥(H0dual ((cycA l l E).comp φ) ρ) → ∀ c ∈ Z1 ρ, ∃ c' ∈ Z1 ρW, (c - fun g => i (c' g)) ∈ finiteCocycles φ ρ := by
  intro hP l _ G _ _ _ φ hφ E _ _ _ _ _ W _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ V _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ ρW ρ hcW hc hpW hp i hi hiρ hdim c hcZ
  obtain ⟨hfinW, hdW⟩ := GlobalTheory.T.finrank_cocycles_eq_crys_add_dual_inv_of_positive hP φ hφ ρW hcW hpW
  obtain ⟨hfin, hd⟩ := GlobalTheory.T.finrank_cocycles_eq_crys_add_dual_inv_of_positive hP φ hφ ρ hc hp
  have hic : Continuous i := IsModuleTopology.continuous_of_linearMap (i.restrictScalars ℚ_[l])
  let S := (finiteCocycles φ ρ).comap (Z1 ρ).subtype
  let SW := (finiteCocycles φ ρW).comap (Z1 ρW).subtype
  let M : Z1 ρW →ₗ[E] Z1 ρ := LinearMap.codRestrict (Z1 ρ) ((LinearMap.compLeft i G).comp (Z1 ρW).subtype)
    (fun c' => ⟨hic.comp c'.2.1, fun g h => by
      simp only [LinearMap.comp_apply, Submodule.subtype_apply, LinearMap.compLeft_apply, Function.comp_apply,
        c'.2.2 g h, map_add, hiρ]⟩)
  have hM : ∀ c' g, ((M c' : Z1 ρ) : G → V) g = i ((c' : G → W) g) := fun _ _ => rfl
  let L : Z1 ρW →ₗ[E] Z1 ρ ⧸ S := S.mkQ ∘ₗ M
  have hker : LinearMap.ker L = SW := by
    ext c'
    simp only [L, LinearMap.mem_ker, LinearMap.comp_apply, Submodule.mkQ_apply, Submodule.Quotient.mk_eq_zero,
      S, SW, Submodule.mem_comap, Submodule.subtype_apply]
    constructor
    · intro h
      exact (posExact hP φ hφ ρW ρ hcW hc hpW i hiρ).2 hi hp _ c'.2 h
    · intro h
      exact (z1fMap φ ρW ρ).2 (i.restrictScalars ℚ_[l]) (fun g x => hiρ g x) _ h
  have eW : SW ≃ₗ[E] finiteCocycles φ ρW := Submodule.comapSubtypeEquivOfLe (fun x hx => hx.1)
  have eV : S ≃ₗ[E] finiteCocycles φ ρ := Submodule.comapSubtypeEquivOfLe (fun x hx => hx.1)
  have h1 := LinearMap.finrank_range_add_finrank_ker L
  have h2 := Submodule.finrank_quotient_add_finrank S
  rw [hker, eW.finrank_eq] at h1
  rw [eV.finrank_eq] at h2
  have hr : LinearMap.range L = ⊤ := Submodule.eq_top_of_finrank_eq (by omega)
  obtain ⟨c', hc'⟩ : S.mkQ ⟨c, hcZ⟩ ∈ LinearMap.range L := hr ▸ Submodule.mem_top
  refine ⟨c', c'.2, ?_⟩
  have := (Submodule.Quotient.eq S).1 hc'.symm
  simp only [S, Submodule.mem_comap, Submodule.subtype_apply, Submodule.coe_sub] at this
  exact this


[OCH_SRC_TRI_LIFT] Actual triangular lifts, their central obstruction and exact reduction adjustment
