open Topology

private def resH {G B : Type} [Group G] [CommRing B] (u : G →* GG 3 B) (H : Subgroup G)
    (h : ∀ g ∈ H, (u g).right = 1) : H →* GL (Fin 3) B where
  toFun g := (u g).left
  map_one' := by simp
  map_mul' g g' := by simp [h g g.2]

private theorem lcr_iff {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {B : Type} [CommRing B] [Algebra ℚ_[l] B] (H : Subgroup (GQ l))
    (u : GQ l →* GG 3 B) (h : ∀ g ∈ H, (u g).right = 1) : LCr H u ↔ IsCr H.subtype (colRep (resH u H h)) := by
  refine ⟨fun ⟨ρ, hρ, hc⟩ => ?_, fun hc => ⟨_, fun _ => rfl, hc⟩⟩
  have : resH u H h = ρ := MonoidHom.ext fun g => (hρ g).symm
  rwa [this]

private theorem chi_res {G B : Type} [Group G] [CommRing B] {η : G →* ℤˣ} (T : Tor η B) (H : Subgroup G)
    (hH : ∀ g ∈ H, η g = 1) :
    ((chRep T.χ2).comp H.subtype : Representation B H B) = chRep (T.ch H hH 0 / T.ch H hH 2) := by
  refine MonoidHom.ext fun g => LinearMap.ext fun v => ?_
  change (T.χ2 g : B) * v = ((T.t g 0 / T.t g 2 : Bˣ) : B) * v
  rw [Tor.χ2_apply, hH g g.2]
  simp

private theorem gam_sub {G B : Type} [Group G] [CommRing B] [TopologicalSpace G] [TopologicalSpace B]
    [IsTopologicalRing B] {η : G →* ℤˣ} {T : Tor η B} {h : B} {β : G → B × B} {γ γ' : G → B}
    (cγ : Continuous γ) (cγ' : Continuous γ') (g1 : T.IsGam h β γ) (g2 : T.IsGam h β γ') :
    γ' - γ ∈ Z1 (chRep T.χ2) := by
  refine ⟨cγ'.sub cγ, fun g g' => ?_⟩
  simp only [Pi.sub_apply, chRep_apply, g1 g g', g2 g g']
  ring

private theorem gam_add {G B : Type} [Group G] [CommRing B] [TopologicalSpace G] [TopologicalSpace B]
    [IsTopologicalRing B] {η : G →* ℤˣ} {T : Tor η B} {h : B} {β : G → B × B} {γ δ : G → B}
    (g1 : T.IsGam h β γ) (hδ : δ ∈ Z1 (chRep T.χ2)) : T.IsGam h β (γ + δ) := by
  intro g g'
  simp only [Pi.add_apply, chRep_apply, g1 g g', hδ.2 g g']
  ring

private theorem gamDiff : LocalPublishedTheorems → ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {E : Type} [Field E] [Algebra ℚ_[l] E] [FiniteDimensional ℚ_[l] E] [Algebra ℤ_[l] E] [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] E] {η : GQ l →* ℤˣ} {Ψ : GQ l →* Eˣ} {H : Subgroup (GQ l)} {e : ℕ} (hD : TriDat η Ψ H e) {B : Type} [CommRing B] [Algebra ℚ_[l] B] [Module.Finite ℚ_[l] B] [TopologicalSpace B] [IsModuleTopology ℚ_[l] B] [IsTopologicalRing B] [Algebra ℤ_[l] B] [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] B] [Algebra E B] [IsScalarTower ℚ_[l] E B] (π : B →ₐ[E] E) (hπ : ∀ a, π a = 0 → IsNilpotent a) (T : Tor η B), T.Good π Ψ → ∀ (h : B) (β : GQ l → B × B) (γ γ' : GQ l → B) (u u' : GQ l →* GG 3 B), Continuous β → Continuous γ → Continuous γ' → (∀ g, π (β g).1 = 0) → T.IsGam h β γ → T.IsGam h β γ' → T.Coord h β γ u → T.Coord h β γ' u' → LCr H u → (LCr H u' ↔ γ' - γ ∈ UpperTheory.V.Loc.finiteCocycles (MonoidHom.id (GQ l)) (chRep T.χ2)) := by
  intro hP l _ E _ _ _ _ _ η Ψ H e hD B _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ π hπ T hT h β γ γ' u u' cβ cγ cγ' hβ g1 g2 c1 c2 hu
  haveI := hD.normal
  haveI := hD.fin
  have hφ : IsOpenEmbedding H.subtype := hD.isOpen.isOpenEmbedding_subtypeVal
  have r1 : ∀ g ∈ H, (u g).right = 1 := fun g hg => (c1.right g).trans (hD.ηH g hg)
  have r2 : ∀ g ∈ H, (u' g).right = 1 := fun g hg => (c2.right g).trans (hD.ηH g hg)
  rw [lcr_iff H u r1] at hu
  have key := (UpperTheory.isCrystalline_triangular_iff_mem_finiteCocycles_of_charData hP H.subtype hφ π hπ (hT.chars hD) (fun g => (β g).1) (fun g => (β g).2)
    (fun g => γ g + h * (β g).1 * (β g).2) (resH u H r1) (by fun_prop) (by fun_prop) (by fun_prop)
    (fun g => c1.mat g) (fun g => hβ g) hu (fun g => γ' g + h * (β g).1 * (β g).2) (resH u' H r2) (by fun_prop)
    (fun g => c2.mat g)).2
  rw [lcr_iff H u' r2, key, z1fDesc (MonoidHom.id _) (chRep T.χ2) H _ (gam_sub cγ cγ' g1 g2), chi_res T H hD.ηH]
  have : ((fun g : H => γ' g + h * (β g).1 * (β g).2) - fun g : H => γ g + h * (β g).1 * (β g).2) =
      fun g : H => (γ' - γ) g := by
    funext g
    simp
  rw [this]
  rfl

private def nE {B : Type} [CommRing B] (c : B) : GL (Fin 3) B where
  val := !![1, 0, c; 0, 1, 0; 0, 0, 1]
  inv := !![1, 0, -c; 0, 1, 0; 0, 0, 1]
  val_inv := by
    ext i j
    fin_cases i <;> fin_cases j <;> simp [Matrix.mul_apply, Fin.sum_univ_three]
  inv_val := by
    ext i j
    fin_cases i <;> fin_cases j <;> simp [Matrix.mul_apply, Fin.sum_univ_three]

private theorem nE_mul {B : Type} [CommRing B] (c : B) (t : Fin 3 → Bˣ) (x y z : B) :
    (nE c : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) B) * triM t x y z = triM t x y (z + c) := by
  ext i j
  fin_cases i <;> fin_cases j <;> simp [nE, triM, Matrix.mul_apply, Fin.sum_univ_three]
  ring

private theorem theta_nE {B : Type} [CommRing B] (c : B) : theta (nE c) = nE (-c) := by
  apply Units.ext
  rw [theta_val]
  ext i j
  fin_cases i <;> fin_cases j <;> simp [nE, Jmat, Matrix.mul_apply, Fin.sum_univ_three, Fin.rev]

private theorem triM_nE {B : Type} [CommRing B] (c : B) (t : Fin 3 → Bˣ) (x y z : B) :
    triM t x y z * (nE c : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) B) =
      (nE (((t 0 / t 2 : Bˣ) : B) * c) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) B) * triM t x y z := by
  have e : ((t 0 / t 2 : Bˣ) : B) * t 2 = t 0 := by rw [← Units.val_mul, div_mul_cancel]
  rw [nE_mul]
  generalize ((t 0 / t 2 : Bˣ) : B) = q at e
  ext i j
  fin_cases i <;> fin_cases j <;> simp [nE, triM, Matrix.mul_apply, Fin.sum_univ_three]
  rw [← e]
  ring

private theorem conj_nE {G B : Type} [Group G] [CommRing B] {η : G →* ℤˣ} {T : Tor η B} {h : B} {β : G → B × B}
    {γ : G → B} {u : G →* GG 3 B} (hu : T.Coord h β γ u) (s : G) (c : B) :
    u s * GG.ofGL (nE c) = GG.ofGL (nE (T.χ2 s * c)) * u s := by
  refine SemidirectProduct.ext ?_ (by simp)
  have hm : ((u s).left : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) B) = _ := hu.mat s
  apply Units.ext
  rcases Int.units_eq_one_or (η s) with hs | hs <;>
    simp only [SemidirectProduct.mul_left, SemidirectProduct.left_inl, SemidirectProduct.right_inl, hu.right, hs,
      sgnAct_one, sgnAct_neg_one, theta_nE, Units.val_mul, hm, triM_nE, Tor.χ2_apply] <;> simp

private theorem gamCr : LocalPublishedTheorems → ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {E : Type} [Field E] [Algebra ℚ_[l] E] [FiniteDimensional ℚ_[l] E] [Algebra ℤ_[l] E] [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] E] {η : GQ l →* ℤˣ} {Ψ : GQ l →* Eˣ} {H : Subgroup (GQ l)} {e : ℕ} (hD : TriDat η Ψ H e) {B : Type} [CommRing B] [Algebra ℚ_[l] B] [Module.Finite ℚ_[l] B] [TopologicalSpace B] [IsModuleTopology ℚ_[l] B] [IsTopologicalRing B] [Algebra ℤ_[l] B] [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] B] [Algebra E B] [IsScalarTower ℚ_[l] E B] (π : B →ₐ[E] E) (hπ : ∀ a, π a = 0 → IsNilpotent a) (T : Tor η B), T.Good π Ψ → ∀ (h : B), 2 * h = 1 → ∀ β ∈ UpperTheory.V.Loc.finiteCocycles (MonoidHom.id (GQ l)) T.M1, (∀ g, π (β g).1 = 0) → ∃ (γ : GQ l → B) (u : GQ l →* GG 3 B), Continuous γ ∧ T.IsGam h β γ ∧ T.Coord h β γ u ∧ LCr H u := by
  intro hP l _ E _ _ _ _ _ η Ψ H e hD B _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ π hπ T hT h hh β hβ hβ0
  haveI := hD.normal
  haveI := hD.fin
  have hφ : IsOpenEmbedding H.subtype := hD.isOpen.isOpenEmbedding_subtypeVal
  have cβ : Continuous β := hβ.1.1
  obtain ⟨γ, cγ, gγ⟩ := UpperTheory.exists_continuous_isGam_of_torus_cocycle hP hD π hπ T hT h β hβ.1
  obtain ⟨u, cu⟩ := UpperTheory.exists_unipotent_from_torus_coords T h hh β γ hβ.1.2 gγ
  have r1 : ∀ g ∈ H, (u g).right = 1 := fun g hg => (cu.right g).trans (hD.ηH g hg)
  obtain ⟨hx, hy⟩ := (UpperTheory.mem_finiteCocycles_iff_restrict_subgroup_mem hD π hπ T hT β hβ.1).1 hβ
  have hch := hT.chars hD
  obtain ⟨z, ρ, cz, hρ, hcr⟩ := UpperTheory.exists_crystalline_triangular_lift_of_mem_finiteCocycles hP H.subtype hφ π hπ hch _ hx _ hy (fun g => hβ0 g)
  have cx : Continuous fun g : H => (β g).1 := by fun_prop
  have cy : Continuous fun g : H => (β g).2 := by fun_prop
  have diff := UpperTheory.isCrystalline_triangular_iff_mem_finiteCocycles_of_charData hP H.subtype hφ π hπ hch _ _ z ρ cx cy cz hρ (fun g => hβ0 g) hcr
  set z₀ : H → B := fun g => γ g + h * (β g).1 * (β g).2 with hz₀
  have cz₀ : Continuous z₀ := by fun_prop
  have hc : z - z₀ ∈ Z1 ((chRep T.χ2).comp H.subtype : Representation B H B) := by
    rw [chi_res T H hD.ηH, ← neg_sub]
    exact Submodule.neg_mem _ (diff z₀ (resH u H r1) cz₀ (fun g => cu.mat g)).1
  have cχ := T.continuous_χ2 hD.ηlc hT.cont hT.cont_inv
  have cρ : Continuous fun g => (ρ g : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) B) := by
    simp only [hρ]
    exact continuous_triM hch.cont cx cy cz
  have fac : ∀ g : H, GG.ofGL (ρ g) = GG.ofGL (nE ((z - z₀) g)) * u g := by
    intro g
    refine SemidirectProduct.ext (Units.ext ?_) (by simp [r1 g g.2])
    have hm : ((u g).left : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) B) = _ := cu.mat g
    simp only [SemidirectProduct.mul_left, SemidirectProduct.left_inl, SemidirectProduct.right_inl, sgnAct_one,
      Units.val_mul, hm, nE_mul, hρ, Pi.sub_apply, hz₀, add_sub_cancel]
    rfl
  obtain ⟨b, hb, hbN⟩ := UpperTheory.exists_cocycle_extension_of_conjugation_invariant_restriction (l := l) (chRep T.χ2) (contAction_chRep cχ) H hD.isOpen
    (UpperTheory.V.Loc.finiteCocycles H.subtype (chRep (T.ch H hD.ηH 0 / T.ch H hD.ηH 2))) (z - z₀) hc (by
      intro s c' hc'
      let τ : H →* H := (MulAut.conjNormal s⁻¹ : MulAut H).toMonoidHom
      have hτ : ∀ g : H, ((τ g : H) : GQ l) = s⁻¹ * g * s := fun g => by simp [τ]
      have cτ : Continuous τ := by
        refine continuous_induced_rng.2 ?_
        have : (Subtype.val ∘ τ) = fun g : H => s⁻¹ * (g : GQ l) * s := funext hτ
        rw [this]
        fun_prop
      have hw : ∀ g : H, u s * GG.ofGL (ρ (τ g)) * (u s)⁻¹ = GG.ofGL (nE (c' g)) * u g := by
        intro g
        rw [fac, hτ, hc' g (τ g) (hτ g), chRep_apply, ← mul_assoc, conj_nE cu]
        simp only [map_mul, map_inv, mul_assoc, mul_inv_cancel, mul_one, mul_inv_cancel_left]
      let ρ' : H →* GL (Fin 3) B :=
        { toFun := fun g => nE (c' g) * (u g).left
          map_one' := by
            have := congrArg SemidirectProduct.left (hw 1)
            simpa using this.symm
          map_mul' := fun g g' => by
            have e : GG.ofGL (nE (c' (g * g'))) * u (g * g' : H) =
                (GG.ofGL (nE (c' g)) * u g) * (GG.ofGL (nE (c' g')) * u g') := by
              rw [← hw, ← hw, ← hw]
              simp only [map_mul, mul_assoc, inv_mul_cancel_left]
            have := congrArg SemidirectProduct.left e
            simpa [r1 g g.2] using this }
      have hρ' : ∀ g : H, GG.ofGL (ρ' g) = GG.ofGL (nE (c' g)) * u g := fun g =>
        SemidirectProduct.ext (by simp [ρ']) (by simp [r1 g g.2])
      have cr' := UpperTheory.conjugate_restriction_open_normal H hD.isOpen ρ ρ' cρ s (u s) (fun g g' e => by
        have : g' = τ g := Subtype.ext (e.trans (hτ g).symm)
        rw [hρ', this, hw]) hcr
      have cc' : Continuous c' := by
        have : c' = fun g => (T.χ2 s : B) * (z - z₀) (τ g) := funext fun g => hc' g (τ g) (hτ g)
        rw [this]
        exact continuous_const.mul ((cz.sub cz₀).comp cτ)
      have := (diff (z₀ + c') ρ' (cz₀.add cc') (fun g => by
        have hm : ((u g).left : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) B) = _ := cu.mat g
        change ((nE (c' g) * (u g).left : GL (Fin 3) B) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) B) = _
        rw [Units.val_mul, hm, nE_mul]
        rfl)).2.1 cr'
      convert this using 1
      abel)
  obtain ⟨u', cu'⟩ := UpperTheory.exists_unipotent_from_torus_coords T h hh β (γ + b) hβ.1.2 (gam_add gγ hb)
  refine ⟨γ + b, u', cγ.add hb.1, gam_add gγ hb, cu', ?_⟩
  have r2 : ∀ g ∈ H, (u' g).right = 1 := fun g hg => (cu'.right g).trans (hD.ηH g hg)
  have cb : Continuous b := hb.1
  rw [lcr_iff H u' r2]
  refine (diff (fun g : H => (γ + b) g + h * (β g).1 * (β g).2) (resH u' H r2) (by fun_prop)
    (fun g => cu'.mat g)).2.2 ?_
  convert hbN using 1
  funext g
  simp only [Pi.sub_apply, Pi.add_apply, hz₀]
  ring

private theorem triM_map {B B' : Type} [CommRing B] [CommRing B'] (p : B →+* B') (t : Fin 3 → Bˣ) (t' : Fin 3 → B'ˣ)
    (ht : ∀ i, p (t i) = t' i) (x y z : B) : (triM t x y z).map p = triM t' (p x) (p y) (p z) := by
  ext i j
  fin_cases i <;> fin_cases j <;> simp [triM, ht]

private theorem gg_ext {B : Type} [CommRing B] {a b : GG 3 B} (h1 : a.mat = b.mat) (h2 : a.right = b.right) : a = b :=
  SemidirectProduct.ext (Units.ext h1) h2

private theorem lcr_map {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {B : Type} [CommRing B] [Algebra ℚ_[l] B] [Module.Finite ℚ_[l] B]
    [TopologicalSpace B] [IsModuleTopology ℚ_[l] B] [IsTopologicalRing B]
    {B' : Type} [CommRing B'] [Algebra ℚ_[l] B'] [Module.Finite ℚ_[l] B']
    [TopologicalSpace B'] [IsModuleTopology ℚ_[l] B'] [IsTopologicalRing B'] (p : B →ₐ[ℚ_[l]] B')
    (hp : Function.Surjective p) {H : Subgroup (GQ l)} (hH : IsOpen (H : Set (GQ l))) {u : GQ l →* GG 3 B}
    (cu : Continuous fun g => (u g).mat) (hu : LCr H u) : LCr H ((GG.map (p : B →+* B')).comp u) := by
  obtain ⟨ρ, hρ, hc⟩ := hu
  have cp : Continuous p := IsModuleTopology.continuous_of_linearMap p.toLinearMap
  have cρ : Continuous fun g => (ρ g : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) B) := by
    simp only [hρ]
    exact cu.comp continuous_subtype_val
  refine ⟨(Matrix.GeneralLinearGroup.map (p : B →+* B')).comp ρ, fun g => ?_, ?_⟩
  · rw [MonoidHom.comp_apply, hρ]
    rfl
  · refine ((isCrys_isDR_sub_quot H.subtype hH.isOpenEmbedding_subtypeVal _ _ (contAction_colRep cρ)
      (contAction_colRep (cρ.matrix_map cp)) (p.toLinearMap.compLeft (Fin 3)) (fun g x => ?_)).2 fun v => ?_).1 hc
    · funext i
      exact RingHom.map_mulVec (p : B →+* B') _ x i
    · exact ⟨fun i => (hp (v i)).choose, funext fun i => (hp (v i)).choose_spec⟩

private theorem triDat {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] [Algebra ℤ_[l] O] (D : Dat3 l O)
    (E : Type) [Field E] [Algebra ℚ_[l] E] [Algebra O E] [Algebra ℤ_[l] E] [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] O E]
    (hc : QCont l fun g => algebraMap O E (D.Ψ g)) :
    TriDat D.η ((Units.map (algebraMap O E : O →* E)).comp D.Ψ) D.HL D.e := by
  have hη : IsOpen (D.η.ker : Set (GQ l)) := by
    have : D.η.ker = GK D.Kv := Subgroup.ext fun g => D.η_ker g
    rw [this]
    haveI := D.Kv.2
    exact IntermediateField.fixingSubgroup_isOpen D.Kv.1
  have ho : IsOpen (D.HL : Set (GQ l)) := hη.inter D.τ_open
  refine ⟨inferInstanceAs (D.η.ker ⊓ D.τ.ker).Normal, ho, ?_, fun g hg => hg.1, D.η_unr, ?_, D.e_pos, fun g hg hi => ?_, hc⟩
  · haveI := Subgroup.quotient_finite_of_isOpen _ ho
    exact Subgroup.finiteIndex_of_finite_quotient
  · refine (IsLocallyConstant.iff_eventually_eq _).2 fun g => ?_
    have : IsOpen {y : GQ l | g⁻¹ * y ∈ D.η.ker} := hη.preimage (by fun_prop)
    filter_upwards [this.mem_nhds (by simp)] with y hy
    rw [MonoidHom.mem_ker, map_mul, map_inv, inv_mul_eq_one] at hy
    exact hy.symm
  · have ht : D.τ g = 1 := hg.2
    rw [MonoidHom.comp_apply, D.Ψ_I g hi, ht, mul_one, map_pow]
    congr 1
    exact Units.ext (IsScalarTower.algebraMap_apply ℤ_[l] O E _).symm

end UpperTheory.V.Loc.TriangularDeformation

open UpperTheory.V.Loc.TriangularDeformation in
theorem UpperTheory.V.Loc.triLift :
    LocalPublishedTheorems → ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [CharZero O] [Algebra ℤ_[l] O] [Module.Finite ℤ_[l] O] [IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal O) O] (D : Dat3 l O) (E : Type) [Field E] [Algebra ℚ_[l] E] [Module.Finite ℚ_[l] E] [Algebra O E] [Algebra ℤ_[l] E] [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] O E] [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] E] [IsFractionRing O E] (B : Type) [CommRing B] [Algebra ℚ_[l] B] [Module.Finite ℚ_[l] B] [Algebra O B] [Algebra ℤ_[l] B] [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] O B] [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] B] [Algebra E B] [IsScalarTower O E B] [IsScalarTower ℚ_[l] E B] (π : B →ₐ[E] E) (hπ : ∀ a, π a = 0 → IsNilpotent a) (B' : Type) [CommRing B'] [Algebra ℚ_[l] B'] [Module.Finite ℚ_[l] B'] [Algebra O B'] [Algebra ℤ_[l] B'] [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] O B'] [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] B'] [Algebra E B'] [IsScalarTower O E B'] [IsScalarTower ℚ_[l] E B'] (π' : B' →ₐ[E] E) (hπ' : ∀ a, π' a = 0 → IsNilpotent a) (p : B →ₐ[E] B'), Function.Surjective p → π'.comp p = π → ∀ u' : GQ l →* GG 3 B', D.InDtri (π' : B' →+* E) u' → ∃ u : GQ l →* GG 3 B, D.InDtri (π : B →+* E) u ∧ (GG.map (p : B →+* B')).comp u = u' := by
  intro hP l _ O _ _ _ _ _ _ _ D E _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ B _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ π hπ B' _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ π' hπ' p hp hpπ u' hu'
  letI : TopologicalSpace B := moduleTopology ℚ_[l] B
  haveI : IsModuleTopology ℚ_[l] B := ⟨rfl⟩
  haveI : IsTopologicalRing B := IsModuleTopology.isTopologicalRing ℚ_[l] B
  letI : TopologicalSpace B' := moduleTopology ℚ_[l] B'
  haveI : IsModuleTopology ℚ_[l] B' := ⟨rfl⟩
  haveI : IsTopologicalRing B' := IsModuleTopology.isTopologicalRing ℚ_[l] B'
  letI : TopologicalSpace E := moduleTopology ℚ_[l] E
  haveI : IsModuleTopology ℚ_[l] E := ⟨rfl⟩
  haveI : IsTopologicalRing E := IsModuleTopology.isTopologicalRing ℚ_[l] E
  have cp : Continuous p := IsModuleTopology.continuous_of_linearMap (p.restrictScalars ℚ_[l]).toLinearMap
  have cπ' : Continuous π' := IsModuleTopology.continuous_of_linearMap (π'.restrictScalars ℚ_[l]).toLinearMap
  have hππ : ∀ b, π' (p b) = π b := fun b => AlgHom.congr_fun hpπ b
  set Ψ : GQ l →* Eˣ := (Units.map (algebraMap O E : O →* E)).comp D.Ψ with hΨ
  have hpow : ∀ g i, algebraMap O E ((D.Ψ g ^ wt1 i : Oˣ) : O) = ((Ψ g ^ wt1 i : Eˣ) : E) := fun g i => by
    rw [hΨ, MonoidHom.comp_apply, ← map_zpow (Units.map (algebraMap O E : O →* E))]
    rfl
  have red' : ∀ g a b, π' ((u' g).mat a b) = if a = b then ((Ψ g ^ wt1 a : Eˣ) : E) else 0 := fun g a b => by
    have := congrFun (congrFun (congrArg GG.mat (DFunLike.congr_fun hu'.red g)) a) b
    refine this.trans ?_
    change algebraMap O E (Matrix.diagonal (fun i => ((D.Ψ g ^ wt1 i : Oˣ) : O)) a b) = _
    rw [Matrix.diagonal_apply]
    split_ifs
    · exact hpow g a
    · exact map_zero _
  have cu' : ∀ a b, Continuous fun g => (u' g).mat a b := hu'.cont
  have hD : TriDat D.η Ψ D.HL D.e := triDat D E (by
    have : (fun g => algebraMap O E (D.Ψ g)) = fun g => π' ((u' g).mat 0 0) := funext fun g => by
      rw [red', if_pos rfl, ← hpow]
      simp [wt1]
    rw [this]
    exact cπ'.comp (cu' 0 0))
  haveI := hD.normal
  set h : B := algebraMap E B 2⁻¹ with hh0
  haveI : CharZero E := charZero_of_injective_algebraMap (algebraMap ℚ_[l] E).injective
  have hh : 2 * h = 1 := by
    rw [hh0, ← map_ofNat (algebraMap E B) 2, ← map_mul, mul_inv_cancel₀ two_ne_zero, map_one]
  have hh' : 2 * p h = 1 := by
    have := congrArg p hh
    rwa [map_mul, map_ofNat, map_one] at this
  obtain ⟨T', β', γ', c', zβ', gγ'⟩ := UpperTheory.exists_torus_coords_of_unipotent (p h) hh' u' hu'.mult hu'.tri
  have d' : ∀ g i, (T'.t g i : B') = (u' g).mat i i := fun g i => by
    rw [c'.mat]
    fin_cases i <;> rfl
  have ct' : ∀ i, Continuous fun g => (T'.t g i : B') := fun i => by
    simp only [d']
    exact cu' i i
  have hT' : T'.Good π' Ψ :=
    { cont := ct'
      cont_inv := fun i => UpperTheory.continuous_units_inv_of_continuous_coe_finite_Qp_algebra (l := l) (fun g => T'.t g i) (ct' i)
      ine := fun g hg i => by
        rw [d', triDiag D E B' π' hπ' u' hu' g hg i, IsScalarTower.algebraMap_apply O E B', ← hpow]
        rfl
      red := fun g i => by rw [d', red', if_pos rfl] }
  have e1 : ∀ g, (β' g).1 = (u' g).mat 0 1 * ((T'.t g 1)⁻¹ : B'ˣ) := fun g => by
    rw [c'.mat]
    simp [triM]
  have e2 : ∀ g, (β' g).2 = (u' g).mat 1 2 * ((T'.t g 2)⁻¹ : B'ˣ) := fun g => by
    rw [c'.mat]
    simp [triM]
  have e3 : ∀ g, γ' g = (u' g).mat 0 2 * ((T'.t g 2)⁻¹ : B'ˣ) - p h * (β' g).1 * (β' g).2 := fun g => by
    rw [c'.mat]
    simp [triM]
  have c1 : Continuous fun g => (β' g).1 := by
    simp only [e1]
    exact (cu' 0 1).mul (hT'.cont_inv 1)
  have c2 : Continuous fun g => (β' g).2 := by
    simp only [e2]
    exact (cu' 1 2).mul (hT'.cont_inv 2)
  have cβ' : Continuous β' := c1.prodMk c2
  have cγ' : Continuous γ' := by
    rw [funext e3]
    exact ((cu' 0 2).mul (hT'.cont_inv 2)).sub ((continuous_const.mul c1).mul c2)
  have β0' : ∀ g, π' (β' g).1 = 0 := fun g => by
    rw [e1, map_mul, red', if_neg (by decide), zero_mul]
  have fβ' : β' ∈ UpperTheory.V.Loc.finiteCocycles (MonoidHom.id (GQ l)) T'.M1 := by
    obtain ⟨ρ, hρ, hcr⟩ := hu'.cr
    refine (UpperTheory.mem_finiteCocycles_iff_restrict_subgroup_mem hD π' hπ' T' hT' β' ⟨cβ', zβ'⟩).2 ?_
    refine UpperTheory.isCrystalline_triangular_iff_of_charData D.HL.subtype hD.isOpen.isOpenEmbedding_subtypeVal π' hπ' (hT'.chars hD) _ _
      (fun g => γ' g + p h * (β' g).1 * (β' g).2) ρ (by fun_prop) (by fun_prop) (by fun_prop) (fun g => ?_) hcr
    rw [hρ]
    exact c'.mat g
  obtain ⟨T, hT, hTp⟩ := UpperTheory.exists_torus_lift_to_nilpotent_thickening hD π hπ π' hπ' p hp hpπ T' hT'
  obtain ⟨β, fβ, hβp⟩ := UpperTheory.torsionDatum_finiteCocycles_baseChange hP hD π hπ π' hπ' p hp T T' hT hT' hTp β' fβ'
  have cβ : Continuous β := fβ.1.1
  have β0 : ∀ g, π (β g).1 = 0 := fun g => by rw [← hππ, (hβp g).1, β0']
  obtain ⟨γ, u, cγ, gγ, cu, hu⟩ := gamCr hP hD π hπ T hT h hh β fβ β0
  have push : ∀ (γ : GQ l → B) (u : GQ l →* GG 3 B), T.Coord h β γ u →
      T'.Coord (p h) β' (fun g => p (γ g)) ((GG.map (p : B →+* B')).comp u) := fun γ u c =>
    ⟨fun g => c.right g, fun g => by
      rw [MonoidHom.comp_apply, GG.map_mat, c.mat, triM_map (p : B →+* B') _ _ (hTp g)]
      simp only [RingHom.coe_coe, map_add, map_mul, (hβp g).1, (hβp g).2]⟩
  have hq : ∀ g i j, p ((T.t g i / T.t g j : Bˣ) : B) = ((T'.t g i / T'.t g j : B'ˣ) : B') := fun g i j => by
    have : ∀ i, Units.map (p : B →* B') (T.t g i) = T'.t g i := fun i => Units.ext (hTp g i)
    rw [← this, ← this, ← _root_.map_div]
    rfl
  have gp : T'.IsGam (p h) β' (fun g => p (γ g)) := fun g g' => by
    simp only [gγ g g', omg, Tor.adjointSuperdiagRep_apply, Tor.χ2_apply]
    split_ifs <;> simp only [map_add, map_mul, map_sub, map_neg, map_intCast, hq, (hβp _).1, (hβp _).2]
  have cmat : ∀ (γ : GQ l → B) (u : GQ l →* GG 3 B), Continuous γ → T.Coord h β γ u →
      Continuous fun g => (u g).mat := fun γ u cγ c => by
    simp only [c.mat]
    exact continuous_triM hT.cont (by fun_prop) (by fun_prop) (by fun_prop)
  have hδ' := (gamDiff hP hD π' hπ' T' hT' (p h) β' (fun g => p (γ g)) γ' _ u' cβ' (cp.comp cγ) cγ' β0' gp gγ'
    (push γ u cu) c' (lcr_map (p.restrictScalars ℚ_[l]) hp hD.isOpen (cmat γ u cγ cu) hu)).1 hu'.cr
  obtain ⟨δ, fδ, hδp⟩ := UpperTheory.torsionDatum_finiteCocycles_compare hP hD π hπ π' hπ' p hp T T' hT hT' hTp _ hδ'
  have g2 := gam_add gγ fδ.1
  obtain ⟨v, cv⟩ := UpperTheory.exists_unipotent_from_torus_coords T h hh β (γ + δ) fβ.1.2 g2
  have c2 : Continuous (γ + δ) := cγ.add fδ.1.1
  have hv : (GG.map (p : B →+* B')).comp v = u' := by
    refine MonoidHom.ext fun g => gg_ext ?_ ?_
    · rw [(push _ v cv).mat, c'.mat]
      simp [hδp]
    · rw [(push _ v cv).right, c'.right]
  refine ⟨v, ⟨⟨cv.right, ?_, fun a b => (cmat _ v c2 cv).matrix_elem a b, ?_⟩, fun g a b hab => ?_⟩, hv⟩
  · rw [← hu'.red, ← hv]
    refine MonoidHom.ext fun g => gg_ext ?_ rfl
    ext a b
    exact (hππ _).symm
  · exact (gamDiff hP hD π hπ T hT h β γ (γ + δ) u v cβ cγ c2 β0 gγ g2 cu cv hu).2 (by simpa using fδ)
  · rw [cv.mat]
    revert hab
    fin_cases a <;> fin_cases b <;> simp [triM, wt, wt1]


[OCH_SRC_TRI_CRYS] Last-column crystalline criterion for the actual triangular rank-three representation
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/ModularityArgument/CrystallineOrdinaryIdeals/TriCrys.lean#L1-L42
import Mathlib
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.PadicHodgeTheory.TriDefs
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.PadicHodgeTheory.IsCrystallineExtensionIffMemFiniteCocycles
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.PadicHodgeTheory.IsCrystallineUpperTriangularIffMemFiniteCocycles
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.PadicHodgeTheory.IsCrysOfInertia
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.ModularityArgument.CrystallineOrdinaryIdeals.DiagonalizeOfDistinctEigenvaluesStatement
open Formalism.BasicNotions Formalism.BasicNotions.Loc ReciprocityTheory.Gal Module UpperTheory.V.Loc UpperTheory.V.U3 UpperTheory.V.Alg IsLocalRing Polynomial Topology

namespace GlobalTheory

theorem tri_crys : LocalPublishedTheorems → ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {G : Type} [Group G] [TopologicalSpace G] [IsTopologicalGroup G] (φ : G →* GQ l) (hφ : IsOpenEmbedding φ) {E : Type} [Field E] [Algebra ℚ_[l] E] [FiniteDimensional ℚ_[l] E] [Algebra ℤ_[l] E] [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] E] {B : Type} [CommRing B] [Algebra ℚ_[l] B] [Module.Finite ℚ_[l] B] [TopologicalSpace B] [IsModuleTopology ℚ_[l] B] [IsTopologicalRing B] [Algebra ℤ_[l] B] [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] B] [Algebra E B] [IsScalarTower ℚ_[l] E B] (π : B →ₐ[E] E) (hπ : ∀ a, π a = 0 → IsNilpotent a) (e : ℕ), 1 ≤ e → ∀ (κ : Fin 3 → G →* Bˣ), (∀ i, Continuous fun g => (κ i g : B)) → (∀ g, φ g ∈ Iq l → ∀ i, κ i g = cycA l l B (φ g) ^ ((e : ℤ) * wt1 i)) → ∀ (x y z : G → B) (ρ : G →* GL (Fin 3) B), Continuous z → (∀ g, (ρ g : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) B) = triM (fun i => κ i g) (x g) (y g) (z g)) → x ∈ finiteCocycles φ (chRep (κ 0 / κ 1)) → y ∈ finiteCocycles φ (chRep (κ 1 / κ 2)) → IsCr φ (colRep ρ) := by
  intro hP l _ G _ _ _ φ hφ E _ _ _ _ _ B _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ π hπ e he κ hκ hI x y z ρ hz hρ hxf hyf
  have hxc : Continuous x := hxf.1.1
  have hyc : Continuous y := hyf.1.1
  have hx : ∀ g h, x (g * h) = x g + ((κ 0 / κ 1) g : Bˣ) * x h := fun g h => hxf.1.2 g h
  have crκ : ∀ i, IsCr φ (chRep (κ i)) := fun i =>
    crUnr φ hφ (chRep (κ i)) (contAction_chRep (hκ i)) ((e : ℤ) * wt1 i) fun g hg v => by
      rw [chRep_apply, hI g hg i, smul_eq_mul]
  have hext : IsCr φ (ext2 (κ 0) (κ 1) x hx) :=
    (UpperTheory.isCrystalline_extension_iff_mem_finiteCocycles φ hφ (κ 0) (κ 1) (hκ 0) (hκ 1) x hx hxc (crκ 0) (crκ 1)).2 hxf
  have hmat : ∀ g h (i j : Fin 3), (ρ (g * h) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) B) i j =
      ((ρ g : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) B) * (ρ h : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) B)) i j := fun g h i j => by
    rw [map_mul, Units.val_mul]
  have hZ : (fun g => (z g, y g)) ∈ Z1 (twist (κ 2)⁻¹ (ext2 (κ 0) (κ 1) x hx)) := by
    refine ⟨hz.prodMk hyc, fun g h => ?_⟩
    have h02 := hmat g h 0 2
    have h12 := hmat g h 1 2
    rw [hρ, hρ, hρ] at h02 h12
    simp [triM, Matrix.mul_apply, Fin.sum_univ_three, map_mul, Units.val_mul] at h02 h12

    have hu : ((κ 2 g : Bˣ) : B) * (((κ 2 g)⁻¹ : Bˣ) : B) = 1 := Units.mul_inv _
    have hv : ((κ 2 h : Bˣ) : B) * (((κ 2 h)⁻¹ : Bˣ) : B) = 1 := Units.mul_inv _
    rw [twist_apply, characterExtensionRep_apply, MonoidHom.inv_apply]
    ext
    · simp
      linear_combination ((((κ 2 g)⁻¹ : Bˣ) : B) * (((κ 2 h)⁻¹ : Bˣ) : B)) * h02 - ((z (g * h) - z g) * ((κ 2 h : Bˣ) : B) * (((κ 2 h)⁻¹ : Bˣ) : B)) * hu - ((z (g * h) - z g) - (((κ 2 g)⁻¹ : Bˣ) : B) * (((κ 0 g : Bˣ) : B) * z h + x g * ((κ 1 g : Bˣ) : B) * y h)) * hv
    · simp
      linear_combination ((((κ 2 g)⁻¹ : Bˣ) : B) * (((κ 2 h)⁻¹ : Bˣ) : B)) * h12 - ((y (g * h) - y g) * ((κ 2 h : Bˣ) : B) * (((κ 2 h)⁻¹ : Bˣ) : B)) * hu - ((y (g * h) - y g) - (((κ 2 g)⁻¹ : Bˣ) : B) * (((κ 1 g : Bˣ) : B) * y h)) * hv
  exact (UpperTheory.isCrystalline_upperTriangular_iff_mem_finiteCocycles φ hφ κ hκ x y z ρ hxc hyc hz hρ hx hext (crκ 2)).2
    (GlobalTheory.T.ordinary_lift_local_iso hP φ hφ π hπ e he κ hκ hI x hx hxc hext y z hZ hyf)

end GlobalTheory


[OCH_SRC_TRI_PUSH] Annihilator-killing push makes the actual triangular rank-three representation crystalline
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/ModularityArgument/CrystallineOrdinaryIdeals/TriangularRankThreeCrystallineAfterPush.lean#L1-L82
import Mathlib
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.ModularityArgument.CrystallineOrdinaryIdeals.Imports
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.ModularityArgument.CrystallineOrdinaryIdeals.ExistsCoefficientFieldOfFiniteLocalPadic
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.ModularityArgument.CrystallineOrdinaryIdeals.TriCrys
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.ModularityArgument.CrystallineOrdinaryIdeals.CocycleCrysAddAnn
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.PadicHodgeTheory.Z1fMap
open Formalism.BasicNotions Formalism.BasicNotions.Loc ReciprocityTheory.Gal Module UpperTheory.V.Loc UpperTheory.V.U3 UpperTheory.V.Alg IsLocalRing Polynomial Topology

namespace GlobalTheory

theorem tri_crys_push : LocalPublishedTheorems → ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {G : Type} [Group G] [TopologicalSpace G] [IsTopologicalGroup G] (φ : G →* GQ l) (hφ : IsOpenEmbedding φ) {B' : Type} [CommRing B'] [IsLocalRing B'] [Algebra ℚ_[l] B'] [Module.Finite ℚ_[l] B'] [TopologicalSpace B'] [IsModuleTopology ℚ_[l] B'] [IsTopologicalRing B'] [Algebra ℤ_[l] B'] [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] B'] {B : Type} [CommRing B] [IsLocalRing B] [Algebra ℚ_[l] B] [Module.Finite ℚ_[l] B] (q : B' →ₐ[ℚ_[l]] B) (e : ℕ), 1 ≤ e → ∀ (κ : Fin 3 → G →* B'ˣ), (∀ i, Continuous fun g => (κ i g : B')) → (∀ g, φ g ∈ Iq l → ∀ i, κ i g = cycA l l B' (φ g) ^ ((e : ℤ) * wt1 i)) → ∀ (x y z : G → B') (ρ : G →* GL (Fin 3) B'), Continuous z → (∀ g, (ρ g : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) B') = triM (fun i => κ i g) (x g) (y g) (z g)) → x ∈ Z1 (chRep (κ 0 / κ 1)) → y ∈ Z1 (chRep (κ 1 / κ 2)) → ∀ σ : G, ((Iq l).comap φ ⊔ Subgroup.zpowers σ).topologicalClosure = ⊤ → (∀ b, (((κ 0 / κ 1) σ : B'ˣ) - (cycA l l B' (φ σ) : B')) * b = 0 → q b = 0) → (∀ b, (((κ 1 / κ 2) σ : B'ˣ) - (cycA l l B' (φ σ) : B')) * b = 0 → q b = 0) → IsCr φ (colRep ((Matrix.GeneralLinearGroup.map (q : B' →+* B)).comp ρ)) := by
  intro hP l _ G _ _ _ φ hφ B' _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ B _ _ _ _ q e he κ hκ hI x y z ρ hz hρ hx hy σ hσ hqx hqy

  obtain ⟨E', _, _, _, _, _, π', hπ'⟩ := GlobalTheory.exists_coefficientField_of_finite_local_padic (l := l) B'
  obtain ⟨E, _, _, _, _, _, π, hπ⟩ := GlobalTheory.exists_coefficientField_of_finite_local_padic (l := l) B
  letI : Algebra ℤ_[l] E' := ((algebraMap ℚ_[l] E').comp (algebraMap ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l])).toAlgebra
  haveI : IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] E' := IsScalarTower.of_algebraMap_eq (fun _ => rfl)
  letI : Algebra ℤ_[l] E := ((algebraMap ℚ_[l] E).comp (algebraMap ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l])).toAlgebra
  haveI : IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] E := IsScalarTower.of_algebraMap_eq (fun _ => rfl)
  letI : Algebra ℤ_[l] B := ((algebraMap ℚ_[l] B).comp (algebraMap ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l])).toAlgebra
  haveI : IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] B := IsScalarTower.of_algebraMap_eq (fun _ => rfl)
  letI : TopologicalSpace B := moduleTopology ℚ_[l] B
  haveI : IsModuleTopology ℚ_[l] B := ⟨rfl⟩
  haveI : IsTopologicalRing B := IsModuleTopology.isTopologicalRing ℚ_[l] B

  have hqc : Continuous (q : B' → B) := IsModuleTopology.continuous_of_linearMap (q.toLinearMap)
  let qm : B' →* B := (q : B' →+* B)
  let κB : Fin 3 → G →* Bˣ := fun i => (Units.map qm).comp (κ i)
  have hqcyc : ∀ h : GQ l, Units.map qm (cycA l l B' h) = cycA l l B h := by
    intro h
    apply Units.ext
    change q (algebraMap ℤ_[l] B' _) = algebraMap ℚ_[l] B (algebraMap ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] _)
    rw [IsScalarTower.algebraMap_apply ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] B', q.commutes]

  have hw01 : ∀ g, φ g ∈ Iq l → (κ 0 / κ 1) g = cycA l l B' (φ g) ^ (e : ℤ) := by
    intro g hg
    rw [MonoidHom.div_apply, hI g hg 0, hI g hg 1]
    simp [wt1]
  have hw12 : ∀ g, φ g ∈ Iq l → (κ 1 / κ 2) g = cycA l l B' (φ g) ^ (e : ℤ) := by
    intro g hg
    rw [MonoidHom.div_apply, hI g hg 1, hI g hg 2]
    simp [wt1]
  have hcdiv : ∀ i j, Continuous fun g => (((κ i / κ j) g : B'ˣ) : B') := by
    intro i j
    have : (fun g => (((κ i / κ j) g : B'ˣ) : B')) = fun g => (κ i g : B') * (κ j g⁻¹ : B') := by
      funext g
      rw [MonoidHom.div_apply, div_eq_mul_inv, Units.val_mul, ← map_inv]
    rw [this]
    exact (hκ i).mul ((hκ j).comp continuous_inv)
  have he : (1 : ℤ) ≤ (e : ℤ) := by exact_mod_cast he

  obtain ⟨cx, hcx, hxf, -⟩ := GlobalTheory.CrysAnn.cocycle_crys_add_ann hP φ hφ π' hπ' (κ 0 / κ 1) (hcdiv 0 1) e he hw01 σ hσ x hx
  obtain ⟨cy, hcy, hyf, -⟩ := GlobalTheory.CrysAnn.cocycle_crys_add_ann hP φ hφ π' hπ' (κ 1 / κ 2) (hcdiv 1 2) e he hw12 σ hσ y hy
  have push : ∀ (i j : Fin 3) (c : G → B'), c ∈ finiteCocycles φ (chRep (κ i / κ j)) →
      (fun g => q (c g)) ∈ finiteCocycles φ (chRep (κB i / κB j)) := by
    intro i j c hc
    refine (z1fMap φ (chRep (κ i / κ j)) (chRep (κB i / κB j))).2 q.toLinearMap (fun g v => ?_) c hc
    show q ((((κ i / κ j) g : B'ˣ) : B') * v) = (((κB i / κB j) g : Bˣ) : B) * q v
    rw [map_mul]
    congr 1
    simp only [κB, MonoidHom.div_apply, MonoidHom.comp_apply, ← _root_.map_div, Units.coe_map]
    rfl
  have hxB : (fun g => q (x g)) ∈ finiteCocycles φ (chRep (κB 0 / κB 1)) := by
    have h := push 0 1 _ hxf
    convert h using 2 with g
    simp [hqx _ (hcx g)]
  have hyB : (fun g => q (y g)) ∈ finiteCocycles φ (chRep (κB 1 / κB 2)) := by
    have h := push 1 2 _ hyf
    convert h using 2 with g
    simp [hqy _ (hcy g)]
  refine GlobalTheory.tri_crys hP φ hφ π hπ e (by exact_mod_cast he) κB (fun i => hqc.comp (hκ i)) ?_ (fun g => q (x g))
    (fun g => q (y g)) (fun g => q (z g)) ((Matrix.GeneralLinearGroup.map (q : B' →+* B)).comp ρ) (hqc.comp hz) ?_ hxB hyB
  · intro g hg i
    simp only [κB, MonoidHom.comp_apply, hI g hg i, map_zpow, hqcyc]
  · intro g
    rw [MonoidHom.comp_apply]
    show (ρ g : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) B').map q = _
    rw [hρ g]
    ext i j
    fin_cases i <;> fin_cases j <;> simp [triM, κB, qm]

end GlobalTheory


[OCH_SRC_ORDINARY_INDUCTION] Socle-line/fibre-product induction proving ordEq at every finite Artin algebra
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/PadicHodgeTheory/ExistsEps.lean#L1-L141
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.PadicHodgeTheory.IsInertiaInfiniteCyclotomic
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.PublishedTheorems.KisinCrTrivial
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.PadicHodgeTheory.TriLift
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.PadicHodgeTheory.OrdDual
open Formalism.BasicNotions Formalism.BasicNotions.Loc ReciprocityTheory.Gal Module UpperTheory.V.Loc UpperTheory.V.U3 UpperTheory.V.Alg UpperTheory.V.Published IsLocalRing Polynomial
open UpperTheory.V.Loc.OrdinaryDeformation TrivSqZeroExt

section Fibre

variable {E B : Type} [Field E] [CommRing B] [Algebra E B] (π : B →ₐ[E] E)

private noncomputable def fib : Subalgebra E (B × DualNumber E) :=
  AlgHom.equalizer (π.comp (AlgHom.fst E B _)) ((fstHom E E E).comp (AlgHom.snd E B _))

private noncomputable def p1 : fib π →ₐ[E] B := (AlgHom.fst E B _).comp (fib π).val

private noncomputable def p2 : fib π →ₐ[E] DualNumber E := (AlgHom.snd E B _).comp (fib π).val

private noncomputable def p3 (ε : B) (h0 : π ε = 0) (hε : ∀ b, b * ε = π b • ε) : fib π →ₐ[E] B where
  toFun c := c.1.1 + c.1.2.snd • ε
  map_one' := by simp
  map_zero' := by simp
  map_add' c c' := by
    simp only [Subalgebra.coe_add, Prod.fst_add, Prod.snd_add, snd_add, add_smul]
    abel
  map_mul' c c' := by
    have h : π c.1.1 = c.1.2.fst := c.2
    have h' : π c'.1.1 = c'.1.2.fst := c'.2
    simp only [Subalgebra.coe_mul, Prod.fst_mul, Prod.snd_mul, DualNumber.snd_mul, add_mul, mul_add, mul_smul_comm,
      smul_mul_assoc, mul_comm ε c'.1.1, hε, h, h', h0, smul_smul, add_smul, mul_zero, zero_smul, add_zero]
    rw [map_add, map_smul, h0, smul_zero, add_zero, h, mul_comm c'.1.2.snd, mul_comm c.1.2.snd]
    abel
  commutes' a := by simp [algebraMap_eq_inl]

private theorem exists_eps [IsNoetherianRing B] (hπ : ∀ a, π a = 0 → IsNilpotent a) (x : B) (hx : π x = 0)
    (hx0 : x ≠ 0) : ∃ ε : B, ε ≠ 0 ∧ π ε = 0 ∧ ∀ b, b * ε = π b • ε := by
  classical
  have hn : ∃ k, RingHom.ker π ^ (k + 1) = ⊥ := by
    obtain ⟨k, hk⟩ := (Ideal.FG.isNilpotent_iff_le_nilradical (IsNoetherian.noetherian (RingHom.ker π))).2
      fun a ha => hπ a ha
    exact ⟨k, by rw [pow_succ, hk]; simp⟩
  have h1 := Nat.find_spec hn
  have h2 : Nat.find hn ≠ 0 := fun h => by
    rw [h, zero_add, pow_one] at h1
    exact hx0 (Ideal.mem_bot.1 (h1 ▸ (hx : x ∈ RingHom.ker π)))
  obtain ⟨k, hk⟩ := Nat.exists_eq_succ_of_ne_zero h2
  obtain ⟨ε, h3, h4⟩ := (Submodule.ne_bot_iff _).1 (Nat.find_min hn (hk ▸ Nat.lt_succ_self k))
  refine ⟨ε, h4, Ideal.pow_le_self k.succ_ne_zero h3, fun b => ?_⟩
  have h5 : ε * (b - algebraMap E B (π b)) ∈ RingHom.ker π ^ (k + 1 + 1) := by
    rw [pow_succ]
    exact Ideal.mul_mem_mul h3 (by simp [RingHom.mem_ker])
  rw [hk] at h1
  rw [h1, Ideal.mem_bot] at h5
  rw [Algebra.smul_def]
  linear_combination h5

end Fibre

theorem UpperTheory.V.Loc.ordEq :
    LocalPublishedTheorems → ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [CharZero O] [Algebra ℤ_[l] O] [Module.Finite ℤ_[l] O] [IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal O) O] (D : Dat3 l O) (E : Type) [Field E] [Algebra ℚ_[l] E] [Module.Finite ℚ_[l] E] [Algebra O E] [Algebra ℤ_[l] E] [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] O E] [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] E] [IsFractionRing O E] (B : Type) [CommRing B] [Algebra ℚ_[l] B] [Module.Finite ℚ_[l] B] [Algebra O B] [Algebra ℤ_[l] B] [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] O B] [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] B] [Algebra E B] [IsScalarTower O E B] [IsScalarTower ℚ_[l] E B] (π : B →ₐ[E] E) (hπ : ∀ a, π a = 0 → IsNilpotent a) (r : GQ l →* GG 3 B), D.InDcr (π : B →+* E) r ↔ D.InDord (π : B →+* E) r := by
  intro hP l _ O _ _ _ _ _ _ _ D E _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ B _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ π hπ r
  refine ⟨fun hr => ?_, dcr_of_dord⟩
  induction hn : finrank ℚ_[l] B using Nat.strong_induction_on generalizing B with
  | _ N ih =>
  by_cases hinj : ∀ x, π x = 0 → x = 0
  · refine ⟨r, 1, ⟨hr, fun g a b h => hinj _ ?_⟩, (congOne_iff _ _).2 (map_one _), fun a b _ => by simp,
      (conjBy_one r).symm⟩
    have hab : a ≠ b := by
      rintro rfl
      simp [wt] at h
    change (((GG.map (π : B →+* E)).comp r) g).mat a b = 0
    rw [hr.red, rz_eq, GG.mat, unitaryThreeReflection_left, tor_val, Matrix.diagonal_apply_ne _ hab]
  push Not at hinj
  obtain ⟨x, hx, hx0⟩ := hinj
  haveI : IsNoetherianRing B := IsNoetherianRing.of_finite ℚ_[l] B
  obtain ⟨ε, hε0, h0, hε⟩ := exists_eps π hπ x hx hx0
  let I : Ideal B := Ideal.span {ε}
  let q : B →ₐ[E] B ⧸ I := Ideal.Quotient.mkₐ E I
  have hq : Function.Surjective q := Ideal.Quotient.mkₐ_surjective E I
  let π' : (B ⧸ I) →ₐ[E] E := Ideal.Quotient.liftₐ I π fun a ha => by
    obtain ⟨c, rfl⟩ := Ideal.mem_span_singleton'.1 ha
    rw [map_mul, h0, mul_zero]
  have hπq : π'.comp q = π := AlgHom.ext fun _ => rfl
  have hπ' : ∀ a, π' a = 0 → IsNilpotent a := fun a ha => by
    obtain ⟨b, rfl⟩ := hq a
    exact (hπ b ha).map q
  have hlt : finrank ℚ_[l] (B ⧸ I) < N := by
    rw [← hn, ← (q.restrictScalars ℚ_[l]).toLinearMap.finrank_range_add_finrank_ker,
      LinearMap.range_eq_top.2 hq, finrank_top]
    refine Nat.lt_add_of_pos_right (Module.finrank_pos_iff_exists_ne_zero.2 ⟨⟨ε, ?_⟩, fun h => hε0 (congrArg Subtype.val h)⟩)
    exact Ideal.Quotient.eq_zero_iff_mem.2 (Ideal.mem_span_singleton_self ε)
  have hr' := dcr_map (q.restrictScalars ℚ_[l]) hq (π' := (π' : (B ⧸ I) →+* E)) (RingHom.ext fun _ => rfl) hr
  obtain ⟨u', n', hu', hn1', hn2', e'⟩ := ih _ hlt (B ⧸ I) π' hπ' _ hr' rfl
  obtain ⟨u₁, hu₁, rfl⟩ := triLift hP D E B π hπ (B ⧸ I) π' hπ' q hq hπq u' hu'
  obtain ⟨n₁, hn₁, rfl⟩ := lift_lower (q : B →+* B ⧸ I) hq n' hn2'
  have hc₁ : Matrix.GeneralLinearGroup.map (π : B →+* E) n₁ = 1 := ((congOne_iff _ _).1 hn1' :)
  have hr₁ := dcr_conj hu₁.toInDcrAt n₁ hc₁
  haveI : Module.Finite ℚ_[l] (DualNumber E) := inferInstanceAs (Module.Finite ℚ_[l] (E × E))
  haveI : Module.Finite ℚ_[l] (fib π) :=
    Module.Finite.of_injective ((fib π).val.restrictScalars ℚ_[l]).toLinearMap Subtype.val_injective
  let f₁ : fib π →ₐ[ℚ_[l]] B := (p1 π).restrictScalars ℚ_[l]
  let f₂ : fib π →ₐ[ℚ_[l]] DualNumber E := (p2 π).restrictScalars ℚ_[l]
  let f₃ : fib π →ₐ[ℚ_[l]] B := (p3 π ε h0 hε).restrictScalars ℚ_[l]
  have i12 : ∀ c c', f₁ c = f₁ c' → f₂ c = f₂ c' → c = c' := fun c c' h h' => Subtype.ext (Prod.ext h h')
  have i13 : ∀ c c', f₁ c = f₁ c' → f₃ c = f₃ c' → c = c' := fun c c' h h' => by
    have h1 : c.1.1 = c'.1.1 := h
    have h2 : c.1.1 + c.1.2.snd • ε = c'.1.1 + c'.1.2.snd • ε := h'
    have h3 : π c.1.1 = c.1.2.fst := c.2
    have h4 : π c'.1.1 = c'.1.2.fst := c'.2
    rw [h1, add_right_inj] at h2
    exact i12 c c' h (TrivSqZeroExt.ext (by rw [h1, h4] at h3; exact h3.symm) (smul_left_injective E hε0 h2))
  have g12 : ∀ (s : B) (t : DualNumber E), π s = t.fst → ∃ c, f₁ c = s ∧ f₂ c = t := fun s t h => ⟨⟨(s, t), h⟩, rfl, rfl⟩
  have g13 : ∀ s t : B, q s = q t → ∃ c, f₁ c = s ∧ f₃ c = t := fun s t h => by
    obtain ⟨c, hc⟩ := Ideal.mem_span_singleton'.1 (Ideal.Quotient.eq.1 h.symm)
    refine ⟨⟨(s, (π s, π c)), rfl⟩, rfl, ?_⟩
    change s + π c • ε = t
    rw [← hε, hc, add_sub_cancel]
  have s2 : Function.Surjective f₂ := fun t => (g12 (algebraMap E B t.fst) t (π.commutes _)).imp fun _ h => h.2
  have s3 : Function.Surjective f₃ := fun t => (g13 t t rfl).imp fun _ h => h.2
  have k2 : (fstHom E E E : DualNumber E →+* E).comp (f₂ : fib π →+* DualNumber E) =
      (π : B →+* E).comp (f₁ : fib π →+* B) := RingHom.ext fun c => Eq.symm c.2
  have k3 : (π : B →+* E).comp (f₃ : fib π →+* B) = (π : B →+* E).comp (f₁ : fib π →+* B) := RingHom.ext fun c => by
    change π (c.1.1 + c.1.2.snd • ε) = π c.1.1
    rw [map_add, map_smul, h0, smul_zero, add_zero]
  obtain ⟨R, hR1, hR3⟩ := glue_hom (f₁ : fib π →+* B) (f₃ : fib π →+* B) (q : B →+* B ⧸ I) (q : B →+* B ⧸ I) i13 g13
    (conjBy n₁ u₁) r ((map_conjBy _ _ _).trans e'.symm)
  have hR := dcr_pair f₁ f₃ i13 (hR1 ▸ hr₁) (hR3 ▸ hr)
  obtain ⟨u₂, n₂, hu₂, hn₂, hn₂', e₂⟩ := dual_ord hP D E _ (dcr_map f₂ s2 k2 hR)
  obtain ⟨u, hu1, hu2⟩ := glue_hom (f₁ : fib π →+* B) (f₂ : fib π →+* DualNumber E) (π : B →+* E)
    (fstHom E E E : DualNumber E →+* E) i12 g12 u₁ u₂ (hu₁.red.trans hu₂.red.symm)
  obtain ⟨n, hn1, hn2⟩ := glue_GL (f₁ : fib π →+* B) (f₂ : fib π →+* DualNumber E) (π : B →+* E)
    (fstHom E E E : DualNumber E →+* E) i12 g12 n₁ n₂ (hc₁.trans ((congOne_iff _ _).1 hn₂).symm)
  subst hu1 hu2 hn1 hn2
  rw [← hR3]
  refine dord_map f₃ s3 k3 ⟨u, n, ⟨dcr_pair f₁ f₂ i12 hu₁.toInDcrAt hu₂.toInDcrAt, fun g a b h => ?_⟩,
    (congOne_iff _ _).2 hc₁, fun a b h => ?_, MonoidHom.ext fun g => ?_⟩
  · exact i12 _ _ ((hu₁.tri g a b h).trans (map_zero _).symm) ((hu₂.tri g a b h).trans (map_zero _).symm)
  · exact i12 _ _ (((map_sub_one _ n a b).symm.trans (hn₁ a b h)).trans (map_zero _).symm)
      (((map_sub_one _ n a b).symm.trans (hn₂' a b h)).trans (map_zero _).symm)
  · exact gg_ext (f₁ : fib π →+* B) (f₂ : fib π →+* DualNumber E) i12
      (DFunLike.congr_fun (hR1.trans (map_conjBy _ n u).symm) g) (DFunLike.congr_fun (e₂.trans (map_conjBy _ n u).symm) g)


[OCH_SRC_ORDINARY_DUAL] Negative-weight finite-cocycle elimination in the actual dual-number deformation
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/PadicHodgeTheory/OrdDual.lean#L1-L206
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.PadicHodgeTheory.OrdinaryDeformation
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.PadicHodgeTheory.DualCr

section Alg

variable {G R S : Type} [Group G] [CommRing R] [CommRing S] {m : ℕ}

private theorem adFun_map (f : R →+* S) (x : GG m R) (X : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) R) :
    (x.adFun X).map f = (GG.map f x).adFun (X.map f) := by
  have h1 : (thetaLie X).map f = thetaLie (X.map f) := by
    unfold thetaLie
    rw [Matrix.map_neg _ (map_neg f), Matrix.map_mul, Matrix.map_mul, Jmat_map, Matrix.transpose_map]
  have h2 : (((GG.map f x).left⁻¹ : GL (Fin m) S) : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) S) =
      ((x.left⁻¹ : GL (Fin m) R) : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) R).map f := rfl
  unfold GG.adFun
  rw [Matrix.map_mul, Matrix.map_mul, h2, GG.map_mat, GG.map_right]
  split_ifs
  · rfl
  · rw [h1]

private abbrev pr (R : Type) [CommRing R] : DualNumber R →+* R := (fstHom R R R : DualNumber R →+* R)

private abbrev inc (R : Type) [CommRing R] : R →+* DualNumber R := algebraMap R (DualNumber R)

private def ep (Y : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) R) : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) (DualNumber R) := Y.map inr

private theorem eps_smul (Z : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) (DualNumber R)) :
    (DualNumber.eps : DualNumber R) • Z = ep (Z.map (pr R)) := by
  ext a b <;> simp [ep]

private theorem inc_pr (Y : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) R) : (Y.map (inc R)).map (pr R) = Y := by
  ext a b; simp [algebraMap_eq_inl]

private theorem ep_eq (Y : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) R) : ep Y = (DualNumber.eps : DualNumber R) • Y.map (inc R) := by
  rw [eps_smul, inc_pr]

private theorem ep_mul (Y : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) R) (Z : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) (DualNumber R)) :
    ep Y * Z = ep (Y * Z.map (pr R)) := by
  rw [ep_eq, Matrix.smul_mul, eps_smul, Matrix.map_mul, inc_pr]

private theorem ad_ep (x : GG m (DualNumber R)) (Y : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) R) :
    GG.ad m _ x (ep Y) = ep ((GG.map (pr R) x).adFun Y) := by
  rw [ep_eq, map_smul, eps_smul, GG.ad_apply, adFun_map, inc_pr]

private theorem ep_add (X Y : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) R) : ep (X + Y) = ep X + ep Y := by
  ext a b <;> simp [ep]

private theorem ep_sub (X Y : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) R) : ep (X - Y) = ep X - ep Y := by
  ext a b <;> simp [ep]

private theorem ep_inj {X Y : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) R} (h : ep X = ep Y) : X = Y :=
  Matrix.ext fun a b => congrArg snd (congrFun (congrFun h a) b)

private theorem ep_pr (X : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) R) : (ep X).map (pr R) = 0 := by
  ext a b; simp [ep]

private theorem ker_sq : RingHom.ker (pr R) * RingHom.ker (pr R) = ⊥ := by
  refine le_bot_iff.1 (Ideal.mul_le.2 fun x hx y hy => ?_)
  have hx' : x.fst = 0 := hx
  have hy' : y.fst = 0 := hy
  rw [Ideal.mem_bot]
  ext <;> simp [hx', hy']

private theorem exists_coc (r : G →* GG m R) (r' : G →* GG m (DualNumber R)) (h : (GG.map (pr R)).comp r' = r) :
    ∃ γ : G → Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) R, IsCoc r γ ∧
      ∀ g, (r' g).mat = (r g).mat.map (inc R) + ep (γ g * (r g).mat) := by
  have e : (Ideal.Quotient.mk (RingHom.ker (pr R))).comp ((inc R).comp (pr R)) = Ideal.Quotient.mk _ := by
    refine RingHom.ext fun x => Ideal.Quotient.eq.2 ?_
    simp [RingHom.mem_ker, algebraMap_eq_inl]
  obtain ⟨γ', h1, h2, h3⟩ := exists_isCoc (RingHom.ker (pr R)) ((GG.map (inc R)).comp r) r' ker_sq (by
    rw [← h]
    refine MonoidHom.ext fun g => ?_
    simp only [MonoidHom.comp_apply, GG.map_map]
    rw [← RingHom.comp_assoc, RingHom.comp_assoc _ _ (Ideal.Quotient.mk _), e])
  have h4 : ∀ g, γ' g = ep ((γ' g).map snd) := fun g => by
    ext a b
    · exact h1 g a b
    · rfl
  have h5 : ∀ g, GG.map (pr R) (GG.map (inc R) (r g)) = r g := fun g =>
    SemidirectProduct.ext (Units.ext (inc_pr _)) rfl
  refine ⟨fun g => (γ' g).map snd, fun g k => ep_inj ?_, fun g => ?_⟩
  · rw [ep_add, ← h4, ← h4, h2 g k, h4 k]
    congr 1
    refine (ad_ep _ _).trans ?_
    rw [MonoidHom.comp_apply, h5]
    rfl
  · have e2 := ep_mul ((γ' g).map snd) (((GG.map (inc R)).comp r) g).mat
    rw [← h4, MonoidHom.comp_apply, GG.map_mat, inc_pr] at e2
    rw [(h3 g).2, Matrix.add_mul, Matrix.one_mul, MonoidHom.comp_apply, GG.map_mat, e2]

private theorem exists_conj (r : G →* GG m R) (r' : G →* GG m (DualNumber R)) (h : (GG.map (pr R)).comp r' = r)
    (γ : G → Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) R) (hγ : ∀ g, (r' g).mat = (r g).mat.map (inc R) + ep (γ g * (r g).mat))
    (X : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) R) :
    ∃ n : GL (Fin m) (DualNumber R), Matrix.GeneralLinearGroup.map (pr R) n = 1 ∧
      ((n⁻¹ : GL (Fin m) (DualNumber R)) : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) (DualNumber R)) - 1 = -ep X ∧
      ∀ g, (conjBy n r' g).mat = (r g).mat.map (inc R) + ep ((γ g + (X - adRep r g X)) * (r g).mat) := by
  obtain ⟨n, hn, hd⟩ := isDeform_cob (RingHom.ker (pr R)) r' (ep X) ker_sq (fun a b => rfl)
  have hg : ∀ g, GG.map (pr R) (r' g) = r g := fun g => DFunLike.congr_fun h g
  refine ⟨n, Units.ext ?_, ?_, fun g => ?_⟩
  · change (n : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) (DualNumber R)).map (pr R) = 1
    rw [hn, Matrix.map_add _ (map_add _), ep_pr, add_zero, Matrix.map_one _ (map_zero _) (map_one _)]
  · have h0 : ep X * ep X = 0 := by
      rw [ep_mul, ep_pr, Matrix.mul_zero]
      ext a b <;> simp [ep]
    have : ((n⁻¹ : GL (Fin m) (DualNumber R)) : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) (DualNumber R)) = 1 - ep X := by
      apply Units.inv_eq_of_mul_eq_one_right
      rw [hn, Matrix.add_mul, Matrix.mul_sub, Matrix.mul_sub, h0]
      simp
    rw [this]
    abel
  · have e : cob r' (ep X) g = ep (X - adRep r g X) := by
      rw [ep_sub, cob]
      congr 1
      refine (ad_ep _ _).trans ?_
      rw [hg]
      rfl
    rw [(hd g).2, e, Matrix.add_mul, Matrix.one_mul, ep_mul, ← GG.map_mat, hg, hγ g, Matrix.add_mul (γ g), ep_add,
      add_assoc]

end Alg

theorem rz_eq {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] [Algebra ℤ_[l] O] (D : Dat3 l O)
    (E : Type) [CommRing E] [Algebra O E] :
    D.rz E = r0 D.η ((Units.map (algebraMap O E : O →* E)).comp D.Ψ) := by
  refine MonoidHom.ext fun g => SemidirectProduct.ext (Units.ext ?_) rfl
  show (tor O (D.Ψ g) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) O).map (algebraMap O E) = tor _ (Units.map (algebraMap O E : O →* E) (D.Ψ g))
  rw [tor_val, tor_val, Matrix.diagonal_map (map_zero _)]
  congr 1
  funext i
  exact congrArg Units.val (map_zpow (Units.map (algebraMap O E : O →* E)) (D.Ψ g) (wt1 i))

theorem dual_ord (hP : LocalPublishedTheorems) {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsDomain O]
    [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [CharZero O] [Algebra ℤ_[l] O] [Module.Finite ℤ_[l] O]
    [IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal O) O] (D : Dat3 l O) (E : Type) [Field E] [Algebra ℚ_[l] E]
    [Module.Finite ℚ_[l] E] [Algebra O E] [Algebra ℤ_[l] E] [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] O E] [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] E]
    (r' : GQ l →* GG 3 (DualNumber E)) (hr : D.InDcr (fstHom E E E : DualNumber E →+* E) r') :
    D.InDord (fstHom E E E : DualNumber E →+* E) r' := by
  haveI : Module.Finite ℚ_[l] (DualNumber E) := inferInstanceAs (Module.Finite ℚ_[l] (E × E))
  obtain ⟨γ, hc, hγ⟩ := exists_coc _ r' hr.red
  have c1 : ∀ a b, QCont l fun g => ((r' g).mat a b).fst := fun a b =>
    qcont_map (LinearMap.fst ℚ_[l] E E) (hr.cont a b)
  have c2 : ∀ a b, QCont l fun g => ((r' g).mat a b).snd := fun a b =>
    qcont_map (LinearMap.snd ℚ_[l] E E) (hr.cont a b)
  have hd : DualDef (D.rz E) γ r' := fun g => ⟨hr.mult g, fun a b => by
    rw [hγ g]
    ext <;> simp [ep, algebraMap_eq_inl]⟩
  obtain ⟨X, hX, hX'⟩ : ∃ X : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) E, (∀ a b, 0 ≤ wt a b → X a b = 0) ∧
      ∀ g a b, wt a b < 0 → (γ g + (X - adRep (D.rz E) g X)) a b = 0 := by
    letI : TopologicalSpace E := moduleTopology ℚ_[l] E
    haveI : IsModuleTopology ℚ_[l] E := ⟨rfl⟩
    haveI : IsTopologicalRing E := IsModuleTopology.isTopologicalRing ℚ_[l] E
    have d1 : Continuous fun g => (D.rz E g).mat := continuous_matrix fun a b => by
      have := c1 a b
      simp only [fun g => (hd g).2 a b] at this
      exact this
    have d2 : Continuous fun g => γ g * (D.rz E g).mat := continuous_matrix fun a b => by
      have := c2 a b
      simp only [fun g => (hd g).2 a b] at this
      exact this
    have d3 : Continuous γ := by
      have e : γ = fun g => γ g * (D.rz E g).mat * (D.rz E g⁻¹).mat := by
        funext g
        rw [Matrix.mul_assoc, rz_eq, GG.mat, GG.mat, unitaryThreeReflection_left, unitaryThreeReflection_left, ← Units.val_mul, ← map_mul, ← map_mul,
          mul_inv_cancel, map_one, map_one, Units.val_one, Matrix.mul_one]
      rw [e]
      exact d2.mul (d1.comp continuous_inv)
    have hf := ((dualCr D E (D.rz E) (fun _ => rfl) d1 γ ⟨d3, hc⟩ r' hd).1 hr.cr).2
    have key : ∀ j < (0 : ℤ), ∃ Y : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) E, ∀ g a b, wt a b = j →
        γ g a b = (adRep (D.rz E) g Y - Y) a b := fun j hj => by
      obtain ⟨-, -, h1, h2⟩ := dim_Z1f_crystalline_deformation hP D E j
      have h3 := h1 γ hf
      obtain ⟨Y, hY⟩ := (mem_coboundaries1 _ _).1 (((h2 _ h3.1 fun g a b h => if_neg h).1 hj).1 h3)
      refine ⟨Y, fun g a b h => ?_⟩
      rw [← hY g, Matrix.of_apply, if_pos h]
    choose Y hY using key
    refine ⟨Matrix.of fun a b => if h : wt a b < 0 then Y _ h a b else 0, fun a b h => dif_neg (not_lt.2 h),
      fun g a b h => ?_⟩
    have hw : wt b.rev a.rev = wt a b := by fin_cases a <;> fin_cases b <;> rfl
    have e : ∀ j (hj : j < 0), j = wt a b → Y j hj = Y _ h := by
      rintro j hj rfl; rfl
    rw [Matrix.add_apply, hY _ h g a b rfl, Matrix.sub_apply, Matrix.sub_apply, rz_eq, adRep_baseLift_apply, adRep_baseLift_apply,
      Matrix.of_apply, Matrix.of_apply, dif_pos h, dif_pos (hw ▸ h), e _ _ hw]
    ring
  obtain ⟨n, hn1, hn2, hn3⟩ := exists_conj _ r' hr.red γ hγ X
  refine ⟨conjBy n r', n⁻¹, ⟨dcr_conj hr n hn1, fun g a b h => ?_⟩, (congOne_iff _ _).2 (by rw [map_inv, hn1, inv_one]),
    fun a b h => ?_, (conjBy_inv_conjBy n r').symm⟩
  · have hab : a ≠ b := by
      rintro rfl
      simp [wt] at h
    have h0 := hX' g a b h
    rw [hn3 g]
    generalize γ g + (X - adRep (D.rz E) g X) = Z at h0
    rw [rz_eq, GG.mat, unitaryThreeReflection_left, tor_val]
    simp [ep, Matrix.mul_diagonal, h0, Matrix.diagonal_apply_ne _ hab]
  · rw [hn2]
    simp [ep, hX a b h]

end UpperTheory.V.Loc.OrdinaryDeformation


[OCH_SRC_CR_LIFT] Full crystalline small-extension lifting after ordinary conjugation
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/PadicHodgeTheory/CrLift.lean#L1-L57
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.PadicHodgeTheory.IsInertiaInfiniteCyclotomic

private theorem map_ofGL {R S : Type} [CommRing R] [CommRing S] (f : R →+* S) (h : GL (Fin 3) R) :
    GG.map f (GG.ofGL h) = GG.ofGL (Matrix.GeneralLinearGroup.map f h) := rfl

private theorem map_conjBy {G R S : Type} [Group G] [CommRing R] [CommRing S] (f : R →+* S) (h : GL (Fin 3) R)
    (r : G →* GG 3 R) :
    (GG.map f).comp (conjBy h r) = conjBy (Matrix.GeneralLinearGroup.map f h) ((GG.map f).comp r) := by
  refine MonoidHom.ext fun g => ?_
  simp only [conjBy, MonoidHom.comp_apply, MulEquiv.coe_toMonoidHom, MulAut.conj_apply, map_mul, map_inv,
    map_ofGL]

private theorem lift_lower {R S : Type} [CommRing R] [CommRing S] (f : R →+* S) (hf : Function.Surjective f)
    (n' : GL (Fin 3) S) (hn : ∀ a b, 0 ≤ wt a b → ((n' : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) S) - 1) a b = 0) :
    ∃ n : GL (Fin 3) R, (∀ a b, 0 ≤ wt a b → ((n : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) R) - 1) a b = 0) ∧
      Matrix.GeneralLinearGroup.map f n = n' := by
  have hX : ∀ a b, ∃ x : R, (0 ≤ wt a b → x = 0) ∧ f x = ((n' : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) S) - 1) a b := by
    intro a b
    by_cases h : 0 ≤ wt a b
    · exact ⟨0, fun _ => rfl, by rw [map_zero, hn a b h]⟩
    · obtain ⟨x, hx⟩ := hf (((n' : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) S) - 1) a b)
      exact ⟨x, fun h' => absurd h' h, hx⟩
  choose X h0 hX using hX
  have hd : (Matrix.of X + 1).det = 1 := by
    have h00 := h0 0 0 (by decide)
    have h01 := h0 0 1 (by decide)
    have h02 := h0 0 2 (by decide)
    have h11 := h0 1 1 (by decide)
    have h12 := h0 1 2 (by decide)
    have h22 := h0 2 2 (by decide)
    simp [Matrix.det_fin_three, h00, h01, h02, h11, h12, h22]
  refine ⟨Matrix.GeneralLinearGroup.mk'' (Matrix.of X + 1) (by rw [hd]; exact isUnit_one), fun a b h => ?_, ?_⟩
  · change (Matrix.of X + 1 - 1) a b = 0
    rw [add_sub_cancel_right]
    exact h0 a b h
  · refine Units.ext (Matrix.ext fun a b => ?_)
    change f ((Matrix.of X + 1) a b) = _
    rw [Matrix.add_apply, map_add, Matrix.of_apply, hX, Matrix.sub_apply, Matrix.one_apply, Matrix.one_apply]
    split_ifs <;> simp

theorem UpperTheory.V.Loc.crLift :
    LocalPublishedTheorems → ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [CharZero O] [Algebra ℤ_[l] O] [Module.Finite ℤ_[l] O] [IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal O) O] (D : Dat3 l O) (E : Type) [Field E] [Algebra ℚ_[l] E] [Module.Finite ℚ_[l] E] [Algebra O E] [Algebra ℤ_[l] E] [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] O E] [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] E] [IsFractionRing O E] (B : Type) [CommRing B] [Algebra ℚ_[l] B] [Module.Finite ℚ_[l] B] [Algebra O B] [Algebra ℤ_[l] B] [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] O B] [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] B] [Algebra E B] [IsScalarTower O E B] [IsScalarTower ℚ_[l] E B] (π : B →ₐ[E] E) (hπ : ∀ a, π a = 0 → IsNilpotent a) (B' : Type) [CommRing B'] [Algebra ℚ_[l] B'] [Module.Finite ℚ_[l] B'] [Algebra O B'] [Algebra ℤ_[l] B'] [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] O B'] [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] B'] [Algebra E B'] [IsScalarTower O E B'] [IsScalarTower ℚ_[l] E B'] (π' : B' →ₐ[E] E) (hπ' : ∀ a, π' a = 0 → IsNilpotent a) (p : B →ₐ[E] B'), Function.Surjective p → π'.comp p = π → ∀ r' : GQ l →* GG 3 B', D.InDcr (π' : B' →+* E) r' → ∃ r : GQ l →* GG 3 B, D.InDcr (π : B →+* E) r ∧ (GG.map (p : B →+* B')).comp r = r' := by
  intro hP l _ O _ _ _ _ _ _ _ D E _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ B _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ π hπ B' _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ π' hπ' p hp hc r' hr'
  obtain ⟨u', n', hu', hn1, hn2, rfl⟩ := (ordEq hP D E B' π' hπ' r').1 hr'
  obtain ⟨u, hu, rfl⟩ := triLift hP D E B π hπ B' π' hπ' p hp hc u' hu'
  obtain ⟨n, hn, rfl⟩ := lift_lower (p : B →+* B') hp n' hn2
  refine ⟨conjBy n u, (ordEq hP D E B π hπ _).2 ⟨u, n, hu, fun a b => ?_, hn, rfl⟩, map_conjBy _ n u⟩
  have h := hn1 a b
  rw [RingHom.mem_ker] at h ⊢
  rw [← h, ← hc, Matrix.sub_apply, Matrix.sub_apply, Matrix.one_apply, Matrix.one_apply]
  change π' (p _) = π' (p _ - _)
  congr 1
  split_ifs <;> simp


[OCH_SRC_ARTIN_REES] Actual finite local thickening killing the saturated annihilator, via Artin--Rees
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/ModularityArgument/CrystallineOrdinaryIdeals/ExistsFiniteLocalLiftOfSaturatedIdeal.lean#L1-L134
import Mathlib
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.PadicHodgeTheory.TriDefs
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.ModularityArgument.CrystallineOrdinaryIdeals.ExistsLeMemPowOfMulMemPowOfNonZeroDivisorStatement
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.ModularityArgument.CrystallineOrdinaryIdeals.ModuleFiniteQuotientMaximalPowStatement
open Formalism.BasicNotions Formalism.BasicNotions.Loc ReciprocityTheory.Gal Module UpperTheory.V.Loc UpperTheory.V.U3 UpperTheory.V.Alg IsLocalRing Polynomial Topology

namespace GlobalTheory.ThickenSaturated

theorem isLocalRing_quot_pow {A : Type} [CommRing A] (m : Ideal A) [hm : m.IsMaximal] (k : ℕ)
    (hk : k ≠ 0) : IsLocalRing (A ⧸ m ^ k) := by
  have hne : m ^ k ≠ ⊤ := fun h => hm.ne_top (top_le_iff.1 (h ▸ Ideal.pow_le_self hk))
  haveI : Nontrivial (A ⧸ m ^ k) := Ideal.Quotient.nontrivial_iff.2 hne
  obtain ⟨M0, hM0⟩ := Ideal.exists_maximal (A ⧸ m ^ k)
  have key : ∀ M : Ideal (A ⧸ m ^ k), M.IsMaximal → M.comap (Ideal.Quotient.mk (m ^ k)) = m := by
    intro M hM
    have h1 : (M.comap (Ideal.Quotient.mk (m ^ k))).IsMaximal :=
      Ideal.comap_isMaximal_of_surjective _ Ideal.Quotient.mk_surjective
    have h2 : m ^ k ≤ M.comap (Ideal.Quotient.mk (m ^ k)) := by
      intro x hx
      rw [Ideal.mem_comap, Ideal.Quotient.eq_zero_iff_mem.2 hx]
      exact M.zero_mem
    have h3 : m ≤ M.comap (Ideal.Quotient.mk (m ^ k)) := h1.isPrime.le_of_pow_le h2
    exact (hm.eq_of_le h1.ne_top h3).symm
  exact IsLocalRing.of_unique_max_ideal ⟨M0, hM0, fun M hM =>
    Ideal.comap_injective_of_surjective _ Ideal.Quotient.mk_surjective
      ((key M hM).trans (key M0 hM0).symm)⟩

theorem core {K : Type} [Field K] {A : Type} [CommRing A] [IsNoetherianRing A] [Algebra K A]
    (δ : A) (hδ : ∀ a, δ * a = 0 → a = 0)
    {B : Type} [CommRing B] [IsLocalRing B] [Algebra K B] [Module.Finite K B] (g : A →ₐ[K] B) :
    ∃ I : Ideal A, IsLocalRing (A ⧸ I) ∧ Module.Finite K (A ⧸ I) ∧ I ≤ RingHom.ker g ∧
      ∀ a, δ * a ∈ I → g a = 0 := by
  set mB := IsLocalRing.maximalIdeal B with hmB
  set m : Ideal A := mB.comap (g : A →+* B) with hm
  haveI : IsArtinianRing B := IsArtinianRing.of_finite K B
  obtain ⟨n, hn⟩ : IsNilpotent mB := by
    have := IsArtinianRing.isNilpotent_jacobson_bot (R := B)
    rwa [IsLocalRing.jacobson_eq_maximalIdeal ⊥ bot_ne_top] at this
  have hj : m ^ (n + 1) ≤ RingHom.ker g := by
    intro x hx
    have h1 : x ∈ (mB ^ (n + 1)).comap (g : A →+* B) := Ideal.le_comap_pow _ _ hx
    rw [pow_succ, hn, zero_mul, Ideal.zero_eq_bot, Ideal.mem_comap, Ideal.mem_bot] at h1
    exact RingHom.mem_ker.2 h1
  obtain ⟨k, hjk, hk⟩ := GlobalTheory.T.exists_le_mem_pow_of_mul_mem_pow_of_nonZeroDivisor m δ hδ (n + 1)
  haveI : m.IsPrime := Ideal.comap_isPrime _ _
  have hinj : Function.Injective (Ideal.quotientMapₐ mB g le_rfl : A ⧸ m →ₐ[K] B ⧸ mB) :=
    Ideal.quotientMap_injective
  haveI : Module.Finite K (B ⧸ mB) :=
    Module.Finite.of_surjective (Ideal.Quotient.mkₐ K mB).toLinearMap Ideal.Quotient.mk_surjective
  haveI hfin : Module.Finite K (A ⧸ m) := Module.Finite.of_injective (Ideal.quotientMapₐ mB g le_rfl : A ⧸ m →ₐ[K] B ⧸ mB).toLinearMap hinj
  haveI : IsArtinianRing (A ⧸ m) := IsArtinianRing.of_finite K (A ⧸ m)
  haveI : m.IsMaximal := Ideal.Quotient.maximal_of_isField _ (IsArtinianRing.isField_of_isDomain _)
  refine ⟨m ^ k, isLocalRing_quot_pow m k (by omega), GlobalTheory.T.module_finite_quotient_maximal_pow m hfin k,
    (Ideal.pow_le_pow_right hjk).trans hj, fun a ha => ?_⟩
  exact RingHom.mem_ker.1 (hj (hk a ha))

theorem main : ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [Algebra ℤ_[l] O] (R : Type) [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] [IsNoetherianRing R] (J : Ideal R) (d : R), (l : R) ∣ d → (∀ a, d * a ∈ J → a ∈ J) → ∀ (B : Type) [CommRing B] [IsLocalRing B] [Algebra ℚ_[l] B] [Module.Finite ℚ_[l] B] [Algebra O B] [Algebra ℤ_[l] B] [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] O B] [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] B] (f : R →ₐ[O] B), J ≤ RingHom.ker f → ∃ (B' : Type) (_ : CommRing B') (_ : IsLocalRing B') (_ : Algebra ℚ_[l] B') (_ : Module.Finite ℚ_[l] B') (_ : Algebra O B') (_ : Algebra ℤ_[l] B') (_ : IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] O B') (_ : IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] B') (f' : R →ₐ[O] B') (q : B' →ₐ[ℚ_[l]] B), J ≤ RingHom.ker f' ∧ (∀ a, q (f' a) = f a) ∧ ∀ b, f' d * b = 0 → q b = 0 := by
  intro l hl O _ _ R _ _ _ J d _ hdJ B _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ f hJf
  let x : R ⧸ J := Ideal.Quotient.mk J (l : R)
  let A := Localization.Away x
  haveI : IsNoetherianRing A := IsLocalization.isNoetherianRing (Submonoid.powers x) A inferInstance
  have hlA : IsUnit ((l : ℕ) : A) := by
    have := IsLocalization.Away.algebraMap_isUnit (S := A) x
    simpa [x, map_natCast] using this
  let ψ₀ : ℤ_[l] →+* A := (algebraMap O A).comp (algebraMap ℤ_[l] O)
  have hψ₀ : ∀ y : nonZeroDivisors ℤ_[l], IsUnit (ψ₀ y) := by
    rintro ⟨y, hy⟩
    have hy0 : y ≠ 0 := nonZeroDivisors.ne_zero hy
    show IsUnit (ψ₀ y)
    rw [PadicInt.unitCoeff_spec hy0, map_mul, map_pow, map_natCast]
    exact ((Units.isUnit _).map ψ₀).mul (hlA.pow _)
  let ψ : ℚ_[l] →+* A := IsLocalization.lift (M := nonZeroDivisors ℤ_[l]) hψ₀
  letI algQA : Algebra ℚ_[l] A := ψ.toAlgebra
  let g₀ : R ⧸ J →+* B := Ideal.Quotient.lift J f.toRingHom (fun a ha => RingHom.mem_ker.1 (hJf ha))
  have hu : IsUnit (g₀ x) := by
    have h1 : g₀ x = algebraMap ℚ_[l] B (l : ℚ_[l]) := by
      simp [g₀, x, map_natCast]
    rw [h1]
    exact (isUnit_iff_ne_zero.2 (Nat.cast_ne_zero.2 hl.out.ne_zero)).map _
  let g : A →+* B := IsLocalization.Away.lift x hu
  have hg : ∀ w, g (algebraMap (R ⧸ J) A w) = g₀ w := fun w => IsLocalization.Away.lift_eq x hu w
  have hgO : ∀ z : O, g (algebraMap O A z) = algebraMap O B z := by
    intro z
    rw [IsScalarTower.algebraMap_apply O (R ⧸ J) A, hg]
    exact f.commutes z
  have hgψ : g.comp ψ = algebraMap ℚ_[l] B := by
    refine IsLocalization.ringHom_ext (nonZeroDivisors ℤ_[l]) ?_
    ext y
    simp only [RingHom.comp_apply, ψ, IsLocalization.lift_eq, ψ₀, hgO]
    rw [← IsScalarTower.algebraMap_apply, ← IsScalarTower.algebraMap_apply]
  let gK : A →ₐ[ℚ_[l]] B := { g with commutes' := fun r => RingHom.congr_fun hgψ r }
  let δ : A := algebraMap (R ⧸ J) A (Ideal.Quotient.mk J d)
  have hδ : ∀ a, δ * a = 0 → a = 0 := by
    intro a ha
    obtain ⟨⟨w, s⟩, rfl⟩ := IsLocalization.mk'_surjective (Submonoid.powers x) a
    change δ * IsLocalization.mk' A w s = 0 at ha
    change IsLocalization.mk' A w s = 0
    have e : δ = IsLocalization.mk' A (Ideal.Quotient.mk J d) (1 : Submonoid.powers x) :=
      (IsLocalization.mk'_one A _).symm
    rw [e, ← IsLocalization.mk'_mul, one_mul, IsLocalization.mk'_eq_zero_iff] at ha
    obtain ⟨c, hc⟩ := ha
    rw [IsLocalization.mk'_eq_zero_iff]
    refine ⟨c, ?_⟩
    obtain ⟨w', rfl⟩ := Ideal.Quotient.mk_surjective w
    obtain ⟨c', hc'⟩ := Ideal.Quotient.mk_surjective (c : R ⧸ J)
    rw [← hc', ← map_mul, ← map_mul, Ideal.Quotient.eq_zero_iff_mem] at hc
    rw [← hc', ← map_mul, Ideal.Quotient.eq_zero_iff_mem]
    apply hdJ
    simpa [mul_comm, mul_left_comm, mul_assoc] using hc
  obtain ⟨I, hloc, hfin, hIker, hI⟩ := core δ hδ gK
  letI algZ : Algebra ℤ_[l] (A ⧸ I) := ((algebraMap ℚ_[l] (A ⧸ I)).comp (algebraMap ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l])).toAlgebra
  have tZO : IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] O (A ⧸ I) := IsScalarTower.of_algebraMap_eq fun y => by
    show Ideal.Quotient.mk I (ψ (algebraMap ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] y)) = Ideal.Quotient.mk I (algebraMap O A (algebraMap ℤ_[l] O y))
    rw [IsLocalization.lift_eq]
    rfl
  have tZQ : IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] (A ⧸ I) := IsScalarTower.of_algebraMap_eq fun _ => rfl
  let f' : R →ₐ[O] A ⧸ I := (Ideal.Quotient.mkₐ O I).comp
    ((IsScalarTower.toAlgHom O (R ⧸ J) A).comp (Ideal.Quotient.mkₐ O J))
  have hf' : ∀ a, f' a = Ideal.Quotient.mk I (algebraMap (R ⧸ J) A (Ideal.Quotient.mk J a)) := fun _ => rfl
  let q : A ⧸ I →ₐ[ℚ_[l]] B := Ideal.Quotient.liftₐ I gK (fun a ha => RingHom.mem_ker.1 (hIker ha))
  have hq : ∀ a, q (Ideal.Quotient.mk I a) = g a := fun _ => rfl
  refine ⟨A ⧸ I, inferInstance, hloc, inferInstance, hfin, inferInstance, algZ, tZO, tZQ, f', q, ?_, ?_, ?_⟩
  · intro a ha
    rw [RingHom.mem_ker, hf', Ideal.Quotient.eq_zero_iff_mem.2 ha, map_zero, map_zero]
  · intro a
    rw [hf', hq, hg]
    rfl
  · intro b hb
    obtain ⟨a, rfl⟩ := Ideal.Quotient.mk_surjective b
    rw [hf', ← map_mul, Ideal.Quotient.eq_zero_iff_mem] at hb
    rw [hq]
    exact hI a hb

end GlobalTheory.ThickenSaturated


[OCH_SRC_SATURATED_POINT] Every finite local saturated-ideal point is crystalline
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/ModularityArgument/CrystallineOrdinaryIdeals/IsLCrystallinePointOfSaturatedOrdIdeal.lean#L1-L66
import Mathlib

theorem main : LocalPublishedTheorems → ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [CharZero O] [Algebra ℤ_[l] O] [Module.Finite ℤ_[l] O] [IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal O) O] (D : Dat3 l O) (U : D.Univ) (B : Type) [CommRing B] [Algebra ℚ_[l] B] [Module.Finite ℚ_[l] B] [Algebra O B] [Algebra ℤ_[l] B] [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] O B] [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] B] [IsLocalRing B] (f : U.R →ₐ[O] B), GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.IY D U ≤ RingHom.ker f → D.IsLCr (push f U.r.hom) := by
  intro hP l _ O _ _ _ _ _ _ _ D U B _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ f hf

  have hord : GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.ordIdeal D U.r.hom ≤ GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.IY D U := fun a ha => ⟨0, by simpa using ha⟩
  have hdiv : (l : U.R) ∣ GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.del D U.r.hom :=
    ⟨GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.dd D U.r.hom 0 * GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.dd D U.r.hom 1, by unfold GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.del; ring⟩
  have hsat : ∀ a, GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.del D U.r.hom * a ∈ GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.IY D U → a ∈ GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.IY D U := by
    rintro a ⟨n, hn⟩
    exact ⟨n + 1, by rw [pow_succ, mul_assoc]; exact hn⟩
  obtain ⟨B', _, _, _, _, _, _, _, _, f', q, hJ, hqf, hkill⟩ :=
    GlobalTheory.T.exists_finite_local_lift_of_saturated_ideal U.R (GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.IY D U) (GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.del D U.r.hom) hdiv hsat B f hf
  letI : TopologicalSpace B' := moduleTopology ℚ_[l] B'
  haveI : IsModuleTopology ℚ_[l] B' := ⟨rfl⟩
  haveI : IsTopologicalRing B' := IsModuleTopology.isTopologicalRing ℚ_[l] B'
  obtain ⟨hnat, -, hdel⟩ := GlobalTheory.T.ordinaryIdeal_saturators_map_algHom D f' U.r.hom
  have hzero : GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.ordIdeal D (push f' U.r.hom) = ⊥ := by
    rw [← hnat, Ideal.map_eq_bot_iff_le_ker]
    exact hord.trans hJ
  have hmult' : HasMult D.η (push f' U.r.hom) := fun g => U.r.hasMult g
  have hcont' : ∀ a b, QCont l fun g => (push f' U.r.hom g).mat a b := fun a b =>
    (ptCont U.R B' f').1 (GQ l) (fun g => (U.r.hom g).mat a b) (U.r.cont a b)
  obtain ⟨h, ρ, κ, x, y, z, hρ, hκc, hκI, hz, hρM, hx, hy, hσd⟩ :=
    GlobalTheory.T.triangular_conj_of_ordIdeal_eq_bot D B' (push f' U.r.hom) hmult' hcont' hzero
  obtain ⟨⟨hσ, hcl⟩, -⟩ := GlobalTheory.T.chosenFrobenius_isFrobenius D
  obtain ⟨-, -, hφ, -, -⟩ := GlobalTheory.T.galoisSubgroupL_basic_properties D
  obtain ⟨⟨v0, hv0⟩, ⟨v1, hv1⟩⟩ := hσd hσ
  have hdel' : f' (GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.del D U.r.hom) = (l : B') * GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.dd D (push f' U.r.hom) 0 *
      GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.dd D (push f' U.r.hom) 1 := hdel
  have hcr := GlobalTheory.T.triangular_rank_three_crystalline_after_push hP D.HL.subtype hφ q D.e D.e_pos κ hκc hκI x y z ρ hz hρM hx hy ⟨_, hσ⟩ hcl
    (fun b hb => hkill b (by
      have hb' : ((((κ 0 / κ 1) ⟨_, hσ⟩ : B'ˣ) : B') - (cycA l l B' (GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.fr D) : B')) * b = 0 := hb
      rw [hdel', hv0]
      linear_combination ((l : B') * (v0 : B') * GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.dd D (push f' U.r.hom) 1) * hb'))
    (fun b hb => hkill b (by
      have hb' : ((((κ 1 / κ 2) ⟨_, hσ⟩ : B'ˣ) : B') - (cycA l l B' (GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.fr D) : B')) * b = 0 := hb
      rw [hdel', hv1]
      linear_combination ((l : B') * GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.dd D (push f' U.r.hom) 0 * (v1 : B')) * hb'))
  have hmult : HasMult D.η (push f U.r.hom) := fun g => U.r.hasMult g
  have hcont : ∀ a b, QCont l fun g => (push f U.r.hom g).mat a b := fun a b =>
    (ptCont U.R B f).1 (GQ l) (fun g => (U.r.hom g).mat a b) (U.r.cont a b)
  have hqf' : (q : B' →+* B).comp (f' : U.R →+* B') = (f : U.R →+* B) := RingHom.ext hqf
  refine GlobalTheory.T.isLCrystalline_conjBy_invariant D B (push f U.r.hom) (Matrix.GeneralLinearGroup.map (q : B' →+* B) h) hmult hcont
    ⟨(Matrix.GeneralLinearGroup.map (q : B' →+* B)).comp ρ, fun g => ?_, hcr⟩
  have e1 : GG.map (q : B' →+* B) (conjBy h (push f' U.r.hom) (g : GQ l)) =
      conjBy (Matrix.GeneralLinearGroup.map (q : B' →+* B) h) (push f U.r.hom) (g : GQ l) := by
    simp only [conjBy, push, MonoidHom.comp_apply, MulEquiv.coe_toMonoidHom, MulAut.conj_apply, map_mul, map_inv,
      GG.map_map, hqf']
    congr 2 <;> simp [GG.ofGL, GG.map, SemidirectProduct.map_inl]
  rw [MonoidHom.comp_apply, hρ g, ← e1]
  rfl

end GlobalTheory.OrdLocPt


[OCH_SRC_FINITE_TARGETS] All finite-algebra targets, including their product/local decomposition
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/ModularityArgument/CrystallineOrdinaryIdeals/SaturatedOrdIdealPointsIsLCrystalline.lean#L1-L52
import Mathlib
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.ModularityArgument.CrystallineOrdinaryIdeals.Imports
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.ModularityArgument.CrystallineOrdinaryIdeals.ExistsCrOfFinset
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.ModularityArgument.CrystallineOrdinaryIdeals.GaloisSubgroupLBasicPropertiesStatement
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.ModularityArgument.CrystallineOrdinaryIdeals.IsLCrystallinePointOfSaturatedOrdIdealStatement
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.PadicHodgeTheory.PtCont
open Formalism.BasicNotions Formalism.BasicNotions.Loc ReciprocityTheory.Gal Module UpperTheory.V.Loc UpperTheory.V.U3 UpperTheory.V.Alg IsLocalRing Polynomial Topology

namespace GlobalTheory.OrdLoc

def glPart {G R : Type} [Group G] [CommRing R] {m : ℕ} (H : Subgroup G) (r : G →* GG m R)
    (h : ∀ g ∈ H, (r g).right = 1) : H →* GL (Fin m) R where
  toFun g := (r g).left
  map_one' := by simp
  map_mul' g g' := by simp [h g g.2]

theorem ptsCr_IY : LocalPublishedTheorems → ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [CharZero O] [Algebra ℤ_[l] O] [Module.Finite ℤ_[l] O] [IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal O) O] (D : Dat3 l O) (U : D.Univ), GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.PtsCr D U (GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.IY D U) := by
  intro hP l _ O _ _ _ _ _ _ _ D U B _ _ _ _ _ _ _ f hf
  obtain ⟨-, -, hemb, -, -⟩ := GlobalTheory.T.galoisSubgroupL_basic_properties D
  have hright : ∀ g ∈ D.HL, (push f U.r.hom g).right = 1 := fun g hg => by
    rw [show (push f U.r.hom g).right = (U.r.hom g).right from rfl, U.r.hasMult g]
    exact hg.1
  let ρ := glPart D.HL (push f U.r.hom) hright
  refine ⟨ρ, fun _ => rfl, ?_⟩
  refine GlobalTheory.CrLocal.isCr_of_local D.HL.subtype hemb ρ (fun i j => ?_) ?_
  · have h := (ptCont (l := l) U.R B f).1 (GQ l) (fun g => (U.r.hom g).mat i j) (U.r.cont i j)
    unfold QCont at h ⊢
    exact @Continuous.comp ↥D.HL (GQ l) B _ _ (moduleTopology ℚ_[l] B) Subtype.val (fun g => f ((U.r.hom g).mat i j)) h continuous_subtype_val
  · intro B' _ _ _ _ _ _
    letI : Algebra O B' := ((algebraMap B B').comp (algebraMap O B)).toAlgebra
    letI : Algebra ℤ_[l] B' := ((algebraMap B B').comp (algebraMap ℤ_[l] B)).toAlgebra
    haveI : IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] O B' := IsScalarTower.of_algebraMap_eq fun x => by
      change algebraMap B B' (algebraMap ℤ_[l] B x) = algebraMap B B' (algebraMap O B (algebraMap ℤ_[l] O x))
      rw [IsScalarTower.algebraMap_apply ℤ_[l] O B]
    haveI : IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] B' := IsScalarTower.of_algebraMap_eq fun x => by
      change algebraMap B B' (algebraMap ℤ_[l] B x) = algebraMap ℚ_[l] B' (algebraMap ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] x)
      rw [IsScalarTower.algebraMap_apply ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] B, IsScalarTower.algebraMap_apply ℚ_[l] B B']
    let f' : U.R →ₐ[O] B' :=
      { toRingHom := (algebraMap B B').comp (f : U.R →+* B)
        commutes' := fun x => by
          change algebraMap B B' (f (algebraMap O U.R x)) = algebraMap B B' (algebraMap O B x)
          rw [f.commutes] }
    obtain ⟨ρ', hρ', hcr⟩ := GlobalTheory.T.isLCrystalline_point_of_saturatedOrdIdeal hP D U B' f' (fun a ha => by
      change algebraMap B B' (f a) = 0
      rw [show f a = 0 from hf ha, map_zero])
    have e : ρ' = (Matrix.GeneralLinearGroup.map (algebraMap B B')).comp ρ := by
      ext g i j
      rw [hρ' g]
      rfl
    exact e ▸ hcr

end GlobalTheory.OrdLoc


[OCH_SRC_CRYS_ANN_IDEAL] Crystalline ordinary points annihilate the saturated ideal through the actual base
