namespace GlobalTheory.CrFrac

theorem push_comp {O R A B' Γ : Type} [CommRing O] [CommRing R] [CommRing A] [CommRing B'] [Algebra O R]
    [Algebra O A] [Algebra O B'] [Group Γ] (g : A →ₐ[O] B') (F : R →ₐ[O] A) (r : Γ →* GG 3 R) :
    push (g.comp F) r = (GG.map (g : A →+* B')).comp (push F r) := rfl

theorem core (hL : LocalPublishedTheorems) {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O]
    [CharZero O] [Algebra ℤ_[l] O] [Module.Finite ℤ_[l] O] [IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal O) O] (D : Dat3 l O)
    (U : D.Univ) (E : Type) [Field E] [Algebra ℚ_[l] E] [Module.Finite ℚ_[l] E] [Algebra O E] [Algebra ℤ_[l] E]
    [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] O E] [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] E] [IsFractionRing O E]
    (B : Type) [CommRing B] [Algebra ℚ_[l] B] [Module.Finite ℚ_[l] B] [Algebra O B] [Algebra ℤ_[l] B]
    [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] O B] [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] B] [Algebra E B] [IsScalarTower O E B]
    [IsScalarTower ℚ_[l] E B] (π : B →ₐ[E] E) (hπ : ∀ a, π a = 0 → IsNilpotent a) (f : U.R →ₐ[O] B)
    (hf : D.InDcr (π : B →+* E) (push f U.r.hom))
    (C : Type) [CommRing C] [Algebra ℚ_[l] C] [Module.Finite ℚ_[l] C] [Algebra O C] [Algebra ℤ_[l] C]
    [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] O C] [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] C] [Algebra E C] [IsScalarTower O E C]
    [IsScalarTower ℚ_[l] E C]
    (T : Type) [CommRing T] [Algebra ℚ_[l] T] [Module.Finite ℚ_[l] T] [Algebra O T] [Algebra ℤ_[l] T]
    [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] O T] [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] T] [Algebra E T] [IsScalarTower O E T]
    [IsScalarTower ℚ_[l] E T]
    (πC : C →ₐ[E] E) (p₁ : T →ₐ[E] B) (p₂ : T →ₐ[E] C) (t : C) (ht : IsNilpotent t)
    (hπC : ∀ a, πC a = 0 → IsNilpotent a) (hπT : ∀ a, π (p₁ a) = 0 → IsNilpotent a)
    (hcomp : π.comp p₁ = πC.comp p₂) (hsurj : ∀ b c, π b = πC c → ∃ s, p₁ s = b ∧ p₂ s = c) (k : ℕ)
    (hcanc : ∀ (P y : T) (j : ℕ) (v : Cˣ), j ≤ 2 * k → p₂ P = t ^ j * v → P * y = 0 → p₁ y = 0)
    (a : U.R) (ha : GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.del D U.r.hom ^ k * a ∈ GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.ordIdeal D U.r.hom) : f a = 0 := by
  obtain ⟨u, hu1, hu2, hu3, j, v, hj, hdel⟩ := GlobalTheory.T.saturatingElement_unramified_twist_eq_pow_mul_unit D E C t ht
  have hr₂ := GlobalTheory.T.crystalline_twist_by_unramified_character D E C πC hπC u hu1 hu2 hu3
  obtain ⟨r, hr, hr1, hr2⟩ := GlobalTheory.T.crystallineDeformation_amalgamate_over_fibre_product_cover hL D E B π hπ C πC hπC T (π.comp p₁) hπT p₁ p₂ rfl hcomp.symm hsurj
    (push f U.r.hom) _ hf hr₂
  have hx : (GG.map (π : B →+* E)).comp (push f U.r.hom) = push ((π.restrictScalars O).comp f) U.r.hom :=
    (push_comp (π.restrictScalars O) f U.r.hom).symm
  obtain ⟨F, hF, -⟩ := unramified_lift_deformation D U E T (π.comp p₁) hπT ((π.restrictScalars O).comp f) r hr.mult
    (hr.red.trans (hf.red.symm.trans hx)) hr.cont
  have hF1 : (p₁.restrictScalars O).comp F = f := by
    obtain ⟨g, -, hg⟩ := unramified_lift_deformation D U E B π hπ ((π.restrictScalars O).comp f) (push f U.r.hom) hf.mult hx hf.cont
    refine (hg _ ?_).trans (hg f rfl).symm
    show push ((p₁.restrictScalars O).comp F) U.r.hom = push f U.r.hom
    rw [push_comp, hF]
    exact hr1
  have h0 := GlobalTheory.T.ordIdeal_eq_bot_of_crystalline_deformation hL D E T (π.comp p₁) hπT r hr
  obtain ⟨hmap, -, hdelF⟩ := GlobalTheory.T.ordinaryIdeal_saturators_map_algHom D F U.r.hom
  have h1 : F (GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.del D U.r.hom ^ k * a) = 0 := by
    have h := Ideal.mem_map_of_mem (F : U.R →+* T) ha
    rw [hmap, hF, h0] at h
    exact (Submodule.mem_bot _).1 h
  rw [map_mul, map_pow, hdelF, hF] at h1
  obtain ⟨-, -, hdel2⟩ := GlobalTheory.T.ordinaryIdeal_saturators_map_algHom D (p₂.restrictScalars O) r
  have hpush : push (p₂.restrictScalars O) r = r0 D.η ((Units.map (algebraMap O C : O →* C)).comp D.Ψ * u) := hr2
  rw [hpush, hdel] at hdel2
  have hP : p₂ (GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.del D r ^ k) = t ^ (j * k) * ((v ^ k : Cˣ) : C) := by
    rw [map_pow, show p₂ (GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.del D r) = t ^ j * v from hdel2, mul_pow, ← pow_mul, Units.val_pow_eq_pow_val]
  have h2 := hcanc _ (F a) (j * k) (v ^ k) (by nlinarith) hP h1
  rw [← hF1]
  exact h2

theorem main (hL : LocalPublishedTheorems) {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O]
    [CharZero O] [Algebra ℤ_[l] O] [Module.Finite ℤ_[l] O] [IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal O) O] (D : Dat3 l O)
    (U : D.Univ) (E : Type) [Field E] [Algebra ℚ_[l] E] [Module.Finite ℚ_[l] E] [Algebra O E] [Algebra ℤ_[l] E]
    [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] O E] [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] E] [IsFractionRing O E]
    (B : Type) [CommRing B] [Algebra ℚ_[l] B] [Module.Finite ℚ_[l] B] [Algebra O B] [Algebra ℤ_[l] B]
    [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] O B] [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] B] [Algebra E B] [IsScalarTower O E B]
    [IsScalarTower ℚ_[l] E B] (π : B →ₐ[E] E) (hπ : ∀ a, π a = 0 → IsNilpotent a) (f : U.R →ₐ[O] B)
    (hf : D.InDcr (π : B →+* E) (push f U.r.hom)) : GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.IY D U ≤ RingHom.ker f := by
  rintro a ⟨k, hk⟩
  haveI : Module.Finite E B := Module.Finite.of_restrictScalars_finite ℚ_[l] E B
  obtain ⟨C, _, _, _, T, _, _, _, πC, p₁, p₂, t, ht, hπC, hπT, hcomp, hsurj, hcanc⟩ := GlobalTheory.T.exists_truncated_polynomial_thickening_fibre_square π hπ (2 * k)
  letI : Algebra O C := Algebra.compHom C (algebraMap O E)
  letI : Algebra ℚ_[l] C := Algebra.compHom C (algebraMap ℚ_[l] E)
  letI : Algebra ℤ_[l] C := Algebra.compHom C (algebraMap ℤ_[l] E)
  haveI : IsScalarTower O E C := IsScalarTower.of_algebraMap_eq fun _ => rfl
  haveI : IsScalarTower ℚ_[l] E C := IsScalarTower.of_algebraMap_eq fun _ => rfl
  haveI : IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] O C := IsScalarTower.of_algebraMap_eq fun x =>
    congrArg (algebraMap E C) (IsScalarTower.algebraMap_apply ℤ_[l] O E x)
  haveI : IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] C := IsScalarTower.of_algebraMap_eq fun x =>
    congrArg (algebraMap E C) (IsScalarTower.algebraMap_apply ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] E x)
  haveI : Module.Finite ℚ_[l] C := Module.Finite.trans E C
  letI : Algebra O T := Algebra.compHom T (algebraMap O E)
  letI : Algebra ℚ_[l] T := Algebra.compHom T (algebraMap ℚ_[l] E)
  letI : Algebra ℤ_[l] T := Algebra.compHom T (algebraMap ℤ_[l] E)
  haveI : IsScalarTower O E T := IsScalarTower.of_algebraMap_eq fun _ => rfl
  haveI : IsScalarTower ℚ_[l] E T := IsScalarTower.of_algebraMap_eq fun _ => rfl
  haveI : IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] O T := IsScalarTower.of_algebraMap_eq fun x =>
    congrArg (algebraMap E T) (IsScalarTower.algebraMap_apply ℤ_[l] O E x)
  haveI : IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] T := IsScalarTower.of_algebraMap_eq fun x =>
    congrArg (algebraMap E T) (IsScalarTower.algebraMap_apply ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] E x)
  haveI : Module.Finite ℚ_[l] T := Module.Finite.trans E T
  exact core hL D U E B π hπ f hf C T πC p₁ p₂ t ht hπC hπT hcomp hsurj k hcanc a hk

end GlobalTheory.CrFrac


[OCH_SRC_TRUNCATED_SQUARE] Actual truncated polynomial fibre square and nilpotent cancellation
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/ModularityArgument/CrystallineOrdinaryIdeals/ExistsTruncatedPolynomialThickeningFibreSquareStatement.lean#L1-L165
import Mathlib
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.ModularityArgument.CrystallineOrdinaryIdeals.Imports

open Formalism.BasicNotions Formalism.BasicNotions.Loc ReciprocityTheory.Gal Module UpperTheory.V.Loc UpperTheory.V.U3 UpperTheory.V.Alg IsLocalRing Polynomial Topology
set_option maxHeartbeats 1000000 in
theorem _root_.GlobalTheory.T.exists_truncated_polynomial_thickening_fibre_square : ∀ {E B : Type} [Field E] [CommRing B] [Algebra E B] [Module.Finite E B] (π : B →ₐ[E] E), (∀ a, π a = 0 → IsNilpotent a) → ∀ J : ℕ, ∃ (C : Type) (_ : CommRing C) (_ : Algebra E C) (_ : Module.Finite E C) (T : Type) (_ : CommRing T) (_ : Algebra E T) (_ : Module.Finite E T) (πC : C →ₐ[E] E) (p₁ : T →ₐ[E] B) (p₂ : T →ₐ[E] C) (t : C), IsNilpotent t ∧ (∀ a, πC a = 0 → IsNilpotent a) ∧ (∀ a, π (p₁ a) = 0 → IsNilpotent a) ∧ π.comp p₁ = πC.comp p₂ ∧ (∀ b c, π b = πC c → ∃ s, p₁ s = b ∧ p₂ s = c) ∧ ∀ (P y : T) (j : ℕ) (v : Cˣ), j ≤ J → p₂ P = t ^ j * v → P * y = 0 → p₁ y = 0 := by
  intro E B _ _ _ _ π hπ J
  classical
  haveI : IsArtinianRing B := IsArtinianRing.of_finite E B
  obtain ⟨e, he⟩ : IsNilpotent (RingHom.ker (π : B →+* E)) := by
    rw [Ideal.FG.isNilpotent_iff_le_nilradical (IsNoetherian.noetherian _)]
    intro c hc
    exact mem_nilradical.mpr (hπ c (by simpa using hc))
  set I := RingHom.ker (π : B →+* E) with hIdef
  set N := J * e + 1 with hN
  have hN0 : N ≠ 0 := by omega
  haveI : Module.Finite E (AdjoinRoot ((X : E[X]) ^ N)) :=
    (AdjoinRoot.powerBasis' (monic_X_pow N)).finite
  haveI : Module.Finite B (AdjoinRoot ((X : B[X]) ^ N)) :=
    (AdjoinRoot.powerBasis' (monic_X_pow N)).finite
  haveI : Module.Finite E (AdjoinRoot ((X : B[X]) ^ N)) := Module.Finite.trans B _
  have hrootE : (AdjoinRoot.root ((X : E[X]) ^ N)) ^ N = 0 := by
    rw [← AdjoinRoot.mk_X, ← map_pow, AdjoinRoot.mk_self]
  have hrootB : (AdjoinRoot.root ((X : B[X]) ^ N)) ^ N = 0 := by
    rw [← AdjoinRoot.mk_X, ← map_pow, AdjoinRoot.mk_self]
  let πC : AdjoinRoot ((X : E[X]) ^ N) →ₐ[E] E :=
    AdjoinRoot.liftAlgHom _ (AlgHom.id E E) 0 (by simp [hN0])
  let p1 : AdjoinRoot ((X : B[X]) ^ N) →ₐ[E] B :=
    AdjoinRoot.liftAlgHom _ (AlgHom.id E B) 0 (by simp [hN0])
  let p2 : AdjoinRoot ((X : B[X]) ^ N) →ₐ[E] AdjoinRoot ((X : E[X]) ^ N) :=
    AdjoinRoot.liftAlgHom _ ((Algebra.ofId E _).comp π) (AdjoinRoot.root _)
      (by rw [eval₂_X_pow]; exact hrootE)
  have hp1 : ∀ q : B[X], p1 (AdjoinRoot.mk _ q) = q.coeff 0 := by
    intro q
    simp [p1, AdjoinRoot.liftAlgHom_mk, eval₂_at_zero]
  have hp1of : ∀ b : B, p1 (AdjoinRoot.of _ b) = b := fun b =>
    AdjoinRoot.liftAlgHom_of _ _ _ _ _
  have hπC : ∀ q : E[X], πC (AdjoinRoot.mk _ q) = q.coeff 0 := by
    intro q
    simp [πC, AdjoinRoot.liftAlgHom_mk, eval₂_at_zero]
  have hp2 : ∀ q : B[X], p2 (AdjoinRoot.mk _ q) = AdjoinRoot.mk _ (q.map (π : B →+* E)) := by
    intro q
    rw [AdjoinRoot.liftAlgHom_mk, ← AdjoinRoot.aeval_eq, aeval_def, eval₂_map]
    rfl
  have hp2root : p2 (AdjoinRoot.root _) = AdjoinRoot.root _ := AdjoinRoot.liftAlgHom_root _ _ _ _
  have hp2map : ∀ q : E[X], p2 (AdjoinRoot.mk _ (q.map (algebraMap E B))) = AdjoinRoot.mk _ q := by
    intro q
    rw [hp2, Polynomial.map_map]
    congr 1
    conv_rhs => rw [← Polynomial.map_id (p := q)]
    congr 1
    ext r
    simp
  have hnilT : ∀ a, π (p1 a) = 0 → IsNilpotent a := by
    intro a ha
    obtain ⟨q, rfl⟩ := AdjoinRoot.mk_surjective a
    rw [hp1] at ha
    obtain ⟨r, hr⟩ : X ∣ q - C (q.coeff 0) := X_dvd_iff.mpr (by simp)
    have hq : q = C (q.coeff 0) + X * r := by rw [← hr]; ring
    rw [hq, map_add, map_mul, AdjoinRoot.mk_X, AdjoinRoot.mk_C]
    exact Commute.isNilpotent_add (Commute.all _ _) ((hπ _ ha).map _)
      ⟨N, by rw [mul_pow, hrootB, zero_mul]⟩
  have hnilC : ∀ a, πC a = 0 → IsNilpotent a := by
    intro a ha
    obtain ⟨q, rfl⟩ := AdjoinRoot.mk_surjective a
    rw [hπC] at ha
    obtain ⟨r, rfl⟩ := X_dvd_iff.mpr ha
    exact ⟨N, by rw [map_mul, AdjoinRoot.mk_X, mul_pow, hrootE, zero_mul]⟩
  have hcomm : π.comp p1 = πC.comp p2 := by
    refine AlgHom.ext fun a => ?_
    obtain ⟨q, rfl⟩ := AdjoinRoot.mk_surjective a
    simp only [AlgHom.comp_apply, hp1, hp2, hπC, coeff_map]
    rfl
  have hsurj : ∀ b c, π b = πC c → ∃ s, p1 s = b ∧ p2 s = c := by
    intro b c hbc
    obtain ⟨q, rfl⟩ := AdjoinRoot.mk_surjective c
    rw [hπC] at hbc
    refine ⟨AdjoinRoot.of _ b + AdjoinRoot.mk _ (q.map (algebraMap E B)) -
      AdjoinRoot.of _ (algebraMap E B (q.coeff 0)), ?_, ?_⟩
    · rw [map_sub, map_add, ← AdjoinRoot.mk_C, ← AdjoinRoot.mk_C, hp1, hp1, hp1]
      simp
    · rw [map_sub, map_add, hp2map, ← AdjoinRoot.mk_C, ← AdjoinRoot.mk_C, hp2, hp2]
      simp [hbc]
  have hker : ∀ Q, p2 Q = 0 → Q ∈ I.map (AdjoinRoot.of ((X : B[X]) ^ N)) := by
    intro Q hQ
    obtain ⟨q, rfl⟩ := AdjoinRoot.mk_surjective Q
    rw [hp2, AdjoinRoot.mk_eq_zero] at hQ
    have hmod : (q %ₘ (X ^ N)).map (π : B →+* E) = 0 := by
      rw [map_modByMonic _ (monic_X_pow N), Polynomial.map_pow, map_X]
      exact (modByMonic_eq_zero_iff_dvd (monic_X_pow N)).mpr hQ
    have hmk : AdjoinRoot.mk ((X : B[X]) ^ N) q = AdjoinRoot.mk _ (q %ₘ (X ^ N)) := by
      conv_lhs => rw [← modByMonic_add_div q (X ^ N)]
      rw [map_add, map_mul, AdjoinRoot.mk_self, zero_mul, add_zero]
    rw [hmk]
    have hin : q %ₘ (X ^ N) ∈ I.map (C : B →+* B[X]) := by
      rw [Ideal.mem_map_C_iff]
      intro n
      rw [hIdef, RingHom.mem_ker]
      have := congrArg (fun p => p.coeff n) hmod
      simpa using this
    have := Ideal.mem_map_of_mem (AdjoinRoot.mk ((X : B[X]) ^ N)) hin
    rw [Ideal.map_map] at this
    exact this
  refine ⟨AdjoinRoot ((X : E[X]) ^ N), inferInstance, inferInstance, inferInstance,
    AdjoinRoot ((X : B[X]) ^ N), inferInstance, inferInstance, inferInstance, πC, p1, p2,
    AdjoinRoot.root _, ⟨N, hrootE⟩, hnilC, hnilT, hcomm, hsurj, ?_⟩
  intro P y j v hj hP hPy
  obtain ⟨qv, hqv⟩ := AdjoinRoot.mk_surjective (v : AdjoinRoot ((X : E[X]) ^ N))
  set V := AdjoinRoot.mk ((X : B[X]) ^ N) (qv.map (algebraMap E B)) with hVdef
  have hV : p2 V = v := by rw [hVdef, hp2map, hqv]
  have hVu : IsUnit V := by
    have hw : π (p1 V) ≠ 0 := by
      rw [← AlgHom.comp_apply, hcomm, AlgHom.comp_apply, hV]
      exact (v.isUnit.map πC).ne_zero
    have h1 : IsNilpotent (V - AdjoinRoot.of _ (algebraMap E B (π (p1 V)))) := by
      apply hnilT
      rw [map_sub, map_sub, hp1of, AlgHom.commutes]
      simp
    have h2 : IsUnit (AdjoinRoot.of ((X : B[X]) ^ N) (algebraMap E B (π (p1 V)))) :=
      ((Ne.isUnit hw).map (algebraMap E B)).map _
    have := h1.isUnit_add_left_of_commute h2 (Commute.all _ _)
    simpa using this
  obtain ⟨Vi, hVi⟩ : ∃ Vi, V * Vi = 1 := hVu.exists_right_inv
  set Q := P - AdjoinRoot.root ((X : B[X]) ^ N) ^ j * V with hQdef
  have hQ2 : p2 Q = 0 := by
    rw [hQdef, map_sub, map_mul, map_pow, hV, hP, hp2root, sub_self]
  set K := I.map (AdjoinRoot.of ((X : B[X]) ^ N)) with hK
  have hQK : Q ∈ K := hker Q hQ2
  set R := -(Q * Vi) with hR
  have hRK : R ∈ K := K.neg_mem (K.mul_mem_right _ hQK)
  have hxR : AdjoinRoot.root ((X : B[X]) ^ N) ^ j * y = R * y := by
    rw [hR]
    linear_combination (-(AdjoinRoot.root ((X : B[X]) ^ N) ^ j * y)) * hVi + Vi * hPy +
      (Vi * y) * hQdef
  have hpow : ∀ k : ℕ, AdjoinRoot.root ((X : B[X]) ^ N) ^ (j * k) * y = R ^ k * y := by
    intro k
    induction k with
    | zero => simp
    | succ k ih =>
      have hsplit : AdjoinRoot.root ((X : B[X]) ^ N) ^ (j * (k + 1)) =
          AdjoinRoot.root ((X : B[X]) ^ N) ^ (j * k) * AdjoinRoot.root ((X : B[X]) ^ N) ^ j := by
        rw [← pow_add]
        congr 1
      calc AdjoinRoot.root ((X : B[X]) ^ N) ^ (j * (k + 1)) * y
          = AdjoinRoot.root ((X : B[X]) ^ N) ^ (j * k) *
              (AdjoinRoot.root ((X : B[X]) ^ N) ^ j * y) := by
            rw [hsplit, mul_assoc]
        _ = R * (AdjoinRoot.root ((X : B[X]) ^ N) ^ (j * k) * y) := by rw [hxR]; ring
        _ = R ^ (k + 1) * y := by rw [ih]; ring
  have hRe : R ^ e = 0 := by
    have h3 : R ^ e ∈ K ^ e := Ideal.pow_mem_pow hRK e
    rw [hK, ← Ideal.map_pow, he, Ideal.zero_eq_bot, Ideal.map_bot] at h3
    exact (Ideal.mem_bot).1 h3
  have hy0 : AdjoinRoot.root ((X : B[X]) ^ N) ^ (j * e) * y = 0 := by
    rw [hpow, hRe, zero_mul]
  have hlt : j * e < N := by
    have := Nat.mul_le_mul_right e hj
    omega
  obtain ⟨qy, rfl⟩ := AdjoinRoot.mk_surjective y
  rw [hp1]
  rw [← AdjoinRoot.mk_X, ← map_pow, ← map_mul, AdjoinRoot.mk_eq_zero] at hy0
  obtain ⟨s, hs⟩ := hy0
  have h4 := congrArg (fun p => p.coeff (j * e)) hs
  simp only [coeff_X_pow_mul'] at h4
  simpa [Nat.not_le.mpr hlt] using h4


[OCH_SRC_TWIST_SATURATION] Unramified formal twist makes del a power t^j times a unit with j<=2

theorem isUnit_algebraMap_of_ne_zero {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsDomain O]
    [Algebra ℤ_[l] O] [Module.Finite ℤ_[l] O] (C : Type) [CommRing C] [Algebra ℚ_[l] C] [Algebra O C]
    [Algebra ℤ_[l] C] [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] O C] [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] C] {c : O} (hc : c ≠ 0) :
    IsUnit (algebraMap O C c) := by
  have hI : (Ideal.span {c}).comap (algebraMap ℤ_[l] O) ≠ ⊥ :=
    Ideal.comap_ne_bot_of_integral_mem hc (Ideal.mem_span_singleton_self c) (Algebra.IsIntegral.isIntegral c)
  obtain ⟨n, hn, hn0⟩ := Submodule.exists_mem_ne_zero_of_ne_bot hI
  rw [Ideal.mem_comap, Ideal.mem_span_singleton] at hn
  obtain ⟨d, hd⟩ := hn
  have hq : algebraMap ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] n ≠ 0 := by
    first
      | exact (map_ne_zero_iff _ (IsFractionRing.injective ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l])).mpr hn0
      | exact (map_ne_zero_iff _ (FaithfulSMul.algebraMap_injective ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l])).mpr hn0
  have hu : IsUnit (algebraMap ℤ_[l] C n) := by
    rw [IsScalarTower.algebraMap_apply ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] C]
    exact (isUnit_iff_ne_zero.mpr hq).map (algebraMap ℚ_[l] C)
  rw [IsScalarTower.algebraMap_apply ℤ_[l] O C, hd, map_mul] at hu
  exact isUnit_of_mul_isUnit_left hu

theorem isUnit_add_nil {C : Type} [CommRing C] {a n : C} (ha : IsUnit a) (hn : IsNilpotent n) : IsUnit (a + n) := by
  first
    | exact hn.isUnit_add_left_of_commute ha (Commute.all _ _)
    | exact IsNilpotent.isUnit_add_left_of_commute hn ha (Commute.all _ _)

theorem nil_mul {C : Type} [CommRing C] {t : C} (ht : IsNilpotent t) (z : C) : IsNilpotent (t * z) := by
  first
    | exact (Commute.all _ _).isNilpotent_mul_left ht
    | exact (Commute.all _ _).isNilpotent_mul_right ht
    | exact ht.mul_right (Commute.all _ _)

theorem prod_eq {C : Type} [CommRing C] (L K0 K1 ε t w a : C) (y : Cˣ) (hy : (y : C) = a * (1 + t * w)) :
    L * (K0 * ((y : C) - ε)) * (K1 * (1 - ε * ((y⁻¹ : Cˣ) : C))) =
      (L * K0 * K1 * ((y⁻¹ : Cˣ) : C)) * ((a - ε) + t * (a * w)) ^ 2 := by
  have h := y.mul_inv
  rw [hy] at h ⊢
  linear_combination (-(L * K0 * K1 * (a * (1 + t * w) - ε))) * h

end GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.NilpotentTwist

theorem GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.del_unr_twist {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsDomain O]
    [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [CharZero O] [Algebra ℤ_[l] O] [Module.Finite ℤ_[l] O]
    [IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal O) O] (D : Dat3 l O) (E : Type) [Field E] [Algebra ℚ_[l] E]
    [Module.Finite ℚ_[l] E] [Algebra O E] [Algebra ℤ_[l] E] [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] O E] [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] E]
    (C : Type) [CommRing C] [Algebra ℚ_[l] C] [Module.Finite ℚ_[l] C] [Algebra O C] [Algebra ℤ_[l] C]
    [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] O C] [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] C] [Algebra E C] [IsScalarTower O E C]
    [IsScalarTower ℚ_[l] E C] (t : C) (ht : IsNilpotent t) :
    ∃ u : GQ l →* Cˣ, QCont l (fun g => (u g : C)) ∧ (∀ g ∈ Iq l, u g = 1) ∧ (∀ g, IsNilpotent ((u g : C) - 1)) ∧
      ∃ (j : ℕ) (v : Cˣ), j ≤ 2 ∧
        GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.del D (r0 D.η ((Units.map (algebraMap O C : O →* C)).comp D.Ψ * u)) = t ^ j * v := by
  obtain ⟨-, σ₀, m, hF, hm, hI⟩ := GlobalTheory.T.chosenFrobenius_isFrobenius D
  obtain ⟨-, -, -, -, hs0⟩ := GlobalTheory.T.galoisSubgroupL_basic_properties D
  obtain ⟨u, hcont, huI, hunil, v, hv⟩ := GlobalTheory.unr_unip_char_frob t ht σ₀ hF
  refine ⟨u, hcont, huI, hunil, ?_⟩
  obtain ⟨b, hb⟩ := hv m
  obtain ⟨-, hcc⟩ := GlobalTheory.T.isUnit_characterDiff_at_chosenInertia D C
  have huσ : u (fr D) = u (σ₀ ^ m) := by
    calc u (fr D) = u (σ₀ ^ m * ((σ₀ ^ m)⁻¹ * fr D)) := by rw [mul_inv_cancel_left]
      _ = u (σ₀ ^ m) := by rw [map_mul, huI _ hI, mul_one]
  have hmat : ∀ g, (r0 D.η ((Units.map (algebraMap O C : O →* C)).comp D.Ψ * u) g).mat =
      Matrix.diagonal (fun i => ((((Units.map (algebraMap O C : O →* C)).comp D.Ψ g * u g) ^ wt1 i : Cˣ) : C)) :=
    fun g => rfl
  have hdiag : (r0 D.η ((Units.map (algebraMap O C : O →* C)).comp D.Ψ * u) (s0 D)).mat =
      Matrix.diagonal (fun a => algebraMap O C (UpperTheory.V.UnitaryThreeInertia.psi D.Ψ a (s0 D))) := by
    rw [hmat, huI _ hs0.2.1, mul_one]
    congr 1
    funext a
    rw [MonoidHom.comp_apply, ← map_zpow, Units.coe_map]
    rfl
  have h0 := GlobalTheory.T.ordinary_derivative_entry_formula D _ hdiag 0
  have h1 := GlobalTheory.T.ordinary_derivative_entry_formula D _ hdiag 1
  rw [hmat, Matrix.diagonal_apply_eq, Matrix.diagonal_apply_eq] at h0 h1
  have e01 : (0 : Fin 3) + 1 = 1 := rfl
  have e12 : (1 : Fin 3) + 1 = 2 := rfl
  have w0 : wt1 (0 : Fin 3) = 1 := rfl
  have w1 : wt1 (1 : Fin 3) = 0 := rfl
  have w2 : wt1 (2 : Fin 3) = -1 := rfl
  rw [e01, w0, w1, zpow_one, zpow_zero, Units.val_one, mul_one] at h0
  rw [e12, w1, w2, zpow_zero, zpow_neg_one, Units.val_one] at h1
  have hyv : (((Units.map (algebraMap O C : O →* C)).comp D.Ψ (fr D) * u (fr D) : Cˣ) : C) =
      algebraMap O C (D.Ψ (fr D) : O) * (1 + t * ((m : C) * v + t * b)) := by
    rw [Units.val_mul, MonoidHom.comp_apply, Units.coe_map, MonoidHom.coe_coe, huσ, ← hb]
    ring
  have hl : IsUnit (l : C) := by
    have : IsUnit ((l : ℚ_[l])) := isUnit_iff_ne_zero.mpr (by exact_mod_cast (Fact.out : l.Prime).ne_zero)
    simpa using this.map (algebraMap ℚ_[l] C)
  have hmu : IsUnit (m : C) := by
    have : IsUnit ((m : ℚ_[l])) := isUnit_iff_ne_zero.mpr (by exact_mod_cast (show m ≠ 0 by omega))
    simpa using this.map (algebraMap ℚ_[l] C)
  have hk0 : IsUnit (algebraMap O C (cc D 0 * cc D 1)) := by rw [map_mul]; exact (hcc 0).mul (hcc 1)
  have hk1 : IsUnit (algebraMap O C (cc D 1 * cc D 2)) := by rw [map_mul]; exact (hcc 1).mul (hcc 2)
  have hw : IsUnit ((m : C) * v + t * b) := isUnit_add_nil (hmu.mul (Units.isUnit v)) (nil_mul ht b)
  have ha : IsUnit (algebraMap O C (D.Ψ (fr D) : O)) := (Units.isUnit (D.Ψ (fr D))).map (algebraMap O C)
  unfold GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.del
  rw [h0, h1, prod_eq _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ hyv, ← map_sub]
  have hQ : IsUnit ((l : C) * algebraMap O C (cc D 0 * cc D 1) * algebraMap O C (cc D 1 * cc D 2) *
      ((((Units.map (algebraMap O C : O →* C)).comp D.Ψ (fr D) * u (fr D))⁻¹ : Cˣ) : C)) :=
    ((hl.mul hk0).mul hk1).mul (Units.isUnit _)
  by_cases hc : (D.Ψ (fr D) : O) - ((cycA l l O (fr D) : Oˣ) : O) = 0
  · rw [hc, map_zero, zero_add]
    refine ⟨2, (hQ.mul ((ha.mul hw).pow 2)).unit, le_rfl, ?_⟩
    rw [IsUnit.unit_spec]
    ring
  · have hn : IsUnit (algebraMap O C ((D.Ψ (fr D) : O) - ((cycA l l O (fr D) : Oˣ) : O)) +
        t * (algebraMap O C (D.Ψ (fr D) : O) * ((m : C) * v + t * b))) :=
      isUnit_add_nil (isUnit_algebraMap_of_ne_zero (l := l) C hc) (nil_mul ht _)
    refine ⟨0, (hQ.mul (hn.pow 2)).unit, by norm_num, ?_⟩
    rw [IsUnit.unit_spec, pow_zero, one_mul]


[OCH_SRC_ARTIN_RESIDUE] Original Artin coefficient/reduction field scope of the reverse crystalline implication

import Mathlib

variable {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [CharZero O]
  [Algebra ℤ_[l] O] [Module.Finite ℤ_[l] O] [IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal O) O] (D : Dat3 l O) (U : D.Univ)

def sub (E₀ E B : Type) [Field E₀] [Field E] [Algebra E₀ E] [CommRing B] [Algebra E B] [Algebra E₀ B]
    [IsScalarTower E₀ E B] (π : B →ₐ[E] E) : Subalgebra E₀ B where
  carrier := {b | π b ∈ Set.range (algebraMap E₀ E)}
  mul_mem' := by
    rintro a b ⟨x, hx⟩ ⟨y, hy⟩
    exact ⟨x * y, by rw [map_mul, hx, hy, map_mul]⟩
  add_mem' := by
    rintro a b ⟨x, hx⟩ ⟨y, hy⟩
    exact ⟨x + y, by rw [map_add, hx, hy, map_add]⟩
  algebraMap_mem' c := ⟨c, by rw [IsScalarTower.algebraMap_apply E₀ E B, π.commutes]; rfl⟩

omit [CharZero O] [Module.Finite ℤ_[l] O] [IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal O) O] in
theorem push_base {x : U.R →ₐ[O] O} (hx : IsBase D.η D.Ψ U.r.hom x) (F : Type) [Field F] [Algebra O F] :
    push ((Algebra.ofId O F).comp x) U.r.hom = D.rz F := by
  unfold Dat3.rz
  rw [← show push x U.r.hom = r0 D.η D.Ψ from hx]
  rfl

theorem reduce (hP : LocalPublishedTheorems)
    (E : Type) [Field E] [Algebra ℚ_[l] E] [Module.Finite ℚ_[l] E] [Algebra O E] [Algebra ℤ_[l] E]
    [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] O E] [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] E]
    (B : Type) [CommRing B] [Algebra ℚ_[l] B] [Module.Finite ℚ_[l] B] [Algebra O B] [Algebra ℤ_[l] B]
    [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] O B] [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] B] [Algebra E B] [IsScalarTower O E B]
    [IsScalarTower ℚ_[l] E B] (π : B →ₐ[E] E) (hπ : ∀ a, π a = 0 → IsNilpotent a) (f : U.R →ₐ[O] B)
    (hD : D.InDcr (π : B →+* E) (push f U.r.hom))
    (E₀ : Type) [Field E₀] [Algebra ℚ_[l] E₀] [Module.Finite ℚ_[l] E₀] [Algebra O E₀] [Algebra ℤ_[l] E₀]
    [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] O E₀] [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] E₀] [IsFractionRing O E₀]
    [Algebra E₀ E] [IsScalarTower O E₀ E] [Algebra E₀ B] [IsScalarTower E₀ E B]
    [IsScalarTower O E₀ B] [IsScalarTower ℚ_[l] E₀ B] [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] E₀ B] :
    IY D U ≤ RingHom.ker f := by
  classical
  obtain ⟨x, hx, -⟩ := univEx D U
  have hfx : ∀ a, π (f a) = algebraMap O E (x a) := by
    intro a
    let y : U.R →ₐ[O] E := (π.restrictScalars O).comp f
    obtain ⟨g, -, hu⟩ := unramified_lift_deformation D U E E (AlgHom.id E E) (fun a ha => by simpa using ha) y (push y U.r.hom)
      (fun g => U.r.hasMult g) rfl
      (fun a b => (ptCont (l := l) U.R E y).1 (GQ l) (fun g => (U.r.hom g).mat a b) (U.r.cont a b))
    have h := (hu y rfl).trans (hu ((Algebra.ofId O E).comp x) ((push_base D U hx E).trans hD.red.symm)).symm
    exact congrArg (fun φ : U.R →ₐ[O] E => φ a) h
  have hinj : Function.Injective (algebraMap E₀ E) := (algebraMap E₀ E).injective
  let B₀ := sub E₀ E B π
  have hmem : ∀ a, f a ∈ B₀ := fun a =>
    ⟨algebraMap O E₀ (x a), by rw [hfx, IsScalarTower.algebraMap_apply O E₀ E]⟩
  let f₀ : U.R →ₐ[O] B₀ :=
    { toFun := fun a => ⟨f a, hmem a⟩
      map_one' := Subtype.ext (map_one f)
      map_mul' := fun a b => Subtype.ext (map_mul f a b)
      map_zero' := Subtype.ext (map_zero f)
      map_add' := fun a b => Subtype.ext (map_add f a b)
      commutes' := fun c => Subtype.ext (f.commutes c) }
  have hb : ∀ b : B₀, ∃ y, algebraMap E₀ E y = π (b : B) := fun b => b.2
  choose p hp using hb
  let π₀ : B₀ →ₐ[E₀] E₀ :=
    { toFun := p
      map_one' := hinj (by rw [hp]; simp)
      map_mul' := fun a b => hinj (by rw [hp, map_mul, hp, hp]; simp)
      map_zero' := hinj (by rw [hp]; simp)
      map_add' := fun a b => hinj (by rw [hp, map_add, hp, hp]; simp)
      commutes' := fun c => hinj (by
        rw [hp]
        show π (algebraMap E₀ B c) = algebraMap E₀ E (algebraMap E₀ E₀ c)
        rw [IsScalarTower.algebraMap_apply E₀ E B, π.commutes]
        rfl) }
  have hπ₀ : ∀ a, π₀ a = 0 → IsNilpotent a := by
    intro a ha
    have h0 : π (a : B) = 0 := by
      rw [← hp]
      change algebraMap E₀ E (π₀ a) = 0
      rw [ha, map_zero]
    obtain ⟨n, hn⟩ := hπ _ h0
    exact ⟨n, Subtype.ext (by simpa using hn)⟩
  let val' : B₀ →ₐ[ℚ_[l]] B :=
    { toFun := Subtype.val
      map_one' := rfl
      map_mul' := fun _ _ => rfl
      map_zero' := rfl
      map_add' := fun _ _ => rfl
      commutes' := fun _ => rfl }
  haveI : Module.Finite ℚ_[l] B₀ := Module.Finite.of_injective val'.toLinearMap Subtype.val_injective
  have hD' : D.InDcrAt (π : B →+* E) (D.rz E) ((GG.map (val' : B₀ →+* B)).comp (push f₀ U.r.hom)) := hD
  have hq := OrdinaryDeformation.dcr_pair val' val' (fun a b h _ => Subtype.ext h) hD' hD'
  have h1 : (π₀.restrictScalars O).comp f₀ = (Algebra.ofId O E₀).comp x := AlgHom.ext fun a => hinj (by
    change algebraMap E₀ E (p (f₀ a)) = algebraMap E₀ E (algebraMap O E₀ (x a))
    rw [hp, ← IsScalarTower.algebraMap_apply O E₀ E]
    exact hfx a)
  have hred : (GG.map (π₀ : B₀ →+* E₀)).comp (push f₀ U.r.hom) = D.rz E₀ := by
    rw [← push_base D U hx E₀, ← h1]
    rfl
  have hD₀ : D.InDcr (π₀ : B₀ →+* E₀) (push f₀ U.r.hom) := ⟨hq.mult, hred, hq.cont, hq.cr⟩
  intro a ha
  have h2 := GlobalTheory.T.crystallineDeformation_points_saturatedOrdinaryIdeal_fracO hP D U E₀ B₀ π₀ hπ₀ f₀ hD₀ ha
  rw [RingHom.mem_ker] at h2 ⊢
  exact congrArg Subtype.val h2

end GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.CriticalPoints

open GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.CriticalPoints in

theorem GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.crPts_IY : LocalPublishedTheorems → ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [CharZero O] [Algebra ℤ_[l] O] [Module.Finite ℤ_[l] O] [IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal O) O] (D : Dat3 l O) (U : D.Univ), GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.CrPts D U (GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.IY D U) := by
  intro hP l _ O _ _ _ _ _ _ _ D U
  intro E _ _ _ _ _ _ _ B _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ π hπ f hD
  have hi : Function.Injective (algebraMap ℤ_[l] O) := by
    rw [RingHom.injective_iff_ker_eq_bot]
    by_contra h
    have hp : (Ideal.comap (algebraMap ℤ_[l] O) ⊥).IsPrime := inferInstance
    have hm : (Ideal.comap (algebraMap ℤ_[l] O) ⊥).IsMaximal := hp.isMaximal h
    have h2 : (⊥ : Ideal O).IsMaximal := Ideal.isMaximal_of_isIntegral_of_isMaximal_comap (R := ℤ_[l]) _ hm
    exact IsDiscreteValuationRing.not_a_field O (IsLocalRing.eq_maximalIdeal h2).symm
  haveI : FaithfulSMul ℤ_[l] O := (faithfulSMul_iff_algebraMap_injective _ _).2 hi
  have hi2 : Function.Injective (algebraMap ℤ_[l] (FractionRing O)) := by
    rw [IsScalarTower.algebraMap_eq ℤ_[l] O (FractionRing O)]
    exact (IsFractionRing.injective O _).comp hi
  letI : Algebra ℚ_[l] (FractionRing O) := (IsFractionRing.lift hi2).toAlgebra
  haveI : IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] (FractionRing O) :=
    IsScalarTower.of_algebraMap_eq fun x => (IsFractionRing.lift_algebraMap hi2 x).symm
  haveI : IsLocalization (Algebra.algebraMapSubmonoid O (nonZeroDivisors ℤ_[l])) (FractionRing O) := inferInstance
  haveI : Module.Finite ℚ_[l] (FractionRing O) :=
    Module.Finite.of_isLocalization ℤ_[l] O (nonZeroDivisors ℤ_[l])
  have hOE : Function.Injective (algebraMap O E) := by
    rw [RingHom.injective_iff_ker_eq_bot]
    refine Ideal.eq_bot_of_comap_eq_bot (R := ℤ_[l]) ?_
    rw [RingHom.comap_ker, ← IsScalarTower.algebraMap_eq, ← RingHom.injective_iff_ker_eq_bot,
      IsScalarTower.algebraMap_eq ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] E]
    exact (algebraMap ℚ_[l] E).injective.comp (IsFractionRing.injective ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l])
  letI : Algebra (FractionRing O) E := (IsFractionRing.lift hOE).toAlgebra
  haveI : IsScalarTower O (FractionRing O) E :=
    IsScalarTower.of_algebraMap_eq fun c => (IsFractionRing.lift_algebraMap hOE c).symm
  letI : Algebra (FractionRing O) B := ((algebraMap E B).comp (algebraMap (FractionRing O) E)).toAlgebra
  haveI : IsScalarTower (FractionRing O) E B := IsScalarTower.of_algebraMap_eq' rfl
  haveI : IsScalarTower O (FractionRing O) B := IsScalarTower.of_algebraMap_eq fun c => by
    change _ = algebraMap E B (algebraMap (FractionRing O) E (algebraMap O (FractionRing O) c))
    rw [← IsScalarTower.algebraMap_apply O (FractionRing O) E, ← IsScalarTower.algebraMap_apply O E B]
  have hQ : (algebraMap (FractionRing O) E).comp (algebraMap ℚ_[l] (FractionRing O)) = algebraMap ℚ_[l] E :=
    IsLocalization.ringHom_ext (nonZeroDivisors ℤ_[l]) (RingHom.ext fun z => by
      simp only [RingHom.comp_apply]
      rw [← IsScalarTower.algebraMap_apply ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] (FractionRing O),
        IsScalarTower.algebraMap_apply ℤ_[l] O (FractionRing O),
        ← IsScalarTower.algebraMap_apply O (FractionRing O) E, ← IsScalarTower.algebraMap_apply ℤ_[l] O E,
        IsScalarTower.algebraMap_apply ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] E])
  haveI : IsScalarTower ℚ_[l] (FractionRing O) B := IsScalarTower.of_algebraMap_eq fun q => by
    rw [IsScalarTower.algebraMap_apply ℚ_[l] E B, ← hQ]
    rfl
  haveI : IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] (FractionRing O) B := IsScalarTower.of_algebraMap_eq fun z => by
    change _ = algebraMap E B (algebraMap (FractionRing O) E (algebraMap ℤ_[l] (FractionRing O) z))
    rw [IsScalarTower.algebraMap_apply ℤ_[l] O (FractionRing O), ← IsScalarTower.algebraMap_apply O (FractionRing O) E,
      ← IsScalarTower.algebraMap_apply O E B, ← IsScalarTower.algebraMap_apply ℤ_[l] O B]
  exact reduce D U hP E B π hπ f hD (FractionRing O)

[OCH_SRC_LOCAL_PRIME] The unique minimal prime through the base and its integral contraction
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/ModularityArgument/CrystallineOrdinaryIdeals/OrdinaryLocusIdealLeKerOfIsBasePoint.lean#L1-L117
import Mathlib
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.ModularityArgument.CrystallineOrdinaryIdeals.Imports
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.ModularityArgument.CrystallineOrdinaryIdeals.SaturatedOrdinaryIdealCutsOutCrystallineLocusStatement
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.PadicHodgeTheory.UnramifiedLiftDeformation
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.PadicHodgeTheory.CrLift
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.PadicHodgeTheory.SmoothCompl
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.PadicHodgeTheory.IsTypeAndIsLocallyCrystallineRz
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.PadicHodgeTheory.PtCont
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.PadicHodgeTheory.UnivEx
open Formalism.BasicNotions Formalism.BasicNotions.Loc ReciprocityTheory.Gal Module UpperTheory.V.Loc UpperTheory.V.U3 UpperTheory.V.Alg IsLocalRing Polynomial Topology

namespace GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.SmoothPushforward

variable {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [CharZero O]
  [Algebra ℤ_[l] O] [Module.Finite ℤ_[l] O] [IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal O) O] (D : Dat3 l O) (U : D.Univ)
  (E : Type) [Field E] [Algebra ℚ_[l] E] [Module.Finite ℚ_[l] E] [Algebra O E] [Algebra ℤ_[l] E]
  [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] O E] [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] E]
  (x : U.R →ₐ[O] O) (hx : IsBase D.η D.Ψ U.r.hom x) (J : Ideal U.R)
  (hPC : GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.PtsCr D U J) (hCP : GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.CrPts D U J)

include hx in
theorem push_base : push ((Algebra.ofId O E).comp x) U.r.hom = D.rz E := by
  unfold Dat3.rz
  rw [← (show push x U.r.hom = r0 D.η D.Ψ from hx)]
  rfl

section Pro

variable {B : Type} [CommRing B] [Algebra ℚ_[l] B] [Module.Finite ℚ_[l] B] [Algebra O B] [Algebra ℤ_[l] B]
  [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] O B] [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] B]

theorem push_cont (f : U.R →ₐ[O] B) (a b : Fin 3) : QCont l fun g => (push f U.r.hom g).mat a b :=
  (ptCont U.R B f).1 _ _ (U.r.cont a b)

variable [Algebra E B] [IsScalarTower O E B] [IsScalarTower ℚ_[l] E B] (π : B →ₐ[E] E)

include hx in
theorem push_red (f : U.R →ₐ[O] B) (hf : ∀ a, π (f a) = algebraMap O E (x a)) :
    (GG.map (π : B →+* E)).comp (push f U.r.hom) = D.rz E := by
  rw [← push_base D U E x hx]
  refine MonoidHom.ext fun g => SemidirectProduct.ext (Units.ext ?_) rfl
  ext a b
  exact hf _

include hx hPC in
theorem inDcr_push (f : U.R →ₐ[O] B) (hf : ∀ a, π (f a) = algebraMap O E (x a))
    (hI : J ≤ RingHom.ker f) : D.InDcr (π : B →+* E) (push f U.r.hom) where
  mult := U.r.hasMult
  red := push_red D U E x hx π f hf
  cont := push_cont D U f
  cr := hPC B f hI

variable (hπ : ∀ a, π a = 0 → IsNilpotent a)

include hx hπ in
theorem push_inj (f f' : U.R →ₐ[O] B) (hf : ∀ a, π (f a) = algebraMap O E (x a))
    (h : push f' U.r.hom = push f U.r.hom) : f' = f :=
  (unramified_lift_deformation D U E B π hπ ((Algebra.ofId O E).comp x) (push f U.r.hom) U.r.hasMult
    ((push_red D U E x hx π f hf).trans (push_base D U E x hx).symm) (push_cont D U f)).unique h rfl

include hx hCP hπ in
theorem exists_push (r : GQ l →* GG 3 B) (hr : D.InDcr (π : B →+* E) r) :
    ∃ f : U.R →ₐ[O] B, push f U.r.hom = r ∧ J ≤ RingHom.ker f ∧ ∀ a, π (f a) = algebraMap O E (x a) := by
  obtain ⟨f, hf, -⟩ := unramified_lift_deformation D U E B π hπ ((Algebra.ofId O E).comp x) r hr.mult
    (hr.red.trans (push_base D U E x hx).symm) hr.cont
  refine ⟨f, hf, hCP E B π hπ f (hf ▸ hr), fun a => ?_⟩
  have h := push_inj D U E x hx (AlgHom.id E E) (fun a h => by rw [show a = 0 from h]; exact IsNilpotent.zero)
    ((Algebra.ofId O E).comp x) ((π.restrictScalars O).comp f) (fun _ => rfl) (by
      rw [push_base D U E x hx, ← hr.red, ← hf]
      rfl)
  exact DFunLike.congr_fun h a

end Pro

include hx hCP in
theorem le_ker [IsFractionRing O E] : J ≤ RingHom.ker x := fun a ha => by
  have hr : D.InDcr ((AlgHom.id E E : E →ₐ[E] E) : E →+* E) (push ((Algebra.ofId O E).comp x) U.r.hom) :=
    { mult := U.r.hasMult
      red := push_red D U E x hx (AlgHom.id E E) _ (fun _ => rfl)
      cont := push_cont D U _
      cr := by rw [push_base D U E x hx]; exact (isType_and_isLocallyCrystalline_rz D E).2 }
  have h := hCP E E (AlgHom.id E E) (fun a h => by rw [show a = 0 from h]; exact IsNilpotent.zero) _ hr ha
  exact IsFractionRing.injective O E (h.trans (map_zero _).symm)

include hx hPC hCP in
theorem smooth (hL : LocalPublishedTheorems) [IsFractionRing O E] :
    ∃ (n : ℕ) (P : Ideal U.R) (φ : U.R →ₐ[O] MvPowerSeries (Fin n) E), UpperTheory.V.Loc.IsCompl P x φ ∧ J ≤ P ∧
      (∃ y, x y ≠ 0 ∧ ∀ a ∈ P, y * a ∈ J) ∧ (∀ Q ∈ J.radical.minimalPrimes, Q ≤ RingHom.ker x ↔ Q = P) ∧
      P ∈ J.radical.minimalPrimes ∧ ringKrullDim (U.R ⧸ P) = (n + 1 : ℕ) := by
  refine smoothCompl (l := l) E U.R J x (le_ker D U E x hx J hCP) ?_
  intro B _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ π hπ B' _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ π' hπ' p hp hpπ f' hI hf'
  obtain ⟨r, hr, hrp⟩ := crLift hL D E B π hπ B' π' hπ' p hp hpπ _ (inDcr_push D U E x hx J hPC π' f' hf' hI)
  obtain ⟨f, hf, hfI, -⟩ := exists_push D U E x hx J hCP π hπ r hr
  refine ⟨f, hfI, fun a => ?_⟩
  have h := push_inj D U E x hx π' hπ' f' ((p.restrictScalars O).comp f) hf' (by
    rw [← hrp, ← hf]
    rfl)
  exact DFunLike.congr_fun h a

end GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.SmoothPushforward

theorem GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.saturatedOrdinaryIdeal_smooth_at_residualRep : LocalPublishedTheorems → ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [CharZero O] [Algebra ℤ_[l] O] [Module.Finite ℤ_[l] O] [IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal O) O] (D : Dat3 l O) (U : D.Univ) (E : Type) [Field E] [Algebra ℚ_[l] E] [Module.Finite ℚ_[l] E] [Algebra O E] [Algebra ℤ_[l] E] [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] O E] [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] E] [IsFractionRing O E] (x : U.R →ₐ[O] O), IsBase D.η D.Ψ U.r.hom x → GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.IY D U ≤ RingHom.ker x ∧ ∃ (n : ℕ) (P : Ideal U.R) (φ : U.R →ₐ[O] MvPowerSeries (Fin n) E), UpperTheory.V.Loc.IsCompl P x φ ∧ GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.IY D U ≤ P ∧ (∃ y, x y ≠ 0 ∧ ∀ a ∈ P, y * a ∈ GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.IY D U) ∧ P = GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.It D U ∧ GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.primesAt0 D U = {P} ∧ ringKrullDim (U.R ⧸ P) = (n + 1 : ℕ) := by
  intro hL l _ O _ _ _ _ _ _ _ D U E _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ x hx
  obtain ⟨hPC, hCP⟩ := GlobalTheory.T.saturatedOrdinaryIdeal_cuts_out_crystalline_locus hL D U
  refine ⟨GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.SmoothPushforward.le_ker D U E x hx _ hCP, ?_⟩
  obtain ⟨n, P, φ, hφ, hIP, hy, hQ, hP, hdim⟩ := GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.SmoothPushforward.smooth D U E x hx _ hPC hCP hL
  have h0 : GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.primesAt0 D U = {P} := by
    ext Q
    constructor
    · rintro ⟨h1, h2⟩
      exact (hQ Q h1).1 (h2 x hx)
    · rintro rfl
      refine ⟨hP, fun x' hx' => ?_⟩
      rw [(univEx D U).unique hx' hx]
      exact (hQ _ hP).2 rfl
  have hIt : GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.It D U = P := by rw [GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.It, h0, sInf_singleton]
  exact ⟨n, P, φ, hφ, hIP, hy, hIt.symm, h0, hdim⟩


[OCH_SRC_LOCAL_COMPLETION] The original twelve-variable completed ordinary component
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/ModularityArgument/CrystallineOrdinaryIdeals/RtCompl.lean#L1-L39
import Mathlib
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.ModularityArgument.CrystallineOrdinaryIdeals.Imports
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.ModularityArgument.CrystallineOrdinaryIdeals.OrdinaryLocusIdealLeKerOfIsBasePointStatement
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.ModularityArgument.CrystallineOrdinaryIdeals.SaturatedOrdinaryIdealTangentFinrankEqTwelveStatement
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.PadicHodgeTheory.FinrankPointDerivationsVanishingEqOfIsCompl
open Formalism.BasicNotions Formalism.BasicNotions.Loc ReciprocityTheory.Gal Module UpperTheory.V.Loc UpperTheory.V.U3 UpperTheory.V.Alg IsLocalRing Polynomial Topology

theorem GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.rtCompl : LocalPublishedTheorems → ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [CharZero O] [Algebra ℤ_[l] O] [Module.Finite ℤ_[l] O] [IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal O) O] (D : Dat3 l O) (U : D.Univ) (E : Type) [Field E] [Algebra ℚ_[l] E] [Module.Finite ℚ_[l] E] [Algebra O E] [Algebra ℤ_[l] E] [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] O E] [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] E] [IsFractionRing O E] (x : U.R →ₐ[O] O), IsBase D.η D.Ψ U.r.hom x → GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.primesAt0 D U = {GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.It D U} ∧ ∃ φ : U.R →ₐ[O] MvPowerSeries (Fin 12) E, UpperTheory.V.Loc.IsCompl (GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.It D U) x φ := by
  intro hL l _ O _ _ _ _ _ _ _ D U E _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ x hx
  obtain ⟨-, n, P, φ, hφ, hIP, ⟨y, hy, hyP⟩, hIt, h0, -⟩ := GlobalTheory.T.exists_unique_ordinaryPrime_dim_succ_near_base hL D U E x hx
  have hPx : P ≤ RingHom.ker x := by
    have hm : P ∈ GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.primesAt0 D U := by rw [h0]; rfl
    exact hm.2 x hx
  let T : Submodule E (U.R →ₗ[O] E) :=
    { carrier := {δ | (∀ a b, δ (a * b) = algebraMap O E (x a) * δ b + algebraMap O E (x b) * δ a) ∧
        ∀ a ∈ GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.IY D U, δ a = 0}
      zero_mem' := ⟨by simp, by simp⟩
      add_mem' := by
        rintro δ δ' ⟨h1, h2⟩ ⟨h3, h4⟩
        refine ⟨fun a b => ?_, fun a ha => ?_⟩
        · simp only [LinearMap.add_apply, h1 a b, h3 a b]; ring
        · simp only [LinearMap.add_apply, h2 a ha, h4 a ha, add_zero]
      smul_mem' := by
        rintro c δ ⟨h1, h2⟩
        refine ⟨fun a b => ?_, fun a ha => ?_⟩
        · simp only [LinearMap.smul_apply, h1 a b, smul_eq_mul]; ring
        · simp only [LinearMap.smul_apply, h2 a ha, smul_zero] }
  have hT : ∀ δ, δ ∈ T ↔ (∀ a b, δ (a * b) = algebraMap O E (x a) * δ b + algebraMap O E (x b) * δ a) ∧
      ∀ a ∈ GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.IY D U, δ a = 0 := fun δ => Iff.rfl
  have h1 := GlobalTheory.T.saturatedOrdinaryIdeal_tangent_finrank_eq_twelve hL D U E x hx T hT
  have h2 : finrank E ↥T = n := UpperTheory.finrank_pointDerivations_vanishing_eq_of_isCompl n P x φ hφ T fun δ => by
    rw [hT]
    refine and_congr_right fun h => ⟨fun h' a ha => ?_, fun h' a ha => h' a (hIP ha)⟩
    have e := h y a
    rw [h' _ (hyP a ha), show x a = 0 from hPx ha, map_zero, zero_mul, add_zero] at e
    exact (mul_eq_zero.1 e.symm).resolve_left fun h0 =>
      hy (IsFractionRing.injective O E (h0.trans (map_zero _).symm))
  obtain rfl : n = 12 := by rw [← h2, h1]
  exact ⟨by rw [← hIt, h0], φ, hIt ▸ hφ⟩


[OCH_SRC_TANGENT_TWELVE] Exact tangent dimension twelve for the specified saturated ordinary ideal
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/ModularityArgument/CrystallineOrdinaryIdeals/SaturatedOrdinaryIdealTangentFinrankEqTwelve.lean#L1-L306
import Mathlib
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.ModularityArgument.CrystallineOrdinaryIdeals.Imports
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.ModularityArgument.CrystallineOrdinaryIdeals.SaturatedOrdinaryIdealCutsOutCrystallineLocusStatement
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.PadicHodgeTheory.UnramifiedLiftDeformation
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.PadicHodgeTheory.DualCr
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.PadicHodgeTheory.IsTypeAndIsLocallyCrystallineRz
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.PadicHodgeTheory.PtCont
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.PadicHodgeTheory.FinrankLocalCocyclesAdjointUnitaryThreeEqTwelve
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.UnitaryThreeGroups.DefCoc
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.UnitaryThreeGroups.EntInMulRight
open Formalism.BasicNotions Formalism.BasicNotions.Loc ReciprocityTheory.Gal Module UpperTheory.V.Loc UpperTheory.V.U3 UpperTheory.V.Alg IsLocalRing Polynomial Topology

namespace GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.TangentDual

section Dual

variable {G E : Type} [Group G] [CommRing E]

private def mR (X : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) E) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) (DualNumber E) :=
  X.map fun a => TrivSqZeroExt.inr a

private theorem mul_mR (A X : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) E) :
    A.map (algebraMap E (DualNumber E)) * mR X = mR (A * X) := by
  ext a b <;> simp [mR, Matrix.mul_apply, Fin.sum_univ_three, TrivSqZeroExt.algebraMap_eq_inl, mul_comm]

private theorem mR_mul (A X : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) E) :
    mR X * A.map (algebraMap E (DualNumber E)) = mR (X * A) := by
  ext a b <;> simp [mR, Matrix.mul_apply, Fin.sum_univ_three, TrivSqZeroExt.algebraMap_eq_inl, mul_comm]

private theorem mR_add (X Y : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) E) : mR (X + Y) = mR X + mR Y := by
  ext a b <;> simp [mR]

private theorem mR_inj {X Y : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) E} (h : mR X = mR Y) : X = Y := by
  ext a b
  exact congrArg TrivSqZeroExt.snd (congrFun (congrFun h a) b)

private theorem theta_mR (X : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) E) : thetaLie (mR X) = mR (thetaLie X) := by
  have h : (mR X).transpose = mR X.transpose := rfl
  unfold thetaLie
  rw [h, ← Jmat_map (algebraMap E (DualNumber E)), mul_mR, mR_mul]
  ext a b <;> simp [mR]

private theorem ad_mR (r : G →* GG 3 E) (g : G) (X : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) E) :
    adRep ((GG.map (algebraMap E (DualNumber E))).comp r) g (mR X) = mR (adRep r g X) := by
  show (GG.map (algebraMap E (DualNumber E)) (r g)).adFun (mR X) = mR ((r g).adFun X)
  unfold GG.adFun
  rw [← mR_mul, ← mul_mR, GG.map_right, GG.map_mat]
  congr 2
  split_ifs
  · rfl
  · exact theta_mR X

private theorem sq_ker : RingHom.ker (TrivSqZeroExt.fstHom E E E : DualNumber E →+* E) *
    RingHom.ker (TrivSqZeroExt.fstHom E E E : DualNumber E →+* E) = ⊥ := by
  rw [eq_bot_iff, Ideal.mul_le]
  intro a ha b hb
  have ha' : a.fst = 0 := ha
  have hb' : b.fst = 0 := hb
  rw [Ideal.mem_bot]
  ext <;> simp [ha', hb']

private theorem dualDef_of (r : G →* GG 3 E) (γ : G → Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) E) (r' : G →* GG 3 (DualNumber E))
    (h : IsDeform ((GG.map (algebraMap E (DualNumber E))).comp r) (fun g => mR (γ g)) r') : DualDef r γ r' := by
  intro g
  refine ⟨(h g).1, fun a b => ?_⟩
  rw [(h g).2, MonoidHom.comp_apply, GG.map_mat, Matrix.add_mul, Matrix.one_mul, mR_mul]
  ext <;> simp [mR, TrivSqZeroExt.algebraMap_eq_inl]

private theorem dualEx (r : G →* GG 3 E) (γ : G → Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) E) (hγ : IsCoc r γ) :
    ∃ r', DualDef r γ r' := by
  obtain ⟨r', h⟩ := exists_isDeform (RingHom.ker (TrivSqZeroExt.fstHom E E E : DualNumber E →+* E))
    ((GG.map (algebraMap E (DualNumber E))).comp r) (fun g => mR (γ g)) sq_ker (fun g a b => rfl)
    (fun g h => by simp only [hγ g h, mR_add, ad_mR])
  exact ⟨r', dualDef_of r γ r' h⟩

private theorem dualCoc (r : G →* GG 3 E) (r' : G →* GG 3 (DualNumber E))
    (h : (GG.map (TrivSqZeroExt.fstHom E E E : DualNumber E →+* E)).comp r' = r) :
    ∃ γ, IsCoc r γ ∧ DualDef r γ r' := by
  obtain ⟨γ, h1, h2, h3⟩ := exists_isCoc (RingHom.ker (TrivSqZeroExt.fstHom E E E : DualNumber E →+* E))
    ((GG.map (algebraMap E (DualNumber E))).comp r) r' sq_ker (by
      subst h
      refine MonoidHom.ext fun g => SemidirectProduct.ext (Units.ext ?_) rfl
      ext a b
      refine Ideal.Quotient.eq.2 ?_
      show TrivSqZeroExt.fst ((r' g).mat a b - algebraMap E (DualNumber E) ((r' g).mat a b).fst) = 0
      simp [TrivSqZeroExt.algebraMap_eq_inl])
  have e : γ = fun g => mR ((γ g).map TrivSqZeroExt.snd) := by
    funext g
    ext a b
    · exact h1 g a b
    · rfl
  refine ⟨fun g => (γ g).map TrivSqZeroExt.snd, fun g h => mR_inj ?_, dualDef_of _ _ _ (e ▸ h3)⟩
  rw [mR_add, ← ad_mR, ← congrFun e, ← congrFun e, ← congrFun e]
  exact h2 g h

end Dual

section Main

variable {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [CharZero O]
  [Algebra ℤ_[l] O] [Module.Finite ℤ_[l] O] [IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal O) O] (D : Dat3 l O) (U : D.Univ)
  (E : Type) [Field E] [Algebra ℚ_[l] E] [Module.Finite ℚ_[l] E] [Algebra O E] [Algebra ℤ_[l] E]
  [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] O E] [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] E]
  (x : U.R →ₐ[O] O) (hx : IsBase D.η D.Ψ U.r.hom x)
  (hPC : GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.PtsCr D U (GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.IY D U)) (hCP : GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.CrPts D U (GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.IY D U))

include hx in
private theorem push_base : push ((Algebra.ofId O E).comp x) U.r.hom = D.rz E := by
  unfold Dat3.rz
  rw [← (show push x U.r.hom = r0 D.η D.Ψ from hx)]
  rfl

section Pro

variable {B : Type} [CommRing B] [Algebra ℚ_[l] B] [Module.Finite ℚ_[l] B] [Algebra O B] [Algebra ℤ_[l] B]
  [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] O B] [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] B]

private theorem push_cont (f : U.R →ₐ[O] B) (a b : Fin 3) : QCont l fun g => (push f U.r.hom g).mat a b :=
  (ptCont U.R B f).1 _ _ (U.r.cont a b)

variable [Algebra E B] [IsScalarTower O E B] [IsScalarTower ℚ_[l] E B] (π : B →ₐ[E] E)

include hx in
private theorem push_red (f : U.R →ₐ[O] B) (hf : ∀ a, π (f a) = algebraMap O E (x a)) :
    (GG.map (π : B →+* E)).comp (push f U.r.hom) = D.rz E := by
  rw [← push_base D U E x hx]
  refine MonoidHom.ext fun g => SemidirectProduct.ext (Units.ext ?_) rfl
  ext a b
  exact hf _

include hx hPC in
private theorem inDcr_push (f : U.R →ₐ[O] B) (hf : ∀ a, π (f a) = algebraMap O E (x a))
    (hI : GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.IY D U ≤ RingHom.ker f) : D.InDcr (π : B →+* E) (push f U.r.hom) where
  mult := U.r.hasMult
  red := push_red D U E x hx π f hf
  cont := push_cont D U f
  cr := hPC B f hI

variable (hπ : ∀ a, π a = 0 → IsNilpotent a)

include hx hπ in
private theorem push_inj (f f' : U.R →ₐ[O] B) (hf : ∀ a, π (f a) = algebraMap O E (x a))
    (h : push f' U.r.hom = push f U.r.hom) : f' = f :=
  (unramified_lift_deformation D U E B π hπ ((Algebra.ofId O E).comp x) (push f U.r.hom) U.r.hasMult
    ((push_red D U E x hx π f hf).trans (push_base D U E x hx).symm) (push_cont D U f)).unique h rfl

include hx hCP hπ in
private theorem exists_push (r : GQ l →* GG 3 B) (hr : D.InDcr (π : B →+* E) r) :
    ∃ f : U.R →ₐ[O] B, push f U.r.hom = r ∧ GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.IY D U ≤ RingHom.ker f ∧ ∀ a, π (f a) = algebraMap O E (x a) := by
  obtain ⟨f, hf, -⟩ := unramified_lift_deformation D U E B π hπ ((Algebra.ofId O E).comp x) r hr.mult
    (hr.red.trans (push_base D U E x hx).symm) hr.cont
  refine ⟨f, hf, hCP E B π hπ f (hf ▸ hr), fun a => ?_⟩
  have h := push_inj D U E x hx (AlgHom.id E E) (fun a h => by rw [show a = 0 from h]; exact IsNilpotent.zero)
    ((Algebra.ofId O E).comp x) ((π.restrictScalars O).comp f) (fun _ => rfl) (by
      rw [push_base D U E x hx, ← hr.red, ← hf]
      rfl)
  exact DFunLike.congr_fun h a

end Pro

include hx hCP in
private theorem le_ker [IsFractionRing O E] : GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.IY D U ≤ RingHom.ker x := fun a ha => by
  have hd : D.InDcr ((AlgHom.id E E : E →ₐ[E] E) : E →+* E) (push ((Algebra.ofId O E).comp x) U.r.hom) :=
    { mult := U.r.hasMult
      red := push_red D U E x hx (AlgHom.id E E) ((Algebra.ofId O E).comp x) (fun _ => rfl)
      cont := push_cont D U _
      cr := by rw [push_base D U E x hx]; exact (isType_and_isLocallyCrystalline_rz D E).2 }
  have h := hCP E E (AlgHom.id E E) (fun a h => by rw [show a = 0 from h]; exact IsNilpotent.zero)
    ((Algebra.ofId O E).comp x) hd ha
  exact IsFractionRing.injective O E (h.trans (map_zero _).symm)

private noncomputable def dualPt {O R E : Type} [CommRing O] [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] [CommRing E] [Algebra O E]
    (x : R →ₐ[O] O) (δ : R →ₗ[O] E)
    (h : ∀ a b, δ (a * b) = algebraMap O E (x a) * δ b + algebraMap O E (x b) * δ a) : R →ₐ[O] DualNumber E :=
  AlgHom.ofLinearMap
    (show R →ₗ[O] DualNumber E from ((Algebra.linearMap O E).comp x.toLinearMap).prod δ)
    (TrivSqZeroExt.ext (show algebraMap O E (x 1) = 1 by simp) (show δ 1 = 0 by simpa using h 1 1))
    (fun a b => TrivSqZeroExt.ext
      (show algebraMap O E (x (a * b)) = algebraMap O E (x a) * algebraMap O E (x b) by simp)
      (show δ (a * b) = algebraMap O E (x a) * δ b + δ a * algebraMap O E (x b) by rw [h a b, mul_comm (δ a)]))

private noncomputable def cochain : (U.R →ₗ[O] E) →ₗ[E] (GQ l → Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) E) where
  toFun δ g := (U.r.hom g).mat.map δ * (((D.rz E g).left⁻¹ : GL (Fin 3) E) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) E)
  map_add' δ δ' := by
    funext g
    rw [Pi.add_apply, ← Matrix.add_mul]
    rfl
  map_smul' c δ := by
    funext g
    rw [Pi.smul_apply, ← Matrix.smul_mul]
    rfl

private theorem cochain_mul (δ : U.R →ₗ[O] E) (g : GQ l) :
    cochain D U E δ g * (D.rz E g).mat = (U.r.hom g).mat.map δ :=
  Units.inv_mul_cancel_right _ _

private theorem cochain_eq (δ : U.R →ₗ[O] E) (γ : GQ l → Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) E)
    (h : ∀ g, γ g * (D.rz E g).mat = (U.r.hom g).mat.map δ) : cochain D U E δ = γ := by
  funext g
  exact (Units.mul_left_inj (D.rz E g).left).1 ((cochain_mul D U E δ g).trans (h g).symm)

private theorem nil_fst (a : DualNumber E) (h : TrivSqZeroExt.fstHom E E E a = 0) : IsNilpotent a :=
  TrivSqZeroExt.isNilpotent_iff_isNilpotent_fst.2 (by rw [show a.fst = 0 from h]; exact IsNilpotent.zero)

private instance : Module.Finite ℚ_[l] (DualNumber E) := inferInstanceAs (Module.Finite ℚ_[l] (E × E))

variable [TopologicalSpace E] [IsModuleTopology ℚ_[l] E] [IsTopologicalRing E]

private theorem qcont_iff {X : Type} [TopologicalSpace X] (f : X → E) : QCont l f ↔ Continuous f := by
  unfold QCont
  rw [← eq_moduleTopology ℚ_[l] E]

private theorem qcont_dual {X : Type} [TopologicalSpace X] (f : X → DualNumber E) :
    QCont l f ↔ Continuous (fun g => (f g).fst) ∧ Continuous fun g => (f g).snd := by
  have h : QCont l f ↔ @Continuous X (E × E) _ (moduleTopology ℚ_[l] (E × E)) f := Iff.rfl
  rw [h, ← eq_moduleTopology ℚ_[l] (E × E)]
  exact continuous_prodMk

include hx in
private theorem rz_cont : Continuous fun g => (D.rz E g).mat := by
  rw [← push_base D U E x hx]
  exact continuous_matrix fun a b => (qcont_iff E _).1 (push_cont D U _ a b)

include hx in
private theorem rz_inv_cont :
    Continuous fun g => (((D.rz E g).left⁻¹ : GL (Fin 3) E) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) E) := by
  have h : ∀ g, (D.rz E g).left⁻¹ = (D.rz E g⁻¹).left := fun g => by
    show (Matrix.GeneralLinearGroup.map (algebraMap O E) (tor O (D.Ψ g)))⁻¹ =
      Matrix.GeneralLinearGroup.map (algebraMap O E) (tor O (D.Ψ g⁻¹))
    simp only [map_inv]
  simp only [h]
  exact (rz_cont D U E x hx).comp continuous_inv

include hx hPC hCP in
private theorem tangent (T : Submodule E (U.R →ₗ[O] E))
    (hT : ∀ δ, δ ∈ T ↔ (∀ a b, δ (a * b) = algebraMap O E (x a) * δ b + algebraMap O E (x b) * δ a) ∧
      ∀ a ∈ GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.IY D U, δ a = 0) (hle : GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.IY D U ≤ RingHom.ker x) :
    finrank E ↥T = finrank E ↥(finiteCocycles (MonoidHom.id _) (adRep (D.rz E))) := by
  have hm : HasMult D.η (D.rz E) := fun _ => rfl
  have hinj : Function.Injective ((cochain D U E).domRestrict T) := by
    rw [injective_iff_map_eq_zero]
    rintro ⟨δ, hδ⟩ h0
    have h1 : ∀ g, (U.r.hom g).mat.map δ = 0 := fun g => by
      rw [← cochain_mul, show cochain D U E δ = 0 from h0, Pi.zero_apply, Matrix.zero_mul]
    have h2 := push_inj D U E x hx (TrivSqZeroExt.fstHom E E E) (nil_fst E)
      (dualPt x (0 : U.R →ₗ[O] E) (by simp)) (dualPt x δ ((hT δ).1 hδ).1) (fun _ => rfl) (by
        refine MonoidHom.ext fun g => SemidirectProduct.ext (Units.ext ?_) rfl
        ext a b
        · rfl
        · exact congrFun (congrFun (h1 g) a) b)
    exact Subtype.ext (LinearMap.ext fun a => congrArg TrivSqZeroExt.snd (DFunLike.congr_fun h2 a))
  rw [← LinearMap.finrank_range_of_inj hinj, LinearMap.range_domRestrict]
  refine congrArg (fun S : Submodule E (GQ l → Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) E) => finrank E ↥S) ?_
  refine le_antisymm ?_ fun γ hγ => ?_
  · rintro _ ⟨δ, hδ, rfl⟩
    obtain ⟨h1, h2⟩ := (hT δ).1 hδ
    have hF := inDcr_push D U E x hx hPC (TrivSqZeroExt.fstHom E E E) (dualPt x δ h1) (fun _ => rfl)
      (fun a ha => TrivSqZeroExt.ext (by
        show algebraMap O E (x a) = 0
        rw [show x a = 0 from hle ha, map_zero]) (h2 a ha))
    obtain ⟨γ, hγ, hd⟩ := dualCoc _ _ hF.red
    have e : cochain D U E δ = γ := cochain_eq D U E δ γ fun g => by
      ext a b
      exact (congrArg TrivSqZeroExt.snd ((hd g).2 a b)).symm
    have hc : Continuous (cochain D U E δ) := continuous_pi_iff.2 <| continuous_pi_iff.1 <|
      (continuous_matrix fun a b => ((qcont_dual E _).1 (hF.cont a b)).2).matrix_mul (rz_inv_cont D U E x hx)
    rw [e] at hc ⊢
    exact ((dualCr D E _ hm (rz_cont D U E x hx) γ ⟨hc, hγ⟩ _ hd).1 hF.cr).2
  · obtain ⟨r', hd⟩ := dualEx (D.rz E) γ hγ.1.2
    have hr' : D.InDcr (TrivSqZeroExt.fstHom E E E : DualNumber E →+* E) r' :=
      { mult := fun g => (hd g).1
        red := by
          refine MonoidHom.ext fun g => SemidirectProduct.ext (Units.ext ?_) (hd g).1
          ext a b
          exact congrArg TrivSqZeroExt.fst ((hd g).2 a b)
        cont := fun a b => by
          rw [qcont_dual]
          simp only [fun g => (hd g).2 a b]
          exact ⟨(rz_cont D U E x hx).matrix_elem a b, (hγ.1.1.matrix_mul (rz_cont D U E x hx)).matrix_elem a b⟩
        cr := (dualCr D E _ hm (rz_cont D U E x hx) γ hγ.1 _ hd).2 ⟨(isType_and_isLocallyCrystalline_rz D E).2, hγ⟩ }
    obtain ⟨F, hF, hFI, hFx⟩ := exists_push D U E x hx hCP (TrivSqZeroExt.fstHom E E E) (nil_fst E) r' hr'
    refine ⟨(TrivSqZeroExt.sndHom E E).restrictScalars O ∘ₗ F.toLinearMap, (hT _).2 ⟨fun a b => ?_, fun a ha => ?_⟩,
      cochain_eq D U E _ γ fun g => ?_⟩
    · show (F (a * b)).snd = _ * (F b).snd + _ * (F a).snd
      rw [← hFx, ← hFx, map_mul]
      simp [mul_comm]
    · show (F a).snd = 0
      rw [show F a = 0 from hFI ha]
      rfl
    · ext a b
      subst hF
      exact (congrArg TrivSqZeroExt.snd ((hd g).2 a b)).symm

end Main

end GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.TangentDual

theorem GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.tangent_finrank_eq_twelve :
    LocalPublishedTheorems → ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [CharZero O] [Algebra ℤ_[l] O] [Module.Finite ℤ_[l] O] [IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal O) O] (D : Dat3 l O) (U : D.Univ) (E : Type) [Field E] [Algebra ℚ_[l] E] [Module.Finite ℚ_[l] E] [Algebra O E] [Algebra ℤ_[l] E] [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] O E] [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] E] [IsFractionRing O E] (x : U.R →ₐ[O] O), IsBase D.η D.Ψ U.r.hom x → ∀ T : Submodule E (U.R →ₗ[O] E), (∀ δ, δ ∈ T ↔ (∀ a b, δ (a * b) = algebraMap O E (x a) * δ b + algebraMap O E (x b) * δ a) ∧ ∀ a ∈ GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.IY D U, δ a = 0) → finrank E ↥T = 12 := by
  intro hL l _ O _ _ _ _ _ _ _ D U E _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ x hx T hT
  obtain ⟨hPC, hCP⟩ := GlobalTheory.T.saturatedOrdinaryIdeal_cuts_out_crystalline_locus hL D U
  letI : TopologicalSpace E := moduleTopology ℚ_[l] E
  haveI : IsModuleTopology ℚ_[l] E := ⟨rfl⟩
  haveI : IsTopologicalRing E := IsModuleTopology.isTopologicalRing ℚ_[l] E
  rw [GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.TangentDual.tangent D U E x hx hPC hCP T hT (GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.TangentDual.le_ker D U E x hx hCP),
    UpperTheory.finrank_local_cocycles_adjoint_unitaryThree_eq_twelve hL D E]


[OCH_SRC_LOCAL_WEIGHTS] Finite adjoint tangent dimensions in all five matrix weights
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/PadicHodgeTheory/DimZ1fCrystallineDeformation.lean#L1-L268
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.PadicHodgeTheory.IsInertiaInfiniteCyclotomic
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.PublishedTheorems.KisinCrTrivial
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.PadicHodgeTheory.ExistsNeOne
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.PadicHodgeTheory.ExistsIntBasis
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.PadicHodgeTheory.ExistsUniqueUnramifiedCocycleFrobeniusValue
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.PadicHodgeTheory.Z1fMap

open Formalism.BasicNotions Formalism.BasicNotions.Loc ReciprocityTheory.Gal Module UpperTheory.V.Loc UpperTheory.V.U3 UpperTheory.V.Alg UpperTheory.V.Published IsLocalRing Polynomial

private theorem dz_pi {E ι M : Type} [Field E] [Fintype ι] [AddCommGroup M] [Module E M]
    {N : Type} [AddCommGroup N] [Module E N] (Φ : N ≃ₗ[E] (ι → M)) (S : ι → Submodule E M) (T : Submodule E N)
    (hT : ∀ x, x ∈ T ↔ ∀ i, Φ x i ∈ S i) (hS : ∀ i, Module.Finite E (S i)) :
    Module.Finite E T ∧ finrank E T = ∑ i, finrank E (S i) := by
  let e : T ≃ₗ[E] (∀ i, S i) :=
    { toFun := fun x i => ⟨Φ x i, (hT _).1 x.2 i⟩
      invFun := fun y => ⟨Φ.symm fun i => y i, (hT _).2 fun i => by simp⟩
      map_add' := fun x y => by ext i; simp
      map_smul' := fun c x => by ext i; simp
      left_inv := fun x => by ext; simp
      right_inv := fun y => by ext i; simp }
  exact ⟨Module.Finite.equiv e.symm, by rw [e.finrank_eq, Module.finrank_pi_fintype]⟩

private theorem dz_ch {G E : Type} [Group G] [CommRing E] (κ : G →* Eˣ) (g : G) (x : E) :
    chRep κ g x = (κ g : E) * x := by simp [chRep]

private theorem dz_dec {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {E : Type} [Field E] [Algebra ℚ_[l] E] [Module.Finite ℚ_[l] E]
    [TopologicalSpace E] [IsModuleTopology ℚ_[l] E] [IsTopologicalRing E]
    {V : Type} [AddCommGroup V] [Module E V] [Module ℚ_[l] V] [IsScalarTower ℚ_[l] E V] [FiniteDimensional ℚ_[l] V]
    [TopologicalSpace V] [IsModuleTopology ℚ_[l] V] [IsTopologicalAddGroup V] [ContinuousConstSMul E V]
    {ι : Type} [Fintype ι] (ρ : Representation E (GQ l) V) (χ : ι → GQ l →* Eˣ) (e : V ≃ₗ[E] (ι → E))
    (he : ∀ g x i, e (ρ g x) i = (χ i g : E) * e x i) (c : GQ l → V) :
    (c ∈ finiteCocycles (MonoidHom.id _) ρ ↔ ∀ i, (fun g => e (c g) i) ∈ finiteCocycles (MonoidHom.id _) (chRep (χ i))) ∧
    (c ∈ B1 ρ ↔ ∀ i, (fun g => e (c g) i) ∈ B1 (chRep (χ i))) ∧
    (c ∈ Z1 ρ → ∀ i, (fun g => e (c g) i) ∈ Z1 (chRep (χ i))) := by
  classical
  refine ⟨⟨fun h i => ?_, fun h => ?_⟩, ⟨fun h i => ?_, fun h => ?_⟩, fun h i => ?_⟩
  · exact (z1fMap _ ρ (chRep (χ i))).2 (((LinearMap.proj i).comp e.toLinearMap).restrictScalars ℚ_[l])
      (fun g x => by simp [he, dz_ch]) c h
  · have h1 : c = ∑ i, fun g => e.symm (Pi.single i (e (c g) i)) := by
      funext g
      rw [Finset.sum_apply, ← map_sum, Finset.univ_sum_single, e.symm_apply_apply]
    rw [h1]
    refine Submodule.sum_mem _ fun i _ => ?_
    refine (z1fMap _ (chRep (χ i)) ρ).2
      ((e.symm.toLinearMap.comp (LinearMap.single E (fun _ => E) i)).restrictScalars ℚ_[l]) (fun g x => ?_) _ (h i)
    apply e.injective
    funext k
    by_cases hk : k = i
    · subst hk; simp [he, dz_ch]
    · simp [he, hk]
  · obtain ⟨m, hm⟩ := (mem_coboundaries1 _ _).1 h
    exact (mem_coboundaries1 _ _).2 ⟨e m i, fun g => by simp [hm g, he, dz_ch]⟩
  · choose m hm using fun i => (mem_coboundaries1 _ _).1 (h i)
    refine (mem_coboundaries1 _ _).2 ⟨e.symm m, fun g => e.injective (funext fun i => ?_)⟩
    simpa [he, dz_ch] using hm i g
  · have hc : Continuous (((LinearMap.proj i).comp e.toLinearMap).restrictScalars ℚ_[l] : V →ₗ[ℚ_[l]] E) :=
      IsModuleTopology.continuous_of_linearMap _
    exact ⟨hc.comp h.1, fun g k => by simp [h.2 g k, he, dz_ch]⟩

private theorem dz_cont {G E : Type} [Group G] [TopologicalSpace G] [CommRing E] [TopologicalSpace E]
    [IsTopologicalRing E] (κ : G →* Eˣ) (hκ : Continuous fun g => (κ g : E)) : ContAction (chRep κ) := by
  have : (fun p : G × E => chRep κ p.1 p.2) = fun p => (κ p.1 : E) * p.2 := by
    funext p; simp [chRep]
  unfold ReciprocityTheory.Gal.ContAction
  rw [this]
  exact (hκ.comp continuous_fst).mul continuous_snd

private theorem dz_unr (hP : LocalPublishedTheorems) {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {E : Type} [Field E] [Algebra ℚ_[l] E]
    [Module.Finite ℚ_[l] E] [TopologicalSpace E] [IsModuleTopology ℚ_[l] E] [IsTopologicalRing E]
    (κ : GQ l →* Eˣ) (hκ : Continuous fun g => (κ g : E)) (hI : ∀ g ∈ Iq l, κ g = 1) :
    (∀ c ∈ Z1 (chRep κ), c ∈ finiteCocycles (MonoidHom.id _) (chRep κ) ↔ ∀ g ∈ Iq l, c g = 0) ∧
    Module.Finite E (finiteCocycles (MonoidHom.id _) (chRep κ)) ∧ finrank E (finiteCocycles (MonoidHom.id _) (chRep κ)) = 1 := by
  have hI' : ∀ g ∈ Iq l, chRep κ g = 1 := fun g hg => by ext; simp [dz_ch, hI g hg]
  have h1 := z1fUnr hP (MonoidHom.id (GQ l)) Topology.IsOpenEmbedding.id (chRep κ) (dz_cont κ hκ) hI'
  refine ⟨h1, ?_⟩
  obtain ⟨σ, hσ⟩ := (iqBasic l).2.2.2.1
  have h2 := existsUnique_unramified_cocycle_frobenius_value (l := l) (chRep κ) (dz_cont κ hκ) hI' σ hσ
  let f : finiteCocycles (MonoidHom.id _) (chRep κ) →ₗ[E] E := (LinearMap.proj σ).comp (Submodule.subtype _)
  have hf : Function.Bijective f := by
    refine ⟨fun c c' h => Subtype.ext ?_, fun x => ?_⟩
    · exact (h2 (c'.1 σ)).unique ⟨c.2.1, (h1 _ c.2.1).1 c.2, h⟩ ⟨c'.2.1, (h1 _ c'.2.1).1 c'.2, rfl⟩
    · obtain ⟨c, hc, hc', hx⟩ := (h2 x).exists
      exact ⟨⟨c, (h1 c hc).2 hc'⟩, hx⟩
  let e := LinearEquiv.ofBijective f hf
  exact ⟨Module.Finite.equiv e.symm, by rw [e.finrank_eq, finrank_self]⟩

private def dzS (E : Type) [Field E] (p : Fin 3 × Fin 3) : E :=
  if p.1.val + p.2.val < 2 then 1 else if p.1.val + p.2.val = 2 then 0 else -1

private noncomputable def dzE (E : Type) [Field E] [CharZero E] : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) E ≃ₗ[E] (Fin 3 × Fin 3 → E) where
  toFun X p := X p.1 p.2 + dzS E p * X p.2.rev p.1.rev
  invFun y := Matrix.of fun a b => (1 + dzS E (a, b) ^ 2)⁻¹ * (y (a, b) - dzS E (a, b) * y (b.rev, a.rev))
  map_add' X Y := by funext p; simp; ring
  map_smul' c X := by funext p; simp; ring
  left_inv X := by
    ext a b
    fin_cases a <;> fin_cases b <;> simp [dzS, Fin.rev] <;> ring
  right_inv y := by
    funext ⟨a, b⟩
    fin_cases a <;> fin_cases b <;> simp [dzS, Fin.rev] <;> ring

private theorem dzE_apply {E : Type} [Field E] [CharZero E] (X : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) E) (p : Fin 3 × Fin 3) :
    dzE E X p = X p.1 p.2 + dzS E p * X p.2.rev p.1.rev := rfl

private theorem dz_wt (a b : Fin 3) : wt b.rev a.rev = wt a b := by
  revert a b; decide

private theorem dz_gw {E : Type} [Field E] [CharZero E] (X : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) E) (j : ℤ) :
    X ∈ gw E j ↔ ∀ p : Fin 3 × Fin 3, wt p.1 p.2 ≠ j → dzE E X p = 0 := by
  refine ⟨fun h p hp => ?_, fun h a b hp => ?_⟩
  · rw [dzE_apply, h _ _ hp, h _ _ (by rwa [dz_wt])]; simp
  · rw [← (dzE E).symm_apply_apply X]
    change _ * (dzE E X (a, b) - _ * dzE E X (b.rev, a.rev)) = 0
    rw [h (a, b) hp, h (b.rev, a.rev) (by rwa [dz_wt])]; simp

private def dzChi {G E : Type} [Group G] [Field E] (η : G →* ℤˣ) (κ : G →* Eˣ) (p : Fin 3 × Fin 3) : G →* Eˣ :=
  κ ^ wt p.1 p.2 * if p.1.val + p.2.val ≤ 2 then (Units.map (Int.castRingHom E : ℤ →* E)).comp η else 1

private theorem dz_eq {G E : Type} [Group G] [Field E] [CharZero E] (η : G →* ℤˣ) (κ : G →* Eˣ) (g : G)
    (X : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) E) (p : Fin 3 × Fin 3) :
    dzE E (adRep (r0 η κ) g X) p = (dzChi η κ p g : E) * dzE E X p := by
  obtain ⟨a, b⟩ := p
  simp only [dzE_apply, adRep_baseLift_apply, dz_wt, dzChi, MonoidHom.mul_apply, MonoidHom.zpow_apply, Units.val_mul]
  generalize ((κ g ^ wt a b : Eˣ) : E) = t
  rcases Int.units_eq_one_or (η g) with h | h <;>
    fin_cases a <;> fin_cases b <;> simp [dzS, Fin.rev, h] <;> ring

private theorem dz_data {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O]
    [CharZero O] [Algebra ℤ_[l] O] [Module.Finite ℤ_[l] O] [IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal O) O] (D : Dat3 l O)
    (E : Type) [Field E] [Algebra ℚ_[l] E] [Module.Finite ℚ_[l] E] [Algebra O E] [Algebra ℤ_[l] E]
    [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] O E] [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] E] [TopologicalSpace E] [IsModuleTopology ℚ_[l] E]
    [IsTopologicalRing E] (κ : GQ l →* Eˣ) (hκ : κ = (Units.map (algebraMap O E : O →* E)).comp D.Ψ) :
    D.rz E = r0 D.η κ ∧ ∀ p : Fin 3 × Fin 3, Continuous (fun g => (dzChi D.η κ p g : E)) ∧
      HasWeight (MonoidHom.id _) (dzChi D.η κ p) (D.e * wt p.1 p.2) ∧
      (wt p.1 p.2 = 0 → ∀ g ∈ Iq l, dzChi D.η κ p g = 1) := by
  have h1 : Continuous fun g => (κ g : E) := by
    have h := (ptCont (l := l) O E (Algebra.ofId O E)).1 _ _ D.Ψ_cont
    unfold QCont at h
    rw [← eq_moduleTopology ℚ_[l] E] at h
    subst hκ
    exact h
  have h2 : Continuous κ := Units.continuous_iff.2 ⟨h1, by
    simpa only [map_inv, Function.comp_def] using h1.comp continuous_inv⟩
  have h3 : Continuous ((Units.map (Int.castRingHom E : ℤ →* E)).comp D.η) := by
    have ho : IsOpen (D.η.ker : Set (GQ l)) := by
      have : D.η.ker = GK D.Kv := Subgroup.ext fun g => D.η_ker g
      rw [this]
      haveI := D.Kv.2
      exact D.Kv.1.fixingSubgroup_isOpen
    refine continuous_of_continuousAt_one _ ?_
    refine (continuousAt_const (y := 1)).congr ?_
    filter_upwards [ho.mem_nhds D.η.ker.one_mem] with g hg
    have hg' : D.η g = 1 := hg
    simp [hg']
  have h4 : ∀ (p : Fin 3 × Fin 3), ∀ g ∈ Iq l, dzChi D.η κ p g = κ g ^ wt p.1 p.2 := by
    intro p g hg
    unfold dzChi
    split_ifs <;> simp [D.η_unr g hg]
  refine ⟨?_, fun p => ⟨?_, ⟨D.τ.ker, D.τ_open, fun g hg hI => ?_⟩, fun h g hg => by rw [h4 p g hg, h, zpow_zero]⟩⟩
  · subst hκ
    refine MonoidHom.ext fun g => SemidirectProduct.ext (Units.ext ?_) rfl
    change (tor O (D.Ψ g) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) O).map (algebraMap O E) = tor E _
    rw [tor_val, tor_val, Matrix.diagonal_map (map_zero _)]
    congr 1
    funext i
    rw [MonoidHom.comp_apply, ← map_zpow (Units.map (algebraMap O E : O →* E))]
    rfl
  · refine Units.continuous_val.comp ?_
    unfold dzChi
    split_ifs
    · exact (h2.zpow _).mul h3
    · exact (h2.zpow _).mul continuous_const
  · have hg' : D.τ g = 1 := hg
    have h5 : κ g = cycA l l E g ^ D.e := by
      subst hκ
      rw [MonoidHom.comp_apply, D.Ψ_I g hI, hg', mul_one, map_pow]
      congr 1
      exact Units.ext (IsScalarTower.algebraMap_apply ℤ_[l] O E _).symm
    rw [h4 p g hI, h5, zpow_mul, zpow_natCast]
    rfl

private theorem dz_sum (j : ℤ) :
    ∑ p : Fin 3 × Fin 3, (if wt p.1 p.2 = j then (if 0 < wt p.1 p.2 then 2 else 1) else 0) = locDims j := by
  by_cases h : -2 ≤ j ∧ j ≤ 2
  · obtain ⟨h1, h2⟩ := h
    interval_cases j <;> decide
  · have hw : ∀ p : Fin 3 × Fin 3, -2 ≤ wt p.1 p.2 ∧ wt p.1 p.2 ≤ 2 := by decide
    rw [Finset.sum_eq_zero fun p _ => if_neg fun (hp : wt p.1 p.2 = j) => h (hp ▸ hw p)]
    unfold locDims
    split_ifs <;> omega

theorem UpperTheory.V.Loc.dim_Z1f_crystalline_deformation :
    LocalPublishedTheorems → ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [CharZero O] [Algebra ℤ_[l] O] [Module.Finite ℤ_[l] O] [IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal O) O] (D : Dat3 l O) (E : Type) [Field E] [Algebra ℚ_[l] E] [Module.Finite ℚ_[l] E] [Algebra O E] [Algebra ℤ_[l] E] [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] O E] [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] E] [TopologicalSpace E] [IsModuleTopology ℚ_[l] E] [IsTopologicalRing E] (j : ℤ), Module.Finite E ↥(finiteCocycles (MonoidHom.id _) (adRep (D.rz E))) ∧ finrank E ↥(finiteCocycles (MonoidHom.id _) (adRep (D.rz E)) ⊓ Submodule.pi Set.univ fun _ => gw E j) = locDims j ∧ (∀ γ ∈ finiteCocycles (MonoidHom.id _) (adRep (D.rz E)), (fun g => Matrix.of fun a b => if wt a b = j then γ g a b else 0) ∈ finiteCocycles (MonoidHom.id _) (adRep (D.rz E))) ∧ ∀ γ ∈ Z1 (adRep (D.rz E)), (∀ g, γ g ∈ gw E j) → (j < 0 → (γ ∈ finiteCocycles (MonoidHom.id _) (adRep (D.rz E)) ↔ γ ∈ B1 (adRep (D.rz E)))) ∧ (j = 0 → (γ ∈ finiteCocycles (MonoidHom.id _) (adRep (D.rz E)) ↔ ∀ g ∈ Iq l, γ g = 0)) := by
  intro hP l _ O _ _ _ _ _ _ _ D E _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ j
  haveI : CharZero E := charZero_of_injective_algebraMap (algebraMap ℚ_[l] E).injective
  haveI : IsModuleTopology ℚ_[l] (Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) E) :=
    inferInstanceAs (IsModuleTopology ℚ_[l] (Fin 3 → Fin 3 → E))
  obtain ⟨hr, hχ⟩ := dz_data D E _ rfl
  rw [hr]
  set κ := (Units.map (algebraMap O E : O →* E)).comp D.Ψ
  set χ := dzChi D.η κ
  have hd := dz_dec (adRep (r0 D.η κ)) χ (dzE E) (dz_eq D.η κ)
  have he : (0 : ℤ) < D.e := by exact_mod_cast D.e_pos
  have hch := fun p => z1fChar hP (MonoidHom.id (GQ l)) Topology.IsOpenEmbedding.id (χ p) (hχ p).1 _ (hχ p).2.1
  have hun := fun p hp => dz_unr hP (χ p) (hχ p).1 ((hχ p).2.2 hp)
  have hdim : ∀ p, Module.Finite E (finiteCocycles (MonoidHom.id _) (chRep (χ p))) ∧
      finrank E (finiteCocycles (MonoidHom.id _) (chRep (χ p))) = if 0 < wt p.1 p.2 then 2 else 1 := by
    intro p
    rcases lt_trichotomy (wt p.1 p.2) 0 with h | h | h
    · obtain ⟨h1, h2⟩ := (hch p).1 (mul_neg_of_pos_of_neg he h)
      rw [h1, h2, if_neg h.le.not_gt]
      exact ⟨by unfold ReciprocityTheory.Gal.B1; infer_instance, rfl⟩
    · rw [if_neg h.le.not_gt]
      exact (hun p h).2
    · obtain ⟨h1, h2⟩ := (hch p).2 (mul_pos he h)
      rw [if_pos h, h2, MonoidHom.range_eq_top.2 Function.surjective_id, Subgroup.index_top]
      exact ⟨h1, rfl⟩
  let Φ : (GQ l → Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) E) ≃ₗ[E] (Fin 3 × Fin 3 → GQ l → E) :=
    (LinearEquiv.piCongrRight fun _ => dzE E).trans
      { Equiv.piComm _ with map_add' := fun _ _ => rfl, map_smul' := fun _ _ => rfl }
  have hΦ : ∀ c p, Φ c p = fun g => dzE E (c g) p := fun _ _ => rfl
  refine ⟨(dz_pi Φ _ _ (fun c => (hd c).1) fun p => (hdim p).1).1, ?_, fun γ hγ => ?_, fun γ hγ hg => ?_⟩
  · rw [← dz_sum, (dz_pi Φ (fun p => if wt p.1 p.2 = j then finiteCocycles (MonoidHom.id _) (chRep (χ p)) else ⊥) _
      (fun c => ?_) fun p => ?_).2]
    · refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun p _ => ?_
      by_cases h : wt p.1 p.2 = j
      · rw [if_pos h, if_pos h, (hdim p).2]
      · rw [if_neg h, if_neg h, finrank_bot]
    · rw [Submodule.mem_inf, (hd c).1, Submodule.mem_pi]
      simp only [dz_gw, hΦ]
      refine ⟨fun h p => ?_, fun h => ⟨fun p => ?_, fun g _ p hp => ?_⟩⟩
      · split_ifs with hp
        · exact h.1 p
        · exact funext fun g => h.2 g trivial p hp
      · have := h p
        split_ifs at this with hp
        · exact this
        · exact SetLike.le_def.1 bot_le this
      · have := h p
        rw [if_neg hp] at this
        exact congrFun this g
    · by_cases h : wt p.1 p.2 = j
      · rw [if_pos h]; exact (hdim p).1
      · rw [if_neg h]; infer_instance
  · rw [(hd _).1] at hγ ⊢
    intro p
    by_cases h : wt p.1 p.2 = j
    · simpa only [dzE_apply, Matrix.of_apply, dz_wt, if_pos h] using hγ p
    · simp only [dzE_apply, Matrix.of_apply, dz_wt, if_neg h, mul_zero, add_zero]
      exact Submodule.zero_mem _
  · have h0 : ∀ p : Fin 3 × Fin 3, wt p.1 p.2 ≠ j → (fun g => dzE E (γ g) p) = 0 :=
      fun p hp => funext fun g => (dz_gw _ _).1 (hg g) p hp
    refine ⟨fun hj => ?_, fun hj => ?_⟩
    · rw [(hd _).1, (hd _).2.1]
      refine forall_congr' fun p => ?_
      by_cases h : wt p.1 p.2 = j
      · rw [((hch p).1 (mul_neg_of_pos_of_neg he (h ▸ hj))).1]
      · rw [h0 p h]; simp
    · have h1 : (∀ g ∈ Iq l, γ g = 0) ↔ ∀ p, ∀ g ∈ Iq l, dzE E (γ g) p = 0 :=
        ⟨fun h p g hg => by rw [h g hg, map_zero]; rfl,
          fun h g hg => (dzE E).map_eq_zero_iff.1 (funext fun p => h p g hg)⟩
      rw [(hd _).1, h1]
      refine forall_congr' fun p => ?_
      by_cases h : wt p.1 p.2 = j
      · exact (hun p (h.trans hj)).1 _ ((hd γ).2.2 hγ p)
      · exact ⟨fun _ g _ => congrFun (h0 p h) g, fun _ => by rw [h0 p h]; exact Submodule.zero_mem _⟩


[OCH_SRC_LOCAL_CHART] Actual weighted local chart from entry coordinates
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/PadicHodgeTheory/RtChart.lean#L1-L74
