private theorem count_weights_eq_local_dims (w : Fin 12 → ℤ) (h : ∀ j, Nat.card {i // w i = j} ≤ locDims j) (j : ℤ) :
    Nat.card {i // w i = j} = locDims j := by
  classical
  have hc : ∀ j, Nat.card {i // w i = j} = (Finset.univ.filter fun i => w i = j).card := fun j => by
    rw [Nat.card_eq_fintype_card, Fintype.card_subtype]
  have hm : ∀ i, w i ∈ ({-2, -1, 0, 1, 2} : Finset ℤ) := fun i => by
    by_contra hn
    have h1 := h (w i)
    have h2 : 0 < Nat.card {k // w k = w i} := by
      haveI : Nonempty {k // w k = w i} := ⟨⟨i, rfl⟩⟩
      exact Nat.card_pos
    simp only [Finset.mem_insert, Finset.mem_singleton, not_or] at hn
    simp only [locDims, hn, if_false] at h1
    omega
  have hs := Finset.card_eq_sum_card_fiberwise (s := Finset.univ) (t := ({-2, -1, 0, 1, 2} : Finset ℤ)) (f := w)
    (fun i _ => hm i)
  rw [Finset.sum_insert (by decide), Finset.sum_insert (by decide), Finset.sum_insert (by decide),
    Finset.sum_insert (by decide), Finset.sum_singleton] at hs
  simp only [← hc, Finset.card_univ, Fintype.card_fin] at hs
  have a1 : _ ≤ 1 := h (-2)
  have a2 : _ ≤ 2 := h (-1)
  have a3 : _ ≤ 3 := h 0
  have a4 : _ ≤ 4 := h 1
  have a5 : _ ≤ 2 := h 2
  have a6 := h j
  unfold locDims at a6 ⊢
  split_ifs at a6 ⊢ with b1 b2 b3 b4 b5
  all_goals (try subst_vars); omega

theorem UpperTheory.V.Loc.rtChart :
    LocalPublishedTheorems → ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [CharZero O] [Algebra ℤ_[l] O] [Module.Finite ℤ_[l] O] [IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal O) O] (D : Dat3 l O) (U : D.Univ) (E : Type) [Field E] [Algebra ℚ_[l] E] [Module.Finite ℚ_[l] E] [Algebra O E] [Algebra ℤ_[l] E] [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] O E] [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] E] [IsFractionRing O E] (x : U.R →ₐ[O] O), IsBase D.η D.Ψ U.r.hom x → ∃ (w : Fin 12 → ℤ) (φ : U.R →ₐ[O] MvPowerSeries (Fin 12) E), UpperTheory.V.Loc.IsCompl (D.It U) x φ ∧ (∀ j, Nat.card {i // w i = j} = locDims j) ∧ ∀ g a b, φ ((U.r.hom g).mat a b) ∈ wtPart E w (wt a b) := by
  intro hP l _ O _ _ _ _ _ _ _ D U E _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ x hx
  obtain ⟨-, φ₀, h₀⟩ := UpperTheory.V.Loc.rtCompl hP D U E x hx
  obtain ⟨t, θ, ht, hθx, hθI, hθ⟩ := torAut hP D U x hx
  let e : GQ l × Fin 3 × Fin 3 → U.R := fun s =>
    (U.r.hom s.1).mat s.2.1 s.2.2 - algebraMap O U.R (x ((U.r.hom s.1).mat s.2.1 s.2.2))
  haveI : IsCO O (DualNumber O) := co_sqz O O O fun _ => rfl
  obtain ⟨u, hu⟩ := chartSpan 12 (D.It U) x φ₀ _ e h₀ (fun f f' h => by
    obtain ⟨F, -, hF⟩ := (U.rep (DualNumber O)).2 (U.r.map f') trivial
    refine (hF f ?_).trans (hF f' rfl).symm
    refine GLift.ext (MonoidHom.ext fun g => SemidirectProduct.ext (Units.ext ?_) rfl)
    ext1 a b
    have := h (g, a, b)
    simp only [e, map_sub, AlgHom.commutes, sub_left_inj] at this
    exact this)
  obtain ⟨φ, hφ, hz⟩ := chartChange 12 (D.It U) x φ₀ (fun i => e (u i)) h₀ (fun i => by simp [e]) hu
  have hxe : ∀ g a b, x ((U.r.hom g).mat a b) = (r0 D.η D.Ψ g).mat a b := fun g a b =>
    congrArg (fun r : GQ l →* GG 3 O => (r g).mat a b) hx
  let w : Fin 12 → ℤ := fun i => wt (u i).2.1 (u i).2.2
  have hgr := chartGraded 12 (D.It U) x φ (fun i => e (u i)) w θ t hφ hz hθx hθI ht (fun i => by
    simp only [e, w, map_sub, AlgHom.commutes, hθ, mul_sub]
    congr 1
    by_cases hab : (u i).2.1 = (u i).2.2
    · simp [hab, wt]
    · rw [hxe, GG.mat, unitaryThreeReflection_left, tor_val, Matrix.diagonal_apply_ne _ hab]
      simp)
  have hg : ∀ g a b, φ ((U.r.hom g).mat a b) ∈ wtPart E w (wt a b) := fun g a b => hgr _ _ (hθ g a b)
  refine ⟨w, φ, hφ, count_weights_eq_local_dims w (chartCount hP D U E x hx 12 w φ u hφ hg fun i j => ?_), hg⟩
  have h1 := hz i
  simp only [e, map_sub, AlgHom.commutes, sub_eq_iff_eq_add] at h1
  rw [h1, map_add, MvPowerSeries.algebraMap_apply, MvPowerSeries.coeff_C, if_neg (by simp), add_zero,
    MvPowerSeries.coeff_X]
  simp [Finsupp.single_left_inj, eq_comm]


[OCH_SRC_LOCGOOD_ORIGINAL] Full original LocGood endpoint with linear surjectivity and scalar-cleared injection
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/PadicHodgeTheory/LocGood.lean#L93-L124
theorem UpperTheory.V.Loc.locGood :
    LocalPublishedTheorems → ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [CharZero O] [Algebra ℤ_[l] O] [Module.Finite ℤ_[l] O] [IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal O) O] (D : Dat3 l O) (U : D.Univ), LocGood U (D.It U) := by
  intro hP l _ O _ _ _ _ _ _ _ D U
  refine ⟨?_, ?_⟩
  · rintro R _ _ _ r r' h hh e ⟨f, hf, hI⟩
    obtain ⟨f', hf', hI'⟩ := rtConj hP D U R r.hom h hh ⟨f, congrArg GLift.hom hf, hI⟩
    exact ⟨f', GLift.ext (hf'.trans e.symm), hI'⟩
  · have hi := zl_inj l O
    haveI : FaithfulSMul ℤ_[l] O := (faithfulSMul_iff_algebraMap_injective _ _).2 hi
    have hi2 : Function.Injective (algebraMap ℤ_[l] (FractionRing O)) := by
      rw [IsScalarTower.algebraMap_eq ℤ_[l] O (FractionRing O)]
      exact (IsFractionRing.injective O _).comp hi
    letI : Algebra ℚ_[l] (FractionRing O) := (IsFractionRing.lift hi2).toAlgebra
    haveI : IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] (FractionRing O) :=
      IsScalarTower.of_algebraMap_eq fun x => (IsFractionRing.lift_algebraMap hi2 x).symm
    haveI : IsLocalization (Algebra.algebraMapSubmonoid O (nonZeroDivisors ℤ_[l])) (FractionRing O) := inferInstance
    haveI : Module.Finite ℚ_[l] (FractionRing O) :=
      Module.Finite.of_isLocalization ℤ_[l] O (nonZeroDivisors ℤ_[l])
    obtain ⟨x, hx, -⟩ := univEx D U
    obtain ⟨w, φ, hc, hd, hg⟩ := rtChart hP D U (FractionRing O) x hx
    refine ⟨x, Fin 12, inferInstance, w, φ, hx, ⟨hc.ker, hg, hc.const, fun i => ?_, fun f hf h1 => ?_⟩, hd⟩
    · obtain ⟨a, c, hc0, ha⟩ := hc.dense 2 (MvPowerSeries.X i)
      have key : ∀ j, MvPowerSeries.coeff (Finsupp.single j 1) (φ a) =
          if j = i then algebraMap O (FractionRing O) c else 0 := fun j => by
        have := coeff_lin_of_mem_sq ha j
        rw [map_sub, sub_eq_zero, ← algebraMap_smul (FractionRing O) c, map_smul, coeff_lin_X] at this
        rw [← this]
        split_ifs <;> simp
      refine ⟨a, ?_, fun j hj => by rw [key, if_neg hj]⟩
      rw [key, if_pos rfl]
      exact (map_ne_zero_iff _ (IsFractionRing.injective O _)).2 hc0
    · exact hc.inj 2 f (mem_sq_of_coeff (by rw [hc.const, hf, map_zero]) h1)


[OCH_SRC_CONJUGATION] Universal formal near-identity conjugation and minimal-prime preservation
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/ModularityArgument/CrystallineOrdinaryIdeals/ExistsConjLift.lean#L1-L159
import Mathlib

import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.ModularityArgument.CrystallineOrdinaryIdeals.ReducedOrdinaryIdealLeKerOfConjOfSaturatingNeZerStatement

private theorem map_conj {A B G : Type} [CommRing A] [CommRing B] [Group G] (f : A →+* B) (h : GL (Fin 3) A)
    (r : G →* GG 3 A) (g : G) :
    GG.map f (conjBy h r g) = GG.ofGL (Matrix.GeneralLinearGroup.map f h) * GG.map f (r g) *
      (GG.ofGL (Matrix.GeneralLinearGroup.map f h))⁻¹ := by
  have e : GG.map f (GG.ofGL h) = GG.ofGL (Matrix.GeneralLinearGroup.map f h) := by
    unfold GG.map
    rw [SemidirectProduct.map_inl]
  have e2 : conjBy h r g = GG.ofGL h * r g * (GG.ofGL h)⁻¹ := rfl
  rw [e2, map_mul, map_mul, map_inv, e]

private theorem push_conj {O A B G : Type} [CommRing O] [CommRing A] [CommRing B] [Algebra O A] [Algebra O B]
    [Group G] (f : A →ₐ[O] B) (h : GL (Fin 3) A) (r : G →* GG 3 A) :
    push f (conjBy h r) = conjBy (Matrix.GeneralLinearGroup.map (f : A →+* B) h) (push f r) :=
  MonoidHom.ext fun g => map_conj _ h r g

private theorem conjBy_one {A G : Type} [CommRing A] [Group G] (r : G →* GG 3 A) : conjBy 1 r = r := by
  refine MonoidHom.ext fun g => ?_
  have e2 : conjBy 1 r g = GG.ofGL 1 * r g * (GG.ofGL 1)⁻¹ := rfl
  rw [e2, map_one, one_mul, inv_one, mul_one]

private theorem exists_conjLift {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] {Γ : Type} [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace Γ]
    {η : Γ →* ℤˣ} {Ψ : Γ →* Oˣ} (hη : IsLocallyConstant η) {R : Type} [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] [IsCO O R]
    (r : GLift O η Ψ R) (h : GL (Fin 3) R) (hh : CongOne (maximalIdeal R) h) :
    ∃ r' : GLift O η Ψ R, r'.hom = conjBy h r.hom := by
  refine ⟨⟨conjBy h r.hom, fun a b N => ?_, fun g => ?_⟩, rfl⟩
  · have h1 : IsLocallyConstant fun g => GG.map (Ideal.Quotient.mk (maximalIdeal R ^ N)) (r.hom g) := by
      rw [IsLocallyConstant.iff_eventually_eq]
      intro x
      have h2 : ∀ᶠ y in nhds x, ∀ a b, Ideal.Quotient.mk (maximalIdeal R ^ N) ((r.hom y).mat a b) =
          Ideal.Quotient.mk (maximalIdeal R ^ N) ((r.hom x).mat a b) :=
        Filter.eventually_all.2 fun a => Filter.eventually_all.2 fun b => (r.cont a b N).eventually_eq x
      filter_upwards [h2, hη.eventually_eq x] with y h3 h4
      refine SemidirectProduct.ext (Units.ext (Matrix.ext h3)) ?_
      rw [GG.map_right, GG.map_right, r.hasMult y, r.hasMult x, h4]
    have h5 := h1.comp fun z => GG.mat (GG.ofGL (Matrix.GeneralLinearGroup.map (Ideal.Quotient.mk _) h) * z *
      (GG.ofGL (Matrix.GeneralLinearGroup.map (Ideal.Quotient.mk _) h))⁻¹) a b
    convert h5 using 2 with g
    rw [Function.comp_apply, ← map_conj]
    rfl
  · have e : Matrix.GeneralLinearGroup.map (res O R : R →+* ResidueField O) h = 1 := by
      refine Units.ext (Matrix.ext fun a b => ?_)
      have h1 := (res_eq_zero_iff O R _).2 (hh a b)
      rw [Matrix.sub_apply, map_sub, sub_eq_zero] at h1
      refine h1.trans ?_
      simp only [Matrix.one_apply, Units.val_one]
      split_ifs <;> simp
    rw [map_conj, e, map_one, one_mul, inv_one, mul_one, r.red]

private theorem eta_lc {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] [Algebra ℤ_[l] O]
    (D : Dat3 l O) : IsLocallyConstant D.η := by
  haveI := D.Kv.2
  have ho : IsOpen (GK D.Kv : Set (GQ l)) := IntermediateField.fixingSubgroup_isOpen D.Kv.1
  rw [IsLocallyConstant.iff_exists_open]
  intro x
  refine ⟨(fun y => x⁻¹ * y) ⁻¹' (GK D.Kv : Set (GQ l)), ho.preimage (by fun_prop), by simp, fun y hy => ?_⟩
  have h1 := (D.η_ker _).2 hy
  rw [map_mul, map_inv, inv_mul_eq_one] at h1
  exact h1.symm

private theorem exists_univMat (R : Type) [CommRing R] :
    ∃ h : GL (Fin 3) (MvPowerSeries (Fin 3 × Fin 3) R), ∀ a b,
      h.val a b = (1 : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) (MvPowerSeries (Fin 3 × Fin 3) R)) a b + MvPowerSeries.X (a, b) := by
  let M : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) (MvPowerSeries (Fin 3 × Fin 3) R) := 1 + Matrix.of fun a b => MvPowerSeries.X (a, b)
  have hM : IsUnit M := by
    rw [Matrix.isUnit_iff_isUnit_det, MvPowerSeries.isUnit_iff_constantCoeff, RingHom.map_det]
    have : (MvPowerSeries.constantCoeff (σ := Fin 3 × Fin 3) (R := R)).mapMatrix M = 1 := by
      rw [map_add, map_one]
      ext a b
      simp
    rw [this, Matrix.det_one]
    exact isUnit_one
  exact ⟨hM.unit, fun a b => rfl⟩

private theorem gl_ext {S C : Type} [CommRing S] [CommRing C] (F : S →+* C) (u : GL (Fin 3) S) (h : GL (Fin 3) C)
    (x : Fin 3 × Fin 3 → S) (hu : ∀ a b, (u : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) S) a b = (1 : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) S) a b + x (a, b))
    (hx : ∀ a b, F (x (a, b)) = ((h : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) C) - 1) a b) :
    Matrix.GeneralLinearGroup.map F u = h := by
  refine Units.ext (Matrix.ext fun a b => ?_)
  show F ((u : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) S) a b) = _
  rw [hu, map_add, hx, Matrix.sub_apply]
  simp only [Matrix.one_apply]
  split_ifs <;> simp

end GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.RootConjugation

open GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.RootConjugation in

theorem GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.rtConj :
    LocalPublishedTheorems → ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [CharZero O] [Algebra ℤ_[l] O] [Module.Finite ℤ_[l] O] [IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal O) O] (D : Dat3 l O) (U : D.Univ) (C : Type) [CommRing C] [Algebra O C] [IsCO O C] (r : GQ l →* GG 3 C) (h : GL (Fin 3) C), UpperTheory.V.UnitaryThreeInertia.InHat h → GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.On D U r → GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.On D U (conjBy h r) := by
  intro hP l _ O _ _ _ _ _ _ _ D U C _ _ _ r h hh hr
  obtain ⟨f, rfl, hf⟩ := hr
  obtain ⟨hpr, hprime, hl, hIt, -, -, -⟩ := GlobalTheory.T.ordinaryComponent_unique_prime_dim_thirteen_near_base hP D U
  have hmin : GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.It D U ∈ (GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.Icr D U).minimalPrimes := by
    have : GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.It D U ∈ GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.primesAt0 D U := by rw [hpr]; rfl
    exact this.1
  haveI := hprime
  let q : U.R →ₐ[O] GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.Rt D U := Ideal.Quotient.mkₐ O (GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.It D U)
  haveI : IsCO O (GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.Rt D U) := co_quot O U.R _ q (Ideal.Quotient.mkₐ_surjective O _)
  let S := MvPowerSeries (Fin 3 × Fin 3) (GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.Rt D U)
  haveI : IsCO O S := co_mvps O _ _
  haveI : IsDomain S := NoZeroDivisors.to_isDomain S
  let i : U.R →ₐ[O] S := (IsScalarTower.toAlgHom O (GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.Rt D U) S).comp q
  have hi : ∀ a, i a = MvPowerSeries.C (q a) := fun a => rfl
  have hiI : ∀ a ∈ GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.It D U, i a = 0 := fun a ha => by
    rw [hi, show q a = 0 from Ideal.Quotient.eq_zero_iff_mem.2 ha, map_zero]
  obtain ⟨u, hu⟩ := exists_univMat (GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.Rt D U)
  have hu1 : CongOne (maximalIdeal S) u := fun a b => by
    rw [Matrix.sub_apply, hu, add_sub_cancel_left]
    intro hx
    have := MvPowerSeries.isUnit_constantCoeff _ hx
    simp at this
  obtain ⟨r', hr'⟩ := exists_conjLift (eta_lc D) (U.r.map i) u hu1
  obtain ⟨α, hα, -⟩ := (U.rep S).2 r' trivial
  have hα' : push α U.r.hom = conjBy u (push i U.r.hom) := (congrArg GLift.hom hα).trans hr'
  have hcomp : ∀ {T : Type} [CommRing T] [Algebra O T] (y : S →ₐ[O] T), push (y.comp α) U.r.hom =
      conjBy (Matrix.GeneralLinearGroup.map (y : S →+* T) u) (push (y.comp i) U.r.hom) := fun y => by
    show push y (push α U.r.hom) = _
    rw [hα', push_conj]
    rfl
  have hIY : GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.IY D U ≤ RingHom.ker i := fun a ha =>
    hiI a (hIt (Ideal.le_radical ha))
  have hdel : i (GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.del D U.r.hom) ≠ 0 := by
    rw [hi]
    intro h0
    have h1 : q (GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.del D U.r.hom) = 0 := by
      have := congrArg MvPowerSeries.constantCoeff h0
      simpa using this
    exact GlobalTheory.T.saturatingElement_notMem_minimalPrimes_reducedOrdinaryIdeal D U _ hmin (Ideal.Quotient.eq_zero_iff_mem.1 h1)
  have h1 : GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.Icr D U ≤ RingHom.ker α := GlobalTheory.T.reducedOrdinaryIdeal_le_ker_of_conj_of_saturating_ne_zero D U S i α u hα' hIY hdel
  obtain ⟨c, ⟨hc1, hc2⟩, -⟩ := co_ev O (GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.Rt D U) (GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.Rt D U) (Fin 3 × Fin 3) (AlgHom.id O _) 0 fun _ => zero_mem _
  have h2 : c.comp α = q := by
    have e1 : c.comp i = q := AlgHom.ext fun a => hc1 _
    refine ((U.rep (GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.Rt D U)).2 (U.r.map q) trivial).unique (GLift.ext ?_) rfl
    show push (c.comp α) U.r.hom = push q U.r.hom
    rw [hcomp, e1, gl_ext (c : S →+* GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.Rt D U) u 1 _ hu fun a b => (hc2 _).trans (by simp), conjBy_one]
  have h3 : RingHom.ker α ≤ GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.It D U := fun a ha => by
    have : q a = 0 := by
      rw [← h2, AlgHom.comp_apply, show α a = 0 from ha, map_zero]
    exact Ideal.Quotient.eq_zero_iff_mem.1 this
  have h4 : GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.It D U ≤ RingHom.ker α := hmin.2 ⟨RingHom.ker_isPrime α, h1⟩ h3
  obtain ⟨F, ⟨hF1, hF2⟩, -⟩ := co_ev O (GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.Rt D U) C (Fin 3 × Fin 3) (Ideal.Quotient.liftₐ (GlobalTheory.OrdLoc.It D U) f fun a ha => hf ha)
    (fun s => ((h : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) C) - 1) s.1 s.2) fun s => hh s.1 s.2
  refine ⟨F.comp α, ?_, fun a ha => ?_⟩
  · have e1 : F.comp i = f := AlgHom.ext fun a => hF1 _
    rw [hcomp, e1, gl_ext (F : S →+* C) u h _ hu fun a b => hF2 _]
  · show F (α a) = 0
    rw [show α a = 0 from h4 ha, map_zero]


[OCH_SRC_FULL_INERTIA] The actual chart gives full-inertia characteristic and ordered-product identities
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/PadicHodgeTheory/ScalarMap.lean#L157-L193
theorem UpperTheory.V.Loc.lemma2a :
    LocalPublishedTheorems → ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [CharZero O] [Algebra ℤ_[l] O] [Module.Finite ℤ_[l] O] [IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal O) O] (D : Dat3 l O) (U : D.Univ) (C : Type) [CommRing C] [Algebra O C] (r : GQ l →* GG 3 C), D.On U r → (∀ s ∈ Iq l, (r s).mat.charpoly = ∏ a, (X - Polynomial.C (algebraMap O C (UpperTheory.V.UnitaryThreeInertia.psi D.Ψ a s)))) ∧ ∀ s : Fin 3 → GQ l, (∀ a, s a ∈ Iq l) → ((r (s 0)).mat - Matrix.scalar _ (algebraMap O C (UpperTheory.V.UnitaryThreeInertia.psi D.Ψ 0 (s 0)))) * ((r (s 1)).mat - Matrix.scalar _ (algebraMap O C (UpperTheory.V.UnitaryThreeInertia.psi D.Ψ 1 (s 1)))) * ((r (s 2)).mat - Matrix.scalar _ (algebraMap O C (UpperTheory.V.UnitaryThreeInertia.psi D.Ψ 2 (s 2)))) = 0 := by
  intro hL l _ O _ _ _ _ _ _ _ D U C _ _ r hr
  obtain ⟨f, rfl, hf⟩ := hr
  show Cond D.Ψ (Iq l) (push f U.r.hom)
  obtain ⟨S, hS⟩ := cond_eqs D.Ψ (Iq l) U.r.hom
  obtain ⟨_, _, _⟩ := frac_alg l O
  obtain ⟨x, hx, -⟩ := univEx D U
  obtain ⟨-, φ, hφ⟩ := rtCompl hL D U (FractionRing O) x hx
  refine (hS C f).2 fun a ha => hf ?_
  rw [← hφ.ker, RingHom.mem_ker, ← Ideal.mem_bot,
    ← Ideal.iInf_pow_eq_bot_of_isLocalRing (maximalIdeal (MvPowerSeries (Fin 12) (FractionRing O)))
      (maximalIdeal.isMaximal _).ne_top, Ideal.mem_iInf]
  intro n
  refine Ideal.pow_le_pow_right n.le_succ ?_
  obtain ⟨π, hπ, hn, hfin⟩ := trunc_data (FractionRing O) n
  let B := MvPowerSeries (Fin 12) (FractionRing O) ⧸ maximalIdeal (MvPowerSeries (Fin 12) (FractionRing O)) ^ (n + 1)
  haveI : Module.Finite ℚ_[l] B := Module.Finite.trans (FractionRing O) B
  let g : U.R →ₐ[O] B := (Ideal.Quotient.mkₐ O _).comp φ
  have hon : D.On U (push g U.r.hom) := ⟨g, rfl, fun y hy => by
    rw [← hφ.ker, RingHom.mem_ker] at hy
    show Ideal.Quotient.mkₐ O _ (φ y) = 0
    rw [hy, map_zero]⟩
  obtain ⟨hc, -, hcr⟩ := rtPoints hL D U B _ hon
  have hred : (GG.map (π : B →+* FractionRing O)).comp (push g U.r.hom) = D.rz (FractionRing O) := by
    have e : (π : B →+* FractionRing O).comp (g : U.R →+* B) =
        (algebraMap O (FractionRing O)).comp (x : U.R →+* O) := RingHom.ext fun y => (hπ (φ y)).trans (hφ.const y)
    unfold Dat3.rz
    rw [← show push x U.r.hom = r0 D.η D.Ψ from hx]
    refine MonoidHom.ext fun s => ?_
    simp only [push, MonoidHom.comp_apply, GG.map_map, e]
  obtain ⟨u, m, hu, -, -, hum⟩ := (ordEq hL D (FractionRing O) B π hn _).1 ⟨U.r.hasMult, hred, hc, hcr⟩
  have hcond : Cond D.Ψ (Iq l) (push g U.r.hom) := by
    rw [hum]
    exact cond_conj _ _ u (fun s hs => (hu.mult s).trans (D.η_unr s hs)) m
      (cond_tri _ _ u hu.tri (triDiag D (FractionRing O) B π hn u hu))
  exact Ideal.Quotient.eq_zero_iff_mem.1 ((hS B g).1 hcond a ha)


[OCH_SRC_ORDERED_FLAG] Actual ordered inertia projectors and normality under the decomposition group
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/PadicHodgeTheory/CommutingUpperTriangularDistinct.lean#L15-L167
  have a10 := hA 1 0 (by decide)
  have a20 := hA 2 0 (by decide)
  have a21 := hA 2 1 (by decide)
  have b10 := hB 1 0 (by decide)
  have b20 := hB 2 0 (by decide)
  have b21 := hB 2 1 (by decide)
  have d01 : d 0 - d 1 ≠ 0 := sub_ne_zero.2 (hd.ne (by decide))
  have d02 : d 0 - d 2 ≠ 0 := sub_ne_zero.2 (hd.ne (by decide))
  have d12 : d 1 - d 2 ≠ 0 := sub_ne_zero.2 (hd.ne (by decide))
  have e20 := congrFun (congrFun h 2) 0
  have e21 := congrFun (congrFun h 2) 1
  have e10 := congrFun (congrFun h 1) 0
  simp only [Matrix.mul_apply, Fin.sum_univ_three, a10, a20, a21, b10, b20, b21, hA', hB'] at e20 e21 e10
  have k20 : k 2 0 = 0 := (mul_eq_zero.1 (show (d 0 - d 2) * k 2 0 = 0 by linear_combination -e20)).resolve_left d02
  rw [k20] at e21 e10
  have k21 : k 2 1 = 0 := (mul_eq_zero.1 (show (d 1 - d 2) * k 2 1 = 0 by linear_combination -e21)).resolve_left d12
  have k10 : k 1 0 = 0 := (mul_eq_zero.1 (show (d 0 - d 1) * k 1 0 = 0 by linear_combination -e10)).resolve_left d01
  intro a b hab
  fin_cases a <;> fin_cases b <;> first | exact absurd hab (by decide) | assumption

private theorem diagonalize_three_by_three_split {K : Type} [Field K] (d : Fin 3 → K) (hd : Function.Injective d)
    (M : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) K) (hM : M.charpoly = ∏ a, (X - C (d a))) :
    ∃ h : GL (Fin 3) K, ((h⁻¹ : GL (Fin 3) K) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) K) * M * h = Matrix.diagonal d :=
  _root_.UpperTheory.Reformulated.B.DiagonalizeThreeByThree.diagonalize_three_by_three_split d hd M hM

private theorem three_eigenvalue_product_zero {K : Type} [Field K] (d p : Fin 3 → K) (hd : Function.Injective d)
    (N : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) K)
    (h0 : (N - Matrix.scalar _ (p 0)) * (Matrix.diagonal d - Matrix.scalar _ (d 1)) *
      (Matrix.diagonal d - Matrix.scalar _ (d 2)) = 0)
    (h1 : (Matrix.diagonal d - Matrix.scalar _ (d 0)) * (N - Matrix.scalar _ (p 1)) *
      (Matrix.diagonal d - Matrix.scalar _ (d 2)) = 0)
    (h2 : (Matrix.diagonal d - Matrix.scalar _ (d 0)) * (Matrix.diagonal d - Matrix.scalar _ (d 1)) *
      (N - Matrix.scalar _ (p 2)) = 0) :
    (∀ a b, wt a b < 0 → N a b = 0) ∧ ∀ a, N a a = p a := by
  have d01 : d 0 - d 1 ≠ 0 := sub_ne_zero.2 (hd.ne (by decide))
  have d02 : d 0 - d 2 ≠ 0 := sub_ne_zero.2 (hd.ne (by decide))
  have d12 : d 1 - d 2 ≠ 0 := sub_ne_zero.2 (hd.ne (by decide))
  have d10 : d 1 - d 0 ≠ 0 := sub_ne_zero.2 (hd.ne (by decide))
  have d20 : d 2 - d 0 ≠ 0 := sub_ne_zero.2 (hd.ne (by decide))
  have d21 : d 2 - d 1 ≠ 0 := sub_ne_zero.2 (hd.ne (by decide))
  have hs : ∀ c : K, Matrix.diagonal d - Matrix.scalar (Fin 3) c = Matrix.diagonal fun i => d i - c := fun c => by
    rw [Matrix.scalar_apply, Matrix.diagonal_sub]
  simp only [hs, Matrix.diagonal_mul_diagonal] at h0 h1 h2
  have e00 := congrFun (congrFun h0 0) 0
  have e10 := congrFun (congrFun h0 1) 0
  have e20 := congrFun (congrFun h0 2) 0
  have e11 := congrFun (congrFun h1 1) 1
  have e21 := congrFun (congrFun h2 2) 1
  have e22 := congrFun (congrFun h2 2) 2
  simp [Matrix.mul_diagonal, Matrix.diagonal_mul, d01, d02, d12, d10, d20, d21, sub_eq_zero] at e00 e10 e20 e11 e21 e22
  constructor
  · intro a b hab
    fin_cases a <;> fin_cases b <;> first | exact absurd hab (by decide) | assumption
  · intro a
    fin_cases a <;> assumption

private theorem conjugate_representation_mat {R D : Type} [CommRing R] [Group D] (r : D →* GG 3 R) (h : GL (Fin 3) R) (g : D)
    (hg : (r g).right = 1) : (conjBy h⁻¹ r g).mat =
      ((h⁻¹ : GL (Fin 3) R) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) R) * (r g).mat * h := by
  simp [conjBy, GG.mat, hg]

private theorem padic_int_ringHom_injective {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {K : Type} [Field K] [CharZero K] (φ : ℤ_[l] →+* K) :
    Function.Injective φ := by
  rw [injective_iff_map_eq_zero]
  intro x hx
  by_contra h0
  rw [PadicInt.unitCoeff_spec h0, map_mul, map_pow, map_natCast] at hx
  exact mul_ne_zero ((Units.isUnit _).map φ).ne_zero
    (pow_ne_zero _ (Nat.cast_ne_zero.2 (Fact.out : l.Prime).ne_zero)) hx

private theorem exists_inertia_distinct_characters {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] [Algebra ℤ_[l] O]
    (D : Dat3 l O) (K : Type) [Field K] [CharZero K] [Algebra O K] :
    ∃ s ∈ Iq l, Function.Injective fun a => algebraMap O K (UpperTheory.V.UnitaryThreeInertia.psi D.Ψ a s) := by
  obtain ⟨s, ⟨hs1, hs2⟩, hs⟩ := ((cycInertia l).2 D.τ.ker D.τ_open).2
  refine ⟨s, hs2, ?_⟩
  let φ : ℤ_[l] →+* K := (algebraMap O K).comp (algebraMap ℤ_[l] O)
  let t : Kˣ := Units.map (algebraMap O K : O →* K) (D.Ψ s)
  have ht : t = Units.map (φ : ℤ_[l] →* K) (cyc l l s) ^ D.e := by
    have h1 : D.τ s = 1 := hs1
    simp only [t]
    rw [D.Ψ_I s hs2, h1, mul_one, map_pow]
    rfl
  have hinf : ¬ IsOfFinOrder t := by
    rw [ht]
    intro h
    have h2 := h.of_pow (by have := D.e_pos; omega)
    exact hs ((Function.Injective.isOfFinOrder_iff (f := Units.map (φ : ℤ_[l] →* K))
      (Units.map_injective (padic_int_ringHom_injective φ))).1 h2)
  have e : ∀ a, algebraMap O K (UpperTheory.V.UnitaryThreeInertia.psi D.Ψ a s) = ((t ^ wt1 a : Kˣ) : K) := fun a =>
    congrArg Units.val (map_zpow (Units.map (algebraMap O K : O →* K)) (D.Ψ s) (wt1 a))
  intro a b hab
  simp only [e] at hab
  have h3 := injective_zpow_iff_not_isOfFinOrder.2 hinf (Units.ext hab)
  simp only [wt1] at h3
  exact Fin.ext (by omega)

end UpperTheory.V.Loc

theorem UpperTheory.V.Loc.lemma2b :
    LocalPublishedTheorems → ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [CharZero O] [Algebra ℤ_[l] O] [Module.Finite ℤ_[l] O] [IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal O) O] (D : Dat3 l O) (U : D.Univ) (K : Type) [Field K] [CharZero K] [Algebra O K] (r : GQ l →* GG 3 K), D.On U r → (∃ h, UpperTheory.V.UnitaryThreeInertia.TriIn D.η D.Ψ (Iq l) r h) ∧ ∀ h h', UpperTheory.V.UnitaryThreeInertia.TriIn D.η D.Ψ (Iq l) r h → UpperTheory.V.UnitaryThreeInertia.TriIn D.η D.Ψ (Iq l) r h' → ∀ a b, wt a b < 0 → ((h⁻¹ * h' : GL (Fin 3) K) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) K) a b = 0 := by
  intro hP l _ O _ _ _ _ _ _ _ D U K _ _ _ r hr
  obtain ⟨hch, hpr⟩ := UpperTheory.V.Loc.lemma2a hP D U K r hr
  have hm : ∀ g, D.η g = 1 → (r g).right = 1 := by
    obtain ⟨f, rfl, -⟩ := hr
    exact fun g hg => (U.r.hasMult g).trans hg
  obtain ⟨s, hs, hd⟩ := exists_inertia_distinct_characters D K
  have hηs := D.η_unr s hs
  constructor
  · obtain ⟨h, hh⟩ := diagonalize_three_by_three_split _ hd _ (hch s hs)
    have hc : ∀ (A : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) K) (c : K),
        ((h⁻¹ : GL (Fin 3) K) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) K) * A * (h : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) K) - Matrix.scalar _ c =
          ((h⁻¹ : GL (Fin 3) K) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) K) * (A - Matrix.scalar _ c) * (h : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) K) := by
      intro A c
      rw [Matrix.mul_sub, Matrix.sub_mul, Matrix.mul_assoc _ (Matrix.scalar _ c),
        Matrix.scalar_commute c (fun _ => Commute.all _ _), ← Matrix.mul_assoc, Units.inv_mul, Matrix.one_mul]
    have hc3 : ∀ A B C : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) K, A * B * C = 0 →
        (((h⁻¹ : GL (Fin 3) K) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) K) * A * (h : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) K)) *
          (((h⁻¹ : GL (Fin 3) K) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) K) * B * (h : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) K)) *
          (((h⁻¹ : GL (Fin 3) K) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) K) * C * (h : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) K)) = 0 := by
      intro A B C h0
      have : ∀ Y : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) K,
          (h : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) K) * (((h⁻¹ : GL (Fin 3) K) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) K) * Y) = Y :=
        fun Y => by rw [← Matrix.mul_assoc, Units.mul_inv, Matrix.one_mul]
      simp only [Matrix.mul_assoc, this]
      rw [← Matrix.mul_assoc B, ← Matrix.mul_assoc A, ← Matrix.mul_assoc A, h0, Matrix.zero_mul, Matrix.mul_zero]
    have hI : ∀ x ∈ Iq l, (∀ a b, wt a b < 0 → (conjBy h⁻¹ r x).mat a b = 0) ∧
        ∀ a, (conjBy h⁻¹ r x).mat a a = algebraMap O K (UpperTheory.V.UnitaryThreeInertia.psi D.Ψ a x) := by
      intro x hx
      rw [conjugate_representation_mat r h x (hm x (D.η_unr x hx))]
      refine three_eigenvalue_product_zero _ (fun a => algebraMap O K (UpperTheory.V.UnitaryThreeInertia.psi D.Ψ a x)) hd _ ?_ ?_ ?_
      · rw [← hh, hc, hc, hc]
        exact hc3 _ _ _ (hpr ![x, s, s] fun a => by fin_cases a <;> assumption)
      · rw [← hh, hc, hc, hc]
        exact hc3 _ _ _ (hpr ![s, x, s] fun a => by fin_cases a <;> assumption)
      · rw [← hh, hc, hc, hc]
        exact hc3 _ _ _ (hpr ![s, s, x] fun a => by fin_cases a <;> assumption)
    refine ⟨h, fun g hg => ?_, fun x hx _ => (hI x hx).2⟩
    have hx : g * s * g⁻¹ ∈ Iq l := (iqBasic l).1.conj_mem s hs g
    have hr1 : ∀ x, D.η x = 1 → (conjBy h⁻¹ r x).right = 1 := fun x hx => by simp [conjBy, hm x hx]
    have hmul : ∀ x y, D.η x = 1 →
        (conjBy h⁻¹ r (x * y)).mat = (conjBy h⁻¹ r x).mat * (conjBy h⁻¹ r y).mat := fun x y hx => by
      rw [map_mul]
      simp only [GG.mat, SemidirectProduct.mul_left, hr1 x hx, sgnAct_one, Units.val_mul]
    refine commuting_upper_triangular_distinct _ hd (conjBy h⁻¹ r (g * s * g⁻¹)).mat _ (conjBy h⁻¹ r s).mat ?_ (hI _ hx).1 (hI s hs).1
      (fun a => ?_) (hI s hs).2
    · rw [← hmul _ _ (D.η_unr _ hx), ← hmul _ _ hg, inv_mul_cancel_right]
    · rw [(hI _ hx).2]
      simp only [UpperTheory.V.UnitaryThreeInertia.psi, map_mul, map_inv, mul_inv_cancel_comm]
  · intro h h' H H'
    refine commuting_upper_triangular_distinct _ hd (conjBy h⁻¹ r s).mat _ (conjBy h'⁻¹ r s).mat ?_ (H.tri s hηs) (H'.tri s hηs)
      (H.ine s hs hηs) (H'.ine s hs hηs)
    rw [conjugate_representation_mat r h s (hm s hηs), conjugate_representation_mat r h' s (hm s hηs), Units.val_mul]
    simp only [Matrix.mul_assoc, Units.mul_inv_cancel_left]


[OCH_SRC_DAT] Original Dat, ULoc, Ring, Marked, IsPt, rhoK, IsExt, IsAutI and IsModular
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/UnitaryThreeGroups/RhoKApply.lean#L16-L94
structure Dat (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (K : Type) [Field K] [NumberField K] [Algebra K Ω]
    {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] (c : Coef l) {N : ℕ} [NeZero N] (e : ℕ) (ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O N) where
  gd : GD Ω K l e ψ
  η : G ℚ Ω →* ℤˣ
  η_ker : η.ker = galOf ℚ K Ω
  cc : G ℚ Ω
  conj : ∃ φ : Ω →+* ℂ, ∀ x, φ (cc x) = starRingEnd ℂ (φ x)
  j : Ω →+* Om l
  ι : GQ l →* G ℚ Ω
  ι_cont : Continuous ι
  ι_spec : ∀ σ x, σ (j x) = j (ι σ x)
  Kv : FinExt l
  Kv_spec : ∀ g, η (ι g) = 1 ↔ g ∈ GK Kv
  η_unr : ∀ g ∈ Loc.Iq l, η (ι g) = 1
  τ_open : IsOpen (((Chr.dirG ℚ Ω (comp l ψ)).comp ι).ker : Set (GQ l))
  Ψ_cont : CharCont (Chr.Psi l e (Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ))
  Ψ_I : ∀ g ∈ Loc.Iq l, Chr.Psi l e (Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ) (ι g) =
    Loc.cycA l l c.O g ^ e * Chr.dirG ℚ Ω (comp l ψ) (ι g)

namespace Dat

variable {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] {K : Type} [Field K] [NumberField K] [Algebra K Ω]
  {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {c : Coef l} {N : ℕ} [NeZero N] {e : ℕ} {ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O N} (D : Dat Ω K c e ψ)

abbrev Ψ (_ : Dat Ω K c e ψ) : G ℚ Ω →* c.Oˣ := Chr.Psi l e (Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ)

def ΨQ : G ℚ Ω →* (PadicAlgCl l)ˣ := (Units.map (algebraMap c.O (PadicAlgCl l)).toMonoidHom).comp D.Ψ

abbrev loc : Loc.Dat3 l c.O where
  η := D.η.comp D.ι
  Ψ := D.Ψ.comp D.ι
  τ := (Chr.dirG ℚ Ω (comp l ψ)).comp D.ι
  e := e
  Kv := D.Kv
  odd := D.gd.l_odd
  e_pos := D.gd.e_pos
  η_ker := D.Kv_spec
  η_unr := D.η_unr
  τ_open := D.τ_open
  Ψ_cont n := by
    have h := (D.Ψ_cont 1 n).comp_continuous D.ι_cont
    simp only [zpow_one] at h
    exact h
  Ψ_I := D.Ψ_I

def Kill : Set (G ℚ Ω) :=
  {g | ∃ A : ValuationSubring Ω, ¬ A.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 ∧ g ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) A ∧ rbar c.O D.η D.Ψ g = 1}

abbrev ULoc : Type 1 := Univ (GCond.noCondition c.O (D.η.comp D.ι) (D.Ψ.comp D.ι))

abbrev Ring (U : D.ULoc) (g₁ : G ℚ Ω) : Type 1 := SRing D.Kill D.ι_cont U (D.loc.It U) D.cc g₁

abbrev Marked (g₁ : G ℚ Ω) : Prop := Marks D.η D.Ψ D.cc g₁

def rhoK {κ : Type} [CommRing κ] (r : G ℚ Ω →* GG 3 κ) (hr : HasMult D.η r) : G K Ω →* GL (Fin 3) κ :=
  (glPart D.η r hr).comp ((galEmb ℚ K Ω).codRestrict D.η.ker fun s => D.η_ker ▸ galEmb_mem ℚ K Ω s)

theorem rhoK_apply {κ : Type} [CommRing κ] (r : G ℚ Ω →* GG 3 κ) (hr : HasMult D.η r) (s : G K Ω) :
    D.rhoK r hr s = (r (galEmb ℚ K Ω s)).left := rfl

structure IsPt (U : D.ULoc) (g₁ : G ℚ Ω) (r : G ℚ Ω →* GG 3 (PadicAlgCl l)) : Prop where
  mult : HasMult D.η r
  cont : Continuous (mats r)
  int : ∀ g a b, ‖(r g).mat a b‖ ≤ 1
  red : ∀ g a b, ‖(r g).mat a b - (r0 D.η D.ΨQ g).mat a b‖ < 1
  norm : Normalised D.cc g₁ r
  kill : ∀ g ∈ D.Kill, r g = 1
  loc : ∃ f : U.R →ₐ[c.O] PadicAlgCl l, IsLocPt f ∧ D.loc.It U ≤ RingHom.ker f ∧ push f U.r.hom = r.comp D.ι

def IsExt {κ : Type} [CommRing κ] (r : G ℚ Ω →* GG 3 κ) (W : G ℚ Ω →* GL (Fin 2) κ) : Prop :=
  (∀ g ∈ D.η.ker, (W g : Matrix (Fin 2) (Fin 2) κ) = corner (r g).mat) ∧ (W D.cc : Matrix (Fin 2) (Fin 2) κ).trace = 0

def IsAutI [IsCMField K] (ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ) (r : G ℚ Ω →* GG 3 (PadicAlgCl l)) (hr : HasMult D.η r) : Prop :=
  Af.IsAutCsd ι (Af.tw (D.rhoK r hr) (cycBar K Ω l)⁻¹)

def IsModular (_ : Dat Ω K c e ψ) (ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ) (W : G ℚ Ω →* GL (Fin 2) (PadicAlgCl l)) : Prop :=
  ∃ f : Af.Newform (2 * e + 1), f.Att ι (Af.tw W (cycBar ℚ Ω l ^ (-(e : ℤ))))

end Dat


[OCH_SRC_EXISTS_DAT] Construction of all fields of the actual Dat from GD
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/UnitaryThreeGroups/ExistsDat.lean#L1-L178
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.UnitaryThreeGroups.KatoUse
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.PadicHodgeTheory.IotaFacts
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.PadicHodgeTheory.OpenField
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.HeightAndVanishing.CompIne
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.CharacterLevelBounds.ClsFacts
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.GaloisLibrary.ClassFieldRamification.IneAbs
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.GaloisLibrary.ClassFieldRamification.UnramIff

import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.GaloisLibrary.SelmerTransport.GalEmbTop
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.GaloisLibrary.SelmerTransport.ContTw

open NumberField IsDedekindDomain Formalism.ConjugateSelfDual ReciprocityTheory.Gal Formalism.MainNotions.N Formalism.BasicNotions Formalism.BasicNotions.Loc GlobalTheory.GalOfCusp Matrix IsLocalRing
open UpperTheory.V UpperTheory.V.Base UpperTheory.V.HV UpperTheory.V.Alg UpperTheory.V.U3

private theorem dx_cc (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] :
    ∃ σ : G ℚ Ω, ∃ φ : Ω →+* ℂ, ∀ x, φ (σ x) = starRingEnd ℂ (φ x) := by
  obtain ⟨σ, h⟩ := exists_isConjOm (E := ℚ) (Ω := Ω)
  exact ⟨AlgEquiv.ofRingEquiv (f := σ) fun x => by rw [eq_ratCast (algebraMap ℚ Ω), map_ratCast], h⟩

private theorem dx_ker (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] (K : Type) [Field K] [NumberField K] [Algebra K Ω]
    [IsGalois ℚ K] (σ : G ℚ Ω) : AlgEquiv.restrictNormalHom (F := ℚ) K σ = 1 ↔ σ ∈ galOf ℚ K Ω := by
  have h : ∀ x : K, algebraMap K Ω (AlgEquiv.restrictNormalHom (F := ℚ) K σ x) = σ (algebraMap K Ω x) :=
    fun x => AlgEquiv.restrictNormal_commutes σ K x
  refine ⟨fun h1 x => ?_, fun h1 => AlgEquiv.ext fun x => (algebraMap K Ω).injective ?_⟩
  · rw [← h, h1]; rfl
  · rw [h, h1]; rfl

private theorem dx_eta (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] (K : Type) [Field K] [NumberField K] [Algebra K Ω]
    [IsGalois ℚ K] (h2 : Module.finrank ℚ K = 2) : ∃ η : G ℚ Ω →* ℤˣ, η.ker = galOf ℚ K Ω := by
  have hc : Nat.card Gal(K/ℚ) = 2 := by rw [IsGalois.card_aut_eq_finrank, h2]
  have hz : Nat.card ℤˣ = 2 := by rw [Nat.card_eq_fintype_card, Fintype.card_units_int]
  let e : Gal(K/ℚ) ≃* ℤˣ := mulEquivOfPrimeCardEq hc hz
  refine ⟨e.toMonoidHom.comp (AlgEquiv.restrictNormalHom (F := ℚ) K), Subgroup.ext fun σ => ?_⟩
  rw [← dx_ker, MonoidHom.mem_ker, MonoidHom.comp_apply]
  exact map_eq_one_iff e e.injective

private theorem dx_unr (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (K : Type) [Field K] [NumberField K]
    [Algebra K Ω] [IsGalois ℚ K] (l : ℕ) [hl : Fact l.Prime] (h : ¬ (l : ℤ) ∣ discr K) (A : ValuationSubring Ω)
    (hA : A.valuation (l : Ω) < 1) : Ine (F := ℚ) A ≤ galOf ℚ K Ω := by
  haveI : IsAlgClosed Ω := IsAlgClosure.isAlgClosed ℚ
  haveI : Algebra.IsAlgebraic K Ω := Algebra.IsAlgebraic.tower_top (K := ℚ) K
  haveI : IsAlgClosure K Ω := ⟨inferInstance, inferInstance⟩
  obtain ⟨p1, p2, p3, -⟩ := places K Ω l A
  obtain ⟨w, hw⟩ := p1 fun ht => (p2 ht).2 hA
  have hm : ((l : ℕ) : 𝓞 K) ∈ w.asIdeal := (p3 w hw).1 hA
  have hb := (CF.unramIff.1 K l hl.out).1 ((CF.unramIff.2.1 K l hl.out).2 h) w hm
  intro σ hσ
  have h1 := Subgroup.mem_map_of_mem (CF.resTo ℚ K Ω) hσ
  rw [CF.ineAbs ℚ K Ω A w hw, hb, Subgroup.mem_bot] at h1
  exact (dx_ker Ω K σ).1 h1

private theorem dx_lc {Γ M : Type} [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace Γ] [IsTopologicalGroup Γ] [Group M] (f : Γ →* M)
    (h : IsOpen (f.ker : Set Γ)) : IsLocallyConstant f := by
  rw [IsLocallyConstant.iff_eventually_eq]
  intro x
  have hc : Continuous fun y : Γ => x⁻¹ * y := by fun_prop
  have h1 : (fun y : Γ => x⁻¹ * y) ⁻¹' (f.ker : Set Γ) ∈ nhds x :=
    (h.preimage hc).mem_nhds (by simp)
  filter_upwards [h1] with y hy
  have h2 : f (x⁻¹ * y) = 1 := hy
  rw [map_mul, map_inv, inv_mul_eq_one] at h2
  exact h2.symm

private theorem dx_zpow {Γ R : Type} [TopologicalSpace Γ] [CommRing R] (I : Ideal R)
    (Ψ : Γ → Rˣ) (h : AdicCont I fun g => (Ψ g : R)) (j : ℤ) : AdicCont I fun g => ((Ψ g ^ j : Rˣ) : R) := by
  classical
  intro N
  have key : (fun g => Ideal.Quotient.mk (I ^ N) ((Ψ g ^ j : Rˣ) : R)) =
      (fun x : R ⧸ I ^ N => if hx : IsUnit x then ((hx.unit ^ j : (R ⧸ I ^ N)ˣ) : R ⧸ I ^ N) else 0) ∘
        fun g => Ideal.Quotient.mk (I ^ N) (Ψ g : R) := by
    funext g
    have hx : IsUnit (Ideal.Quotient.mk (I ^ N) (Ψ g : R)) := (Ψ g).isUnit.map _
    have e : hx.unit = Units.map (Ideal.Quotient.mk (I ^ N)).toMonoidHom (Ψ g) := Units.ext rfl
    simp only [Function.comp_apply, dif_pos hx]
    rw [e, ← map_zpow]
    rfl
  rw [key]
  exact (h N).comp _

private theorem dx_alg {Γ R : Type} [TopologicalSpace Γ] [CommRing R] {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] [Algebra ℤ_[l] R]
    (I : Ideal R) (hI : (l : R) ∈ I) (f : Γ → ℤ_[l]) (hf : Continuous f) :
    AdicCont I fun g => algebraMap ℤ_[l] R (f g) := by
  intro n
  rw [IsLocallyConstant.iff_eventually_eq]
  intro x
  have hpos : (0 : ℝ) < (l : ℝ) ^ (-(n : ℤ)) := zpow_pos (by exact_mod_cast (Fact.out : l.Prime).pos) _
  filter_upwards [hf.continuousAt.eventually (Metric.closedBall_mem_nhds (f x) hpos)] with y hy
  rw [dist_eq_norm, PadicInt.norm_le_pow_iff_mem_span_pow, Ideal.mem_span_singleton'] at hy
  obtain ⟨a, ha⟩ := hy
  rw [Ideal.Quotient.eq, ← map_sub, ← ha, map_mul, map_pow, map_natCast]
  exact Ideal.mul_mem_left _ _ (Ideal.pow_mem_pow hI n)

private theorem dx_lmem (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (O : Type) [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] [Algebra ℤ_[l] O]
    [Module.Finite ℤ_[l] O] : (l : O) ∈ IsLocalRing.maximalIdeal O := by
  have h : (IsLocalRing.maximalIdeal O).comap (algebraMap ℤ_[l] O) = IsLocalRing.maximalIdeal ℤ_[l] :=
    IsLocalRing.eq_maximalIdeal (Ideal.isMaximal_comap_of_isIntegral_of_isMaximal _)
  have h1 : (l : ℤ_[l]) ∈ IsLocalRing.maximalIdeal ℤ_[l] := by
    rw [PadicInt.maximalIdeal_eq_span_p]; exact Ideal.mem_span_singleton_self _
  rw [← h, Ideal.mem_comap, map_natCast] at h1
  exact h1

private theorem dx_dir (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] {O : Type} [CommRing O] {N : ℕ} [NeZero N]
    (ψ : DirichletCharacter O N) : IsLocallyConstant (Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ) :=
  (dx_lc _ (Chr.clsFacts ℚ Ω N).1).comp ψ.toUnitHom

private theorem dx_cont (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime]
    {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] [Algebra ℤ_[l] O] [Module.Finite ℤ_[l] O]
    {N : ℕ} [NeZero N] (e : ℕ) (ψ : DirichletCharacter O N) : CharCont (Chr.Psi l e (Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ)) := by
  refine dx_zpow _ _ fun n => ?_
  have h1 : AdicCont (IsLocalRing.maximalIdeal O) fun g => (chiO ℚ Ω l O g : O) :=
    dx_alg _ (dx_lmem l O) (fun g => (cycZ ℚ Ω l g : ℤ_[l])) (Units.continuous_val.comp (contTw ℚ Ω l).1)
  have h2 := dx_zpow _ _ h1 (e : ℤ) n
  have h3 := (dx_dir Ω ψ).comp fun u : Oˣ => Ideal.Quotient.mk (IsLocalRing.maximalIdeal O ^ n) (u : O)
  have h4 := h2.mul h3
  convert h4 using 1
  funext g
  simp only [Chr.Psi, MonoidHom.mul_apply, Units.val_mul, map_mul, Pi.mul_apply, Function.comp_apply]
  rfl

private theorem dx_place {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] {l : ℕ} [hl : Fact l.Prime] (j : Ω →+* Om l) :
    (Loc.placeJ j).valuation (l : Ω) < 1 := by
  have h0 : (l : Ω) ≠ 0 := Nat.cast_ne_zero.2 hl.out.ne_zero
  have h1 : (l : Ω)⁻¹ ∉ Loc.placeJ j := by
    intro h
    have h2 : ‖j (l : Ω)⁻¹‖ ≤ 1 := h
    have h3 : j (l : Ω)⁻¹ = algebraMap ℚ_[l] (Om l) (l : ℚ_[l])⁻¹ := by simp
    rw [h3, PadicAlgCl.norm_extends, norm_inv, Padic.norm_p, inv_inv] at h2
    exact absurd hl.out.one_lt (by exact_mod_cast h2.not_gt)
  rw [← ValuationSubring.valuation_le_one_iff, map_inv₀, not_le,
    one_lt_inv₀ ((Valuation.pos_iff _).2 h0)] at h1
  exact h1

theorem UpperTheory.V.U3.exists_dat :
    ∀ {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] {K : Type} [Field K] [NumberField K] [Algebra K Ω]
      {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {c : Coef l} {N : ℕ} [NeZero N] {e : ℕ} {ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O N}, GD Ω K l e ψ → Nonempty (Dat Ω K c e ψ) := by
  intro Ω _ _ _ K _ _ _ l hl c N _ e ψ h
  haveI : Algebra.IsQuadraticExtension ℚ K := ⟨h.quad⟩
  haveI : IsGalois ℚ K := inferInstance
  haveI : IsAlgClosed Ω := IsAlgClosure.isAlgClosed ℚ
  haveI : Algebra.IsAlgebraic K Ω := Algebra.IsAlgebraic.tower_top (K := ℚ) K
  haveI : IsAlgClosure K Ω := ⟨inferInstance, inferInstance⟩
  obtain ⟨η, hη⟩ := dx_eta Ω K h.quad
  obtain ⟨cc, hcc⟩ := dx_cc Ω
  obtain ⟨j⟩ := Loc.exists_emb (Ω := Ω) (v := l)
  obtain ⟨i1, -, i3, -, i5, -⟩ := Loc.iotaFacts j
  have hA : (Loc.placeJ j).valuation (l : Ω) < 1 := dx_place j
  have hI : ∀ g ∈ Loc.Iq l, Loc.iota j g ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) (Loc.placeJ j) := fun g hg => by
    rw [← i3] at hg; exact hg
  have hopen : IsOpen (((η.comp (Loc.iota j)).ker : Subgroup (GQ l)) : Set (GQ l)) := by
    have h1 := (galEmbTop ℚ K Ω).2.1
    rw [← hη] at h1
    exact h1.preimage i1.continuous
  obtain ⟨Kv, -, -, -, -, hKv, -⟩ := Loc.openField (η.comp (Loc.iota j)).ker.subtype hopen.isOpenEmbedding_subtypeVal
  rw [Subgroup.range_subtype] at hKv
  have hd : ¬ (l : ℤ) ∣ discr K := fun hd => h.l_cop (Dvd.dvd.mul_right (Int.natCast_dvd.1 hd) _)
  exact ⟨{
    gd := h
    η := η
    η_ker := hη
    cc := cc
    conj := hcc
    j := j
    ι := Loc.iota j
    ι_cont := i1.continuous
    ι_spec := fun σ x => (Loc.iota_spec j σ x).symm
    Kv := Kv
    Kv_spec := fun g => by rw [← hKv]; rfl
    η_unr := fun g hg => by
      have h1 := dx_unr Ω K l hd _ hA (hI g hg)
      rw [← hη] at h1
      exact h1
    τ_open := ((dx_dir Ω (comp l ψ)).comp_continuous i1.continuous).isOpen_fiber 1
    Ψ_cont := dx_cont Ω e ψ
    Ψ_I := fun g hg => by
      rw [← ((compIne Ω N l hl.out _ hA).2 c.O ψ).1 _ (hI g hg), Chr.Psi, MonoidHom.mul_apply, zpow_natCast,
        MonoidHom.pow_apply, Loc.cycA, ← i5 l]
      rfl }⟩


[OCH_SRC_DAT_LOCAL] The specified embedding identifies decomposition and inertia groups
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/UnitaryThreeGroups/DatLoc.lean#L1-L45
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.UnitaryThreeGroups.KatoUse

open UpperTheory.V UpperTheory.V.Base UpperTheory.V.HV UpperTheory.V.Alg UpperTheory.V.U3

theorem UpperTheory.V.U3.dat_loc :
    ∀ {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] {K : Type} [Field K] [NumberField K] [Algebra K Ω]
      {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {c : Coef l} {N : ℕ} [NeZero N] {e : ℕ} {ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O N} (D : Dat Ω K c e ψ),
      ∃ A : ValuationSubring Ω, A.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 ∧ D.ι.range = Dec (F := ℚ) A ∧ (Loc.Iq l).map D.ι = Ine (F := ℚ) A ∧
        Function.Injective D.ι ∧ (∀ x : Ω, x ∈ A ↔ ‖D.j x‖ ≤ 1) ∧ (∀ σ, cycZ ℚ Ω l (D.ι σ) = Loc.cyc l l σ) ∧
        ∀ A' : ValuationSubring Ω, A'.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 → ∃ g : G ℚ Ω,
          (∀ s, s ∈ Dec (F := ℚ) A' ↔ g⁻¹ * s * g ∈ Dec (F := ℚ) A) ∧ ∀ s, s ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) A' ↔ g⁻¹ * s * g ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) A := by
  intro Ω _ _ _ K _ _ _ l _ c N _ e ψ D
  have hι : D.ι = Loc.iota D.j := by
    ext σ x
    apply D.j.injective
    rw [Loc.iota_spec, ← D.ι_spec]
  obtain ⟨hemb, hrange, -, hmap, hcyc, -, -⟩ := Loc.iotaFacts D.j
  have hmem : ∀ x : Ω, x ∈ Loc.placeJ D.j ↔ Valued.v (D.j x) ≤ 1 := fun x => Iff.rfl
  have hA : (Loc.placeJ D.j).valuation (l : Ω) < 1 := by
    have hnot : (l : Ω)⁻¹ ∉ Loc.placeJ D.j := by
      rw [hmem, map_inv₀, map_natCast, map_inv₀, PadicAlgCl.valuation_p, not_le]
      have hl : (1 : NNReal) < l := by exact_mod_cast (Fact.out : l.Prime).one_lt
      simpa using hl
    by_contra h
    push_neg at h
    apply hnot
    have hle : (Loc.placeJ D.j).valuation (l : Ω) ≤ 1 :=
      ((Loc.placeJ D.j).valuation_le_one_iff _).2 (natCast_mem _ l)
    rw [← ValuationSubring.valuation_le_one_iff, map_inv₀, le_antisymm hle h, inv_one]
  refine ⟨Loc.placeJ D.j, hA, ?_, ?_, ?_, ?_, ?_, ?_⟩
  · rw [hι]; exact hrange
  · rw [hι]; exact hmap
  · rw [hι]; exact hemb.injective
  · intro x
    rw [hmem, ← NNReal.coe_le_coe, PadicAlgCl.valuation_coe, NNReal.coe_one]
  · intro σ
    rw [hι]
    exact DFunLike.congr_fun (hcyc l) σ
  · intro A' hA'
    obtain ⟨-, -, -, -, -, -, -, hconj, -, hact⟩ := ML.placeBasic Ω
    obtain ⟨g, rfl⟩ := hconj (Loc.placeJ D.j) A' l Fact.out hA hA'
    exact ⟨g, (hact _ g).1, (hact _ g).2.1⟩


[OCH_SRC_DAT_FACTS] Original residual, killed-inertia and inertia-unit consequences of GD
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/UnitaryThreeGroups/DatFacts.lean#L1-L133
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.UnitaryThreeGroups.KatoUse

import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.HeightAndVanishing.GdIffIndex

open NumberField IsDedekindDomain Formalism.ConjugateSelfDual ReciprocityTheory.Gal Formalism.MainNotions.N Formalism.BasicNotions Formalism.BasicNotions.Loc GlobalTheory.GalOfCusp Matrix IsLocalRing
open UpperTheory.V UpperTheory.V.Base UpperTheory.V.HV UpperTheory.V.Alg UpperTheory.V.U3

private theorem locConst {Γ A : Type} [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace Γ] [IsTopologicalGroup Γ] [Group A] (ψ : Γ →* A)
    (h : IsOpen (ψ.ker : Set Γ)) : IsLocallyConstant ψ :=
  _root_.UpperTheory.Reformulated.B.LocallyConstantOfKernel.lc_of_ker ψ h

private theorem open_galOf (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (K : Type) [Field K] [NumberField K]
    [Algebra K Ω] : IsOpen (galOf ℚ K Ω : Set (G ℚ Ω)) := by
  let b := Module.finBasis ℚ K
  have e : (galOf ℚ K Ω : Set (G ℚ Ω)) = ⋂ i, {σ : G ℚ Ω | σ (algebraMap K Ω (b i)) = algebraMap K Ω (b i)} := by
    ext σ
    simp only [Set.mem_iInter, Set.mem_setOf_eq, SetLike.mem_coe, mem_galOf]
    refine ⟨fun h i => h _, fun h x => ?_⟩
    have := b.ext (f₁ := σ.toLinearMap ∘ₗ (IsScalarTower.toAlgHom ℚ K Ω).toLinearMap)
      (f₂ := (IsScalarTower.toAlgHom ℚ K Ω).toLinearMap) h
    exact LinearMap.congr_fun this x
  rw [e]
  exact isOpen_iInter_of_finite fun i => isOpen_setOf_apply (F := ℚ) _ (fun t => t = algebraMap K Ω (b i))

private theorem cyc_ine (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime]
    (A : ValuationSubring Ω) (hA : ¬ A.valuation (l : Ω) < 1) {σ : G ℚ Ω} (hσ : σ ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) A) :
    cycZ ℚ Ω l σ = 1 := by
  have : IsAlgClosed Ω := IsAlgClosure.isAlgClosed ℚ
  have : NeZero ((l : ℕ) : Ω) := ⟨Nat.cast_ne_zero.mpr (Fact.out : l.Prime).ne_zero⟩
  show cyclotomicCharacter Ω l σ.toRingEquiv = 1
  refine Units.ext (PadicInt.ext_of_toZModPow.mp fun n => ?_)
  obtain ⟨ζ, hζ⟩ := HasEnoughRootsOfUnity.exists_primitiveRoot Ω (l ^ n)
  have hN0 : l ^ n ≠ 0 := pow_ne_zero _ (Fact.out : l.Prime).ne_zero
  have : NeZero (l ^ n) := ⟨hN0⟩
  have hspec := cyclotomicCharacter.spec (L := Ω) l σ.toRingEquiv ζ hζ.pow_eq_one
  have hv : ¬ A.valuation ((l ^ n : ℕ) : Ω) < 1 := by
    rw [Nat.cast_pow, map_pow, not_lt]
    exact one_le_pow₀ (not_lt.1 hA)
  have h1 : Chr.cls ℚ Ω (l ^ n) σ = 1 := (Chr.clsFacts ℚ Ω (l ^ n)).2.2.2.2 A hv hσ
  have h2 := Chr.cls_spec (l ^ n) σ ζ hζ.pow_eq_one
  rw [h1] at h2
  have h3 := hζ.pow_inj (ZMod.val_lt _) (ZMod.val_lt _) (hspec.symm.trans h2)
  rw [ZMod.val_injective _ h3]
  simp

private theorem tor_eq_one {R : Type} [CommRing R] {t : Rˣ} (h : tor R t = 1) : t = 1 := by
  have h1 := congrArg (fun x : GL (Fin 3) R => (x : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) R) 0 0) h
  apply Units.ext
  simpa [tor_val, wt1] using h1

private theorem diagonal_rep_eq_one_iff {Γ R : Type} [Group Γ] [CommRing R] (η : Γ →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : Γ →* Rˣ) (g : Γ) :
    r0 η Ψ g = 1 ↔ η g = 1 ∧ Ψ g = 1 := by
  refine ⟨fun h => ⟨congrArg SemidirectProduct.right h, tor_eq_one (congrArg SemidirectProduct.left h)⟩, fun h => ?_⟩
  refine SemidirectProduct.ext ?_ h.1
  rw [unitaryThreeReflection_left, h.2, map_one]
  rfl

private theorem bar_lc {O Γ : Type} [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace Γ] (Ψ : Γ →* Oˣ)
    (hΨ : CharCont Ψ) : IsLocallyConstant (barChar O Ψ) := by
  refine IsLocallyConstant.desc _ Units.val ?_ Units.val_injective
  have h := (hΨ 1 1).comp (Ideal.Quotient.factor (pow_one (maximalIdeal O)).le)
  simp only [zpow_one] at h
  exact h

theorem UpperTheory.V.U3.dat_facts :
    ∀ {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] {K : Type} [Field K] [NumberField K] [Algebra K Ω]
      {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {c : Coef l} {N : ℕ} [NeZero N] {e : ℕ} {ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O N} (D : Dat Ω K c e ψ),
      D.cc * D.cc = 1 ∧ D.η D.cc = -1 ∧ D.Ψ D.cc = 1 ∧ IsLocallyConstant D.η ∧ IsLocallyConstant (barChar c.O D.Ψ) ∧
      (∀ g ∈ D.Kill, r0 D.η D.Ψ g = 1) ∧ IsUnit (2 : c.O) ∧ IsCMField K ∧ Continuous D.ΨQ ∧
      (∀ g, g ∈ D.Kill ↔ ∃ A : ValuationSubring Ω, ¬ A.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 ∧ g ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) A ∧ D.η g = 1 ∧ Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ g = 1) ∧
      (∀ A : ValuationSubring Ω, A.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 → Ine (F := ℚ) A ≤ D.η.ker ∧
        ∃ s ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) A, IsUnit ((D.Ψ s : c.O) ^ 2 - 1)) := by
  intro Ω _ _ _ K _ _ _ l hl c N _ e ψ D
  obtain ⟨w, η', a, hlit⟩ := (gdIff Ω K l c e N ψ).1.1 D.gd
  have himag := D.gd.imag
  have hΨ : ∀ A : ValuationSubring Ω, ¬ A.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 → ∀ g ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) A,
      D.Ψ g = Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ g := fun A hA g hg => by
    show (chiO ℚ Ω l c.O g) ^ (e : ℤ) * Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ g = _
    have : chiO ℚ Ω l c.O g = 1 := by
      unfold chiO
      rw [MonoidHom.comp_apply, cyc_ine Ω l A hA hg, map_one]
    rw [this, _root_.one_zpow, one_mul]
  have hkill : ∀ g, g ∈ D.Kill ↔ ∃ A : ValuationSubring Ω, ¬ A.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 ∧ g ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) A ∧
      D.η g = 1 ∧ Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ g = 1 := fun g => by
    refine exists_congr fun A => and_congr_right fun hA => and_congr_right fun hg => ?_
    unfold rbar
    rw [diagonal_rep_eq_one_iff]
    refine and_congr_right fun _ => ?_
    unfold barChar
    rw [MonoidHom.comp_apply, hΨ A hA g hg]
    refine ⟨fun h => ?_, fun h => by rw [h, map_one]⟩
    refine (congrFacts Ω l c).2.2.2.1 _ 1 _ hlit.ord ?_ (one_pow _) ?_
    · rw [← MonoidHom.pow_apply, pow_orderOf_eq_one, MonoidHom.one_apply]
    · rw [← residue_eq_zero_iff, map_sub, sub_eq_zero, Units.val_one, map_one]
      exact congrArg Units.val h
  obtain ⟨φ, hφ⟩ := D.conj
  refine ⟨?_, ?_, hlit.par D.cc ⟨φ, hφ⟩, ?_, ?_, fun g hg => ?_, ?_, ?_, ?_, hkill, fun A hA => ?_⟩
  · refine AlgEquiv.ext fun x => φ.injective ?_
    rw [AlgEquiv.mul_apply, hφ, hφ]
    simp
  · refine (Int.units_eq_one_or _).resolve_left fun h => ?_
    have h1 : D.cc ∈ galOf ℚ K Ω := D.η_ker ▸ h
    refine IsTotallyComplex.complexEmbedding_not_isReal (φ.comp (algebraMap K Ω)) ?_
    rw [ComplexEmbedding.isReal_iff]
    ext x
    rw [ComplexEmbedding.conjugate_coe_eq, RingHom.comp_apply, ← hφ, h1 x]
  · exact locConst _ (D.η_ker ▸ open_galOf Ω K)
  · exact bar_lc _ D.Ψ_cont
  · obtain ⟨A, hA, hg, h1, h2⟩ := (hkill g).1 hg
    exact (diagonal_rep_eq_one_iff _ _ _).2 ⟨h1, (hΨ A hA g hg).trans h2⟩
  · have := (dvrFacts l c.O).2.2.2.2.2.2.2.2 2 fun h => D.gd.l_odd ((Nat.prime_dvd_prime_iff_eq hl.out Nat.prime_two).1 h)
    simpa using this
  · have : Algebra.IsQuadraticExtension ℚ K := ⟨D.gd.quad⟩
    exact IsCMField.ofCMExtension ℚ K
  · have hf : Continuous (algebraMap ℤ_[l] (PadicAlgCl l)).toMonoidHom := by
      refine ((continuous_algebraMap ℚ_[l] (PadicAlgCl l)).comp continuous_subtype_val).congr fun x => ?_
      exact (IsScalarTower.algebraMap_apply ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] (PadicAlgCl l) x).symm
    have h1 := ((hf.units_map _).comp (continuous_cycZ ℚ Ω l)).zpow (e : ℤ)
    have h2 := (((locConst _ (Chr.clsFacts ℚ Ω N).1).comp ψ.toUnitHom).comp
      (Units.map (algebraMap c.O (PadicAlgCl l)).toMonoidHom)).continuous
    refine (h1.mul h2).congr fun g => ?_
    show _ = Units.map (algebraMap c.O (PadicAlgCl l)).toMonoidHom (chiO ℚ Ω l c.O g ^ (e : ℤ) * Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ g)
    rw [map_mul, map_zpow]
    rfl
  · obtain ⟨h1, s, hs, h2⟩ := (gdLoc Ω K l c e N ψ w η' a hlit).1 A hA
    refine ⟨D.η_ker ▸ h1, s, hs, ?_⟩
    rw [Units.val_pow_eq_pow_val] at h2
    exact not_not.1 h2


[OCH_SRC_LOCAL_UNIVERSAL] Actual local universal lift from local finite-cohomology and Phi
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/UnitaryThreeGroups/ULocEx.lean#L1-L51
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.UnitaryThreeGroups.KatoUse
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.UnitaryThreeGroups.UnivEx
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.UnitaryThreeGroups.DatFacts
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.PadicHodgeTheory.PhiCond
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.GaloisLibrary.SelmerTransport.DvrFacts

open NumberField IsDedekindDomain Formalism.ConjugateSelfDual ReciprocityTheory.Gal Formalism.MainNotions.N Formalism.BasicNotions Formalism.BasicNotions.Loc GlobalTheory.GalOfCusp Matrix IsLocalRing
open UpperTheory.V UpperTheory.V.Base UpperTheory.V.HV UpperTheory.V.Alg UpperTheory.V.U3

private theorem phiCond_quot {Γ : Type} [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace Γ] (l : ℕ) (M : Subgroup Γ) [M.Normal]
    (h : PhiCond Γ l) : PhiCond (Γ ⧸ M) l := by
  intro H hH
  have hc : Continuous (QuotientGroup.mk' M) := QuotientGroup.continuous_mk
  haveI := h (H.comap (QuotientGroup.mk' M)) (hH.preimage hc)
  let π : H.comap (QuotientGroup.mk' M) →* H := (QuotientGroup.mk' M).subgroupComap H
  have hπ : Continuous π := (hc.comp continuous_subtype_val).subtype_mk _
  have hs : Function.Surjective π :=
    MonoidHom.subgroupComap_surjective_of_surjective _ _ (QuotientGroup.mk'_surjective M)
  refine Finite.of_injective (β := {φ : H.comap (QuotientGroup.mk' M) →* Multiplicative (ZMod l) //
    IsOpen (φ.ker : Set (H.comap (QuotientGroup.mk' M)))}) (fun φ => ⟨φ.1.comp π, φ.2.preimage hπ⟩) ?_
  intro φ φ' e
  exact Subtype.ext ((MonoidHom.cancel_right hs).1 (congrArg Subtype.val e))

private theorem exists_univ_loc (hP : Loc.LocalPublishedTheorems) (l : ℕ) [hl : Fact l.Prime] (O : Type) [CommRing O] [IsDomain O]
    [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [CharZero O] [Algebra ℤ_[l] O] [Module.Finite ℤ_[l] O] (η : GQ l →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : GQ l →* Oˣ)
    (hη : IsLocallyConstant η) (hΨ : IsLocallyConstant (barChar O Ψ)) : Nonempty (Univ (GCond.noCondition O η Ψ)) := by
  haveI : IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal O) O := (dvrFacts l O).2.2.2.1
  haveI : IsLocalHom (algebraMap ℤ_[l] O) := (dvrFacts l O).2.2.1
  haveI : Finite (ResidueField O) := by
    haveI : Finite (ResidueField ℤ_[l]) := Finite.of_equiv _ (PadicInt.residueField (p := l)).symm.toEquiv
    exact Module.finite_of_finite (ResidueField ℤ_[l])
  haveI : CharP (ResidueField O) l := by
    refine (CharP.charP_iff_prime_eq_zero hl.out).2 ?_
    rw [← map_natCast (residue O), residue_eq_zero_iff, ← map_natCast (algebraMap ℤ_[l] O)]
    refine map_nonunit _ _ ?_
    rw [PadicInt.maximalIdeal_eq_span_p]
    exact Ideal.mem_span_singleton_self _
  haveI : IsGalois ℚ_[l] (Om l) := IsAlgClosure.isGalois _ _
  haveI : (Subgroup.normalClosure (∅ : Set (GQ l))).topologicalClosure.Normal :=
    Subgroup.is_normal_topologicalClosure _
  obtain ⟨U⟩ := exists_univ l η Ψ hη hΨ ∅ (fun g hg => hg.elim) _ rfl
    (phiCond_quot l _ fun H hH => Loc.phiCond hP l l H hH)
  refine ⟨{ R := U.R, r := U.r, rep := fun R' _ _ _ => ⟨fun _ => trivial, fun r' _ => ?_⟩ }⟩
  exact (U.rep R').2 r' fun g hg => hg.elim

theorem UpperTheory.V.U3.exists_uLoc :
    CohInp → ∀ {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] {K : Type} [Field K] [NumberField K] [Algebra K Ω]
      {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {c : Coef l} {N : ℕ} [NeZero N] {e : ℕ} {ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O N} (D : Dat Ω K c e ψ), Nonempty D.ULoc := by
  intro hC Ω _ _ _ K _ _ _ l hl c N _ e ψ D
  obtain ⟨-, -, -, hη, hΨ, -⟩ := dat_facts D
  exact exists_univ_loc hC.loc l c.O _ _ (hη.comp_continuous D.ι_cont) (hΨ.comp_continuous D.ι_cont)


[OCH_SRC_GLOBAL_RING] Actual normalized global ring and its universal property for every original target
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/UnitaryThreeGroups/RingEx.lean#L1-L153
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.UnitaryThreeGroups.KatoUse
