private def permR (R : Type) [CommRing R] {Γ : Type} [Group Γ] (H : Subgroup Γ) :
    Representation R Γ (Γ ⧸ H → R) where
  toFun g := LinearMap.funLeft R R fun x => g⁻¹ • x
  map_one' := by ext f x; simp
  map_mul' g h := by ext f x; simp [mul_smul]

private theorem permR_apply (R : Type) [CommRing R] {Γ : Type} [Group Γ] (H : Subgroup Γ) (g : Γ) (f : Γ ⧸ H → R)
    (x : Γ ⧸ H) : permR R H g f x = f (g⁻¹ • x) := rfl

private theorem sec_mem {Γ : Type} [Group Γ] (H : Subgroup Γ) (g : Γ) (x : Γ ⧸ H) :
    (x.out)⁻¹ * g * (g⁻¹ • x).out ∈ H := by
  have h : ((g⁻¹ * x.out : Γ) : Γ ⧸ H) = (((g⁻¹ • x).out : Γ) : Γ ⧸ H) := by
    rw [QuotientGroup.out_eq', ← smul_eq_mul, ← MulAction.Quotient.smul_mk, QuotientGroup.out_eq']
  simpa [mul_assoc] using QuotientGroup.eq.1 h

private theorem phi_condition_of_finite_cohomology {Γ : Type} [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace Γ] [IsTopologicalGroup Γ] [CompactSpace Γ] (n : ℕ)
    [NeZero n]
    (h : ∀ (M : Type) [AddCommGroup M] [Module (ZMod n) M] [TopologicalSpace M] [DiscreteTopology M] [Finite M]
      (ρ : Representation (ZMod n) Γ M), ContAction ρ → Finite (H1 ρ)) : PhiCond Γ n := by
  intro H hH
  haveI : DiscreteTopology (Γ ⧸ H) := QuotientGroup.discreteTopology hH
  haveI : Finite (Γ ⧸ H) := finite_of_compact_of_discrete
  letI : TopologicalSpace (ZMod n) := ⊥
  haveI : DiscreteTopology (ZMod n) := ⟨rfl⟩
  have hs : ∀ x : Γ ⧸ H, Continuous fun g : Γ => g⁻¹ • x := fun x => continuous_inv.smul continuous_const
  have hc : ContAction (permR (ZMod n) H) := by
    refine continuous_pi fun x => ?_
    have h1 : Continuous fun p : (Γ ⧸ H → ZMod n) × (Γ ⧸ H) => p.1 p.2 := continuous_of_discreteTopology
    exact h1.comp (continuous_snd.prodMk ((hs x).comp continuous_fst))
  haveI h1 := h _ _ hc
  haveI : Finite (Z1 (permR (ZMod n) H)) := by
    have hB : Finite (B1 (permR (ZMod n) H)) := (Set.finite_range _).to_subtype
    have hB' : Finite ((B1 (permR (ZMod n) H)).comap (Z1 (permR (ZMod n) H)).subtype).toAddSubgroup :=
      Finite.of_injective (fun z => (⟨z.1.1, z.2⟩ : B1 (permR (ZMod n) H)))
        fun a b hab => Subtype.ext (Subtype.ext (Subtype.mk.inj hab))
    exact @Finite.of_addSubgroup_quotient _ _ _ hB' h1
  have hφ : ∀ φ : H →* Multiplicative (ZMod n), IsOpen (φ.ker : Set H) → Continuous φ := fun φ hφ =>
    continuous_of_continuousAt_one φ (by
      rw [ContinuousAt, map_one, nhds_discrete (Multiplicative (ZMod n)), Filter.tendsto_pure]
      exact hφ.mem_nhds φ.ker.one_mem)
  let c : {φ : H →* Multiplicative (ZMod n) // IsOpen (φ.ker : Set H)} → Z1 (permR (ZMod n) H) := fun φ =>
    ⟨fun g x => Multiplicative.toAdd (φ.1 ⟨_, sec_mem H g x⟩), by
      refine ⟨continuous_pi fun x => continuous_toAdd.comp ((hφ φ.1 φ.2).comp (Continuous.subtype_mk ?_ _)), fun g g' => ?_⟩
      · exact (continuous_const.mul continuous_id).mul
          ((continuous_of_discreteTopology (f := fun y : Γ ⧸ H => y.out)).comp (hs x))
      · funext x
        rw [Pi.add_apply, permR_apply, ← toAdd_mul, ← map_mul]
        refine congrArg (fun u => Multiplicative.toAdd (φ.1 u)) (Subtype.ext ?_)
        simp [mul_smul, mul_assoc]⟩
  refine Finite.of_injective c fun φ φ' e => Subtype.ext (MonoidHom.ext fun u => ?_)
  let x : Γ ⧸ H := ((1 : Γ) : Γ ⧸ H)
  have hx : x.out ∈ H := by
    have := QuotientGroup.eq.1 (QuotientGroup.out_eq' x).symm
    simpa using this
  have hfix : (x.out * u * (x.out)⁻¹)⁻¹ • x = x := by
    change (((x.out * u * (x.out)⁻¹)⁻¹ * 1 : Γ) : Γ ⧸ H) = ((1 : Γ) : Γ ⧸ H)
    rw [QuotientGroup.eq]
    simpa using H.mul_mem (H.mul_mem hx u.2) (H.inv_mem hx)
  have hu : (⟨_, sec_mem H (x.out * u * (x.out)⁻¹) x⟩ : H) = u := by
    apply Subtype.ext
    simp only [hfix]
    group
  have := congrFun (congrFun (congrArg Subtype.val e) (x.out * u * (x.out)⁻¹)) x
  simp only [c, hu] at this
  exact Multiplicative.toAdd.injective this

private theorem phi_surj {Γ Γ' : Type} [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace Γ] [Group Γ'] [TopologicalSpace Γ'] (π : Γ →* Γ')
    (hc : Continuous π) (hπ : Function.Surjective π) {l : ℕ} (h : PhiCond Γ l) : PhiCond Γ' l := fun H hH => by
  haveI := h (H.comap π) (hH.preimage hc)
  let p : H.comap π →* H := π.subgroupComap H
  have hp : Continuous p := (hc.comp continuous_subtype_val).subtype_mk _
  have hs : Function.Surjective p := MonoidHom.subgroupComap_surjective_of_surjective _ _ hπ
  refine Finite.of_injective
    (β := {φ : H.comap π →* Multiplicative (ZMod l) // IsOpen (φ.ker : Set (H.comap π))})
    (fun φ => ⟨φ.1.comp p, φ.2.preimage hp⟩) fun φ φ' e => ?_
  exact Subtype.ext ((MonoidHom.cancel_right hs).1 (congrArg Subtype.val e))

private theorem phi_unr (hGF : GlobalFiniteness) {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (l : ℕ)
    [hl : Fact l.Prime] (S : Finset (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 ℚ))) (hS : ContainsAbove S l) (M : Subgroup (G ℚ Ω)) [M.Normal]
    (hM : NS Ω (S : Set (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 ℚ))) ≤ M) : PhiCond (G ℚ Ω ⧸ M) l := by
  haveI : IsGalois ℚ Ω := IsAlgClosure.isGalois ℚ Ω
  haveI hK : CompactSpace (GS Ω (S : Set (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 ℚ)))) := inferInstance
  haveI : NeZero (l ^ 1) := ⟨pow_ne_zero _ hl.out.ne_zero⟩
  have key : PhiCond (GS Ω (S : Set (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 ℚ)))) (l ^ 1) := phi_condition_of_finite_cohomology (l ^ 1) fun M _ _ _ _ _ ρ hρ => by
    obtain ⟨-, f1, -⟩ := hGF ℚ Ω l 1 M S ρ one_pos hS hρ
    exact f1
  rw [pow_one] at key
  refine phi_surj (QuotientGroup.lift _ (QuotientGroup.mk' M) fun g hg => (QuotientGroup.eq_one_iff g).2 (hM hg))
    ((QuotientGroup.isQuotientMap_mk _).continuous_iff.2 continuous_quotient_mk') (fun y => ?_) key
  obtain ⟨g, rfl⟩ := QuotientGroup.mk_surjective y
  exact ⟨QuotientGroup.mk g, rfl⟩

end UpperTheory.V.U3

theorem UpperTheory.V.U3.exists_ring :
    CohInp → ∀ {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] {K : Type} [Field K] [NumberField K] [Algebra K Ω]
      {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {c : Coef l} {N : ℕ} [NeZero N] {e : ℕ} {ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O N} (D : Dat Ω K c e ψ) (U : D.ULoc) (g₁ : G ℚ Ω), D.Marked g₁ → Nonempty (D.Ring U g₁) := by
  intro hI Ω _ _ _ K _ _ _ l hl c N hN e ψ D U g₁ hg₁
  haveI : IsGalois ℚ Ω := IsAlgClosure.isGalois ℚ Ω
  obtain ⟨hc, hf, hlm, -⟩ := UpperTheory.V.abelian₂.dvr_finite l c.O
  haveI := hc
  haveI : Finite (ResidueField c.O) := hf
  haveI : CharP (ResidueField c.O) l := (CharP.charP_iff_prime_eq_zero hl.out).2 (by
    rw [← map_natCast (residue c.O), residue_eq_zero_iff]; exact hlm)
  obtain ⟨-, -, -, hη, hΨ, hK, -, -, -, hKill, -⟩ := dat_facts D
  haveI : IsScalarTower ℚ K Ω := IsScalarTower.of_algebraMap_eq' (Subsingleton.elim _ _)
  haveI : IsAlgClosed Ω := IsAlgClosure.isAlgClosed ℚ
  haveI : IsAlgClosure K Ω := ⟨inferInstance, Algebra.IsAlgebraic.tower_top (K := ℚ) K⟩
  obtain ⟨T, hT⟩ := (ramFinite ℚ ℚ K Ω).1
  obtain ⟨-, -, -, -, hIne⟩ := Chr.clsFacts ℚ Ω N
  have hfin : {v : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 ℚ) | v.asIdeal ∣ Ideal.span {((l * N : ℕ) : 𝓞 ℚ)}}.Finite :=
    Ideal.finite_factors (by simpa using ⟨hl.out.ne_zero, hN.out⟩)
  classical
  have hmem : ∀ v, ((l * N : ℕ) : 𝓞 ℚ) ∈ v.asIdeal → v ∈ T ∪ hfin.toFinset := fun v hv =>
    Finset.mem_union_right _ (hfin.mem_toFinset.2 (Ideal.dvd_span_singleton.2 hv))
  have hval : ∀ (A : ValuationSubring Ω) (v : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 ℚ)), IsPlaceOver A v → ∀ n : ℕ,
      A.valuation (n : Ω) < 1 → ((n : ℕ) : 𝓞 ℚ) ∈ v.asIdeal := by
    intro A v hv n hn
    have h := hv ((n : ℕ) : 𝓞 ℚ)
    have e : algebraMap ℚ Ω (((n : ℕ) : 𝓞 ℚ) : ℚ) = (n : Ω) := by simp
    rw [e] at h
    exact h.1 hn
  haveI : (Subgroup.normalClosure D.Kill).topologicalClosure.Normal := Subgroup.is_normal_topologicalClosure _
  have hΦ : PhiCond (G ℚ Ω ⧸ (Subgroup.normalClosure D.Kill).topologicalClosure) l := by
    refine phi_unr hI.gf l (T ∪ hfin.toFinset) (fun v hv => hmem v ?_) _
      (Subgroup.topologicalClosure_mono (Subgroup.normalClosure_mono fun g hg => ?_))
    · rw [Nat.cast_mul]
      exact Ideal.mul_mem_right _ _ hv
    · simp only [Set.mem_iUnion] at hg
      obtain ⟨v, hv, A, hA, hg⟩ := hg
      have hv' : ((l * N : ℕ) : 𝓞 ℚ) ∉ v.asIdeal := fun h => hv (hmem v h)
      rw [Nat.cast_mul] at hv'
      refine (hKill g).2 ⟨A, fun h => hv' (v.asIdeal.mul_mem_right _ (hval A v hA l h)), hg, ?_, ?_⟩
      · rw [← MonoidHom.mem_ker, D.η_ker]
        exact hT v (fun h => hv (Finset.mem_union_left _ h)) A hA ⟨by rw [galOf_self]; trivial, hg⟩
      · have h1 : Chr.cls ℚ Ω N g = 1 := hIne A (fun h => hv' (v.asIdeal.mul_mem_left _ (hval A v hA N h))) hg
        simp only [Chr.dirG, MonoidHom.comp_apply, h1, map_one]
  exact (exists_sRing D.ι_cont U (D.loc.It U) D.cc g₁ hg₁ (Loc.locGood hI.loc D.loc U) D.Ψ_cont hK
    (exists_univ l D.η D.Ψ hη hΨ D.Kill (fun g ⟨_, _, _, h⟩ => h) _ rfl hΦ)).2


[OCH_SRC_GLOBAL_PREDICATES] The original representation, entry algebra, push, tangent, rigidity and strict local point predicates
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/UnitaryThreeGroups/GRepresents.lean#L19-L171
    simp [GG.mat, h]
  map_mul' x y := by
    have h : ((-1 : ℤˣ) = 1) = False := by decide
    obtain ⟨a, s⟩ := x
    obtain ⟨b, t⟩ := y
    rcases Int.units_eq_one_or s with rfl | rfl <;> rcases Int.units_eq_one_or t with rfl | rfl <;>
      simp [GG.mat, h, theta_mul, theta_theta, Matrix.fromBlocks_multiply]

def wt6 : Fin 3 ⊕ Fin 3 → ℤ := Sum.elim wt1 wt1

end Emb

section Lift

variable (O : Type) [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] {Γ : Type} [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace Γ]

def barChar (Ψ : Γ →* Oˣ) : Γ →* (ResidueField O)ˣ := (Units.map (residue O).toMonoidHom).comp Ψ

variable (η : Γ →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : Γ →* Oˣ)

def rbar : Γ →* GG 3 (ResidueField O) := r0 η (barChar O Ψ)

@[ext] structure GLift (R : Type) [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] [IsCO O R] where
  hom : Γ →* GG 3 R
  cont : ∀ a b, AdicCont (maximalIdeal R) fun g => (hom g).mat a b
  red : ∀ g, GG.map (res O R : R →+* ResidueField O) (hom g) = rbar O η Ψ g

variable {O η Ψ} {R R' : Type} [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] [IsCO O R] [CommRing R'] [Algebra O R'] [IsCO O R']

theorem GLift.hasMult (r : GLift O η Ψ R) : HasMult η r.hom := fun g => congrArg SemidirectProduct.right (r.red g)

theorem GG.map_map {m : ℕ} {A B C : Type*} [CommRing A] [CommRing B] [CommRing C] (f : A →+* B) (f' : B →+* C)
    (x : GG m A) : GG.map f' (GG.map f x) = GG.map (f'.comp f) x := rfl

def GLift.map (f : R →ₐ[O] R') (r : GLift O η Ψ R) : GLift O η Ψ R' where
  hom := (GG.map (f : R →+* R')).comp r.hom
  cont a b N := by
    have hle : maximalIdeal R ^ N ≤ (maximalIdeal R' ^ N).comap (f : R →+* R') := by
      refine le_trans (Ideal.pow_right_mono (fun y hy => ?_) N) (Ideal.le_comap_pow _ N)
      show f y ∈ maximalIdeal R'
      rw [← res_eq_zero_iff O, res_map, res_eq_zero_iff]
      exact hy
    exact (r.cont a b N).comp (Ideal.quotientMap _ (f : R →+* R') hle)
  red g := by
    have e : (res O R' : R' →+* ResidueField O).comp (f : R →+* R') = (res O R : R →+* ResidueField O) :=
      RingHom.ext fun x => res_map f x
    rw [MonoidHom.comp_apply, GG.map_map, e, r.red]

def GLift.comap {H : Type} [Group H] [TopologicalSpace H] (φ : H →* Γ) (hφ : Continuous φ) (r : GLift O η Ψ R) :
    GLift O (η.comp φ) (Ψ.comp φ) R where
  hom := r.hom.comp φ
  cont a b N := (r.cont a b N).comp_continuous hφ
  red g := r.red (φ g)

variable (O η Ψ) in

def GCond : Type 1 := ∀ (R : Type) [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] [IsCO O R], GLift O η Ψ R → Prop

variable (O η Ψ) in

def GCond.noCondition : GCond O η Ψ := fun _ _ _ _ _ => True

def GRepresents (P : GCond O η Ψ) (r : GLift O η Ψ R) : Prop :=
  ∀ (R' : Type) [CommRing R'] [Algebra O R'] [IsCO O R'],
    (∀ f : R →ₐ[O] R', P R' (r.map f)) ∧ ∀ r' : GLift O η Ψ R', P R' r' → ∃! f : R →ₐ[O] R', r.map f = r'

structure Univ (P : GCond O η Ψ) : Type 1 where
  R : Type
  [cr : CommRing R]
  [al : Algebra O R]
  [co : IsCO O R]
  r : GLift O η Ψ R
  rep : GRepresents P r

attribute [instance] Univ.cr Univ.al Univ.co

def OnI (U : Univ (GCond.noCondition O η Ψ)) (I : Ideal U.R) : GCond O η Ψ :=
  fun R _ _ _ r => ∃ f : U.R →ₐ[O] R, U.r.map f = r ∧ I ≤ RingHom.ker f

def Kills (N : Set Γ) : GCond O η Ψ := fun _ _ _ _ r => ∀ g ∈ N, r.hom g = 1

def BoxCond (N : Set Γ) {H : Type} [Group H] [TopologicalSpace H] (ι : H →* Γ) (hι : Continuous ι)
    (U : Univ (GCond.noCondition O (η.comp ι) (Ψ.comp ι))) (I : Ideal U.R) : GCond O η Ψ :=
  fun R _ _ _ r => Kills N R r ∧ OnI U I R (r.comap ι hι)

def NormCond (c g₁ : Γ) (P : GCond O η Ψ) : GCond O η Ψ :=
  fun R _ _ _ r => Normalised c g₁ r.hom ∧ P R r

end Lift

section Points

variable (O : Type) [CommRing O] {Γ R : Type} [Group Γ] [CommRing R] [Algebra O R]

def entAlg (r : Γ →* GG 3 R) : Subalgebra O R := Algebra.adjoin O {x | ∃ g a b, x = (r g).mat a b}

def entMon (r : Γ →* GG 3 R) (j : ℤ) : Set R :=
  {x | ∃ L : List (Γ × Fin 3 × Fin 3), (L.map fun s => wt s.2.1 s.2.2).sum = j ∧
    x = (L.map fun s => (r s.1).mat s.2.1 s.2.2).prod}

def weightZeroSubalgebra (r : Γ →* GG 3 R) : Subalgebra O R := Algebra.adjoin O (entMon r 0)

variable {O} {κ : Type} [CommRing κ] [Algebra O κ]

def push (f : R →ₐ[O] κ) (r : Γ →* GG 3 R) : Γ →* GG 3 κ := (GG.map (f : R →+* κ)).comp r

def IsPush {A : Subalgebra O R} (ξ : A →ₐ[O] κ) (r : Γ →* GG 3 R) (r' : Γ →* GG 3 κ) : Prop :=
  ∀ g, (r' g).right = (r g).right ∧ ∀ a b, ∃ h : (r g).mat a b ∈ A, (r' g).mat a b = ξ ⟨_, h⟩

def tanCoc (F : R →ₐ[O] DualNumber κ) (r : Γ →* GG 3 R) (g : Γ) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) κ :=
  ((r g).mat.map fun x => (F x).snd) *
    (((r g).left⁻¹ : GL (Fin 3) R) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) R).map fun x => (F x).fst

def dualFst (F : R →ₐ[O] DualNumber κ) : R →ₐ[O] κ := (TrivSqZeroExt.fstHom O κ κ).comp F

def Rigid (r : Γ →* GG 3 R) (f : R →ₐ[O] κ) : Prop :=
  ∀ F : R →ₐ[O] DualNumber κ, dualFst F = f → ∃ X, tanCoc F r = cob (push f r) X

def RigidWt (j : ℤ) (r : Γ →* GG 3 R) (f : R →ₐ[O] κ) : Prop :=
  ∀ F : R →ₐ[O] DualNumber κ, dualFst F = f → (∀ g, tanCoc F r g ∈ gw κ j) → ∃ X, tanCoc F r = cob (push f r) X

def IsLocPt [IsLocalRing R] {κ : Type} [NormedField κ] [Algebra O κ] (f : R →ₐ[O] κ) : Prop :=
  ∀ x ∈ maximalIdeal R, ‖f x‖ < 1

end Points

section Base

variable (O : Type) [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] [IsNoetherianRing O] [IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal O) O]
  {Γ : Type} [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace Γ] (η : Γ →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : Γ →* Oˣ)

def baseLift (hΨ : ∀ j : ℤ, AdicCont (maximalIdeal O) fun g => ((Ψ g ^ j : Oˣ) : O)) : GLift O η Ψ O where
  hom := r0 η Ψ
  cont a b := by
    by_cases h : a = b
    · subst h
      simpa [GG.mat, tor_val] using hΨ (wt1 a)
    · intro N
      simp only [GG.mat, unitaryThreeReflection_left, tor_val, Matrix.diagonal_apply_ne _ h]
      exact IsLocallyConstant.const _
  red g := by
    have e : ∀ x : O, res O O x = residue O x := fun x => (res O O).commutes x
    refine SemidirectProduct.ext (Units.ext ?_) rfl
    show (tor O (Ψ g) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) O).map (res O O) = tor _ (barChar O Ψ g)
    rw [tor_val, tor_val, Matrix.diagonal_map (map_zero _)]
    congr 1
    funext i
    rw [e]
    exact congrArg Units.val (map_zpow (Units.map (residue O).toMonoidHom) (Ψ g) (wt1 i))

end Base

end UpperTheory.V.U3


[OCH_SRC_LOCAL_CONTRACT] Literal ConjStable, locDims, LocChart and LocGood fields
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/UnitaryThreeGroups/ConjStable.lean#L15-L37
def IsBase {Γ R : Type} [Group Γ] [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] (η : Γ →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : Γ →* Oˣ) (r : Γ →* GG 3 R)
    (x : R →ₐ[O] O) : Prop := push x r = r0 η Ψ

def ConjStable : Prop :=
  ∀ (R : Type) [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] [IsCO O R] (r r' : GLift O η Ψ R) (h : GL (Fin 3) R),
    CongOne (maximalIdeal R) h → r'.hom = conjBy h r.hom → OnI U I R r → OnI U I R r'

def locDims (j : ℤ) : ℕ :=
  if j = -2 then 1 else if j = -1 then 2 else if j = 0 then 3 else if j = 1 then 4 else if j = 2 then 2 else 0

structure LocChart (x : U.R →ₐ[O] O) {τ : Type} (w : τ → ℤ) (φ : U.R →ₐ[O] MvPowerSeries τ (FractionRing O)) :
    Prop where
  ker : RingHom.ker φ = I
  graded : ∀ g a b, φ ((U.r.hom g).mat a b) ∈ wtPart (FractionRing O) w (wt a b)
  const : ∀ f, constantCoeff (φ f) = algebraMap O (FractionRing O) (x f)
  onto : ∀ i, ∃ f : U.R, coeff (Finsupp.single i 1) (φ f) ≠ 0 ∧ ∀ i', i' ≠ i → coeff (Finsupp.single i' 1) (φ f) = 0
  inj : ∀ f, x f = 0 → (∀ i, coeff (Finsupp.single i 1) (φ f) = 0) →
    ∃ c : O, c ≠ 0 ∧ c • f ∈ RingHom.ker x ^ 2 ⊔ I

structure LocGood : Prop where
  conj : ConjStable U I
  chart : ∃ (x : U.R →ₐ[O] O) (τ : Type) (_ : Fintype τ) (w : τ → ℤ) (φ : U.R →ₐ[O] MvPowerSeries τ (FractionRing O)),
    IsBase η Ψ U.r.hom x ∧ LocChart U I x w φ ∧ ∀ j, Nat.card {i // w i = j} = locDims j


[OCH_SRC_MODEL_CONTRACT] All fields of the actual graded Model and its length
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/UnitaryThreeGroups/ConjStable.lean#L49-L90
abbrev SRing : Type 1 := Univ (NormCond c g₁ (BoxCond N ι hι U I))

def EntWt {G R Q : Type} [Group G] [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] [AddCommGroup Q] [Module O Q] (r : G →* GG 3 R) (j : ℤ)
    (D : R →ₗ[O] Q) : Prop := ∀ g a b, wt a b ≠ j → D ((r g).mat a b) = 0

structure Model (S : SRing N hι U I c g₁) (K : Type) [NormedField K] [Algebra O K] : Type 1 where
  A : Type
  [cr : CommRing A]
  [al : Algebra O A]
  𝒜 : ℤ → Submodule O A
  [gr : GradedAlgebra 𝒜]
  x : A →ₐ[O] O
  wring : IsWRing 𝒜 x
  r : ℕ
  s : ℕ
  r' : ℕ
  s' : ℕ
  m : ℕ
  Q : Eqns 𝒜 x r s r' s' m
  one_le : 1 ≤ r
  smooth : SmoothPt 𝒜 x (mDims r s r' s' m)
  q : A →ₐ[O] S.R
  ker_q : RingHom.ker q = Q.ideal
  x₀ : S.R →ₐ[O] O
  base : IsBase η Ψ S.r.hom x₀
  x₀_q : x₀.comp q = x
  ent : ∀ g a b, ∃ f ∈ 𝒜 (wt a b), q f = (S.r.hom g).mat a b
  dense : ∀ j, ∀ f ∈ 𝒜 j, ∀ n : ℕ, ∃ y ∈ Submodule.span O (entMon S.r.hom j), q f - y ∈ maximalIdeal S.R ^ n
  pts : M2 𝒜 Q.ideal → ∀ ξ : A →ₐ[O] K, OnS Q.ideal ξ →
    ∃ (t : Kˣ) (f : S.R →ₐ[O] K), IsLocPt f ∧ act 𝒜 (tz t) ξ = f.comp q
  der : M2 𝒜 Q.ideal → ∀ f : S.R →ₐ[O] K, IsLocPt f → ∀ D ∈ PtDer (f.comp q) K, (∀ y ∈ Q.ideal, D y = 0) →
    ∃ F : S.R →ₐ[O] DualNumber K, dualFst F = f ∧ ∀ y, (F (q y)).snd = D y
  lift : ∀ a : O, a ≠ 0 → ∀ D ∈ PtDer x₀ (O ⧸ Ideal.span {a}), EntWt S.r.hom (-1) D →
    ∀ xl : U.R →ₐ[O] O, IsBase (η.comp ι) (Ψ.comp ι) U.r.hom xl → ∀ D₁ ∈ PtDer xl O, (∀ y ∈ I, D₁ y = 0) →
    EntWt U.r.hom (-1) D₁ →
    (∀ h a' b', Ideal.Quotient.mk _ (D₁ ((U.r.hom h).mat a' b')) = D ((S.r.hom (ι h)).mat a' b')) →
    ∃ D' ∈ PtDer x O, HasWt 𝒜 (-1) D' ∧ ∀ f, D (q f) = Ideal.Quotient.mk _ (D' f)

attribute [instance] Model.cr Model.al Model.gr

def Model.len {S : SRing N hι U I c g₁} {K : Type} [NormedField K] [Algebra O K] (M : Model S K) : ℕ∞ :=
  cotLen M.x M.𝒜 (-1) M.Q.F


[OCH_SRC_POINTS_SHA_CONTRACT] Original TwoPt, CoversPt, trDiff and shaCoc predicates
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/UnitaryThreeGroups/ConjStable.lean#L94-L112
def TwoPt {R K : Type} [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] [IsLocalRing R] [NormedField K] [Algebra O K] (r : Γ →* GG 3 R)
    (f : R →ₐ[O] K) : Prop := IsLocPt f ∧ ¬ Upper η (push f r) ∧ ¬ Lower η (push f r)

def CoversPt {R K : Type} [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] [IsLocalRing R] [NormedField K] [Algebra O K] (η : Γ →* ℤˣ)
    (r : Γ →* GG 3 R) (T : Finset (R →ₐ[O] K)) : Prop :=
  (∀ f ∈ T, TwoPt η r f) ∧ ∀ f : R →ₐ[O] K, TwoPt η r f → ∃ f' ∈ T, ∃ t : Kˣ, push f r = conjBy (tor K t) (push f' r)

def trDiff {R K : Type} [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] [CommRing K] [Algebra O K] (η : Γ →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : Γ →* Oˣ)
    (r : Γ →* GG 3 R) (σ : Γ) (f : R →ₐ[O] K) : K :=
  f (r σ).mat.trace - algebraMap O K (r0 η Ψ σ).mat.trace

variable (η Ψ N ι c g₁) in

def shaCoc (a : O) : Set (Γ → Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) (O ⧸ Ideal.span {a})) :=
  {γ | IsLocallyConstant γ ∧ (∀ g, γ g ∈ gw _ (-1)) ∧
    IsCoc ((GG.map (Ideal.Quotient.mk (Ideal.span {a}))).comp (r0 η Ψ)) γ ∧
    NormCoc c g₁ ((GG.map (Ideal.Quotient.mk (Ideal.span {a}))).comp (r0 η Ψ)) γ ∧ (∀ g ∈ N, γ g = 0) ∧
    ∃ X ∈ gw (O ⧸ Ideal.span {a}) (-1), ∀ h,
      γ (ι h) = cob ((GG.map (Ideal.Quotient.mk (Ideal.span {a}))).comp (r0 η Ψ)) X (ι h)}


[OCH_SRC_BOX_CONTRACT] Exact local-presentation and five-weight BoxPres count contract
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/UnitaryThreeGroups/IsLocPres.lean#L9-L41

section Coord

variable {O G R : Type} [CommRing O] [Group G] [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] (η : G →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : G →* Oˣ)
  (r : G →* GG 3 R) (W : WPres O ℤ R)

def IsCoord (s : W.σ) : Prop :=
  ∃ g a b, W.wt s = wt6 a - wt6 b ∧ W.z s = emb (r g) a b - algebraMap O R (emb (r0 η Ψ g) a b)

def EntHomog : Prop := ∀ g a b, emb (r g) a b ∈ W.part (wt6 a - wt6 b)

end Coord

def boxDef (j : ℤ) : ℕ := if |j| ≤ 1 then 1 else 0

variable {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] {Γ H : Type} [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace Γ]
  [Group H] [TopologicalSpace H]

def IsLocPres {η : H →* ℤˣ} {Ψ : H →* Oˣ} (U : Univ (GCond.noCondition O η Ψ)) (I : Ideal U.R) (WB : WPres O ℤ (U.R ⧸ I)) :
    Prop :=
  (∀ s, IsCoord η Ψ (push (Ideal.Quotient.mkₐ O I) U.r.hom) WB s) ∧ EntHomog (push (Ideal.Quotient.mkₐ O I) U.r.hom) WB

variable {η : Γ →* ℤˣ} {Ψ : Γ →* Oˣ} {N : Set Γ} {ι : H →* Γ} {hι : Continuous ι}
  {U : Univ (GCond.noCondition O (η.comp ι) (Ψ.comp ι))} {I : Ideal U.R}

def BoxPres (B : Univ (BoxCond N ι hι U I)) (WB : WPres O ℤ (U.R ⧸ I)) : Prop :=
  ∃ (W : WPres O ℤ B.R) (i : (U.R ⧸ I) →ₐ[O] B.R) (τ κ : Type) (_ : Fintype τ) (_ : Fintype κ) (e : WB.σ ⊕ τ ≃ W.σ)
    (f : κ → MvPowerSeries W.σ O) (wf : κ → ℤ),
    IsRelPres WB i W e f wf ∧ push (i.comp (Ideal.Quotient.mkₐ O I)) U.r.hom = B.r.hom.comp ι ∧
    (∀ t, IsCoord η Ψ B.r.hom W (e (.inr t))) ∧ EntHomog B.r.hom W ∧
    ∀ j, Nat.card {u // wf u = j} = Nat.card {t // W.wt (e (.inr t)) = j} + boxDef j

end UpperTheory.V.U3


[OCH_SRC_BOX_ABSOLUTE] Specialization of the actual framed presentation to the box quotient
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/UnitaryThreeGroups/BoxAbs.lean#L1-L193
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.UnitaryThreeGroups.BoxDefs
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.UnitaryThreeGroups.GLiftToLiftInHAndSurjective
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.CommutativeAlgebra.CoQuot
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.CommutativeAlgebra.HhGroup

noncomputable section

namespace UpperTheory.V.U3

open Matrix IsLocalRing UpperTheory.V.Alg Topology

variable {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O]

section Tools

variable {Γ : Type} [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace Γ] {η : Γ →* ℤˣ} {Ψ : Γ →* Oˣ}
  {R R' R'' : Type} [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] [IsCO O R] [CommRing R'] [Algebra O R'] [IsCO O R']
  [CommRing R''] [Algebra O R''] [IsCO O R'']

private theorem toLift_map (hη : IsLocallyConstant η) (r : GLift O η Ψ R) (f : R →ₐ[O] R') :
    (r.map f).toLift hη = (r.toLift hη).map f :=
  Lift.ext (MonoidHom.ext fun g => emb_map' (f : R →+* R') (r.hom g))

private theorem toLift_inj (hη : IsLocallyConstant η) {r r' : GLift O η Ψ R} (h : r.toLift hη = r'.toLift hη) :
    r = r' :=
  GLift.ext (MonoidHom.ext fun g => eq_of_emb ((r.hasMult g).trans (r'.hasMult g).symm)
    (DFunLike.congr_fun (congrArg Lift.hom h) g))

private theorem descend {G : Type} [Group G] [TopologicalSpace G] (π : G →* Γ) (hq : IsQuotientMap π)
    (r : GLift O (η.comp π) (Ψ.comp π) R) (h : π.ker ≤ r.hom.ker) :
    ∃ r' : GLift O η Ψ R, r'.comap π hq.continuous = r := by
  have hs := hq.surjective
  let φ := π.liftOfRightInverse (Function.surjInv hs) (Function.rightInverse_surjInv hs) ⟨r.hom, h⟩
  have hφ : ∀ g, φ (π g) = r.hom g := fun g => π.liftOfRightInverse_comp_apply _ _ _ g
  refine ⟨⟨φ, fun a b n s => ?_, fun g => ?_⟩, GLift.ext (MonoidHom.ext hφ)⟩
  · rw [← hq.isOpen_preimage]
    have := r.cont a b n s
    simp only [← hφ] at this
    exact this
  · obtain ⟨g, rfl⟩ := hs g
    rw [hφ]
    exact r.red g

private theorem ker_closed (hη : IsLocallyConstant η) (r : GLift O η Ψ R) : IsClosed (r.hom.ker : Set Γ) := by
  have : (r.hom.ker : Set Γ) = ⋂ n : ℕ, {g | GG.map (Ideal.Quotient.mk (maximalIdeal R ^ n)) (r.hom g) = 1} := by
    ext g
    simp only [SetLike.mem_coe, MonoidHom.mem_ker, Set.mem_iInter, Set.mem_setOf_eq]
    refine ⟨fun h n => by rw [h, map_one], fun h => ?_⟩
    refine SemidirectProduct.ext (Units.ext ?_) (congrArg SemidirectProduct.right (h 0) :)
    ext a b
    rw [← sub_eq_zero]
    refine IsHausdorff.haus (I := maximalIdeal R) inferInstance _ fun n => ?_
    rw [SModEq.zero, smul_eq_mul, Ideal.mul_top, ← Ideal.Quotient.eq]
    have := congrArg (fun x : GG 3 _ => x.mat a b) ((h n).trans (map_one (GG.map (Ideal.Quotient.mk _))).symm)
    exact this
  rw [this]
  exact isClosed_iInter fun n => (r.locConst hη n).isClosed_fiber 1

end Tools

theorem boxPres_abs (hP : LemmaP) (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] [IsNoetherianRing O] [IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal O) O]
    [Finite (ResidueField O)] [CharP (ResidueField O) l] (hl : l ≠ 2) {G Γ H : Type} [Group G] [TopologicalSpace G]
    [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace Γ] [IsTopologicalGroup Γ] [CompactSpace Γ] [TotallyDisconnectedSpace Γ] [T2Space Γ]
    [Group H] [TopologicalSpace H] (π : G →* Γ) (hq : IsQuotientMap π) (hΦ : PhiCond Γ l) {η : Γ →* ℤˣ}
    {Ψ : Γ →* Oˣ} (hη : IsLocallyConstant η) (hΨ : ∀ j : ℤ, AdicCont (maximalIdeal O) fun g => ((Ψ g ^ j : Oˣ) : O))
    {Kill : Set G} {ι : H →* G} (hι : Continuous ι) {Q : Type} [Fintype Q] (D N : Q → Subgroup G)
    (hND : ∀ q, N q ≤ D q)
    (hK : ∀ (M : Subgroup G) [M.Normal], IsClosed (M : Set G) → ((∀ g ∈ Kill, g ∈ M) ↔ π.ker ≤ M ∧ ∀ q, N q ≤ M))
    (hN : ∀ q, ∀ n ∈ N q, r0 η Ψ (π n) = 1)
    (hloc : ∀ q, (form6 O).IsLocCond ((emb.comp (r0 η Ψ)).comp (π.comp (D q).subtype)) ((N q).subgroupOf (D q))
      (killCond ((emb.comp (rbar O η Ψ)).comp (π.comp (D q).subtype)) ((N q).subgroupOf (D q))))
    (hc : ∀ j, Module.Finite (ResidueField O) ((unitFrame π hq.continuous ι hι D N).Ob (adBar O η Ψ j)) ∧
      Module.finrank (ResidueField O) ((unitFrame π hq.continuous ι hι D N).Ob (adBar O η Ψ j)) =
        Module.finrank (ResidueField O) ((unitFrame π hq.continuous ι hι D N).H1c (adBar O η Ψ j)) + boxDef j)
    (U : Univ (GCond.noCondition O ((η.comp π).comp ι) ((Ψ.comp π).comp ι))) (I : Ideal U.R)
    (B : Univ (BoxCond Kill ι hι U I)) (WB : WPres O ℤ (U.R ⧸ I)) (hW : IsLocPres U I WB) : BoxPres B WB := by
  have hπ := hq.continuous
  have hηG : IsLocallyConstant (η.comp π) := hη.comp_continuous hπ
  have hηH : IsLocallyConstant ((η.comp π).comp ι) := hηG.comp_continuous hι
  obtain ⟨hBK, f₀, hf₀, hI⟩ : BoxCond Kill ι hι U I B.R B.r := (B.rep B.R).1 (AlgHom.id O _)
  have hne : I ≠ ⊤ := fun h => RingHom.ker_ne_top f₀ (top_le_iff.1 (h ▸ hI))
  haveI : Nontrivial (U.R ⧸ I) := Ideal.Quotient.nontrivial_iff.2 hne
  haveI : IsCO O (U.R ⧸ I) :=
    co_quot O U.R (U.R ⧸ I) (Ideal.Quotient.mkₐ O I) (Ideal.Quotient.mkₐ_surjective O I)
  let F := unitFrame π hπ ι hι D N
  let u := U.r.map (Ideal.Quotient.mkₐ O I)
  let ρ : ∀ v, Lift O ((emb.comp (rbar O η Ψ)).comp (F.φ v)) (U.R ⧸ I) := fun _ => u.toLift hηH
  obtain ⟨R, _, _, _, i, r, α, W, τ, κ, _, _, e, f, wf, ⟨⟨h1, h2, -, h4, h5⟩, hu⟩, hrel, hhom, -, hz, hτ, hκ⟩ :=
    hP l O hl _ ℤ wt6 (form6 O) Γ hΦ _ ((baseLift O η Ψ hΨ).toLift hη) (blockPairingForm_normalises_baseLift O η (barChar O Ψ))
      (blockPairingForm_normalises_baseLift O η Ψ) F (fun q => killCond _ (F.N q))
      (fun q n hn => by
        show emb (rbar O η Ψ (π n)) = 1
        rw [← baseLift_map_residue, hN q n hn, map_one, map_one])
      hloc (fun q n hn => by
        show emb (r0 η Ψ (π n)) = 1
        rw [hN q n hn, map_one])
      (fun j => (hc j).1) (U.R ⧸ I) WB ρ
      (fun s => by
        obtain ⟨g, a, b, -, h⟩ := hW.1 s
        have h1 : res O _ (emb (u.hom g) a b) = emb (rbar O _ _ g) a b := (u.toLift hηH).red g a b
        rw [← baseLift_map_residue, emb_map'] at h1
        rw [h, ← res_eq_zero_iff O, map_sub, AlgHom.commutes, sub_eq_zero]
        exact h1)
      (fun _ => (UpperTheory.gLift_toLift_inH_and_surjective hηH).1 u) (fun _ => hW.2)
  have hα : α = fun _ => 1 := funext fun _ => h4
  subst hα
  obtain ⟨r', rfl⟩ := (UpperTheory.gLift_toLift_inH_and_surjective hη).2 r h1
  have hloc' : U.r.map (i.comp (Ideal.Quotient.mkₐ O I)) = (r'.comap π hπ).comap ι hι := by
    refine toLift_inj hηH (Lift.ext (MonoidHom.ext fun s => ?_))
    have := h5 () s
    rw [mul_one, one_mul] at this
    exact (emb_map' (i : (U.R ⧸ I) →+* R) (u.hom s)).trans this.symm
  have hbox : BoxCond Kill ι hι U I R (r'.comap π hπ) := by
    refine ⟨(hK _ (ker_closed hηG (r'.comap π hπ))).2 ⟨fun g hg => ?_, fun q n hn => ?_⟩, _, hloc', fun y hy => ?_⟩
    · show r'.hom (π g) = 1
      rw [show π g = 1 from hg, map_one]
    · have h6 : emb (r'.hom (π n)) = 1 := h2 q ⟨n, hND q hn⟩ hn
      refine eq_of_emb ((r'.hasMult _).trans (congrArg SemidirectProduct.right (hN q n hn))) (h6.trans (map_one _).symm)
    · show i (Ideal.Quotient.mk I y) = 0
      rw [Ideal.Quotient.eq_zero_iff_mem.2 hy, map_zero]
  obtain ⟨a, ha, -⟩ := (B.rep R).2 _ hbox
  obtain ⟨hB1, hB2⟩ := (hK _ (ker_closed hηG B.r)).1 hBK
  obtain ⟨r'', hr''⟩ := descend π hq B.r hB1
  have hr2 : ∀ g, r''.hom (π g) = B.r.hom g := fun g => DFunLike.congr_fun (congrArg GLift.hom hr'') g
  let i' : (U.R ⧸ I) →ₐ[O] B.R := Ideal.Quotient.liftₐ I f₀ hI
  have hi' : i'.comp (Ideal.Quotient.mkₐ O I) = f₀ := AlgHom.ext fun _ => rfl
  have hpush : push f₀ U.r.hom = B.r.hom.comp ι := congrArg GLift.hom hf₀
  obtain ⟨b, ⟨hb1, hb2, -⟩, -⟩ := hu B.R i' (r''.toLift hη) (fun _ => 1)
    ⟨(UpperTheory.gLift_toLift_inH_and_surjective hη).1 r'', fun q n hn => by
        show emb (r''.hom (π n)) = 1
        rw [hr2, show B.r.hom n = 1 from hB2 q hn, map_one],
      fun _ => (hh_group O _ ℤ wt6 (form6 O) B.R).1, rfl, fun _ s => by
        rw [mul_one, one_mul]
        refine Eq.trans ?_ (emb_map' (i' : (U.R ⧸ I) →+* B.R) (u.hom s))
        show emb (r''.hom (π (ι s))) = emb (push f₀ U.r.hom s)
        rw [hr2, hpush]
        rfl⟩
  have hb3 : r'.map b = r'' := toLift_inj hη ((toLift_map hη r' b).trans hb2)
  have hba : b.comp a = AlgHom.id O _ := by
    refine ((B.rep B.R).2 B.r ⟨hBK, f₀, hf₀, hI⟩).unique ?_ rfl
    calc B.r.map (b.comp a) = (B.r.map a).map b := rfl
      _ = (r'.map b).comap π hπ := by rw [ha]; rfl
      _ = B.r := by rw [hb3, hr'']
  have ha2 : r''.map a = r' := by
    refine GLift.ext (MonoidHom.ext fun g => ?_)
    obtain ⟨g, rfl⟩ := hq.surjective g
    show GG.map _ (r''.hom (π g)) = _
    rw [hr2]
    exact DFunLike.congr_fun (congrArg GLift.hom ha) g
  have hab : a.comp b = AlgHom.id O _ := by
    refine (hu R i (r'.toLift hη) (fun _ => 1) ⟨h1, h2, fun _ => (hh_group O _ ℤ wt6 (form6 O) R).1, rfl, h5⟩).unique
      ⟨?_, ?_, fun _ => Matrix.map_one _ (map_zero _) (map_one _)⟩ ⟨rfl, rfl, fun _ => Matrix.map_one _ rfl rfl⟩
    · rw [AlgHom.comp_assoc, hb1]
      refine Ideal.Quotient.algHom_ext O (((U.rep R).2 _ trivial).unique ?_ hloc')
      rw [AlgHom.comp_assoc, hi', ← ha]
      calc U.r.map (a.comp f₀) = (U.r.map f₀).map a := rfl
        _ = _ := by rw [hf₀]; rfl
    · rw [← toLift_map]
      congr 1
      calc r'.map (a.comp b) = (r'.map b).map a := rfl
        _ = r' := by rw [hb3, ha2]
  have hbs : Function.Surjective b := fun y => ⟨a y, DFunLike.congr_fun hba y⟩
  have hbi : Function.Injective b := Function.LeftInverse.injective (g := a) fun y => DFunLike.congr_fun hab y
  have hker : RingHom.ker (b.comp W.π) = RingHom.ker W.π := by
    ext x
    simp only [RingHom.mem_ker, AlgHom.comp_apply, map_eq_zero_iff b hbi]
  have hent : ∀ g x y, b (emb (r'.hom (π g)) x y) = emb (B.r.hom g) x y := fun g x y => by
    rw [← hr2, ← hb3]
    exact (congrFun (congrFun (emb_map' (b : R →+* B.R) (r'.hom (π g))) x) y).symm
  refine ⟨⟨W.σ, W.wt, b.comp W.π, hbs.comp W.surj, hker ▸ W.homog⟩, i', τ, κ, inferInstance, inferInstance, e, f, wf,
    ⟨hrel.1, fun x => ?_, hrel.2.2.1, hker.trans hrel.2.2.2⟩, hi' ▸ hpush, fun t => ?_, fun g x y => ?_, fun j => ?_⟩
  · show b (W.π _) = _
    rw [hrel.2.1, ← hb1]
    rfl
  · obtain ⟨x, y, hw, ⟨s, hs⟩ | ⟨v, hv⟩⟩ := hz t
    · obtain ⟨g, rfl⟩ := hq.surjective s
      refine ⟨g, x, y, hw, ?_⟩
      show b (W.z _) = _
      rw [hs, map_sub, AlgHom.commutes, ← hent]
      rfl
    · refine ⟨1, x, y, hw, ?_⟩
      show b (W.z _) = _
      rw [hv, map_one, map_one, map_one]
      by_cases hxy : x = y <;> simp [Matrix.one_apply, hxy]
  · obtain ⟨p, hp, hp'⟩ := hhom (π g) x y
    refine ⟨p, hp, ?_⟩
    show b (W.π p) = _
    rw [← hent]
    exact congrArg b hp'
  · rw [hκ, hτ]
    exact (hc j).2

end UpperTheory.V.U3

section SlopeTestRemainingChoices

variable {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] {Γ : Type} [Group Γ] (η : Γ →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : Γ →* Oˣ)

private theorem adBar_eq_one (j : ℤ) {g : Γ} (h : rbar O η Ψ g = 1) : adBar O η Ψ j g = 1 := by
  refine LinearMap.ext fun Y => Subtype.ext ?_
  show emb (rbar O η Ψ g) * Y.1 * emb (rbar O η Ψ g⁻¹) = Y.1
  rw [map_inv, h, inv_one, map_one, one_mul, mul_one]

variable [TopologicalSpace Γ] [IsTopologicalGroup Γ]

omit [IsTopologicalGroup Γ] in
private theorem rbar_open (hη : IsLocallyConstant η) (hΨ : IsLocallyConstant (barChar O Ψ)) :
    IsOpen ((rbar O η Ψ).ker : Set Γ) :=
  ((hη.prodMk hΨ).comp fun p => (⟨tor _ p.2, p.1⟩ : GG 3 (ResidueField O))).isOpen_fiber 1

private theorem adBar_open (hη : IsLocallyConstant η) (hΨ : IsLocallyConstant (barChar O Ψ)) (j : ℤ) :
    IsOpen ((adBar O η Ψ j).ker : Set Γ) :=
  Subgroup.isOpen_mono (fun _ hg => adBar_eq_one η Ψ j hg) (rbar_open η Ψ hη hΨ)

private theorem locCond (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] [IsNoetherianRing O] [IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal O) O]
    [Finite (ResidueField O)] [CharP (ResidueField O) l] (h2 : IsUnit (2 : O)) [CompactSpace Γ]
    [TotallyDisconnectedSpace Γ] [T2Space Γ] (hη : IsLocallyConstant η) (hΨ : IsLocallyConstant (barChar O Ψ))
    (hΨ' : ∀ j : ℤ, AdicCont (maximalIdeal O) fun g => ((Ψ g ^ j : Oˣ) : O)) (I N : Subgroup Γ) [I.Normal] [N.Normal]
    (hI : IsClosed (I : Set Γ)) (hN : IsClosed (N : Set Γ)) (hNI : N ≤ I) (h0 : ∀ n ∈ N, r0 η Ψ n = 1)
    (hi : ¬ l ∣ N.relIndex I) (hf : ∃ fr : Γ, (I ⊔ Subgroup.zpowers fr).topologicalClosure = ⊤)
    (hU : ∀ n : ℕ, ∃ U : Subgroup Γ, IsOpen (U : Set Γ) ∧ I ≤ U ∧ U.index = l ^ n) :
    (form6 O).IsLocCond (emb.comp (r0 η Ψ)) N (killCond (emb.comp (rbar O η Ψ)) N) :=
  kill_loc O _ ℤ wt6 (form6 O) h2 Γ _ ((baseLift O η Ψ hΨ').toLift hη) (blockPairingForm_normalises_baseLift O η (barChar O Ψ))
    (blockPairingForm_normalises_baseLift O η Ψ) N (fun n hn => by
      show emb (r0 η Ψ n) = 1
      rw [h0 n hn, map_one])
    fun j => exists_locallyConstant_dOne_eq_cocycle_of_tame_inertia l _ Γ _ (adBar O η Ψ j) (adBar_open η Ψ hη hΨ j) I N hI hN hNI
      (fun n hn => adBar_eq_one η Ψ j (by rw [← baseLift_map_residue, h0 n hn, map_one])) hi hf hU

end SlopeTestRemainingChoices

private theorem lc_desc {X Y Z : Type} [TopologicalSpace X] [TopologicalSpace Y] {π : X → Y}
    (hq : Topology.IsQuotientMap π) {f : Y → Z} (h : IsLocallyConstant fun x => f (π x)) : IsLocallyConstant f :=
  fun s => hq.isOpen_preimage.1 (h s)
