private theorem aux (hI : CohInp) (hP : LemmaP) {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (l : ℕ)
    [hl : Fact l.Prime] (hl2 : l ≠ 2) {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] [IsNoetherianRing O]
    [IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal O) O] [Finite (ResidueField O)] [CharP (ResidueField O) l] (h2 : IsUnit (2 : O))
    (η : G ℚ Ω →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : G ℚ Ω →* Oˣ) (hη : IsLocallyConstant η) (hΨ : IsLocallyConstant (barChar O Ψ))
    (hΨc' : CharCont Ψ) (cc : G ℚ Ω) (hcc : ∃ φ : Ω →+* ℂ, ∀ x, φ (cc x) = starRingEnd ℂ (φ x)) (hηc : η cc = -1)
    (hΨc : Ψ cc = 1) (ι : GQ l →* G ℚ Ω) (hι : Continuous ι) (hinj : Function.Injective ι) (Kill : Set (G ℚ Ω))
    (hKi : ∀ g, g ∈ Kill ↔ ∃ A : ValuationSubring Ω, ¬ A.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 ∧ g ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) A ∧
      g ∈ (rbar O η Ψ).ker) (hK : ∀ g ∈ Kill, r0 η Ψ g = 1)
    (hg : ∃ g, η g = 1 ∧ barChar O Ψ g = 1 ∧ cycMod ℚ Ω l 1 g = -1)
    (S : Finset (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 ℚ))) (A : S → ValuationSubring Ω) (v₀ : S)
    (η' : GS Ω (S : Set (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 ℚ))) →* ℤˣ) (Ψ' : GS Ω (S : Set (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 ℚ))) →* Oˣ)
    (hS : ContainsAbove S l) (hA : ∀ v, IsPlaceOver (A v) v.1) (hr : ι.range = Dec (F := ℚ) (A v₀))
    (hv : ∀ v : S, ((l : ℕ) : 𝓞 ℚ) ∈ v.1.asIdeal ↔ v = v₀) (e1 : η'.comp (toGS Ω _) = η)
    (e2 : Ψ'.comp (toGS Ω _) = Ψ) :
    ∀ (U : Univ (GCond.noCondition O (η.comp ι) (Ψ.comp ι))) (I : Ideal U.R) (B : Univ (BoxCond Kill ι hι U I))
      (WB : WPres O ℤ (U.R ⧸ I)), IsLocPres U I WB → BoxPres B WB := by
  classical
  haveI : IsGalois ℚ Ω := IsAlgClosure.isGalois ℚ Ω
  obtain ⟨hΦ, _, _, _⟩ := UpperTheory.galois_group_phi_condition hI.gf Ω l S hS
  have hq : Topology.IsQuotientMap (toGS Ω (S : Set (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 ℚ)))) := QuotientGroup.isQuotientMap_mk _
  have hπ := hq.continuous
  have e1' : ∀ g, η' (toGS Ω _ g) = η g := DFunLike.congr_fun e1
  have e2' : ∀ g, Ψ' (toGS Ω _ g) = Ψ g := DFunLike.congr_fun e2
  have e3 : ∀ g, barChar O Ψ' (toGS Ω _ g) = barChar O Ψ g := fun g =>
    congrArg (Units.map (residue O).toMonoidHom) (e2' g)
  have e4 : ∀ g, rbar O η' Ψ' (toGS Ω _ g) = rbar O η Ψ g := fun g =>
    SemidirectProduct.ext (congrArg (tor _) (e3 g)) (e1' g)
  have e5 : ∀ g, r0 η' Ψ' (toGS Ω _ g) = r0 η Ψ g := fun g =>
    SemidirectProduct.ext (congrArg (tor _) (e2' g)) (e1' g)
  have hη' : IsLocallyConstant η' := lc_desc hq (by simpa only [e1'] using hη)
  have hΨ' : IsLocallyConstant (barChar O Ψ') := lc_desc hq (by simpa only [e3] using hΨ)
  have hΨ'' : ∀ j : ℤ, AdicCont (maximalIdeal O) fun g => ((Ψ' g ^ j : Oˣ) : O) := fun j n =>
    lc_desc hq (by simpa only [e2'] using hΨc' j n)
  let H := (rbar O η Ψ).ker
  have hHc : IsClosed (H : Set (G ℚ Ω)) := H.isClosed_of_isOpen (rbar_open _ _ hη hΨ)
  let Nq : {v : S // v ≠ v₀} → Subgroup (G ℚ Ω) := fun q => Ine (F := ℚ) (A q.1) ⊓ H
  have hKill := UpperTheory.inertia_le_normal_iff_ker_le_and_inertia_le Ω l S A v₀ hS hA hv H fun g hg => by
    show rbar O η Ψ g = 1
    rw [← e4, show toGS Ω _ g = 1 from hg, map_one]
  have hN0 : ∀ q, ∀ n ∈ Nq q, r0 η' Ψ' (toGS Ω _ n) = 1 := fun q n hn => by
    rw [e5]
    refine hK n ((hKi n).2 ⟨A q.1, fun h => q.2 ((hv q.1).1 ?_), hn.1, hn.2⟩)
    have h' := hA q.1 ((l : ℕ) : 𝓞 ℚ)
    have e : algebraMap ℚ Ω (((l : ℕ) : 𝓞 ℚ) : ℚ) = (l : Ω) := by simp
    rw [e] at h'
    exact h'.1 h
  have hNn : ∀ q, ((Nq q).subgroupOf (Dec (F := ℚ) (A q.1))).Normal := fun q => by
    haveI := (UpperTheory.exists_decompositionField_frobenius_data Ω (A q.1) q.1.1 (hA q.1)).2.1
    exact inferInstanceAs ((Ine (F := ℚ) (A q.1)).subgroupOf (Dec (F := ℚ) (A q.1)) ⊓ H.subgroupOf _).Normal
  have hNc : ∀ q, IsClosed (Nq q : Set (G ℚ Ω)) := fun q =>
    (UpperTheory.exists_decompositionField_frobenius_data Ω (A q.1) q.1.1 (hA q.1)).2.2.1.inter hHc
  have hNi : ∀ q, ¬ l ∣ (Nq q).relIndex (Ine (F := ℚ) (A q.1)) := fun q =>
    UpperTheory.not_prime_dvd_relIndex_ker_inducedUnitaryRep l _ hl2 η (barChar O Ψ) _
  have key := boxPres_abs hP l hl2 (toGS Ω _) hq hΦ hη' hΨ'' (Kill := Kill) hι
    (fun q : {v : S // v ≠ v₀} => Dec (F := ℚ) (A q.1)) Nq (fun q => inf_le_left.trans (Ine_le_Dec _))
    (fun M _ hM => ((forall_congr' fun g => imp_congr_left (hKi g)).trans (hKill M hM)).trans (and_congr_right' Subtype.forall'))
    hN0
    (fun q => by
      obtain ⟨d1, d2, d3, d4, d5⟩ := UpperTheory.exists_decompositionField_frobenius_data Ω (A q.1) q.1.1 (hA q.1)
      haveI := hNn q
      have hφ : Continuous ((toGS Ω (S : Set (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 ℚ)))).comp (Dec (F := ℚ) (A q.1)).subtype) :=
        hπ.comp continuous_subtype_val
      refine locCond (η'.comp ((toGS Ω _).comp (Dec (F := ℚ) (A q.1)).subtype))
        (Ψ'.comp ((toGS Ω _).comp (Dec (F := ℚ) (A q.1)).subtype)) l h2 (hη'.comp_continuous hφ)
        (hΨ'.comp_continuous hφ) (fun j n => (hΨ'' j n).comp_continuous hφ) _ _ (d3.preimage continuous_subtype_val)
        ((hNc q).preimage continuous_subtype_val) (fun x hx => hx.1) (fun n hn => hN0 q n hn) ?_ d4
        fun n => d5 _ (pow_pos hl.out.pos n)
      rw [Subgroup.relIndex_subgroupOf (Ine_le_Dec _)]
      exact hNi q)
    (fun j => by
      obtain ⟨g, g1, g2, g3⟩ := hg
      have h := box_count hI Ω l _ _ hl2 S A v₀ hS hA hv ι hι hinj hr (adBar O η' Ψ' j)
        (adBar_open _ _ hη' hΨ' j) Nq (fun q => inf_le_left) hNn hNc
        (fun q n hn => adBar_eq_one _ _ j ((e4 n).trans hn.2)) hNi
        ⟨g, g3, adBar_eq_one _ _ j ((e4 g).trans (SemidirectProduct.ext (by
          show tor _ (barChar O Ψ g) = 1
          rw [g2, map_one]) g1))⟩ cc hcc
      have h2' : (2 : ResidueField O) ≠ 0 := map_ofNat (residue O) 2 ▸ (h2.map (residue O)).ne_zero
      have h3 := UpperTheory.finrank_ker_adRep_complexConj_sub_one_eq_boxDef O _ h2' η' (barChar O Ψ') (toGS Ω _ cc) ((e1' _).trans hηc)
        ((e3 _).trans (by
          show Units.map (residue O).toMonoidHom (Ψ cc) = 1
          rw [hΨc, map_one])) j
      exact ⟨h.1, h.2.trans (congrArg _ h3)⟩)
  subst e1 e2
  exact key

theorem UpperTheory.V.U3.boxPres :
    CohInp → LemmaP → ∀ {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] {K : Type} [Field K] [NumberField K] [Algebra K Ω]
      {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {c : Coef l} {N : ℕ} [NeZero N] {e : ℕ} {ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O N} (D : Dat Ω K c e ψ) (U : D.ULoc) (B : Univ (BoxCond D.Kill D.ι D.ι_cont U (D.loc.It U))) (WB : WPres c.O ℤ (U.R ⧸ D.loc.It U)),
      IsLocPres U (D.loc.It U) WB → BoxPres B WB := by
  intro hI hP Ω _ _ _ K _ _ _ l hl c N hN e ψ D U B WB hW
  obtain ⟨hc, hf, hlm, -⟩ := UpperTheory.V.abelian₂.dvr_finite l c.O
  haveI := hc
  haveI : Finite (ResidueField c.O) := hf
  haveI : CharP (ResidueField c.O) l := (CharP.charP_iff_prime_eq_zero hl.out).2 (by
    rw [← map_natCast (residue c.O), residue_eq_zero_iff]; exact hlm)
  haveI : IsNoetherianRing c.O := @PrincipalIdealRing.isNoetherianRing c.O _ c.dvr.toIsPrincipalIdealRing
  obtain ⟨-, hηc, hΨc, hη, hΨ, hK, h2, -⟩ := dat_facts D
  obtain ⟨-, -, -, -, hinj, -⟩ := dat_loc D
  obtain ⟨S, A, v₀, η', Ψ', hS, hA, hr, hv, e1, e2⟩ := UpperTheory.exists_finite_place_set_factoring_through_GS D
  exact aux hI hP l D.gd.l_odd h2 D.η D.Ψ hη hΨ D.Ψ_cont D.cc D.conj hηc hΨc D.ι D.ι_cont hinj D.Kill
    (fun _ => Iff.rfl) hK (UpperTheory.exists_galois_cyclotomic_neg_one_trivial_on_eta_psi D) S A v₀ η' Ψ' hS hA hr hv e1 e2 U _ B WB hW


[OCH_SRC_GLOBAL_DUAL_ZERO] Actual GD.eta element kills all residual Tate-dual invariants
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/UnitaryThreeGroups/ExistsGaloisCyclotomicNegOneTrivialOnEtaPsi.lean#L1-L77
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.UnitaryThreeGroups.KatoUse
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.UnitaryThreeGroups.BoxDefs
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.UnitaryThreeGroups.DatFacts

open NumberField IsDedekindDomain Formalism.ConjugateSelfDual ReciprocityTheory.Gal Matrix IsLocalRing
open UpperTheory.V UpperTheory.V.Base UpperTheory.V.HV UpperTheory.V.Alg UpperTheory.V.U3

private theorem cycMod_cc {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] {σ : G ℚ Ω}
    (h : IsConjOm σ.toRingEquiv) : cycMod ℚ Ω l 1 σ = -1 := by
  obtain ⟨j, hj⟩ := h
  have hlp : l.Prime := Fact.out
  haveI : IsAlgClosed Ω := IsAlgClosure.isAlgClosed ℚ
  haveI : NeZero (l : Ω) := ⟨Nat.cast_ne_zero.mpr hlp.ne_zero⟩
  haveI : ∀ i, HasEnoughRootsOfUnity Ω (l ^ i) := fun i => inferInstance
  obtain ⟨ζ, hζ⟩ := HasEnoughRootsOfUnity.exists_primitiveRoot Ω l
  have hζl : ζ ^ l = 1 := hζ.pow_eq_one
  have hσζ : σ.toRingEquiv ζ = ζ⁻¹ := by
    apply j.injective
    rw [hj, map_inv₀]
    have hn : ‖j ζ‖ = 1 := Complex.norm_eq_one_of_pow_eq_one (n := l)
      (by rw [← map_pow, hζl, map_one]) hlp.ne_zero
    rw [Complex.inv_def, Complex.normSq_eq_norm_sq, hn]
    simp
  have hspec := cyclotomicCharacter.spec (L := Ω) l σ.toRingEquiv ζ (n := 1) (by rw [pow_one]; exact hζl)
  have hval : ((cycMod ℚ Ω l 1 σ : (ZMod (l ^ 1))ˣ) : ZMod (l ^ 1)) =
      PadicInt.toZModPow 1 (cyclotomicCharacter Ω l σ.toRingEquiv).val := rfl
  set a : ZMod (l ^ 1) := PadicInt.toZModPow 1 (cyclotomicCharacter Ω l σ.toRingEquiv).val with ha
  haveI : NeZero (l ^ 1) := ⟨by simp [hlp.ne_zero]⟩
  have hlt : a.val < l := by simpa using ZMod.val_lt a
  have hinv : ζ⁻¹ = ζ ^ (l - 1) := by
    rw [eq_comm, inv_eq_of_mul_eq_one_right]
    rw [← pow_succ', Nat.sub_add_cancel hlp.one_le, hζl]
  have hav : a.val = l - 1 := by
    apply hζ.pow_inj hlt (Nat.sub_lt hlp.pos one_pos)
    rw [← hspec, hσζ, hinv]
  apply Units.ext
  rw [hval, ← ZMod.natCast_zmod_val a, hav, Units.val_neg, Units.val_one, eq_neg_iff_add_eq_zero]
  have h0 : ((l : ℕ) : ZMod (l ^ 1)) = 0 := by
    rw [ZMod.natCast_eq_zero_iff]
    simp
  rw [← h0, ← Nat.cast_succ, Nat.succ_eq_add_one, Nat.sub_add_cancel hlp.one_le]

theorem UpperTheory.exists_galois_cyclotomic_neg_one_trivial_on_eta_psi :
    ∀ {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] {K : Type} [Field K] [NumberField K] [Algebra K Ω]
          {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {c : Coef l} {N : ℕ} [NeZero N] {e : ℕ} {ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O N} (D : Dat Ω K c e ψ),
          ∃ g : G ℚ Ω, D.η g = 1 ∧ barChar c.O D.Ψ g = 1 ∧ cycMod ℚ Ω l 1 g = -1 := by
  intro Ω _ _ _ K _ _ _ l _ c N _ e ψ D
  obtain ⟨-, hηcc, hΨcc, -⟩ := dat_facts D
  have hcyc_cc : cycMod ℚ Ω l 1 D.cc = -1 := by
    apply cycMod_cc l
    obtain ⟨φ, hφ⟩ := D.conj
    exact ⟨φ, hφ⟩
  have hgd := D.gd.eta
  rw [← D.η_ker] at hgd
  obtain ⟨h, hmem, hnot⟩ := SetLike.not_le_iff_exists.mp hgd
  obtain ⟨hh1, hh2⟩ := Subgroup.mem_inf.mp hmem
  rw [MonoidHom.mem_ker] at hh1 hh2 hnot
  have hηh : D.η h = -1 := (Int.units_eq_one_or _).resolve_left hnot
  obtain ⟨⟨w, hw, -⟩, hall⟩ := omT Ω l c
  have hwk : w.ker = (cycMod ℚ Ω l 1).ker := (hall w hw).1
  have hwh : w h = 1 := by
    rw [← MonoidHom.mem_ker, hwk, MonoidHom.mem_ker]
    exact hh1
  obtain ⟨b, -, -, hcong⟩ := (psiBar Ω l c w hw N ψ).1
  have hΨh : barChar c.O D.Ψ h = 1 := by
    have hm := hcong e h
    rw [MonoidHom.mul_apply, MonoidHom.pow_apply, hwh, hh2, one_pow, mul_one, Units.val_one] at hm
    apply Units.ext
    show (residue c.O) ((D.Ψ h : (c.O)ˣ) : c.O) = ((1 : (ResidueField c.O)ˣ) : ResidueField c.O)
    rw [Units.val_one, ← map_one (residue c.O), ← sub_eq_zero, ← map_sub, residue_eq_zero_iff]
    exact hm
  refine ⟨D.cc * h, ?_, ?_, ?_⟩
  · rw [map_mul, hηcc, hηh]
    rfl
  · rw [map_mul, hΨh, mul_one]
    simp only [barChar, MonoidHom.coe_comp, Function.comp_apply, hΨcc, map_one]
  · rw [map_mul, hcyc_cc, hh1, mul_one]


[OCH_SRC_NORMALIZATIONS] Eight actual normalization equations and their exact weights
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/UnitaryThreeGroups/MopBase.lean#L1-L111
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.UnitaryThreeGroups.IsLocPres
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.CommutativeAlgebra.Graded
import Development.AbelianVarietyTheory.Proofs.FoundationProofs.ExistsUniqueFixedPointOfAdicContraction

open Matrix IsLocalRing UpperTheory.V.Alg

namespace UpperTheory.V.U3.Mop

theorem emb_map {R S : Type} [CommRing R] [CommRing S] (f : R →+* S) (x : GG 3 R)
    (a b : Fin 3 ⊕ Fin 3) : emb (GG.map f x) a b = f (emb x a b) := by
  have h : (theta (GG.map f x).left : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) S) =
      (theta x.left : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) R).map f :=
    (congrArg Units.val (map_theta f x.left)).symm
  show (if (GG.map f x).right = 1 then Matrix.fromBlocks (GG.map f x).mat 0 0 (theta (GG.map f x).left : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) S)
    else Matrix.fromBlocks 0 (GG.map f x).mat (theta (GG.map f x).left : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) S) 0) a b =
    f ((if x.right = 1 then Matrix.fromBlocks x.mat 0 0 (theta x.left : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) R)
    else Matrix.fromBlocks 0 x.mat (theta x.left : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) R) 0) a b)
  rw [h, GG.map_mat, GG.map_right]
  split_ifs <;> rcases a with a | a <;> rcases b with b | b <;> simp

theorem emb_push {O G R S : Type} [CommRing O] [Group G] [CommRing R] [CommRing S] [Algebra O R] [Algebra O S]
    (f : R →ₐ[O] S) (r : G →* GG 3 R) (g : G) (a b : Fin 3 ⊕ Fin 3) :
    emb (push f r g) a b = f (emb (r g) a b) := emb_map (f : R →+* S) (r g) a b

theorem emb_apply {R : Type} [CommRing R] (x : GG 3 R) : emb x =
    if x.right = 1 then Matrix.fromBlocks x.mat 0 0 (theta x.left : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) R)
    else Matrix.fromBlocks 0 x.mat (theta x.left : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) R) 0 := rfl

theorem mat_eq_emb {R : Type} [CommRing R] (x : GG 3 R) (a b : Fin 3) :
    x.mat a b = emb x (.inl a) (.inl b) + emb x (.inl a) (.inr b) := by
  rw [emb_apply]
  split_ifs <;> simp

theorem unitaryEmbedding_residualRep_eq_zero_of_weight_ne {G R : Type} [Group G] [CommRing R] (η : G →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : G →* Rˣ) (g : G)
    (a b : Fin 3 ⊕ Fin 3) (h : wt6 a ≠ wt6 b) : emb (r0 η Ψ g) a b = 0 := by
  show (if (r0 η Ψ g).right = 1 then Matrix.fromBlocks (tor R (Ψ g) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) R) 0 0
    (theta (tor R (Ψ g)) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) R) else Matrix.fromBlocks 0 (tor R (Ψ g) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) R)
    (theta (tor R (Ψ g)) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) R) 0) a b = 0
  rw [theta_tor, tor_val]
  split_ifs <;> rcases a with a | a <;> rcases b with b | b <;>
    first
    | rfl
    | exact Matrix.diagonal_apply_ne (i := a) (j := b) (fun i => ((Ψ g ^ wt1 i : Rˣ) : R)) (by rintro rfl; exact h rfl)

theorem blockWeight_sub_mem_Icc (a b : Fin 3 ⊕ Fin 3) : -2 ≤ wt6 a - wt6 b ∧ wt6 a - wt6 b ≤ 2 := by
  revert a b; decide

theorem origin_of {O X R : Type} [CommRing O] [AddCommGroup X] [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] (W : WPres O X R)
    (y : R →ₐ[O] O) (hy : ∀ s, y (W.z s) = 0) : ∃ h : W.HasOrigin, W.origin h = y := by
  have key : ∀ F, y (W.π F) = MvPowerSeries.constantCoeff F := fun F => by
    have := GlobalTheory.algHom_sub_constantCoeff_mem (K := ⊥) (y.comp W.π) (fun s => hy s) F
    rw [Ideal.mem_bot, sub_eq_zero] at this
    exact this
  refine ⟨fun F hF => by rw [← key, hF, map_zero], AlgHom.ext fun x => ?_⟩
  obtain ⟨F, rfl⟩ := W.surj x
  exact (W.origin_π _ F).trans (key F).symm

theorem origin_presentation_var_eq_zero {O X R : Type} [CommRing O] [AddCommGroup X] [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] (W : WPres O X R)
    (h : W.HasOrigin) (s : W.σ) : W.origin h (W.z s) = 0 := by
  rw [WPres.z, W.origin_π, MvPowerSeries.constantCoeff_X]

theorem mem_max_of_zero {O R : Type} [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] [IsNoetherianRing O]
    [IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal O) O] [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] [IsCO O R] (x : R →ₐ[O] O) {z : R} (hz : x z = 0) :
    z ∈ maximalIdeal R := by
  rw [← res_eq_zero_iff O, ← res_map x, hz, map_zero]

theorem base_coord {O G R : Type} [CommRing O] [Group G] [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] {η : G →* ℤˣ} {Ψ : G →* Oˣ}
    {r : G →* GG 3 R} {x : R →ₐ[O] O} (hx : push x r = r0 η Ψ) (g : G) (a b : Fin 3 ⊕ Fin 3) :
    x (emb (r g) a b - algebraMap O R (emb (r0 η Ψ g) a b)) = 0 := by
  rw [map_sub, AlgHom.commutes, ← emb_push, hx]
  exact sub_self _

theorem approx {O X R σ : Type} [CommRing O] [AddCommGroup X] [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] [Finite σ] (wt : σ → X)
    (Φ : MvPowerSeries σ O →ₐ[O] R) (m : Ideal R) (𝓑 : X → Submodule O R) (h1 : (1 : R) ∈ 𝓑 0)
    (hmul : ∀ i j, ∀ a ∈ 𝓑 i, ∀ b ∈ 𝓑 j, a * b ∈ 𝓑 (i + j))
    (hm : ∀ s, Φ (MvPowerSeries.X s) ∈ m) (hB : ∀ s, Φ (MvPowerSeries.X s) ∈ 𝓑 (wt s)) (j : X)
    (F : MvPowerSeries σ O) (hF : F ∈ wtPart O wt j) (n : ℕ) : ∃ y ∈ 𝓑 j, Φ F - y ∈ m ^ n := by
  classical
  refine ⟨_, ?_, GlobalTheory.algHom_sub_aeval_truncTotal_mem Φ hm n F⟩
  have hpow : ∀ s (k : ℕ), Φ (MvPowerSeries.X s) ^ k ∈ 𝓑 (k • wt s) := fun s k => by
    induction k with
    | zero => simpa using h1
    | succ k ih => rw [pow_succ, succ_nsmul]; exact hmul _ _ _ ih _ (hB s)
  have hmon : ∀ d : σ →₀ ℕ, (d.prod fun s k => Φ (MvPowerSeries.X s) ^ k) ∈ 𝓑 (Finsupp.weight wt d) := fun d => by
    induction d using Finsupp.induction_linear with
    | zero => simpa using h1
    | add f g hf hg =>
      rw [Finsupp.prod_add_index' (by simp) (fun _ _ _ => pow_add _ _ _), map_add]
      exact hmul _ _ _ hf _ hg
    | single i k =>
      rw [Finsupp.prod_single_index (by simp), Finsupp.weight_single]
      exact hpow i k
  rw [(MvPowerSeries.truncTotal n F).as_sum, map_sum]
  refine Submodule.sum_mem _ fun d hd => ?_
  rw [MvPolynomial.aeval_monomial, ← Algebra.smul_def]
  refine Submodule.smul_mem _ _ ?_
  have hw : Finsupp.weight wt d = j := by
    by_contra hne
    refine MvPolynomial.mem_support_iff.1 hd ?_
    by_cases hlt : Finsupp.degree d < n
    · rw [MvPowerSeries.coeff_truncTotal _ hlt]; exact hF d hne
    · exact MvPowerSeries.coeff_truncTotal_eq_zero _ (not_lt.1 hlt)
  exact hw ▸ hmon d

def nEq {G R : Type} [Group G] [CommRing R] (c g₁ : G) (r : G →* GG 3 R) : Fin 8 → R :=
  ![(r c).mat 0 0 - 1, (r c).mat 1 1 - 1, (r c).mat 0 1, (r c).mat 1 0, (r c).mat 0 2, (r c).mat 2 0,
    zmat c g₁ r 0 1, zmat c g₁ r 1 0]

def nWt : Fin 8 → ℤ := ![0, 0, 1, -1, 2, -2, 1, -1]

end UpperTheory.V.U3.Mop


[OCH_SRC_MODEL_PRESENTATION] Actual weighted integral presentation, numerical dimensions, model maps and full model_of_pres
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/UnitaryThreeGroups/ModelOfPres.lean#L23-L329
private theorem mop_rename_mem {O : Type} [CommRing O] {X : Type} [AddCommGroup X] {σ τ : Type} [Finite σ]
    (u : σ → τ) (wt : τ → X) (ws : σ → X) (h : ∀ s, wt (u s) = ws s) {j : X} {g : MvPowerSeries σ O}
    (hg : g ∈ wtPart O ws j) : MvPowerSeries.rename u g ∈ wtPart O wt j := fun n hn => by
  rw [MvPowerSeries.coeff_rename]
  refine Finset.sum_eq_zero fun d hd => hg d fun hj => hn ?_
  have hd' : Finsupp.mapDomain u d = n := by simpa using hd
  rw [← hd', ← hj, Finsupp.weight_apply, Finsupp.weight_apply,
    Finsupp.sum_mapDomain_index (fun _ => by simp) (fun _ _ _ => by rw [add_smul])]
  simp [h]

private theorem mop_homog {O : Type} [CommRing O] {X : Type} [AddCommGroup X] {σ : Type} [Fintype σ]
    (wt : σ → X) {κ : Type} [Finite κ] (g : κ → MvPowerSeries σ O) (c : κ → X)
    (hg : ∀ i, g i ∈ wtPart O wt (c i)) : IsHomog O wt (Ideal.span (Set.range g)) := by
  have hp : ∀ i, g i ∈ (WPres.free O wt).part (c i) := fun i => ⟨g i, hg i, rfl⟩
  exact (homog_span O _ X (WPres.free O wt) κ (fun i => ⟨g i, (WPres.free O wt).part_le_A (hp i)⟩) c hp).1

private theorem mop_cover {O Bl B S : Type} [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] [IsNoetherianRing O]
    [IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal O) O] [CommRing Bl] [Algebra O Bl] [CommRing B] [Algebra O B] [CommRing S]
    [Algebra O S] [Nontrivial S] (WB : WPres O ℤ Bl) (i : Bl →ₐ[O] B) (W : WPres O ℤ B) {τ κ κ' : Type}
    (e : WB.σ ⊕ τ ≃ W.σ) (f : κ → MvPowerSeries W.σ O) (wf : κ → ℤ) (hrel : IsRelPres WB i W e f wf)
    (p : B →ₐ[O] S) (hp : Function.Surjective p) (n : κ' → B) (wn : κ' → ℤ) (hn : ∀ k, n k ∈ W.part (wn k))
    (hk : RingHom.ker p = Ideal.span (Set.range n)) :
    ∃ (R : Type) (_ : CommRing R) (_ : Algebra O R) (_ : IsCO O R) (WA : WPres O ℤ R) (ε : WB.σ ⊕ τ ≃ WA.σ)
      (qh : R →ₐ[O] S) (g : κ ⊕ κ' → WA.A),
      (∀ s, WA.wt (ε s) = W.wt (e s)) ∧ (∀ s, qh (WA.z (ε s)) = p (W.z (e s))) ∧
      RingHom.ker WA.π = (RingHom.ker WB.π).map (MvPowerSeries.rename fun s => ε (.inl s)) ∧
      Function.Surjective qh ∧ (∀ k, g k ∈ WA.gr (Sum.elim wf wn k)) ∧
      RingHom.ker qh = Ideal.span (Set.range fun k => (g k : R)) ∧
      ∀ j, ∀ y ∈ W.part j, ∃ y' ∈ WA.part j, qh y' = p y := by
  obtain ⟨he1, -, hf, hker⟩ := hrel
  set J : Ideal (MvPowerSeries W.σ O) := (RingHom.ker WB.π).map (MvPowerSeries.rename fun s => e (.inl s)) with hJ
  have hJh : IsHomog O W.wt J := by
    obtain ⟨k, f0, c0, hf0, h0⟩ := ker_gen O ℤ WB.σ WB.wt _ WB.homog
    rw [hJ, h0, Ideal.map_span, ← Set.range_comp]
    exact mop_homog W.wt _ c0 fun t => mop_rename_mem _ W.wt WB.wt he1 (hf0 t)
  have hle : ∀ F ∈ J, (p.comp W.π) F = 0 := fun F hF => by
    have : W.π F = 0 := (hker ▸ Ideal.mem_sup_left hF : F ∈ RingHom.ker W.π)
    rw [AlgHom.comp_apply, this, map_zero]
  let qh : (MvPowerSeries W.σ O ⧸ J) →ₐ[O] S := Ideal.Quotient.liftₐ J (p.comp W.π) hle
  have hqh : ∀ F, qh (Ideal.Quotient.mk J F) = p (W.π F) := fun _ => rfl
  haveI : Nontrivial (MvPowerSeries W.σ O ⧸ J) := qh.toRingHom.domain_nontrivial
  haveI := co_mvps O O W.σ
  haveI : IsCO O (MvPowerSeries W.σ O ⧸ J) :=
    co_quot O _ _ (Ideal.Quotient.mkₐ O J) (Ideal.Quotient.mkₐ_surjective O J)
  have hkm : RingHom.ker (Ideal.Quotient.mkₐ O J) = J := Ideal.Quotient.mkₐ_ker O J
  let WA : WPres O ℤ (MvPowerSeries W.σ O ⧸ J) :=
    { σ := W.σ, wt := W.wt, π := Ideal.Quotient.mkₐ O J, surj := Ideal.Quotient.mkₐ_surjective O J
      homog := by rw [hkm]; exact hJh }
  choose N hN hN' using hn
  have hG : ∀ k : κ ⊕ κ', Sum.elim f N k ∈ wtPart O W.wt (Sum.elim wf wn k) := by
    rintro (k | k)
    · exact hf k
    · exact hN k
  have hG' : ∀ k : κ ⊕ κ', Ideal.Quotient.mk J (Sum.elim f N k) ∈ WA.part (Sum.elim wf wn k) :=
    fun k => ⟨_, hG k, rfl⟩
  refine ⟨_, inferInstance, inferInstance, inferInstance, WA, e, qh, fun k => ⟨_, WA.part_le_A (hG' k)⟩,
    fun _ => rfl, fun _ => rfl, hkm, ?_, hG', ?_, ?_⟩
  · intro y
    obtain ⟨y, rfl⟩ := hp y
    obtain ⟨F, rfl⟩ := W.surj y
    exact ⟨_, hqh F⟩
  · refine le_antisymm (fun y hy => ?_) (Ideal.span_le.2 ?_)
    · obtain ⟨F, rfl⟩ := Ideal.Quotient.mk_surjective y
      have h1 : W.π F ∈ (Ideal.span (Set.range N)).map W.π := by
        have h2 : W.π ∘ N = n := funext hN'
        rw [Ideal.map_span, ← Set.range_comp, h2, ← hk]
        exact hy
      obtain ⟨G, hG1, hG2⟩ := (Ideal.mem_map_iff_of_surjective _ W.surj).1 h1
      have h3 : F - G ∈ J ⊔ Ideal.span (Set.range f) := by
        rw [← hker, RingHom.mem_ker, map_sub, hG2, sub_self]
      have h4 : (J ⊔ Ideal.span (Set.range f)) ⊔ Ideal.span (Set.range N) ≤
          (Ideal.span (Set.range fun k => Ideal.Quotient.mk J (Sum.elim f N k))).comap (Ideal.Quotient.mk J) := by
        refine sup_le (sup_le (fun y hy => ?_) (Ideal.span_le.2 ?_)) (Ideal.span_le.2 ?_)
        · rw [Ideal.mem_comap, Ideal.Quotient.eq_zero_iff_mem.2 hy]
          exact zero_mem _
        · rintro _ ⟨k, rfl⟩
          exact Ideal.subset_span ⟨.inl k, rfl⟩
        · rintro _ ⟨k, rfl⟩
          exact Ideal.subset_span ⟨.inr k, rfl⟩
      have h5 := h4 (Ideal.add_mem _ (Ideal.mem_sup_left h3) (Ideal.mem_sup_right hG1))
      rw [sub_add_cancel] at h5
      exact h5
    · rintro _ ⟨k | k, rfl⟩
      · have : W.π (f k) = 0 :=
          (hker ▸ Ideal.mem_sup_right (Ideal.subset_span ⟨k, rfl⟩) : f k ∈ RingHom.ker W.π)
        show p (W.π (f k)) = 0
        rw [this, map_zero]
      · have : n k ∈ RingHom.ker p := hk ▸ Ideal.subset_span ⟨k, rfl⟩
        rw [← hN' k] at this
        exact this
  · rintro j _ ⟨F, hF, rfl⟩
    exact ⟨Ideal.Quotient.mk J F, ⟨F, hF, rfl⟩, rfl⟩

private theorem mop_card {α β : Type} [Finite α] [Finite β] (a : α → ℤ) (b : β → ℤ) (j : ℤ) :
    Nat.card {i // Sum.elim a b i = j} = Nat.card {i // a i = j} + Nat.card {i // b i = j} := by
  rw [Nat.card_congr Equiv.subtypeSum, Nat.card_sum]
  rfl

private theorem mop_nWt : Nat.card {i // nWt i = -1} = 2 ∧ Nat.card {i // nWt i = -2} = 1 ∧
    Nat.card {i // nWt i = 1} = 2 ∧ Nat.card {i // nWt i = 2} = 1 ∧ Nat.card {i // nWt i = 0} = 2 := by
  simp only [Nat.card_eq_fintype_card]
  decide

private theorem mop_mat_mem {O G R : Type} [CommRing O] [Group G] [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] {r : G →* GG 3 R}
    {W : WPres O ℤ R} (h : EntHomog r W) (g : G) (a b : Fin 3) : (r g).mat a b ∈ W.part (wt a b) := by
  rw [mat_eq_emb]
  exact add_mem (h g (.inl a) (.inl b)) (h g (.inl a) (.inr b))

private theorem mop_nEq_mem {O G R : Type} [CommRing O] [Group G] [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] {r : G →* GG 3 R}
    {W : WPres O ℤ R} (h : EntHomog r W) (c g₁ : G) (k : Fin 8) : nEq c g₁ r k ∈ W.part (nWt k) := by
  have h1 : (1 : R) ∈ W.part 0 := SetLike.GradedOne.one_mem
  have hm := mop_mat_mem h
  fin_cases k
  · exact sub_mem (hm c 0 0) h1
  · exact sub_mem (hm c 1 1) h1
  · exact hm c 0 1
  · exact hm c 1 0
  · exact hm c 0 2
  · exact hm c 2 0
  · exact sub_mem (hm g₁ 0 1) (hm _ 0 1)
  · exact sub_mem (hm g₁ 1 0) (hm _ 1 0)

private theorem mop_der_coord {O : Type} [CommRing O] (s : ℤˣ) (t : Oˣ) (a b : Fin 3 ⊕ Fin 3) :
    ∃ (u : O) (a' b' : Fin 3), wt a' b' = wt6 a - wt6 b ∧
      ∀ {R Q : Type} [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] [AddCommGroup Q] [Module O Q] (x : R →ₐ[O] O), ∀ D ∈ PtDer x Q,
        ∀ y : GG 3 R, GG.map (x : R →+* O) y = ⟨tor O t, s⟩ → ∀ c : O,
          D (emb y a b - algebraMap O R c) = u • D (y.mat a' b') := by
  obtain ⟨u, a', b', h1, h2⟩ := UpperTheory.exists_pointDerivation_emb_entry_eq_smul_mat_entry s t a b
  refine ⟨u, a', b', h1, fun x D hD y hy c => ?_⟩
  have h3 : D 1 = 0 := by simpa using hD 1 1
  rw [map_sub, h2 x D hD y hy, Algebra.algebraMap_eq_smul_one, map_smul, h3, smul_zero, sub_zero]

private theorem mop_main {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsLocalRing O] [IsNoetherianRing O]
    [IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal O) O] {K : Type} [NormedField K] [Algebra O K]
    (hpts : ∀ {R S : Type} [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] [IsCO O R] [CommRing S] [Algebra O S] [IsCO O S]
      (W : WPres O ℤ R) (h : W.HasOrigin) (qh : R →ₐ[O] S), Function.Surjective qh → ∀ {κ : Type} (g : κ → W.A) (w : κ → ℤ), (∀ i, g i ∈ W.gr (w i)) →
      RingHom.ker qh = Ideal.span (Set.range fun i => (g i : R)) → M2 W.gr (Ideal.span (Set.range g)) →
      ∀ ξ : W.A →ₐ[O] K, OnS (Ideal.span (Set.range g)) ξ →
        ∃ (t : Kˣ) (f : S →ₐ[O] K), IsLocPt f ∧ act W.gr (tz t) ξ = f.comp (qh.comp W.A.val))
    (hder : ∀ {R S : Type} [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] [IsCO O R] [CommRing S] [Algebra O S] [IsCO O S]
      (W : WPres O ℤ R) (h : W.HasOrigin) (qh : R →ₐ[O] S), Function.Surjective qh → ∀ {κ : Type} (g : κ → W.A) (w : κ → ℤ), (∀ i, g i ∈ W.gr (w i)) →
      RingHom.ker qh = Ideal.span (Set.range fun i => (g i : R)) → M2 W.gr (Ideal.span (Set.range g)) →
      ∀ f : S →ₐ[O] K, IsLocPt f → ∀ D ∈ PtDer (f.comp (qh.comp W.A.val)) K, (∀ y ∈ Ideal.span (Set.range g), D y = 0) →
        ∃ F : S →ₐ[O] DualNumber K, dualFst F = f ∧ ∀ y : W.A, (F (qh y)).snd = D y)
    {Γ H : Type} [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace Γ] [Group H] [TopologicalSpace H]
    {η : Γ →* ℤˣ} {Ψ : Γ →* Oˣ} {N : Set Γ} {ι : H →* Γ} {hι : Continuous ι}
    {U : Univ (GCond.noCondition O (η.comp ι) (Ψ.comp ι))} {I : Ideal U.R} {cc g₁ : Γ} (S : SRing N hι U I cc g₁)
    (B : Univ (BoxCond N ι hι U I)) (WB : WPres O ℤ (U.R ⧸ I)) (hM : Marks η Ψ cc g₁) (hL : LocGood U I)
    (hΨ : CharCont Ψ) (hN : ∀ g ∈ N, r0 η Ψ g = 1) (hWB : IsLocPres U I WB) (hbox : BoxPres B WB) :
    Nonempty (Model S K) := by
  classical
  obtain ⟨W, i, τ, κ, _, _, e, f, wf, hrel, hi, hcoord, hent, hcount⟩ := hbox
  obtain ⟨x, τ₀, _, w, φ, hx, hφ, hdim⟩ := hL.chart
  obtain ⟨hB, hBx, φb, hφb⟩ := UpperTheory.exists_origin_and_chart_of_isLocPres U I x w φ WB hx hφ hWB
  obtain ⟨p, hp, hpr, hpk⟩ := UpperTheory.exists_surjection_univ_normCond_ker_span_nEq cc g₁ B S hM.csq hM.ηc
  have hIx : I ≤ RingHom.ker x := fun y hy => by
    rw [← hBx]
    show WB.origin hB (Ideal.Quotient.mk I y) = 0
    rw [Ideal.Quotient.eq_zero_iff_mem.2 hy, map_zero]
  obtain ⟨x₀, hx₀, -⟩ := (S.rep O).2 (baseLift O η Ψ hΨ)
    ⟨baseRepGL3_normalised_of_psi_eq_one η Ψ g₁ hM.Ψc, hN, x, GLift.ext hx, hIx⟩
  have hbase : IsBase η Ψ S.r.hom x₀ := congrArg GLift.hom hx₀
  have hSr : S.r.hom = push p B.r.hom := (congrArg GLift.hom hpr).symm
  have hnorm : Normalised cc g₁ S.r.hom := ((S.rep S.R).1 (AlgHom.id _ _)).1
  obtain ⟨R, _, _, _, WA, ε, qh, g, hw, hz, hkA, hq, hg, hkq, hpart⟩ :=
    mop_cover WB i W e f wf hrel p hp (nEq cc g₁ B.r.hom) nWt (mop_nEq_mem hent cc g₁) hpk
  have hwl : ∀ s, WA.wt (ε (.inl s)) = WB.wt s := fun s => (hw _).trans (hrel.1 s)
  have hl : ∀ s, ∃ h a b, WB.wt s = wt6 a - wt6 b ∧
      WB.z s = Ideal.Quotient.mk I (emb (U.r.hom h) a b - algebraMap O U.R (emb (r0 η Ψ (ι h)) a b)) ∧
      qh (WA.z (ε (.inl s))) = emb (S.r.hom (ι h)) a b - algebraMap O S.R (emb (r0 η Ψ (ι h)) a b) := fun s => by
    obtain ⟨h, a, b, h1, h2⟩ := hWB.1 s
    have h3 : WB.z s = Ideal.Quotient.mkₐ O I
        (emb (U.r.hom h) a b - algebraMap O U.R (emb (r0 η Ψ (ι h)) a b)) := by
      rw [h2, map_sub, AlgHom.commutes, emb_push]
      rfl
    refine ⟨h, a, b, h1, h3, ?_⟩
    have h4 : W.z (e (.inl s)) = i (WB.z s) := by
      rw [WPres.z, WPres.z, ← hrel.2.1, MvPowerSeries.rename_X]
    rw [hz, h4, h3, ← AlgHom.comp_apply i, map_sub, AlgHom.commutes, ← emb_push, hi, map_sub, AlgHom.commutes,
      hSr, emb_push]
    rfl
  have hco : ∀ s, ∃ γ a b, WA.wt s = wt6 a - wt6 b ∧
      qh (WA.z s) = emb (S.r.hom γ) a b - algebraMap O S.R (emb (r0 η Ψ γ) a b) := fun s => by
    obtain ⟨s | t, rfl⟩ := ε.surjective s
    · obtain ⟨h, a, b, h1, -, h3⟩ := hl s
      exact ⟨ι h, a, b, (hwl s).trans h1, h3⟩
    · obtain ⟨γ, a, b, h1, h2⟩ := hcoord t
      refine ⟨γ, a, b, (hw _).trans h1, ?_⟩
      rw [hz, h2, map_sub, AlgHom.commutes, hSr, emb_push]
  have hz0 : ∀ s, x₀ (qh (WA.z s)) = 0 := fun s => by
    obtain ⟨γ, a, b, -, h2⟩ := hco s
    rw [h2]
    exact base_coord hbase γ a b
  obtain ⟨h, hor⟩ := origin_of WA (x₀.comp qh) hz0
  have hbd : ∀ s, -2 ≤ WA.wt s ∧ WA.wt s ≤ 2 := fun s => by
    obtain ⟨γ, a, b, h1, -⟩ := hco s
    rw [h1]
    exact blockWeight_sub_mem_Icc a b
  set a : ℤ → ℕ := fun j => Nat.card {t // W.wt (e (.inr t)) = j} with ha
  have ha0 : ∀ j, ¬ (-2 ≤ j ∧ j ≤ 2) → a j = 0 := fun j hj => by
    haveI : IsEmpty {t // W.wt (e (.inr t)) = j} := ⟨fun t => hj (by rw [← t.2, ← hw]; exact hbd _)⟩
    exact Nat.card_of_isEmpty
  have hcnt : ∀ j, Nat.card {u // wf u = j} = a j + boxDef j := hcount
  have hgb : ∀ k, -2 ≤ Sum.elim wf nWt k ∧ Sum.elim wf nWt k ≤ 2 := by
    rintro (k | k)
    · by_contra hj
      have h1 := hcnt (wf k)
      have h2 : boxDef (wf k) = 0 := if_neg fun h3 => hj (by
        have := abs_le.1 h3
        exact ⟨by show -2 ≤ wf k; omega, by show wf k ≤ 2; omega⟩)
      rw [ha0 (wf k) hj, h2] at h1
      haveI : Nonempty {u // wf u = wf k} := ⟨⟨k, rfl⟩⟩
      exact Nat.card_pos.ne' h1
    · revert k
      show ∀ k, -2 ≤ nWt k ∧ nWt k ≤ 2
      decide
  have hgq : ∀ k, qh (g k) = 0 := fun k =>
    (hkq ▸ Ideal.subset_span ⟨k, rfl⟩ : (g k : R) ∈ RingHom.ker qh)
  obtain ⟨n1, n2, n3, n4, n5⟩ := mop_nWt
  obtain ⟨Q, hQ⟩ := UpperTheory.exists_eqns_of_weighted_generators WA.gr (WA.originA h) (a (-1) + 2) (a (-2) + 1) (a 1 + 3) (a 2 + 1) (a 0 + 3) g
    (Sum.elim wf nWt) hg (fun k => by
      show WA.origin h (g k) = 0
      rw [hor, AlgHom.comp_apply, hgq, map_zero]) hgb
    (by rw [mop_card, hcnt, n1]; rfl) (by rw [mop_card, hcnt, n2]; rfl) (by rw [mop_card, hcnt, n3]; rfl)
    (by rw [mop_card, hcnt, n4]; rfl) (by rw [mop_card, hcnt, n5]; rfl)
  obtain ⟨h', ψ, hψ, -⟩ := chart_add O ℤ _ WB hB τ₀ w φb hφb R WA τ ε hwl hkA
  refine ⟨{
    A := WA.A
    𝒜 := WA.gr
    x := WA.originA h
    wring := isWRing O R WA h hbd
    r := a (-1) + 2
    s := a (-2) + 1
    r' := a 1 + 3
    s' := a 2 + 1
    m := a 0 + 3
    Q := Q
    one_le := by omega
    smooth := ⟨τ₀ ⊕ τ, inferInstance, _, _, chart_gr O ℤ R WA h _ _ ψ hψ, fun j => ?_⟩
    q := qh.comp WA.A.val
    ker_q := ?_
    x₀ := x₀
    base := hbase
    x₀_q := by rw [← AlgHom.comp_assoc, ← hor]; rfl
    ent := fun γ a b => ?_
    dense := fun j y hy n => ?_
    pts := ?_
    der := ?_
    lift := ?_ }⟩
  · simp only [hw]
    rw [mop_card, hdim]
    show locDims j + a j = _
    have := ha0 j
    unfold locDims mDims
    split_ifs <;> first | omega | (subst_vars; omega)
  · rw [hQ]
    ext y
    rw [← (homog_span O R ℤ WA _ g _ hg).2 y, ← hkq]
    rfl
  · obtain ⟨y, hy, hy'⟩ := hpart _ _ (mop_mat_mem hent γ a b)
    refine ⟨⟨y, WA.part_le_A hy⟩, hy, ?_⟩
    rw [hSr]
    exact hy'
  · obtain ⟨F, hF, hFy⟩ := hy
    have h1 : (qh.comp WA.A.val) y = (qh.comp WA.π) F := congrArg qh hFy.symm
    rw [h1]
    exact UpperTheory.exists_span_entMon_approx_of_mem_wtPart η Ψ S.r.hom cc ((S.r.hasMult cc).trans hM.ηc) hnorm.atc WA.wt (qh.comp WA.π) _
      (fun s => mem_max_of_zero x₀ (hz0 s)) hco j F hF n
  · rw [hQ]
    exact hpts WA h qh hq g _ hg hkq
  · rw [hQ]
    exact hder WA h qh hq g _ hg hkq
  · intro a₀ _ D hD hDw xl hxl D₁ hD₁ hD₁I hD₁w hcomp
    have hxx : xl = x := ((U.rep O).2 (U.r.map x) trivial).unique (GLift.ext (hxl.trans hx.symm)) rfl
    subst hxx
    let Db : (U.R ⧸ I) →ₗ[O] O := (Submodule.liftQ (I.restrictScalars O) D₁ hD₁I) ∘ₗ
      (Submodule.Quotient.restrictScalarsEquiv O I).symm.toLinearMap
    have hDb : ∀ y, Db (Ideal.Quotient.mk I y) = D₁ y := fun _ => rfl
    have hxo : ∀ y, WB.origin hB (Ideal.Quotient.mk I y) = xl y := fun y => DFunLike.congr_fun hBx y
    refine UpperTheory.exists_base_pointDerivation_of_weight_homogeneous WB hB WA h ε hwl hkA qh x₀ hor.symm (Ideal.Quotient.mkₐ O _).toLinearMap
      (Ideal.Quotient.mkₐ_surjective O _) (-1) D hD (fun s hs => ?_) Db (fun y y' => ?_) (fun s hs => ?_) fun s => ?_
    · obtain ⟨γ, a, b, h1, h2⟩ := hco s
      obtain ⟨u, a', b', h3, h4⟩ := mop_der_coord (η γ) (Ψ γ) a b
      rw [h2, h4 x₀ D hD _ (DFunLike.congr_fun hbase γ), hDw γ a' b' (by rwa [h3, ← h1]), smul_zero]
    · obtain ⟨y, rfl⟩ := Ideal.Quotient.mk_surjective y
      obtain ⟨y', rfl⟩ := Ideal.Quotient.mk_surjective y'
      rw [← map_mul, hDb, hDb, hDb, hxo, hxo]
      exact hD₁ y y'
    · obtain ⟨γ, a, b, h1, h2, -⟩ := hl s
      obtain ⟨u, a', b', h3, h4⟩ := mop_der_coord (η (ι γ)) (Ψ (ι γ)) a b
      rw [h2, hDb, h4 xl D₁ hD₁ _ (DFunLike.congr_fun hxl γ), hD₁w γ a' b' (by rwa [h3, ← h1]), smul_zero]
    · obtain ⟨γ, a, b, -, h2, h5⟩ := hl s
      obtain ⟨u, a', b', -, h4⟩ := mop_der_coord (η (ι γ)) (Ψ (ι γ)) a b
      rw [h2, h5, hDb, h4 xl D₁ hD₁ _ (DFunLike.congr_fun hxl γ), h4 x₀ D hD _ (DFunLike.congr_fun hbase (ι γ)),
        map_smul, ← hcomp]
      rfl

theorem UpperTheory.V.U3.model_of_pres :
    ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {c : Coef l} {Γ H : Type} [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace Γ] [IsTopologicalGroup Γ] [Group H] [TopologicalSpace H] [IsTopologicalGroup H]
      {η : Γ →* ℤˣ} {Ψ : Γ →* (c.O)ˣ} {N : Set Γ} {ι : H →* Γ} {hι : Continuous ι}
      {U : Univ (GCond.noCondition c.O (η.comp ι) (Ψ.comp ι))} {I : Ideal U.R} {cc g₁ : Γ} (S : SRing N hι U I cc g₁) (B : Univ (BoxCond N ι hι U I)) (WB : WPres c.O ℤ (U.R ⧸ I)),
      l ≠ 2 → Marks η Ψ cc g₁ → LocGood U I → CharCont Ψ → (∀ g ∈ N, r0 η Ψ g = 1) →
      IsLocPres U I WB → BoxPres B WB → Nonempty (Model S (PadicAlgCl l)) := by
  intro l _ c Γ H _ _ _ _ _ _ η Ψ N ι hι U I cc g₁ S B WB _ hM hL hΨ hN hWB hbox
  haveI := (UpperTheory.V.abelian₂.dvr_finite l c.O).1
  haveI : IsNoetherianRing c.O := PrincipalIdealRing.isNoetherianRing
  exact mop_main (@UpperTheory.exists_torus_twist_eq_localPoint_of_weightedPres l _ c) (@UpperTheory.exists_dualNumber_point_of_pointDerivation_vanishing l _ c) S B WB hM hL hΨ hN hWB hbox


[OCH_SRC_MODEL_EXISTS] Complete original exists_model with unchanged CohInp and LemmaP antecedents
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/UnitaryThreeGroups/ModelEx.lean#L156-L178
theorem UpperTheory.V.U3.exists_model :
    CohInp → LemmaP → ∀ {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] {K : Type} [Field K] [NumberField K] [Algebra K Ω]
      {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {c : Coef l} {N : ℕ} [NeZero N] {e : ℕ} {ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O N} (D : Dat Ω K c e ψ) {U : D.ULoc} {g₁ : G ℚ Ω}, D.Marked g₁ → ∀ (S : D.Ring U g₁), Nonempty (Model S (PadicAlgCl l)) := by
  intro hC hP Ω _ _ _ K _ _ _ l hl c N _ e ψ D U g₁ hg S
  haveI : IsGalois ℚ Ω := IsAlgClosure.isGalois ℚ Ω
  obtain ⟨-, -, -, hη, hΨ, hK, -⟩ := dat_facts D
  haveI : IsLocalRing c.O := c.dvr.toIsLocalRing
  haveI : IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal c.O) c.O := (dvrFacts l c.O).2.2.2.1
  haveI : IsLocalHom (algebraMap ℤ_[l] c.O) := (dvrFacts l c.O).2.2.1
  haveI : Finite (ResidueField ℤ_[l]) := Finite.of_equiv _ (PadicInt.residueField (p := l)).symm.toEquiv
  haveI : Finite (ResidueField c.O) := Module.finite_of_finite (ResidueField ℤ_[l])
  haveI : CharP (ResidueField c.O) l := by
    rw [CharP.charP_iff_prime_eq_zero hl.out, ← map_natCast (residue c.O), residue_eq_zero_iff,
      mem_maximalIdeal, mem_nonunits_iff, ← map_natCast (algebraMap ℤ_[l] c.O)]
    intro hu
    exact PadicInt.prime_p.not_unit (isUnit_of_map_unit _ _ hu)
  have hL : LocGood U (D.loc.It U) := Loc.locGood hC.loc D.loc U
  have hcont : ∀ j : ℤ, AdicCont (maximalIdeal c.O) fun g => ((D.Ψ g ^ j : (c.O)ˣ) : c.O) := D.Ψ_cont
  haveI := Subgroup.is_normal_topologicalClosure (Subgroup.normalClosure D.Kill)
  have hV := exists_univ l D.η D.Ψ hη hΨ D.Kill (fun g h => h.choose_spec.2.2) _ rfl (phi_kill hC.gf D _ rfl)
  obtain ⟨⟨B⟩, -⟩ := exists_sRing D.ι_cont U (D.loc.It U) D.cc g₁ hg hL hcont hK hV
  obtain ⟨WB, hWB⟩ := exists_locPres U (D.loc.It U) (hη.comp_continuous D.ι_cont) hL
  exact model_of_pres S B WB D.gd.l_odd hg hL hcont hK hWB (boxPres hC hP D U B WB hWB)


[OCH_SRC_MODEL_FINITE] FinA gives the finite quotient weight-zero module M2
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/UnitaryThreeGroups/ModelIdealFinitenessOfWeightZeroFinite.lean#L1-L189
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.UnitaryThreeGroups.Rigid0

open Matrix IsLocalRing UpperTheory.V.U3 UpperTheory.V.Alg UpperTheory.V.HV

private theorem map_sub_map_mem_pow_of_dist_le_zpow_neg {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {R : Type} [CommRing R] (J : Ideal R)
    (hl : (l : R) ∈ J) {k : ℕ} (φ : (Fin k → ℤ_[l]) →+ R) (n : ℕ) (v w : Fin k → ℤ_[l])
    (h : dist v w ≤ (l : ℝ) ^ (-(n : ℤ))) : φ v - φ w ∈ J ^ n := by
  have hdiv : ∀ i, ∃ u : ℤ_[l], v i - w i = (l : ℤ_[l]) ^ n * u := by
    intro i
    have hi : ‖v i - w i‖ ≤ (l : ℝ) ^ (-(n : ℤ)) := by
      rw [← dist_eq_norm]
      exact (dist_le_pi_dist v w i).trans h
    rw [PadicInt.norm_le_pow_iff_mem_span_pow, Ideal.mem_span_singleton] at hi
    obtain ⟨u, hu⟩ := hi
    exact ⟨u, hu⟩
  choose u hu using hdiv
  have hvw : v - w = (l ^ n) • u := by
    ext i
    simp [hu i, nsmul_eq_mul]
  rw [← map_sub, hvw, map_nsmul, nsmul_eq_mul]
  refine Ideal.mul_mem_right _ _ ?_
  rw [Nat.cast_pow]
  exact Ideal.pow_mem_pow hl n

private theorem mem_range_of_approx {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {R : Type} [CommRing R] (J : Ideal R)
    [IsHausdorff J R] (hl : (l : R) ∈ J) {k : ℕ} (φ : (Fin k → ℤ_[l]) →+ R) (y : R)
    (h : ∀ n : ℕ, ∃ v, y - φ v ∈ J ^ n) : ∃ v, φ v = y := by
  choose v hv using h
  obtain ⟨a, ψ, hψ, htend⟩ := SeqCompactSpace.tendsto_subseq v
  refine ⟨a, ?_⟩
  have hall : ∀ m : ℕ, y - φ a ∈ J ^ m := by
    intro m
    have hpos : (0 : ℝ) < (l : ℝ) ^ (-(m : ℤ)) := by
      have hl0 : (0 : ℝ) < l := by exact_mod_cast (Fact.out : l.Prime).pos
      exact zpow_pos hl0 _
    have hev1 : ∀ᶠ j in Filter.atTop, (v ∘ ψ) j ∈ Metric.closedBall a ((l : ℝ) ^ (-(m : ℤ))) :=
      htend.eventually (Metric.closedBall_mem_nhds a hpos)
    have hev2 : ∀ᶠ j in Filter.atTop, m ≤ ψ j := hψ.tendsto_atTop.eventually_ge_atTop m
    obtain ⟨j, hj1, hj2⟩ := (hev1.and hev2).exists
    have e1 : y - φ (v (ψ j)) ∈ J ^ m := Ideal.pow_le_pow_right hj2 (hv (ψ j))
    have e2 : φ (v (ψ j)) - φ a ∈ J ^ m := map_sub_map_mem_pow_of_dist_le_zpow_neg J hl φ m _ _ hj1
    have e3 := add_mem e1 e2
    rwa [sub_add_sub_cancel] at e3
  have h0 : y - φ a = 0 := by
    refine IsHausdorff.haus' (I := J) (y - φ a) (fun n => ?_)
    rw [SModEq.zero, smul_eq_mul, Ideal.mul_top]
    exact hall n
  exact (sub_eq_zero.mp h0).symm

private theorem norm_coe_coefO_le_one {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] (c : Coef l) (x : c.O) :
    ‖(x : PadicAlgCl l)‖ ≤ 1 := by
  haveI := c.fin
  have hint : IsIntegral ℤ_[l] (x : PadicAlgCl l) := by
    have hx : IsIntegral ℤ_[l] x := Algebra.IsIntegral.isIntegral x
    exact hx.map c.O.val
  obtain ⟨p, hmon, hp⟩ := hint
  by_contra hgt
  push_neg at hgt
  rw [hmon.as_sum, Polynomial.eval₂_add, Polynomial.eval₂_X_pow, Polynomial.eval₂_finset_sum] at hp
  have hcoef : ∀ i ∈ Finset.range p.natDegree,
      ‖Polynomial.eval₂ (algebraMap ℤ_[l] (PadicAlgCl l)) (x : PadicAlgCl l)
        (Polynomial.C (p.coeff i) * Polynomial.X ^ i)‖ ≤ ‖(x : PadicAlgCl l)‖ ^ i := by
    intro i _
    rw [Polynomial.eval₂_mul, Polynomial.eval₂_C, Polynomial.eval₂_X_pow, norm_mul, norm_pow]
    have hc : ‖algebraMap ℤ_[l] (PadicAlgCl l) (p.coeff i)‖ ≤ 1 := by
      rw [IsScalarTower.algebraMap_apply ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] (PadicAlgCl l), PadicAlgCl.norm_extends]
      exact PadicInt.norm_le_one _
    calc _ ≤ 1 * ‖(x : PadicAlgCl l)‖ ^ i := by gcongr
      _ = _ := one_mul _
  have hlt : ‖∑ i ∈ Finset.range p.natDegree, Polynomial.eval₂ (algebraMap ℤ_[l] (PadicAlgCl l))
      (x : PadicAlgCl l) (Polynomial.C (p.coeff i) * Polynomial.X ^ i)‖ <
      ‖(x : PadicAlgCl l)‖ ^ p.natDegree := by
    rcases Nat.eq_zero_or_pos p.natDegree with hn | hn
    · rw [hn]
      simp
    · obtain ⟨i, hi, hle⟩ := IsUltrametricDist.exists_norm_finset_sum_le_of_nonempty
        (Finset.nonempty_range_iff.mpr hn.ne') (fun i => Polynomial.eval₂
          (algebraMap ℤ_[l] (PadicAlgCl l)) (x : PadicAlgCl l) (Polynomial.C (p.coeff i) * Polynomial.X ^ i))
      exact lt_of_le_of_lt (hle.trans (hcoef i hi)) (pow_lt_pow_right₀ hgt (Finset.mem_range.mp hi))
  have heq := eq_neg_of_add_eq_zero_left hp
  have hn := congrArg norm heq
  rw [norm_neg, norm_pow] at hn
  linarith

private theorem natCast_not_isUnit {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] (c : Coef l) : ¬ IsUnit (l : c.O) := by
  rintro ⟨u, hu⟩
  have h1 := norm_coe_coefO_le_one c (↑u⁻¹ : c.O)
  have hprod : (l : PadicAlgCl l) * ((↑u⁻¹ : c.O) : PadicAlgCl l) = 1 := by
    have h := congrArg (fun z : c.O => (z : PadicAlgCl l)) (Units.mul_inv u)
    rw [hu] at h
    simpa using h
  have hl : ‖(l : PadicAlgCl l)‖ = (l : ℝ)⁻¹ := by
    rw [← map_natCast (algebraMap ℚ_[l] (PadicAlgCl l)), PadicAlgCl.norm_extends, Padic.norm_p]
  have hn := congrArg norm hprod
  rw [norm_mul, norm_one, hl] at hn
  have hl2 : (2 : ℝ) ≤ l := by exact_mod_cast (Fact.out : l.Prime).two_le
  have hle : (l : ℝ)⁻¹ * ‖((↑u⁻¹ : c.O) : PadicAlgCl l)‖ ≤ (l : ℝ)⁻¹ * 1 :=
    mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left h1 (by positivity)
  have hinv : (l : ℝ)⁻¹ < 1 := inv_lt_one_of_one_lt₀ (by linarith)
  linarith

theorem UpperTheory.V.U3.modelIdeal_finiteness_of_weightZero_finite :
    ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {c : Coef l} {Γ H : Type} [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace Γ] [IsTopologicalGroup Γ] [Group H] [TopologicalSpace H] [IsTopologicalGroup H]
      {η : Γ →* ℤˣ} {Ψ : Γ →* (c.O)ˣ} {N : Set Γ} {ι : H →* Γ} {hι : Continuous ι}
      {U : Univ (GCond.noCondition c.O (η.comp ι) (Ψ.comp ι))} {I : Ideal U.R} {cc g₁ : Γ} (S : SRing N hι U I cc g₁) (M : Model S (PadicAlgCl l)), l ≠ 2 → Marks η Ψ cc g₁ →
      Module.Finite c.O (weightZeroSubalgebra c.O S.r.hom) → M2 M.𝒜 M.Q.ideal := by
  intro l _ c Γ H _ _ _ _ _ _ η Ψ N ι hι U I cc g₁ S M hl2 hmk hfin
  haveI := c.dvr
  haveI := c.fin
  haveI hH : IsHausdorff (maximalIdeal S.R) S.R := (IsCO.complete (O := c.O) (R := S.R)).toIsHausdorff
  haveI hloc : IsLocalHom (algebraMap c.O S.R) := IsCO.isLocalHom (O := c.O) (R := S.R)
  have hlm : ((l : ℕ) : S.R) ∈ maximalIdeal S.R := by
    rw [IsLocalRing.mem_maximalIdeal, mem_nonunits_iff]
    intro hu
    apply natCast_not_isUnit c
    rw [← map_natCast (algebraMap c.O S.R)] at hu
    exact isUnit_of_map_unit (algebraMap c.O S.R) _ hu
  obtain ⟨n, b, hb⟩ := Module.Finite.exists_fin (R := c.O) (M := weightZeroSubalgebra c.O S.r.hom)
  obtain ⟨t, o, ho⟩ := Module.Finite.exists_fin (R := ℤ_[l]) (M := c.O)
  let F : (Fin n × Fin t → ℤ_[l]) → Fin n × Fin t → S.R := fun z q =>
    algebraMap c.O S.R (algebraMap ℤ_[l] c.O (z q) * o q.2) * ((b q.1 : weightZeroSubalgebra c.O S.r.hom) : S.R)
  let φ : (Fin (n * t) → ℤ_[l]) →+ S.R :=
    { toFun := fun v => ∑ q : Fin n × Fin t, F (v ∘ finProdFinEquiv) q
      map_zero' := by simp [F]
      map_add' := by
        intro v w
        simp only [F, Function.comp_apply, Pi.add_apply, map_add, add_mul, Finset.sum_add_distrib] }
  have hφB : ∀ v, φ v ∈ weightZeroSubalgebra c.O S.r.hom := by
    intro v
    show (∑ q : Fin n × Fin t, F (v ∘ finProdFinEquiv) q) ∈ _
    refine Subalgebra.sum_mem _ (fun q _ => ?_)
    exact Subalgebra.mul_mem _ (Subalgebra.algebraMap_mem _ _) (b q.1).2
  have hBφ : ∀ x ∈ weightZeroSubalgebra c.O S.r.hom, ∃ v, φ v = x := by
    intro x hx
    have hx' : (⟨x, hx⟩ : weightZeroSubalgebra c.O S.r.hom) ∈ Submodule.span c.O (Set.range b) := by
      rw [hb]
      trivial
    obtain ⟨a, ha⟩ := (Submodule.mem_span_range_iff_exists_fun c.O).mp hx'
    have hz : ∀ i, ∃ z : Fin t → ℤ_[l], ∑ j, z j • o j = a i := by
      intro i
      have hai : a i ∈ Submodule.span ℤ_[l] (Set.range o) := by
        rw [ho]
        trivial
      exact (Submodule.mem_span_range_iff_exists_fun ℤ_[l]).mp hai
    choose z hz using hz
    have hx2 : ∑ i, a i • ((b i : weightZeroSubalgebra c.O S.r.hom) : S.R) = x := by
      have h := congrArg Subtype.val ha
      simpa using h
    refine ⟨(fun q : Fin n × Fin t => z q.1 q.2) ∘ finProdFinEquiv.symm, ?_⟩
    show ∑ q : Fin n × Fin t,
      F (((fun q : Fin n × Fin t => z q.1 q.2) ∘ finProdFinEquiv.symm) ∘ finProdFinEquiv) q = x
    have hcomp : ((fun q : Fin n × Fin t => z q.1 q.2) ∘ finProdFinEquiv.symm) ∘ finProdFinEquiv =
        fun q => z q.1 q.2 := by
      funext q
      simp
    rw [hcomp, Fintype.sum_prod_type, ← hx2]
    refine Finset.sum_congr rfl (fun i _ => ?_)
    simp only [F]
    rw [← Finset.sum_mul, ← map_sum, Algebra.smul_def, ← hz i]
    congr 2
    refine Finset.sum_congr rfl (fun j _ => ?_)
    rw [Algebra.smul_def]
  have key : ∀ f ∈ M.𝒜 0, M.q f ∈ weightZeroSubalgebra c.O S.r.hom := by
    intro f hf
    obtain ⟨v, hv⟩ := mem_range_of_approx (maximalIdeal S.R) hlm φ (M.q f) (fun m => by
      obtain ⟨y, hy, hdiff⟩ := M.dense 0 f hf m
      have hle : Submodule.span c.O (entMon S.r.hom 0) ≤ Subalgebra.toSubmodule (weightZeroSubalgebra c.O S.r.hom) :=
        Submodule.span_le.mpr (fun z hz => Algebra.subset_adjoin hz)
      obtain ⟨v, hv⟩ := hBφ y (hle hy)
      exact ⟨v, by rw [hv]; exact hdiff⟩)
    rw [← hv]
    exact hφB v
  have hsub : (M.𝒜 0).map M.q.toLinearMap ≤ Subalgebra.toSubmodule (weightZeroSubalgebra c.O S.r.hom) := by
    rintro _ ⟨f, hf, rfl⟩
    exact key f hf
  haveI : Module.Finite c.O (Subalgebra.toSubmodule (weightZeroSubalgebra c.O S.r.hom)) := hfin
  haveI hpir : IsPrincipalIdealRing c.O := c.dvr.toIsPrincipalIdealRing
  haveI hnr : IsNoetherianRing c.O := PrincipalIdealRing.isNoetherianRing
  haveI : IsNoetherian c.O (Subalgebra.toSubmodule (weightZeroSubalgebra c.O S.r.hom)) :=
    isNoetherian_of_isNoetherianRing_of_finite _ _
  have hfg : ((M.𝒜 0).map M.q.toLinearMap).FG := isNoetherian_submodule.mp inferInstance _ hsub
  show (Submodule.map (Ideal.Quotient.mkₐ c.O M.Q.ideal).toLinearMap (M.𝒜 0)).FG
  rw [← M.ker_q]
  refine Submodule.fg_of_fg_map_injective (Ideal.kerLiftAlg M.q).toLinearMap
    (Ideal.kerLiftAlg_injective M.q) ?_
  rw [← Submodule.map_comp]
  convert hfg using 2
  ext a
  simp [Ideal.kerLiftAlg_mk]


[OCH_SRC_MODEL_POINTS] Actual torus rescaling, convergent evaluation and the all-points field
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/UnitaryThreeGroups/ExistsTorusTwistEqLocalPointOfWeightedPres.lean#L1-L137
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.UnitaryThreeGroups.MopBase

open Polynomial in
private theorem pts_int {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] (x : PadicAlgCl l) : IsIntegral ℤ_[l] x ↔ ‖x‖ ≤ 1 := by
  have halg : IsIntegral ℚ_[l] x := (Algebra.IsAlgebraic.isAlgebraic (R := ℚ_[l]) x).isIntegral
  have hmon : (minpoly ℚ_[l] x).Monic := minpoly.monic halg
  have hn : ‖x‖ = spectralValue (minpoly ℚ_[l] x) := rfl
  rw [hn, spectralValue_le_one_iff hmon]
  constructor
  · intro hx n
    rw [minpoly.isIntegrallyClosed_eq_field_fractions' ℚ_[l] hx, Polynomial.coeff_map]
    exact PadicInt.norm_le_one ((minpoly ℤ_[l] x).coeff n)
  · intro h
    have hl : minpoly ℚ_[l] x ∈ Polynomial.lifts (algebraMap ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l]) := by
      rw [Polynomial.lifts_iff_coeff_lifts]
      intro n
      exact ⟨⟨_, h n⟩, rfl⟩
    obtain ⟨q, hq, -, hqm⟩ := Polynomial.lifts_and_natDegree_eq_and_monic hl hmon
    refine ⟨q, hqm, ?_⟩
    rw [IsScalarTower.algebraMap_eq ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] (PadicAlgCl l), ← Polynomial.eval₂_map, hq,
      ← Polynomial.aeval_def]
    exact minpoly.aeval ℚ_[l] x

private theorem pts_unit {B K : Type} [CommRing B] [Field K] [Algebra B K] (hi : Function.Injective (algebraMap B K))
    (b : B) (h0 : algebraMap B K b ≠ 0) (h : IsIntegral B (algebraMap B K b)⁻¹) : IsUnit b := by
  classical
  obtain ⟨p, hpm, hp⟩ := h
  set y := (algebraMap B K b)⁻¹ with hy
  have hy0 : y ≠ 0 := inv_ne_zero h0
  letI : Invertible y := invertibleOfNonzero hy0
  have hrev := Polynomial.eval₂_reverse_mul_pow (algebraMap B K) y p
  rw [hp, invOf_eq_inv, hy, inv_inv] at hrev
  have hq : Polynomial.eval₂ (algebraMap B K) (algebraMap B K b) p.reverse = 0 :=
    (mul_eq_zero.mp hrev).resolve_right (pow_ne_zero _ (inv_ne_zero h0))
  rw [Polynomial.eval₂_at_apply] at hq
  have hq' : Polynomial.eval b p.reverse = 0 := hi (by rw [hq, map_zero])
  obtain ⟨r, hr⟩ := Polynomial.X_dvd_sub_C (p := p.reverse)
  have he := congrArg (Polynomial.eval b) hr
  rw [Polynomial.eval_sub, Polynomial.eval_C, Polynomial.coeff_zero_reverse, hpm.leadingCoeff, hq',
    Polynomial.eval_mul, Polynomial.eval_X] at he
  have h1 : b * -Polynomial.eval b r = 1 := by
    rw [mul_neg, ← he]
    ring
  exact ⟨⟨b, _, h1, (mul_comm _ _).trans h1⟩, rfl⟩

private theorem automorphic_points_exist {O R : Type} [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O]
    [IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal O) O] [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] [IsCO O R] (W : WPres O ℤ R) (h : W.HasOrigin)
    (s : W.σ) : W.z s ∈ maximalIdeal R := mem_max_of_zero (W.origin h) (origin_presentation_var_eq_zero W h s)

private theorem pts_sur {O R K : Type} [CommRing O] [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] [IsLocalRing R] [Field K] [Algebra O K]
    (f : R →ₐ[O] K) (x : R) (h0 : f x ≠ 0) (h3 : IsIntegral O (f x)⁻¹) : IsUnit x := by
  classical
  have hu : IsUnit (⟨f x, AlgHom.mem_range_self f x⟩ : f.range) :=
    pts_unit (B := f.range) (K := K) (fun a b hab => Subtype.ext hab) _ h0 h3.tower_top
  obtain ⟨v, hv⟩ := hu.exists_right_inv
  obtain ⟨r, hr⟩ := (AlgHom.mem_range f).1 v.2
  have hfr : f x * f r = 1 := by
    rw [hr]
    exact congrArg Subtype.val hv
  by_contra hxu
  have hm : x * r ∈ maximalIdeal R := Ideal.mul_mem_right r _ hxu
  have hunit := (IsLocalRing.isUnit_one_sub_self_of_mem_nonunits (x * r) hm).map f
  rw [map_sub, map_one, map_mul, hfr, sub_self] at hunit
  exact not_isUnit_zero hunit

private theorem pts_loc {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {c : Coef l} {R : Type} [CommRing R] [Algebra c.O R] [IsLocalRing R]
    (f : R →ₐ[c.O] PadicAlgCl l) : IsLocPt f := by
  intro x hx
  by_contra hge
  push_neg at hge
  have h0 : f x ≠ 0 := by
    intro hz
    rw [hz, norm_zero] at hge
    linarith
  have hint : IsIntegral ℤ_[l] (f x)⁻¹ :=
    (pts_int _).mpr (by rw [norm_inv]; exact inv_le_one_of_one_le₀ hge)
  exact hx (pts_sur f x h0 hint.tower_top)

private theorem fg_lemma {O A : Type} [CommRing O] [CommRing A] [Algebra O A] {K : Type} [Field K]
    [Algebra O K] (P : Submodule O A) (I : Ideal A) (ξ : A →ₐ[O] K) (hξ : ∀ a ∈ I, ξ a = 0)
    (h : (Submodule.map (Ideal.Quotient.mkₐ O I).toLinearMap P).FG) :
    (Submodule.map ξ.toLinearMap P).FG := by
  have he : ξ.toLinearMap = (Ideal.Quotient.liftₐ I ξ hξ).toLinearMap ∘ₗ
      (Ideal.Quotient.mkₐ O I).toLinearMap := by
    ext a
    rfl
  rw [he, Submodule.map_comp]
  exact h.map _

theorem UpperTheory.exists_torus_twist_eq_localPoint_of_weightedPres :
    ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {c : Coef l} {R S : Type} [CommRing R] [Algebra c.O R] [IsCO c.O R] [CommRing S] [Algebra c.O S] [IsCO c.O S]
          (W : WPres c.O ℤ R) (h : W.HasOrigin) (qh : R →ₐ[c.O] S), Function.Surjective qh → ∀ {κ : Type} (g : κ → W.A) (w : κ → ℤ), (∀ i, g i ∈ W.gr (w i)) →
          RingHom.ker qh = Ideal.span (Set.range fun i => (g i : R)) → M2 W.gr (Ideal.span (Set.range g)) →
          ∀ ξ : W.A →ₐ[c.O] PadicAlgCl l, OnS (Ideal.span (Set.range g)) ξ →
            ∃ (t : (PadicAlgCl l)ˣ) (f : S →ₐ[c.O] PadicAlgCl l), IsLocPt f ∧ act W.gr (tz t) ξ = f.comp (qh.comp W.A.val) := by
  intro l _ c R S _ _ _ _ _ _ W h qh hqh κ g w hg hk hM ξ hOn
  have hfg : (Submodule.map ξ.toLinearMap (W.gr 0)).FG := fg_lemma (W.gr 0) _ ξ hOn hM
  haveI : IsLocalRing c.O := IsDiscreteValuationRing.toIsLocalRing
  haveI : Module.Finite ℤ_[l] c.O := c.fin
  obtain ⟨hcomp, -, -, hinj, -, -⟩ := UpperTheory.V.abelian₂.dvr_finite l c.O
  haveI := hcomp
  haveI : Algebra.IsAlgebraic c.O (PadicAlgCl l) := Algebra.IsAlgebraic.mk fun x =>
    ((IsFractionRing.isAlgebraic_iff ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] (PadicAlgCl l)).mpr
      (Algebra.IsAlgebraic.isAlgebraic x)).extendScalars hinj
  obtain ⟨t, Ξ, hΞ, -⟩ := points_finite c.O ℤ R W (automorphic_points_exist W h) (PadicAlgCl l) ξ hfg
  have hker : ∀ a ∈ RingHom.ker qh, Ξ a = 0 := by
    intro a ha
    rw [hk] at ha
    have hle : Ideal.span (Set.range fun i => (g i : R)) ≤ RingHom.ker Ξ := by
      refine Ideal.span_le.mpr ?_
      rintro _ ⟨i, rfl⟩
      rw [SetLike.mem_coe, RingHom.mem_ker]
      have h1 := congrArg (fun φ : W.A →ₐ[c.O] PadicAlgCl l => φ (g i)) hΞ
      simp only [AlgHom.comp_apply, Subalgebra.coe_val] at h1
      rw [h1, act_of_mem W.gr t ξ (hg i), hOn (g i) (Ideal.subset_span ⟨i, rfl⟩), mul_zero]
    exact RingHom.mem_ker.mp (hle ha)
  let f : S →ₐ[c.O] PadicAlgCl l := (Ideal.Quotient.liftₐ (RingHom.ker qh) Ξ hker).comp
    (Ideal.quotientKerAlgEquivOfSurjective hqh).symm.toAlgHom
  have hf : ∀ r, f (qh r) = Ξ r := by
    intro r
    have hmk : (Ideal.quotientKerAlgEquivOfSurjective hqh).symm (qh r) =
        Ideal.Quotient.mk (RingHom.ker qh) r := by
      rw [AlgEquiv.symm_apply_eq]
      rfl
    show Ideal.Quotient.liftₐ (RingHom.ker qh) Ξ hker
      ((Ideal.quotientKerAlgEquivOfSurjective hqh).symm (qh r)) = Ξ r
    rw [hmk]
    rfl
  refine ⟨(zpowersHom (PadicAlgCl l)ˣ).symm t, f, pts_loc f, ?_⟩
  rw [show tz ((zpowersHom (PadicAlgCl l)ˣ).symm t) = t from Equiv.apply_symm_apply _ _, ← hΞ]
  refine AlgHom.ext fun a => ?_
  simp only [AlgHom.comp_apply, hf]


[OCH_SRC_MODEL_DERIVATIONS] Actual all-derivations extension through the complete normalized ring
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/UnitaryThreeGroups/ExistsDualNumberPointOfPointDerivationVanishing.lean#L1-L225
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.UnitaryThreeGroups.MopBase

section SlopeTestRemainingChoices

open Polynomial in
private theorem md_intIff {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] (x : PadicAlgCl l) : IsIntegral ℤ_[l] x ↔ ‖x‖ ≤ 1 := by
  have hi : IsIntegral ℚ_[l] x := Algebra.IsIntegral.isIntegral x
  have h0 : ‖x‖ = spectralValue (minpoly ℚ_[l] x) := rfl
  rw [h0, spectralValue_le_one_iff (minpoly.monic hi)]
  constructor
  · intro h n
    rw [minpoly.isIntegrallyClosed_eq_field_fractions' ℚ_[l] h, coeff_map]
    exact PadicInt.norm_le_one _
  · intro h
    have hl' : minpoly ℚ_[l] x ∈ lifts (algebraMap ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l]) :=
      (lifts_iff_coeff_lifts _).mpr fun n => ⟨⟨_, h n⟩, rfl⟩
    obtain ⟨q, hq, -, hqm⟩ := lifts_and_degree_eq_and_monic hl' (minpoly.monic hi)
    exact ⟨q, hqm, by rw [← aeval_def, ← aeval_map_algebraMap ℚ_[l], hq, minpoly.aeval]⟩

private theorem md_ext {O X R K : Type} [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O]
    [IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal O) O] [AddCommGroup X] [Module.Free ℤ X] [Module.Finite ℤ X] [DecidableEq X]
    [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] (W : WPres O X R) [NormedField K] [Algebra O K]
    (hinj : Function.Injective (algebraMap O K)) (hw : ∀ x : K, ‖x‖ ≤ 1 → IsIntegral O x)
    (ξ : W.A →ₐ[O] DualNumber K) (h1 : ∀ x, ‖(ξ x).fst‖ ≤ 1) (hz : ∀ s, ‖(ξ (W.zA s)).fst‖ < 1)
    (hfg : ((W.gr 0).map ξ.toLinearMap).FG) :
    ∃ Ξ : R →ₐ[O] DualNumber K, (∀ x : W.A, Ξ x = ξ x) ∧ ∀ x, IsIntegral O (Ξ x) := by
  have hw' : ∀ x : DualNumber K, ‖x.fst‖ ≤ 1 → IsIntegral O x := fun x hx => by
    rw [← TrivSqZeroExt.inl_fst_add_inr_snd_eq x]
    refine IsIntegral.add ((hw _ hx).map (TrivSqZeroExt.inlAlgHom O K K)) ?_
    exact ⟨Polynomial.X ^ 2, Polynomial.monic_X_pow 2, by simp [pow_two]⟩
  obtain ⟨p, hp⟩ := IsDiscreteValuationRing.exists_irreducible O
  have hp0 : algebraMap O K p ≠ 0 := (map_ne_zero_iff _ hinj).2 hp.ne_zero
  let y : W.σ → DualNumber K := fun s => ξ (W.zA s)
  have hc : ∀ s, ∃ n : ℕ, IsIntegral O ((algebraMap O K p)⁻¹ • y s ^ n) := fun s => by
    obtain ⟨n, hn⟩ := exists_pow_lt_of_lt_one (norm_pos_iff.2 hp0) (hz s)
    refine ⟨n, hw' _ ?_⟩
    rw [TrivSqZeroExt.fst_smul, TrivSqZeroExt.fst_pow, smul_eq_mul, norm_mul, norm_inv, norm_pow, ← div_eq_inv_mul]
    exact div_le_one_of_le₀ hn.le (norm_nonneg _)
  choose e he using hc
  obtain ⟨T, hT⟩ := id hfg
  let G : Set (DualNumber K) := (T : Set _) ∪ Set.range y ∪ Set.range fun s => (algebraMap O K p)⁻¹ • y s ^ e s
  have hG : G.Finite := (T.finite_toSet.union (Set.finite_range _)).union (Set.finite_range _)
  have hGi : ∀ x ∈ G, IsIntegral O x := by
    rintro x ((hx | ⟨s, rfl⟩) | ⟨s, rfl⟩)
    · have : x ∈ (W.gr 0).map ξ.toLinearMap := hT ▸ Submodule.subset_span hx
      obtain ⟨a, -, rfl⟩ := this
      exact hw' _ (h1 a)
    · exact hw' _ (hz s).le
    · exact he s
  let B : Subalgebra O (DualNumber K) := Algebra.adjoin O G
  haveI : Module.Finite O B := Algebra.finite_adjoin_of_finite_of_isIntegral hG hGi
  haveI : IsNoetherianRing B := IsNoetherianRing.of_finite O B
  haveI := GlobalTheory.isAdicComplete_map_of_finite (maximalIdeal O) B
  let J : Ideal B := ((maximalIdeal O).map (algebraMap O B)).radical
  obtain ⟨N, hN⟩ := Ideal.exists_radical_pow_le_of_fg ((maximalIdeal O).map (algebraMap O B))
    (IsNoetherian.noetherian _)
  haveI : IsAdicComplete J B :=
    { toIsHausdorff := GlobalTheory.NilpotentEval.isHausdorff_of_pow_le hN
      toIsPrecomplete := GlobalTheory.NilpotentEval.isPrecomplete_of_pow_le hN Ideal.le_radical }
  have hB : ∀ x, ξ x ∈ B := fun x => by
    have h2 : (⊤ : Subalgebra O W.A) ≤ B.comap ξ := by
      rw [← gr_gen O R X W]
      refine Algebra.adjoin_le ?_
      rintro a (ha | ⟨s, rfl⟩)
      · have h1 : ξ a ∈ Submodule.span O (T : Set (DualNumber K)) := hT ▸ Submodule.mem_map_of_mem ha
        have h2 : Submodule.span O (T : Set (DualNumber K)) ≤ B.toSubmodule :=
          Submodule.span_le.2 fun a ha => Algebra.subset_adjoin (Or.inl (Or.inl ha))
        exact h2 h1
      · exact Algebra.subset_adjoin (Or.inl (Or.inr ⟨s, rfl⟩))
    exact h2 trivial
  have hyB : ∀ s, ξ.codRestrict B hB (W.zA s) ∈ J := fun s => by
    refine ⟨e s, ?_⟩
    have : ξ.codRestrict B hB (W.zA s) ^ e s = algebraMap O B p *
        ⟨(algebraMap O K p)⁻¹ • y s ^ e s, Algebra.subset_adjoin (Or.inr ⟨s, rfl⟩)⟩ := by
      apply Subtype.ext
      show y s ^ e s = algebraMap O (DualNumber K) p * ((algebraMap O K p)⁻¹ • y s ^ e s)
      rw [← Algebra.smul_def, ← algebraMap_smul K p, smul_inv_smul₀ hp0]
    rw [this]
    exact Ideal.mul_mem_right _ _ (Ideal.mem_map_of_mem _ hp.not_isUnit)
  obtain ⟨f, hf⟩ := UpperTheory.weightedPres_exists_algHom_extension_of_adicComplete W J (ξ.codRestrict B hB) hyB
  refine ⟨B.val.comp f, fun x => congrArg Subtype.val (DFunLike.congr_fun hf x), fun x => ?_⟩
  exact (Algebra.IsIntegral.isIntegral (R := O) (f x)).map B.val

private theorem md_uniq {O σ K : Type} [CommRing O] [Fintype σ] [NormedField K] [IsUltrametricDist K] [Algebra O K]
    (φ ψ : MvPowerSeries σ O →ₐ[O] K) (h1 : ∀ F, ‖φ F‖ ≤ 1) (h2 : ∀ F, ‖ψ F‖ ≤ 1)
    (hz : ∀ s, ‖φ (MvPowerSeries.X s)‖ < 1) (h : ∀ s, φ (MvPowerSeries.X s) = ψ (MvPowerSeries.X s)) : φ = ψ := by
  let V : Subalgebra O K :=
    { carrier := {x | ‖x‖ ≤ 1}
      mul_mem' := fun {a b} ha hb => by
        show ‖a * b‖ ≤ 1
        rw [norm_mul]
        exact mul_le_one₀ ha (norm_nonneg _) hb
      add_mem' := fun {a b} ha hb => (IsUltrametricDist.norm_add_le_max _ _).trans (max_le ha hb)
      algebraMap_mem' := fun a => by
        have := h1 (algebraMap O _ a)
        rwa [AlgHom.commutes] at this }
  let ρ : NNReal := Finset.univ.sup fun s => ‖φ (MvPowerSeries.X s)‖₊
  have hρ : ρ < 1 := (Finset.sup_lt_iff (by norm_num)).2 fun s _ => hz s
  let J : Ideal V :=
    { carrier := {x | ‖(x : K)‖ ≤ ρ}
      add_mem' := fun {a b} ha hb => (IsUltrametricDist.norm_add_le_max _ _).trans (max_le ha hb)
      zero_mem' := by simp
      smul_mem' := fun a b hb => by
        show ‖(a : K) * b‖ ≤ ρ
        rw [norm_mul]
        exact (mul_le_of_le_one_left (norm_nonneg _) a.2).trans hb }
  have key : ∀ n : ℕ, ∀ z ∈ J ^ n, ‖(z : K)‖ ≤ (ρ : ℝ) ^ n := by
    intro n
    induction n with
    | zero => intro z _; rw [pow_zero]; exact z.2
    | succ n ih =>
      intro z hz
      rw [pow_succ] at hz
      refine Submodule.mul_induction_on hz (fun a ha b hb => ?_) (fun a b ha hb => ?_)
      · rw [Subalgebra.coe_mul, norm_mul, pow_succ]
        exact mul_le_mul (ih a ha) hb (norm_nonneg _) (pow_nonneg ρ.2 _)
      · rw [Subalgebra.coe_add]
        exact (IsUltrametricDist.norm_add_le_max _ _).trans (max_le ha hb)
  have hJ : ∀ s, φ.codRestrict V h1 (MvPowerSeries.X s) ∈ J := fun s =>
    Finset.le_sup (f := fun s => ‖φ (MvPowerSeries.X s)‖₊) (Finset.mem_univ s)
  have he : ∀ s, φ.codRestrict V h1 (MvPowerSeries.X s) = ψ.codRestrict V h2 (MvPowerSeries.X s) :=
    fun s => Subtype.ext (h s)
  refine AlgHom.ext fun F => ?_
  rw [← sub_eq_zero, ← norm_le_zero_iff]
  refine ge_of_tendsto' (tendsto_pow_atTop_nhds_zero_of_lt_one ρ.2 hρ) fun n => ?_
  exact key n _ (GlobalTheory.algHom_sub_mem_pow (φ.codRestrict V h1) (ψ.codRestrict V h2) hJ (fun s => he s ▸ hJ s)
    (fun s => by rw [he s, sub_self]; exact zero_mem _) F)

private theorem md_main {O R S K : Type} [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O]
    [IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal O) O] [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] [IsCO O R] [CommRing S] [Algebra O S] [IsCO O S]
    [NormedField K] [IsUltrametricDist K] [Algebra O K] (hinj : Function.Injective (algebraMap O K))
    (hw : ∀ x : K, IsIntegral O x ↔ ‖x‖ ≤ 1)
    (W : WPres O ℤ R) (h : W.HasOrigin) (qh : R →ₐ[O] S) (hq : Function.Surjective qh) {κ : Type} (g : κ → W.A)
    (hker : RingHom.ker qh = Ideal.span (Set.range fun i => (g i : R))) (hM : M2 W.gr (Ideal.span (Set.range g)))
    (f : S →ₐ[O] K) (hf : IsLocPt f) (D : W.A →ₗ[O] K) (hD : D ∈ PtDer (f.comp (qh.comp W.A.val)) K)
    (hDI : ∀ y ∈ Ideal.span (Set.range g), D y = 0) :
    ∃ F : S →ₐ[O] DualNumber K, dualFst F = f ∧ ∀ y : W.A, (F (qh y)).snd = D y := by
  have hO : ∀ a : O, ‖algebraMap O K a‖ ≤ 1 := fun a => (hw _).1 isIntegral_algebraMap
  have hf1 : ∀ x, ‖f x‖ ≤ 1 := fun x => by
    obtain ⟨a, ha⟩ := IsCO.res_surj (O := O) x
    have := hf _ ha
    rw [map_sub, AlgHom.commutes] at this
    rw [← sub_add_cancel (f x) (algebraMap O _ a)]
    exact (IsUltrametricDist.norm_add_le_max _ _).trans (max_le this.le (hO a))
  have hz : ∀ s, ‖f (qh (W.z s))‖ < 1 := fun s => hf _ (by
    rw [← res_eq_zero_iff O, res_map, res_eq_zero_iff]
    exact mem_max_of_zero (W.origin h) (origin_presentation_var_eq_zero W h s))
  letI : Module Oᵐᵒᵖ K := Module.compHom K ((RingHom.id O).fromOpposite mul_comm)
  haveI : IsCentralScalar O K := ⟨fun _ _ => rfl⟩
  obtain ⟨τ, hτ⟩ := (der_hom O W.A K K (f.comp (qh.comp W.A.val)) D).1 ⟨D, hD, rfl⟩
  have hg0 : ∀ i, τ (g i) = 0 := fun i => by
    have h1 : qh (g i) = 0 := by
      rw [← RingHom.mem_ker, hker]
      exact Ideal.subset_span ⟨i, rfl⟩
    ext
    · rw [(hτ _).1]
      show f (qh (g i)) = 0
      rw [h1, map_zero]
    · rw [(hτ _).2]
      exact hDI _ (Ideal.subset_span ⟨i, rfl⟩)
  have hτI : ∀ y ∈ Ideal.span (Set.range g), τ y = 0 := by
    have : Ideal.span (Set.range g) ≤ RingHom.ker τ := Ideal.span_le.2 (by
      rintro _ ⟨i, rfl⟩
      exact hg0 i)
    exact this
  have hfg : ((W.gr 0).map τ.toLinearMap).FG := by
    have := Submodule.FG.map (Ideal.Quotient.liftₐ _ τ hτI).toLinearMap hM
    rw [← Submodule.map_comp] at this
    exact this
  obtain ⟨Ξ, hΞ, hint⟩ := md_ext W (K := K) hinj (fun x => (hw x).2) τ (fun x => by rw [(hτ x).1]; exact hf1 _)
    (fun s => by rw [(hτ _).1]; exact hz s) hfg
  have hfst : (dualFst Ξ).comp W.π = (f.comp qh).comp W.π := by
    have e : ∀ s, (Ξ (W.z s)).fst = f (qh (W.z s)) := fun s => by
      have := hΞ (W.zA s)
      rw [show W.z s = (W.zA s : R) from rfl, this, (hτ _).1]
      rfl
    refine md_uniq _ _ (fun F => ?_) (fun F => hf1 _) (fun s => ?_) e
    · exact (hw _).1 ((hint (W.π F)).map (TrivSqZeroExt.fstHom O K K))
    · exact (congrArg norm (e s)).trans_lt (hz s)
  have hK : ∀ x ∈ RingHom.ker qh, Ξ x = 0 := by
    have : RingHom.ker qh ≤ RingHom.ker Ξ := by
      rw [hker]
      refine Ideal.span_le.2 ?_
      rintro _ ⟨i, rfl⟩
      exact (hΞ _).trans (hg0 i)
    exact this
  let F : S →ₐ[O] DualNumber K := (Ideal.Quotient.liftₐ (RingHom.ker qh) Ξ hK).comp
    (Ideal.quotientKerAlgEquivOfSurjective hq).symm.toAlgHom
  have hF : ∀ x, F (qh x) = Ξ x := by
    intro x
    have : (Ideal.quotientKerAlgEquivOfSurjective hq).symm (qh x) = Ideal.Quotient.mk _ x := by
      rw [AlgEquiv.symm_apply_eq]; rfl
    simp only [F, AlgHom.comp_apply, AlgEquiv.coe_algHom]
    erw [this]
    rfl
  refine ⟨F, AlgHom.ext fun x => ?_, fun y => by rw [hF, hΞ, (hτ y).2]⟩
  obtain ⟨x, rfl⟩ := hq x
  obtain ⟨G, rfl⟩ := W.surj x
  show (F (qh (W.π G))).fst = _
  rw [hF]
  exact DFunLike.congr_fun hfst G

end SlopeTestRemainingChoices

theorem UpperTheory.exists_dualNumber_point_of_pointDerivation_vanishing :
    ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {c : Coef l} {R S : Type} [CommRing R] [Algebra c.O R] [IsCO c.O R] [CommRing S] [Algebra c.O S] [IsCO c.O S]
          (W : WPres c.O ℤ R) (h : W.HasOrigin) (qh : R →ₐ[c.O] S), Function.Surjective qh → ∀ {κ : Type} (g : κ → W.A) (w : κ → ℤ), (∀ i, g i ∈ W.gr (w i)) →
          RingHom.ker qh = Ideal.span (Set.range fun i => (g i : R)) → M2 W.gr (Ideal.span (Set.range g)) →
          ∀ f : S →ₐ[c.O] PadicAlgCl l, IsLocPt f → ∀ D ∈ PtDer (f.comp (qh.comp W.A.val)) (PadicAlgCl l), (∀ y ∈ Ideal.span (Set.range g), D y = 0) →
            ∃ F : S →ₐ[c.O] DualNumber (PadicAlgCl l), dualFst F = f ∧ ∀ y : W.A, (F (qh y)).snd = D y := by
  intro l _ c R S _ _ _ _ _ _ W h qh hq κ g w _ hker hM f hf D hD hDI
  haveI := c.dvr
  haveI := c.fin
  haveI : IsLocalRing c.O := c.dvr.toIsLocalRing
  haveI : IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal c.O) c.O := (UpperTheory.V.Base.dvrFacts l c.O).2.2.2.1
  haveI : Algebra.IsIntegral ℤ_[l] c.O := Algebra.IsIntegral.of_finite _ _
  exact md_main Subtype.val_injective (fun x => ⟨fun hx => (md_intIff x).1 (isIntegral_trans (A := c.O) x hx),
    fun hx => ((md_intIff x).2 hx).tower_top⟩) W h qh hq g hker hM f hf D hD hDI


[OCH_SRC_ACTUAL_POINTS] All literal IsPt fields of a strict local point of the normalized ring
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/UnitaryThreeGroups/IsPtOf.lean#L1-L166
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.UnitaryThreeGroups.KatoUse

open NumberField IsDedekindDomain Formalism.ConjugateSelfDual ReciprocityTheory.Gal Formalism.MainNotions.N Formalism.BasicNotions Formalism.BasicNotions.Loc GlobalTheory.GalOfCusp Matrix IsLocalRing

section SlopeTestRemainingChoices

variable {O R K : Type} [CommRing O] [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] [IsLocalRing R] [IsNoetherianRing R]
  [NormedField K] [IsUltrametricDist K] [Algebra O K]

omit [IsNoetherianRing R] [IsUltrametricDist K] in
private theorem ipo_mul {f : R →ₐ[O] K} (hf : IsLocPt f) (x : R) {z : R} (hz : z ∈ maximalIdeal R) :
    ‖f (x * z)‖ ≤ ‖f z‖ := by
  rw [map_mul, norm_mul]
  rcases eq_or_ne (f z) 0 with h0 | h0
  · simp [h0]
  have hpos : 0 < ‖f z‖ := norm_pos_iff.mpr h0
  suffices hx : ‖f x‖ ≤ 1 by nlinarith [norm_nonneg (f x)]
  by_contra hx
  push_neg at hx
  obtain ⟨n, hn⟩ := pow_unbounded_of_one_lt (‖f z‖⁻¹) hx
  have hmem : x ^ n * z ∈ maximalIdeal R := Ideal.mul_mem_left _ _ hz
  have h1 := hf _ hmem
  rw [map_mul, map_pow, norm_mul, norm_pow] at h1
  have := (inv_lt_iff_one_lt_mul₀ hpos).mp hn
  linarith [mul_comm (‖f x‖ ^ n) ‖f z‖]

private theorem ipo_rho {f : R →ₐ[O] K} (hf : IsLocPt f) :
    ∃ ρ : ℝ, 0 ≤ ρ ∧ ρ < 1 ∧ ∀ z ∈ maximalIdeal R, ‖f z‖ ≤ ρ := by
  obtain ⟨s, hs⟩ := (IsNoetherian.noetherian (maximalIdeal R) : (maximalIdeal R).FG)
  refine ⟨((s.sup fun g => ‖f g‖₊ : NNReal) : ℝ), NNReal.coe_nonneg _, ?_, ?_⟩
  · have : (s.sup fun g => ‖f g‖₊) < 1 := by
      rw [Finset.sup_lt_iff (by simp)]
      intro g hg
      have hgm : g ∈ maximalIdeal R := hs ▸ Ideal.subset_span hg
      have := hf g hgm
      exact_mod_cast this
    exact_mod_cast this
  · intro z hz
    rw [← hs] at hz
    induction hz using Submodule.span_induction with
    | mem x hx =>
      have : ‖f x‖₊ ≤ s.sup fun g => ‖f g‖₊ := Finset.le_sup (f := fun g => ‖f g‖₊) hx
      exact_mod_cast this
    | zero => simp
    | add x y hx hy ihx ihy =>
      rw [map_add]
      exact (IsUltrametricDist.norm_add_le_max _ _).trans (max_le ihx ihy)
    | smul a x hx ihx =>
      rw [smul_eq_mul]
      refine (ipo_mul hf a ?_).trans ihx
      exact hs ▸ hx

private theorem ipo_pow {f : R →ₐ[O] K} (hf : IsLocPt f) {ε : ℝ} (hε : 0 < ε) :
    ∃ n : ℕ, ∀ z ∈ maximalIdeal R ^ n, ‖f z‖ < ε := by
  obtain ⟨ρ, hρ0, hρ1, hρ⟩ := ipo_rho hf
  have key : ∀ n : ℕ, ∀ z ∈ maximalIdeal R ^ (n + 1), ‖f z‖ ≤ ρ ^ (n + 1) := by
    intro n
    induction n with
    | zero =>
      intro z hz
      rw [zero_add, pow_one] at hz ⊢
      exact hρ z hz
    | succ n ih =>
      intro z hz
      rw [pow_succ] at hz
      refine Submodule.mul_induction_on hz ?_ ?_
      · intro a ha b hb
        rw [map_mul, norm_mul, pow_succ]
        exact mul_le_mul (ih a ha) (hρ b hb) (norm_nonneg _) (pow_nonneg hρ0 _)
      · intro x y hx hy
        rw [map_add]
        exact (IsUltrametricDist.norm_add_le_max _ _).trans (max_le hx hy)
  obtain ⟨n, hn⟩ := exists_pow_lt_of_lt_one hε hρ1
  refine ⟨n + 1, fun z hz => (key n z hz).trans_lt ?_⟩
  calc ρ ^ (n + 1) ≤ ρ ^ n := pow_le_pow_of_le_one hρ0 hρ1.le (Nat.le_succ n)
    _ < ε := hn

private theorem ipo_cont {f : R →ₐ[O] K} (hf : IsLocPt f) {X : Type} [TopologicalSpace X] (u : X → R)
    (hu : AdicCont (maximalIdeal R) u) : Continuous fun g => f (u g) := by
  rw [continuous_iff_continuousAt]
  intro g
  rw [ContinuousAt, Metric.tendsto_nhds]
  intro ε hε
  obtain ⟨n, hn⟩ := ipo_pow hf hε
  have hopen := (hu n).isOpen_fiber (Ideal.Quotient.mk (maximalIdeal R ^ n) (u g))
  filter_upwards [hopen.mem_nhds rfl] with g' hg'
  have heq : Ideal.Quotient.mk (maximalIdeal R ^ n) (u g') = Ideal.Quotient.mk (maximalIdeal R ^ n) (u g) := hg'
  rw [Ideal.Quotient.eq] at heq
  rw [dist_eq_norm, ← map_sub]
  exact hn _ heq

end SlopeTestRemainingChoices

private theorem ipo_le_one {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {c : Coef l} {R : Type} [CommRing R] [Algebra c.O R] [IsCO c.O R]
    {f : R →ₐ[c.O] PadicAlgCl l} (hf : IsLocPt f) (x : R) : ‖f x‖ ≤ 1 := by
  haveI := c.dvr
  haveI : IsLocalHom (algebraMap c.O R) := IsCO.isLocalHom
  obtain ⟨ϖ, hϖ⟩ := IsDiscreteValuationRing.exists_irreducible c.O
  have hm : algebraMap c.O R ϖ ∈ maximalIdeal R := by
    rw [mem_maximalIdeal, mem_nonunits_iff]
    intro hu
    exact hϖ.not_isUnit ((isUnit_map_iff (algebraMap c.O R) ϖ).mp hu)
  have hne : f (algebraMap c.O R ϖ) ≠ 0 := by
    rw [AlgHom.commutes]
    intro h
    apply hϖ.ne_zero
    exact Subtype.ext (by simpa using h)
  have hpos : 0 < ‖f (algebraMap c.O R ϖ)‖ := norm_pos_iff.mpr hne
  have h := ipo_mul hf x hm
  rw [map_mul, norm_mul] at h
  nlinarith [norm_nonneg (f x)]

private theorem diagonal_rep_map_entries {G A B : Type} [Group G] [CommRing A] [CommRing B] (φ : A →+* B) (η : G →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : G →* Aˣ)
    (g : G) (a b : Fin 3) :
    (r0 η ((Units.map φ.toMonoidHom).comp Ψ) g).mat a b = φ ((r0 η Ψ g).mat a b) := by
  simp only [GG.mat, unitaryThreeReflection_left, tor_val, Matrix.diagonal_apply, MonoidHom.comp_apply]
  split_ifs
  · first
      | (rw [← map_zpow, Units.coe_map]; rfl)
      | (rw [← map_pow, Units.coe_map]; rfl)
  · simp

theorem UpperTheory.V.U3.isPt_push :
    ∀ {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] {K : Type} [Field K] [NumberField K] [Algebra K Ω]
      {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {c : Coef l} {N : ℕ} [NeZero N] {e : ℕ} {ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O N} (D : Dat Ω K c e ψ) {U : D.ULoc} {g₁ : G ℚ Ω}, D.Marked g₁ → ∀ (S : D.Ring U g₁),
      ∀ f : S.R →ₐ[c.O] PadicAlgCl l, IsLocPt f → D.IsPt U g₁ (push f S.r.hom) := by
  intro Ω _ _ _ K _ _ _ l _ c N _ e ψ D U g₁ _ S f hf
  have hid : S.r.map (AlgHom.id c.O S.R) = S.r := GLift.ext rfl
  have h0 := (S.rep S.R).1 (AlgHom.id c.O S.R)
  rw [hid] at h0
  obtain ⟨hn, hk, φ, hφ, hI⟩ := h0
  have hm : ∀ g a b, (push f S.r.hom g).mat a b = f ((S.r.hom g).mat a b) := fun _ _ _ => rfl
  refine ⟨fun g => S.r.hasMult g, ?_, fun g a b => ipo_le_one hf _, fun g a b => ?_, ⟨?_, ?_, ?_⟩, fun g hg => ?_,
    f.comp φ, fun x hx => hf _ ?_, fun x hx => ?_, ?_⟩
  · exact continuous_matrix fun a b => ipo_cont hf _ (S.r.cont a b)
  · have h1 : (r0 D.η D.ΨQ g).mat a b = algebraMap c.O (PadicAlgCl l) ((r0 D.η D.Ψ g).mat a b) :=
      diagonal_rep_map_entries _ _ _ _ _ _
    rw [hm, h1, ← f.commutes, ← map_sub]
    refine hf _ ((res_eq_zero_iff c.O S.R _).1 ?_)
    have h2 : res c.O S.R ((S.r.hom g).mat a b) = (rbar c.O D.η D.Ψ g).mat a b :=
      congrArg (fun x : GG 3 (ResidueField c.O) => x.mat a b) (S.r.red g)
    have h3 : (rbar c.O D.η D.Ψ g).mat a b = residue c.O ((r0 D.η D.Ψ g).mat a b) := diagonal_rep_map_entries _ _ _ _ _ _
    rw [map_sub, AlgHom.commutes, h2, h3]
    exact sub_self _
  · show (S.r.hom D.cc).mat.map (f : S.R →+* PadicAlgCl l) = 1
    rw [hn.atc, Matrix.map_one _ (map_zero _) (map_one _)]
  · have := congrArg f hn.z12
    rw [map_zero, zmat, Matrix.sub_apply, map_sub] at this
    exact this
  · have := congrArg f hn.z21
    rw [map_zero, zmat, Matrix.sub_apply, map_sub] at this
    exact this
  · show GG.map (f : S.R →+* PadicAlgCl l) (S.r.hom g) = 1
    rw [hk g hg, map_one]
  · show φ x ∈ maximalIdeal S.R
    rw [← res_eq_zero_iff c.O, res_map, res_eq_zero_iff]
    exact hx
  · have : φ x = 0 := hI hx
    show f (φ x) = 0
    rw [this, map_zero]
  · have h1 : (U.r.map φ).hom = S.r.hom.comp D.ι := congrArg GLift.hom hφ
    refine MonoidHom.ext fun g => ?_
    show GG.map (f : S.R →+* PadicAlgCl l) ((U.r.map φ).hom g) = _
    rw [h1]
    rfl


[OCH_SRC_POINT_SHAPE] Type-I/type-II alternatives and actual torus conjugacy from an intertwiner
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/UnitaryThreeGroups/PtShape.lean#L85-L171
theorem UpperTheory.V.U3.twoPt_shape :
    ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {c : Coef l} {Γ H : Type} [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace Γ] [IsTopologicalGroup Γ] [Group H] [TopologicalSpace H] [IsTopologicalGroup H]
      {η : Γ →* ℤˣ} {Ψ : Γ →* (c.O)ˣ} {N : Set Γ} {ι : H →* Γ} {hι : Continuous ι}
      {U : Univ (GCond.noCondition c.O (η.comp ι) (Ψ.comp ι))} {I : Ideal U.R} {cc g₁ : Γ} (S : SRing N hι U I cc g₁), l ≠ 2 → Marks η Ψ cc g₁ →
      ∀ f : S.R →ₐ[c.O] PadicAlgCl l, TwoPt η S.r.hom f →
        (TypeI η (push f S.r.hom) ∨ TypeII η (push f S.r.hom)) ∧
        ∀ f' : S.R →ₐ[c.O] PadicAlgCl l, IsLocPt f' → MatIso η.ker (mats (push f S.r.hom)) (mats (push f' S.r.hom)) →
          ∃ t : (PadicAlgCl l)ˣ, push f' S.r.hom = conjBy (tor _ t) (push f S.r.hom) := by
  intro l _ c Γ H _ _ _ _ _ _ η Ψ N ι hι U I cc g₁ S hl2 hmk f htwo
  have hid : (GLift.map (AlgHom.id c.O S.R) S.r).hom = S.r.hom := by
    ext g : 1
    refine SemidirectProduct.ext ?_ ?_
    · ext i j
      simp [GLift.map, GG.map]
    · simp [GLift.map, GG.map]
  have hN : Normalised cc g₁ S.r.hom := by
    have h := ((S.rep S.R).1 (AlgHom.id c.O S.R)).1
    rwa [hid] at h
  have hmult : ∀ g : S.R →ₐ[c.O] PadicAlgCl l, HasMult η (push g S.r.hom) := by
    intro g x
    simpa [push, GG.map] using S.r.hasMult x
  have hnorm_g : ∀ g : S.R →ₐ[c.O] PadicAlgCl l, Normalised cc g₁ (push g S.r.hom) :=
    fun g => normalised_map (g : S.R →+* PadicAlgCl l) hN
  have hzeta_g : ∀ g : S.R →ₐ[c.O] PadicAlgCl l,
      zeta cc g₁ (push g S.r.hom) = g (zeta cc g₁ S.r.hom) :=
    fun g => zmat_map (g : S.R →+* PadicAlgCl l) cc g₁ S.r.hom 0 0
  obtain ⟨_, hz0u⟩ := zeta_reducible_rep_eq_sub_inv_and_isUnit η Ψ cc g₁ hmk
  have hmem := zeta_sub_mem S.r cc g₁
  have ha : ‖algebraMap c.O (PadicAlgCl l) (zeta cc g₁ (r0 η Ψ))‖ = 1 := by
    obtain ⟨u, hu⟩ := hz0u
    have h1 := norm_coe_coefO_le_one c (zeta cc g₁ (r0 η Ψ))
    have h2 := norm_coe_coefO_le_one c (↑u⁻¹ : c.O)
    have hprod : algebraMap c.O (PadicAlgCl l) (zeta cc g₁ (r0 η Ψ)) *
        algebraMap c.O (PadicAlgCl l) (↑u⁻¹ : c.O) = 1 := by
      rw [← map_mul, ← hu, Units.mul_inv, map_one]
    have hn := congrArg norm hprod
    rw [norm_mul, norm_one] at hn
    change ‖((zeta cc g₁ (r0 η Ψ) : c.O) : PadicAlgCl l)‖ * ‖((↑u⁻¹ : c.O) : PadicAlgCl l)‖ = 1 at hn
    change ‖((zeta cc g₁ (r0 η Ψ) : c.O) : PadicAlgCl l)‖ = 1
    have key := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left h2 (norm_nonneg ((zeta cc g₁ (r0 η Ψ) : c.O) : PadicAlgCl l))
    rw [mul_one] at key
    exact le_antisymm h1 (by linarith)
  have hdec : ∀ g : S.R →ₐ[c.O] PadicAlgCl l, IsLocPt g →
      ‖zeta cc g₁ (push g S.r.hom) - algebraMap c.O (PadicAlgCl l) (zeta cc g₁ (r0 η Ψ))‖ < 1 := by
    intro g hg
    rw [hzeta_g, ← AlgHom.commutes g, ← map_sub]
    exact hg _ hmem
  have hne0 : ∀ g : S.R →ₐ[c.O] PadicAlgCl l, IsLocPt g → zeta cc g₁ (push g S.r.hom) ≠ 0 := by
    intro g hg h0
    have h := hdec g hg
    rw [h0, zero_sub, norm_neg, ha] at h
    exact lt_irrefl _ h
  have h2 : ‖(2 : PadicAlgCl l)‖ = 1 := by
    have h2e : (2 : PadicAlgCl l) = algebraMap ℚ_[l] (PadicAlgCl l) ((2 : ℕ) : ℚ_[l]) := by
      rw [Nat.cast_ofNat, map_ofNat]
    rw [h2e, PadicAlgCl.norm_extends, Padic.norm_natCast_eq_one_iff]
    exact (Nat.coprime_primes (Fact.out) Nat.prime_two).mpr hl2
  have hnn : ∀ g g' : S.R →ₐ[c.O] PadicAlgCl l, IsLocPt g → IsLocPt g' →
      zeta cc g₁ (push g' S.r.hom) ≠ -zeta cc g₁ (push g S.r.hom) := by
    intro g g' hg hg' heq
    have e1 := hdec g hg
    have e2 := hdec g' hg'
    have hsum : (2 : PadicAlgCl l) * algebraMap c.O (PadicAlgCl l) (zeta cc g₁ (r0 η Ψ)) =
        -((zeta cc g₁ (push g S.r.hom) - algebraMap c.O (PadicAlgCl l) (zeta cc g₁ (r0 η Ψ))) +
          (zeta cc g₁ (push g' S.r.hom) - algebraMap c.O (PadicAlgCl l) (zeta cc g₁ (r0 η Ψ)))) := by
      rw [heq]
      ring
    have hle := IsUltrametricDist.norm_add_le_max
      (zeta cc g₁ (push g S.r.hom) - algebraMap c.O (PadicAlgCl l) (zeta cc g₁ (r0 η Ψ)))
      (zeta cc g₁ (push g' S.r.hom) - algebraMap c.O (PadicAlgCl l) (zeta cc g₁ (r0 η Ψ)))
    have hn := congrArg norm hsum
    rw [norm_neg, norm_mul, h2, ha, one_mul] at hn
    have hm := max_lt e1 e2
    linarith
