  have hf := htwo.1
  have hcases := shape_cases η (push f S.r.hom) cc g₁ two_ne_zero (hmult f) hmk.ηc hmk.ηg
    (hnorm_g f) (hne0 f hf)
  have htype : TypeI η (push f S.r.hom) ∨ TypeII η (push f S.r.hom) := by
    rcases hcases with h | h | h | h
    · exact Or.inl h
    · exact absurd h htwo.2.1
    · exact absurd h htwo.2.2
    · exact Or.inr h
  refine ⟨htype, ?_⟩
  intro f' hf' hiso
  exact conjBy_tor_of_iso η (push f S.r.hom) (push f' S.r.hom) cc g₁ two_ne_zero (hmult f) (hmult f')
    hmk.ηc hmk.ηg (hnorm_g f) (hnorm_g f') (hne0 f hf) (hne0 f' hf') (hnn f f' hf hf') htype hiso


[OCH_SRC_COORDINATE_SHAPE] Ordered marked eigenvalues force invariant coordinate subspaces and exact six cases
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/UnitaryThreeGroups/ExistsCoordOfNotMatIrr.lean#L1-L123
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.UnitaryThreeGroups.BaseRepGL3NormalisedOfPsiEqOne
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.UnitaryThreeGroups.ConjugateStarMatrix
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.UnitaryThreeGroups.MatGstar
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.IntegralCasts.CapelliIdentities.SingleMemOfDiagonal

open Matrix UpperTheory.V.U3

private theorem single_mem_of_diag {κ : Type} [Field κ] {m : ℕ} (d : Fin m → κ)
    (hd : Function.Injective d) (W : Submodule κ (Fin m → κ))
    (hW : ∀ v ∈ W, Matrix.diagonal d *ᵥ v ∈ W) (v : Fin m → κ) (hv : v ∈ W) (i : Fin m)
    (hi : v i ≠ 0) : (Pi.single i 1 : Fin m → κ) ∈ W :=
  _root_.UpperTheory.Reformulated.B.SingleMemOfDiagonal.single_mem_of_diag d hd W hW v hv i hi

private theorem exists_coord_of_not_matIrr {G κ : Type} [Group G] [Field κ] {m : ℕ}
    (H : Subgroup G) (a : G → Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) κ) (d : Fin m → κ)
    (hd : Function.Injective d) (g₁ g₂ : G) (h₁ : g₁ ∈ H) (h₂ : g₂ ∈ H)
    (hD : a g₁ - a g₂ = Matrix.diagonal d) (h : ¬ MatIrr H a) :
    ∃ S : Fin m → Prop, (∃ j, S j) ∧ (∃ i, ¬ S i) ∧
      ∀ i j, S j → ¬ S i → ∀ g ∈ H, a g i j = 0 := by
  classical
  unfold MatIrr at h
  push Not at h
  obtain ⟨W, hW, hb, ht⟩ := h
  have hWd : ∀ v ∈ W, Matrix.diagonal d *ᵥ v ∈ W := fun v hv => by
    rw [← hD, Matrix.sub_mulVec]
    exact W.sub_mem (hW _ h₁ _ hv) (hW _ h₂ _ hv)
  have hs := single_mem_of_diag d hd W hWd
  refine ⟨fun j => (Pi.single j 1 : Fin m → κ) ∈ W, ?_, ?_, ?_⟩
  · obtain ⟨v, hv, hv0⟩ := Submodule.exists_mem_ne_zero_of_ne_bot hb
    obtain ⟨j, hj⟩ := Function.ne_iff.1 hv0
    exact ⟨j, hs v hv j hj⟩
  · by_contra hc
    push Not at hc
    apply ht
    rw [eq_top_iff]
    intro v _
    rw [pi_eq_sum_univ' v]
    exact W.sum_mem fun i _ => W.smul_mem _ (hc i)
  · intro i j hj hi g hg
    by_contra hc
    apply hi
    refine hs _ (hW g hg _ hj) i ?_
    simpa using hc

theorem UpperTheory.V.U3.shape_cases :
    ∀ {G κ : Type} [Group G] [Field κ] (η : G →* ℤˣ) (r : G →* GG 3 κ) (c g₁ : G), (2 : κ) ≠ 0 → HasMult η r → η c = -1 → η g₁ = 1 →
      Normalised c g₁ r → zeta c g₁ r ≠ 0 → TypeI η r ∨ Upper η r ∨ Lower η r ∨ TypeII η r := by
  intro G κ _ _ η r c g₁ h2 hm hc hg hn hz
  have hc' : (r c).right = -1 := (hm c).trans hc
  have hD := zmat_diag r c g₁ h2.isUnit hc' ((hm g₁).trans hg) hn
  have hst : ∀ g ∈ η.ker, gstar c g ∈ η.ker := fun g hg =>
    (hm _).symm.trans (mat_gstar r c g hn.atc hc' ((hm g).trans hg)).2
  set ζ := zeta c g₁ r with hζ
  have hne : ζ ≠ -ζ := fun h => hz (by
    have : (2 : κ) * ζ = 0 := by linear_combination h
    exact (mul_eq_zero.1 this).resolve_left h2)
  let Z : Fin 3 → Fin 3 → Prop := fun i j => ∀ g ∈ η.ker, (r g).mat i j = 0
  have hsym : ∀ i j : Fin 3, Z i j → Z j.rev i.rev := by
    intro i j h g hg
    have h1 := h _ (hst g hg)
    rw [(mat_gstar r c g hn.atc hc' ((hm g).trans hg)).1] at h1
    revert h1
    fin_cases i <;> fin_cases j <;>
      simp [Matrix.mul_apply, Fin.sum_univ_three, Jmat, Fin.rev]
  have s10 : Z 1 0 → Z 2 1 := hsym 1 0
  have s21 : Z 2 1 → Z 1 0 := hsym 2 1
  have s01 : Z 0 1 → Z 1 2 := hsym 0 1
  have s12 : Z 1 2 → Z 0 1 := hsym 1 2
  have hU : Z 1 0 → Z 2 0 → Upper η r := by
    intro h1 h2 g hg a b hab
    have := h1 g hg
    have := h2 g hg
    have := s10 h1 g hg
    fin_cases a <;> fin_cases b <;> first | assumption | (exfalso; revert hab; decide)
  have hL : Z 0 1 → Z 0 2 → Lower η r := by
    intro h1 h2 g hg a b hab
    have := h1 g hg
    have := h2 g hg
    have := s01 h1 g hg
    fin_cases a <;> fin_cases b <;> first | assumption | (exfalso; revert hab; decide)
  have hB : Z 0 1 → Z 1 0 → Upper η r ∨ Lower η r ∨ TypeII η r := by
    intro h1 h2
    by_cases hI : MatIrr η.ker fun g => corner (r g).mat
    · refine Or.inr (Or.inr ⟨?_, hI⟩)
      intro g hg a b hab
      have := h1 g hg
      have := h2 g hg
      have := s01 h1 g hg
      have := s10 h2 g hg
      fin_cases a <;> fin_cases b <;> first | assumption | (exfalso; revert hab; decide)
    · have hinj : Function.Injective ![ζ, -ζ] := by
        intro i j h
        fin_cases i <;> fin_cases j <;> simp_all [hne.symm]
      obtain ⟨S, ⟨j, hj⟩, ⟨i, hi⟩, hS⟩ := exists_coord_of_not_matIrr η.ker _ ![ζ, -ζ] hinj g₁
        (gstar c g₁) hg (hst g₁ hg) (by
          ext i j
          have := congrFun (congrFun hD (![0, 2] i)) (![0, 2] j)
          refine Eq.trans this ?_
          fin_cases i <;> fin_cases j <;> simp) hI
      have h3 := hS i j hj hi
      fin_cases i <;> fin_cases j
      · exact absurd hj hi
      · exact Or.inr (Or.inl (hL h1 h3))
      · exact Or.inl (hU h2 h3)
      · exact absurd hj hi
  by_cases hI : TypeI η r
  · exact Or.inl hI
  right
  have hinj : Function.Injective ![ζ, 0, -ζ] := by
    intro i j h
    fin_cases i <;> fin_cases j <;> simp_all [hne.symm]
  obtain ⟨S, ⟨j, hj⟩, ⟨i, hi⟩, hS⟩ := exists_coord_of_not_matIrr η.ker (mats r) _ hinj g₁
    (gstar c g₁) hg (hst g₁ hg) hD hI
  have hS' : ∀ i j, S j → ¬ S i → Z i j := hS
  by_cases a0 : S 0 <;> by_cases a1 : S 1 <;> by_cases a2 : S 2
  · fin_cases i <;> contradiction
  · exact Or.inl (hU (s21 (hS' _ _ a1 a2)) (hS' _ _ a0 a2))
  · exact hB (s12 (hS' _ _ a2 a1)) (hS' _ _ a0 a1)
  · exact Or.inl (hU (hS' _ _ a0 a1) (hS' _ _ a0 a2))
  · exact Or.inr (Or.inl (hL (hS' _ _ a1 a0) (hS' _ _ a2 a0)))
  · exact hB (hS' _ _ a1 a0) (s21 (hS' _ _ a1 a2))
  · exact Or.inr (Or.inl (hL (s12 (hS' _ _ a2 a1)) (hS' _ _ a2 a0)))
  · fin_cases j <;> contradiction


[OCH_SRC_TORUS_INTERTWINER] The actual diagonal intertwiner and the square-root outer-block case
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/UnitaryThreeGroups/FreeEtaleSheafZmodExistsHomSendingGenerator.lean#L1-L120
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.UnitaryThreeGroups.BaseRepGL3NormalisedOfPsiEqOne
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.UnitaryThreeGroups.MatGstar
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.UnitaryThreeGroups.ConjugateStarMatrix
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.UnitaryThreeGroups.ConjTor

open Matrix UpperTheory.V.U3

private theorem offdiag_zero {κ : Type} [Field κ] (M : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) κ) (z z' : κ)
    (hz : z ≠ 0) (hz' : z' ≠ 0) (hne : z' ≠ -z)
    (h : M * Matrix.diagonal ![z, 0, -z] = Matrix.diagonal ![z', 0, -z'] * M) :
    ∀ i j, i ≠ j → M i j = 0 := by
  intro i j hij
  have e := congrFun (congrFun h i) j
  rw [Matrix.mul_diagonal, Matrix.diagonal_mul, mul_comm] at e
  by_contra hM
  have e' := mul_right_cancel₀ hM e
  have hne' : z ≠ -z' := fun h => hne (by rw [h, neg_neg])
  fin_cases i <;> fin_cases j <;> simp_all [eq_comm]

private theorem free_etale_sheaf_zmod_exists_hom_sending_generator {κ ι : Type} [Field κ] [IsAlgClosed κ] (m : Fin 3 → κ) (hm : ∀ i, m i ≠ 0)
    (a a' : ι → Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) κ)
    (h1 : ∀ g i j, m i * a g i j = a' g i j * m j)
    (h2 : ∀ g i j, m j.rev * a g i j = a' g i j * m i.rev) :
    ∃ t : κˣ, ∀ g i j, a' g i j = ((t ^ wt i j : κˣ) : κ) * a g i j := by
  have h0 := hm 0
  have h1' := hm 1
  have h2' := hm 2
  have ha : ∀ g i j, a' g i j = m i / m j * a g i j := by
    intro g i j
    have := hm j
    field_simp
    linear_combination -h1 g i j
  by_cases hd : m 0 * m 2 = m 1 * m 1
  · refine ⟨Units.mk0 (m 0 / m 1) (div_ne_zero h0 h1'), fun g i j => ?_⟩
    rw [ha]
    congr 1
    have e2 : m 2 = m 1 * m 1 / m 0 := by field_simp; linear_combination hd
    fin_cases i <;> fin_cases j <;> simp [wt, wt1, e2, zpow_ofNat, h0, h1'] <;> field_simp
  · obtain ⟨s, hs⟩ := IsAlgClosed.exists_eq_mul_self (m 0 / m 2)
    have hs0 : s ≠ 0 := by
      rintro rfl
      exact div_ne_zero h0 h2' (by simpa using hs)
    have hz : ∀ g i j, (m i * m i.rev - m j * m j.rev) * a g i j = 0 := by
      intro g i j
      linear_combination m i.rev * h1 g i j - m j * h2 g i j
    have hd1 : m 0 * m 2 - m 1 * m 1 ≠ 0 := sub_ne_zero.2 hd
    have hd2 : m 2 * m 0 - m 1 * m 1 ≠ 0 := by rwa [mul_comm (m 2)]
    have hd3 : m 1 * m 1 - m 0 * m 2 ≠ 0 := sub_ne_zero.2 (Ne.symm hd)
    have hd4 : m 1 * m 1 - m 2 * m 0 ≠ 0 := by rwa [mul_comm (m 2)]
    refine ⟨Units.mk0 s hs0, fun g i j => ?_⟩
    have z01 : a g 0 1 = 0 := (mul_eq_zero.1 (hz g 0 1)).resolve_left hd1
    have z21 : a g 2 1 = 0 := (mul_eq_zero.1 (hz g 2 1)).resolve_left hd2
    have z10 : a g 1 0 = 0 := (mul_eq_zero.1 (hz g 1 0)).resolve_left hd3
    have z12 : a g 1 2 = 0 := (mul_eq_zero.1 (hz g 1 2)).resolve_left hd4
    have e0 : m 0 = s * s * m 2 := by rw [← hs]; field_simp
    rw [ha]
    fin_cases i <;> fin_cases j <;>
      simp [wt, wt1, z01, z21, z10, z12, e0, zpow_ofNat, h1', h2', hs0] <;> left <;> field_simp

theorem UpperTheory.V.U3.conjBy_tor_of_iso :
    ∀ {G κ : Type} [Group G] [Field κ] [IsAlgClosed κ] (η : G →* ℤˣ) (r r' : G →* GG 3 κ) (c g₁ : G), (2 : κ) ≠ 0 → HasMult η r → HasMult η r' →
      η c = -1 → η g₁ = 1 → Normalised c g₁ r → Normalised c g₁ r' → zeta c g₁ r ≠ 0 → zeta c g₁ r' ≠ 0 → zeta c g₁ r' ≠ -zeta c g₁ r →
      (TypeI η r ∨ TypeII η r) → MatIso η.ker (mats r) (mats r') → ∃ t : κˣ, r' = conjBy (tor κ t) r := by
  intro G κ _ _ _ η r r' c g₁ h2 hr hr' hc hg hN hN' hz hz' hne _ hiso
  obtain ⟨M, hM⟩ := hiso
  have hstar : ∀ g, η g = 1 → η (gstar c g) = 1 := by
    intro g h
    simp [gstar, hc, h]
  have hZ : (M : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) κ) * zmat c g₁ r = zmat c g₁ r' * M := by
    have e1 := hM g₁ hg
    have e2 := hM (gstar c g₁) (hstar g₁ hg)
    unfold mats at e1 e2
    rw [zmat, zmat, Matrix.mul_sub, Matrix.sub_mul, e1, e2]
  rw [zmat_diag r c g₁ h2.isUnit ((hr c).trans hc) ((hr g₁).trans hg) hN,
    zmat_diag r' c g₁ h2.isUnit ((hr' c).trans hc) ((hr' g₁).trans hg) hN'] at hZ
  have hoff := offdiag_zero _ _ _ hz hz' hne hZ
  have hdiag : (M : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) κ) = Matrix.diagonal fun i => M i i := by
    ext i j
    by_cases hij : i = j
    · subst hij
      simp
    · rw [Matrix.diagonal_apply_ne _ hij, hoff i j hij]
  have hm : ∀ i, (M : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) κ) i i ≠ 0 := by
    have hdet : (M : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) κ).det ≠ 0 := M.det_ne_zero
    rw [hdiag, Matrix.det_diagonal, Finset.prod_ne_zero_iff] at hdet
    exact fun i => hdet i (Finset.mem_univ i)
  have h1 : ∀ g, η g = 1 → ∀ i j, M i i * (r g).mat i j = (r' g).mat i j * M j j := by
    intro g h i j
    have e := hM g h
    rw [hdiag] at e
    have e' := congrFun (congrFun e i) j
    rwa [Matrix.mul_diagonal, Matrix.diagonal_mul] at e'
  have hJ : ∀ (X : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) κ) (i j : Fin 3),
      (Jmat κ 3 * Xᵀ * Jmat κ 3) j.rev i.rev = X i j := by
    intro X i j
    fin_cases i <;> fin_cases j <;>
      simp [Matrix.mul_apply, Fin.sum_univ_three, Jmat, Fin.rev]
  have h2' : ∀ g, η g = 1 → ∀ i j : Fin 3,
      M j.rev j.rev * (r g).mat i j = (r' g).mat i j * M i.rev i.rev := by
    intro g h i j
    have e := h1 _ (hstar g h) j.rev i.rev
    rwa [(mat_gstar r c g hN.atc ((hr c).trans hc) ((hr g).trans h)).1,
      (mat_gstar r' c g hN'.atc ((hr' c).trans hc) ((hr' g).trans h)).1, hJ, hJ] at e
  obtain ⟨t, ht⟩ := free_etale_sheaf_zmod_exists_hom_sending_generator (ι := η.ker) (fun i => M i i) hm (fun g => (r g).mat)
    (fun g => (r' g).mat) (fun g => h1 g g.2) (fun g => h2' g g.2)
  obtain ⟨k1, k2, k3, -⟩ := conjBy_tor r t
  refine ⟨t, ?_⟩
  have hker : ∀ g, η g = 1 → r' g = conjBy (tor κ t) r g := by
    intro g h
    refine SemidirectProduct.ext (Units.ext ?_) (by rw [k2, hr g, hr' g])
    ext i j
    exact (ht ⟨g, h⟩ i j).trans (k1 g i j).symm
  have hcc : r' c = conjBy (tor κ t) r c :=
    SemidirectProduct.ext (Units.ext (hN'.atc.trans (k3 c g₁ hN).atc.symm))
      (by rw [k2, hr c, hr' c])
  refine MonoidHom.ext fun g => ?_
  rcases Int.units_eq_one_or (η g) with h | h
  · exact hker g h
  · have e : g = g * c⁻¹ * c := by simp
    rw [e, map_mul r' _ c, map_mul _ _ c, hcc, hker (g * c⁻¹) (by simp [h, hc])]


[OCH_SRC_FIXED_POINT] Exact inertia root matching, class-group argument and pt_fixed
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/UnitaryThreeGroups/RootOfCharpoly.lean#L1-L225
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.UnitaryThreeGroups.KatoUse
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.UnitaryThreeGroups.PowEqOneOfUnr
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.UnitaryThreeGroups.PtInertiaAway
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.UnitaryThreeGroups.PtIneL
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.UnitaryThreeGroups.DatFacts
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.IntegralCasts.CapelliIdentities.NormLeOneOfIsIntegral

open NumberField IsDedekindDomain Formalism.ConjugateSelfDual ReciprocityTheory.Gal Formalism.MainNotions.N Formalism.BasicNotions Formalism.BasicNotions.Loc GlobalTheory.GalOfCusp Matrix IsLocalRing
open UpperTheory.V UpperTheory.V.Base UpperTheory.V.HV UpperTheory.V.Alg UpperTheory.V.U3

private theorem quot_mem {H M : Type} [Group H] [CommGroup M] (U : Subgroup M) (a t : H →* M) (g : H) (i j : Fin 3)
    (h1 : a g / t g ^ wt1 i ∈ U) (h2 : a g = t g ^ wt1 j) : t g ^ (wt1 j - wt1 i) ∈ U := by
  rwa [h2, div_eq_mul_inv, ← _root_.zpow_sub] at h1

private theorem eq_away {H M : Type} [Group H] [CommGroup M] (U : Subgroup M) (I : Subgroup H) (a t : H →* M)
    (i : Fin 3) (hred : ∀ g, a g / t g ^ wt1 i ∈ U) (hroot : ∀ g ∈ I, ∃ j, a g = t g ^ wt1 j)
    (hI : ∀ g ∈ I, t g ∈ U → t g = 1) (g : H) (hg : g ∈ I) : a g = t g ^ wt1 i := by
  obtain ⟨j, hj⟩ := hroot g hg
  have h := quot_mem U a t g i j (hred g) hj
  rw [← map_zpow] at h
  have h' := hI _ (I.zpow_mem hg _) h
  rw [map_zpow, _root_.zpow_sub, mul_inv_eq_one] at h'
  rw [hj, h']

private theorem eq_of_sq {H M : Type} [Group H] [CommGroup M] (U : Subgroup M) (a t : H →* M)
    (i : Fin 3) (g : H) (hred : a g / t g ^ wt1 i ∈ U) (hroot : ∃ j, a g = t g ^ wt1 j)
    (hs : t g ^ 2 ∉ U) : a g = t g ^ wt1 i := by
  obtain ⟨j, hj⟩ := hroot
  by_cases hij : j = i
  · rw [hj, hij]
  exfalso
  have h := quot_mem U a t g i j hred hj
  have hd : (wt1 j - wt1 i) ∣ 2 := by
    revert hij
    fin_cases i <;> fin_cases j <;> simp [wt1]
  obtain ⟨m, hm⟩ := hd
  apply hs
  have := U.zpow_mem h m
  rwa [← _root_.zpow_mul, ← hm, zpow_ofNat] at this

private theorem eq_above {H M : Type} [Group H] [CommGroup M] (U : Subgroup M) (I : Subgroup H) (a t : H →* M)
    (i : Fin 3) (hred : ∀ g, a g / t g ^ wt1 i ∈ U) (hroot : ∀ g ∈ I, ∃ j, a g = t g ^ wt1 j)
    (s : H) (hs : s ∈ I) (hs2 : t s ^ 2 ∉ U) (g : H) (hg : g ∈ I) : a g = t g ^ wt1 i := by
  by_cases h : t g ^ 2 ∈ U
  · have h1 := eq_of_sq U a t i s (hred s) (hroot s hs) hs2
    have h2 := eq_of_sq U a t i (g * s) (hred _) (hroot _ (I.mul_mem hg hs)) (by
      rw [map_mul, mul_pow]
      exact fun h' => hs2 ((U.mul_mem_cancel_left h).1 h'))
    rw [map_mul, map_mul, mul_zpow, h1] at h2
    exact mul_right_cancel h2
  · exact eq_of_sq U a t i g (hred g) (hroot g hg) h

private theorem norm_eq_one_of {F : Type} [NormedField F] [IsUltrametricDist F] {u : F} (h : ‖u - 1‖ < 1) : ‖u‖ = 1 := by
  have e : u = (u - 1) + 1 := by ring
  rw [e, IsUltrametricDist.norm_add_eq_max_of_norm_ne_norm (by rw [norm_one]; exact h.ne), norm_one]
  exact max_eq_right h.le

private def U1 (F : Type) [NormedField F] [IsUltrametricDist F] : Subgroup Fˣ where
  carrier := {u | ‖(u : F) - 1‖ < 1}
  one_mem' := by simp
  mul_mem' := by
    intro u v hu hv
    have e : ((u * v : Fˣ) : F) - 1 = u * (v - 1) + (u - 1) := by push_cast; ring
    show ‖((u * v : Fˣ) : F) - 1‖ < 1
    rw [e]
    refine lt_of_le_of_lt (IsUltrametricDist.norm_add_le_max _ _) (max_lt ?_ hu)
    rw [norm_mul, norm_eq_one_of hu, one_mul]
    exact hv
  inv_mem' := by
    intro u hu
    have e : (u : F) * (((u⁻¹ : Fˣ) : F) - 1) = -((u : F) - 1) := by rw [mul_sub, Units.mul_inv]; ring
    have h := congrArg norm e
    rw [norm_mul, norm_eq_one_of hu, one_mul, norm_neg] at h
    show ‖((u⁻¹ : Fˣ) : F) - 1‖ < 1
    rw [h]
    exact hu

private theorem root_of_charpoly {F : Type} [Field F] (a b : Fin 3 → F)
    (h : (Matrix.diagonal a).charpoly = (Matrix.diagonal b).charpoly) (i : Fin 3) : ∃ j, a i = b j := by
  have h1 := congrArg (Polynomial.eval (a i)) h
  rw [Matrix.charpoly_diagonal, Matrix.charpoly_diagonal, Polynomial.eval_prod, Polynomial.eval_prod,
    Finset.prod_eq_zero (Finset.mem_univ i) (by simp), eq_comm, Finset.prod_eq_zero_iff] at h1
  obtain ⟨j, -, hj⟩ := h1
  exact ⟨j, by simpa [sub_eq_zero] using hj⟩

private theorem exists_diag {Γ R : Type} [Group Γ] [CommRing R] (η : Γ →* ℤˣ) (r : Γ →* GG 3 R) (hm : HasMult η r)
    (hU : Upper η r) (hL : Lower η r) :
    ∃ a : Fin 3 → η.ker →* Rˣ, ∀ g : η.ker, (r g).mat = Matrix.diagonal fun i => (a i g : R) := by
  have hd : ∀ g : η.ker, (r g).mat = Matrix.diagonal fun i => (r g).mat i i := by
    intro g
    ext i j
    by_cases hij : i = j
    · subst hij
      simp
    rw [Matrix.diagonal_apply_ne _ hij]
    rcases lt_trichotomy (wt i j) 0 with h | h | h
    · exact hU g g.2 i j h
    · exact absurd (Fin.ext (by simp only [wt, wt1] at h; omega)) hij
    · exact hL g g.2 i j h
  let b : Fin 3 → η.ker →* R := fun i =>
    { toFun := fun g => (r g).mat i i
      map_one' := by simp [GG.mat]
      map_mul' := fun g h => by
        have e : (r ((g * h : η.ker) : Γ)).mat = (r g).mat * (r h).mat :=
          congrArg Units.val (map_mul (glPart η r hm) g h)
        rw [e, hd g, Matrix.diagonal_mul, Matrix.diagonal_apply_eq] }
  exact ⟨fun i => (b i).toHomUnits, hd⟩

private theorem norm_le_one_int (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (x : PadicAlgCl l) (hx : IsIntegral ℤ_[l] x) :
    ‖x‖ ≤ 1 :=
  _root_.UpperTheory.Reformulated.B.NormLeOneOfIsIntegral.norm_le_one_of_isIntegral l x hx

private theorem norm_unit {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] (c : Coef l) {x : c.O} (hx : IsUnit x) :
    ‖algebraMap c.O (PadicAlgCl l) x‖ = 1 := by
  have hle : ∀ y : c.O, ‖(y : PadicAlgCl l)‖ ≤ 1 := fun y =>
    norm_le_one_int l _ ((Algebra.IsIntegral.isIntegral (R := ℤ_[l]) y).map c.O.val)
  obtain ⟨u, rfl⟩ := hx
  show ‖((u : c.O) : PadicAlgCl l)‖ = 1
  have h1 : ‖((u : c.O) : PadicAlgCl l)‖ * ‖((u⁻¹ : c.Oˣ) : c.O).1‖ = 1 := by
    rw [← norm_mul, ← Subalgebra.coe_mul, Units.mul_inv]; simp
  have h2 := hle u
  have h3 := hle (u⁻¹ : c.Oˣ)
  have h4 := norm_nonneg ((u : c.O) : PadicAlgCl l)
  nlinarith

theorem UpperTheory.V.U3.pt_fixed :
    CohInp → ∀ {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] {K : Type} [Field K] [NumberField K] [Algebra K Ω]
      {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {c : Coef l} {N : ℕ} [NeZero N] {e : ℕ} {ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O N} (D : Dat Ω K c e ψ) {U : D.ULoc} {g₁ : G ℚ Ω}, D.Marked g₁ → ∀ {r : G ℚ Ω →* GG 3 (PadicAlgCl l)} (h : D.IsPt U g₁ r),
      Upper D.η r → Lower D.η r → r = r0 D.η D.ΨQ := by
  intro hC Ω _ _ _ K _ _ _ l _ c N _ e ψ D U g₁ hM r h hU hL
  obtain ⟨a, ha⟩ := exists_diag D.η r h.mult hU hL
  obtain ⟨-, -, -, -, -, hkill, -, -, hcont, -, hab⟩ := dat_facts D
  let t : D.η.ker →* (PadicAlgCl l)ˣ := D.ΨQ.comp D.η.ker.subtype
  have htv : ∀ g : G ℚ Ω, (D.ΨQ g : PadicAlgCl l) = algebraMap c.O (PadicAlgCl l) (D.Ψ g : c.O) := fun _ => rfl
  have hr0 : ∀ g : D.η.ker, (r0 D.η D.ΨQ g).mat = Matrix.diagonal fun i => ((t g ^ wt1 i : (PadicAlgCl l)ˣ) : PadicAlgCl l) :=
    fun _ => rfl
  have hai : ∀ i (g : D.η.ker), (a i g : PadicAlgCl l) = (r g).mat i i := fun i g => by
    rw [ha g, Matrix.diagonal_apply_eq]
  have hred : ∀ i g, a i g / t g ^ wt1 i ∈ U1 (PadicAlgCl l) := by
    intro i g
    have h1 := h.red g i i
    rw [← hai, hr0, Matrix.diagonal_apply_eq] at h1
    have h2 : ‖((t g ^ wt1 i : (PadicAlgCl l)ˣ) : PadicAlgCl l)‖ = 1 := by
      rw [Units.val_zpow_eq_zpow_val, norm_zpow]
      have : ‖(t g : PadicAlgCl l)‖ = 1 := norm_unit c (D.Ψ g).isUnit
      rw [this, _root_.one_zpow]
    show ‖((a i g / t g ^ wt1 i : (PadicAlgCl l)ˣ) : PadicAlgCl l) - 1‖ < 1
    rw [Units.val_div_eq_div_val, div_sub_one (Units.ne_zero _), norm_div, h2, div_one]
    exact h1
  have hroot : ∀ i (g : D.η.ker), (r g).mat.charpoly = (r0 D.η D.ΨQ g).mat.charpoly → ∃ j, a i g = t g ^ wt1 j := by
    intro i g hg
    rw [ha, hr0] at hg
    obtain ⟨j, hj⟩ := root_of_charpoly _ _ hg i
    exact ⟨j, Units.ext hj⟩
  have hine : ∀ i (A : ValuationSubring Ω) (g : D.η.ker), (g : G ℚ Ω) ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) A → a i g = t g ^ wt1 i := by
    intro i A g hg
    by_cases hA : A.valuation (l : Ω) < 1
    · obtain ⟨hle, s, hs, hsu⟩ := hab A hA
      refine eq_above (U1 _) ((Ine (F := ℚ) A).subgroupOf D.η.ker) (a i) t i (hred i)
        (fun x hx => hroot i x ((pt_inertia_above hC D hM h A hA).1 x hx)) ⟨s, hle hs⟩ hs (fun hu => ?_) g hg
      have h1 := norm_unit c hsu
      rw [map_sub, map_pow, map_one] at h1
      exact absurd hu (not_lt.2 h1.ge)
    · obtain ⟨M, hMc⟩ := pt_inertia_away D hM h A hA
      refine eq_away (U1 _) ((Ine (F := ℚ) A).subgroupOf D.η.ker) (a i) t i (hred i)
        (fun x hx => hroot i x ?_) (fun x hx hu => ?_) g hg
      · have h1 : (r0 D.η D.ΨQ x).mat = (M : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) (PadicAlgCl l)) * (r x).mat *
            ((M⁻¹ : GL (Fin 3) (PadicAlgCl l)) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) (PadicAlgCl l)) := by
          rw [hMc x hx x.2, Matrix.mul_assoc, Units.mul_inv, Matrix.mul_one]
        rw [h1, Matrix.charpoly_mul_comm, ← Matrix.mul_assoc, Units.inv_mul, Matrix.one_mul]
      · have h1 : ¬ IsUnit ((D.Ψ x : c.O) - 1) := fun hx1 => by
          have h1 := norm_unit c hx1
          rw [map_sub, map_one] at h1
          exact absurd hu (not_lt.2 h1.ge)
        have h2 : barChar c.O D.Ψ x = 1 := by
          apply Units.ext
          show residue c.O (D.Ψ x : c.O) = 1
          rw [← sub_eq_zero, ← map_one (residue c.O), ← map_sub, residue_eq_zero_iff]
          exact h1
        have h3 : (x : G ℚ Ω) ∈ D.Kill := by
          refine ⟨A, hA, hx, SemidirectProduct.ext ?_ x.2⟩
          show tor _ (barChar c.O D.Ψ x) = 1
          rw [h2, map_one]
        have h4 := congrArg (fun y => GG.mat y 0 0) (hkill x h3)
        have h5 : (D.Ψ x : c.O) = 1 := by simpa [GG.mat, tor_val, wt1] using h4
        apply Units.ext
        show (D.ΨQ x : PadicAlgCl l) = 1
        rw [htv, h5, map_one]
  have hall : ∀ i g, a i g = t g ^ wt1 i := by
    intro i g
    have hk := char_eq_one D (a i / (zpowGroupHom (wt1 i)).comp t) ?_ (hred i)
      (fun A g hg => div_eq_one.2 (hine i A g hg))
    · exact div_eq_one.1 (DFunLike.congr_fun hk g)
    · have c1 : Continuous fun g : D.η.ker => (r g).mat i i :=
        (h.cont.comp continuous_subtype_val).matrix_elem i i
      have c2 : Continuous fun g : D.η.ker => (t g : PadicAlgCl l) :=
        Units.continuous_val.comp (hcont.comp continuous_subtype_val)
      refine (c1.div (c2.zpow₀ (wt1 i) fun g => Or.inl (Units.ne_zero _))
        fun g => zpow_ne_zero _ (Units.ne_zero _)).congr fun g => ?_
      show _ = ((a i g / t g ^ wt1 i : (PadicAlgCl l)ˣ) : PadicAlgCl l)
      rw [Units.val_div_eq_div_val, Units.val_zpow_eq_zpow_val, hai]
      rfl
  have hker : ∀ g ∈ D.η.ker, r g = r0 D.η D.ΨQ g := by
    intro g hg
    refine SemidirectProduct.ext (Units.ext ?_) (h.mult g)
    show (r (⟨g, hg⟩ : D.η.ker)).mat = (r0 D.η D.ΨQ (⟨g, hg⟩ : D.η.ker)).mat
    rw [ha, hr0]
    simp only [hall]
  have hc : r D.cc = r0 D.η D.ΨQ D.cc := by
    refine SemidirectProduct.ext (Units.ext ?_) (h.mult _)
    have h1 : D.ΨQ D.cc = 1 := by
      apply Units.ext
      rw [htv, hM.Ψc]
      simp
    rw [unitaryThreeReflection_left, h1, map_one]
    exact h.norm.atc
  refine MonoidHom.ext fun g => ?_
  rcases Int.units_eq_one_or (D.η g) with hg | hg
  · exact hker g hg
  · have h1 : g * D.cc ∈ D.η.ker := by
      rw [MonoidHom.mem_ker, map_mul, hg, hM.ηc]
      simp
    have h2 := hker _ h1
    rw [map_mul, map_mul, hc] at h2
    exact mul_right_cancel h2


[OCH_SRC_INERTIA_AWAY] Actual finite prime-to-ell averaging conjugacy on inertia
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/UnitaryThreeGroups/PtInertiaAway.lean#L1-L109
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.UnitaryThreeGroups.KatoUse
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.UnitaryThreeGroups.DatFacts
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.IntegralCasts.CapelliIdentities.ImportsMoreTail
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.IntegralCasts.CapelliIdentities.NormSumLt

open NumberField IsDedekindDomain Formalism.ConjugateSelfDual ReciprocityTheory.Gal Formalism.MainNotions.N Formalism.BasicNotions Formalism.BasicNotions.Loc GlobalTheory.GalOfCusp Matrix IsLocalRing
open UpperTheory.V UpperTheory.V.Base UpperTheory.V.HV UpperTheory.V.Alg UpperTheory.V.U3

private theorem norm_sum_lt' {F ι : Type} [NormedField F] [IsUltrametricDist F] (s : Finset ι) (f : ι → F) {C : ℝ}
    (hC : 0 < C) (h : ∀ i ∈ s, ‖f i‖ < C) : ‖∑ i ∈ s, f i‖ < C :=
  _root_.UpperTheory.Reformulated.B.Qe17.norm_sum_lt s f hC h

private theorem det_ne_zero' {F : Type} [NormedField F] [IsUltrametricDist F] {m : ℕ} (t : F) (ht : ‖t‖ = 1)
    (E : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) F) (hE : ∀ a b, ‖E a b‖ < 1) : (t • (1 : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) F) + E).det ≠ 0 :=
  _root_.UpperTheory.Reformulated.B.Qi6.det_ne_zero t ht E hE

private theorem conj_of_close {F Γ : Type} [NormedField F] [IsUltrametricDist F] [Group Γ] [Fintype Γ] {m : ℕ}
    (hΓ : ‖(Fintype.card Γ : F)‖ = 1) (ρ ρ₀ : Γ →* GL (Fin m) F)
    (hi : ∀ g a b, ‖(ρ g : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) F) a b‖ ≤ 1)
    (hc : ∀ g a b, ‖(ρ g : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) F) a b - (ρ₀ g : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) F) a b‖ < 1) :
    ∃ M : GL (Fin m) F, ∀ g, (M : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) F) * ρ g = ρ₀ g * M := by
  have hi₀ : ∀ g a b, ‖(ρ₀ g : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) F) a b‖ ≤ 1 := fun g a b => by
    have e : (ρ₀ g : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) F) a b = (ρ g : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) F) a b +
      -((ρ g : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) F) a b - (ρ₀ g : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) F) a b) := by ring
    rw [e]
    exact (IsUltrametricDist.norm_add_le_max _ _).trans (max_le (hi g a b) (by rw [norm_neg]; exact (hc g a b).le))
  set M : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) F := ∑ g, (ρ₀ g⁻¹ : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) F) * ρ g with hM
  have hsplit : M = (Fintype.card Γ : F) • (1 : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) F) +
      ∑ g, (ρ₀ g⁻¹ : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) F) * ((ρ g : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) F) - ρ₀ g) := by
    have e : ∀ g, (ρ₀ g⁻¹ : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) F) * ρ g =
        1 + (ρ₀ g⁻¹ : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) F) * ((ρ g : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) F) - ρ₀ g) := fun g => by
      rw [Matrix.mul_sub, ← Units.val_mul (ρ₀ g⁻¹) (ρ₀ g), ← map_mul, inv_mul_cancel, map_one, Units.val_one]
      abel
    rw [hM, Finset.sum_congr rfl fun g _ => e g, Finset.sum_add_distrib, Finset.sum_const, Finset.card_univ,
      Nat.cast_smul_eq_nsmul]
  have hdet : M.det ≠ 0 := by
    rw [hsplit]
    refine det_ne_zero' _ hΓ _ fun a b => ?_
    rw [Matrix.sum_apply]
    refine norm_sum_lt' _ _ one_pos fun g _ => ?_
    rw [Matrix.mul_apply]
    refine norm_sum_lt' _ _ one_pos fun k _ => ?_
    rw [norm_mul, Matrix.sub_apply]
    exact mul_lt_one_of_nonneg_of_lt_one_right (hi₀ _ _ _) (norm_nonneg _) (hc g k b)
  refine ⟨Matrix.GeneralLinearGroup.mkOfDetNeZero M hdet, fun h => ?_⟩
  show M * _ = _ * M
  rw [hM, Finset.sum_mul, Finset.mul_sum]
  refine Fintype.sum_equiv (Equiv.mulRight h) _ _ fun g => ?_
  simp only [Equiv.coe_mulRight]
  rw [← Matrix.mul_assoc, ← Units.val_mul (ρ₀ h), ← map_mul, map_mul ρ, Units.val_mul, ← Matrix.mul_assoc]
  congr 4
  group

theorem UpperTheory.V.U3.pt_inertia_away :
    ∀ {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] {K : Type} [Field K] [NumberField K] [Algebra K Ω]
      {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {c : Coef l} {N : ℕ} [NeZero N] {e : ℕ} {ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O N} (D : Dat Ω K c e ψ) {U : D.ULoc} {g₁ : G ℚ Ω}, D.Marked g₁ → ∀ {r : G ℚ Ω →* GG 3 (PadicAlgCl l)} (h : D.IsPt U g₁ r),
      ∀ A : ValuationSubring Ω, ¬ A.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 → ∃ M : GL (Fin 3) (PadicAlgCl l), ∀ g ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) A, D.η g = 1 →
        (M : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) (PadicAlgCl l)) * (r g).mat = (r0 D.η D.ΨQ g).mat * M := by
  intro Ω _ _ _ K _ _ _ l hl c N _ e ψ D U g₁ _ r h A hA
  obtain ⟨-, -, -, -, -, hk0, -, -, -, hK, -⟩ := dat_facts D
  let H : Subgroup (G ℚ Ω) := Ine (F := ℚ) A ⊓ D.η.ker
  let i : H →* D.η.ker := Subgroup.inclusion inf_le_right
  let χ : H →* c.Oˣ := (Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ).comp H.subtype
  let ρ : H →* GL (Fin 3) (PadicAlgCl l) := (glPart D.η r h.mult).comp i
  let ρ₀ : H →* GL (Fin 3) (PadicAlgCl l) := (glPart D.η (r0 D.η D.ΨQ) (hasSimilitudeFactor_diagonalRepr _ _)).comp i
  have hkill : ∀ g : H, g ∈ χ.ker → (g : G ℚ Ω) ∈ D.Kill := fun g hg => (hK g).2 ⟨A, hA, g.2.1, g.2.2, hg⟩
  have hρ : χ.ker ≤ ρ.ker := fun g hg => by
    show (r g).left = 1
    rw [h.kill g (hkill g hg)]; rfl
  have hρ₀ : χ.ker ≤ ρ₀.ker := fun g hg => by
    show tor _ (D.ΨQ g) = 1
    have h1 : tor _ (D.Ψ g) = 1 := congrArg SemidirectProduct.left (hk0 g (hkill g hg))
    have h2 : D.Ψ g = 1 := by
      have : (tor _ (D.Ψ g) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) c.O) 0 0 = 1 := by rw [h1]; simp
      rw [tor_val] at this
      apply Units.ext
      simpa [wt1] using this
    have h3 : D.ΨQ g = 1 := by
      unfold Dat.ΨQ
      rw [MonoidHom.comp_apply, h2, map_one]
    rw [h3, map_one]
  have hfin : Finite χ.range := by
    have : χ.range ≤ ψ.toUnitHom.range := by
      rintro _ ⟨g, rfl⟩
      exact ⟨_, rfl⟩
    have h2 : Finite ψ.toUnitHom.range := Finite.of_surjective _ ψ.toUnitHom.rangeRestrict_surjective
    exact Finite.of_injective _ (Subgroup.inclusion_injective this)
  have hfinQ : Finite (H ⧸ χ.ker) := Finite.of_equiv _ (QuotientGroup.quotientKerEquivRange χ).symm.toEquiv
  letI : Fintype (H ⧸ χ.ker) := Fintype.ofFinite _
  have hpow : ∀ x : H ⧸ χ.ker, x ^ orderOf ψ = 1 := fun x => by
    obtain ⟨g, rfl⟩ := QuotientGroup.mk_surjective x
    rw [← QuotientGroup.mk_pow, QuotientGroup.eq_one_iff, MonoidHom.mem_ker, map_pow]
    apply Units.ext
    show ((ψ.toUnitHom (Chr.cls ℚ Ω N g) ^ orderOf ψ : c.Oˣ) : c.O) = 1
    rw [Units.val_pow_eq_pow_val, MulChar.coe_toUnitHom, ← MulChar.pow_apply_coe, pow_orderOf_eq_one, MulChar.one_apply_coe]
  have hnd : ¬ l ∣ Fintype.card (H ⧸ χ.ker) := fun hd => by
    obtain ⟨x, hx⟩ := exists_prime_orderOf_dvd_card l hd
    exact D.gd.ord (hx.symm.dvd.trans (orderOf_dvd_of_pow_eq_one (hpow x)))
  have hnorm : ‖(Fintype.card (H ⧸ χ.ker) : PadicAlgCl l)‖ = 1 := by
    rw [← map_natCast (algebraMap ℚ_[l] (PadicAlgCl l)), norm_algebraMap']
    exact Padic.norm_natCast_eq_one_iff.2 ((Nat.Prime.coprime_iff_not_dvd hl.out).2 hnd)
  obtain ⟨M, hM⟩ := conj_of_close hnorm (QuotientGroup.lift _ ρ hρ) (QuotientGroup.lift _ ρ₀ hρ₀)
    (fun x a b => by
      obtain ⟨g, rfl⟩ := QuotientGroup.mk_surjective x
      exact h.int g a b)
    (fun x a b => by
      obtain ⟨g, rfl⟩ := QuotientGroup.mk_surjective x
      exact h.red g a b)
  exact ⟨M, fun g hg hη => hM (QuotientGroup.mk (⟨g, hg, hη⟩ : H))⟩


[OCH_SRC_INERTIA_ELL] All ell-place ordered inertia conditions and potential crystallinity
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/UnitaryThreeGroups/PtIneL.lean#L1-L128
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.UnitaryThreeGroups.KatoUse
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.UnitaryThreeGroups.DatLoc
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.PadicHodgeTheory.CommutingUpperTriangularDistinct
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.GaloisLibrary.SelmerTransport.DvrFacts

open NumberField IsDedekindDomain Formalism.ConjugateSelfDual ReciprocityTheory.Gal Formalism.MainNotions.N Formalism.BasicNotions Formalism.BasicNotions.Loc GlobalTheory.GalOfCusp Matrix IsLocalRing
open UpperTheory.V UpperTheory.V.Base UpperTheory.V.HV UpperTheory.V.Alg UpperTheory.V.U3

private theorem up_iff {R : Type} [CommRing R] (X : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) R) :
    (∀ a b, wt a b < 0 → X a b = 0) ↔ X.BlockTriangular id := by
  refine forall_congr' fun a => forall_congr' fun b => imp_congr_left ?_
  show _ ↔ (b : ℕ) < a
  simp only [wt, wt1]
  omega

private theorem theta_entry {R : Type} [CommRing R] (u : GL (Fin 3) R) (a b : Fin 3) :
    (theta u : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) R) a b = ((u⁻¹ : GL (Fin 3) R) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) R) b.rev a.rev := by
  rw [theta_val]
  generalize ((u⁻¹ : GL (Fin 3) R) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) R) = v
  fin_cases a <;> fin_cases b <;> simp [Matrix.mul_apply, Fin.sum_univ_three, Jmat, Fin.rev]

private theorem sgn_up {R : Type} [Field R] (s : ℤˣ) (u : GL (Fin 3) R)
    (hu : ∀ a b, wt a b < 0 → (u : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) R) a b = 0) :
    (∀ a b, wt a b < 0 → (sgnAct 3 R s u : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) R) a b = 0) ∧
      ∀ t : Rˣ, (∀ a, (u : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) R) a a = ((t ^ wt1 a : Rˣ) : R)) →
        ∀ a, (sgnAct 3 R s u : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) R) a a = ((t ^ wt1 a : Rˣ) : R) := by
  rcases Int.units_eq_one_or s with rfl | rfl
  · rw [sgnAct_one]
    exact ⟨hu, fun _ h => h⟩
  · rw [sgnAct_neg_one]
    have hv : ∀ a b, wt a b < 0 → ((u⁻¹ : GL (Fin 3) R) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) R) a b = 0 := by
      rw [up_iff] at hu ⊢
      letI := u.invertible
      rw [Matrix.coe_units_inv]
      exact Matrix.blockTriangular_inv_of_blockTriangular hu
    refine ⟨fun a b hab => ?_, fun t ht a => ?_⟩
    · rw [theta_entry]
      refine hv _ _ ?_
      have : wt b.rev a.rev = wt a b := by fin_cases a <;> fin_cases b <;> rfl
      rwa [this]
    · rw [theta_entry]
      have hd : ∀ i, ((u⁻¹ : GL (Fin 3) R) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) R) i i *
          (u : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) R) i i = 1 := by
        intro i
        have e := congrFun (congrFun (Units.inv_mul u) i) i
        have u10 := hu 1 0 (by decide)
        have u20 := hu 2 0 (by decide)
        have u21 := hu 2 1 (by decide)
        have v10 := hv 1 0 (by decide)
        have v20 := hv 2 0 (by decide)
        have v21 := hv 2 1 (by decide)
        generalize ((u⁻¹ : GL (Fin 3) R) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) R) = v at *
        fin_cases i <;> simpa [Matrix.mul_apply, Fin.sum_univ_three, u10, u20, u21, v10, v20, v21] using e
      have e := hd a.rev
      rw [ht] at e
      have w : wt1 a.rev = -wt1 a := by fin_cases a <;> rfl
      rw [w, _root_.zpow_neg] at e
      exact Units.mul_inv_eq_one.1 e

private theorem move {G R : Type} [Group G] [CommRing R] (r : G →* GG 3 R) (h : GL (Fin 3) R) (g : G) :
    ∃ M : GL (Fin 3) R, ∀ s, (r (g⁻¹ * s * g)).right = 1 →
      (conjBy M r s).left = sgnAct 3 R (r g).right (conjBy h r (g⁻¹ * s * g)).left := by
  let w : GG 3 R := SemidirectProduct.inr (r g).right
  have hM : GG.ofGL (w * GG.ofGL h * (r g)⁻¹).left = w * GG.ofGL h * (r g)⁻¹ :=
    SemidirectProduct.ext rfl (by simp [w])
  refine ⟨(w * GG.ofGL h * (r g)⁻¹).left, fun s hs => ?_⟩
  have e : conjBy (w * GG.ofGL h * (r g)⁻¹).left r s = w * conjBy h r (g⁻¹ * s * g) * w⁻¹ := by
    show GG.ofGL _ * r s * (GG.ofGL _)⁻¹ = w * (GG.ofGL h * r (g⁻¹ * s * g) * (GG.ofGL h)⁻¹) * w⁻¹
    rw [hM, map_mul, map_mul, map_inv]
    group
  rw [e]
  simp [w]

private theorem charpoly_conj {G R : Type} [Group G] [CommRing R] (r : G →* GG 3 R) (M : GL (Fin 3) R) (s : G)
    (hs : (r s).right = 1) : (conjBy M r s).mat.charpoly = (r s).mat.charpoly := by
  have : (conjBy M r s).left = M * (r s).left * M⁻¹ := by simp [conjBy, hs]
  simp only [GG.mat, this, Units.val_mul]
  rw [Matrix.coe_units_inv, Matrix.charpoly_units_conj]

theorem UpperTheory.V.U3.pt_inertia_above :
    CohInp → ∀ {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] {K : Type} [Field K] [NumberField K] [Algebra K Ω]
      {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {c : Coef l} {N : ℕ} [NeZero N] {e : ℕ} {ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O N} (D : Dat Ω K c e ψ) {U : D.ULoc} {g₁ : G ℚ Ω}, D.Marked g₁ → ∀ {r : G ℚ Ω →* GG 3 (PadicAlgCl l)} (h : D.IsPt U g₁ r),
      ∀ A : ValuationSubring Ω, A.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 →
        (∀ g ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) A, (r g).mat.charpoly = (r0 D.η D.ΨQ g).mat.charpoly) ∧
        ∃ M : GL (Fin 3) (PadicAlgCl l), (∀ g ∈ Dec (F := ℚ) A, D.η g = 1 → ∀ a b, wt a b < 0 → (conjBy M r g).mat a b = 0) ∧
          ∀ g ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) A, ∀ a, (conjBy M r g).mat a a = (r0 D.η D.ΨQ g).mat a a := by
  intro hI Ω _ _ _ K _ _ _ l _ c N _ e ψ D U g₁ _ r h A hA
  obtain ⟨A₀, -, hD, hIn, -, -, -, hc⟩ := dat_loc D
  obtain ⟨x, hxD, hxI⟩ := hc A hA
  obtain ⟨f, -, hf1, hf2⟩ := h.loc
  obtain ⟨⟨b, hb⟩, -⟩ := @Loc.lemma2b hI.loc l _ c.O _ _ c.dvr _ _ c.fin
    (UpperTheory.V.Base.dvrFacts l c.O).2.2.2.1 D.loc U (PadicAlgCl l) _ _ _ (r.comp D.ι) ⟨f, hf2, hf1⟩
  obtain ⟨M, hM⟩ := move r b⁻¹ x
  have hη : ∀ s, D.η (x⁻¹ * s * x) = D.η s := fun s => by
    simp only [map_mul, map_inv, inv_mul_cancel_comm]
  have hΨ : ∀ s, D.ΨQ (x⁻¹ * s * x) = D.ΨQ s := fun s => by
    simp only [map_mul, map_inv, inv_mul_cancel_comm]
  have key : ∀ s σ, D.ι σ = x⁻¹ * s * x → D.η s = 1 → (∀ a a', wt a a' < 0 → (conjBy M r s).mat a a' = 0) ∧
      (σ ∈ Loc.Iq l → ∀ a, (conjBy M r s).mat a a = (r0 D.η D.ΨQ s).mat a a) := by
    intro s σ hσ hs
    have h1 : D.η (D.ι σ) = 1 := by rw [hσ, hη, hs]
    have e1 := hM s (by rw [h.mult, hη, hs])
    rw [← hσ] at e1
    obtain ⟨k1, k2⟩ := sgn_up (r x).right (conjBy b⁻¹ r (D.ι σ)).left (hb.tri σ h1)
    rw [← e1] at k1 k2
    refine ⟨k1, fun hσI a => ?_⟩
    rw [show (r0 D.η D.ΨQ s).mat a a = ((D.ΨQ s ^ wt1 a : (PadicAlgCl l)ˣ) : PadicAlgCl l) by
      simp only [GG.mat, unitaryThreeReflection_left, tor_val, Matrix.diagonal_apply_eq]]
    refine k2 (D.ΨQ s) (fun a => (hb.ine σ hσI h1 a).trans ?_) a
    rw [← hΨ, ← hσ]
    exact congrArg Units.val (map_zpow (Units.map (algebraMap c.O (PadicAlgCl l)).toMonoidHom) (D.Ψ (D.ι σ)) (wt1 a))
  have kI : ∀ s ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) A, D.η s = 1 ∧ (∀ a a', wt a a' < 0 → (conjBy M r s).mat a a' = 0) ∧
      ∀ a, (conjBy M r s).mat a a = (r0 D.η D.ΨQ s).mat a a := by
    intro s hs
    have := (hxI s).1 hs
    rw [← hIn] at this
    obtain ⟨σ, hσI, hσ⟩ := this
    have h1 : D.η s = 1 := by rw [← hη, ← hσ]; exact D.η_unr σ hσI
    exact ⟨h1, (key s σ hσ h1).1, (key s σ hσ h1).2 hσI⟩
  refine ⟨fun s hs => ?_, M, fun s hs h1 => ?_, fun s hs => (kI s hs).2.2⟩
  · obtain ⟨h1, h2, h3⟩ := kI s hs
    rw [← charpoly_conj r M s ((h.mult s).trans h1), Matrix.charpoly_of_upperTriangular _ ((up_iff _).1 h2),
      Matrix.charpoly_of_upperTriangular (r0 D.η D.ΨQ s).mat (Matrix.blockTriangular_diagonal _)]
    simp only [h3]
  · have := (hxD s).1 hs
    rw [← hD] at this
    obtain ⟨σ, hσ⟩ := this
    exact (key s σ hσ h1).1


[OCH_SRC_TYPEI_CYCLO] Full infinite-cyclotomic-kernel irreducibility for the original type-I point
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/UnitaryThreeGroups/TypeIIrrCyc.lean#L1-L103
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.UnitaryThreeGroups.KatoUse
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.UnitaryThreeGroups.Clifford
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.UnitaryThreeGroups.PtIneL
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.UnitaryThreeGroups.DatFacts
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.CharacterLevelBounds.PlacesQ
import Development.AbelianVarietyTheory.Proofs.InertiaEigenvalues.CyclotomicCharInertiaSurjective

open NumberField IsDedekindDomain Formalism.ConjugateSelfDual ReciprocityTheory.Gal Formalism.MainNotions.N Formalism.BasicNotions Formalism.BasicNotions.Loc GlobalTheory.GalOfCusp Matrix IsLocalRing
open UpperTheory.V UpperTheory.V.Base UpperTheory.V.HV UpperTheory.V.Alg UpperTheory.V.U3

private theorem twist_triv {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] {K : Type} [Field K] [NumberField K]
    [Algebra K Ω] {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {c : Coef l} {N : ℕ} [NeZero N] {e : ℕ} {ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O N}
    (D : Dat Ω K c e ψ) (A : ValuationSubring Ω) (hA : A.valuation (l : Ω) < 1) {C : Type} [Group C]
    (φ : G K Ω →* C) (hφ : (cycBar K Ω l).ker ≤ φ.ker) (m : ℕ) (hm : m ≠ 0)
    (h : ∀ s : G K Ω, galEmb ℚ K Ω s ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) A → φ s ≠ 1 → D.ΨQ (galEmb ℚ K Ω s) ^ m = 1) (g : G K Ω) :
    φ g = 1 := by
  have hl : l.Prime := Fact.out
  have hI : Ine (F := ℚ) A ≤ (galEmb ℚ K Ω).range := by
    rw [range_galEmb, ← D.η_ker]
    exact ((dat_facts D).2.2.2.2.2.2.2.2.2.2 A hA).1
  have hsur : ∀ u : ℤ_[l]ˣ, ∃ s : G K Ω, galEmb ℚ K Ω s ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) A ∧ cycZ K Ω l s = u := fun u => by
    obtain ⟨σ, hσ, hu⟩ := GlobalTheory.CycInertia.cycZ_Ine_surjective Ω l A hA u
    obtain ⟨s, rfl⟩ := hI hσ
    exact ⟨s, hσ, hu⟩
  by_contra hg
  obtain ⟨s, hs, hsg⟩ := hsur (cycZ K Ω l g)
  have hs1 : φ s ≠ 1 := by
    intro h1
    have : φ (g * s⁻¹) = 1 := hφ (by
      rw [MonoidHom.mem_ker, cycBar, MonoidHom.comp_apply, map_mul, map_inv, hsg, mul_inv_cancel, map_one])
    rw [map_mul, map_inv, h1, inv_one, mul_one] at this
    exact hg this
  have hu : IsUnit ((1 + l : ℕ) : ℤ_[l]) := by
    rw [PadicInt.isUnit_iff, PadicInt.norm_natCast_eq_one_iff]
    simp
  obtain ⟨t, ht, htu⟩ := hsur hu.unit
  have ht1 : D.ΨQ (galEmb ℚ K Ω t) ^ m = 1 := by
    by_cases h1 : φ t = 1
    · have h2 := h (s * t) (by rw [map_mul]; exact mul_mem hs ht) (by rwa [map_mul, h1, mul_one])
      rwa [map_mul, map_mul, mul_pow, h s hs hs1, one_mul] at h2
    · exact h t ht h1
  have hn : Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ (galEmb ℚ K Ω t) ^ Nat.card (ZMod N)ˣ = 1 := by
    rw [Chr.dirG, MonoidHom.comp_apply, ← map_pow, pow_card_eq_one', map_one]
  have hc : (chiO ℚ Ω l c.O (galEmb ℚ K Ω t) : c.O) = ((1 + l : ℕ) : c.O) := by
    have : cycZ ℚ Ω l (galEmb ℚ K Ω t) = hu.unit := htu
    rw [chiO, MonoidHom.comp_apply, this]
    simp
  have h3 : ((D.Ψ (galEmb ℚ K Ω t) ^ Nat.card (ZMod N)ˣ : c.Oˣ) : c.O) = ((1 + l : ℕ) : c.O) ^ (e * Nat.card (ZMod N)ˣ) := by
    rw [Dat.Ψ, Chr.Psi, MonoidHom.mul_apply, mul_pow, hn, mul_one, zpow_natCast, MonoidHom.pow_apply, ← pow_mul,
      Units.val_pow_eq_pow_val, hc]
  have h4 : ((D.ΨQ (galEmb ℚ K Ω t) ^ Nat.card (ZMod N)ˣ : (PadicAlgCl l)ˣ) : PadicAlgCl l) =
      ((1 + l : ℕ) : PadicAlgCl l) ^ (e * Nat.card (ZMod N)ˣ) := by
    have := congrArg (algebraMap c.O (PadicAlgCl l)) h3
    rw [map_pow, map_natCast] at this
    rw [← this, Dat.ΨQ, MonoidHom.comp_apply, ← map_pow]
    rfl
  have h5 : ((1 + l : ℕ) : PadicAlgCl l) ^ (e * Nat.card (ZMod N)ˣ * m) = 1 := by
    rw [pow_mul, ← h4, ← Units.val_pow_eq_pow_val, ← pow_mul, mul_comm, pow_mul, ht1, one_pow, Units.val_one]
  have h6 : (1 + l) ^ (e * Nat.card (ZMod N)ˣ * m) = 1 := by exact_mod_cast h5
  have he := D.gd.e_pos
  have hN : 0 < Nat.card (ZMod N)ˣ := Nat.card_pos
  rcases Nat.pow_eq_one.1 h6 with h7 | h7
  · exact hl.ne_zero (by omega)
  · exact (mul_ne_zero (mul_ne_zero (by omega) hN.ne') hm) h7

theorem UpperTheory.V.U3.typeI_irr_cyc :
    CohInp → ∀ {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] {K : Type} [Field K] [NumberField K] [Algebra K Ω]
      {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {c : Coef l} {N : ℕ} [NeZero N] {e : ℕ} {ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O N} (D : Dat Ω K c e ψ) {U : D.ULoc} {g₁ : G ℚ Ω}, D.Marked g₁ → ∀ {r : G ℚ Ω →* GG 3 (PadicAlgCl l)} (h : D.IsPt U g₁ r),
      TypeI D.η r → Af.IsIrr ((D.rhoK r h.mult).comp (cycBar K Ω l).ker.subtype) := by
  intro hC Ω _ _ _ K _ _ _ l _ c N _ e ψ D U g₁ hg r h hI
  have hirr : MatIrr ⊤ fun g => (D.rhoK r h.mult g : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) (PadicAlgCl l)) := by
    intro W hW
    refine hI W fun g hg v hv => ?_
    rw [D.η_ker, ← range_galEmb] at hg
    obtain ⟨s, rfl⟩ := hg
    exact hW s trivial v hv
  have key : MatIrr (cycBar K Ω l).ker fun g => (D.rhoK r h.mult g : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) (PadicAlgCl l)) := by
    by_contra hred
    obtain ⟨φ, hφ1, hker, -, M, hM⟩ := exists_selfTwist (D.rhoK r h.mult) (cycBar K Ω l).ker
      (fun a b => by simp [MonoidHom.mem_ker, mul_comm (cycBar K Ω l a)]) hirr hred
    obtain ⟨A, hA⟩ := (Chr.placesQ Ω).1 l Fact.out
    refine hφ1 (MonoidHom.ext (twist_triv D A hA φ hker 3 (by norm_num) fun s hs h1 => ?_))
    have hch := (pt_inertia_above hC D hg h A hA).1 _ hs
    have htr : (D.rhoK r h.mult s : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) (PadicAlgCl l)).trace =
        (r0 D.η D.ΨQ (galEmb ℚ K Ω s)).mat.trace := by
      rw [Matrix.trace_eq_neg_charpoly_coeff, Matrix.trace_eq_neg_charpoly_coeff, ← hch]
      rfl
    have h2 := congrArg (fun X => (X * ((M⁻¹ : GL (Fin 3) (PadicAlgCl l)) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) (PadicAlgCl l))).trace)
      (hM s trivial)
    simp only [Matrix.mul_smul, Matrix.smul_mul, Matrix.trace_smul] at h2
    rw [Matrix.trace_mul_cycle, Units.inv_mul, Matrix.one_mul, Matrix.mul_assoc, Units.mul_inv, Matrix.mul_one, htr,
      smul_eq_mul] at h2
    have h3 : (r0 D.η D.ΨQ (galEmb ℚ K Ω s)).mat.trace = 0 := by
      by_contra h0
      exact h1 (Units.ext (mul_right_cancel₀ h0 (h2.trans (one_mul _).symm)))
    rw [GG.mat, unitaryThreeReflection_left, tor_val, Matrix.trace_diagonal, Fin.sum_univ_three] at h3
    generalize D.ΨQ (galEmb ℚ K Ω s) = a at h3 ⊢
    have h4 : (a : PadicAlgCl l) + 1 + (a : PadicAlgCl l)⁻¹ = 0 := by simpa [wt1] using h3
    have ha : (a : PadicAlgCl l) * (a : PadicAlgCl l)⁻¹ = 1 := mul_inv_cancel₀ a.ne_zero
    apply Units.ext
    rw [Units.val_pow_eq_pow_val, Units.val_one]
    linear_combination (a : PadicAlgCl l) * (a - 1) * h4 - ((a : PadicAlgCl l) - 1) * ha
  refine ⟨top_ne_bot, fun _ _ _ => trivial, fun W _ hW => key W fun g hg v hv => hW ⟨g, hg⟩ v hv⟩


[OCH_SRC_CLIFFORD] Elementary normal-subgroup semisimplicity and abelian-quotient self-twist producer
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/UnitaryThreeGroups/Clifford.lean#L1-L237
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.UnitaryThreeGroups.BaseRepGL3NormalisedOfPsiEqOne

open Matrix UpperTheory.V.U3

private theorem common_eigen {κ E ι : Type*} [Field κ] [IsAlgClosed κ] [AddCommGroup E] [Module κ E]
    [FiniteDimensional κ E] (f : ι → Module.End κ E) (S : Submodule κ E) (hS : S ≠ ⊥)
    (hst : ∀ i, ∀ v ∈ S, f i v ∈ S) (hc : ∀ i j, ∀ v ∈ S, f i (f j v) = f j (f i v)) :
    ∃ v ∈ S, v ≠ 0 ∧ ∀ i, ∃ c : κ, f i v = c • v := by
  obtain ⟨F, ⟨hF0, hFS, hFst⟩, hmin⟩ := (wellFounded_lt (α := Submodule κ E)).has_min
    {F | F ≠ ⊥ ∧ F ≤ S ∧ ∀ i, ∀ v ∈ F, f i v ∈ F} ⟨S, hS, le_rfl, hst⟩
  obtain ⟨v, hvF, hv0⟩ := Submodule.exists_mem_ne_zero_of_ne_bot hF0
  refine ⟨v, hFS hvF, hv0, fun i => ?_⟩
  haveI : Nontrivial F := Submodule.nontrivial_iff_ne_bot.mpr hF0
  obtain ⟨c, hc'⟩ := Module.End.exists_eigenvalue ((f i).restrict (hFst i) : Module.End κ F)
  obtain ⟨w, hw⟩ := hc'.exists_hasEigenvector
  have hw1 : f i w = c • (w : E) := congrArg Subtype.val hw.apply_eq_smul
  have hw0 : (w : E) ≠ 0 := fun h => hw.2 (Subtype.ext h)
  have key : ¬ F ⊓ Module.End.eigenspace (f i) c < F := by
    refine hmin _ ⟨?_, inf_le_left.trans hFS, ?_⟩
    · exact (Submodule.ne_bot_iff _).mpr ⟨w, ⟨w.2, Module.End.mem_eigenspace_iff.mpr hw1⟩, hw0⟩
    · rintro j u ⟨huF, hu⟩
      refine ⟨hFst j u huF, Module.End.mem_eigenspace_iff.mpr ?_⟩
      rw [hc i j u (hFS huF), Module.End.mem_eigenspace_iff.mp hu, map_smul]
  have h2 : F ⊓ Module.End.eigenspace (f i) c = F := (eq_or_lt_of_le inf_le_left).resolve_right key
  have : v ∈ F ⊓ Module.End.eigenspace (f i) c := by rw [h2]; exact hvF
  exact ⟨c, Module.End.mem_eigenspace_iff.mp this.2⟩

private theorem exists_compl {Γ κ V : Type*} [Group Γ] [Field κ] [AddCommGroup V] [Module κ V]
    [FiniteDimensional κ V] (σ : Γ →* Module.End κ V) (H : Subgroup Γ) [H.Normal]
    (hirr : ∀ W : Submodule κ V, (∀ g, ∀ v ∈ W, σ g v ∈ W) → W = ⊥ ∨ W = ⊤)
    (W₀ : Submodule κ V) (h0 : ∀ g ∈ H, ∀ v ∈ W₀, σ g v ∈ W₀) (hb : W₀ ≠ ⊥) (ht : W₀ ≠ ⊤) :
    ∃ U W : Submodule κ V, IsCompl U W ∧ U ≠ ⊥ ∧ W ≠ ⊥ ∧ (∀ g ∈ H, ∀ v ∈ U, σ g v ∈ U) ∧
      (∀ g ∈ H, ∀ v ∈ W, σ g v ∈ W) := by
  have hmul : ∀ a b v, σ a (σ b v) = σ (a * b) v := fun a b v => by rw [map_mul]; rfl
  have hcom : ∀ (X : Submodule κ V) (g : Γ), (∀ h ∈ H, ∀ v ∈ X, σ h v ∈ X) →
      ∀ h ∈ H, ∀ v ∈ X.comap (σ g), σ h v ∈ X.comap (σ g) := by
    intro X g hX h hh v hv
    have := hX (g * h * g⁻¹) (Subgroup.Normal.conj_mem ‹_› h hh g) _ hv
    rw [hmul, inv_mul_cancel_right, ← hmul] at this
    exact this
  obtain ⟨W, ⟨hW0, hWst⟩, hmin⟩ := (wellFounded_lt (α := Submodule κ V)).has_min
    {F | F ≠ ⊥ ∧ ∀ g ∈ H, ∀ v ∈ F, σ g v ∈ F} ⟨W₀, hb, h0⟩
  obtain ⟨U, ⟨hU0, hUt, hUst⟩, hmax⟩ := (wellFounded_gt (α := Submodule κ V)).has_min
    {F | F ≠ ⊥ ∧ F ≠ ⊤ ∧ ∀ g ∈ H, ∀ v ∈ F, σ g v ∈ F} ⟨W₀, hb, ht, h0⟩
  have hex : ∃ g, ¬ W ≤ U.comap (σ g) := by
    by_contra hne
    push_neg at hne
    rcases hirr (⨅ g, U.comap (σ g)) (fun g v hv => by
      rw [Submodule.mem_iInf] at hv ⊢
      intro k
      have := hv (k * g)
      rw [Submodule.mem_comap, ← hmul] at this
      exact this) with h | h
    · exact hW0 (le_bot_iff.mp (h ▸ le_iInf hne))
    · apply hUt
      rw [eq_top_iff]
      intro v _
      have : v ∈ ⨅ g, U.comap (σ g) := by rw [h]; trivial
      simpa using (Submodule.mem_iInf _).mp this 1
  obtain ⟨g, hg⟩ := hex
  have hU' := hcom U g hUst
  have hback : ∀ u ∈ U, σ g⁻¹ u ∈ U.comap (σ g) := fun u hu => by
    rw [Submodule.mem_comap, hmul]; simpa using hu
  refine ⟨U.comap (σ g), W, ⟨?_, ?_⟩, ?_, hW0, hU', hWst⟩
  · rw [disjoint_iff]
    by_contra hne
    refine hmin _ ⟨hne, fun h hh v hv => ⟨hU' h hh v hv.1, hWst h hh v hv.2⟩⟩ ?_
    exact lt_of_le_of_ne inf_le_right fun h => hg (inf_eq_right.mp h)
  · rw [codisjoint_iff]
    by_contra hne
    have hX : ∀ h ∈ H, ∀ v ∈ U.comap (σ g) ⊔ W, σ h v ∈ U.comap (σ g) ⊔ W := by
      intro h hh v hv
      obtain ⟨a, ha, b, hb, rfl⟩ := Submodule.mem_sup.mp hv
      rw [map_add]
      exact Submodule.add_mem_sup (hU' h hh a ha) (hWst h hh b hb)
    have hle : U ≤ (U.comap (σ g) ⊔ W).comap (σ g⁻¹) := fun u hu =>
      Submodule.mem_sup_left (hback u hu)
    refine hmax ((U.comap (σ g) ⊔ W).comap (σ g⁻¹)) ⟨?_, ?_, hcom _ _ hX⟩ ?_
    · exact fun h => hU0 (le_bot_iff.mp (h ▸ hle))
    · intro h
      apply hne
      rw [eq_top_iff]
      intro v _
      have : σ g v ∈ (U.comap (σ g) ⊔ W).comap (σ g⁻¹) := by rw [h]; trivial
      rw [Submodule.mem_comap, hmul] at this
      simpa using this
    · refine lt_of_le_of_ne hle fun h => hg fun w hw => ?_
      rw [Submodule.mem_comap, h, Submodule.mem_comap, hmul]
      simpa using Submodule.mem_sup_right hw
  · obtain ⟨u, hu, hu0⟩ := Submodule.exists_mem_ne_zero_of_ne_bot hU0
    refine (Submodule.ne_bot_iff _).mpr ⟨_, hback u hu, fun h => hu0 ?_⟩
    have := congrArg (σ g) h
    rw [hmul] at this
    simpa using this

private theorem exists_traceless {Γ κ V : Type*} [Group Γ] [Field κ] [CharZero κ] [AddCommGroup V]
    [Module κ V] [FiniteDimensional κ V] (σ : Γ →* Module.End κ V) (H : Subgroup Γ)
    (U W : Submodule κ V) (hc : IsCompl U W) (hU0 : U ≠ ⊥) (hW0 : W ≠ ⊥)
    (hU : ∀ g ∈ H, ∀ v ∈ U, σ g v ∈ U) (hW : ∀ g ∈ H, ∀ v ∈ W, σ g v ∈ W) :
    ∃ P : Module.End κ V, P ≠ 0 ∧ LinearMap.trace κ V P = 0 ∧ ∀ h ∈ H, σ h * P = P * σ h := by
  let Q : Module.End κ V := U.subtype ∘ₗ U.linearProjOfIsCompl W hc
  have hQU : ∀ u ∈ U, Q u = u := fun u hu => by
    exact congrArg Subtype.val (Submodule.linearProjOfIsCompl_apply_left hc ⟨u, hu⟩)
  have hQW : ∀ u ∈ W, Q u = 0 := fun u hu => by
    exact congrArg Subtype.val (Submodule.linearProjOfIsCompl_apply_right hc ⟨u, hu⟩)
  have hQ : ∀ h ∈ H, σ h * Q = Q * σ h := by
    intro h hh
    ext v
    have : v ∈ U ⊔ W := by rw [hc.sup_eq_top]; trivial
    obtain ⟨a, ha, b, hb, rfl⟩ := Submodule.mem_sup.mp this
    simp [hQU a ha, hQW b hb, hQU _ (hU h hh a ha), hQW _ (hW h hh b hb)]
  obtain ⟨u, hu, hu0⟩ := Submodule.exists_mem_ne_zero_of_ne_bot hU0
  obtain ⟨w, hw, hw0⟩ := Submodule.exists_mem_ne_zero_of_ne_bot hW0
  haveI : Nontrivial V := ⟨u, 0, hu0⟩
  have hn : (Module.finrank κ V : κ) ≠ 0 := Nat.cast_ne_zero.mpr Module.finrank_pos.ne'
  refine ⟨(Module.finrank κ V : κ) • Q - LinearMap.trace κ V Q • 1, fun h => ?_, ?_, ?_⟩
  · have h1 := congrArg (fun T : Module.End κ V => T w) h
    have h2 := congrArg (fun T : Module.End κ V => T u) h
    simp [hQW w hw, hw0] at h1
    simp [hQU u hu, h1, hu0] at h2
    exact hn (by simp [h2])
  · simp [mul_comm]
  · intro h hh
    simp [mul_sub, sub_mul, hQ h hh]

private theorem exists_twist {Γ κ V : Type*} [Group Γ] [Field κ] [IsAlgClosed κ] [CharZero κ]
    [AddCommGroup V] [Module κ V] [FiniteDimensional κ V] (σ : Γ →* Module.End κ V)
    (H : Subgroup Γ) [H.Normal] (hab : ∀ a b : Γ, a * b * a⁻¹ * b⁻¹ ∈ H)
    (P : Module.End κ V) (hP0 : P ≠ 0) (hPt : LinearMap.trace κ V P = 0)
    (hP : ∀ h ∈ H, σ h * P = P * σ h) :
    ∃ T : Module.End κ V, T ≠ 0 ∧ LinearMap.trace κ V T = 0 ∧ (∀ h ∈ H, σ h * T = T * σ h) ∧
      ∀ g, ∃ c : κ, σ g * T = c • (T * σ g) := by
  let S : Submodule κ (Module.End κ V) :=
    { carrier := {T | LinearMap.trace κ V T = 0 ∧ ∀ h ∈ H, σ h * T = T * σ h}
      add_mem' := fun ha hb => ⟨by simp [ha.1, hb.1], fun h hh => by
        rw [mul_add, add_mul, ha.2 h hh, hb.2 h hh]⟩
      zero_mem' := by simp
      smul_mem' := fun c a ha => ⟨by simp [ha.1], fun h hh => by simp [ha.2 h hh]⟩ }
  let f : Γ → Module.End κ (Module.End κ V) := fun g =>
    LinearMap.mulLeft κ (σ g) ∘ₗ LinearMap.mulRight κ (σ g⁻¹)
  have hf : ∀ g T, f g T = σ g * (T * σ g⁻¹) := fun _ _ => rfl
  have hff : ∀ a b T, f a (f b T) = f (a * b) T := fun a b T => by
    simp only [hf, _root_.mul_inv_rev, map_mul, mul_assoc]
  have hH : ∀ h ∈ H, ∀ T ∈ S, f h T = T := fun h hh T hT => by
    rw [hf, ← mul_assoc, hT.2 h hh, mul_assoc, ← map_mul]; simp
  have hst : ∀ g, ∀ T ∈ S, f g T ∈ S := by
    intro g T hT
    refine ⟨?_, fun h hh => ?_⟩
    · rw [hf, LinearMap.trace_mul_comm, mul_assoc, ← map_mul]; simpa using hT.1
    · have h1 := hT.2 (g⁻¹ * h * g) (by simpa using Subgroup.Normal.conj_mem ‹_› h hh g⁻¹)
      have e1 : σ h * σ g = σ g * σ (g⁻¹ * h * g) := by
        rw [← map_mul, ← map_mul]; congr 1; group
      have e2 : σ g⁻¹ * σ h = σ (g⁻¹ * h * g) * σ g⁻¹ := by
        rw [← map_mul, ← map_mul]; congr 1; group
      rw [hf, ← mul_assoc, e1, mul_assoc, ← mul_assoc _ T, h1, mul_assoc, ← e2]
      simp only [mul_assoc]
  obtain ⟨T, hT, hT0, hTc⟩ := common_eigen f S ((Submodule.ne_bot_iff _).mpr ⟨P, ⟨hPt, hP⟩, hP0⟩) hst
    (fun a b T hT => by
      rw [← hH _ (hab a b) _ (hst b _ (hst a T hT)), hff, hff, hff]
      congr 2; group)
  refine ⟨T, hT0, hT.1, hT.2, fun g => ?_⟩
  obtain ⟨c, hc⟩ := hTc g
  refine ⟨c, ?_⟩
  rw [← smul_mul_assoc, ← hc, hf, mul_assoc, mul_assoc, ← map_mul]; simp

theorem UpperTheory.V.U3.exists_selfTwist :
    ∀ {Γ κ : Type} [Group Γ] [Field κ] [IsAlgClosed κ] [CharZero κ] {m : ℕ} (ρ : Γ →* GL (Fin m) κ) (H : Subgroup Γ) [H.Normal],
      (∀ a b : Γ, a * b * a⁻¹ * b⁻¹ ∈ H) → MatIrr ⊤ (fun g => (ρ g : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) κ)) → ¬ MatIrr H (fun g => (ρ g : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) κ)) →
      ∃ φ : Γ →* κˣ, φ ≠ 1 ∧ H ≤ φ.ker ∧ φ ^ m = 1 ∧
        MatIso ⊤ (fun g => ((φ g : κˣ) : κ) • (ρ g : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) κ)) (fun g => (ρ g : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) κ)) := by
  intro Γ κ _ _ _ _ m ρ H _ hab hirr hred
  let σ : Γ →* Module.End κ (Fin m → κ) :=
    { toFun := fun g => Matrix.toLin' (ρ g : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) κ)
      map_one' := by simp [Module.End.one_eq_id]
      map_mul' := fun a b => by simp [Matrix.toLin'_mul, Module.End.mul_eq_comp] }
  have hσ : ∀ g v, σ g v = (ρ g : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) κ) *ᵥ v := fun g v => Matrix.toLin'_apply _ _
  have hirr' : ∀ W : Submodule κ (Fin m → κ), (∀ g, ∀ v ∈ W, σ g v ∈ W) → W = ⊥ ∨ W = ⊤ :=
    fun W hW => hirr W fun g _ v hv => hσ g v ▸ hW g v hv
  unfold MatIrr at hred
  push_neg at hred
  obtain ⟨W₀, h0, hb, ht⟩ := hred
  obtain ⟨U, W, hc, hU0, hW0, hU, hW⟩ := exists_compl σ H hirr' W₀
    (fun g hg v hv => by rw [hσ]; exact h0 g hg v hv) hb ht
  obtain ⟨P, hP0, hPt, hP⟩ := exists_traceless σ H U W hc hU0 hW0 hU hW
  obtain ⟨T, hT0, hTt, hTH, hT⟩ := exists_twist σ H hab P hP0 hPt hP
  choose c hc using hT
  have hunit : ∀ g, IsUnit (σ g) := fun g => (Group.isUnit g).map σ
  have hne : ∀ g, T * σ g ≠ 0 := fun g h => hT0 ((hunit g).mul_left_eq_zero.mp h)
  have hc0 : ∀ g, c g ≠ 0 := fun g h => hT0 ((hunit g).mul_right_eq_zero.mp (by rw [hc, h, zero_smul]))
  have huniq : ∀ g d, σ g * T = d • (T * σ g) → c g = d := fun g d h =>
    smul_left_injective κ (hne g) ((hc g).symm.trans h)
  let φ : Γ →* κˣ :=
    { toFun := fun g => Units.mk0 (c g) (hc0 g)
      map_one' := Units.ext (huniq 1 1 (by simp))
      map_mul' := fun a b => Units.ext (huniq _ _ (by
        rw [map_mul, mul_assoc, hc b, mul_smul_comm, ← mul_assoc, hc a, smul_mul_assoc, smul_smul,
          mul_assoc, mul_comm (c b)]
        rfl)) }
  have hφ : ∀ g, (φ g : κ) = c g := fun _ => rfl
  have hker : LinearMap.ker T = ⊥ := by
    refine (hirr' _ fun g v hv => ?_).resolve_right fun h => hT0 (LinearMap.ker_eq_top.mp h)
    have := congrArg (fun A : Module.End κ (Fin m → κ) => A v) (hc g)
    simp only [Module.End.mul_apply, LinearMap.smul_apply, LinearMap.mem_ker.mp hv, map_zero] at this
    exact (smul_eq_zero.mp this.symm).resolve_left (hc0 g)
  have hTu : IsUnit T := (LinearMap.isUnit_iff_ker_eq_bot T).mpr hker
  have hdet : (LinearMap.toMatrix' T).det ≠ 0 := by
    rw [LinearMap.det_toMatrix']
    exact (hTu.map LinearMap.det).ne_zero
  have hM : ∀ g, LinearMap.toMatrix' T * (c g • (ρ g : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) κ)) =
      (ρ g : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) κ) * LinearMap.toMatrix' T := fun g => by
    have := congrArg LinearMap.toMatrix' (hc g)
    rw [map_smul, LinearMap.toMatrix'_mul, LinearMap.toMatrix'_mul] at this
    simpa [σ] using this.symm
  refine ⟨φ, fun h1 => ?_, fun h hh => ?_, ?_, ⟨GeneralLinearGroup.mkOfDetNeZero _ hdet, fun g _ => hM g⟩⟩
  · haveI : Nontrivial (Fin m → κ) := by
      obtain ⟨u, -, hu0⟩ := Submodule.exists_mem_ne_zero_of_ne_bot hU0
      exact ⟨u, 0, hu0⟩
    obtain ⟨a, ha⟩ := Module.End.exists_eigenvalue T
    have htop : Module.End.eigenspace T a = ⊤ := by
      refine (hirr' _ fun g v hv => ?_).resolve_left ha
      have := congrArg (fun A : Module.End κ (Fin m → κ) => A v) (hc g)
      rw [Module.End.mem_eigenspace_iff] at hv ⊢
      simp only [Module.End.mul_apply, LinearMap.smul_apply, hv, map_smul, ← hφ, h1] at this
      simpa using this.symm
    have hTa : T = a • 1 := by
      refine LinearMap.ext fun v => ?_
      exact Module.End.mem_eigenspace_iff.mp (by rw [htop]; trivial)
    have hn : (Module.finrank κ (Fin m → κ) : κ) ≠ 0 := Nat.cast_ne_zero.mpr Module.finrank_pos.ne'
    rw [hTa, map_smul, LinearMap.trace_one, smul_eq_mul, mul_eq_zero] at hTt
    exact hT0 (by rw [hTa, hTt.resolve_right hn, zero_smul])
  · exact Units.ext (huniq h 1 (by rw [one_smul, hTH h hh]))
  · ext g
    have := congrArg Matrix.det (hM g)
    rw [Matrix.det_mul, Matrix.det_mul, Matrix.det_smul, mul_comm _ (LinearMap.toMatrix' T).det] at this
    have h2 := mul_left_cancel₀ hdet this
    have h3 : (ρ g : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) κ).det ≠ 0 := (ρ g).det_ne_zero
    simpa [hφ, h3] using h2


[OCH_SRC_BASE_RIGIDITY] Negative and zero matrix-weight global finite-cohomology vanishing
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/UnitaryThreeGroups/BlochKatoNonpositiveWeight.lean#L194-L414
private theorem neg_case (hP : CohInp) (θ χ : G ℚ Ω →* (PadicAlgCl l)ˣ) (hχ : IsOpen (χ.ker : Set (G ℚ Ω))) (e : ℕ)
    (he : 1 ≤ e) (hθc : Continuous fun g => (θ g : PadicAlgCl l))
    (hθ : ∀ g, (θ g : PadicAlgCl l) * χ g * algebraMap ℤ_[l] (PadicAlgCl l) (cycZ ℚ Ω l g : ℤ_[l]) ^ e = 1)
    (c : G ℚ Ω → PadicAlgCl l) (hc : c ∈ Z1 (Loc.chRep θ)) (hb : Af.IsBK l (Loc.chRep θ) c) :
    ∃ m, ∀ g, c g = θ g * m - m := by
  have hcoc : ∀ g h, c (g * h) = c g + θ g * c h := hc.2
  obtain ⟨CK, hK⟩ := hP.lemK
  obtain ⟨h1, j, hj, -, h2⟩ := bk_loc θ c hc hb
  obtain ⟨i1, i2, -, -, i5, -, -⟩ := Loc.iotaFacts j
  obtain ⟨K₀, hK₀, hψK⟩ := char_finext l (G ℚ Ω) χ hχ
  have hθK : ∀ g, (θ g : PadicAlgCl l) ∈ K₀ := fun g => by
    have h := hθ g
    rw [mul_assoc] at h
    rw [eq_inv_of_mul_eq_one_left h]
    refine inv_mem (mul_mem (hψK g) (pow_mem ?_ _))
    rw [IsScalarTower.algebraMap_apply ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] (PadicAlgCl l)]
    exact K₀.algebraMap_mem _
  obtain ⟨E, _, κ, c', hκ, hc', hf⟩ := desc _ (θ.comp (Loc.iota j)) K₀ (fun g => hθK _) _ hj
  haveI : IsModuleTopology ℚ_[l] E := isModuleTopologyOfFiniteDimensional
  have hw : Loc.HasWeight (MonoidHom.id (GQ l)) κ (-(e : ℤ)) := by
    refine ⟨χ.ker.comap (Loc.iota j), hχ.preimage i1.continuous, fun g hg _ => ?_⟩
    rw [_root_.zpow_neg, zpow_natCast]
    refine eq_inv_of_mul_eq_one_left (Units.ext (Subtype.ext ?_))
    have h3 := hθ (Loc.iota j g)
    have h4 : χ (Loc.iota j g) = 1 := hg
    have h5 : cycZ ℚ Ω l (Loc.iota j g) = Loc.cyc l l g := DFunLike.congr_fun (i5 l) g
    rw [h4, h5, Units.val_one, mul_one] at h3
    rw [Units.val_mul, MulMemClass.coe_mul, hκ, Units.val_pow_eq_pow_val, SubmonoidClass.coe_pow]
    exact h3
  have hz := (Loc.z1fChar hP.loc (MonoidHom.id (GQ l)) Topology.IsOpenEmbedding.id κ
    (continuous_induced_rng.2 ((hθc.comp i1.continuous).congr fun g => (hκ g).symm)) _ hw).1 (by omega)
  rw [hz.1, mem_coboundaries1] at hf
  obtain ⟨m, hm⟩ := hf
  have h3 : ∀ A : ValuationSubring Ω, A.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 → ∃ m, ∀ g ∈ Dec (F := ℚ) A, c g = θ g * m - m := by
    intro A hA
    obtain ⟨g, -, hg⟩ := h2 A hA
    refine cob_conj θ c hcoc _ _ g hg ⟨m, fun s hs => ?_⟩
    rw [← i2] at hs
    obtain ⟨σ, rfl⟩ := hs
    have h4 : c' σ = κ σ * m - m := hm σ
    have h5 := hc' σ
    rw [← h5, h4, AddSubgroupClass.coe_sub, MulMemClass.coe_mul, hκ]
    rfl
  refine X.soBar CK hK (GlobalTheory.PoitouTateConj.poitouTateDuality_of_normalised hP.pt) hP.loc.lfK hP.loc.ltK ℚ Ω l χ hχ e he
    (fun g => θ g) hθ c hc.1 hcoc (fun A => ?_) h3
  by_cases hA : A.valuation (l : Ω) < 1
  · obtain ⟨m, hm⟩ := h3 A hA
    exact ⟨m, fun g hg => hm g hg.1⟩
  · exact h1 A hA

private theorem zero_case (hP : CohInp) (K : Type) [Field K] [NumberField K] [Algebra K Ω]
    (θ : G ℚ Ω →* (PadicAlgCl l)ˣ) (hθ : galOf ℚ K Ω ≤ θ.ker)
    (hI : ∀ A : ValuationSubring Ω, A.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 → Ine (F := ℚ) A ≤ galOf ℚ K Ω)
    (c : G ℚ Ω → PadicAlgCl l) (hc : c ∈ Z1 (Loc.chRep θ)) (hb : Af.IsBK l (Loc.chRep θ) c) :
    ∀ g ∈ galOf ℚ K Ω, c g = 0 := by
  have hcoc : ∀ g h, c (g * h) = c g + θ g * c h := hc.2
  haveI : IsAlgClosure K Ω := ⟨IsAlgClosure.isAlgClosed ℚ, Algebra.IsAlgebraic.tower_top (K := ℚ) K⟩
  obtain ⟨g1, g2, -⟩ := galEmbTop ℚ K Ω
  obtain ⟨-, hIne, -, -⟩ := Kum.toGalDict ℚ Ω K _ le_rfl
  have hχ : IsOpen (θ.ker : Set (G ℚ Ω)) := Subgroup.isOpen_mono hθ g2
  have hθc : Continuous θ := continuous_of_continuousAt_one θ (by
    refine (continuousAt_const (y := (1 : (PadicAlgCl l)ˣ))).congr ?_
    filter_upwards [hχ.mem_nhds θ.ker.one_mem] with g hg using hg.symm)
  obtain ⟨h1, j, hj, hjl, h2⟩ := bk_loc θ c hc hb
  obtain ⟨i1, -, -, i4, -, -, -⟩ := Loc.iotaFacts j
  obtain ⟨K₀, hK₀, hψK⟩ := char_finext l (G ℚ Ω) θ hχ
  obtain ⟨E, _, κ, c', hκ, hc', hf⟩ := desc _ (θ.comp (Loc.iota j)) K₀ (fun g => hψK _) _ hj
  haveI : IsModuleTopology ℚ_[l] E := isModuleTopologyOfFiniteDimensional
  have hκc : Continuous fun g => (κ g : E) :=
    continuous_induced_rng.2 (((Units.continuous_val.comp hθc).comp i1.continuous).congr fun g => (hκ g).symm)
  have hz := (Loc.z1fUnr hP.loc (MonoidHom.id (GQ l)) Topology.IsOpenEmbedding.id (Loc.chRep κ)
    ((hκc.comp continuous_fst).mul continuous_snd) (fun g hg => by
      have h3 : θ (Loc.iota j g) = 1 := hθ (hI _ hjl (i4 ▸ ⟨g, hg, rfl⟩))
      have h4 : (κ g : E) = 1 := Subtype.ext ((hκ g).trans (congrArg Units.val h3))
      refine LinearMap.ext fun y => ?_
      change (κ g : E) * y = y
      rw [h4, one_mul]) c' hf.1).1 hf
  have h3 : ∀ A : ValuationSubring Ω, ∃ m, ∀ g ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) A, c g = θ g * m - m := by
    intro A
    by_cases hA : A.valuation (l : Ω) < 1
    · obtain ⟨g, hg, -⟩ := h2 A hA
      refine cob_conj θ c hcoc _ _ g hg ⟨0, fun s hs => ?_⟩
      rw [← i4] at hs
      obtain ⟨σ, hσ, rfl⟩ := hs
      have h5 := hc' σ
      rw [← h5, hz σ hσ]
      simp
    · exact h1 A hA
  have h4 : ∀ s : G K Ω, θ (galEmb ℚ K Ω s) = 1 := fun s => hθ (galEmb_mem ℚ K Ω s)
  intro g hg
  rw [← range_galEmb] at hg
  obtain ⟨s, rfl⟩ := hg
  refine unr_zero K Ω l (fun s => c (galEmb ℚ K Ω s)) (hc.1.comp g1.continuous) (fun s t => ?_) (fun A s hs => ?_) s
  · rw [map_mul, hcoc, h4, Units.val_one, one_mul]
  · obtain ⟨m, hm⟩ := h3 A
    rw [hm _ ((hIne A ⟨_, galEmb_mem ℚ K Ω s⟩).1 hs), h4, Units.val_one, one_mul, sub_self]

end Char

private theorem wt_rev (a b : Fin 3) : wt b.rev a.rev = wt a b := by
  revert a b
  decide

theorem UpperTheory.V.U3.bloch_kato_nonpositive_weight :
    CohInp → ∀ {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] {K : Type} [Field K] [NumberField K] [Algebra K Ω]
      {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {c : Coef l} {N : ℕ} [NeZero N] {e : ℕ} {ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O N} (D : Dat Ω K c e ψ) (j : ℤ), j ≤ 0 → ∀ γ ∈ Z1 (adRep (r0 D.η D.ΨQ)), (∀ g, γ g ∈ gw (PadicAlgCl l) j) →
      Af.IsBK l (adRep (r0 D.η D.ΨQ)) γ → γ ∈ ReciprocityTheory.Gal.B1 (adRep (r0 D.η D.ΨQ)) := by
  intro hP Ω _ _ _ K _ _ _ l _ c N _ e ψ D j hj γ hγ hw hb
  obtain ⟨d1, d2, d3, d4, -, -, -, -, d5, -, d6⟩ := dat_facts D
  let η : G ℚ Ω →* (PadicAlgCl l)ˣ := (Units.map (Int.castRingHom (PadicAlgCl l)).toMonoidHom).comp D.η
  have hη1 : ∀ g, D.η g = 1 → η g = 1 := fun g h => by simp [η, h]
  have hη2 : ∀ g, D.η g ≠ 1 → η g = -1 := fun g h => by
    apply Units.ext
    simp [η, (Int.units_eq_one_or _).resolve_left h]
  have hηo : IsOpen (D.η.ker : Set (G ℚ Ω)) := d4.isOpen_fiber 1

  have hch : ∀ (θ : G ℚ Ω →* (PadicAlgCl l)ˣ), θ = 1 ∨ θ = η → ∀ f ∈ Z1 (Loc.chRep (D.ΨQ ^ j * θ)),
      Af.IsBK l (Loc.chRep (D.ΨQ ^ j * θ)) f → ∃ m, ∀ g, f g = (D.ΨQ g ^ j * θ g : (PadicAlgCl l)ˣ) * m - m := by
    intro θ hθ f hf hfb
    have hk : D.η.ker ≤ θ.ker := fun g hg => by
      rcases hθ with rfl | rfl
      · rfl
      · exact hη1 g hg
    rcases hj.eq_or_lt with rfl | hj
    · simp only [zpow_zero, one_mul] at hf hfb ⊢
      have hcoc : ∀ g h, f (g * h) = f g + θ g * f h := hf.2
      have h0 := zero_case hP K θ (D.η_ker ▸ hk) (fun A hA => D.η_ker ▸ (d6 A hA).1) f hf hfb
      rw [← D.η_ker] at h0
      have h1 : ∀ g, D.η g ≠ 1 → f g = f D.cc := fun g hg => by
        have h2 : g * D.cc ∈ D.η.ker := by
          rw [MonoidHom.mem_ker, map_mul, d2, (Int.units_eq_one_or _).resolve_left hg]
          simp
        have h3 := hcoc (g * D.cc) D.cc
        rwa [mul_assoc, d1, mul_one, h0 _ h2, show θ (g * D.cc) = 1 from hk h2, Units.val_one, one_mul, zero_add] at h3
      refine ⟨-f D.cc / 2, fun g => ?_⟩
      by_cases hg : D.η g = 1
      · rw [h0 g hg, show θ g = 1 from hk hg]
        simp
      · rw [h1 g hg]
        rcases hθ with rfl | rfl
        · have h3 := hcoc D.cc D.cc
          rw [d1, h0 1 (one_mem _)] at h3
          simp only [MonoidHom.one_apply, Units.val_one] at h3 ⊢
          linear_combination (-1 / 2 : PadicAlgCl l) * h3
        · rw [hη2 g hg]
          simp only [Units.val_neg, Units.val_one]
          ring
    · obtain ⟨n, rfl⟩ : ∃ n : ℕ, j = -(n : ℤ) := ⟨(-j).toNat, by omega⟩
      let ψQ : G ℚ Ω →* (PadicAlgCl l)ˣ :=
        (Units.map (algebraMap c.O (PadicAlgCl l)).toMonoidHom).comp (Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ)
      have hθc : Continuous θ := continuous_of_continuousAt_one θ (by
        refine (continuousAt_const (y := (1 : (PadicAlgCl l)ˣ))).congr ?_
        filter_upwards [hηo.mem_nhds D.η.ker.one_mem] with g hg using (hk hg).symm)
      refine neg_case hP _ (ψQ ^ n * θ⁻¹) ?_ (e * n) (Nat.mul_pos D.gd.e_pos (by omega))
        (Units.continuous_val.comp ((d5.zpow _).mul hθc)) (fun g => ?_) f hf hfb
      · refine Subgroup.isOpen_mono (H₁ := (Chr.cls ℚ Ω N).ker ⊓ D.η.ker) (fun g hg => ?_)
          ((Chr.clsFacts ℚ Ω N).1.inter hηo)
        have h1 : Chr.cls ℚ Ω N g = 1 := hg.1
        have h2 : θ g = 1 := hk hg.2
        simp [ψQ, Chr.dirG, h1, h2]
      · have h1 : (D.ΨQ g : PadicAlgCl l) = algebraMap ℤ_[l] (PadicAlgCl l) (cycZ ℚ Ω l g : ℤ_[l]) ^ e * ψQ g := by
          simp [Dat.ΨQ, Dat.Ψ, Chr.Psi, chiO, ψQ]
        simp only [MonoidHom.mul_apply, MonoidHom.zpow_apply, MonoidHom.pow_apply, MonoidHom.inv_apply, Units.val_mul,
          _root_.zpow_neg, zpow_natCast, Units.val_inv_eq_inv_val, Units.val_pow_eq_pow_val, pow_mul]
        have h2 := (D.ΨQ g).ne_zero
        have h3 := (θ g).ne_zero
        field_simp
        rw [h1, mul_pow, mul_comm]

  have hpr : ∀ (s : PadicAlgCl l) (θ : G ℚ Ω →* (PadicAlgCl l)ˣ), (s = -1 ∧ θ = 1) ∨ (s = 1 ∧ θ = η) → ∀ a b : Fin 3,
      ∃ m, ∀ g, γ g a b + s * γ g b.rev a.rev = (D.ΨQ g ^ wt a b * θ g : (PadicAlgCl l)ˣ) * m - m := by
    intro s θ hs a b
    by_cases hab : wt a b = j
    swap
    · exact ⟨0, fun g => by rw [hw g a b hab, hw g _ _ (by rwa [wt_rev])]; simp⟩
    let f : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) (PadicAlgCl l) →ₗ[ℚ_[l]] PadicAlgCl l :=
      { toFun := fun X => X a b + s * X b.rev a.rev
        map_add' := fun X Y => by simp only [Matrix.add_apply]; ring
        map_smul' := fun r X => by simp only [Matrix.smul_apply, RingHom.id_apply, smul_add, mul_smul_comm] }
    have hfc : Continuous f := by
      change Continuous fun X : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) (PadicAlgCl l) => X a b + s * X b.rev a.rev
      fun_prop
    have hfe : ∀ g X, f (adRep (r0 D.η D.ΨQ) g X) = Loc.chRep (D.ΨQ ^ j * θ) g (f X) := fun g X => by
      change adRep (r0 D.η D.ΨQ) g X a b + s * adRep (r0 D.η D.ΨQ) g X b.rev a.rev =
        (D.ΨQ g ^ j * θ g : (PadicAlgCl l)ˣ) * (X a b + s * X b.rev a.rev)
      rw [adRep_baseLift_apply, adRep_baseLift_apply, wt_rev, hab, Fin.rev_rev, Fin.rev_rev, Units.val_mul]
      by_cases hg : D.η g = 1
      · rcases hs with ⟨rfl, rfl⟩ | ⟨rfl, rfl⟩
        · simp [hg, mul_add]
        · simp [hg, hη1 g hg, mul_add]
      · rcases hs with ⟨rfl, rfl⟩ | ⟨rfl, rfl⟩
        · simp only [hg, if_false, MonoidHom.one_apply, Units.val_one]
          ring
        · simp only [hg, if_false, hη2 g hg, Units.val_neg, Units.val_one]
          ring
    rw [hab]
    refine hch θ (hs.imp And.right And.right) (fun g => f (γ g)) ⟨hfc.comp hγ.1, fun g h => ?_⟩
      ⟨fun w hw => ?_, fun τ => ?_⟩
    · exact (congrArg f (hγ.2 g h)).trans (by rw [map_add, hfe])
    · obtain ⟨A, hA, x, hx⟩ := hb.away w hw
      exact ⟨A, hA, f x, fun s hs => by rw [hx s hs, map_sub, hfe]⟩
    · obtain ⟨i, hi, hx⟩ := hb.above τ
      refine ⟨i, hi, Loc.z1fCoef _ _ _ f hfc ?_ _ hx⟩
      exact fun g x => hfe g.1.1 x
  choose p hp using hpr 1 η (Or.inr ⟨rfl, rfl⟩)
  choose q hq using hpr (-1) 1 (Or.inl ⟨rfl, rfl⟩)
  refine (mem_coboundaries1 _ _).2 ⟨Matrix.of fun a b => (p a b + p b.rev a.rev + q a b - q b.rev a.rev) / 4, fun g => ?_⟩
  ext a b
  have h1 := hp a b g
  have h2 := hp b.rev a.rev g
  have h3 := hq a b g
  have h4 := hq b.rev a.rev g
  rw [Matrix.sub_apply, adRep_baseLift_apply]
  simp only [wt_rev, Fin.rev_rev, Units.val_mul, MonoidHom.one_apply, Units.val_one, Matrix.of_apply] at h1 h2 h3 h4 ⊢
  by_cases hg : D.η g = 1
  · rw [hη1 g hg, Units.val_one] at h1 h2
    rw [if_pos hg]
    linear_combination (h1 + h2 + h3 - h4) / 4
  · rw [hη2 g hg, Units.val_neg, Units.val_one] at h1 h2
    rw [if_neg hg]
    linear_combination (h1 + h2 + h3 - h4) / 4


[OCH_SRC_TANGENT_BK] Actual normalized tangent cocycle, finite coefficient continuity and all local primitives
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/UnitaryThreeGroups/TanCocIsBK.lean#L1-L222
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.UnitaryThreeGroups.KatoUse
