open UpperTheory.V UpperTheory.V.Base UpperTheory.V.HV UpperTheory.V.Alg UpperTheory.V.U3

private theorem tanBK_avg {k G W : Type} [Field k] [CharZero k] [Group G] [AddCommGroup W] [Module k W]
    (a : G → W →ₗ[k] W) (hmul : ∀ g h w, a (g * h) w = a g (a h w)) (f : G → W)
    (hf : ∀ g h, f (g * h) = f g + a g (f h)) (H : Subgroup G) [H.FiniteIndex] (x : W)
    (hx : ∀ h ∈ H, f h = a h x - x) : ∃ y, ∀ g, f g = a g y - y := by
  classical
  haveI : Fintype (G ⧸ H) := Fintype.ofFinite _
  set f' : G → W := fun g => f g - (a g x - x) with hf'
  have hc : ∀ g h, f' (g * h) = f' g + a g (f' h) := by
    intro g h
    simp only [hf', hf, hmul, map_sub]
    abel
  have hH : ∀ g h, h ∈ H → f' (g * h) = f' g := by
    intro g h hh
    rw [hc]
    have : f' h = 0 := by simp only [hf', hx h hh, sub_self]
    rw [this, map_zero, add_zero]
  let F : G ⧸ H → W := Quotient.lift f' (by
    intro g g' hgg
    have h1 : g⁻¹ * g' ∈ H := QuotientGroup.leftRel_apply.1 hgg
    have := hH g _ h1
    rw [mul_inv_cancel_left] at this
    exact this.symm)
  have hF : ∀ (g : G) (q : G ⧸ H), F (g • q) = f' g + a g (F q) := by
    intro g q
    induction q using QuotientGroup.induction_on with
    | H z => exact hc g z
  set S : W := ∑ q, F q with hS
  have hsum : ∀ g, S = (Fintype.card (G ⧸ H) : k) • f' g + a g S := by
    intro g
    have e1 : ∑ q, F (g • q) = S := Equiv.sum_comp (MulAction.toPerm g) F
    refine e1.symm.trans ?_
    simp only [hF, Finset.sum_add_distrib, Finset.sum_const, Finset.card_univ, ← map_sum,
      Nat.cast_smul_eq_nsmul, ← hS]
  have hn : (Fintype.card (G ⧸ H) : k) ≠ 0 := Nat.cast_ne_zero.2 Fintype.card_ne_zero
  refine ⟨x - (Fintype.card (G ⧸ H) : k)⁻¹ • S, fun g => ?_⟩
  have e : f' g = (Fintype.card (G ⧸ H) : k)⁻¹ • (S - a g S) := by
    rw [eq_inv_smul_iff₀ hn, eq_sub_iff_add_eq]
    exact (hsum g).symm
  have e2 : f g = f' g + (a g x - x) := by simp only [hf', sub_add_cancel]
  rw [e2, e, map_sub, map_smul, smul_sub]
  abel

section Coc

variable {O Γ R κ : Type} [CommRing O] [Group Γ] [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] [CommRing κ] [Algebra O κ]

private def sndM (F : R →ₐ[O] DualNumber κ) (X : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) R) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) κ :=
  X.map fun x => (F x).snd

private def fstM (F : R →ₐ[O] DualNumber κ) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) R →+* Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) κ :=
  (dualFst F : R →+* κ).mapMatrix

private theorem sndM_mul (F : R →ₐ[O] DualNumber κ) (X Y : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) R) :
    sndM F (X * Y) = fstM F X * sndM F Y + sndM F X * fstM F Y := by
  ext a b
  simp [sndM, fstM, dualFst, Matrix.mul_apply, Fin.sum_univ_three, DualNumber.snd_mul]
  ring

private theorem sndM_one (F : R →ₐ[O] DualNumber κ) : sndM F 1 = 0 := by
  ext a b
  by_cases h : a = b <;> simp [sndM, Matrix.one_apply, h]

private theorem sndM_J (F : R →ₐ[O] DualNumber κ) : sndM F (Jmat R 3) = 0 := by
  ext a b
  by_cases h : b = a.rev <;> simp [sndM, Jmat, h]

private theorem fstM_J (F : R →ₐ[O] DualNumber κ) : fstM F (Jmat R 3) = Jmat κ 3 := Jmat_map _ 3

private theorem sndM_tr (F : R →ₐ[O] DualNumber κ) (X : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) R) : sndM F Xᵀ = (sndM F X)ᵀ := rfl

private theorem fstM_tr (F : R →ₐ[O] DualNumber κ) (X : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) R) : fstM F Xᵀ = (fstM F X)ᵀ := rfl

private theorem sndM_inv (F : R →ₐ[O] DualNumber κ) (a : GL (Fin 3) R) :
    sndM F ((a⁻¹ : GL (Fin 3) R) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) R) =
      -(fstM F ((a⁻¹ : GL (Fin 3) R) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) R) * sndM F (a : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) R) *
        fstM F ((a⁻¹ : GL (Fin 3) R) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) R)) := by
  have h := sndM_mul F ((a⁻¹ : GL (Fin 3) R) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) R) a
  rw [Units.inv_mul, sndM_one] at h
  have h2 := congrArg (· * fstM F ((a⁻¹ : GL (Fin 3) R) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) R)) h
  simp only [Matrix.zero_mul, Matrix.add_mul] at h2
  rw [Matrix.mul_assoc (sndM F _), ← map_mul, Units.mul_inv, map_one, Matrix.mul_one] at h2
  exact eq_neg_of_add_eq_zero_right h2.symm

private theorem tan_coc (F : R →ₐ[O] DualNumber κ) (r : Γ →* GG 3 R) (g h : Γ) :
    tanCoc F r (g * h) = tanCoc F r g + adRep (push (dualFst F) r) g (tanCoc F r h) := by
  have e1 : ∀ g, tanCoc F r g = sndM F ((r g).left : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) R) *
      fstM F (((r g).left⁻¹ : GL (Fin 3) R) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) R) := fun g => rfl
  have e2 : ∀ X, adRep (push (dualFst F) r) g X = fstM F ((r g).left : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) R) *
      (if (r g).right = 1 then X else thetaLie X) *
      fstM F (((r g).left⁻¹ : GL (Fin 3) R) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) R) := fun X => rfl
  have hn : ((-1 : ℤˣ) = 1) = False := by decide
  have hJ : ∀ Y : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) κ, Jmat κ 3 * (Jmat κ 3 * Y) = Y := fun Y => by
    rw [← Matrix.mul_assoc, Jmat_mul_Jmat, Matrix.one_mul]
  have hc : ∀ (b : GL (Fin 3) R) (Y : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) κ), fstM F (b : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) R) *
      (fstM F ((b⁻¹ : GL (Fin 3) R) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) R) * Y) = Y := fun b Y => by
    rw [← Matrix.mul_assoc, ← map_mul, Units.mul_inv, map_one, Matrix.one_mul]
  have hc' : ∀ (b : GL (Fin 3) R) (Y : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) κ),
      (fstM F ((b⁻¹ : GL (Fin 3) R) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) R))ᵀ *
      ((fstM F (b : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) R))ᵀ * Y) = Y := fun b Y => by
    rw [← Matrix.mul_assoc, ← Matrix.transpose_mul, ← map_mul, Units.mul_inv, map_one, Matrix.transpose_one,
      Matrix.one_mul]
  rw [e2, e1, e1, e1, map_mul, SemidirectProduct.mul_left]
  generalize (r g).left = a
  generalize (r h).left = b
  rcases Int.units_eq_one_or (r g).right with hs | hs <;> rw [hs]
  · simp only [sgnAct_one, if_true, _root_.mul_inv_rev, Units.val_mul, sndM_mul, map_mul, Matrix.add_mul,
      Matrix.mul_assoc, hc]
    rw [add_comm]
  · simp only [sgnAct_neg_one, hn, if_false, _root_.mul_inv_rev, Units.val_mul, theta_val, theta_inv_val, sndM_mul,
      map_mul, sndM_J, fstM_J, sndM_tr, fstM_tr, sndM_inv, thetaLie, Matrix.add_mul, Matrix.mul_zero, Matrix.zero_mul,
      add_zero, zero_add, Matrix.transpose_neg, Matrix.transpose_mul, Matrix.neg_mul, Matrix.mul_neg,
      Matrix.mul_assoc, hJ, hc']
    rw [add_comm]

end Coc

private theorem tan_cont {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [CharZero O]
    [Algebra ℤ_[l] O] [Module.Finite ℤ_[l] O] [Algebra O (PadicAlgCl l)] [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] O (PadicAlgCl l)]
    (R : Type) [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] [IsCO O R]
    (F : R →ₐ[O] DualNumber (PadicAlgCl l)) (hF : IsLocPt (dualFst F)) {X : Type} [TopologicalSpace X] (f : X → R)
    (hf : AdicCont (maximalIdeal R) f) :
    Continuous (fun x => (F (f x)).fst) ∧ Continuous fun x => (F (f x)).snd := by
  haveI : IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal O) O := (abelian₂.dvr_finite l O).1
  obtain ⟨E, hE⟩ := Loc.ptFin (l := l) R F hF
  haveI : FiniteDimensional ℚ_[l] E.1 := E.2
  let B : Subalgebra ℚ_[l] (DualNumber (PadicAlgCl l)) :=
    { carrier := {x | x.fst ∈ E.1 ∧ x.snd ∈ E.1}
      mul_mem' := fun {x y} hx hy => ⟨mul_mem hx.1 hy.1, by
        rw [DualNumber.snd_mul]; exact add_mem (mul_mem hx.1 hy.2) (mul_mem hx.2 hy.1)⟩
      add_mem' := fun {x y} hx hy => ⟨add_mem hx.1 hy.1, add_mem hx.2 hy.2⟩
      algebraMap_mem' := fun a => ⟨E.1.algebraMap_mem a, zero_mem _⟩ }
  let p₁ : B →ₗ[ℚ_[l]] PadicAlgCl l := (TrivSqZeroExt.fstHom ℚ_[l] _ _).toLinearMap.comp B.val.toLinearMap
  let p₂ : B →ₗ[ℚ_[l]] PadicAlgCl l :=
    ((TrivSqZeroExt.sndHom (PadicAlgCl l) (PadicAlgCl l)).restrictScalars ℚ_[l]).comp B.val.toLinearMap
  haveI : Module.Finite ℚ_[l] B := Module.Finite.of_injective
    ((p₁.codRestrict E.1.toSubmodule fun x => x.2.1).prod (p₂.codRestrict E.1.toSubmodule fun x => x.2.2))
    (fun x y h => Subtype.ext (TrivSqZeroExt.ext (congrArg (fun z => (z.1 : PadicAlgCl l)) h)
      (congrArg (fun z => (z.2 : PadicAlgCl l)) h)))
  let x₀ : R →+* B := (F : R →+* DualNumber (PadicAlgCl l)).codRestrict B hE
  letI : Algebra O B := (x₀.comp (algebraMap O R)).toAlgebra
  haveI : IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] O B := IsScalarTower.of_algebraMap_eq fun a => Subtype.ext (by
    show algebraMap ℤ_[l] (DualNumber (PadicAlgCl l)) a = F (algebraMap O R _)
    rw [AlgHom.commutes, ← IsScalarTower.algebraMap_apply])
  let x : R →ₐ[O] B := { x₀ with commutes' := fun _ => rfl }
  have h := (Loc.ptCont (l := l) R B x).1 X f hf
  letI : TopologicalSpace B := moduleTopology ℚ_[l] B
  haveI : IsModuleTopology ℚ_[l] B := ⟨rfl⟩
  exact ⟨(IsModuleTopology.continuous_of_linearMap p₁).comp h, (IsModuleTopology.continuous_of_linearMap p₂).comp h⟩

theorem UpperTheory.V.U3.tanCoc_isBK :
    CohInp → ∀ {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] {K : Type} [Field K] [NumberField K] [Algebra K Ω]
      {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {c : Coef l} {N : ℕ} [NeZero N] {e : ℕ} {ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O N} (D : Dat Ω K c e ψ) {U : D.ULoc} {g₁ : G ℚ Ω}, D.Marked g₁ → ∀ (S : D.Ring U g₁),
      ∀ F : S.R →ₐ[c.O] DualNumber (PadicAlgCl l), IsLocPt (dualFst F) →
        tanCoc F S.r.hom ∈ Z1 (adRep (push (dualFst F) S.r.hom)) ∧ Af.IsBK l (adRep (push (dualFst F) S.r.hom)) (tanCoc F S.r.hom) := by
  intro hI Ω _ _ _ K _ _ _ l _ c N _ e ψ D U g₁ _ S F hF
  haveI : IsLocalRing c.O := c.dvr.toIsLocalRing
  haveI := (abelian₂.dvr_finite l c.O).1
  obtain ⟨-, hkill, f, hf, hfI⟩ := (S.rep S.R).1 (AlgHom.id _ _)
  have hZ : tanCoc F S.r.hom ∈ Z1 (adRep (push (dualFst F) S.r.hom)) := by
    refine ⟨?_, tan_coc F _⟩
    have h1 : Continuous fun g => fstM F (S.r.hom g).mat :=
      continuous_matrix fun a b => (tan_cont S.R F hF _ (S.r.cont a b)).1
    have h2 : Continuous fun g => sndM F (S.r.hom g).mat :=
      continuous_matrix fun a b => (tan_cont S.R F hF _ (S.r.cont a b)).2
    have h0 : ∀ g, fstM F (S.r.hom g).mat *
        fstM F (((S.r.hom g).left⁻¹ : GL (Fin 3) S.R) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) S.R) = 1 := fun g => by
      rw [← map_mul, GG.mat, Units.mul_inv, map_one]
    have h3 : tanCoc F S.r.hom = fun g => sndM F (S.r.hom g).mat *
        ((fstM F (S.r.hom g).mat).det⁻¹ • (fstM F (S.r.hom g).mat).adjugate) := funext fun g => by
      rw [← Ring.inverse_eq_inv, ← Matrix.inv_def, Matrix.inv_eq_right_inv (h0 g)]
      rfl
    rw [h3]
    exact h2.matrix_mul ((h1.matrix_det.inv₀ fun g => (Matrix.isUnit_det_of_right_inverse (h0 g)).ne_zero).smul
      h1.matrix_adjugate)
  refine ⟨hZ, fun w hw => ?_, fun τ => ⟨D.j, Subsingleton.elim _ _, ?_⟩⟩
  · refine ⟨placeOf Ω w, isPlaceOver_placeOf Ω w, ?_⟩
    have hA : ¬ (placeOf Ω w).valuation (l : Ω) < 1 := fun h =>
      hw (((places ℚ Ω l _).2.2.1 w (isPlaceOver_placeOf Ω w)).1 h)
    haveI : Finite (ResidueField c.O) := (abelian₂.dvr_finite l c.O).2.1
    haveI : Finite (GG 3 (ResidueField c.O)) := Finite.of_equiv _ SemidirectProduct.equivProd.symm
    obtain ⟨y, hy⟩ := tanBK_avg (k := PadicAlgCl l) (G := Ine (F := ℚ) (placeOf Ω w))
      (fun g => adRep (push (dualFst F) S.r.hom) g) (fun g h x => by simp)
      (fun g => tanCoc F S.r.hom g) (fun g h => hZ.2 g h)
      ((rbar c.O D.η D.Ψ).comp (Ine (F := ℚ) (placeOf Ω w)).subtype).ker 0 (fun g hg => by
        have h1 : S.r.hom g = 1 := hkill g ⟨_, hA, g.2, hg⟩
        have h2 : tanCoc F S.r.hom g = sndM F (S.r.hom g).mat *
          fstM F (((S.r.hom g).left⁻¹ : GL (Fin 3) S.R) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) S.R) := rfl
        rw [h2, h1]
        have h3 : sndM F (1 : GG 3 S.R).mat = 0 := sndM_one F
        simp [h3])
    exact ⟨y, fun s hs => hy ⟨s, hs⟩⟩
  · have hfg : ∀ h, GG.map (f : U.R →+* S.R) (U.r.hom h) = S.r.hom (D.ι h) := fun h =>
      DFunLike.congr_fun (congrArg GLift.hom hf) h
    have h1 := Loc.rtDual hI.loc D.loc U (F.comp f)
      (fun a ha => by
        have : f a = 0 := hfI ha
        show F (f a) = 0
        rw [this, map_zero])
      (fun a ha => hF (f a) (by rwa [← res_eq_zero_iff c.O, res_map, res_eq_zero_iff]))
    have e1 : tanCoc (F.comp f) U.r.hom = fun h => tanCoc F S.r.hom (D.ι h) := funext fun h => by
      unfold tanCoc
      rw [← hfg]
      rfl
    have e2 : adRep (push (dualFst (F.comp f)) U.r.hom) = (adRep (push (dualFst F) S.r.hom)).comp D.ι := by
      refine MonoidHom.ext fun h => ?_
      show _ = GG.ad 3 _ (GG.map _ (S.r.hom (D.ι h)))
      rw [← hfg]
      rfl
    rw [e1, e2] at h1
    exact (Loc.bkAbove (F := ℚ) D.j (MonoidHom.id _) D.ι Topology.IsOpenEmbedding.id D.ι_spec _ _ hZ).1 h1


[OCH_SRC_NORMAL_COB] Exact normalized coboundary primitive equals a scalar diagonal(1,0,-1)
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/UnitaryThreeGroups/NormCob.lean#L1-L103
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.UnitaryThreeGroups.BaseRepGL3NormalisedOfPsiEqOne

open Matrix UpperTheory.V.U3

private theorem gstar_right_and_mat_of_right_neg_one_mat_one {G R : Type} [Group G] [CommRing R] (r : G →* GG 3 R) (c g₁ : G)
    (hc : (r c).right = -1) (h1 : (r g₁).right = 1) (hN : (r c).mat = 1) :
    (r (gstar c g₁)).right = 1 ∧ (r (gstar c g₁)).mat = Jmat R 3 * ((r g₁).mat)ᵀ * Jmat R 3 := by
  have hcl : (r c).left = 1 := Units.ext hN
  refine ⟨by simp [gstar, hc, h1], ?_⟩
  have hL : (r (gstar c g₁)).left = theta ((r g₁).left⁻¹) := by
    simp only [gstar, map_mul, map_inv, SemidirectProduct.mul_left, SemidirectProduct.inv_left,
      SemidirectProduct.mul_right, SemidirectProduct.inv_right, hcl, hc, h1]
    simp [sgnAct, thetaAut]
    have e1 : theta (1 : GL (Fin 3) R) = 1 := map_one (thetaAut 3 R)
    have e2 : theta ((r g₁).left⁻¹) = (theta (r g₁).left)⁻¹ := map_inv (thetaAut 3 R) _
    rw [e1, mul_one, e2]
  show ((r (gstar c g₁)).left : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) R) = _
  rw [hL, theta_val, inv_inv]
  rfl

private theorem cob_mul_mat_eq_commutator_of_right_eq_one {G R : Type} [Group G] [CommRing R] (r : G →* GG 3 R)
    (X : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) R) (g : G) (hg : (r g).right = 1) :
    cob r X g * (r g).mat = X * (r g).mat - (r g).mat * X := by
  show (X - (r g).adFun X) * (r g).mat = _
  simp only [GG.adFun, hg, if_true]
  show (X - (r g).mat * X * ((r g).left⁻¹ : GL (Fin 3) R)) * ((r g).left : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) R) = _
  rw [sub_mul, mul_assoc, Units.inv_mul, mul_one]
  rfl

theorem UpperTheory.V.U3.normCoc_cob_iff :
    ∀ {G R : Type} [Group G] [CommRing R] (r : G →* GG 3 R) (c g₁ : G) (X : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) R), IsUnit (2 : R) → (r c).right = -1 → (r g₁).right = 1 →
      Normalised c g₁ r → IsUnit (zeta c g₁ r) → (NormCoc c g₁ r (cob r X) ↔ X ∈ unitaryThreeTorusLieAlgebra R) := by
  intro G R _ _ r c g₁ X h2 hc h1 hn hz
  obtain ⟨hsr, hsm⟩ := gstar_right_and_mat_of_right_neg_one_mat_one r c g₁ hc h1 hn.atc
  have hcl : (r c).left = 1 := Units.ext hn.atc
  have hA : cob r X c = X - thetaLie X := by
    show X - (r c).adFun X = _
    have hne : (r c).right ≠ 1 := by rw [hc]; decide
    simp only [GG.adFun, if_neg hne, hn.atc, one_mul]
    rw [hcl, inv_one, Units.val_one, mul_one]
  have hW : zvar c g₁ r (cob r X) = X * ((r g₁).mat - Jmat R 3 * ((r g₁).mat)ᵀ * Jmat R 3)
      - ((r g₁).mat - Jmat R 3 * ((r g₁).mat)ᵀ * Jmat R 3) * X := by
    show cob r X g₁ * (r g₁).mat - cob r X (gstar c g₁) * (r (gstar c g₁)).mat = _
    rw [cob_mul_mat_eq_commutator_of_right_eq_one r X g₁ h1, cob_mul_mat_eq_commutator_of_right_eq_one r X _ hsr, hsm]
    noncomm_ring
  set M := (r g₁).mat with hM
  have z12 : M 0 1 - M 1 2 = 0 := by
    have h := hn.z12
    simp only [zmat, hsm] at h
    simp [Jmat, Matrix.mul_apply, Fin.sum_univ_three, Fin.rev] at h
    linear_combination h
  have z21 : M 1 0 - M 2 1 = 0 := by
    have h := hn.z21
    simp only [zmat, hsm] at h
    simp [Jmat, Matrix.mul_apply, Fin.sum_univ_three, Fin.rev] at h
    linear_combination h
  have hu : IsUnit (M 0 0 - M 2 2) := by
    have e : zeta c g₁ r = M 0 0 - M 2 2 := by
      simp only [zeta, zmat, hsm]
      simp [Jmat, Matrix.mul_apply, Fin.sum_univ_three, Fin.rev]
      rfl
    rw [← e]; exact hz
  constructor
  · rintro ⟨ha, h12, h21⟩
    rw [hA] at ha
    rw [hW] at h12 h21
    have a00 := congrFun (congrFun ha 0) 0
    have a11 := congrFun (congrFun ha 1) 1
    have a02 := congrFun (congrFun ha 0) 2
    have a20 := congrFun (congrFun ha 2) 0
    have a01 := congrFun (congrFun ha 0) 1
    have a10 := congrFun (congrFun ha 1) 0
    simp [thetaLie, Jmat, Matrix.mul_apply, Fin.sum_univ_three, Fin.rev] at a00 a11 a02 a20 a01 a10
    simp [Jmat, Matrix.mul_apply, Fin.sum_univ_three, Fin.rev] at h12 h21
    have x11 : X 1 1 = 0 := h2.mul_left_cancel (by linear_combination a11)
    have x02 : X 0 2 = 0 := h2.mul_left_cancel (by linear_combination a02)
    have x20 : X 2 0 = 0 := h2.mul_left_cancel (by linear_combination a20)
    have x01 : X 0 1 = 0 := hu.mul_left_cancel
      (by linear_combination -h12 + X 0 0 * z12 - X 0 2 * z21 - X 1 1 * z12)
    have x10 : X 1 0 = 0 := hu.mul_left_cancel
      (by linear_combination h21 - X 1 1 * z21 + X 0 0 * z21 - X 2 0 * z12)
    have x12 : X 1 2 = 0 := by linear_combination a01 - x01
    have x21 : X 2 1 = 0 := by linear_combination a10 - x10
    have x22 : X 2 2 = -X 0 0 := by linear_combination a00
    rw [unitaryThreeTorusLieAlgebra, Submodule.mem_span_singleton]
    refine ⟨X 0 0, ?_⟩
    ext i j
    fin_cases i <;> fin_cases j <;>
      simp [wt1, Matrix.diagonal, x01, x10, x02, x20, x11, x12, x21, x22] <;> ring
  · intro hX
    rw [unitaryThreeTorusLieAlgebra, Submodule.mem_span_singleton] at hX
    obtain ⟨a, rfl⟩ := hX
    refine ⟨?_, ?_, ?_⟩
    · rw [hA]
      ext i j
      fin_cases i <;> fin_cases j <;>
        simp [thetaLie, Jmat, wt1, Matrix.mul_apply, Fin.sum_univ_three, Fin.rev, Matrix.diagonal] <;> ring
    · rw [hW]
      simp [Jmat, wt1, Matrix.mul_apply, Fin.sum_univ_three, Fin.rev, Matrix.diagonal]
      linear_combination a * z12
    · rw [hW]
      simp [Jmat, wt1, Matrix.mul_apply, Fin.sum_univ_three, Fin.rev, Matrix.diagonal]
      linear_combination a * z21


[OCH_SRC_TYPEI_RIGIDITY] Exact polarized type-I AdVanishK application with epsilon^-1 twist
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/UnitaryThreeGroups/LiftingTheory.lean#L40-L84
theorem UpperTheory.V.U3.bloch_kato_type_one :
    CohInp → Af.AdVanishK → ∀ {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] {K : Type} [Field K] [NumberField K] [Algebra K Ω]
      {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {c : Coef l} {N : ℕ} [NeZero N] {e : ℕ} {ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O N} (D : Dat Ω K c e ψ) {U : D.ULoc} {g₁ : G ℚ Ω}, D.Marked g₁ → ∀ {r : G ℚ Ω →* GG 3 (PadicAlgCl l)} (h : D.IsPt U g₁ r), ∀ [IsCMField K] (ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ),
      TypeI D.η r → D.IsAutI ι r h.mult → Af.BlochKatoSelmerVanishes l (adRep r) := by
  intro hC hA Ω _ _ _ K _ _ _ l _ c N _ e ψ D U g₁ hM r h _ ι hI hAut
  haveI : IsAlgClosed Ω := IsAlgClosure.isAlgClosed ℚ
  haveI : IsAlgClosure K Ω := ⟨inferInstance, Algebra.IsAlgebraic.tower_top (K := ℚ) K⟩
  have hK : ∀ s : G K Ω, (r (galEmb ℚ K Ω s)).right = 1 := fun s =>
    (h.mult _).trans (show galEmb ℚ K Ω s ∈ D.η.ker from D.η_ker ▸ galEmb_mem ℚ K Ω s)
  have hc : (r D.cc).right = -1 := (h.mult _).trans hM.ηc
  have hz : Continuous fun s : G K Ω => ((cycZ K Ω l s : ℤ_[l]ˣ) : ℤ_[l]) :=
    Units.continuous_val.comp (continuous_cycZ K Ω l)
  have hcyc : Continuous fun s : G K Ω => (cycBar K Ω l s : Qbar l) :=
    (continuous_algebraMap ℚ_[l] (PadicAlgCl l)).comp
      (continuous_subtype_val.comp hz)
  refine hA K D.gd.quad 3 (by norm_num) l Ω ι _ ?_ hAut ?_ _ ⟨D.cc, Jgl _ 3, D.conj, ?_, ?_, ?_⟩
  · refine cont_hom _ ?_
    simp only [Af.tw_val, MonoidHom.inv_apply, ← map_inv]
    exact (hcyc.comp continuous_inv).smul (h.cont.comp (galEmbTop ℚ K Ω).1.continuous)
  · rw [irr_tw]
    exact typeI_irr_cyc hC D hM h hI
  · intro s s' hs
    have hs' : galEmb ℚ K Ω s' = D.cc * galEmb ℚ K Ω s * D.cc⁻¹ := hs
    have h1 : cycBar K Ω l s' = cycBar K Ω l s := by
      show cycBar ℚ Ω l (galEmb ℚ K Ω s') = cycBar ℚ Ω l (galEmb ℚ K Ω s)
      rw [hs', map_mul, map_mul, map_inv, mul_inv_cancel_comm]
    have h2 : D.rhoK r h.mult s' = theta (D.rhoK r h.mult s) := by
      rw [D.rhoK_apply, D.rhoK_apply, hs', map_mul, map_mul, map_inv]
      exact left_conj _ _ h.norm.atc hc (hK s)
    rw [Af.tw_val, Af.tw_val, h2, MonoidHom.inv_apply, MonoidHom.inv_apply, h1, Matrix.transpose_smul,
      Matrix.smul_mul, Matrix.smul_mul, Matrix.mul_smul, smul_smul, ← Units.val_mul]
    have h3 : (cycBar K Ω l ^ (1 - ((3 : ℕ) : ℤ))) s = (cycBar K Ω l s)⁻¹ * (cycBar K Ω l s)⁻¹ := by
      rw [MonoidHom.zpow_apply, show (1 - ((3 : ℕ) : ℤ)) = -1 + -1 by norm_num, _root_.zpow_add, _root_.zpow_neg_one]
    rw [h3]
    exact congrArg _ (pairing_theta _)
  · intro s X
    rw [ad_tw]
    show (r (galEmb ℚ K Ω s)).adFun X = _
    rw [GG.adFun, if_pos (hK s)]
    rfl
  · intro X
    have hl : (r D.cc).left = 1 := Units.ext h.norm.atc
    show (r D.cc).adFun X = _
    rw [GG.adFun, hc, if_neg (by decide), h.norm.atc, hl, inv_one, Units.val_one, Matrix.one_mul, Matrix.mul_one]
    rfl


[OCH_SRC_POINT_HYPOTHESES] Literal PtHyp and each of its three original fields
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/UnitaryThreeGroups/Rigid0.lean#L9-L22

def vl (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (x : PadicAlgCl l) : ℝ := -Real.logb l ‖x‖

variable {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {c : Coef l} {Γ R : Type} [Group Γ] [CommRing R] [Algebra c.O R]

abbrev toBar (x : R →ₐ[c.O] c.O) : R →ₐ[c.O] PadicAlgCl l := (Algebra.ofId c.O (PadicAlgCl l)).comp x

structure PtHyp [IsLocalRing R] (η : Γ →* ℤˣ) (r : Γ →* GG 3 R) (x₀ : R →ₐ[c.O] c.O) : Prop where
  fixed : ∀ f : R →ₐ[c.O] PadicAlgCl l, IsLocPt f → Upper η (push f r) → Lower η (push f r) →
    push f r = push (toBar x₀) r
  rigid0 : ∀ j : ℤ, j ≤ 0 → RigidWt j r (toBar x₀)
  rigid : ∀ f : R →ₐ[c.O] PadicAlgCl l, TwoPt η r f → Rigid r f

end UpperTheory.V.U3


[OCH_SRC_POINT_HYP_PRODUCER] Complete original ptHyp_of implication on the same actual ring
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/ModularityArgument/KatoQuadraticCharacters/MapReducibleThreeDim.lean#L64-L89
theorem UpperTheory.V.Z.Kato.ptHyp_of :
    UpperTheory.V.Z.Kato.InpZ → ∀ {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] {K : Type} [Field K] [NumberField K] [Algebra K Ω]
      {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {c : Coef l} {N : ℕ} [NeZero N] {e : ℕ} {ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O N} (D : Dat Ω K c e ψ) {U : D.ULoc} {g₁ : G ℚ Ω}, D.Marked g₁ → ∀ (S : D.Ring U g₁), ∀ [IsCMField K] (ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ), Aut.AutB D ι g₁ → Aut.LemW D ι g₁ →
      ∀ x₀ : S.R →ₐ[c.O] c.O, IsBase D.η D.Ψ S.r.hom x₀ → PtHyp D.η S.r.hom x₀ := by
  intro hI Ω _ _ _ K _ _ _ l _ c N _ e ψ D U g₁ hM S _ ι hB hW x₀ hx
  have h0 : push (toBar x₀) S.r.hom = r0 D.η D.ΨQ := by
    have e1 : push (toBar x₀) S.r.hom =
        (GG.map (algebraMap c.O (PadicAlgCl l))).comp (push x₀ S.r.hom) := rfl
    rw [e1, show push x₀ S.r.hom = r0 D.η D.Ψ from hx, map_reducible_three_dim]
    rfl
  refine ⟨fun f hf hU hL => ?_, fun j hj F hF hw => ?_, fun f hf F hF => ?_⟩
  · rw [h0]
    exact pt_fixed hI.toCohInp D hM (isPt_push D hM S f hf) hU hL
  · have h1 := tanCoc_isBK hI.toCohInp D hM S F (hF ▸ isLocPt_toBar x₀)
    rw [hF, h0] at h1
    rw [h0]
    exact coboundary_of_mem_B1 _ _ (bloch_kato_nonpositive_weight hI.toCohInp D j hj _ h1.1 hw h1.2)
  · have h1 := tanCoc_isBK hI.toCohInp D hM S F (hF ▸ hf.1)
    rw [hF] at h1
    have hp := isPt_push D hM S f hf.1
    have hz : Af.BlochKatoSelmerVanishes l (adRep (push f S.r.hom)) := by
      rcases (twoPt_shape S D.gd.l_odd hM f hf).1 with h | h
      · exact bloch_kato_type_one hI.toCohInp hI.adK D hM hp ι h (hB U S _ _ hp.mult (isPush_push f _) h)
      · exact hI.rigII D hM hp ι h
          (hW U S _ _ (isPush_push f _) h (typeII_facts hI.toCohInp D hM hp h).1)
    exact coboundary_of_mem_B1 _ _ (hz _ h1.1 h1.2)


[OCH_SRC_ADM_CONTRACT] Original cyclotomic-weight and LevelO clauses
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/AutomorphicForms/CongruenceNumberRankThree.lean#L33-L44
def IsCycWt (wt : ℤ) (θ : G F Ω →* (Qbar l)ˣ) : Prop :=
  ∃ (M : ℕ) (χ : G F Ω →* (Qbar l)ˣ), 1 ≤ M ∧ KillsMu M χ ∧ θ = cycBar F Ω l ^ wt * χ

variable (l) in

structure LevelO (M : ℕ) (m : Fin n → ℤ) (r : G F Ω →* GL (Fin n) (Qbar l)) : Prop extends Level l M m r where
  odd : Odd n

structure AdmK [IsCMField F] (ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ) (M : ℕ) (m : Fin n → ℤ) (r : G F Ω →* GL (Fin n) (Qbar l)) : Prop where
  irr : IsIrr r
  aut : IsAutCsd ι r
  level : LevelO l M m r


[OCH_SRC_ADM_RANKS] All exact Adm3 and Adm2 fields, weights and direct-sum isomorphism
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/AutomorphicForms/CongruenceNumberRankThree.lean#L103-L119

variable {K Ω : Type} [Field K] [NumberField K] [Field Ω] [Algebra ℚ Ω] [Algebra K Ω] [IsScalarTower ℚ K Ω]
  {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime]

structure Adm3 [IsCMField K] (ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ) (e N : ℕ) (ρ : G K Ω →* GL (Fin 3) (Qbar l)) : Prop where
  irr : IsIrr ρ
  aut : IsAutCsd ι (tw ρ (cycBar K Ω l)⁻¹)
  level : Level l N ![(e : ℤ), 0, -(e : ℤ)] ρ

def Adm2 (ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ) (e M : ℕ) (ρ : G K Ω →* GL (Fin 3) (Qbar l)) : Prop :=
  ∃ W : G ℚ Ω →* GL (Fin 2) (Qbar l), IsIrr W ∧ (∃ f : Newform (2 * e + 1), f.Att ι (tw W (cycBar ℚ Ω l ^ (-(e : ℤ))))) ∧
    Level l M ![(e : ℤ), -(e : ℤ)] W ∧ IsCycWt l 0 (Matrix.GeneralLinearGroup.det.comp W) ∧
    ∃ g : GL (Fin 3) (Qbar l), ∀ s : G K Ω, ((g * ρ s * g⁻¹ : GL (Fin 3) (Qbar l)) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) (Qbar l)) =
      !![W (s.restrictScalars ℚ) 0 0, 0, W (s.restrictScalars ℚ) 0 1; 0, 1, 0;
         W (s.restrictScalars ℚ) 1 0, 0, W (s.restrictScalars ℚ) 1 1]

end U3


[OCH_SRC_ADK_CONTRACT] Original full polarized adjoint action and AdVanishK
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/AutomorphicForms/CongruenceNumberRankThree.lean#L158-L175
def IsAdOf (r : G F Ω →* GL (Fin n) k) (μ : G F Ω →* kˣ) (ρ : Representation k (G F₀ Ω) (Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) k)) : Prop :=
  ∃ (c : G F₀ Ω) (P : GL (Fin n) k), (∃ j : Ω →+* ℂ, ∀ x, j (c x) = starRingEnd ℂ (j x)) ∧
    (∀ s s' : G F Ω, s'.restrictScalars F₀ = c * s.restrictScalars F₀ * c⁻¹ →
      (r s : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) k)ᵀ * (P : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) k) * (r s' : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) k) =
        (μ s : k) • (P : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) k)) ∧
    (∀ (s : G F Ω) (X : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) k), ρ (s.restrictScalars F₀) X =
      (r s : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) k) * X * ((r s)⁻¹ : GL (Fin n) k)) ∧
    ∀ X : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) k, ρ c X = -((P⁻¹ : GL (Fin n) k) * Xᵀ * (P : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) k))

end Ad

def AdVanishK : Prop :=
  ∀ (K : Type) [Field K] [NumberField K] [IsCMField K], Module.finrank ℚ K = 2 → ∀ (n : ℕ), 2 ≤ n →
    ∀ (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [Algebra ℚ Ω] [Algebra K Ω] [IsScalarTower ℚ K Ω] [IsAlgClosure K Ω]
      (ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ) (r : G K Ω →* GL (Fin n) (Qbar l)), Continuous r → IsAutCsd ι r →
      IsIrr (r.comp (cycBar K Ω l).ker.subtype) →
      ∀ ρ : Representation (Qbar l) (G ℚ Ω) (Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) (Qbar l)),
        IsAdOf r (cycBar K Ω l ^ (1 - (n : ℤ))) ρ → BlochKatoSelmerVanishes l ρ


[OCH_SRC_ADM_THREE] Actual all-place ordered flags and complete rank-three admissibility

open UpperTheory.V UpperTheory.V.Base UpperTheory.V.HV UpperTheory.V.Alg UpperTheory.V.U3

private theorem conj_left_block_of_right_one {Γ R : Type} [Group Γ] [CommRing R] (M : GL (Fin 3) R) (r : Γ →* GG 3 R) (g : Γ)
    (hg : (r g).right = 1) : (conjBy M r g).left = M * (r g).left * M⁻¹ := by
  simp [conjBy, hg]

private theorem conj_left_block_eq_theta {Γ R : Type} [Group Γ] [CommRing R] (r : Γ →* GG 3 R) (c g : Γ)
    (hc : (r c).mat = 1) (hc' : (r c).right = -1) (hg : (r g).right = 1) : (r (c⁻¹ * g * c)).left = theta (r g).left := by
  have h1 : (r c).left = 1 := Units.ext hc
  have h2 : theta (1 : GL (Fin 3) R) = 1 := map_one (thetaAut 3 R)
  simp [h1, hc', hg, h2]

private theorem exists_flag_characters_of_upper_triangular {Γ k : Type} [Group Γ] [Field k] {n : ℕ} (ρ : Γ →* GL (Fin n) k) (H : Subgroup Γ) (M : GL (Fin n) k)
    (h : ∀ s ∈ H, ∀ i j, j < i → (M * ρ s * M⁻¹ : GL (Fin n) k) i j = 0) :
    ∃ κ : Fin n → ↥H →* kˣ, Af.IsFlagOn ρ H κ ∧ ∀ i (s : ↥H), (κ i s : k) = (M * ρ s * M⁻¹ : GL (Fin n) k) i i := by
  have hm : ∀ i, ∀ s ∈ H, ∀ t ∈ H, (M * ρ (s * t) * M⁻¹ : GL (Fin n) k) i i =
      (M * ρ s * M⁻¹ : GL (Fin n) k) i i * (M * ρ t * M⁻¹ : GL (Fin n) k) i i := by
    intro i s hs t ht
    have e : M * ρ (s * t) * M⁻¹ = (M * ρ s * M⁻¹) * (M * ρ t * M⁻¹) := by simp [mul_assoc]
    rw [e, Units.val_mul, Matrix.mul_apply, Finset.sum_eq_single i]
    · intro j _ hj
      rcases lt_or_gt_of_ne hj with h1 | h1
      · rw [h s hs i j h1, zero_mul]
      · rw [h t ht j i h1, mul_zero]
    · simp
  let κ : Fin n → ↥H →* k := fun i =>
    { toFun := fun s => (M * ρ s * M⁻¹ : GL (Fin n) k) i i
      map_one' := by simp
      map_mul' := fun s t => hm i s s.2 t t.2 }
  exact ⟨fun i => (κ i).toHomUnits, ⟨M, fun s => ⟨h s s.2, fun i => rfl⟩⟩, fun i s => rfl⟩

private theorem crystalline_of_theta_twist_gl3 {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {H : Type} [Group H] [TopologicalSpace H] [IsTopologicalGroup H]
    (φ : H →* GQ l) (hφ : Topology.IsOpenEmbedding φ) (E : FinExt l) (ρ : H →* GL (Fin 3) ↥E.1) (hρ : Continuous ρ)
    (h : Loc.IsCr φ (Loc.colRep ρ)) :
    Loc.IsCr φ (Loc.colRep ((thetaAut 3 ↥E.1).toMonoidHom.comp ρ)) := by
  haveI := E.2
  haveI : IsModuleTopology ℚ_[l] ↥E.1 := isModuleTopologyOfFiniteDimensional
  haveI : IsModuleTopology ℚ_[l] (Fin 3 → ↥E.1) := isModuleTopologyOfFiniteDimensional
  have h0 : Loc.IsCr φ (Loc.chRep (1 : H →* (↥E.1)ˣ)) :=
    Loc.crUnr φ hφ _ (by
      have : ∀ p : H × ↥E.1, Loc.chRep (1 : H →* (↥E.1)ˣ) p.1 p.2 = p.2 := fun p => by simp [Loc.chRep]
      exact continuous_snd.congr fun p => (this p).symm) 0 fun g _ x => by simp [Loc.chRep]
  have hc : ∀ σ : H →* GL (Fin 3) ↥E.1, Continuous (fun g => (σ g : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) ↥E.1)) →
      ContAction (Loc.colRep σ) := fun σ hσ =>
    (hσ.comp continuous_fst).matrix_mulVec continuous_snd
  let b : (Fin 3 → ↥E.1) →ₗ[ℚ_[l]] (Fin 3 → ↥E.1) →ₗ[ℚ_[l]] ↥E.1 :=
    LinearMap.mk₂ ℚ_[l] (fun w x => (Jmat _ 3 *ᵥ w) ⬝ᵥ x) (fun _ _ _ => by simp [Matrix.mulVec_add])
      (fun _ _ _ => by simp [Matrix.mulVec_smul]) (fun _ _ _ => by simp) (fun _ _ _ => by simp)
  refine (Loc.isCrys_isDR_internalHom φ hφ (Loc.colRep ρ) (Loc.chRep (1 : H →* (↥E.1)ˣ)) _ (hc ρ (Units.continuous_val.comp hρ)) ?_
    (hc _ ?_) b (fun g w x => ?_) ?_).1 h h0
  · have : ∀ p : H × ↥E.1, Loc.chRep (1 : H →* (↥E.1)ˣ) p.1 p.2 = p.2 := fun p => by simp [Loc.chRep]
    exact continuous_snd.congr fun p => (this p).symm
  · exact (continuous_const.matrix_mul (Units.continuous_coe_inv.comp hρ).matrix_transpose).matrix_mul continuous_const
  · change (Jmat _ 3 *ᵥ ((theta (ρ g) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) ↥E.1) *ᵥ w)) ⬝ᵥ ((ρ g : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) ↥E.1) *ᵥ x) = (1 : ↥E.1) • ((Jmat _ 3 *ᵥ w) ⬝ᵥ x)
    rw [one_smul, theta_val, Matrix.mulVec_mulVec, ← Matrix.mul_assoc, ← Matrix.mul_assoc, Jmat_mul_Jmat, Matrix.one_mul,
      ← Matrix.mulVec_mulVec, Matrix.mulVec_transpose, ← Matrix.dotProduct_mulVec, Matrix.mulVec_mulVec, Units.inv_mul,
      Matrix.one_mulVec]
  · rw [injective_iff_map_eq_zero]
    intro w hw
    have h1 : Jmat _ 3 *ᵥ w = 0 := dotProduct_eq_zero _ fun x => LinearMap.congr_fun hw x
    have h2 := congrArg (Jmat _ 3 *ᵥ ·) h1
    simpa [Matrix.mulVec_mulVec, Jmat_mul_Jmat] using h2

private theorem continuous_of_matrix_units {Γ k : Type} [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace Γ] [IsTopologicalGroup Γ] [CommRing k] [TopologicalSpace k]
    {n : ℕ} (ρ : Γ →* GL (Fin n) k) (h : Continuous fun g => (ρ g : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) k)) : Continuous ρ :=
  Units.continuous_iff.2 ⟨h, (h.comp continuous_inv).congr fun g => by simp only [Function.comp_apply, map_inv]⟩

section

variable {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] {K : Type} [Field K] [NumberField K] [Algebra K Ω]
  {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {c : Coef l} {N : ℕ} [NeZero N] {e : ℕ} {ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O N} (D : Dat Ω K c e ψ)
  {U : D.ULoc} {g₁ : G ℚ Ω} {r : G ℚ Ω →* GG 3 (PadicAlgCl l)}

private theorem isAlgClosure_of_number_field (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (K : Type) [Field K] [NumberField K]
    [Algebra K Ω] : IsAlgClosure K Ω :=
  ⟨IsAlgClosure.isAlgClosed ℚ, Algebra.IsAlgebraic.tower_top (K := ℚ) K⟩

private theorem continuous_rho_of_point (h : D.IsPt U g₁ r) : Continuous (D.rhoK r h.mult) := by
  haveI := isAlgClosure_of_number_field Ω K
  exact continuous_of_matrix_units _ (h.cont.comp (galEmbTop ℚ K Ω).1.continuous)

private theorem exists_local_crystalline_gl3_model (hI : CohInp) (h : D.IsPt U g₁ r) :
    ∃ (E : FinExt l) (ρ : ↥D.loc.HL →* GL (Fin 3) ↥E.1),
      (∀ g, GeneralLinearGroup.map (E.1.val : ↥E.1 →+* PadicAlgCl l) (ρ g) = (r (D.ι g)).left) ∧
      Loc.IsCr D.loc.HL.subtype (Loc.colRep ρ) := by
  obtain ⟨f, hf, hker, hpush⟩ := h.loc
  haveI : IsLocalRing c.O := c.dvr.toIsLocalRing
  haveI := (dvrFacts l c.O).2.2.2.1
  obtain ⟨E, hE⟩ := Loc.ptFin (l := l) U.R ((TrivSqZeroExt.inlAlgHom c.O _ _).comp f) hf
  have hE' : ∀ a, f a ∈ E.1 := fun a => (hE a).1
  haveI := E.2
  letI : Algebra c.O ↥E.1 := ((algebraMap c.O (PadicAlgCl l)).codRestrict E.1 fun a => f.commutes a ▸ hE' _).toAlgebra
  haveI : IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] c.O ↥E.1 := IsScalarTower.of_algebraMap_eq fun _ => Subtype.ext rfl
  let f' : U.R →ₐ[c.O] ↥E.1 :=
    { (f : U.R →+* PadicAlgCl l).codRestrict E.1 hE' with commutes' := fun a => Subtype.ext (f.commutes a) }
  obtain ⟨-, -, ρ, h1, h2⟩ := Loc.rtPoints hI.loc D.loc U ↥E.1 (push f' U.r.hom)
    ⟨f', rfl, fun a ha => Subtype.ext (hker ha)⟩
  refine ⟨E, ρ, fun g => ?_, h2⟩
  rw [h1, ← MonoidHom.comp_apply r D.ι, ← hpush]
  rfl

private theorem potentially_crystalline_conjugate_place (hI : CohInp) (h : D.IsPt U g₁ r) (g : G ℚ Ω) (hg : g = 1 ∨ g = D.cc) :
    Af.PotCrysAt (D.rhoK r h.mult) (Loc.placeJ (D.j.comp (g : Ω →+* Ω))) := by
  haveI := isAlgClosure_of_number_field Ω K
  obtain ⟨E, ρ, h1, h2⟩ := exists_local_crystalline_gl3_model D hI h
  obtain ⟨-, hcc, -, hη, -⟩ := dat_facts D
  have hop : IsOpen (D.loc.HL : Set (GQ l)) :=
    ((hη.comp_continuous D.ι_cont).isOpen_fiber 1).inter D.τ_open
  have hφ : Topology.IsOpenEmbedding D.loc.HL.subtype := hop.isOpenEmbedding_subtypeVal
  have hinj := (galEmbTop ℚ K Ω).1.injective
  have hη1 : ∀ s : ↥D.loc.HL, D.η (D.ι s) = 1 := fun s => s.2.1
  let ψ₀ : ↥D.loc.HL →* G ℚ Ω := (MulAut.conj g⁻¹).toMonoidHom.comp (D.ι.comp D.loc.HL.subtype)
  have hψ₀ : ∀ s, ψ₀ s = g⁻¹ * D.ι s * g := fun s => by simp [ψ₀]
  have hmem : ∀ s, ψ₀ s ∈ (galEmb ℚ K Ω).range := fun s => by
    rw [range_galEmb, ← D.η_ker, MonoidHom.mem_ker, hψ₀]
    simp [hη1]
  let ψ' : ↥D.loc.HL →* G K Ω := (MonoidHom.ofInjective hinj).symm.toMonoidHom.comp (ψ₀.codRestrict _ hmem)
  have hψ' : ∀ s, galEmb ℚ K Ω (ψ' s) = g⁻¹ * D.ι s * g := fun s =>
    (MonoidHom.apply_ofInjective_symm hinj _).trans (hψ₀ s)
  have hspec : ∀ (s : ↥D.loc.HL) (y : Ω), D.loc.HL.subtype s ((D.j.comp (g : Ω →+* Ω)) y) = (D.j.comp (g : Ω →+* Ω)) (ψ' s y) := by
    intro s y
    change (s : GQ l) (D.j (g y)) = D.j (g (galEmb ℚ K Ω (ψ' s) y))
    rw [D.ι_spec, hψ']
    simp
  have hρ : Continuous ρ := by
    refine continuous_of_matrix_units _ (continuous_pi fun a => continuous_pi fun b => continuous_induced_rng.2 ?_)
    have h3 : Continuous fun s : ↥D.loc.HL => (r (D.ι s)).mat a b :=
      (h.cont.comp (D.ι_cont.comp continuous_subtype_val)).matrix_elem a b
    exact h3.congr fun s => by rw [GG.mat, ← h1]; rfl
  rcases hg with rfl | rfl
  · refine Loc.potCrysOf _ _ ψ' hφ hspec _ (continuous_rho_of_point D h) E ρ (fun s a b => ?_) h2
    rw [Dat.rhoK_apply, hψ', inv_one, one_mul, mul_one, ← h1]
    rfl
  · refine Loc.potCrysOf _ _ ψ' hφ hspec _ (continuous_rho_of_point D h) E _ (fun s a b => ?_) (crystalline_of_theta_twist_gl3 _ hφ E ρ hρ h2)
    rw [Dat.rhoK_apply, hψ', conj_left_block_eq_theta r _ _ h.norm.atc ((h.mult _).trans hcc) ((h.mult _).trans (hη1 s)), ← h1,
      ← map_theta]
    rfl

private theorem exists_place_over_conjugate_embedding (w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 K)) (hw : ((l : ℕ) : 𝓞 K) ∈ w.asIdeal) :
    ∃ g : G ℚ Ω, (g = 1 ∨ g = D.cc) ∧ IsPlaceOver (Loc.placeJ (D.j.comp (g : Ω →+* Ω))) w := by
  haveI := isAlgClosure_of_number_field Ω K
  obtain ⟨-, hcc, -⟩ := dat_facts D
  obtain ⟨j, hj⟩ := (Loc.decEmb (F := K) (Ω := Ω) (v := l)).1 _ w (isPlaceOver_placeOf Ω w) hw
  obtain ⟨g, hg⟩ : ∃ g : G ℚ Ω, ∀ x, j x = D.j (g x) := by
    letI : Algebra Ω (Om l) := D.j.toAlgebra
    haveI : IsScalarTower ℚ Ω (Om l) := IsScalarTower.of_algebraMap_eq' (Subsingleton.elim _ _)
    exact ⟨AlgHom.restrictNormal' j.toRatAlgHom Ω, fun x => (AlgHom.restrictNormal_commutes j.toRatAlgHom Ω x).symm⟩
  have key : ∀ g' : G ℚ Ω, D.η g' = D.η g → IsPlaceOver (Loc.placeJ (D.j.comp (g' : Ω →+* Ω))) w := by
    intro g' hg' x
    have h1 : g'⁻¹ * g ∈ (galEmb ℚ K Ω).range := by
      rw [range_galEmb, ← D.η_ker, MonoidHom.mem_ker]
      simp [hg']
    obtain ⟨k, hk⟩ := h1
    have h2 : ∀ y : K, (D.j.comp (g' : Ω →+* Ω)) (algebraMap K Ω y) = j (algebraMap K Ω y) := fun y => by
      rw [hg, ← mul_inv_cancel_left g' g, ← hk, AlgEquiv.mul_apply, galEmb_apply, AlgEquiv.commutes]
      rfl
    rw [← isPlaceOver_placeOf Ω w x, ← hj, ← ValuationSubring.mem_nonunits_iff, ← ValuationSubring.mem_nonunits_iff,
      ValuationSubring.mem_nonunits_iff_or, ValuationSubring.mem_nonunits_iff_or, ← map_inv₀]
    simp only [Loc.placeJ, ValuationSubring.mem_comap, h2]
  rcases Int.units_eq_one_or (D.η g) with h1 | h1
  · exact ⟨1, Or.inl rfl, key _ (by rw [h1, map_one])⟩
  · exact ⟨_, Or.inr rfl, key _ (by rw [h1, hcc])⟩

end

theorem UpperTheory.V.U3.admissible_rank_three_type_one :
    CohInp → ∀ {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] {K : Type} [Field K] [NumberField K] [Algebra K Ω]
      {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {c : Coef l} {N : ℕ} [NeZero N] {e : ℕ} {ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O N} (D : Dat Ω K c e ψ) {U : D.ULoc} {g₁ : G ℚ Ω}, D.Marked g₁ → ∀ {r : G ℚ Ω →* GG 3 (PadicAlgCl l)} (h : D.IsPt U g₁ r), ∀ [IsCMField K] (ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ),
      TypeI D.η r → D.IsAutI ι r h.mult → Af.Adm3 ι e N (D.rhoK r h.mult) := by
  intro hI Ω _ _ _ K _ _ _ l _ c N _ e ψ D U g₁ hM r h _ ι hT hA
  haveI := isAlgClosure_of_number_field Ω K
  have hη : ∀ s : G K Ω, D.η (galEmb ℚ K Ω s) = 1 := fun s => by
    rw [← MonoidHom.mem_ker, D.η_ker]
    exact galEmb_mem ℚ K Ω s
  let θ : G K Ω →* (Qbar l)ˣ := (Units.map (algebraMap c.O (PadicAlgCl l)).toMonoidHom).comp
    ((Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ).comp (galEmb ℚ K Ω))
  have hθ : ∀ i : Fin 3, Af.KillsMu N (θ ^ wt1 i) := fun i s hs => by
    have : Chr.cls ℚ Ω N (galEmb ℚ K Ω s) = 1 := (Chr.clsFacts ℚ Ω N).2.2.1 _ 1 fun ζ hζ => by
      rw [Units.val_one, ZMod.val_one_eq_one_mod, ← pow_eq_pow_mod 1 hζ, pow_one, galEmb_apply, hs ζ hζ]
    simp [θ, Chr.dirG, this]
  have hΨ : ∀ s : G K Ω, D.ΨQ (galEmb ℚ K Ω s) = cycBar K Ω l s ^ (e : ℤ) * θ s := fun s => by
    have h1 : Units.map (algebraMap c.O (PadicAlgCl l)).toMonoidHom (chiO ℚ Ω l c.O (galEmb ℚ K Ω s)) =
        cycBar K Ω l s := Units.ext rfl
    rw [← h1, ← map_zpow]
    exact map_mul (Units.map (algebraMap c.O (PadicAlgCl l)).toMonoidHom) _ _
  refine ⟨⟨top_ne_bot, fun _ _ _ => trivial, fun W _ hW => hT W fun g hg v hv => ?_⟩, hA, continuous_rho_of_point D h,
    fun w hw => ?_, fun w hw => ?_⟩
  · rw [D.η_ker, ← range_galEmb] at hg
    obtain ⟨s, rfl⟩ := hg
    exact hW s v hv
  · have hB := isPlaceOver_placeOf Ω w
    have hl : ¬ (placeOf Ω w).valuation (l : Ω) < 1 := fun h => hw (((places K Ω l _).2.2.1 w hB).1 h)
    obtain ⟨M, hM⟩ := pt_inertia_away D hM h _ hl
    refine ⟨_, hB, fun i => θ ^ wt1 i, hθ, M, fun s hs => ?_⟩
    have h1 := hM (galEmb ℚ K Ω s) hs (hη s)
    have h2 : cycBar K Ω l s = 1 := by
      have : cycZ K Ω l s = 1 := (places K Ω l _).2.2.2 hl hs
      simp [cycBar, this]
    rw [Units.val_mul, Units.val_mul, Dat.rhoK_apply, ← GG.mat, h1, Matrix.mul_assoc, Units.mul_inv, Matrix.mul_one,
      GG.mat, unitaryThreeReflection_left, tor_val, hΨ, h2, _root_.one_zpow, one_mul]
    rfl
  · obtain ⟨g, hg, hB⟩ := exists_place_over_conjugate_embedding D w hw
    have hl := ((places K Ω l _).2.2.1 w hB).2 hw
    obtain ⟨-, M, h1, h2⟩ := pt_inertia_above hI D hM h _ hl
    have hF := (galEmbFam ℚ K Ω l).1 (Loc.placeJ (D.j.comp (g : Ω →+* Ω)))
    have hc : ∀ s : G K Ω, (conjBy M r (galEmb ℚ K Ω s)).left = M * D.rhoK r h.mult s * M⁻¹ := fun s =>
      conj_left_block_of_right_one M r _ ((h.mult _).trans (hη s))
    obtain ⟨κ, hκ, hκ'⟩ := exists_flag_characters_of_upper_triangular (D.rhoK r h.mult) (Dec (F := K) (Loc.placeJ (D.j.comp (g : Ω →+* Ω)))) M
      fun s hs i j hij => by
        rw [← hc]
        rw [← hF.1] at hs
        exact h1 _ hs (hη s) i j (by have : (j : ℕ) < i := hij; simp only [wt, wt1]; omega)
    refine ⟨_, hB, fun i => θ ^ wt1 i, κ, hθ, hκ, fun i s hs => Units.ext ?_, Or.inr (potentially_crystalline_conjugate_place D hI h g hg)⟩
    have h3 : ![(e : ℤ), 0, -(e : ℤ)] i = e * wt1 i := by fin_cases i <;> simp [wt1]
    rw [hκ', ← hc, h3, _root_.zpow_mul, MonoidHom.zpow_apply, ← _root_.mul_zpow, ← hΨ]
    refine (h2 _ hs i).trans ?_
    rw [GG.mat, unitaryThreeReflection_left, tor_val, Matrix.diagonal_apply_eq]


[OCH_SRC_ADM_TWO] Actual type-II extension, conductor allowance and complete rank-two admissibility
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/UnitaryThreeGroups/AdmissibleOfTypeII.lean#L1-L218

open UpperTheory.V UpperTheory.V.Base UpperTheory.V.HV UpperTheory.V.Alg UpperTheory.V.U3

private theorem cls_eq_one {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (N : ℕ) [NeZero N] (g : G ℚ Ω)
    (hg : ∀ ζ : Ω, ζ ^ N = 1 → g ζ = ζ) : Chr.cls ℚ Ω N g = 1 := by
  haveI : IsAlgClosed Ω := IsAlgClosure.isAlgClosed ℚ
  obtain ⟨ζ, hζ⟩ := HasEnoughRootsOfUnity.exists_primitiveRoot Ω N
  have h1 := Chr.cls_spec (k := ℚ) N g ζ hζ.pow_eq_one
  rw [hg ζ hζ.pow_eq_one] at h1
  have h2 : ζ ^ 1 = ζ ^ ((Chr.cls ℚ Ω N g : ZMod N)).val := by rw [pow_one]; exact h1
  have hfin : IsOfFinOrder ζ := isOfFinOrder_iff_pow_eq_one.mpr ⟨N, Nat.pos_of_ne_zero (NeZero.ne N), hζ.pow_eq_one⟩
  rw [hfin.pow_eq_pow_iff_modEq, ← hζ.eq_orderOf] at h2
  ext
  rw [Units.val_one]
  have := (ZMod.natCast_eq_natCast_iff _ _ _).mpr h2
  rw [ZMod.natCast_val, ZMod.cast_id', id, Nat.cast_one] at this
  exact this.symm

private theorem block_form {k : Type} [Field k] (X : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) k) (Y : Matrix (Fin 2) (Fin 2) k)
    (hb : ∀ a b, Odd (wt a b) → X a b = 0) (h1 : X 1 1 = 1) (hY : Y = corner X) :
    X = !![Y 0 0, 0, Y 0 1; 0, 1, 0; Y 1 0, 0, Y 1 1] := by
  subst hY
  have e01 := hb 0 1 (by decide)
  have e10 := hb 1 0 (by decide)
  have e12 := hb 1 2 (by decide)
  have e21 := hb 2 1 (by decide)
  ext i j
  fin_cases i <;> fin_cases j <;> simp [corner, e01, e10, e12, e21, h1]

private theorem conj_mat {Γ k : Type} [Group Γ] [Field k] (M : GL (Fin 3) k) (r : Γ →* GG 3 k) (g : Γ)
    (hg : (r g).right = 1) : (conjBy M r g).mat = (M : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) k) * (r g).mat * ((M⁻¹ : GL (Fin 3) k) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) k) := by
  have e : (conjBy M r g) = GG.ofGL M * r g * (GG.ofGL M)⁻¹ := rfl
  rw [e, GG.mat, ← map_inv]
  simp [SemidirectProduct.mul_left, SemidirectProduct.mul_right, hg, GG.mat, Units.val_mul]

private theorem eigvec {k : Type} [Field k] (M : GL (Fin 3) k) (X : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) k) (a : k)
    (h0 : ((M : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) k) * X * ((M⁻¹ : GL (Fin 3) k) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) k)) 0 0 = a)
    (h : ∀ i j, wt i j < 0 → ((M : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) k) * X * ((M⁻¹ : GL (Fin 3) k) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) k)) i j = 0) :
    X *ᵥ (((M⁻¹ : GL (Fin 3) k) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) k) *ᵥ Pi.single 0 1) =
      a • (((M⁻¹ : GL (Fin 3) k) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) k) *ᵥ Pi.single 0 1) := by
  set A := (M : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) k) * X * ((M⁻¹ : GL (Fin 3) k) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) k) with hA
  set Mi := ((M⁻¹ : GL (Fin 3) k) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) k)
  have hMi : Mi * (M : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) k) = 1 := by
    simp [Mi, ← Units.val_mul]
  have hXM : X * Mi = Mi * A := by
    rw [hA, ← Matrix.mul_assoc, ← Matrix.mul_assoc, hMi, Matrix.one_mul]
  have hcol : A *ᵥ Pi.single 0 1 = a • Pi.single 0 1 := by
    have h10 : A 1 0 = 0 := h 1 0 (by simp [wt, wt1])
    have h20 : A 2 0 = 0 := h 2 0 (by simp [wt, wt1])
    ext i
    fin_cases i <;> simp [Matrix.mulVec_single, h0, h10, h20]
  rw [Matrix.mulVec_mulVec, hXM, ← Matrix.mulVec_mulVec, hcol, Matrix.mulVec_smul]

private theorem exists_two {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] {l : ℕ} [hl : Fact l.Prime] (h2 : l ≠ 2)
    (A : ValuationSubring Ω) (hA : A.valuation (l : Ω) < 1) :
    ∃ s ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) A, ∀ n : ℤ, cycBar ℚ Ω l s ^ n = 1 → n = 0 := by
  have hnd : ¬ ((l : ℤ) ∣ 2) := by
    intro hd
    have := Int.le_of_dvd (by norm_num) hd
    have hle : l ≤ 2 := by exact_mod_cast this
    have := hl.out.two_le
    omega
  have hu : IsUnit (2 : ℤ_[l]) := by
    rw [PadicInt.isUnit_iff]
    have a1 := PadicInt.norm_le_one (2 : ℤ_[l])
    have a2 : ¬ ‖((2 : ℤ) : ℤ_[l])‖ < 1 := by rw [PadicInt.norm_int_lt_one_iff_dvd]; exact hnd
    push_cast at a2
    exact le_antisymm a1 (not_lt.mp a2)
  obtain ⟨s, hs, hsv⟩ := GlobalTheory.CycInertia.cycZ_Ine_surjective Ω l A hA hu.unit
  refine ⟨s, hs, fun n hn => ?_⟩
  have hinj : Function.Injective ((algebraMap ℚ_[l] (Qbar l)).comp PadicInt.Coe.ringHom) :=
    (algebraMap ℚ_[l] (Qbar l)).injective.comp Subtype.val_injective
  have hpow : hu.unit ^ n = 1 := by
    apply Units.map_injective (f := ((algebraMap ℚ_[l] (Qbar l)).comp PadicInt.Coe.ringHom).toMonoidHom) hinj
    rw [map_zpow, map_one, ← hsv]
    exact hn
  have hq : ((2 : ℚ) : ℚ_[l]) ^ n = 1 := by
    have := congrArg (fun x : ℤ_[l]ˣ => ((Units.map (PadicInt.Coe.ringHom (p := l)).toMonoidHom x : ℚ_[l]ˣ) : ℚ_[l])) hpow
    simp only [map_zpow, map_one, Units.val_one, Units.val_zpow_eq_zpow_val, Units.coe_map, IsUnit.unit_spec] at this
    have e : (PadicInt.Coe.ringHom (p := l)) (2 : ℤ_[l]) = 2 := map_ofNat _ 2
    change (PadicInt.Coe.ringHom (p := l)) 2 ^ n = 1 at this
    rw [e] at this
    simpa using this
  have hq' : (2 : ℚ) ^ n = 1 := by
    have := hq
    rw [← Rat.cast_zpow, ← Rat.cast_one] at this
    exact_mod_cast this
  exact (zpow_eq_one_iff_right₀ (by norm_num) (by norm_num)).mp hq'

theorem UpperTheory.V.U3.admissible_of_typeII :
    CohInp → ∀ {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] {K : Type} [Field K] [NumberField K] [Algebra K Ω]
      {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {c : Coef l} {N : ℕ} [NeZero N] {e : ℕ} {ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O N} (D : Dat Ω K c e ψ) {U : D.ULoc} {g₁ : G ℚ Ω}, D.Marked g₁ → ∀ {r : G ℚ Ω →* GG 3 (PadicAlgCl l)} (h : D.IsPt U g₁ r), ∀ (ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ),
      TypeII D.η r → (∀ W : G ℚ Ω →* GL (Fin 2) (PadicAlgCl l), D.IsExt r W → D.IsModular ι W) →
      Af.Adm2 ι e (N * (discr K).natAbs) (D.rhoK r h.mult) := by
  intro hC Ω _ _ _ K _ _ _ l hl c N _ e ψ D U g₁ hM r h ι hII hmod
  obtain ⟨h11, -, W, hext, hcont, hirr, -, hdet⟩ := typeII_facts hC D hM h hII
  obtain ⟨-, -, -, -, -, -, -, -, -, -, hl1⟩ := dat_facts D
  let ψQ : G ℚ Ω →* (Qbar l)ˣ := (Units.map (algebraMap c.O (PadicAlgCl l)).toMonoidHom).comp (Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ)
  have hΨ : ∀ g, D.ΨQ g = cycBar ℚ Ω l g ^ (e : ℤ) * ψQ g := fun g => by
    show Units.map (algebraMap c.O (PadicAlgCl l)).toMonoidHom (chiO ℚ Ω l c.O g ^ (e : ℤ) * Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ g) = _
    rw [map_mul, map_zpow]
    rfl
  have hfin : ∀ g, ψQ g ^ Nat.card (ZMod N)ˣ = 1 := fun g => by
    show Units.map (algebraMap c.O (PadicAlgCl l)).toMonoidHom (ψ.toUnitHom (Chr.cls ℚ Ω N g)) ^ _ = 1
    rw [← map_pow, ← map_pow, pow_card_eq_one', map_one, map_one]
  have hodd : Odd (discr K).natAbs := Nat.coprime_two_right.1 (D.gd.cop.coprime_dvd_right D.gd.N_even.two_dvd)
  have hkill : ∀ g : G ℚ Ω, (∀ ζ : Ω, ζ ^ (N * (discr K).natAbs) = 1 → g ζ = ζ) → ψQ g = 1 ∧ D.η g = 1 := fun g hg => by
    constructor
    · show Units.map (algebraMap c.O (PadicAlgCl l)).toMonoidHom (ψ.toUnitHom (Chr.cls ℚ Ω N g)) = 1
      rw [cls_eq_one N g fun ζ hζ => hg ζ (by rw [pow_mul, hζ, one_pow]), map_one, map_one]
    · rw [← MonoidHom.mem_ker, D.η_ker]
      exact UpperTheory.mem_galOf_quadratic_of_fixes_discr_roots_of_unity Ω K D.gd.quad hodd g fun ζ hζ => hg ζ (by rw [pow_mul', hζ, one_pow])
  have hblk : ∀ g ∈ D.η.ker, (r g).mat = !![W g 0 0, 0, W g 0 1; 0, 1, 0; W g 1 0, 0, W g 1 1] := fun g hg =>
    block_form _ _ (hII.1 g hg) (h11 g hg) (hext.1 g hg)
  refine ⟨W, hirr, hmod W hext, ⟨hcont, fun w hw => ?_, fun w hw => ?_⟩, ?_, 1, fun s => ?_⟩
  · obtain ⟨A, hA⟩ := exists_isPlaceOver Ω w
    have hAl : ¬ A.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 := fun h' => hw (((places ℚ Ω l A).2.2.1 w hA).1 h')
    obtain ⟨M, hMM⟩ := pt_inertia_away D hM h A hAl
    have h1 := UpperTheory.diagonal_on_subgroup_of_conjugate (Ine (F := ℚ) A ⊓ D.η.ker) W ψQ M fun g hg => by
      have hc : cycZ ℚ Ω l g = 1 := (places ℚ Ω l A).2.2.2 hAl hg.1
      have hc' : cycBar ℚ Ω l g = 1 := by
        rw [cycBar, MonoidHom.comp_apply, hc, map_one]
      rw [← hblk g hg.2, hMM g hg.1 hg.2, GG.mat, unitaryThreeReflection_left, tor_val, hΨ, hc', _root_.one_zpow, one_mul]
      congr 2
      funext i
      fin_cases i <;> simp [wt1]
    obtain ⟨χ, hχ, hd⟩ := UpperTheory.exists_isDiagOn_of_isDiagOn_inf_ker_sign _ _ W ψQ h1
    exact ⟨A, hA, χ, fun i s hs => hχ i s (hkill s hs).1 (hkill s hs).2, hd⟩
  · obtain ⟨A, hA⟩ := exists_isPlaceOver Ω w
    have hAl : A.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 := ((places ℚ Ω l A).2.2.1 w hA).2 hw
    obtain ⟨-, M, hup, hdiag⟩ := pt_inertia_above hC D hM h A hAl
    obtain ⟨hIk, s₀, hs₀, hu⟩ := hl1 A hAl
    obtain ⟨hnorm, hID, -⟩ := Chr.decCyc Ω l A hl.out hAl
    have hs₀' : (D.ΨQ s₀ : Qbar l) ^ 2 ≠ 1 := by
      have := (hu.map (algebraMap c.O (PadicAlgCl l))).ne_zero
      rw [map_sub, map_pow, map_one, sub_ne_zero] at this
      exact this
    set v : Fin 3 → Qbar l := ((M⁻¹ : GL (Fin 3) (Qbar l)) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) (Qbar l)) *ᵥ Pi.single 0 1 with hv
    have hv0 : v ≠ 0 := fun h0 => by
      have := congrArg (fun x => ((M : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) (Qbar l)) *ᵥ x) 0) (hv.symm.trans h0)
      simp only [Matrix.mulVec_mulVec, Units.mul_inv, Matrix.one_mulVec, Matrix.mulVec_zero] at this
      simp at this
    have hev : ∀ s ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) A,
        !![W s 0 0, 0, W s 0 1; 0, 1, 0; W s 1 0, 0, W s 1 1] *ᵥ v = (D.ΨQ s : Qbar l) • v := fun s hs => by
      have hr : (r s).right = 1 := (h.mult s).trans (hIk hs)
      rw [← hblk s (hIk hs)]
      refine eigvec M _ _ ?_ fun i j hij => ?_
      · rw [← conj_mat M r s hr, hdiag s hs 0]
        simp [GG.mat, tor_val, wt1]
      · rw [← conj_mat M r s hr]
        exact hup s (hID hs) (hIk hs) i j hij
    have hv1 : v 1 = 0 := by
      have := congrFun (hev s₀ hs₀) 1
      simp [Matrix.mulVec, dotProduct, Fin.sum_univ_three] at this
      by_contra hne
      have h3 : (D.ΨQ s₀ : Qbar l) = 1 := (mul_right_cancel₀ hne ((one_mul _).trans this)).symm
      exact hs₀' (by rw [h3, one_pow])
    obtain ⟨κ, hflag, hκ⟩ := UpperTheory.exists_isFlagOn_of_eigenvector_normal_subgroup (Dec (F := ℚ) A) (Ine (F := ℚ) A) W D.ΨQ ![v 0, v 2]
      (fun d hd s hs => hnorm.conj_mem' ⟨s, hID hs⟩ hs ⟨d, hd⟩)
      (fun h0 => hv0 (by
        funext i
        fin_cases i
        · exact congrFun h0 0
        · exact hv1
        · exact congrFun h0 1))
      (fun s hs => by
        have a0 := congrFun (hev s hs) 0
        have a2 := congrFun (hev s hs) 2
        funext i
        fin_cases i
        · simpa [Matrix.mulVec, dotProduct, Fin.sum_univ_three, Fin.sum_univ_two] using a0
        · simpa [Matrix.mulVec, dotProduct, Fin.sum_univ_three, Fin.sum_univ_two] using a2)
      (fun s hs => by rw [hdet, show D.η s = 1 from hIk hs]; simp)
      ⟨s₀, hs₀, hs₀'⟩
    have hκ' : ∀ i (s : ↥(Dec (F := ℚ) A)), (s : G ℚ Ω) ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) A →
        κ i s = cycBar ℚ Ω l s ^ (![(e : ℤ), -(e : ℤ)] i) * (![ψQ, ψQ⁻¹] i) s := fun i s hs => by
      fin_cases i
      · exact (hκ s hs).1.trans (hΨ s)
      · refine (hκ s hs).2.trans ?_
        rw [hΨ, mul_inv, ← _root_.zpow_neg]
        rfl
    refine ⟨A, hA, ![ψQ, ψQ⁻¹], κ, fun i s hs => ?_, hflag, hκ', Or.inl fun i j hij => ?_⟩
    · fin_cases i <;> simp [(hkill s hs).1]
    · obtain ⟨s, hs, hn⟩ := exists_two D.gd.l_odd A hAl
      have h1 := DFunLike.congr_fun hij ⟨s, hID hs⟩
      rw [MonoidHom.mul_apply, hκ' i _ hs, hκ' j _ hs] at h1
      have hf : ∀ i, ((![ψQ, ψQ⁻¹] i) s) ^ Nat.card (ZMod N)ˣ = 1 := fun i => by
        fin_cases i
        · exact hfin s
        · show (ψQ s)⁻¹ ^ _ = 1
          rw [inv_pow, hfin, inv_one]
      have h2 := congrArg (· ^ Nat.card (ZMod N)ˣ) h1
      simp only [mul_pow, hf, mul_one] at h2
      change (cycBar ℚ Ω l s ^ _) ^ _ = (cycBar ℚ Ω l s ^ _) ^ _ * cycBar ℚ Ω l s ^ _ at h2
      rw [← mul_pow, ← _root_.zpow_add_one, ← mul_inv_eq_one, ← inv_pow, ← mul_pow, ← _root_.zpow_sub, ← _root_.zpow_natCast,
        ← _root_.zpow_mul] at h2
      have h3 := (mul_eq_zero.1 (hn _ h2)).resolve_right (by exact_mod_cast Nat.card_pos.ne')
      fin_cases i <;> fin_cases j <;> simp at h3 <;> omega
  · refine ⟨N * (discr K).natAbs, Matrix.GeneralLinearGroup.det.comp W,
      Nat.mul_pos (NeZero.pos N) hodd.pos, fun s hs => Units.ext ?_, by simp⟩
    have := hdet s
    rw [(hkill s hs).2] at this
    simpa using this
  · rw [one_mul, inv_one, mul_one, D.rhoK_apply]
    exact hblk _ (D.η_ker ▸ galEmb_mem ℚ K Ω s)


[OCH_SRC_SHA_NORMALIZATION] Full canonical normalized torsion-Selmer cocycle and its additive injection
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/UnitaryThreeGroups/ShaInjGen.lean#L1-L361
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.UnitaryThreeGroups.ConjStable
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.UnitaryThreeGroups.AdRepBaseLiftApply
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.PrimitiveFrobeniusFixed.QocEquiv
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.CharacterLevelBounds.ShaFamMono

open NumberField Formalism.ConjugateSelfDual ReciprocityTheory.Gal Matrix
open UpperTheory.V UpperTheory.V.Base UpperTheory.V.U3

private theorem shaInj_phi {O W : Type} [CommRing O] [AddCommGroup W] [Module O W] (b : O) (w : W)
    (hw : ∀ y : O, y • w = 0 ↔ b ∣ y) :
    ∃ φ : W → O ⧸ Ideal.span {b}, ∀ y : O, φ (y • w) = Ideal.Quotient.mk _ y := by
  classical
  refine ⟨fun w' => if h : ∃ y : O, w' = y • w then Ideal.Quotient.mk _ h.choose else 0, fun y => ?_⟩
  have h : ∃ y' : O, y • w = y' • w := ⟨y, rfl⟩
  dsimp only
  rw [dif_pos h, Ideal.Quotient.eq, Ideal.mem_span_singleton, ← hw, sub_smul, ← h.choose_spec, sub_self]

private theorem continuous_and_cocycle_eq_of_z1U_whole_group {O Γ W : Type} [CommRing O] [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace Γ] [IsTopologicalGroup Γ]
    [AddCommGroup W] [Module O W] [TopologicalSpace W] [DiscreteTopology W] [IsTopologicalAddGroup W]
    [ContinuousConstSMul O W] (α : Γ →* Oˣ) (z : Z1U (Formalism.GaloisCohomology.scalRep W α) (⊤ : Subgroup Γ)) :
    Continuous (fun g : Γ => z.1 ⟨g, trivial⟩) ∧
      ∀ g h : Γ, z.1 ⟨g * h, trivial⟩ = z.1 ⟨g, trivial⟩ + (α g : O) • z.1 ⟨h, trivial⟩ := by
  have hz : z.1 ∈ ReciprocityTheory.Gal.Z1 ((Formalism.GaloisCohomology.scalRep W α).comp (⊤ : Subgroup Γ).subtype) := z.2
  rw [ReciprocityTheory.Gal.mem_Z1_iff_cocycle] at hz
  refine ⟨hz.1.comp (continuous_id.subtype_mk _), fun g h => ?_⟩
  have := hz.2 ⟨g, trivial⟩ ⟨h, trivial⟩
  simp [Formalism.GaloisCohomology.scalRep] at this
  exact this

private theorem shaInj_norm {O Γ W : Type} [CommRing O] [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace Γ] [IsTopologicalGroup Γ]
    [AddCommGroup W] [Module O W] [TopologicalSpace W] [DiscreteTopology W] [IsTopologicalAddGroup W]
    [ContinuousConstSMul O W] (α : Γ →* Oˣ) (δ : Γ) (hδ : IsUnit ((α δ : O) - 1)) :
    ∃ ν : H1U (Formalism.GaloisCohomology.scalRep W α) (⊤ : Subgroup Γ) →ₗ[O] (Γ → W), Function.Injective ν ∧
      ∀ z : Z1U (Formalism.GaloisCohomology.scalRep W α) (⊤ : Subgroup Γ),
      IsLocallyConstant (ν (Submodule.Quotient.mk z)) ∧
      (∀ g h, ν (Submodule.Quotient.mk z) (g * h) =
        ν (Submodule.Quotient.mk z) g + (α g : O) • ν (Submodule.Quotient.mk z) h) ∧
      ν (Submodule.Quotient.mk z) δ = 0 ∧
      ∃ m : W, ∀ g, ν (Submodule.Quotient.mk z) g = z.1 ⟨g, trivial⟩ - ((α g : O) • m - m) := by
  obtain ⟨u, hu⟩ := hδ
  let L : Z1U (Formalism.GaloisCohomology.scalRep W α) (⊤ : Subgroup Γ) →ₗ[O] (Γ → W) :=
    { toFun := fun z g => z.1 ⟨g, trivial⟩ -
        ((u⁻¹ : Oˣ) : O) • (z.1 ⟨g * δ, trivial⟩ - z.1 ⟨g, trivial⟩ - z.1 ⟨δ, trivial⟩)
      map_add' := by
        intro a b
        funext g
        simp only [Submodule.coe_add, Pi.add_apply, smul_sub, smul_add]
        abel
      map_smul' := by
        intro c a
        funext g
        simp only [SetLike.val_smul, Pi.smul_apply, RingHom.id_apply, smul_sub]
        rw [smul_comm ((u⁻¹ : Oˣ) : O) c, smul_comm ((u⁻¹ : Oˣ) : O) c, smul_comm ((u⁻¹ : Oˣ) : O) c] }
  have hL : ∀ z g, L z g = z.1 ⟨g, trivial⟩ -
      ((α g : O) • (((u⁻¹ : Oˣ) : O) • z.1 ⟨δ, trivial⟩) - ((u⁻¹ : Oˣ) : O) • z.1 ⟨δ, trivial⟩) := by
    intro z g
    have e : (α g : O) • z.1 ⟨δ, trivial⟩ = z.1 ⟨g * δ, trivial⟩ - z.1 ⟨g, trivial⟩ := by
      rw [(continuous_and_cocycle_eq_of_z1U_whole_group α z).2 g δ]
      abel
    show z.1 ⟨g, trivial⟩ - ((u⁻¹ : Oˣ) : O) • (z.1 ⟨g * δ, trivial⟩ - z.1 ⟨g, trivial⟩ - z.1 ⟨δ, trivial⟩) = _
    rw [smul_comm (α g : O), e, ← smul_sub]
  have hu' : ∀ x : W, (α δ : O) • x - x = (u : O) • x := fun x => by rw [hu, sub_smul, one_smul]
  have hB : ∀ z : Z1U (Formalism.GaloisCohomology.scalRep W α) (⊤ : Subgroup Γ),
      z.1 ∈ ReciprocityTheory.Gal.B1 ((Formalism.GaloisCohomology.scalRep W α).comp (⊤ : Subgroup Γ).subtype) ↔ L z = 0 := by
    intro z
    rw [ReciprocityTheory.Gal.mem_coboundaries1]
    constructor
    · rintro ⟨m, hm⟩
      have hm' : ∀ g : Γ, z.1 ⟨g, trivial⟩ = (α g : O) • m - m := by
        intro g
        have := hm ⟨g, trivial⟩
        simpa [Formalism.GaloisCohomology.scalRep] using this
      have hmδ : ((u⁻¹ : Oˣ) : O) • z.1 ⟨δ, trivial⟩ = m := by
        rw [hm' δ, hu', smul_smul, Units.inv_mul, one_smul]
      funext g
      rw [hL, hmδ, ← hm' g, Pi.zero_apply, sub_self]
    · intro h0
      refine ⟨((u⁻¹ : Oˣ) : O) • z.1 ⟨δ, trivial⟩, fun g => ?_⟩
      obtain ⟨g, hg⟩ := g
      have := congrFun h0 g
      rw [hL, Pi.zero_apply, sub_eq_zero] at this
      simp [Formalism.GaloisCohomology.scalRep]
      rw [smul_comm ((u⁻¹ : Oˣ) : O) (α g : O)]
      exact this
  refine ⟨Submodule.liftQ _ L (fun z hz => LinearMap.mem_ker.2 ((hB z).1 hz)), ?_, fun z => ?_⟩
  · rw [injective_iff_map_eq_zero]
    intro x hx
    obtain ⟨z, rfl⟩ := Submodule.Quotient.mk_surjective _ x
    rw [Submodule.liftQ_apply] at hx
    exact (Submodule.Quotient.mk_eq_zero _).2 ((hB z).2 hx)
  · simp only [Submodule.liftQ_apply]
    have hz := continuous_and_cocycle_eq_of_z1U_whole_group α z
    refine ⟨?_, ?_, ?_, ((u⁻¹ : Oˣ) : O) • z.1 ⟨δ, trivial⟩, fun g => hL z g⟩
    · rw [IsLocallyConstant.iff_continuous]
      exact hz.1.sub ((((hz.1.comp (continuous_mul_right δ)).sub hz.1).sub continuous_const).const_smul _)
    · intro g h
      rw [hL, hL, hL, hz.2 g h, map_mul, Units.val_mul, mul_smul, smul_sub, smul_sub]
      abel
    · rw [hL, hu', smul_smul, Units.mul_inv, one_smul, sub_self]

private def shaInj_mat {C : Type} [CommRing C] (t : C) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) C := !![0, 0, 0; t, 0, 0; 0, -t, 0]

private theorem shaInj_mat_add {C : Type} [CommRing C] (s t : C) :
    shaInj_mat (s + t) = shaInj_mat s + shaInj_mat t := by
  unfold shaInj_mat
  ext i j
  fin_cases i <;> fin_cases j <;> simp <;> ring

private theorem shaInj_mat_sub {C : Type} [CommRing C] (s t : C) :
    shaInj_mat (s - t) = shaInj_mat s - shaInj_mat t := by
  unfold shaInj_mat
  ext i j
  fin_cases i <;> fin_cases j <;> simp <;> ring

private theorem shaInj_mat_zero {C : Type} [CommRing C] : shaInj_mat (0 : C) = 0 := by
  unfold shaInj_mat
  ext i j
  fin_cases i <;> fin_cases j <;> simp

private theorem shaInj_mat_gw {C : Type} [CommRing C] (t : C) : shaInj_mat t ∈ gw C (-1) := by
  unfold gw
  intro a b hab
  unfold shaInj_mat
  fin_cases a <;> fin_cases b <;> simp_all [wt, wt1]

private theorem shaInj_ad {Γ C : Type} [Group Γ] [CommRing C] (η : Γ →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : Γ →* Cˣ) (g : Γ) (t : C) :
    adRep (r0 η Ψ) g (shaInj_mat t) = shaInj_mat ((((Ψ g)⁻¹ : Cˣ) : C) * t) := by
  ext a b
  rw [adRep_baseLift_apply]
  unfold shaInj_mat
  by_cases hη : η g = 1 <;> fin_cases a <;> fin_cases b <;> simp [hη, wt, wt1]

private theorem shaInj_map {Γ O C : Type} [Group Γ] [CommRing O] [CommRing C] (f : O →+* C) (η : Γ →* ℤˣ)
    (Ψ : Γ →* Oˣ) : (GG.map f).comp (r0 η Ψ) = r0 η ((Units.map f.toMonoidHom).comp Ψ) := by
  ext g i j
  · unfold GG.map r0
    simp [tor_val, Matrix.diagonal_apply]
    split_ifs
    · rw [← map_zpow (Units.map (f : O →* C)) (Ψ g), Units.coe_map]
      rfl
    · simp
  · unfold GG.map r0
    simp

private theorem shaInj_mem {O Γ H : Type} [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsLocalRing O] [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace Γ]
    [Group H] [TopologicalSpace H] (η : Γ →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : Γ →* Oˣ) (N : Set Γ) (ι : H →* Γ) (c g₁ : Γ) (a : O)
    (t : Γ → O ⧸ Ideal.span {a}) (hl : IsLocallyConstant t)
    (hc : ∀ g h, t (g * h) = t g + Ideal.Quotient.mk _ (((Ψ g)⁻¹ : Oˣ) : O) * t h)
    (h0 : t c = 0) (h1 : t g₁ = 0) (h2 : t (gstar c g₁) = 0) (hN : ∀ g ∈ N, t g = 0)
    (hι : ∃ s, ∀ h, t (ι h) = s - Ideal.Quotient.mk _ (((Ψ (ι h))⁻¹ : Oˣ) : O) * s) :
    (fun g => shaInj_mat (t g)) ∈ shaCoc η Ψ N ι c g₁ a := by
  have hρ := shaInj_map (Ideal.Quotient.mk (Ideal.span {a})) η Ψ
  have hU : ∀ g : Γ, ((((Units.map (Ideal.Quotient.mk (Ideal.span {a})).toMonoidHom).comp Ψ g)⁻¹ :
      (O ⧸ Ideal.span {a})ˣ) : O ⧸ Ideal.span {a}) = Ideal.Quotient.mk _ (((Ψ g)⁻¹ : Oˣ) : O) := by
    intro g
    simp [map_inv]
  have had : ∀ (g : Γ) (x : O ⧸ Ideal.span {a}),
      adRep ((GG.map (Ideal.Quotient.mk (Ideal.span {a}))).comp (r0 η Ψ)) g (shaInj_mat x) =
        shaInj_mat (Ideal.Quotient.mk _ (((Ψ g)⁻¹ : Oˣ) : O) * x) := by
    intro g x
    rw [hρ, shaInj_ad, hU]
  have hz : shaInj_mat (0 : O ⧸ Ideal.span {a}) = 0 := shaInj_mat_zero
  unfold shaCoc
  refine ⟨hl.comp shaInj_mat, fun g => shaInj_mat_gw _, ?_, ?_, ?_, ?_⟩
  · unfold IsCoc
    intro g h
    beta_reduce
    rw [had, hc, shaInj_mat_add]
  · refine ⟨?_, ?_, ?_⟩
    · beta_reduce
      rw [h0, hz]
    · unfold zvar
      beta_reduce
      rw [h1, h2, hz]
      simp
    · unfold zvar
      beta_reduce
      rw [h1, h2, hz]
      simp
  · intro g hg
    beta_reduce
    rw [hN g hg, hz]
  · obtain ⟨s, hs⟩ := hι
    refine ⟨shaInj_mat s, shaInj_mat_gw _, fun h => ?_⟩
    unfold cob
    beta_reduce
    rw [had, hs h, shaInj_mat_sub]

private theorem shaInj_unit {O : Type} [CommRing O] (x : Oˣ) (h : IsUnit ((x : O) ^ 2 - 1)) :
    IsUnit (((x⁻¹ : Oˣ) : O) - 1) := by
  have h1 : IsUnit ((x : O) - 1) :=
    isUnit_of_mul_isUnit_left (by rwa [show ((x : O) - 1) * ((x : O) + 1) = (x : O) ^ 2 - 1 by ring])
  have e : ((x⁻¹ : Oˣ) : O) - 1 = ((-x⁻¹ : Oˣ) : O) * ((x : O) - 1) := by
    rw [Units.val_neg, neg_mul, mul_sub, Units.inv_mul]
    ring
  rw [e]
  exact (Units.isUnit _).mul h1

private theorem shaInj_kill {O W : Type} [CommRing O] [AddCommGroup W] [Module O W] {x : O} (hx : IsUnit (x - 1))
    {m : W} (h : x • m - m = 0) : m = 0 := by
  obtain ⟨u, hu⟩ := hx
  have h' : (x - 1) • m = 0 := by rw [sub_smul, one_smul]; exact h
  calc m = (((u⁻¹ : Oˣ) : O) * (x - 1)) • m := by rw [← hu, Units.inv_mul, one_smul]
    _ = 0 := by rw [mul_smul, h', smul_zero]

private theorem shaInj_sel {O Γ W : Type} [CommRing O] [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace Γ] [IsTopologicalGroup Γ]
    [AddCommGroup W] [Module O W] [TopologicalSpace W] [DiscreteTopology W] [IsTopologicalAddGroup W]
    [ContinuousConstSMul O W] (α : Γ →* Oˣ) (δ : Γ) (hδ : IsUnit ((α δ : O) - 1)) (𝒟 : Set (Subgroup Γ)) :
    ∃ ν : H1U (Formalism.GaloisCohomology.scalRep W α) (⊤ : Subgroup Γ) →ₗ[O] (Γ → W), Function.Injective ν ∧
      ∀ x ∈ LocSel (Formalism.GaloisCohomology.scalRep W α) ⊤ 𝒟, IsLocallyConstant (ν x) ∧
        (∀ g h, ν x (g * h) = ν x g + (α g : O) • ν x h) ∧ ν x δ = 0 ∧
        ∀ D ∈ 𝒟, ∃ m : W, ∀ g ∈ D, ν x g = (α g : O) • m - m := by
  obtain ⟨ν, hνi, hν⟩ := shaInj_norm (W := W) α δ hδ
  refine ⟨ν, hνi, fun x hx => ?_⟩
  obtain ⟨z, rfl⟩ := Submodule.Quotient.mk_surjective _ x
  obtain ⟨h1, h2, h3, m0, h4⟩ := hν z
  refine ⟨h1, h2, h3, fun D hD => ?_⟩
  obtain ⟨m, hm⟩ := UpperTheory.V.Base.mk_mem_locSel.1 hx D hD
  refine ⟨m - m0, fun g hg => ?_⟩
  have := hm ⟨g, trivial⟩ hg
  simp [Formalism.GaloisCohomology.scalRep] at this
  rw [h4, this, smul_sub]
  abel

private theorem shaInj_gen {O Γ H W X : Type} [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsLocalRing O] [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace Γ]
    [Group H] [TopologicalSpace H]
    [AddCommGroup W] [Module O W] [AddCommGroup X] [Module O X]
    (η : Γ →* ℤˣ) (Ψ α : Γ →* Oˣ) (N : Set Γ) (ι : H →* Γ) (c g₁ : Γ) (b : O)
    (𝒟 : Set (Subgroup Γ)) (hαΨ : ∀ g, α g = (Ψ g)⁻¹) (hM : Marks η Ψ c g₁) (h2 : IsUnit (2 : O))
    (w : W) (hw : ∀ y : O, y • w = 0 ↔ b ∣ y) (hgen : ∀ w' : W, b • w' = 0 → ∃ y : O, w' = y • w)
    (hK0 : ∀ g ∈ N, r0 η Ψ g = 1) (hK : ∀ g ∈ N, ∃ D ∈ 𝒟, g ∈ D) (hD : ι.range ∈ 𝒟)
    (s' : H) (hsu : IsUnit ((Ψ (ι s') : O) ^ 2 - 1)) (S : Submodule O X) (ν : X →ₗ[O] (Γ → W))
    (hνi : Function.Injective ν)
    (hν : ∀ x ∈ S, IsLocallyConstant (ν x) ∧ (∀ g h, ν x (g * h) = ν x g + (α g : O) • ν x h) ∧ ν x g₁ = 0 ∧
      ∀ D ∈ 𝒟, ∃ m : W, ∀ g ∈ D, ν x g = (α g : O) • m - m) :
    ∃ γ : Submodule.torsionBy O S b →+ (Γ → Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) (O ⧸ Ideal.span {b})),
      Function.Injective γ ∧ ∀ x, γ x ∈ shaCoc η Ψ N ι c g₁ b := by
  obtain ⟨φ, hφ⟩ := shaInj_phi b w hw
  have hcancel : ∀ (u : Oˣ) (m : W), (u : O) • m = 0 → m = 0 := by
    intro u m h
    calc m = (((u⁻¹ : Oˣ) : O) * (u : O)) • m := by rw [Units.inv_mul, one_smul]
      _ = 0 := by rw [mul_smul, h, smul_zero]
  have hφ0 : φ 0 = 0 := by rw [← zero_smul O w, hφ, map_zero]
  have hφadd : ∀ u v : W, b • u = 0 → b • v = 0 → ∀ y : O,
      φ (u + y • v) = φ u + Ideal.Quotient.mk _ y * φ v := by
    intro u v hu hv y
    obtain ⟨yu, rfl⟩ := hgen u hu
    obtain ⟨yv, rfl⟩ := hgen v hv
    rw [smul_smul, ← add_smul, hφ, hφ, hφ, map_add, map_mul]
  have hφinj : ∀ u : W, b • u = 0 → φ u = 0 → u = 0 := by
    intro u hu h
    obtain ⟨y, rfl⟩ := hgen u hu
    rw [hφ, Ideal.Quotient.eq_zero_iff_mem, Ideal.mem_span_singleton] at h
    exact (hw y).mpr h
  have htor : ∀ x : Submodule.torsionBy O S b, ∀ g, b • ν ((x : S) : X) g = 0 := by
    intro x g
    have hx : b • (x : S) = 0 := (Submodule.mem_torsionBy_iff _ _).mp x.2
    have hx' : b • ((x : S) : X) = 0 := congrArg Subtype.val hx
    rw [← Pi.smul_apply, ← map_smul, hx', map_zero, Pi.zero_apply]
  refine ⟨{ toFun := fun x g => shaInj_mat (φ (ν ((x : S) : X) g)), map_zero' := ?_, map_add' := ?_ }, ?_, ?_⟩
  · funext g
    simp [hφ0, shaInj_mat_zero]
  · intro x y
    funext g
    have e := hφadd _ _ (htor x g) (htor y g) 1
    rw [one_smul, map_one, one_mul] at e
    simp only [Submodule.coe_add, map_add, Pi.add_apply]
    rw [e, shaInj_mat_add]
  · rw [injective_iff_map_eq_zero]
    intro x hx
    have ht : ∀ g, ν ((x : S) : X) g = 0 := by
      intro g
      have e := congrFun (congrFun (congrFun hx g) 1) 0
      change shaInj_mat (φ (ν ((x : S) : X) g)) 1 0 = 0 at e
      unfold shaInj_mat at e
      simp at e
      exact hφinj _ (htor x g) e
    have hv : ((x : S) : X) = 0 := by
      apply hνi
      rw [map_zero]
      funext g
      exact ht g
    apply Subtype.ext
    apply Subtype.ext
    simpa using hv
  · intro x
    obtain ⟨hlc, hcoc, hg1, hloc⟩ := hν ((x : S) : X) (x : S).2
    have hb' := htor x
    have hone : ν ((x : S) : X) 1 = 0 := by simpa using hcoc 1 1
    have hinv : ∀ g, ν ((x : S) : X) g = 0 → ν ((x : S) : X) g⁻¹ = 0 := by
      intro g hg
      have e := hcoc g g⁻¹
      rw [mul_inv_cancel, hone, hg, zero_add] at e
      exact hcancel (α g) _ e.symm
    have hc0 : ν ((x : S) : X) c = 0 := by
      have e := hcoc c c
      rw [hM.csq, hone, hαΨ, hM.Ψc, inv_one, Units.val_one, one_smul] at e
      obtain ⟨u, hu⟩ := h2
      apply hcancel u
      rw [hu, two_smul]
      exact e.symm
    have hN0 : ∀ g, r0 η Ψ g = 1 → Ψ g = 1 := by
      intro g h
      unfold r0 at h
      have h1 := congrArg SemidirectProduct.left h
      simp only [MonoidHom.coe_mk, OneHom.coe_mk, SemidirectProduct.one_left] at h1
      have h2 := congrArg (fun M : GL (Fin 3) O => (M : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) O) 0 0) h1
      simpa [tor_val, wt1] using h2
    apply shaInj_mem η Ψ N ι c g₁ b (fun g => φ (ν ((x : S) : X) g)) (hlc.comp φ)
    · intro g h
      rw [hcoc g h, hαΨ g]
      exact hφadd _ _ (hb' g) (hb' h) _
    · rw [hc0, hφ0]
    · rw [hg1, hφ0]
    · have e1 := hcoc c g₁⁻¹
      rw [hc0, hinv g₁ hg1, smul_zero, add_zero] at e1
      have e2 := hcoc (c * g₁⁻¹) c
      rw [hc0, smul_zero, add_zero, e1] at e2
      unfold gstar
      rw [e2, hφ0]
    · intro g hg
      obtain ⟨D, hD', hgD⟩ := hK g hg
      obtain ⟨m, hm⟩ := hloc D hD'
      rw [hm g hgD, hαΨ, hN0 g (hK0 g hg), inv_one, Units.val_one, one_smul, sub_self, hφ0]
    · obtain ⟨m, hm⟩ := hloc ι.range hD
      have hbm : b • m = 0 := by
        have e := hb' (ι s')
        rw [hm _ (MonoidHom.mem_range.mpr ⟨s', rfl⟩), smul_sub, smul_comm b] at e
        have hu := shaInj_unit (Ψ (ι s')) hsu
        rw [← hαΨ] at hu
        exact shaInj_kill hu e
      obtain ⟨y, rfl⟩ := hgen m hbm
      refine ⟨Ideal.Quotient.mk _ (-y), fun h => ?_⟩
      rw [hm _ (MonoidHom.mem_range.mpr ⟨h, rfl⟩), hαΨ, smul_smul, ← sub_smul, hφ, map_sub, map_mul, map_neg]
      ring

set_option maxHeartbeats 1000000 in
theorem UpperTheory.V.U3.sha_inj_gen {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [Algebra ℚ Ω] {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O]
    [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [Algebra ℤ_[l] O] [Module.Finite ℤ_[l] O] {H : Type} [Group H]
    [TopologicalSpace H] (e n : ℕ) (θ : G ℚ Ω →* Oˣ) (η : G ℚ Ω →* ℤˣ) (N : Set (G ℚ Ω)) (ι : H →* G ℚ Ω)
    (c g₁ : G ℚ Ω) (b : O) (hb : b ≠ 0) (hM : Marks η (Chr.Psi l e θ) c g₁) (h2 : IsUnit (2 : O))
    (hK0 : ∀ g ∈ N, r0 η (Chr.Psi l e θ) g = 1) (hK : ∀ g ∈ N, ∃ D ∈ shaFam ℚ Ω n, g ∈ D)
    (hD : ι.range ∈ shaFam ℚ Ω n) (s' : H) (hsu : IsUnit ((Chr.Psi l e θ (ι s') : O) ^ 2 - 1)) :
    ∃ γ : Submodule.torsionBy O (ShaU (Chr.rho l e θ) n ⊤) b →+
        (G ℚ Ω → Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) (O ⧸ Ideal.span {b})),
      Function.Injective γ ∧ ∀ x, γ x ∈ shaCoc η (Chr.Psi l e θ) N ι c g₁ b := by
  have hq := ML.qoCyc l O
  have hw2 : ∀ a : O, a ≠ 0 → ∃ w : QO l O, ∀ y : O, y • w = 0 ↔ a ∣ y := hq.2.1
  have hw3 : ∀ (a : O) (w : QO l O), a ≠ 0 → (∀ y : O, y • w = 0 ↔ a ∣ y) →
      ∀ w' : QO l O, a • w' = 0 → ∃ y : O, w' = y • w := hq.2.2.1
  obtain ⟨w, hw⟩ := hw2 b hb
  have hgen := hw3 b w hb hw
  have hαΨ : ∀ g, (θ⁻¹ * chiO ℚ Ω l O ^ (-(e : ℤ))) g = (Chr.Psi l e θ g)⁻¹ := by
    intro g
    rw [← MonoidHom.inv_apply, Chr.Psi_inv]
  have hδ : IsUnit ((((θ⁻¹ * chiO ℚ Ω l O ^ (-(e : ℤ))) g₁ : Oˣ) : O) - 1) := by
    rw [hαΨ]
    exact shaInj_unit _ hM.Ψg
  obtain ⟨ν, hνi, hν⟩ := shaInj_sel (W := QO l O) (θ⁻¹ * chiO ℚ Ω l O ^ (-(e : ℤ))) g₁ hδ (shaFam ℚ Ω n)
  exact shaInj_gen η (Chr.Psi l e θ) (θ⁻¹ * chiO ℚ Ω l O ^ (-(e : ℤ))) N ι c g₁ b (shaFam ℚ Ω n) hαΨ hM h2
    w hw hgen hK0 hK hD s' hsu (ShaU (Chr.rho l e θ) n ⊤) ν hνi hν


[OCH_SRC_SHA_INJECTION] Original shaFam(ell*N) local placement and literal sha_inj
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/UnitaryThreeGroups/ShaInj.lean#L13-L37
    D.ι.range ∈ shaFam ℚ Ω (l * N) ∧ ∃ s', IsUnit ((D.Ψ (D.ι s') : c.O) ^ 2 - 1) := by
  obtain ⟨A, hA, hD, hIn, -⟩ := dat_loc D
  obtain ⟨s, hs, hsu⟩ := ((dat_facts D).2.2.2.2.2.2.2.2.2.2 A hA).2
  rw [← hIn] at hs
  obtain ⟨s', -, hs'⟩ := Subgroup.mem_map.1 hs
  refine ⟨?_, s', by rw [hs']; exact hsu⟩
  rw [hD]
  exact Chr.shaFam_mono (dvd_mul_right l N) (Or.inl ⟨A, hA, rfl⟩)

private theorem shaInj_away {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] {K : Type} [Field K] [NumberField K] [Algebra K Ω]
    {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {c : Coef l} {N : ℕ} [NeZero N] {e : ℕ} {ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O N} (D : Dat Ω K c e ψ) :
    ∀ g ∈ D.Kill, ∃ D' ∈ shaFam ℚ Ω (l * N), g ∈ D' := by
  rintro g ⟨A', -, hg', -⟩
  exact ⟨_, Or.inr ⟨A', rfl⟩, hg'⟩
