theorem UpperTheory.V.U3.sha_inj :
    ∀ {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] {K : Type} [Field K] [NumberField K] [Algebra K Ω]
      {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {c : Coef l} {N : ℕ} [NeZero N] {e : ℕ} {ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O N} (D : Dat Ω K c e ψ) (g₁ : G ℚ Ω), D.Marked g₁ → ∀ b : c.O, b ≠ 0 → ∃ a : c.O, a ≠ 0 ∧
      ∃ γ : Submodule.torsionBy c.O (ShaU (Chr.rho l e (Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ)) (l * N) ⊤) b →+ (G ℚ Ω → CocVal a),
        Function.Injective γ ∧ ∀ x, γ x ∈ shaCoc D.η D.Ψ D.Kill D.ι D.cc g₁ a := by
  intro Ω _ _ _ K _ _ _ l _ c N _ e ψ D g₁ hM b hb
  exact ⟨b, hb, sha_inj_gen (O := c.O) e (l * N) (Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ) D.η D.Kill D.ι D.cc g₁ b hb hM
    (dat_facts D).2.2.2.2.2.2.1 (dat_facts D).2.2.2.2.2.1 (shaInj_away D) (shaInj_loc D).1 _
    (shaInj_loc D).2.choose_spec⟩


[OCH_SRC_MODEL_LOWER] Actual square-zero ring, map and integral lift into the lower length comparison
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/UnitaryThreeGroups/ModelLower.lean#L296-L401
private theorem main_der (S : SRing N hι U I cc g₁) (M : Model S K) (hc : Ψ cc = 1) (hU : LocGood U I)
    (hΨ : ∀ j : ℤ, AdicCont (maximalIdeal O) fun g => ((Ψ g ^ j : Oˣ) : O)) (hN : ∀ g ∈ N, r0 η Ψ g = 1)
    {a : O} (ha : a ≠ 0) (γ : Γ → Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) (O ⧸ Ideal.span {a})) (hγ : γ ∈ shaCoc η Ψ N ι cc g₁ a) :
    ∃ D ∈ PtDer M.x₀ (O ⧸ Ideal.span {a}),
      (∀ g i j, D ((S.r.hom g).mat i j) = ((Ψ g ^ wt1 j : Oˣ) : O) • γ g i j) ∧
      ∃ D' ∈ PtDer M.x O, HasWt M.𝒜 (-1) D' ∧ ∀ f, D (M.q f) = Ideal.Quotient.mk _ (D' f) := by
  haveI : IsCO O (TrivSqZeroExt O O) := co_sqz O O O fun _ => rfl
  haveI : IsCO O (TrivSqZeroExt O (O ⧸ Ideal.span {a})) := co_sqz O O _ fun _ => rfl
  obtain ⟨hlc, hw, hcoc, hnorm, hkill, X, hX, hcob⟩ := hγ
  change IsCoc (rb η Ψ _) γ at hcoc
  change NormCoc cc g₁ (rb η Ψ _) γ at hnorm
  change ∀ h, γ (ι h) = cob (rb (η.comp ι) (Ψ.comp ι) _) X h at hcob
  have hΨ' : ∀ j : ℤ, AdicCont (maximalIdeal O) fun g => (((Ψ.comp ι) g ^ j : Oˣ) : O) :=
    fun j n => (hΨ j n).comp_continuous hι
  obtain ⟨r, hr⟩ := ex_lift hΨ γ hcoc fun i j => ac_lc (hlc.comp fun Z => inr (Z i j))
  obtain ⟨Y, hYw, hYX, hY⟩ := lift_X (η := η.comp ι) hΨ' ha X hX (by
    rw [← funext hcob]
    exact hlc.comp_continuous hι)
  obtain ⟨xl, F₁, hxl, hFI, hF₁⟩ := loc_pt U I hU hΨ' Y hY
  obtain ⟨D₁, hD₁, hD₁F, hD₁e⟩ := ex_der U xl hxl _ F₁ hF₁
  have hred : ∀ h i j, Ideal.Quotient.mk (Ideal.span {a}) (cob (rb (η.comp ι) (Ψ.comp ι) O) Y h i j) =
      γ (ι h) i j := fun h i j => by
    rw [hcob, ← hYX]
    simp only [cob, Matrix.sub_apply, rb_ad, map_sub, Matrix.map_apply, Algebra.smul_def, map_mul]
    split_ifs
    · rfl
    · rw [map_neg]; rfl
  have hP : NormCond cc g₁ (BoxCond N ι hι U I) _ r := by
    refine ⟨(isDeform_normalised_iff _ _ _ cc g₁ ?_ hr).2 ⟨?_, ?_, ?_⟩, fun g hg => ?_,
      (TrivSqZeroExt.map (Algebra.linearMap O (O ⧸ Ideal.span {a}))).comp F₁, glift_ext fun h i j => ?_,
      fun f hf => ?_⟩
    · rw [rb_eq]
      exact baseRepGL3_normalised_of_psi_eq_one _ _ _ (by simp [hc])
    · show (γ cc).map inr = 0
      rw [hnorm.atc]
      exact Matrix.ext fun i j => rfl
    · have h := hnorm.z12
      simp only [zvar, Matrix.sub_apply, mul_rb, Matrix.map_apply] at h ⊢
      rw [← inr_smul, ← inr_smul, ← inr_sub, h, inr_zero]
    · have h := hnorm.z21
      simp only [zvar, Matrix.sub_apply, mul_rb, Matrix.map_apply] at h ⊢
      rw [← inr_smul, ← inr_smul, ← inr_sub, h, inr_zero]
    · have h1 : rb η Ψ (TrivSqZeroExt O (O ⧸ Ideal.span {a})) g = 1 := by
        rw [MonoidHom.comp_apply, hN g hg, map_one]
      refine SemidirectProduct.ext (Units.ext ?_) ((hr g).1.trans (congrArg SemidirectProduct.right h1))
      refine (hr g).2.trans ?_
      have h2 : (γ g).map (inr : _ → TrivSqZeroExt O (O ⧸ Ideal.span {a})) = 0 := by
        rw [hkill g hg]
        exact Matrix.ext fun i j => rfl
      beta_reduce
      rw [h1, h2, add_zero, Matrix.one_mul]
      rfl
    · have h1 := deform_mat hF₁ h i j
      refine (congrArg (TrivSqZeroExt.map (Algebra.linearMap O (O ⧸ Ideal.span {a}))) h1).trans ?_
      refine Eq.trans ?_ (deform_mat hr (ι h) i j).symm
      rw [map_add, map_smul, AlgHom.commutes, Matrix.map_apply, Matrix.map_apply, TrivSqZeroExt.map_inr, ← hred]
      rfl
    · show TrivSqZeroExt.map _ (F₁ f) = 0
      rw [hFI hf, map_zero]
  obtain ⟨F, hF, -⟩ := (S.rep _).2 r hP
  obtain ⟨D, hD, -, hDe⟩ := ex_der S M.x₀ M.base γ F (hF ▸ hr)
  refine ⟨D, hD, hDe, M.lift a ha D hD (fun g i j h => ?_) xl hxl D₁ hD₁ (fun y hy => ?_) (fun g i j h => ?_)
    fun h i j => ?_⟩
  · rw [hDe, hw g i j h, smul_zero]
  · rw [hD₁F, hFI hy]; rfl
  · rw [hD₁e, cob, Matrix.sub_apply, rb_ad, hYw i j h, hYw j.rev i.rev (by
      revert h i j; decide)]
    simp
  · rw [hD₁e, hDe, ← hred, Algebra.smul_def, Algebra.smul_def, map_mul]
    rfl

end Main

end UpperTheory.V.U3

end

theorem UpperTheory.V.U3.model_lower :
    ∀ {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal O) O] [Finite (ResidueField O)] {K : Type} [NormedField K] [Algebra O K] {Γ H : Type} [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace Γ] [IsTopologicalGroup Γ] [Group H] [TopologicalSpace H] [IsTopologicalGroup H]
      {η : Γ →* ℤˣ} {Ψ : Γ →* (O)ˣ} {N : Set Γ} {ι : H →* Γ} {hι : Continuous ι}
      {U : Univ (GCond.noCondition O (η.comp ι) (Ψ.comp ι))} {I : Ideal U.R} {cc g₁ : Γ} (S : SRing N hι U I cc g₁) (M : Model S K), IsUnit (2 : O) → Marks η Ψ cc g₁ →
      LemmaM' → LocGood U I → (∀ j : ℤ, AdicCont (maximalIdeal O) fun g => ((Ψ g ^ j : Oˣ) : O)) → (∀ g ∈ N, r0 η Ψ g = 1) →
      ∀ a : O, a ≠ 0 → ∀ (MM : Type) [AddCommGroup MM] [Module O MM]
        (γ : MM →+ (Γ → Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) (O ⧸ Ideal.span {a}))), Function.Injective γ → (∀ x, γ x ∈ shaCoc η Ψ N ι cc g₁ a) →
        Module.length O MM ≤ M.len := by
  intro O _ _ _ _ _ K _ _ Γ H _ _ _ _ _ _ η Ψ N ι hι U I cc g₁ S M _ hm hM hU hΨ hN a ha MM _ _ γ hinj hγ
  haveI : IsCO O (TrivSqZeroExt O (O ⧸ Ideal.span {a})) := co_sqz O O _ fun _ => rfl
  choose D hD hDe hl using fun μ => main_der S M hm.Ψc hU hΨ hN ha (γ μ) (hγ μ)
  have hadd : ∀ μ ν, D (μ + ν) = D μ + D ν := fun μ ν =>
    der_uniq S M.x₀ (hD _) (add_mem (hD μ) (hD ν)) fun g i j => by
      rw [LinearMap.add_apply, hDe, hDe, hDe, map_add, Pi.add_apply, Matrix.add_apply, smul_add]
  refine hM O M.A ℤ M.𝒜 M.x (-1) (M.wring.fixed _ (by decide)) _ M.Q.F M.Q.hF a ha MM
    (AddMonoidHom.mk' (fun μ => (D μ).comp M.q.toLinearMap) fun μ ν => by rw [hadd, LinearMap.add_comp])
    ((injective_iff_map_eq_zero _).2 fun μ h => hinj ?_) (fun μ i => ?_) hl
  · rw [map_zero]
    funext g
    refine Matrix.ext fun i j => ?_
    obtain ⟨f, -, hf⟩ := M.ent g i j
    have h1 : D μ (M.q f) = 0 := DFunLike.congr_fun h f
    rw [hf, hDe] at h1
    exact ((Ψ g ^ wt1 j).isUnit.smul_eq_zero).1 h1
  · have h1 : M.Q.F i ∈ RingHom.ker M.q := by
      rw [M.ker_q]
      exact Ideal.subset_span (by simp)
    show D μ (M.q _) = 0
    rw [h1, map_zero]


[OCH_SRC_VLEN_LOWER] Full original vlen_le before finiteness, via the union of torsion layers
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/UnitaryThreeGroups/VlenLe.lean#L13-L67
private theorem first_cohomology_torsion {Γ : Type} [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace Γ] [IsTopologicalGroup Γ] [CompactSpace Γ]
    (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (O : Type)
    [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [Algebra ℤ_[l] O] [Module.Finite ℤ_[l] O]
    (α : Γ →* Oˣ) (x : H1U (twist₂ l α) (⊤ : Subgroup Γ)) : ∃ a : O, a ≠ 0 ∧ a • x = 0 := by
  classical
  choose k hk0 hk using (UpperTheory.V.ML.qoCyc l O).1
  obtain ⟨z, rfl⟩ := Submodule.Quotient.mk_surjective _ x
  have hc : IsLocallyConstant fun g : Γ => z.1 ⟨g, trivial⟩ :=
    (IsLocallyConstant.iff_continuous _).2 (z.2.1.comp (continuous_id.subtype_mk _))
  have hfin := hc.range_finite
  refine ⟨∏ w ∈ hfin.toFinset, k w, Finset.prod_ne_zero_iff.2 fun w _ => hk0 w, ?_⟩
  have hz : (∏ w ∈ hfin.toFinset, k w) • z = 0 := Subtype.ext (funext fun g =>
    (hk _ _).2 (Finset.dvd_prod_of_mem _ (hfin.mem_toFinset.2 ⟨g.1, rfl⟩)))
  rw [← Submodule.Quotient.mk_smul, hz, Submodule.Quotient.mk_zero]

private theorem len_of_torsion (O : Type) [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O]
    (M : Type) [AddCommGroup M] [Module O M] (h : ∀ x : M, ∃ a : O, a ≠ 0 ∧ a • x = 0) (n : ℕ∞)
    (hn : ∀ b : O, b ≠ 0 → Module.length O (Submodule.torsionBy O M b) ≤ n) : Module.length O M ≤ n := by
  obtain ⟨ϖ, hϖ⟩ := IsDiscreteValuationRing.exists_irreducible O
  rw [← Module.length_top]
  refine Kum.lenDirected O _ _ (fun k => Submodule.torsionBy O M (ϖ ^ k)) n ?_ ?_
    fun k => ⟨k, le_rfl, hn _ (pow_ne_zero _ hϖ.ne_zero)⟩
  · intro i j hij
    exact Submodule.torsionBy_le_torsionBy_of_dvd _ _ (pow_dvd_pow _ hij)
  · intro x _
    obtain ⟨a, ha, hx⟩ := h x
    obtain ⟨k, hk⟩ := IsDiscreteValuationRing.associated_pow_irreducible ha hϖ
    exact Submodule.mem_iSup_of_mem k (Submodule.torsionBy_le_torsionBy_of_dvd _ _ hk.dvd
      ((Submodule.mem_torsionBy_iff _ _).2 hx))

private theorem vs_le {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (O : Type)
    [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [Algebra ℤ_[l] O] [Module.Finite ℤ_[l] O]
    (m e : ℕ) (θ : G ℚ Ω →* Oˣ) (n : ℕ∞)
    (hn : ∀ b : O, b ≠ 0 → Module.length O (Submodule.torsionBy O (ShaU (Chr.rho l e θ) m ⊤) b) ≤ n) :
    Chr.VS l m e θ ≤ n := by
  refine len_of_torsion O _ (fun x => ?_) _ hn
  obtain ⟨a, ha, hx⟩ := first_cohomology_torsion l O _ x.1
  exact ⟨a, ha, Subtype.ext hx⟩

theorem UpperTheory.V.U3.vlen_le :
    CohInp → LemmaM' → ∀ {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] {K : Type} [Field K] [NumberField K] [Algebra K Ω]
      {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {c : Coef l} {N : ℕ} [NeZero N] {e : ℕ} {ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O N} (D : Dat Ω K c e ψ) {U : D.ULoc} {g₁ : G ℚ Ω}, D.Marked g₁ → ∀ (S : D.Ring U g₁), ∀ M : Model S (PadicAlgCl l), Vlen Ω c e ψ ≤ M.len := by
  intro hC hM' Ω _ _ _ K _ _ _ l _ c N _ e ψ D U g₁ hg S M
  haveI : IsLocalRing c.O := c.dvr.toIsLocalRing
  haveI : IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal c.O) c.O := (UpperTheory.V.Base.dvrFacts l c.O).2.2.2.1
  haveI : Finite (ResidueField c.O) := by
    haveI : IsLocalHom (algebraMap ℤ_[l] c.O) := (UpperTheory.V.Base.dvrFacts l c.O).2.2.1
    haveI : Finite (ResidueField ℤ_[l]) := Finite.of_equiv _ (PadicInt.residueField (p := l)).symm.toEquiv
    exact Module.finite_of_finite (ResidueField ℤ_[l])
  obtain ⟨-, -, -, -, -, hK, h2, -⟩ := dat_facts D
  have hG : LocGood U (D.loc.It U) := Loc.locGood hC.loc D.loc U
  refine vs_le l c.O (l * N) e (Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ) _ fun b hb => ?_
  have h := sha_inj D g₁ hg b hb
  refine h.elim fun a h => h.2.elim fun γ hγ => ?_
  exact model_lower S M h2 hg hM' hG D.Ψ_cont hK a h.1 _ γ hγ.1 hγ.2


[OCH_SRC_VLEN_NORM] Original Vlen and normalized Vn with exact level ell*N
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/HeightAndVanishing/DirichletPrimeToLPartMulLPowerPart.lean#L103-L118

def PsiEven (e : ℕ) (ψ : DirichletCharacter O N) : Prop := ψ (-1) = (-1) ^ e

def EV (l : ℕ) (ψ : DirichletCharacter O N) : Prop := Even (orderOf (chiP l ψ))

theorem dirichletPrimeToLPart_mul_lPowerPart (l : ℕ) (ψ : DirichletCharacter O N) : th0 l ψ * kap l ψ = ψ := lPrime_mul_lPart l ψ

end Data

section Len

variable (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] (c : Coef l) {N : ℕ} [NeZero N]

def Vlen (e : ℕ) (ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O N) : ℕ∞ := Chr.V' l N e (Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ)

def Vn (e : ℕ) (ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O N) : ℝ := Chr.nrm l c.O (Vlen Ω c e ψ)


[OCH_SRC_MODEL_M1] Actual rational cotangent spanning from normalized nonpositive-weight rigidity
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/UnitaryThreeGroups/GradedModelSpansCotOfRigidWeights.lean#L1-L244
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.UnitaryThreeGroups.Rigid0
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.UnitaryThreeGroups.TanDet
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.UnitaryThreeGroups.NormCob
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.UnitaryThreeGroups.AdRepBaseLiftApply
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.UnitaryThreeGroups.ZetaReducibleRepEqSubInvAndIsUnit
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.GaloisLibrary.SelmerTransport.NormO

open Matrix IsLocalRing UpperTheory.V.U3 UpperTheory.V.Alg UpperTheory.V.HV

private theorem baer_field {O K : Type} [CommRing O] [Field K] [Algebra O K]
    (hi : Function.Injective (algebraMap O K)) : Module.Baer O K := by
  intro I g
  by_cases hI : I = ⊥
  · subst hI
    refine ⟨0, fun x hx => ?_⟩
    have : x = 0 := hx
    subst this
    exact (map_zero g).symm
  · obtain ⟨a, ha, ha0⟩ := Submodule.exists_mem_ne_zero_of_ne_bot hI
    have hk : algebraMap O K a ≠ 0 := fun h => ha0 (hi (by rw [h, map_zero]))
    refine ⟨LinearMap.toSpanSingleton O K (g ⟨a, ha⟩ / algebraMap O K a), fun x hx => ?_⟩
    have h1 : a • g ⟨x, hx⟩ = x • g ⟨a, ha⟩ := by
      rw [← map_smul, ← map_smul]
      congr 1
      exact Subtype.ext (mul_comm a x)
    rw [LinearMap.toSpanSingleton_apply, Algebra.smul_def, mul_div_assoc', div_eq_iff hk, ← Algebra.smul_def, ← h1,
      Algebra.smul_def, mul_comm]

private theorem exists_form {O K P : Type} [CommRing O] [Field K] [Algebra O K] [AddCommGroup P] [Module O P]
    (hi : Function.Injective (algebraMap O K)) (T : Submodule O P) (p : P) (hp : ∀ c : O, c ≠ 0 → c • p ∉ T) :
    ∃ lam : P →ₗ[O] K, lam p = 1 ∧ ∀ y ∈ T, lam y = 0 := by
  have hinj : Function.Injective (LinearMap.toSpanSingleton O (P ⧸ T) (T.mkQ p)) := by
    rw [injective_iff_map_eq_zero]
    intro c hc
    by_contra h0
    apply hp c h0
    rw [LinearMap.toSpanSingleton_apply, ← map_smul, Submodule.mkQ_apply, Submodule.Quotient.mk_eq_zero] at hc
    exact hc
  obtain ⟨h, hh⟩ := (baer_field hi).extension_property _ hinj (Algebra.linearMap O K)
  refine ⟨h ∘ₗ T.mkQ, ?_, fun y hy => ?_⟩
  · have := LinearMap.congr_fun hh 1
    simpa using this
  · rw [LinearMap.comp_apply, Submodule.mkQ_apply, (Submodule.Quotient.mk_eq_zero T).2 hy, map_zero]

private theorem spans_of_der {O A K ι : Type} [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [CommRing A] [Algebra O A] [Field K]
    [Algebra O K] (hi : Function.Injective (algebraMap O K)) (𝒜 : ℤ → Submodule O A) [GradedAlgebra 𝒜]
    (x : A →ₐ[O] O) (hx : ∀ j, j ≠ 0 → ∀ f ∈ 𝒜 j, x f = 0) (j : ℤ) (F : ι → A) (hF : ∀ i, F i ∈ 𝒜 j)
    (T : Set A) (hT : ∀ t ∈ T, x t = 0 ∧ (t ∈ Set.range F ∨ ∃ i, i ≠ j ∧ t ∈ 𝒜 i))
    (h : ∀ D ∈ PtDer ((Algebra.ofId O K).comp x) K, HasWt 𝒜 j D → (∀ y ∈ Ideal.span T, D y = 0) → D = 0) :
    SpansCot x 𝒜 j F := by
  rintro p ⟨f, ⟨hfj, hfx⟩, rfl⟩
  by_contra hn
  push Not at hn
  obtain ⟨lam, h1, h0⟩ := exists_form hi (cotSpan x F) _ hn
  have hfx' : x f = 0 := hfx
  let e : A →ₗ[O] A := LinearMap.id - Algebra.linearMap O A ∘ₗ x.toLinearMap
  have he : ∀ a, e a = a - algebraMap O A (x a) := fun _ => rfl
  let δ : A →ₗ[O] K := lam ∘ₗ (satSq x).mkQ ∘ₗ e
  have hδ : ∀ a b, δ (a * b) = x a • δ b + x b • δ a := by
    intro a b
    have hm : ∀ a, e a ∈ RingHom.ker x := fun a => by simp [he, RingHom.mem_ker]
    have h2 : e a * e b ∈ satSq x := ⟨1, one_ne_zero, by
      rw [one_smul, pow_two]; exact Ideal.mul_mem_mul (hm a) (hm b)⟩
    have h3 : e (a * b) = e a * e b + (x a • e b + x b • e a) := by
      simp only [he, map_mul, Algebra.smul_def]; ring
    have h4 : (satSq x).mkQ (e a * e b) = 0 := (Submodule.Quotient.mk_eq_zero _).2 h2
    simp only [δ, LinearMap.comp_apply]
    rw [h3, map_add, h4, zero_add, map_add, map_smul, map_smul, map_add, map_smul, map_smul]
  let D : A →ₗ[O] K := δ ∘ₗ GradedAlgebra.proj 𝒜 j
  have hD1 : ∀ a ∈ 𝒜 j, D a = δ a := fun a ha => by
    simp only [D, LinearMap.comp_apply, GradedAlgebra.proj_apply, DirectSum.decompose_of_mem_same 𝒜 ha]
  have hD0 : HasWt 𝒜 j D := fun i hi a ha => by
    simp only [D, LinearMap.comp_apply, GradedAlgebra.proj_apply, DirectSum.decompose_of_mem_ne 𝒜 ha hi, map_zero]
  have hhom : ∀ i k, ∀ a ∈ 𝒜 i, ∀ b ∈ 𝒜 k, D (a * b) = x a • D b + x b • D a := by
    intro i k a ha b hb
    have hab : a * b ∈ 𝒜 (i + k) := SetLike.mul_mem_graded ha hb
    by_cases hik : i + k = j
    · rw [hD1 _ (hik ▸ hab), hδ]
      congr 1
      · by_cases hk : k = j
        · rw [hD1 b (hk ▸ hb)]
        · rw [hx i (by omega) a ha, zero_smul, zero_smul]
      · by_cases hk : i = j
        · rw [hD1 a (hk ▸ ha)]
        · rw [hx k (by omega) b hb, zero_smul, zero_smul]
    · rw [hD0 _ hik _ hab]
      have e1 : x a • D b = 0 := by
        by_cases h0 : i = 0
        · rw [hD0 k (by omega) b hb, smul_zero]
        · rw [hx i h0 a ha, zero_smul]
      have e2 : x b • D a = 0 := by
        by_cases h0 : k = 0
        · rw [hD0 i (by omega) a ha, smul_zero]
        · rw [hx k h0 b hb, zero_smul]
      rw [e1, e2, add_zero]
  have hder : ∀ a b, D (a * b) = x a • D b + x b • D a := by
    intro a b
    induction a using DirectSum.Decomposition.inductionOn 𝒜 with
    | zero => simp
    | add a a' h h' => simp only [add_mul, map_add, h, h', add_smul, smul_add]; abel
    | homogeneous a =>
      induction b using DirectSum.Decomposition.inductionOn 𝒜 with
      | zero => simp
      | add b b' h h' => simp only [mul_add, map_add, h, h', add_smul, smul_add]; abel
      | homogeneous b => exact hhom _ _ _ a.2 _ b.2
  have hδ0 : ∀ a, x a = 0 → δ a = lam ((satSq x).mkQ a) := fun a ha => by
    simp only [δ, LinearMap.comp_apply, he, ha, map_zero, sub_zero]
  have hz : D = 0 := by
    refine h D (fun a b => ?_) hD0 (fun y hy => ?_)
    · rw [hder]
      simp [Algebra.smul_def]
    · suffices x y = 0 ∧ D y = 0 from this.2
      induction hy using Submodule.span_induction with
      | mem t ht =>
        obtain ⟨t0, ⟨i, rfl⟩ | ⟨i, hi, ht'⟩⟩ := hT t ht
        · refine ⟨t0, ?_⟩
          rw [hD1 _ (hF i), hδ0 _ t0]
          exact h0 _ (Submodule.subset_span ⟨i, rfl⟩)
        · exact ⟨t0, hD0 i hi t ht'⟩
      | zero => simp
      | add a b _ _ ha hb => simp [ha.1, ha.2, hb.1, hb.2]
      | smul a b _ hb =>
        rw [smul_eq_mul, map_mul, hder, hb.1, hb.2]
        simp
  have := hD1 f hfj
  rw [hz, hδ0 f hfx', h1] at this
  exact zero_ne_one this

private theorem norm_lt_one_of_mem {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] (c : Coef l) (a : c.O) (ha : ¬ IsUnit a) :
    ‖(a : PadicAlgCl l)‖ < 1 := by
  by_cases h0 : a = 0
  · subst h0; simp
  have h := UpperTheory.V.Base.normO l c.O a h0
  have h1 : IsDiscreteValuationRing.addVal c.O a ≠ 0 := fun h =>
    ha (IsDiscreteValuationRing.addVal_eq_zero_iff.1 h)
  have h2 : IsDiscreteValuationRing.addVal c.O a ≠ ⊤ := fun h => h0 (IsDiscreteValuationRing.addVal_eq_top_iff.1 h)
  have h3 : (IsDiscreteValuationRing.addVal c.O a).toNat ≠ 0 := by
    rw [Ne, ENat.toNat_eq_zero]; tauto
  have hl : (1 : ℝ) < l := by exact_mod_cast (Fact.out : l.Prime).one_lt
  have h4 := pow_lt_one₀ (a := (l : ℝ)⁻¹) (by positivity) (inv_lt_one_of_one_lt₀ hl) h3
  by_contra hn
  exact absurd (h ▸ h4) (not_lt.2 (one_le_pow₀ (not_lt.1 hn)))

private theorem local_of_res {O R : Type} [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] [IsCO O R]
    (x : R →ₐ[O] O) (y : R) (hy : y ∈ maximalIdeal R) : ¬ IsUnit (x y) := by
  intro hu
  have h2 : IsUnit (y - algebraMap O R (x y)) := by
    by_contra hn
    have h3 : y - algebraMap O R (x y) ∈ maximalIdeal R := hn
    have h4 : algebraMap O R (x y) ∈ maximalIdeal R := by simpa using Ideal.sub_mem _ hy h3
    exact h4 (hu.map _)
  have h5 := h2.map x
  simp at h5

private theorem map_reducible_three_dim {G R K : Type} [Group G] [CommRing R] [CommRing K] (f : R →+* K) (η : G →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : G →* Rˣ) :
    (GG.map f).comp (r0 η Ψ) = r0 η ((Units.map f.toMonoidHom).comp Ψ) := by
  refine MonoidHom.ext fun g => SemidirectProduct.ext (Units.ext ?_) rfl
  show (tor R (Ψ g) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) R).map f = tor _ (((Units.map f.toMonoidHom).comp Ψ) g)
  rw [tor_val, tor_val, Matrix.diagonal_map (map_zero _)]
  congr 1
  funext i
  exact congrArg Units.val (map_zpow (Units.map f.toMonoidHom) (Ψ g) (wt1 i))

private theorem coboundary_torus_lie_zero {G K : Type} [Group G] [CommRing K] (η : G →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : G →* Kˣ)
    (X : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) K) (hX : X ∈ unitaryThreeTorusLieAlgebra K) : cob (r0 η Ψ) X = 0 := by
  rw [unitaryThreeTorusLieAlgebra, Submodule.mem_span_singleton] at hX
  obtain ⟨a, rfl⟩ := hX
  funext g
  ext i j
  rw [cob, Matrix.sub_apply, adRep_baseLift_apply]
  fin_cases i <;> fin_cases j <;> split_ifs <;> simp [wt, wt1, Fin.rev] <;> ring

private theorem spans_three {O A K : Type} [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [CommRing A] [Algebra O A] [Field K]
    [Algebra O K] (hi : Function.Injective (algebraMap O K)) (𝒜 : ℤ → Submodule O A) [GradedAlgebra 𝒜]
    (x : A →ₐ[O] O) (hx : ∀ j, j ≠ 0 → ∀ f ∈ 𝒜 j, x f = 0) {r s r' s' m : ℕ} (Q : Eqns 𝒜 x r s r' s' m)
    (key : ∀ j : ℤ, j ≤ 0 → ∀ D ∈ PtDer ((Algebra.ofId O K).comp x) K, HasWt 𝒜 j D → (∀ y ∈ Q.ideal, D y = 0) →
      D = 0) : SpansCot x 𝒜 (-1) Q.F ∧ SpansCot x 𝒜 (-2) Q.G ∧ SpansCot x 𝒜 0 Q.Z := by
  have hF0 := fun i => hx _ (by decide) _ (Q.hF i)
  have hG0 := fun i => hx _ (by decide) _ (Q.hG i)
  have hE0 := fun i => hx _ (by decide) _ (Q.hE i)
  have hE0' := fun i => hx _ (by decide) _ (Q.hE' i)
  refine ⟨?_, ?_, ?_⟩
  · refine spans_of_der hi 𝒜 x hx _ _ Q.hF _ ?_ (key _ (by decide))
    rintro t ((((⟨i, rfl⟩ | ⟨i, rfl⟩) | ⟨i, rfl⟩) | ⟨i, rfl⟩) | ⟨i, rfl⟩)
    · exact ⟨hF0 i, Or.inl ⟨i, rfl⟩⟩
    · exact ⟨hG0 i, Or.inr ⟨_, by decide, Q.hG i⟩⟩
    · exact ⟨hE0 i, Or.inr ⟨_, by decide, Q.hE i⟩⟩
    · exact ⟨hE0' i, Or.inr ⟨_, by decide, Q.hE' i⟩⟩
    · exact ⟨(Q.hZ i).2, Or.inr ⟨_, by decide, (Q.hZ i).1⟩⟩
  · refine spans_of_der hi 𝒜 x hx _ _ Q.hG _ ?_ (key _ (by decide))
    rintro t ((((⟨i, rfl⟩ | ⟨i, rfl⟩) | ⟨i, rfl⟩) | ⟨i, rfl⟩) | ⟨i, rfl⟩)
    · exact ⟨hF0 i, Or.inr ⟨_, by decide, Q.hF i⟩⟩
    · exact ⟨hG0 i, Or.inl ⟨i, rfl⟩⟩
    · exact ⟨hE0 i, Or.inr ⟨_, by decide, Q.hE i⟩⟩
    · exact ⟨hE0' i, Or.inr ⟨_, by decide, Q.hE' i⟩⟩
    · exact ⟨(Q.hZ i).2, Or.inr ⟨_, by decide, (Q.hZ i).1⟩⟩
  · refine spans_of_der hi 𝒜 x hx _ _ (fun i => (Q.hZ i).1) _ ?_ (key _ (by decide))
    rintro t ((((⟨i, rfl⟩ | ⟨i, rfl⟩) | ⟨i, rfl⟩) | ⟨i, rfl⟩) | ⟨i, rfl⟩)
    · exact ⟨hF0 i, Or.inr ⟨_, by decide, Q.hF i⟩⟩
    · exact ⟨hG0 i, Or.inr ⟨_, by decide, Q.hG i⟩⟩
    · exact ⟨hE0 i, Or.inr ⟨_, by decide, Q.hE i⟩⟩
    · exact ⟨hE0' i, Or.inr ⟨_, by decide, Q.hE' i⟩⟩
    · exact ⟨(Q.hZ i).2, Or.inl ⟨i, rfl⟩⟩

theorem UpperTheory.V.U3.gradedModel_spansCot_of_rigid_weights :
    ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {c : Coef l} {Γ H : Type} [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace Γ] [IsTopologicalGroup Γ] [Group H] [TopologicalSpace H] [IsTopologicalGroup H]
      {η : Γ →* ℤˣ} {Ψ : Γ →* (c.O)ˣ} {N : Set Γ} {ι : H →* Γ} {hι : Continuous ι}
      {U : Univ (GCond.noCondition c.O (η.comp ι) (Ψ.comp ι))} {I : Ideal U.R} {cc g₁ : Γ} (S : SRing N hι U I cc g₁) (M : Model S (PadicAlgCl l)), l ≠ 2 → Marks η Ψ cc g₁ →
      M2 M.𝒜 M.Q.ideal → (∀ j : ℤ, j ≤ 0 → RigidWt j S.r.hom (toBar M.x₀)) → M1 M.Q := by
  intro l _ c Γ H _ _ _ _ _ _ η Ψ N ι hι U I cc g₁ S M hl hm h2 hrig
  have hi : Function.Injective (algebraMap c.O (PadicAlgCl l)) := Subtype.val_injective
  have hloc : IsLocPt (toBar M.x₀) := fun y hy => norm_lt_one_of_mem c _ (local_of_res M.x₀ y hy)
  have hq : (toBar M.x₀).comp M.q = (Algebra.ofId c.O (PadicAlgCl l)).comp M.x := by
    rw [← M.x₀_q]; rfl
  let Ψ' : Γ →* (PadicAlgCl l)ˣ := (Units.map (algebraMap c.O (PadicAlgCl l)).toMonoidHom).comp Ψ
  have hpush : push (toBar M.x₀) S.r.hom = r0 η Ψ' := by
    rw [← map_reducible_three_dim, ← M.base]; rfl
  have hm' : Marks η Ψ' cc g₁ := by
    refine ⟨hm.csq, hm.ηc, by simp [Ψ', hm.Ψc], hm.ηg, ?_⟩
    simpa [Ψ'] using hm.Ψg.map (algebraMap c.O (PadicAlgCl l))
  have key : ∀ j : ℤ, j ≤ 0 → ∀ D ∈ PtDer ((Algebra.ofId c.O (PadicAlgCl l)).comp M.x) (PadicAlgCl l),
      HasWt M.𝒜 j D → (∀ y ∈ M.Q.ideal, D y = 0) → D = 0 := by
    intro j hj D hD hw hI
    rw [← hq] at hD
    obtain ⟨F, hF, hFD⟩ := M.der h2 _ hloc D hD hI
    obtain ⟨-, hn, -, -, hdet⟩ := tanCoc_determines S M hl hm F (hF ▸ hloc)
    have hgw : ∀ g, tanCoc F S.r.hom g ∈ gw (PadicAlgCl l) j := by
      intro g a b hab
      have e : ((((S.r.hom g).left⁻¹ : GL (Fin 3) S.R) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) S.R).map fun x => (F x).fst) =
          (((push (dualFst F) S.r.hom g).left⁻¹ : GL (Fin 3) (PadicAlgCl l)) :
            Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) (PadicAlgCl l)) := rfl
      rw [hF, hpush, unitaryThreeReflection_left, ← map_inv, tor_val] at e
      obtain ⟨f, hf, hfq⟩ := M.ent g a b
      rw [tanCoc, e, Matrix.mul_diagonal, Matrix.map_apply, ← hfq, hFD, hw _ hab f hf, zero_mul]
    obtain ⟨X, hX⟩ := hrig j hj F hF hgw
    rw [hF, hX, hpush] at hn
    have hT := (normCoc_cob_iff (r0 η Ψ') cc g₁ X (Ne.isUnit two_ne_zero) hm.ηc hm.ηg
      (baseRepGL3_normalised_of_psi_eq_one η Ψ' g₁ hm'.Ψc) (zeta_reducible_rep_eq_sub_inv_and_isUnit η Ψ' cc g₁ hm').2).1 hn
    rw [hpush, coboundary_torus_lie_zero η Ψ' X hT] at hX
    ext y
    have := hdet F rfl 1 0 (by rw [hX]; simp) (M.q y)
    rw [one_mul, zero_mul, add_zero] at this
    exact (hFD y).symm.trans this
  exact ⟨M.one_le, M.smooth, spans_three hi M.𝒜 M.x M.wring.fixed M.Q key⟩


[OCH_SRC_MODEL_M3] Actual fixed-point condition for the graded model
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/UnitaryThreeGroups/GradedModelPointConditionOfPtHyp.lean#L1-L90
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.UnitaryThreeGroups.ModelSigns
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.GaloisLibrary.SelmerTransport.NormO

open Matrix IsLocalRing UpperTheory.V.U3 UpperTheory.V.Alg UpperTheory.V.HV

private theorem norm_lt_one_of_nonunit {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] (c : Coef l) (b : c.O) (hb : ¬ IsUnit b) :
    ‖(b : PadicAlgCl l)‖ < 1 := by
  classical
  haveI := c.dvr
  haveI := c.fin
  by_cases h0 : b = 0
  · subst h0
    simp
  have key := UpperTheory.V.Base.normO l c.O b h0
  have hv0 : IsDiscreteValuationRing.addVal c.O b ≠ 0 := fun h =>
    hb ((IsDiscreteValuationRing.addVal_eq_zero_iff).1 h)
  have hvt : IsDiscreteValuationRing.addVal c.O b ≠ ⊤ := fun h =>
    h0 ((IsDiscreteValuationRing.addVal_eq_top_iff).1 h)
  have hn : (IsDiscreteValuationRing.addVal c.O b).toNat ≠ 0 := by
    intro h
    rw [ENat.toNat_eq_zero] at h
    tauto
  have hl : (1 : ℝ) < l := by exact_mod_cast (Fact.out : l.Prime).one_lt
  have hr : ((l : ℝ)⁻¹) ^ (IsDiscreteValuationRing.addVal c.O b).toNat < 1 :=
    pow_lt_one₀ (by positivity) (inv_lt_one_of_one_lt₀ hl) hn
  rw [← key] at hr
  by_cases he : (UpperTheory.V.Base.eO l c.O).toNat = 0
  · rw [he, pow_zero] at hr
    exact absurd hr (lt_irrefl 1)
  · exact (pow_lt_one_iff_of_nonneg (norm_nonneg _) he).1 hr

private theorem isLocPt_toBar {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {c : Coef l} {R : Type} [CommRing R] [Algebra c.O R]
    [IsCO c.O R] (x : R →ₐ[c.O] c.O) : IsLocPt (toBar x) := by
  intro y hy
  refine norm_lt_one_of_nonunit c (x y) fun hu => ?_
  have h2 : IsUnit (y - algebraMap c.O R (x y)) := by
    by_contra hn
    have h3 : y - algebraMap c.O R (x y) ∈ maximalIdeal R := hn
    have h4 : algebraMap c.O R (x y) ∈ maximalIdeal R := by simpa using Ideal.sub_mem _ hy h3
    exact h4 (hu.map _)
  have h5 := h2.map x
  rw [map_sub, AlgHom.commutes] at h5
  simp at h5

private theorem hom_ext_gr {O A K : Type} [CommRing O] [CommRing A] [Algebra O A] (𝒜 : ℤ → Submodule O A)
    [GradedAlgebra 𝒜] [CommRing K] [Algebra O K] (ξ ξ' : A →ₐ[O] K) (h : ∀ j, ∀ f ∈ 𝒜 j, ξ f = ξ' f) : ξ = ξ' := by
  refine AlgHom.ext fun f => ?_
  induction f using DirectSum.Decomposition.inductionOn 𝒜 with
  | zero => simp
  | homogeneous m => exact h _ _ m.2
  | add a b ha hb => rw [map_add, map_add, ha, hb]

theorem UpperTheory.V.U3.gradedModel_pointCondition_of_ptHyp :
    ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {c : Coef l} {Γ H : Type} [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace Γ] [IsTopologicalGroup Γ] [Group H] [TopologicalSpace H] [IsTopologicalGroup H]
      {η : Γ →* ℤˣ} {Ψ : Γ →* (c.O)ˣ} {N : Set Γ} {ι : H →* Γ} {hι : Continuous ι}
      {U : Univ (GCond.noCondition c.O (η.comp ι) (Ψ.comp ι))} {I : Ideal U.R} {cc g₁ : Γ} (S : SRing N hι U I cc g₁) (M : Model S (PadicAlgCl l)), l ≠ 2 → Marks η Ψ cc g₁ →
      M2 M.𝒜 M.Q.ideal → PtHyp η S.r.hom M.x₀ → M3 M.𝒜 (PadicAlgCl l) M.x M.Q.ideal := by
  intro l _ c Γ H _ _ _ _ _ _ η Ψ N ι hι U I cc g₁ S M hl hm h2 hpt
  intro x₁ hS hN hP hne
  exfalso
  apply hne
  obtain ⟨t, f, hf, hact⟩ := M.pts h2 x₁ hS
  have sg := model_signs S M hl hm f hf
  have hN' : NegZero M.𝒜 (f.comp M.q) := by
    intro j hj a ha
    rw [← hact, act_of_mem _ _ _ ha, hN j hj a ha, mul_zero]
  have hP' : PosZero M.𝒜 (f.comp M.q) := by
    intro j hj a ha
    rw [← hact, act_of_mem _ _ _ ha, hP j hj a ha, mul_zero]
  have hfix := hpt.fixed f hf (sg.1.1 hN') (sg.2.1.1 hP')
  have hb : IsLocPt (toBar M.x₀) := isLocPt_toBar M.x₀
  have hconj : push (toBar M.x₀) S.r.hom = conjBy ((tor (PadicAlgCl l)) 1) (push f S.r.hom) := by
    rw [hfix]
    ext g : 1
    simp [conjBy]
  have heq := sg.2.2 (toBar M.x₀) hb 1 hconj
  apply hom_ext_gr M.𝒜
  intro j a ha
  by_cases hj : j = 0
  · subst hj
    have e1 := congrArg (fun φ => φ a) hact
    have e2 := congrArg (fun φ => φ a) heq
    simp only [act_of_mem _ _ _ ha] at e1 e2
    simp at e1 e2
    rw [← M.x₀_q]
    simp
    rw [e1, e2]
  · rcases lt_or_gt_of_ne hj with h | h
    · rw [hN j h a ha, AlgHom.comp_apply, M.wring.fixed j hj a ha, map_zero]
    · rw [hP j h a ha, AlgHom.comp_apply, M.wring.fixed j hj a ha, map_zero]


[OCH_SRC_MODEL_M4] The exact dimension-one point derivation condition on the same model
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/UnitaryThreeGroups/GradedModelDerivationConditionOfPtHyp.lean#L1-L228
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.UnitaryThreeGroups.Rigid0
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.UnitaryThreeGroups.ModelSigns
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.UnitaryThreeGroups.TanDet
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.UnitaryThreeGroups.NormCob
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.UnitaryThreeGroups.ZetaReducibleRepEqSubInvAndIsUnit

open Matrix IsLocalRing UpperTheory.V.U3 UpperTheory.V.Alg UpperTheory.V.HV

section SlopeTestRemainingChoices

variable {O A K : Type} [CommRing O] [CommRing A] [Algebra O A] (𝒜 : ℤ → Submodule O A) [GradedAlgebra 𝒜]
  [Field K] [Algebra O K]

private theorem exists_twist (t : Kˣ) (D : A →ₗ[O] K) :
    ∃ E : A →ₗ[O] K, ∀ j, ∀ a ∈ 𝒜 j, E a = ((t ^ j : Kˣ) : K) * D a := by
  classical
  refine ⟨(DirectSum.toModule O ℤ K (fun j => ((t ^ j : Kˣ) : K) • (D ∘ₗ (𝒜 j).subtype))) ∘ₗ
    (DirectSum.decomposeLinearEquiv 𝒜).toLinearMap, ?_⟩
  intro j a ha
  simp only [LinearMap.comp_apply, LinearEquiv.coe_toLinearMap, DirectSum.decomposeLinearEquiv_apply,
    DirectSum.decompose_of_mem 𝒜 ha]
  rw [← DirectSum.lof_eq_of O, DirectSum.toModule_lof]
  simp [smul_eq_mul]

private theorem act_tz (t : Kˣ) (ξ : A →ₐ[O] K) {j : ℤ} {a : A} (ha : a ∈ 𝒜 j) :
    act 𝒜 (tz t) ξ a = ((t ^ j : Kˣ) : K) * ξ a := act_of_mem 𝒜 _ ξ ha

private theorem twist_der (t : Kˣ) (ξ : A →ₐ[O] K) (D E : A →ₗ[O] K) (hD : D ∈ PtDer ξ K)
    (hE : ∀ j, ∀ a ∈ 𝒜 j, E a = ((t ^ j : Kˣ) : K) * D a) : E ∈ PtDer (act 𝒜 (tz t) ξ) K := by
  have hD' : ∀ f g : A, D (f * g) = ξ f • D g + ξ g • D f := hD
  show ∀ f g : A, E (f * g) = act 𝒜 (tz t) ξ f • E g + act 𝒜 (tz t) ξ g • E f
  intro f g
  refine DirectSum.Decomposition.inductionOn 𝒜 (motive := fun f => E (f * g) = act 𝒜 (tz t) ξ f • E g + act 𝒜 (tz t) ξ g • E f) ?_ ?_ ?_ f
  · simp
  · intro i a
    refine DirectSum.Decomposition.inductionOn 𝒜 (motive := fun g => E (a * g) = act 𝒜 (tz t) ξ a • E g + act 𝒜 (tz t) ξ g • E a) ?_ ?_ ?_ g
    · simp
    · intro j b
      have hab : (a : A) * b ∈ 𝒜 (i + j) := SetLike.GradedMul.mul_mem a.2 b.2
      rw [hE _ _ hab, hE _ _ b.2, hE _ _ a.2, act_tz 𝒜 t ξ a.2, act_tz 𝒜 t ξ b.2, hD', _root_.zpow_add, Units.val_mul]
      simp only [smul_eq_mul]
      ring
    · intro g g' h h'
      simp only [mul_add, map_add, smul_add, add_smul, smul_eq_mul] at h h' ⊢
      rw [h, h']
      ring
  · intro f f' h h'
    simp only [add_mul, map_add, smul_add, add_smul, smul_eq_mul] at h h' ⊢
    rw [h, h']
    ring

omit [GradedAlgebra 𝒜] in
private theorem twist_kills {x : A →ₐ[O] O} {r s r' s' m : ℕ} (Q : Eqns 𝒜 x r s r' s' m) (t : Kˣ)
    (ξ : A →ₐ[O] K) (D E : A →ₗ[O] K) (hξ : OnS Q.ideal ξ) (hE' : E ∈ PtDer ξ K)
    (hE : ∀ j, ∀ a ∈ 𝒜 j, E a = ((t ^ j : Kˣ) : K) * D a) (hD : ∀ y ∈ Q.ideal, D y = 0) :
    ∀ y ∈ Q.ideal, E y = 0 := by
  intro y hy
  have hξ' : ∀ f ∈ Q.ideal, ξ f = 0 := hξ
  have hE'' : ∀ f g : A, E (f * g) = ξ f • E g + ξ g • E f := hE'
  have hgen : ∀ z ∈ (Set.range Q.F ∪ Set.range Q.G ∪ Set.range Q.E ∪ Set.range Q.E' ∪ Set.range Q.Z), E z = 0 := by
    intro z hz
    have hzI : z ∈ Q.ideal := Ideal.subset_span hz
    obtain ⟨j, hj⟩ : ∃ j, z ∈ 𝒜 j := by
      rcases hz with (((⟨i, rfl⟩ | ⟨i, rfl⟩) | ⟨i, rfl⟩) | ⟨i, rfl⟩) | ⟨i, rfl⟩
      exacts [⟨_, Q.hF i⟩, ⟨_, Q.hG i⟩, ⟨_, Q.hE i⟩, ⟨_, Q.hE' i⟩, ⟨_, (Q.hZ i).1⟩]
    rw [hE j z hj, hD z hzI, mul_zero]
  have hy' : y ∈ Submodule.span A (Set.range Q.F ∪ Set.range Q.G ∪ Set.range Q.E ∪ Set.range Q.E' ∪ Set.range Q.Z) := hy
  clear hy
  induction hy' using Submodule.span_induction with
  | mem z hz => exact hgen z hz
  | zero => simp
  | add a b _ _ ha hb => simp [ha, hb]
  | smul a b hb hb0 =>
    rw [smul_eq_mul, hE'', hb0, hξ' b hb]
    simp

private theorem cob_smul {G C : Type} [Group G] [CommRing C] {m : ℕ} (r : G →* GG m C) (a : C)
    (X : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) C) : cob r (a • X) = a • cob r X := by
  funext g
  simp only [cob, map_smul, Pi.smul_apply, smul_sub]

private theorem zmat_map {G R R' : Type} [Group G] [CommRing R] [CommRing R'] (f : R →+* R') (r : G →* GG 3 R)
    (c g₁ : G) (a b : Fin 3) : zmat c g₁ ((GG.map f).comp r) a b = f (zmat c g₁ r a b) := by
  simp [zmat]

private theorem normalised_map {G R R' : Type} [Group G] [CommRing R] [CommRing R'] (f : R →+* R')
    (r : G →* GG 3 R) (c g₁ : G) (h : Normalised c g₁ r) : Normalised c g₁ ((GG.map f).comp r) := by
  refine ⟨?_, ?_, ?_⟩
  · simp [GG.map_mat, h.atc]
  · rw [zmat_map, h.z12, map_zero]
  · rw [zmat_map, h.z21, map_zero]

private theorem isUnit_zeta {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] {Γ : Type} [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace Γ]
    {η : Γ →* ℤˣ} {Ψ : Γ →* Oˣ} {R : Type} [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] [IsCO O R] (r : GLift O η Ψ R)
    {c g₁ : Γ} (h : Marks η Ψ c g₁) : IsUnit (zeta c g₁ r.hom) := by
  have hm : Marks η (barChar O Ψ) c g₁ := by
    refine ⟨h.csq, h.ηc, by simp [barChar, h.Ψc], h.ηg, ?_⟩
    have := h.Ψg.map (residue O)
    rw [map_sub, map_pow, map_one] at this
    exact this
  have h1 := (zeta_reducible_rep_eq_sub_inv_and_isUnit η (barChar O Ψ) c g₁ hm).2
  have h2 : (GG.map (res O R : R →+* ResidueField O)).comp r.hom = r0 η (barChar O Ψ) :=
    MonoidHom.ext r.red
  rw [← h2, zeta, zmat_map] at h1
  by_contra hn
  have h3 : res O R (zeta c g₁ r.hom) = 0 := (res_eq_zero_iff O R _).2 hn
  exact h1.ne_zero h3

end SlopeTestRemainingChoices

theorem UpperTheory.V.U3.gradedModel_derivationCondition_of_ptHyp :
    ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {c : Coef l} {Γ H : Type} [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace Γ] [IsTopologicalGroup Γ] [Group H] [TopologicalSpace H] [IsTopologicalGroup H]
      {η : Γ →* ℤˣ} {Ψ : Γ →* (c.O)ˣ} {N : Set Γ} {ι : H →* Γ} {hι : Continuous ι}
      {U : Univ (GCond.noCondition c.O (η.comp ι) (Ψ.comp ι))} {I : Ideal U.R} {cc g₁ : Γ} (S : SRing N hι U I cc g₁) (M : Model S (PadicAlgCl l)), l ≠ 2 → Marks η Ψ cc g₁ →
      M2 M.𝒜 M.Q.ideal → PtHyp η S.r.hom M.x₀ → M4 M.𝒜 (PadicAlgCl l) M.Q.ideal := by
  intro l _ c Γ H _ _ _ _ _ _ η Ψ N ι hι U I cc g₁ S M hl hm hM2 hpt
  intro ξ hξ hsign D hD D' hD' hDI hD'I
  obtain ⟨t, f, hf, hact⟩ := M.pts hM2 ξ hξ
  obtain ⟨E, hE⟩ := exists_twist M.𝒜 t D
  obtain ⟨E', hE'⟩ := exists_twist M.𝒜 t D'
  have hEd : E ∈ PtDer (f.comp M.q) (PadicAlgCl l) := hact ▸ twist_der M.𝒜 t ξ D E hD hE
  have hE'd : E' ∈ PtDer (f.comp M.q) (PadicAlgCl l) := hact ▸ twist_der M.𝒜 t ξ D' E' hD' hE'
  have hon : OnS M.Q.ideal (f.comp M.q) := by
    intro y hy
    have : M.q y = 0 := by
      rw [← RingHom.mem_ker, M.ker_q]
      exact hy
    simp [this]
  have hEI : ∀ y ∈ M.Q.ideal, E y = 0 := twist_kills M.𝒜 M.Q t (f.comp M.q) D E hon hEd hE hDI
  have hE'I : ∀ y ∈ M.Q.ideal, E' y = 0 := twist_kills M.𝒜 M.Q t (f.comp M.q) D' E' hon hE'd hE' hD'I
  obtain ⟨F, hF, hFE⟩ := M.der hM2 f hf E hEd hEI
  obtain ⟨F', hF', hFE'⟩ := M.der hM2 f hf E' hE'd hE'I
  have htne : ∀ j : ℤ, ((t ^ j : (PadicAlgCl l)ˣ) : PadicAlgCl l) ≠ 0 := fun j => Units.ne_zero _
  have hneg : NegZero M.𝒜 (f.comp M.q) ↔ NegZero M.𝒜 ξ := by
    rw [← hact]
    constructor
    · intro h j hj y hy
      have := h j hj y hy
      rw [act_tz M.𝒜 t ξ hy] at this
      exact (mul_eq_zero.1 this).resolve_left (htne j)
    · intro h j hj y hy
      rw [act_tz M.𝒜 t ξ hy, h j hj y hy, mul_zero]
  have hpos : PosZero M.𝒜 (f.comp M.q) ↔ PosZero M.𝒜 ξ := by
    rw [← hact]
    constructor
    · intro h j hj y hy
      have := h j hj y hy
      rw [act_tz M.𝒜 t ξ hy] at this
      exact (mul_eq_zero.1 this).resolve_left (htne j)
    · intro h j hj y hy
      rw [act_tz M.𝒜 t ξ hy, h j hj y hy, mul_zero]
  have hsg := model_signs S M hl hm f hf
  have htwo : TwoPt η S.r.hom f :=
    ⟨hf, fun h => hsign.1 (hneg.1 (hsg.1.2 h)), fun h => hsign.2 (hpos.1 (hsg.2.1.2 h))⟩
  have hrig := hpt.rigid f htwo
  obtain ⟨X, hX⟩ := hrig F hF
  obtain ⟨X', hX'⟩ := hrig F' hF'
  have hN0 := ((S.rep S.R).1 (AlgHom.id _ _)).1
  have hN : Normalised cc g₁ S.r.hom := by
    refine ⟨?_, ?_, ?_⟩
    · have := hN0.atc
      simpa [GLift.map, GG.map_mat] using this
    · have := hN0.z12
      simp only [GLift.map] at this
      rw [zmat_map ((AlgHom.id c.O S.R : S.R →ₐ[c.O] S.R) : S.R →+* S.R)] at this
      simpa using this
    · have := hN0.z21
      simp only [GLift.map] at this
      rw [zmat_map ((AlgHom.id c.O S.R : S.R →ₐ[c.O] S.R) : S.R →+* S.R)] at this
      simpa using this
  have hright : ∀ g, (S.r.hom g).right = η g := by
    intro g
    have := congrArg SemidirectProduct.right (S.r.red g)
    simpa [GG.map, SemidirectProduct.map_right, rbar, r0] using this
  have hc : (push f S.r.hom cc).right = -1 := by
    simp [push, GG.map, SemidirectProduct.map_right, hright, hm.ηc]
  have hg : (push f S.r.hom g₁).right = 1 := by
    simp [push, GG.map, SemidirectProduct.map_right, hright, hm.ηg]
  have hPN : Normalised cc g₁ (push f S.r.hom) := normalised_map (f : S.R →+* PadicAlgCl l) S.r.hom cc g₁ hN
  have hz : IsUnit (zeta cc g₁ (push f S.r.hom)) := by
    have := (isUnit_zeta S.r hm).map (f : S.R →+* PadicAlgCl l)
    simpa [zeta, push, zmat_map] using this
  have h2 : IsUnit (2 : PadicAlgCl l) := isUnit_iff_ne_zero.2 two_ne_zero
  have hdet := tanCoc_determines S M hl hm F (hF ▸ hf)
  have hdet' := tanCoc_determines S M hl hm F' (hF' ▸ hf)
  have hXm : X ∈ unitaryThreeTorusLieAlgebra (PadicAlgCl l) := by
    have := hdet.2.1
    rw [hF, hX] at this
    exact (normCoc_cob_iff _ cc g₁ X h2 hc hg hPN hz).1 this
  have hX'm : X' ∈ unitaryThreeTorusLieAlgebra (PadicAlgCl l) := by
    have := hdet'.2.1
    rw [hF', hX'] at this
    exact (normCoc_cob_iff _ cc g₁ X' h2 hc hg hPN hz).1 this
  obtain ⟨x, rfl⟩ := Submodule.mem_span_singleton.1 hXm
  obtain ⟨x', rfl⟩ := Submodule.mem_span_singleton.1 hX'm
  obtain ⟨a, a', hne, hrel⟩ : ∃ a a' : PadicAlgCl l, (a ≠ 0 ∨ a' ≠ 0) ∧
      a • tanCoc F S.r.hom + a' • tanCoc F' S.r.hom = 0 := by
    rw [hX, hX', cob_smul, cob_smul]
    by_cases hx' : x' = 0
    · refine ⟨0, 1, Or.inr one_ne_zero, ?_⟩
      simp [hx']
    · refine ⟨x', -x, Or.inl hx', ?_⟩
      funext g
      simp only [Pi.add_apply, Pi.smul_apply, Pi.zero_apply, smul_smul]
      rw [← add_smul]
      have : x' * x + -x * x' = 0 := by ring
      rw [this, zero_smul]
  have hall := hdet.2.2.2.2 F' (by rw [hF, hF']) a a' hrel
  have hEE : ∀ y : M.A, a * E y + a' * E' y = 0 := by
    intro y
    rw [← hFE y, ← hFE' y]
    exact hall (M.q y)
  refine ⟨a, a', hne, ?_⟩
  apply LinearMap.ext
  intro y
  simp only [LinearMap.add_apply, LinearMap.smul_apply, LinearMap.zero_apply, smul_eq_mul]
  refine DirectSum.Decomposition.inductionOn M.𝒜 (motive := fun y => a * D y + a' * D' y = 0) ?_ ?_ ?_ y
  · simp
  · intro j z
    have h := hEE z
    rw [hE j z z.2, hE' j z z.2] at h
    have h' : ((t ^ j : (PadicAlgCl l)ˣ) : PadicAlgCl l) * (a * D z + a' * D' z) = 0 := by
      rw [← h]
      ring
    exact (mul_eq_zero.1 h').resolve_left (htne j)
  · intro y y' h h'
    simp only [map_add]
    linear_combination h + h'


[OCH_SRC_MODEL_UPPER] Every-cover valuation inequality and actual irredundant point-orbit cover
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/UnitaryThreeGroups/ModelUpper.lean#L103-L250
theorem UpperTheory.V.U3.model_upper :
    ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {c : Coef l} {Γ H : Type} [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace Γ] [IsTopologicalGroup Γ] [Group H] [TopologicalSpace H] [IsTopologicalGroup H]
      {η : Γ →* ℤˣ} {Ψ : Γ →* (c.O)ˣ} {N : Set Γ} {ι : H →* Γ} {hι : Continuous ι}
      {U : Univ (GCond.noCondition c.O (η.comp ι) (Ψ.comp ι))} {I : Ideal U.R} {cc g₁ : Γ} (S : SRing N hι U I cc g₁) (M : Model S (PadicAlgCl l)), l ≠ 2 → Marks η Ψ cc g₁ →
      LemmaM → Module.Finite c.O (weightZeroSubalgebra c.O S.r.hom) → PtHyp η S.r.hom M.x₀ → ∀ σ : Γ, η σ = 1 →
      (∀ f : S.R →ₐ[c.O] PadicAlgCl l, TwoPt η S.r.hom f → trDiff η Ψ S.r.hom σ f ≠ 0) →
      M.len ≠ ⊤ ∧ ∃ T : Finset (S.R →ₐ[c.O] PadicAlgCl l), CoversPt η S.r.hom T ∧
        (∀ f ∈ T, ∀ f' ∈ T, ∀ t : (PadicAlgCl l)ˣ, push f' S.r.hom = conjBy (tor _ t) (push f S.r.hom) → f = f') ∧
        UpperTheory.V.Chr.nrm l c.O M.len ≤ 2 * ∑ f ∈ T, vl l (trDiff η Ψ S.r.hom σ f) := by
  classical
  intro l _ c Γ H _ _ _ _ _ _ η Ψ N ι hι U I cc g₁ S M hl hmk hLM hfin hpt σ hσ htr
  letI := M.cr
  letI := M.al
  letI := M.gr
  have hM2 := modelIdeal_finiteness_of_weightZero_finite S M hl hmk hfin
  have hM1 := gradedModel_spansCot_of_rigid_weights S M hl hmk hM2 hpt.rigid0
  have hM3 := gradedModel_pointCondition_of_ptHyp S M hl hmk hM2 hpt
  have hM4 := gradedModel_derivationCondition_of_ptHyp S M hl hmk hM2 hpt
  have hsg := model_signs S M hl hmk
  have hv := isNVal l c
  obtain ⟨-, -, -, hcomp, -, he0, hefin, -, -⟩ := UpperTheory.V.Base.dvrFacts l c.O
  haveI := hcomp
  have htp : ∀ f : S.R →ₐ[c.O] PadicAlgCl l, IsLocPt f →
      (TwoPt η S.r.hom f ↔ TwoSigned M.𝒜 (f.comp M.q)) := fun f hf =>
    ⟨fun h => ⟨fun hn => h.2.1 ((hsg f hf).1.1 hn), fun hp => h.2.2 ((hsg f hf).2.1.1 hp)⟩,
     fun h => ⟨hf, fun hu => h.1 ((hsg f hf).1.2 hu), fun hw => h.2 ((hsg f hf).2.1.2 hw)⟩⟩
  have hon : ∀ f : S.R →ₐ[c.O] PadicAlgCl l, OnS M.Q.ideal (f.comp M.q) := by
    intro f y hy
    have hy' : y ∈ RingHom.ker M.q := by rw [M.ker_q]; exact hy
    rw [AlgHom.comp_apply, RingHom.mem_ker.1 hy', map_zero]
  choose E hE hqE using M.ent σ
  have hmem : ∀ i, E i i ∈ M.𝒜 0 := fun i => by
    have := hE i i
    simpa [wt] using this
  set a : M.A := E 0 0 + E 1 1 + E 2 2 - algebraMap c.O M.A ((r0 η Ψ σ).mat.trace) with ha
  have ha0 : a ∈ M.𝒜 0 :=
    Submodule.sub_mem _ (add_mem (add_mem (hmem 0) (hmem 1)) (hmem 2)) (SetLike.algebraMap_mem_graded M.𝒜 _)
  have hqa : ∀ f : S.R →ₐ[c.O] PadicAlgCl l, f (M.q a) = trDiff η Ψ S.r.hom σ f := by
    intro f
    simp [ha, hqE, trDiff, Matrix.trace_fin_three]
  have hb : (r0 η Ψ σ).mat = (S.r.hom σ).mat.map M.x₀ := by
    rw [← M.base]
    exact GG.map_mat _ _
  have hxa : M.x a = 0 := by
    rw [← M.x₀_q, AlgHom.comp_apply]
    simp [ha, hqE, hb, Matrix.trace_fin_three]
  have hM5 : M5 M.𝒜 (PadicAlgCl l) M.x M.Q.ideal a := by
    refine ⟨ha0, hxa, fun ξ hξ hts => ?_⟩
    obtain ⟨t, f, hf, hact⟩ := M.pts hM2 ξ hξ
    have h1 := congrArg (fun g => g a) hact
    simp only [AlgHom.comp_apply] at h1
    rw [act_tz M.𝒜 t ξ ha0, zpow_zero, Units.val_one, one_mul, hqa] at h1
    rw [h1]
    apply htr f
    rw [htp f hf, ← hact]
    exact twoSigned_act M.𝒜 t hts
  obtain ⟨⟨T₀, hT₀⟩, hlen, hineq⟩ := hLM c.O (PadicAlgCl l) _ hv M.A M.𝒜 M.x M.wring M.r M.s M.r' M.s' M.m
    M.Q a hM1 hM2 hM3 hM4 hM5
  have hpm : ∀ (f : S.R →ₐ[c.O] PadicAlgCl l) g i j,
      ((push f S.r.hom) g).mat i j = f ((S.r.hom g).mat i j) := fun f g i j => by
    show ((GG.map (f : S.R →+* PadicAlgCl l)) (S.r.hom g)).mat i j = _
    rw [GG.map_mat]
    rfl
  have hpr : ∀ (f : S.R →ₐ[c.O] PadicAlgCl l) g, ((push f S.r.hom) g).right = (S.r.hom g).right :=
    fun f g => GG.map_right _ _
  have hext : ∀ r₁ r₂ : Γ →* GG 3 (PadicAlgCl l), (∀ g i j, (r₁ g).mat i j = (r₂ g).mat i j) →
      (∀ g, (r₁ g).right = (r₂ g).right) → r₁ = r₂ := by
    intro r₁ r₂ hm hr
    apply MonoidHom.ext
    intro g
    apply SemidirectProduct.ext
    · exact Units.ext (Matrix.ext (hm g))
    · exact hr g
  have hac : ∀ (f f' : S.R →ₐ[c.O] PadicAlgCl l) (t : (PadicAlgCl l)ˣ),
      f.comp M.q = act M.𝒜 (tz t) (f'.comp M.q) →
      push f S.r.hom = conjBy (tor _ t) (push f' S.r.hom) := by
    intro f f' t h
    apply hext
    · intro g i j
      rw [(conjBy_tor _ t).1, hpm, hpm]
      obtain ⟨e, he, hqe⟩ := M.ent g i j
      rw [← hqe]
      have h2 := congrArg (fun φ => φ e) h
      simp only [AlgHom.comp_apply] at h2
      rw [h2, act_tz M.𝒜 t _ he, AlgHom.comp_apply]
    · intro g
      rw [(conjBy_tor _ t).2.1, hpr, hpr]
  have hch : ∀ ξ ∈ T₀, ∃ f : S.R →ₐ[c.O] PadicAlgCl l, ∃ t : (PadicAlgCl l)ˣ,
      IsLocPt f ∧ act M.𝒜 (tz t) ξ = f.comp M.q := fun ξ hξ => by
    obtain ⟨t, f, h1, h2⟩ := M.pts hM2 ξ (hT₀.1 ξ hξ).1
    exact ⟨f, t, h1, h2⟩
  choose F tF hF using hch
  let Rel : (S.R →ₐ[c.O] PadicAlgCl l) → (S.R →ₐ[c.O] PadicAlgCl l) → Prop :=
    fun f' f => ∃ t : (PadicAlgCl l)ˣ, push f S.r.hom = conjBy (tor _ t) (push f' S.r.hom)
  have htrans : ∀ f₁ f₂ f₃, Rel f₁ f₂ → Rel f₂ f₃ → Rel f₁ f₃ := by
    rintro f₁ f₂ f₃ ⟨t₁, h₁⟩ ⟨t₂, h₂⟩
    exact ⟨t₂ * t₁, by rw [h₂, h₁, conj_conj]⟩
  let T₁ := T₀.attach.image (fun ξ => F ξ.1 ξ.2)
  have hT₁ : (∀ f ∈ T₁, TwoPt η S.r.hom f) ∧ ∀ f, TwoPt η S.r.hom f → ∃ f' ∈ T₁, Rel f' f := by
    constructor
    · intro f hf
      obtain ⟨⟨ξ, hξ⟩, -, rfl⟩ := Finset.mem_image.1 hf
      rw [htp _ (hF ξ hξ).1, ← (hF ξ hξ).2]
      exact twoSigned_act M.𝒜 _ (hT₀.1 ξ hξ).2
    · intro f hf
      obtain ⟨ξ', hξ', t, hξeq⟩ := hT₀.2 _ (hon f) ((htp f hf.1).1 hf)
      have hf' := hF ξ' hξ'
      have hξ'' : ξ' = act M.𝒜 (tz (tF ξ' hξ')⁻¹) ((F ξ' hξ').comp M.q) := by
        rw [← hf'.2, act_act, inv_mul_cancel, act_one]
      refine ⟨F ξ' hξ', Finset.mem_image.2 ⟨⟨ξ', hξ'⟩, Finset.mem_attach _ _, rfl⟩, t * (tF ξ' hξ')⁻¹, ?_⟩
      apply hac
      rw [hξeq]
      exact (congrArg (act M.𝒜 (tz t)) hξ'').trans (act_act M.𝒜 _ _ _)
  obtain ⟨T, hTP, hirr⟩ := exists_irred Rel htrans _ T₁ hT₁
  refine ⟨hlen, T, hTP, fun f hf f' hf' t h => hirr f hf f' hf' ⟨t, h⟩, ?_⟩
  let T' := T.image (fun f => f.comp M.q)
  have hcov : Covers M.𝒜 M.Q.ideal T' := by
    constructor
    · intro ξ hξ
      obtain ⟨f, hf, rfl⟩ := Finset.mem_image.1 hξ
      exact ⟨hon f, (htp f (hTP.1 f hf).1).1 (hTP.1 f hf)⟩
    · intro ξ hξ hts
      obtain ⟨t, f, hfl, hact⟩ := M.pts hM2 ξ hξ
      have hf2 : TwoPt η S.r.hom f := by
        rw [htp f hfl, ← hact]
        exact twoSigned_act M.𝒜 t hts
      obtain ⟨f', hf', t', hpush⟩ := hTP.2 f hf2
      have hcq := (hsg f' (hTP.1 f' hf').1).2.2 f hfl t' hpush
      refine ⟨f'.comp M.q, Finset.mem_image.2 ⟨f', hf', rfl⟩, t⁻¹ * t', ?_⟩
      rw [← act_act, ← hcq, ← hact, act_act, inv_mul_cancel, act_one]
  have hinj : Set.InjOn (fun f : S.R →ₐ[c.O] PadicAlgCl l => f.comp M.q) T := by
    intro f hf f' hf' h
    exact hirr f hf f' hf' ⟨1, hac f' f 1 (by rw [act_one]; exact h.symm)⟩
  have hsum := hineq T' hcov
  rw [Finset.sum_image hinj] at hsum
  simp only [AlgHom.comp_apply, hqa] at hsum
  rw [← Finset.mul_sum] at hsum
  have hepos : (0 : ℝ) < ((UpperTheory.V.Base.eO l c.O).toNat : ℝ) := by
    have hne : UpperTheory.V.Base.eO l c.O ≠ ⊤ := ne_top_of_le_ne_top (ENat.coe_ne_top _) hefin
    have hz : (UpperTheory.V.Base.eO l c.O).toNat ≠ 0 := by
      rw [Ne, ENat.toNat_eq_zero]
      push_neg
      exact ⟨he0, hne⟩
    exact_mod_cast Nat.pos_of_ne_zero hz
  rw [UpperTheory.V.Chr.nrm, div_le_iff₀ hepos]
  have hML : M.len = cotLen M.x M.𝒜 (-1) M.Q.F := rfl
  rw [hML]
  linarith


[OCH_SRC_LENGTH_CONTRACT] All M1--M5, count, valuation, LemmaM and LemmaM-prime contracts
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/CommutativeAlgebra/OrbitVanishingLocal.lean#L9-L128
structure IsNVal (O K : Type) [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [Field K] [Algebra O K]
    (v : K → ℝ) : Prop where
  mul : ∀ x y, x ≠ 0 → y ≠ 0 → v (x * y) = v x + v y
  add : ∀ x y, x ≠ 0 → y ≠ 0 → x + y ≠ 0 → min (v x) (v y) ≤ v (x + y)
  base : ∀ c : O, c ≠ 0 → v (algebraMap O K c) = ((IsDiscreteValuationRing.addVal O c).toNat : ℝ)

abbrev tz {K : Type} [CommRing K] (t : Kˣ) : Multiplicative ℤ →* Kˣ := zpowersHom Kˣ t

section Data

variable {O A : Type} [CommRing O] [CommRing A] [Algebra O A] (𝒜 : ℤ → Submodule O A) [GradedAlgebra 𝒜]

structure IsWRing [IsLocalRing O] (x : A →ₐ[O] O) : Prop where
  co : IsCO O (𝒜 0)
  gen : ∃ s : Finset A, (∀ z ∈ s, z ∈ 𝒜 (-2) ∨ z ∈ 𝒜 (-1) ∨ z ∈ 𝒜 1 ∨ z ∈ 𝒜 2) ∧
    Algebra.adjoin O ((𝒜 0 : Set A) ∪ s) = ⊤
  fixed : ∀ j, j ≠ 0 → ∀ f ∈ 𝒜 j, x f = 0

variable {K : Type} [Field K] [Algebra O K]

def OnS (I : Ideal A) (ξ : A →ₐ[O] K) : Prop := ∀ f ∈ I, ξ f = 0

def NegZero (ξ : A →ₐ[O] K) : Prop := ∀ j < 0, ∀ f ∈ 𝒜 j, ξ f = 0

def PosZero (ξ : A →ₐ[O] K) : Prop := ∀ j > 0, ∀ f ∈ 𝒜 j, ξ f = 0

def TwoSigned (ξ : A →ₐ[O] K) : Prop := ¬ NegZero 𝒜 ξ ∧ ¬ PosZero 𝒜 ξ

def Same0 (ξ ξ' : A →ₐ[O] K) : Prop := ∀ b ∈ 𝒜 0, ξ b = ξ' b

def SameOrbit (ξ ξ' : A →ₐ[O] K) : Prop := ∃ t : Kˣ, ξ' = act 𝒜 (tz t) ξ

def M2 (I : Ideal A) : Prop := ((𝒜 0).map (Ideal.Quotient.mkₐ O I).toLinearMap).FG

variable (K) in

def M3 (x : A →ₐ[O] O) (I : Ideal A) : Prop :=
  ∀ x₁ : A →ₐ[O] K, OnS I x₁ → NegZero 𝒜 x₁ → PosZero 𝒜 x₁ → x₁ ≠ (Algebra.ofId O K).comp x →
    (¬ ∃ ξ : A →ₐ[O] K, OnS I ξ ∧ NegZero 𝒜 ξ ∧ ¬ PosZero 𝒜 ξ ∧ Same0 𝒜 ξ x₁) ∨
    (¬ ∃ ξ : A →ₐ[O] K, OnS I ξ ∧ PosZero 𝒜 ξ ∧ ¬ NegZero 𝒜 ξ ∧ Same0 𝒜 ξ x₁)

variable (K) in

def M4 (I : Ideal A) : Prop :=
  ∀ ξ : A →ₐ[O] K, OnS I ξ → TwoSigned 𝒜 ξ → ∀ D ∈ PtDer ξ K, ∀ D' ∈ PtDer ξ K, (∀ f ∈ I, D f = 0) →
    (∀ f ∈ I, D' f = 0) → ∃ c c' : K, (c ≠ 0 ∨ c' ≠ 0) ∧ c • D + c' • D' = 0

variable (K) in

def OrbitVanishingLocal (I : Ideal A) : Prop :=
  ∀ ξ : A →ₐ[O] K, OnS I ξ → TwoSigned 𝒜 ξ → ∀ f : TensorProduct O K A,
    (∀ t : Kˣ, Algebra.TensorProduct.lift (AlgHom.id O K) (act 𝒜 (tz t) ξ) (fun _ _ => Commute.all _ _) f = 0) →
    ∃ g : TensorProduct O K A, Algebra.TensorProduct.lift (AlgHom.id O K) ξ (fun _ _ => Commute.all _ _) g ≠ 0 ∧
      g * f ∈ I.map (Algebra.TensorProduct.includeRight : A →ₐ[O] TensorProduct O K A)

variable (K) in

def M5 (x : A →ₐ[O] O) (I : Ideal A) (a : A) : Prop :=
  a ∈ 𝒜 0 ∧ x a = 0 ∧ ∀ ξ : A →ₐ[O] K, OnS I ξ → TwoSigned 𝒜 ξ → ξ a ≠ 0

def Covers (I : Ideal A) (T : Finset (A →ₐ[O] K)) : Prop :=
  (∀ ξ ∈ T, OnS I ξ ∧ TwoSigned 𝒜 ξ) ∧ ∀ ξ, OnS I ξ → TwoSigned 𝒜 ξ → ∃ ξ' ∈ T, SameOrbit 𝒜 ξ' ξ

end Data

structure IsChart {O A X τ : Type} [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [CommRing A] [Algebra O A] [AddCommGroup X]
    (𝒜 : X → Submodule O A) (x : A →ₐ[O] O) (wt : τ → X) (φ : A →ₐ[O] MvPowerSeries τ (FractionRing O)) : Prop where
  graded : ∀ j, ∀ f ∈ 𝒜 j, φ f ∈ wtPart (FractionRing O) wt j
  const : ∀ f, constantCoeff (φ f) = algebraMap O (FractionRing O) (x f)
  onto : ∀ i, ∃ f : A, coeff (Finsupp.single i 1) (φ f) ≠ 0 ∧ ∀ i', i' ≠ i → coeff (Finsupp.single i' 1) (φ f) = 0
  inj : ∀ f, x f = 0 → (∀ i, coeff (Finsupp.single i 1) (φ f) = 0) → f ∈ satSq x

def SmoothPt {O A X : Type} [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [CommRing A] [Algebra O A] [AddCommGroup X]
    (𝒜 : X → Submodule O A) (x : A →ₐ[O] O) (n : X → ℕ) : Prop :=
  ∃ (τ : Type) (_ : Fintype τ) (wt : τ → X) (φ : A →ₐ[O] MvPowerSeries τ (FractionRing O)),
    IsChart 𝒜 x wt φ ∧ ∀ j, Nat.card {i // wt i = j} = n j

structure Eqns {O A : Type} [CommRing O] [CommRing A] [Algebra O A] (𝒜 : ℤ → Submodule O A) (x : A →ₐ[O] O)
    (r s r' s' m : ℕ) where
  F : Fin (r + 1) → A
  G : Fin s → A
  E : Fin r' → A
  E' : Fin s' → A
  Z : Fin m → A
  hF : ∀ i, F i ∈ 𝒜 (-1)
  hG : ∀ i, G i ∈ 𝒜 (-2)
  hE : ∀ i, E i ∈ 𝒜 1
  hE' : ∀ i, E' i ∈ 𝒜 2
  hZ : ∀ i, Z i ∈ 𝒜 0 ∧ x (Z i) = 0

def Eqns.ideal {O A : Type} [CommRing O] [CommRing A] [Algebra O A] {𝒜 : ℤ → Submodule O A} {x : A →ₐ[O] O}
    {r s r' s' m : ℕ} (Q : Eqns 𝒜 x r s r' s' m) : Ideal A :=
  Ideal.span (Set.range Q.F ∪ Set.range Q.G ∪ Set.range Q.E ∪ Set.range Q.E' ∪ Set.range Q.Z)

def mDims (r s r' s' m : ℕ) (j : ℤ) : ℕ :=
  if j = -1 then r else if j = -2 then s else if j = 1 then r' + 1 else if j = 2 then s' + 1 else
    if j = 0 then m else 0

def M1 {O A : Type} [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [CommRing A] [Algebra O A] {𝒜 : ℤ → Submodule O A} {x : A →ₐ[O] O}
    {r s r' s' m : ℕ} (Q : Eqns 𝒜 x r s r' s' m) : Prop :=
  1 ≤ r ∧ SmoothPt 𝒜 x (mDims r s r' s' m) ∧ SpansCot x 𝒜 (-1) Q.F ∧ SpansCot x 𝒜 (-2) Q.G ∧ SpansCot x 𝒜 0 Q.Z

def LemmaM : Prop :=
  ∀ (O : Type) [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal O) O] [CharZero O]
    (K : Type) [Field K] [Algebra O K] [IsAlgClosed K] [FaithfulSMul O K] (v : K → ℝ), IsNVal O K v →
  ∀ (A : Type) [CommRing A] [Algebra O A] (𝒜 : ℤ → Submodule O A) [GradedAlgebra 𝒜] (x : A →ₐ[O] O), IsWRing 𝒜 x →
  ∀ (r s r' s' m : ℕ) (Q : Eqns 𝒜 x r s r' s' m) (a : A),
    M1 Q → M2 𝒜 Q.ideal → M3 𝒜 K x Q.ideal → M4 𝒜 K Q.ideal → M5 𝒜 K x Q.ideal a →
    (∃ T : Finset (A →ₐ[O] K), Covers 𝒜 Q.ideal T) ∧ cotLen x 𝒜 (-1) Q.F ≠ ⊤ ∧
    ∀ T : Finset (A →ₐ[O] K), Covers 𝒜 Q.ideal T → ((cotLen x 𝒜 (-1) Q.F).toNat : ℝ) ≤ 2 * ∑ ξ ∈ T, v (ξ a)

def LemmaM' : Prop :=
  ∀ (O : Type) [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [Finite (ResidueField O)]
    (A X : Type) [CommRing A] [Algebra O A] [AddCommGroup X] [DecidableEq X] (𝒜 : X → Submodule O A) [GradedAlgebra 𝒜]
    (x : A →ₐ[O] O) (j : X), (∀ f ∈ 𝒜 j, x f = 0) →
  ∀ (ι : Type) (F : ι → A), (∀ i, F i ∈ 𝒜 j) →
  ∀ (c : O), c ≠ 0 → ∀ (M : Type) [AddCommGroup M] [Module O M] (D : M →+ (A →ₗ[O] O ⧸ Ideal.span {c})),
    Function.Injective D → (∀ μ i, D μ (F i) = 0) →
    (∀ μ, ∃ D' ∈ PtDer x O, HasWt 𝒜 j D' ∧ ∀ f, D μ f = Ideal.Quotient.mk _ (D' f)) →
    Module.length O M ≤ cotLen x 𝒜 j F


[OCH_SRC_COEFFICIENT_INDEPENDENCE] Original full coefficient-independence and normalized-length equality target
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/HeightAndVanishing/CoefIndep.lean#L1-L77
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.HeightAndVanishing.LayerLe
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.HeightAndVanishing.CoefSup
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.GaloisLibrary.SelmerTransport.QoExt
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.GaloisLibrary.SelmerTransport.DvrFacts

open NumberField IsDedekindDomain Formalism.ConjugateSelfDual Formalism.HC ReciprocityTheory.Gal Formalism.GaloisCohomology UpperTheory.V UpperTheory.V.Base UpperTheory.Top UpperTheory.V.HV

private theorem enat_cmp {a a' x x' : ℕ∞} (h : a' * x = a * x') (hx : x ≠ 0) (hx' : x' ≠ 0) (tx : x ≠ ⊤) (tx' : x' ≠ ⊤) :
    (a = ⊤ ↔ a' = ⊤) ∧ (a.toNat : ℝ) / (x.toNat : ℝ) = (a'.toNat : ℝ) / (x'.toNat : ℝ) := by
  lift x to ℕ using tx
  lift x' to ℕ using tx'
  have hx0 : (x : ℝ) ≠ 0 := by exact_mod_cast (fun h0 => hx (by simp [h0]))
  have hx0' : (x' : ℝ) ≠ 0 := by exact_mod_cast (fun h0 => hx' (by simp [h0]))
  induction a using ENat.recTopCoe with
  | top =>
    induction a' using ENat.recTopCoe with
    | top => simp
    | coe b =>
      exfalso
      rw [ENat.top_mul hx'] at h
      exact ENat.coe_ne_top _ (by exact_mod_cast h)
  | coe a =>
    induction a' using ENat.recTopCoe with
    | top =>
      exfalso
      rw [ENat.top_mul hx] at h
      exact ENat.coe_ne_top _ (by exact_mod_cast h.symm)
    | coe b =>
      have h' : b * x = a * x' := by exact_mod_cast h
      refine ⟨by simp, ?_⟩
      simp only [ENat.toNat_coe]
      rw [div_eq_div_iff hx0 hx0']
      exact_mod_cast h'.symm

private theorem eO_ne {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] (c : Coef l) : eO l c.O ≠ 0 ∧ eO l c.O ≠ ⊤ := by
  obtain ⟨-, -, -, -, -, h0, hle, -⟩ := dvrFacts l c.O
  exact ⟨h0, ne_top_of_le_ne_top (ENat.coe_ne_top _) hle⟩

private theorem coef_le (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (c c' : Coef l)
    (hle : c.O ≤ c'.O) (N : ℕ) [NeZero N] (e : ℕ) (ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O N) (ψ' : DirichletCharacter c'.O N)
    (hψ : ∀ a : ZMod N, ((ψ a : c.O) : PadicAlgCl l) = ((ψ' a : c'.O) : PadicAlgCl l)) :
    (Vlen Ω c e ψ = ⊤ ↔ Vlen Ω c' e ψ' = ⊤) ∧ Vn Ω c e ψ = Vn Ω c' e ψ' := by
  haveI := c.dvr
  haveI := c'.dvr
  haveI := c.fin
  haveI := c'.fin
  letI : Algebra c.O c'.O := (Subalgebra.inclusion hle).toRingHom.toAlgebra
  haveI : IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] c.O c'.O := IsScalarTower.of_algebraMap_eq (fun x => Subtype.ext rfl)
  have hcoe : ∀ y : c.O, ((algebraMap c.O c'.O y : c'.O) : PadicAlgCl l) = (y : PadicAlgCl l) := fun y => rfl
  set θ : Formalism.ConjugateSelfDual.G ℚ Ω →* c.O ˣ := (Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ)⁻¹ * chiO ℚ Ω l c.O ^ (-(e : ℤ)) with hθ
  obtain ⟨hq, -⟩ := qoExt l (Formalism.ConjugateSelfDual.G ℚ Ω) c.O c'.O θ ⊤ (shaFam ℚ Ω (l * N))
  have hmap : (Units.map (algebraMap c.O c'.O : c.O →* c'.O)).comp θ
      = (Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ')⁻¹ * chiO ℚ Ω l c'.O ^ (-(e : ℤ)) := by
    ext g
    have h1 : (Units.map (algebraMap c.O c'.O : c.O →* c'.O)) (Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ g) = Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ' g := by
      apply Units.ext
      apply Subtype.ext
      simp only [Units.coe_map, MonoidHom.coe_coe, hcoe]
      exact hψ _
    have h2 : (Units.map (algebraMap c.O c'.O : c.O →* c'.O)) (chiO ℚ Ω l c.O g) = chiO ℚ Ω l c'.O g := by
      apply Units.ext
      simp only [chiO, Units.coe_map, MonoidHom.coe_comp, Function.comp_apply, MonoidHom.coe_coe]
      exact (IsScalarTower.algebraMap_apply ℤ_[l] c.O c'.O _).symm
    simp only [hθ, MonoidHom.comp_apply, MonoidHom.mul_apply, MonoidHom.inv_apply, MonoidHom.zpow_apply, map_mul, map_inv, map_zpow, h1, h2]
  erw [hmap] at hq
  exact enat_cmp hq (eO_ne c).1 (eO_ne c').1 (eO_ne c).2 (eO_ne c').2

theorem UpperTheory.V.HV.coefIndep :
    ∀ (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (c c' : Coef l) (N : ℕ) [NeZero N] (e : ℕ) (ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O N) (ψ' : DirichletCharacter c'.O N),
      (∀ a : ZMod N, ((ψ a : c.O) : PadicAlgCl l) = ((ψ' a : c'.O) : PadicAlgCl l)) →
      (Vlen Ω c e ψ = ⊤ ↔ Vlen Ω c' e ψ' = ⊤) ∧ Vn Ω c e ψ = Vn Ω c' e ψ' := by
  intro Ω _ _ _ l _ c c' N _ e ψ ψ' h
  obtain ⟨c'', h1, h2⟩ := (coefSup l).1 c c'
  let ψ'' : DirichletCharacter c''.O N := ψ.ringHomComp (Subalgebra.inclusion h1).toRingHom
  obtain ⟨a1, b1⟩ := coef_le Ω l c c'' h1 N e ψ ψ'' fun _ => rfl
  obtain ⟨a2, b2⟩ := coef_le Ω l c' c'' h2 N e ψ' ψ'' fun a => (h a).symm
  exact ⟨a1.trans a2.symm, b1.trans b2.symm⟩
